          right_x: preservePaired ? observation.right_x : (recoveredWindow.match(/X+$/)?.[0].length || 0),
          seed_recovered: true });
        break;
      }
    }
  }
  return evidence;
}

export function applyClassification(
  readSeq: string,
  readQual: QualityScores | null,
  classes: ClassInfo[],
  phredThreshold: number,
  margin: number,
  cutSiteDistanceWeight: number = 0.0,
  cutSiteExclusionFlank: number = 0
): ClassificationResult {
  const eligibleClasses: Array<{ classInfo: ClassInfo; targetSeq: string }> = [];
  const evidence=classificationEvidence(readSeq, readQual, classes, phredThreshold);
  for (const [c, res] of evidence) {
    eligibleClasses.push({ classInfo: c, targetSeq: res.classification_window || res.read_window });
  }

  if (eligibleClasses.length === 0) {
    return { assigned: false, reason: 'filtered' };
  }

  const comparableScores=competitionScores(evidence,cutSiteDistanceWeight,cutSiteExclusionFlank);
  const scores: Array<[number, string, string]> = eligibleClasses.map(item => [
    comparableScores.get(item.classInfo)!,
    item.classInfo.gene,
    item.classInfo.target,
  ]);
  scores.sort((a, b) => b[0] - a[0]);

  const [top1Score, top1Gene, top1Target] = scores[0];

  if (classes.length === 1) {
    return {
      assigned: true,
      predicted_gene: top1Gene,
      predicted_target: top1Target,
      top1_score: Math.round(top1Score * 10000) / 10000,
    };
  }

  const top2Score = scores.length > 1 ? scores[1][0] : 0.0;
  const gap = top1Score - top2Score;

  if (gap >= margin) {
    return {
      assigned: true,
      predicted_gene: top1Gene,
      predicted_target: top1Target,
      top1_score: Math.round(top1Score * 10000) / 10000,
      top2_score: Math.round(top2Score * 10000) / 10000,
      gap: Math.round(gap * 10000) / 10000,
    };
  }

  return {
    assigned: false,
    reason: 'ambiguous',
    top1_score: Math.round(top1Score * 10000) / 10000,
  };
}

// ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
// Gene-Level Classification
// ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

export function applyGeneClassification(
  readSeq: string,
  readQual: QualityScores | null,
  geneClasses: Record<string, ClassInfo[]>,
  phredThreshold: number,
  margin: number,
  cutSiteDistanceWeight: number = 0.0,
  cutSiteExclusionFlank: number = 0
): ClassificationResult {
  const geneNames = Object.keys(geneClasses);

  // Unified gene-level classification: score each gene candidate by its best target window
  const geneScores: Array<[number, string]> = [];
  const geneDebug: Record<string, any> = {};
  let anyUsable = false;

  const evidence = classificationEvidence(readSeq, readQual, geneNames.flatMap(g => geneClasses[g]), phredThreshold);
  const comparableScores=competitionScores(evidence,cutSiteDistanceWeight,cutSiteExclusionFlank);

  for (const geneName of geneNames) {
    const targets = geneClasses[geneName];
    let bestScore = -1.0;
    let bestTarget: string | null = null;
    let geneUsable = false;
    let usableCount = 0;

    for (const t of targets) {
      const res = evidence.get(t);
      if (res) {
        geneUsable = true;
        anyUsable = true;
        usableCount++;
        const score = comparableScores.get(t)!;
        if (score > bestScore) {
          bestScore = score;
          bestTarget = t.target;
        }
      }
    }

    geneDebug[geneName] = {
      best_score: bestScore >= 0 ? Math.round(bestScore * 10000) / 10000 : null,
      best_target: bestTarget,
      usable: usableCount,
      total: targets.length,
    };

    if (geneUsable) geneScores.push([bestScore, geneName]);
  }

  if (!anyUsable) {
    return {
      assigned: false, reason: 'filtered',
      debug: {
        gene_scores: geneDebug,
        best_gene: null, second_gene: null, gap: null, margin,
        outcome: 'filtered_no_usable_window',
      },
    };
  }

  geneScores.sort((a, b) => b[0] - a[0]);
  const [top1Score, top1Gene] = geneScores[0];
  const recoveredWindows: Record<string, ValidatedTargetWindow> = {};
  for (const t of geneClasses[top1Gene]) {
    const res = evidence.get(t);
    if (res?.seed_recovered) recoveredWindows[t.target] = {
      ref_window: t.ref_window, cut_index_in_window: t.cut_index_in_window ?? -1, result: res
    };
  }
  const validatedWindows = Object.keys(recoveredWindows).length ? recoveredWindows : undefined;

  if (geneScores.length === 1) {
    return {
      assigned: true,
      predicted_gene: top1Gene,
      validated_windows: validatedWindows,
      top1_score: Math.round(top1Score * 10000) / 10000,
      debug: {
        gene_scores: geneDebug,
        best_gene: top1Gene, second_gene: null, gap: null, margin,
        outcome: 'assigned_only_one_gene_usable',
      },
    };
  }

  const [top2Score, top2Gene] = geneScores[1];
  const gap = top1Score - top2Score;

  const debugBlock: any = {
    gene_scores: geneDebug,
    best_gene: top1Gene, second_gene: top2Gene,
    gap: Math.round(gap * 10000) / 10000, margin,
  };

  if (gap >= margin) {
    debugBlock.outcome = 'assigned_margin_pass';
    return {
      assigned: true,
      predicted_gene: top1Gene,
      validated_windows: validatedWindows,
      top1_score: Math.round(top1Score * 10000) / 10000,
      top2_score: Math.round(top2Score * 10000) / 10000,
      gap: Math.round(gap * 10000) / 10000,
      debug: debugBlock,
    };
  }

  debugBlock.outcome = 'ambiguous_margin_fail';
  return {
    assigned: false, reason: 'ambiguous',
    top1_score: Math.round(top1Score * 10000) / 10000,
    top2_score: Math.round(top2Score * 10000) / 10000,
    gap: Math.round(gap * 10000) / 10000,
    debug: debugBlock,
  };
}

END MODULE


BEGIN MODULE src/app/workers/core/exact-mixture-fiber.ts
/** Exact rational arithmetic for equivalence certificates. Floating tolerances
 * are deliberately not used to decide whether two mixtures are identical. */
export interface ExactFraction {n:string;d:string}
interface Q {n:bigint;d:bigint}
const gcd=(a:bigint,b:bigint):bigint=>{a=a<0n?-a:a;while(b){const t=a%b;a=b;b=t;}return a;};
const q=(n:bigint,d=1n):Q=>{if(!n)return {n:0n,d:1n};if(d<0n){n=-n;d=-d;}const g=gcd(n,d);return {n:n/g,d:d/g};};
const add=(a:Q,b:Q)=>q(a.n*b.d+b.n*a.d,a.d*b.d),sub=(a:Q,b:Q)=>q(a.n*b.d-b.n*a.d,a.d*b.d),mul=(a:Q,b:Q)=>q(a.n*b.n,a.d*b.d),div=(a:Q,b:Q)=>q(a.n*b.d,a.d*b.n),cmp=(a:Q,b:Q)=>a.n*b.d-b.n*a.d;
const numberQ=(v:number):Q=>{if(!Number.isFinite(v))throw Error('Non-finite mixture coefficient.');if(!v)return q(0n);const view=new DataView(new ArrayBuffer(8));view.setFloat64(0,v);const bits=view.getBigUint64(0),exp=Number((bits>>52n)&2047n),mantissa=(bits&((1n<<52n)-1n))+(exp?1n<<52n:0n),power=(exp||1)-1023-52,sign=bits>>63n?-1n:1n;return power>=0?q(sign*(mantissa<<BigInt(power))):q(sign*mantissa,1n<<BigInt(-power));};
const encode=(v:Q):ExactFraction=>({n:String(v.n),d:String(v.d)}),decode=(v:ExactFraction)=>q(BigInt(v.n),BigInt(v.d));
export const exactNumber=(v:ExactFraction)=>Number(v.n)/Number(v.d);
export const exactProduct=(a:ExactFraction,b:ExactFraction)=>encode(mul(decode(a),decode(b)));
export const exactScale=(v:ExactFraction,numerator:number,denominator:number)=>encode(mul(decode(v),q(BigInt(numerator),BigInt(denominator))));
export const exactSubtract=(a:ExactFraction,b:ExactFraction)=>encode(sub(decode(a),decode(b)));
export const exactMin=(a:ExactFraction,b:ExactFraction)=>cmp(decode(a),decode(b))<=0?a:b;
export const exactSum=(values:ExactFraction[])=>encode(values.reduce((sum,v)=>add(sum,decode(v)),q(0n)));
export const exactFromNumber=(v:number)=>encode(numberQ(v));
export function exactWeightedEquality(a:{weight:ExactFraction;column:number[]}[],b:{weight:ExactFraction;column:number[]}[]):boolean{
 if(!a.length||!b.length||a[0].column.length!==b[0].column.length||[...a,...b].some(c=>c.column.length!==a[0].column.length))return false;
 const weightsA=a.map(c=>decode(c.weight)),weightsB=b.map(c=>decode(c.weight)),mass=(weights:Q[])=>weights.reduce(add,q(0n));if(cmp(mass(weightsA),mass(weightsB))!==0n)return false;
 const verified=new Set<string>();
 for(let k=0;k<a[0].column.length;k++){
  const common=a[0].column[k];if(a.every(c=>c.column[k]===common)&&b.every(c=>c.column[k]===common))continue;
  // Repeated channel patterns have exactly the same rational equation. Cache
  // the equation, never a tolerance or rounded fluorescence value.
  const pattern=JSON.stringify([...a.map(c=>c.column[k]),...b.map(c=>c.column[k])]);if(verified.has(pattern))continue;
  const sum=(rows:typeof a,weights:Q[])=>rows.reduce((s,c,i)=>add(s,mul(weights[i],numberQ(c.column[k]))),q(0n));if(cmp(sum(a,weightsA),sum(b,weightsB))!==0n)return false;verified.add(pattern);
 }
 return true;
}

export interface ExactHaplotype {sequence:string;weight:number;exactWeight:ExactFraction}
export interface HaplotypeRepresentatives {options:ExactHaplotype[][];verticesComplete:boolean;}
/** Feasible vertices of the same per-position marginal constraints. For large
 * tracts, deterministic quantile couplings give certified representatives.
 * The caller must additionally certify equality under its full emission model. */
export function exactHaplotypeRepresentatives(input:{sequence:string;weight:number}[],limit=16):HaplotypeRepresentatives{
 if(!input.length)return {options:[],verticesComplete:true};const length=input[0].sequence.length;
 if(!length||input.some(x=>x.sequence.length!==length||/[^ACGT]/.test(x.sequence)||x.weight<0))return {options:[],verticesComplete:false};
 const merged=new Map<string,Q>();for(const x of input)merged.set(x.sequence,add(merged.get(x.sequence)||q(0n),numberQ(x.weight)));
 const base=[...merged].filter(([,w])=>w.n>0n).sort(([a],[b])=>a.localeCompare(b)),mass=base.reduce((s,[,w])=>add(s,w),q(0n)),alphabet=Array.from({length},(_,p)=>[...new Set(base.map(([s])=>s[p]))].sort());
 const rowLabels=alphabet.flatMap((letters,p)=>letters.slice(0,-1).map(base=>({p,base}))),margins=alphabet.map((letters,p)=>new Map(letters.map(letter=>[letter,base.reduce((sum,[s,w])=>s[p]===letter?add(sum,w):sum,q(0n))]))),features=(word:string)=>[1,...rowLabels.map(({p,base})=>Number(word[p]===base))],rhs=[mass,...rowLabels.map(({p,base})=>margins[p].get(base)!)],rank=rhs.length;
 const toRows=(rows:[string,Q][])=>rows.filter(([,w])=>w.n>0n).sort(([a],[b])=>a.localeCompare(b)).map(([sequence,w])=>({sequence,weight:Number(w.n)/Number(w.d),exactWeight:encode(w)})),options:ExactHaplotype[][]=[toRows(base)],keys=new Set([JSON.stringify(options[0].map(x=>[x.sequence,x.exactWeight]))]);
 const keep=(rows:[string,Q][])=>{if(options.length>=limit)return;const result=toRows(rows),key=JSON.stringify(result.map(x=>[x.sequence,x.exactWeight]));if(!keys.has(key)){keys.add(key);options.push(result);}};
 let count=1;for(const a of alphabet){count*=a.length;if(count>16)break;}let verticesComplete=false;
 if(count<=16&&count>rank){let words=[''];for(const letters of alphabet)words=words.flatMap(prefix=>letters.map(b=>prefix+b));let combinations=1;for(let k=0;k<rank;k++)combinations=combinations*(words.length-k)/(k+1);
  if(combinations<=5000){let visited=0;const solve=(ids:number[])=>{const matrix=rhs.map((value,r)=>[...ids.map(id=>q(BigInt(features(words[id])[r]))),value]);for(let col=0;col<rank;col++){const pivot=matrix.findIndex((row,r)=>r>=col&&row[col].n!==0n);if(pivot<0)return;[matrix[col],matrix[pivot]]=[matrix[pivot],matrix[col]];const d=matrix[col][col];matrix[col]=matrix[col].map(v=>div(v,d));for(let r=0;r<rank;r++)if(r!==col){const scale=matrix[r][col];if(scale.n)matrix[r]=matrix[r].map((v,k)=>sub(v,mul(scale,matrix[col][k])));}}const values=matrix.map(row=>row[rank]);if(values.every(v=>v.n>=0n))keep(ids.map((id,i)=>[words[id],values[i]]));};
   const visit=(ids:number[],start:number)=>{if(options.length>=limit)return;if(ids.length===rank){visited++;solve(ids);return;}for(let i=start;i<=words.length-(rank-ids.length);i++)visit([...ids,i],i+1);};visit([],0);verticesComplete=visited===Math.round(combinations);
  }
 }
 // These couplings preserve marginals exactly without enumerating 4^length.
 if(!verticesComplete&&options.length<limit){const variable=alphabet.flatMap((a,p)=>a.length>1?[p]:[]),patterns=[new Set<number>(),new Set(variable),new Set(variable.filter((_,i)=>i%2===0)),...variable.map(p=>new Set([p]))];for(const reversed of patterns){if(options.length>=limit)break;const intervals=alphabet.map((letters,p)=>{let at=q(0n);return (reversed.has(p)?[...letters].reverse():letters).map(letter=>{const start=at;at=add(at,margins[p].get(letter)!);return {letter,start,end:at};});}),breaks=new Map<string,Q>();for(const rows of intervals)for(const x of rows)for(const point of [x.start,x.end])breaks.set(point.n+'/'+point.d,point);const sorted=[...breaks.values()].sort((a,b)=>cmp(a,b)<0n?-1:cmp(a,b)>0n?1:0),rows:[string,Q][]=[];for(let i=1;i<sorted.length;i++){const mid=div(add(sorted[i-1],sorted[i]),q(2n)),word=intervals.map(entries=>entries.find(x=>cmp(mid,x.start)>=0n&&cmp(mid,x.end)<0n)!.letter).join('');rows.push([word,sub(sorted[i],sorted[i-1])]);}keep(rows);}}
 return {options,verticesComplete};
}

/** Find a bounded mixture, then certify it with rational arithmetic. The
 * floating simplex only proposes active bounds; it can never certify equality.
 * A null result means no certificate was found, not a proof of infeasibility. */
export function exactBoundedMixture(columns:number[][],source:{weight:ExactFraction;column:number[]}[],bounds:{lower:ExactFraction;upper:ExactFraction}[]):ExactFraction[]|null{
 const n=columns.length;if(!n||bounds.length!==n||!source.length||columns.some(c=>c.length!==columns[0].length)||source.some(c=>c.column.length!==columns[0].length))return null;
 const lower=bounds.map(b=>decode(b.lower)),upper=bounds.map(b=>decode(b.upper));if(lower.some((l,i)=>l.n<0n||cmp(l,upper[i])>0n))return null;
 const equations:Q[][]=[],seen=new Set<string>();
 const keep=(coeff:number[],rhs:Q)=>{const key=JSON.stringify(coeff)+'='+rhs.n+'/'+rhs.d;if(!seen.has(key)){seen.add(key);equations.push([...coeff.map(numberQ),rhs]);}};
 keep(Array(n).fill(1),source.reduce((s,r)=>add(s,decode(r.weight)),q(0n)));
 for(let p=0;p<columns[0].length;p++)keep(columns.map(c=>c[p]),source.reduce((s,r)=>add(s,mul(decode(r.weight),numberQ(r.column[p]))),q(0n)));
 // Reduce redundant cycle/channel constraints before asking for a vertex.
 const basis:Q[][]=[],pivots:number[]=[];
 const insert=(input:Q[],required:boolean)=>{const row=input.slice();for(let i=0;i<basis.length;i++){const f=row[pivots[i]];if(f.n)for(let j=0;j<=n;j++)row[j]=sub(row[j],mul(f,basis[i][j]));}const pivot=row.findIndex((x,j)=>j<n&&x.n!==0n);if(pivot<0)return !required||row[n].n===0n;const scale=row[pivot];for(let j=0;j<=n;j++)row[j]=div(row[j],scale);for(let i=0;i<basis.length;i++){const f=basis[i][pivot];if(f.n)for(let j=0;j<=n;j++)basis[i][j]=sub(basis[i][j],mul(f,row[j]));}basis.push(row);pivots.push(pivot);return true;};
 for(const row of equations)if(!insert(row,true))return null;
 const reduced=basis.map(row=>row.slice()),reducedPivots=[...pivots],validate=(result:Q[])=>{if(result.some((x,i)=>cmp(x,lower[i])<0n||cmp(x,upper[i])>0n))return null;for(const row of equations)if(cmp(row.slice(0,n).reduce((s,x,i)=>add(s,mul(x,result[i])),q(0n)),row[n])!==0n)return null;return result.map(encode);};
 const rationalFallback=()=>{const free=Array.from({length:n},(_,i)=>i).filter(i=>!reducedPivots.includes(i)),constraints:Q[][]=[],rhs:Q[]=[];for(const [r,pivot]of reducedPivots.entries()){const row=reduced[r],coeff=free.map(j=>row[j]);constraints.push(coeff,coeff.map(v=>q(-v.n,v.d)));rhs.push(sub(row[n],lower[pivot]),sub(upper[pivot],row[n]));}for(const [j,id]of free.entries()){const row=free.map((_,k)=>q(j===k?1n:0n));constraints.push(row,row.map(v=>q(-v.n,v.d)));rhs.push(upper[id],q(-lower[id].n,lower[id].d));}const solution=free.length?rationalFeasibleSimplex(constraints,rhs):[];if(!solution)return null;const result=Array.from({length:n},()=>q(0n));free.forEach((id,i)=>result[id]=solution[i]);reduced.forEach((row,i)=>result[reducedPivots[i]]=sub(row[n],free.reduce((sum,id,k)=>add(sum,mul(row[id],solution[k])),q(0n))));return validate(result);};
 const value=(v:Q)=>Number(v.n)/Number(v.d),A:number[][]=[],b:number[]=[];
 for(const row of basis){const coefficients=row.slice(0,n).map(value),rhs=value(sub(row[n],row.slice(0,n).reduce((s,x,i)=>add(s,mul(x,lower[i])),q(0n))));A.push(coefficients,coefficients.map(v=>-v));b.push(rhs,-rhs);}
 for(let i=0;i<n;i++){A.push(Array.from({length:n},(_,j)=>Number(i===j)));b.push(value(sub(upper[i],lower[i])));}
 const proposal=feasibleSimplex(A,b);if(!proposal)return rationalFallback();
 const active=bounds.flatMap((_,i)=>[{i,v:lower[i],distance:Math.abs(proposal[i])},{i,v:upper[i],distance:Math.abs(proposal[i]-value(sub(upper[i],lower[i])))}]).sort((a,b)=>a.distance-b.distance);
 for(const bound of active){if(basis.length===n)break;if(bound.distance>1e-7)break;const row=Array.from({length:n+1},(_,j)=>j===n?bound.v:q(j===bound.i?1n:0n));insert(row,false);}
 if(basis.length!==n)return rationalFallback();const result=Array.from({length:n},()=>q(0n));basis.forEach((row,i)=>result[pivots[i]]=row[n]);return validate(result)||rationalFallback();
}
/** Phase-I simplex for a nonnegative feasibility proposal, Ax <= b. */
function feasibleSimplex(A:number[][],b:number[]):number[]|null{
 const m=b.length,n=A[0].length,B=Array.from({length:m},(_,i)=>n+i),N=Array.from({length:n+1},(_,i)=>i<n?i:-1),D=Array.from({length:m+2},()=>Array(n+2).fill(0));
 for(let i=0;i<m;i++){for(let j=0;j<n;j++)D[i][j]=A[i][j];D[i][n]=-1;D[i][n+1]=b[i];}D[m+1][n]=1;
 const pivot=(r:number,s:number)=>{const inv=1/D[r][s];for(let i=0;i<m+2;i++)if(i!==r)for(let j=0;j<n+2;j++)if(j!==s)D[i][j]-=D[r][j]*D[i][s]*inv;for(let j=0;j<n+2;j++)if(j!==s)D[r][j]*=inv;for(let i=0;i<m+2;i++)if(i!==r)D[i][s]*=-inv;D[r][s]=inv;[B[r],N[s]]=[N[s],B[r]];};
 let r=0;for(let i=1;i<m;i++)if(D[i][n+1]<D[r][n+1])r=i;
 if(D[r][n+1]<-1e-10){pivot(r,n);let done=false;for(let iteration=0;iteration<10000;iteration++){let s=0;for(let j=1;j<=n;j++)if(D[m+1][j]<D[m+1][s]-1e-10||Math.abs(D[m+1][j]-D[m+1][s])<=1e-10&&N[j]<N[s])s=j;if(D[m+1][s]>=-1e-10){done=true;break;}r=-1;for(let i=0;i<m;i++)if(D[i][s]>1e-10&&(r<0||D[i][n+1]/D[i][s]<D[r][n+1]/D[r][s]-1e-10||Math.abs(D[i][n+1]/D[i][s]-D[r][n+1]/D[r][s])<=1e-10&&B[i]<B[r]))r=i;if(r<0)return null;pivot(r,s);}if(!done||Math.abs(D[m+1][n+1])>1e-8)return null;}
 const x=Array(n).fill(0);for(let i=0;i<m;i++)if(B[i]>=0&&B[i]<n)x[B[i]]=D[i][n+1];return x;
}

/** Exact phase-I fallback on the reduced free-variable polytope. Bland's rule
 * avoids degenerate cycling. Exhaustion returns no certificate. */
function rationalFeasibleSimplex(A:Q[][],b:Q[]):Q[]|null{
 const m=b.length,n=A[0]?.length||0,B=Array.from({length:m},(_,i)=>n+i),N=Array.from({length:n+1},(_,i)=>i<n?i:-1),D=Array.from({length:m+1},()=>Array.from({length:n+2},()=>q(0n)));
 for(let i=0;i<m;i++){for(let j=0;j<n;j++)D[i][j]=A[i][j];D[i][n]=q(-1n);D[i][n+1]=b[i];}D[m][n]=q(1n);
 const pivot=(r:number,s:number)=>{const d=D[r][s];for(let i=0;i<=m;i++)if(i!==r&&D[i][s].n){const f=div(D[i][s],d);for(let j=0;j<n+2;j++)if(j!==s&&D[r][j].n)D[i][j]=sub(D[i][j],mul(f,D[r][j]));D[i][s]=q(-f.n,f.d);}for(let j=0;j<n+2;j++)if(j!==s)D[r][j]=div(D[r][j],d);D[r][s]=div(q(1n),d);[B[r],N[s]]=[N[s],B[r]];};
 let r=0;for(let i=1;i<m;i++)if(cmp(D[i][n+1],D[r][n+1])<0n)r=i;
 if(D[r][n+1].n<0n){pivot(r,n);let done=false;for(let iteration=0;iteration<5000;iteration++){let s=-1;for(let j=0;j<=n;j++)if(D[m][j].n<0n&&(s<0||N[j]<N[s]))s=j;if(s<0){done=true;break;}r=-1;for(let i=0;i<m;i++)if(D[i][s].n>0n&&(r<0||cmp(div(D[i][n+1],D[i][s]),div(D[r][n+1],D[r][s]))<0n||cmp(div(D[i][n+1],D[i][s]),div(D[r][n+1],D[r][s]))===0n&&B[i]<B[r]))r=i;if(r<0)return null;pivot(r,s);}if(!done||D[m][n+1].n!==0n)return null;}
 const x=Array.from({length:n},()=>q(0n));for(let i=0;i<m;i++)if(B[i]>=0&&B[i]<n)x[B[i]]=D[i][n+1];return x;
}

END MODULE


BEGIN MODULE src/app/workers/core/fastq-parser.ts
/**
 * fastq-parser.ts — Streaming FASTQ parser for browser-side analysis.
 *
 * Reads FASTQ files using the File API without loading the entire file
 * into memory. Yields (sequence, quality_scores) tuples matching the
 * output of BioPython's SeqIO.parse(handle, "fastq").
 *
 * FASTQ format (4 lines per record):
 *   Line 1: @identifier
 *   Line 2: sequence
 *   Line 3: + (separator)
 *   Line 4: quality string (ASCII-encoded Phred scores)
 */

import { Gunzip, gunzipSync, strFromU8 } from 'fflate';

/** FASTQ Phred scores are bytes (0–93), not general-purpose numbers. */
export type QualityScores = number[] | Uint8Array;

export interface FastqRead {
  id?: string;
  seq: string;
  qual: QualityScores;
}

export function normalizeFastqReadId(identifier: string): string {
  const firstToken = identifier.trim().replace(/^@/, '').split(/\s+/)[0] || '';
  return firstToken.replace(/\/[12]$/, '');
}

/**
 * Parse an entire FASTQ file from a string.
 * Used in Web Workers where the file has already been read to text.
 */
export function parseFastqString(text: string): FastqRead[] {
  const results: FastqRead[] = [];
  const lines = text.split('\n');

  let i = 0;
  while (i < lines.length) {
    // Skip empty lines
    if (lines[i].trim() === '') {
      i++;
      continue;
    }

    // Line 1: identifier (starts with @)
    if (!lines[i].startsWith('@')) {
      i++;
      continue;
    }

    // Ensure we have all 4 lines
    if (i + 3 >= lines.length) break;

    // Line 2: sequence
    const seq = lines[i + 1].trim();

    // Line 3: separator (starts with +)
    // Line 4: quality string
    const qualStr = lines[i + 3].trim();

    if (seq.length > 0 && qualStr.length > 0) {
      // Convert ASCII quality to Phred scores (ASCII - 33)
      const qual = new Uint8Array(qualStr.length);
      for (let q = 0; q < qualStr.length; q++) {
        qual[q] = qualStr.charCodeAt(q) - 33;
      }
      results.push({ id: lines[i].trim().replace(/^@/, ''), seq, qual });
    }

    i += 4;
  }

  return results;
}

/**
 * Legacy text helper used only by the explicit “export filtered FASTQ” action.
 * Analysis itself uses `parseFastqFile()` above and never takes this path.
 */
export async function readFileAsText(file: File): Promise<string> {
  const buffer = await file.arrayBuffer();
  return /\.gz$/i.test(file.name)
    ? strFromU8(gunzipSync(new Uint8Array(buffer)))
    : new TextDecoder().decode(buffer);
}

/**
 * Read files in small chunks. The former whole-file `arrayBuffer()` path made
 * Safari keep the compressed file, decompressed text, line array, and parsed
 * reads alive together — enough to reload a tab during multi-file analysis.
 */
export async function parseFastqFileInBatches(
  file: File,
  onBatch: (reads: FastqRead[]) => Promise<void> | void,
  batchSize = 10000,
): Promise<number> {
  const decoder = new TextDecoder();
  const chunkSize = 1024 * 1024;
  let pending = '';
  let batch: FastqRead[] = [];
  let parsedCount = 0;
  const readyBatches: FastqRead[][] = [];

  const queueBatch = () => {
    if (!batch.length) return;
    readyBatches.push(batch);
    batch = [];
  };
  const flushReadyBatches = async () => {
    while (readyBatches.length) await onBatch(readyBatches.shift()!);
  };

  const consume = (text: string) => {
    pending += text;
    const lines = pending.split(/\r?\n/);
    pending = lines.pop() || '';
    let index = 0;
    while (index + 3 < lines.length) {
      if (!lines[index].trim() || !lines[index].startsWith('@')) {
        index++;
        continue;
      }
      const seq = lines[index + 1].trim();
      const quality = lines[index + 3].trim();
      if (seq && quality) {
        const qual = new Uint8Array(quality.length);
        for (let q = 0; q < quality.length; q++) qual[q] = quality.charCodeAt(q) - 33;
        batch.push({ id: lines[index].trim().replace(/^@/, ''), seq, qual });
        parsedCount++;
        if (batch.length >= batchSize) queueBatch();
      }
      index += 4;
    }
    // At most one incomplete four-line FASTQ record is kept for the next chunk.
    pending = lines.slice(index).join('\n') + (pending ? `\n${pending}` : '');
  };

  const emit = (chunk: Uint8Array, final = false) => {
    const text = decoder.decode(chunk, { stream: !final });
    if (text) consume(text);
  };
  const isGz = /\.gz$/i.test(file.name);
  const gunzip = isGz ? new Gunzip((chunk, final) => emit(chunk, final)) : null;

  for (let offset = 0; offset < file.size; offset += chunkSize) {
    const end = Math.min(offset + chunkSize, file.size);
    const chunk = new Uint8Array(await file.slice(offset, end).arrayBuffer());
    if (gunzip) gunzip.push(chunk, end === file.size);
    else emit(chunk, end === file.size);
    await flushReadyBatches();
  }
  if (pending) {
    const trailing = parseFastqString(pending);
    batch.push(...trailing);
    parsedCount += trailing.length;
  }
  queueBatch();
  await flushReadyBatches();
  return parsedCount;
}

/** Parse a FASTQ file when a complete in-memory read list is genuinely needed. */
export async function parseFastqFile(file: File): Promise<FastqRead[]> {
  const reads: FastqRead[] = [];
  await parseFastqFileInBatches(file, batch => { reads.push(...batch); });
  return reads;
}

END MODULE


BEGIN MODULE src/app/workers/core/grna-designer.ts
import {GuideReference,GuideSettings,GuideSite,GuideCandidate,GuideResult} from '../../models/grna-design';
import {reverseComplement} from '../../utils/biology.utils';
/** Coordinates are zero-based half-open. Cut N is the boundary after input base N. */
export function scanSpCas9(sequence:string,reference:number):GuideSite[]{
 const sites:GuideSite[]=[];const n=sequence.length;
 for(const strand of ['+','-'] as const){const dna=strand==='+'?sequence:reverseComplement(sequence);for(let p=0;p+23<=n;p++){
  if(dna[p+21]!=='G'||dna[p+22]!=='G')continue;
  const spacer=dna.slice(p,p+20),pam=dna.slice(p+20,p+23);if(!/^[ACGT]{20}$/.test(spacer)||!/^[ACGT]GG$/.test(pam))continue;
  sites.push({reference,start:strand==='+'?p:n-p-20,end:strand==='+'?p+20:n-p,pamStart:strand==='+'?p+20:n-p-23,pamEnd:strand==='+'?p+23:n-p-20,pam,strand,cut:strand==='+'?p+17:n-p-17,sequence:spacer});
 }}return sites;
}
export function validateGuideInput(refs:GuideReference[],backgrounds:{name:string;sequence:string}[],s:GuideSettings){
 if(!refs.length||refs.length>8)throw new Error('Use 1–8 target references.');
 const all=[...refs,...backgrounds];if(all.some(r=>!r.name.trim())||new Set(all.map(r=>r.name)).size!==all.length)throw new Error('Give every target and background a unique name.');
 if(all.some(r=>!r.sequence||/[^ACGTN]/.test(r.sequence)))throw new Error('Use DNA references containing A/C/G/T/N.');
 if(all.some(r=>r.sequence.length>500000)||all.reduce((sum,r)=>sum+r.sequence.length,0)>2000000)throw new Error('Use ≤500 kb per reference and ≤2 Mb total, including backgrounds.');
 if(!['single','shared','specific'].includes(s.mode)||!Number.isInteger(s.mismatches)||s.mismatches<0||s.mismatches>3)throw new Error('Choose a valid target mode and 0–3 mismatches.');
 if(!Number.isFinite(s.gcMin)||!Number.isFinite(s.gcMax)||s.gcMin<0||s.gcMax>100||s.gcMin>s.gcMax)throw new Error('Use a valid GC interval within 0–100%.');
 if(!Number.isInteger(s.maxCandidates)||s.maxCandidates<1||s.maxCandidates>2000)throw new Error('Screen 1–2,000 ranked candidates.');
 const selected=refs.filter(r=>r.selected);if(s.mode==='single'?selected.length!==1:selected.length<2)throw new Error(s.mode==='single'?'Select exactly one target reference.':'Select at least two target references.');
 refs.forEach(r=>{if((r.regionStart!==null&&(!Number.isInteger(r.regionStart)||r.regionStart<1||r.regionStart>r.sequence.length))||(r.regionEnd!==null&&(!Number.isInteger(r.regionEnd)||r.regionEnd<1||r.regionEnd>r.sequence.length))||(r.regionStart!==null&&r.regionEnd!==null&&r.regionStart>r.regionEnd))throw new Error(`${r.name}: check the 1-based cut-region bounds.`);});
}
export function designGuides(refs:GuideReference[],backgrounds:{name:string;sequence:string}[],settings:GuideSettings,progress:(stage:string)=>void=()=>{}):GuideResult{
 validateGuideInput(refs,backgrounds,settings);const all=[...refs,...backgrounds];progress('Scanning both strands for NGG PAMs');const scanned=all.flatMap((r,i)=>scanSpCas9(r.sequence,i)),exact=new Map<string,GuideSite[]>();for(const site of scanned){if(!exact.has(site.sequence))exact.set(site.sequence,[]);exact.get(site.sequence)!.push(site);}
 const targets=refs.flatMap((r,i)=>r.selected?[i]:[]),pool:GuideCandidate[]=[];
 const inRegion=(h:GuideSite)=>{const r=refs[h.reference];return h.cut>=(r.regionStart??1)&&h.cut<=(r.regionEnd??r.sequence.length);};
 for(const [sequence,allSites] of exact){const gc=100*[...sequence].filter(b=>'GC'.includes(b)).length/20,polyT=sequence.includes('TTTT');if(gc<settings.gcMin||gc>settings.gcMax||settings.excludePolyT&&polyT)continue;
  const groups=targets.map(i=>allSites.filter(h=>h.reference===i));const make=(sites:GuideSite[],suffix:string)=>pool.push({id:sequence+suffix,sequence,gc,polyT,sites,otherHits:[],nearestOtherMismatch:null,exampleOtherSites:[]});
  if(settings.mode==='shared'){if(groups.every(g=>g.length&&g.some(inRegion)&&(!settings.uniqueTargets||g.length===1)))make(groups.flatMap(g=>g.filter(inRegion)),'-shared');}
  else for(let k=0;k<targets.length;k++){const g=groups[k];if(!g.length||settings.uniqueTargets&&g.length!==1)continue;const eligible=g.filter(inRegion);if(eligible.length)make(eligible,'-'+targets[k]);}
 }
 const distance=(c:GuideCandidate)=>c.sites.reduce((sum,h)=>{const r=refs[h.reference];return sum+(r.regionStart!==null||r.regionEnd!==null?Math.abs(h.cut-((r.regionStart??1)+(r.regionEnd??r.sequence.length))/2):0);},0)/c.sites.length;
 pool.sort((a,b)=>distance(a)-distance(b)||Math.abs(a.gc-50)-Math.abs(b.gc-50)||Number(a.polyT)-Number(b.polyT)||a.sequence.localeCompare(b.sequence)||a.id.localeCompare(b.id));
 // Pigeonhole retrieval: <=m substitutions guarantees one unchanged disjoint seed.
 const parts=settings.mismatches+1,index=Array.from({length:parts},()=>new Map<string,number[]>()),bounds=Array.from({length:parts},(_,i)=>[Math.floor(i*20/parts),Math.floor((i+1)*20/parts)]);
 scanned.forEach((h,i)=>bounds.forEach(([lo,hi],k)=>{const key=h.sequence.slice(lo,hi);if(!index[k].has(key))index[k].set(key,[]);index[k].get(key)!.push(i);}));
 const checked=pool.slice(0,settings.maxCandidates),candidates:GuideCandidate[]=[];
 checked.forEach((c,i)=>{if(i%50===0)progress(`Screening input-reference near sites ${i+1}/${checked.length}`);const ids=new Set<number>();bounds.forEach(([lo,hi],k)=>index[k].get(c.sequence.slice(lo,hi))?.forEach(id=>ids.add(id)));const intended=new Set(c.sites.map(h=>`${h.reference}:${h.start}:${h.strand}`));c.otherHits=Array(parts).fill(0);
  for(const id of ids){const h=scanned[id];if(intended.has(`${h.reference}:${h.start}:${h.strand}`))continue;let mm=0;for(let j=0;j<20;j++)if(h.sequence[j]!==c.sequence[j]&&++mm>settings.mismatches)break;if(mm>settings.mismatches)continue;c.otherHits[mm]++;c.nearestOtherMismatch=Math.min(c.nearestOtherMismatch??Infinity,mm);if(c.exampleOtherSites.length<20)c.exampleOtherSites.push({...h,mismatches:mm});}
  if(settings.mode!=='specific'||c.otherHits.every(n=>n===0))candidates.push(c);
 });
 candidates.sort((a,b)=>a.otherHits.reduce((s,n)=>s+n,0)-b.otherHits.reduce((s,n)=>s+n,0)||distance(a)-distance(b)||Math.abs(a.gc-50)-Math.abs(b.gc-50)||a.id.localeCompare(b.id));
 return {references:refs,backgrounds,settings:{...settings},candidates,eligible:pool.length,screened:checked.length,complete:checked.length===pool.length,sitesScanned:scanned.length};
}

END MODULE


BEGIN MODULE src/app/workers/core/sanger-allele-groups-oracle-prototype.ts
import {linearSequenceOracle} from './sanger-linear-sequence-oracle';
import {discoverExactAlternatives} from './sanger-exact-alternatives';
import {substitutionLinkageAliases} from './sanger-substitution-linkage';
import {compoundBaseProposals} from './sanger-compound-refine';
import {SangerFitCache,rawMixtureMse} from './sanger-fit-cache';
import {CandidateSignalScreen} from './sanger-candidate-screen';
import {polishSignalDictionary} from './sanger-dictionary-polish';
import {substitutionProposals} from './sanger-substitution-search';
import {normalizeMoleculeEdits} from './sanger-edit-normalize';
import {repairSignalTraceCycles} from './sanger-cycle';
import {SangerAnalysisResult,SangerJointEvent,SangerTrace,TRACE_BASES} from '../../models/sanger-analysis';
import {reverseComplement} from '../../utils/biology.utils';
import {registerTrace} from './sanger-pooled';
import {contrastPeaks,integratedPeaks,calibrateSignal,correctGain,gramCondition,rawSignalScore} from './sanger-signal';
import {emissionVector,fitContextEmission,fitNormalizedEmissionMixture as fitEmissionMixtureOriginal} from './sanger-emission';
import {plotTrace} from './sanger-analysis';
const fitEmissionMixture=(columns:number[][],observed:number[])=>fitEmissionMixtureOriginal(columns,observed);
import {SignalPathInput,PathEdit,Model,materialize,prepare,assertSignalTraceGrid,eventsRespectIndelOrigin} from './sanger-path-common';
/** Whole-molecule candidate discovery and joint fluorescence refit. These are
 * best-fit cut-local hypotheses, not a posterior over physical haplotypes.
 * All measured positions enter discovery, so no independent held-out score is claimed. */
export function analyzeAlleleGroupsOracle(input:SignalPathInput,progress:(s:string)=>void=()=>{},audit?:(state:any)=>void):SangerAnalysisResult{
 if(!input.ampliconConfirmed||!input.control||!input.sample)throw Error('Confirm submitted products and provide matched control/sample chromatograms.');
 const controlRepair=repairSignalTraceCycles(input.control),sampleRepair=repairSignalTraceCycles(input.sample);input={...input,control:controlRepair.trace,sample:sampleRepair.trace};
 assertSignalTraceGrid(input.control);assertSignalTraceGrid(input.sample);
 const {refs,report,anchor,maxDeletion,maxInsertion}=prepare(input.amplicons,input.primer,input.settings),s=input.settings;
 progress('Registering traces to the sequencing primer');const registered=refs.map(r=>({name:r.name,sequence:r.sequence,cut:Math.min(...r.cuts)}));
 const cr=registerTrace(input.control,registered,s,integratedPeaks(input.control,[0,0,0,0]).vectors),sr=registerTrace(input.sample,registered,s,integratedPeaks(input.sample,[0,0,0,0]).vectors),ctl=contrastPeaks(input.control).vectors,raw=contrastPeaks(input.sample).vectors;
 const calibrate=(vectors:number[][],trim:number)=>{const obs=Array.from({length:vectors.length+trim},(_,p)=>vectors[p-trim]||[0,0,0,0]),rows=Array.from({length:Math.min(...refs.flatMap(r=>r.cuts))-12},(_,p)=>p).filter(p=>p>=Math.max(s.readStart,trim+10)&&p-trim<vectors.length-2);return calibrateSignal(obs,rows,refs.map(r=>Array.from(r.sequence,(_,p)=>TRACE_BASES.map(b=>Number(r.sequence[p]===b)))));};
 const cc=calibrate(ctl,cr.trim),sc=calibrate(raw,sr.trim);if(cc.validationR2<.8||sc.validationR2<.8)throw Error('Signal-path upstream calibration failed.');
 const end=Math.min(Math.max(...report.positions)+1,cr.trim+ctl.length-3,sr.trim+raw.length-3),positions=report.positions.filter(p=>p<end);if(positions.length<120)throw Error('Insufficient actual trace coverage for path inference.');
 const wt=ctl.map(v=>correctGain(v,cc.gains)),sample=raw.map(v=>correctGain(v,sc.gains)),controlObs=Array.from({length:wt.length+cr.trim},(_,p)=>wt[p-cr.trim]||[0,0,0,0]);
 const context=fitContextEmission(refs.map(r=>r.sequence),positions,controlObs,cc.weights,cc.lag),theta=context.theta;
 const emit=(sequence:string,p:number,lag:number)=>{const v=emissionVector(sequence,p,lag,theta);if(sequence[p]==='N')for(let b=0;b<4;b++)v[b]+=(1-Math.abs(lag))/4;return v;};
 const models:Model[]=refs.map((r,h)=>({reference:h,sequence:r.sequence,events:[],aliases:[{reference:h,events:[]}]})),cols=models.map(m=>positions.flatMap(p=>emit(m.sequence,p,sc.lag))),controlCols=models.map(m=>positions.flatMap(p=>emit(m.sequence,p,cc.lag)));
 const ctlFit=fitEmissionMixture(controlCols,positions.flatMap(p=>wt[p-cr.trim])),ctlScore=rawSignalScore(controlCols,positions.flatMap(p=>ctl[p-cr.trim]),ctlFit.weights,cc.gains);if(ctlScore.r2<.85)throw Error('WT product references do not explain the unedited control.');
 const y=positions.flatMap(p=>sample[p-sr.trim]),train=positions.map((_,i)=>i),tune=train,test=train,subset=(v:number[],rows:number[])=>rows.flatMap(i=>v.slice(4*i,4*i+4));
 const positionIndex=new Int32Array(end+maxInsertion+2).fill(-1);positions.forEach((p,i)=>positionIndex[p]=i);
 const rawY=positions.flatMap(p=>raw[p-sr.trim]),fitCache=new SangerFitCache(cols,y);
 const fitIds=(ids:number[])=>fitCache.fit(ids);
 const score=(ids:number[],weights:number[],rows:number[])=>rawSignalScore(ids.map(id=>rows===train?cols[id]:subset(cols[id],rows)),rows===train?rawY:rows.flatMap(i=>raw[positions[i]-sr.trim]),weights,sc.gains);
 const objective=(ids:number[],weights:number[])=>rawMixtureMse(ids.map(id=>cols[id]),rawY,weights,sc.gains)+.0000001*ids.reduce((sum,id,h)=>sum+weights[h]*models[id].events.reduce((a,e)=>a+1+.05*e.inserted.length,0),0);
 let ids=refs.map((_,i)=>i),fit=fitIds(ids),best=objective(ids,fit.weights),iterations=0,alignments=0,substitutionRounds=0,insertionPolishes=0,discoveryLimited=false;
 const multiTarget=refs.some(r=>r.cuts.length>1),screen=new CandidateSignalScreen(cols);

 // Experimental fully corrective column generation. No truth or recipe input.
 if(context.used)throw Error('Unit-emission oracle prototype excludes context-dependent yields.');
 const signature=(sequence:string)=>sequence.slice(positions[0],positions.at(-1)!+1).padEnd(positions.length,'-');
 const seenEdits=refs.map(()=>new Set<string>()),signatures=new Map(models.map((m,id)=>[signature(m.sequence),id]));
  const addVariant=(h:number,events:PathEdit[])=>{events=normalizeMoleculeEdits(refs[h].sequence,events);const editKey=JSON.stringify(events);if(seenEdits[h].has(editKey))return;seenEdits[h].add(editKey);if(!eventsRespectIndelOrigin(events,refs[h].cuts,s.editStartRadius))return;const sequence=materialize(refs[h].sequence,events);if(!sequence)return;const key=signature(sequence),old=signatures.get(key),alias={reference:h,events};if(old!==undefined){if(!models[old].aliases.some(a=>a.reference===h&&JSON.stringify(a.events)===JSON.stringify(events)))models[old].aliases.push(alias);return;}const id=models.length;models.push({reference:h,sequence,events,aliases:[alias]});cols.push(positions.flatMap(p=>emit(sequence,p,sc.lag)));signatures.set(key,id);};

 let complete=false;
 for(let round=0;round<256;round++){
  progress(`Optimizing sequence mixture · round ${round+1}`);
  const cost=Array.from({length:end+2*maxInsertion+4},()=>[0,0,0,0]);
  for(let k=0;k<positions.length;k++){
   const value=[0,0,0,0];for(let j=0;j<ids.length;j++)for(let b=0;b<4;b++)value[b]+=fit.weights[j]*cols[ids[j]][4*k+b];
   const total=value.reduce((a,b)=>a+b,0);if(!total)continue;const prediction=value.map(v=>v/total),residual=prediction.map((v,b)=>v-y[4*k+b]),center=prediction.reduce((sum,v,b)=>sum+v*residual[b],0),p=positions[k],q=p+(sc.lag<0?1:-1);
   for(let b=0;b<4;b++){const gradient=(residual[b]-center)/total;cost[p][b]+=(1-Math.abs(sc.lag))*gradient;if(q>=0&&q<cost.length)cost[q][b]+=Math.abs(sc.lag)*gradient;}
  }
  const proposed:number[]=[];let maximumGap=0;
  for(let h=0;h<refs.length;h++){
   const oracle=linearSequenceOracle({reference:refs[h].sequence,cuts:refs[h].cuts,cost,radius:s.editStartRadius??10,maxDeletion,maxInsertion,maxSubstitution:10});maximumGap=Math.max(maximumGap,-oracle.cost/y.length);
   if(-oracle.cost/y.length<=1e-10)continue;addVariant(h,oracle.events);const id=signatures.get(signature(oracle.sequence));if(id!==undefined&&!ids.includes(id))proposed.push(id);
  }
  if(!proposed.length){complete=maximumGap<=1e-9;break;}
  ids=[...new Set([...ids,...proposed])];fit=fitIds(ids);ids=ids.filter((id,j)=>id<refs.length||fit.weights[j]>1e-9);fit=fitIds(ids);iterations++;
 }
 discoveryLimited=!complete;
 progress('Validating fitted target profiles');
 const qc=score(ids,fit.weights,test),full=qc,geometry=gramCondition(ids.map(id=>cols[id]));
 const net=(events:PathEdit[])=>events.reduce((sum,e)=>sum+e.inserted.length-(e.end-e.start),0),inputEvents=(h:number,events:PathEdit[]):SangerJointEvent[]=>{const r=refs[h];return events.map(e=>{const distances=r.cuts.map(c=>Math.min(Math.abs(e.start-c),Math.abs(e.end-c))),near=distances.indexOf(Math.min(...distances)),span=r.cuts.length===2&&e.start<=r.cuts[0]&&e.end>=r.cuts[1],target=span?0:Math.min(...distances)>60?-1:(r.reverse?r.cuts.length-near:near+1),a=r.offset+e.start,b=r.offset+e.end;return {target,start:r.reverse?r.input.sequence.length-b:a,end:r.reverse?r.input.sequence.length-a:b,inserted:e.inserted.length,insertedSequence:r.reverse?reverseComplement(e.inserted):e.inserted};}).sort((a,b)=>a.start-b.start);};
 const pathSizes=(h:number,events:PathEdit[])=>{const sizes=Array(refs[h].cuts.length).fill(0);for(const e of inputEvents(h,events)){if(e.target>0)sizes[e.target-1]+=e.inserted-(e.end-e.start);}return sizes;};
 const chosen=ids.flatMap((id,i)=>fit.weights[i]>1e-9?[{model:models[id],fraction:fit.weights[i]}]:[]),aliasKey=(a:{reference:number;events:PathEdit[]})=>a.reference+':'+JSON.stringify(inputEvents(a.reference,a.events));
 const ambiguous=chosen.filter(c=>new Set(c.model.aliases.map(a=>a.reference+':'+net(a.events)+':'+Number(a.events.length===0)+':'+[...new Set(inputEvents(a.reference,a.events).map(e=>e.target))].sort().join('/'))).size>1),refAmbiguous=ambiguous.filter(c=>new Set(c.model.aliases.map(a=>a.reference)).size>1);
 const semantic=(a:{reference:number;events:PathEdit[]})=>a.reference+':'+(a.events.length?'edit':'WT')+':'+net(a.events)+':'+[...new Set(inputEvents(a.reference,a.events).map(e=>e.target))].sort().join('/');
 const attributionAmbiguous=ambiguous.filter(c=>new Set(c.model.aliases.map(semantic)).size>1);
 const groups=chosen.map((c,i)=>({id:`P${i+1}`,reference:refAmbiguous.includes(c)?null:refs[c.model.reference].name,fraction:c.fraction,netSize:new Set(c.model.aliases.map(a=>net(a.events))).size>1?null:net(c.model.events),pathSizes:pathSizes(c.model.reference,c.model.events),events:ambiguous.includes(c)?[]:inputEvents(c.model.reference,c.model.events),alternatives:ambiguous.includes(c)?c.model.aliases.map(a=>({reference:refs[a.reference].name,events:inputEvents(a.reference,a.events)})):[]}));
 const substitutionLinkage=substitutionLinkageAliases(refs,chosen.map(c=>({...c.model,fraction:c.fraction})),sequence=>positions.flatMap(p=>emit(sequence,p,sc.lag)));
 const warnings:string[]=[multiTarget?'Best-fit molecules allow one contiguous edit per target within cut ±10 bp. Target profiles are reported separately; molecule linkage is unresolved.':'Best-fit sequence groups assume one contiguous edit per molecule within cut ±10 bp. A single mixed read does not establish unique allele linkage.'];if(s.editStartRadius!==undefined)warnings.push(`Indel initiation constrained to cut ±${s.editStartRadius} bp; off-target-window edits are outside this model.`);if(controlRepair.added+controlRepair.removed+sampleRepair.added+sampleRepair.removed)warnings.push(`Raw-peak-supported cycle metadata repair: control +${controlRepair.added}/-${controlRepair.removed}, sample +${sampleRepair.added}/-${sampleRepair.removed}.`);if(qc.r2<.995)warnings.push('Signal-guided paths failed internal trace reconstruction QC; editing estimates are withheld.');if(ambiguous.length)warnings.push('Some discovered paths have indistinguishable reference or event explanations. Inspect alternatives.');if(discoveryLimited)warnings.push('Path discovery reached its iteration limit.');if(geometry.condition===null||geometry.condition>100)warnings.push('Selected path contributions are poorly conditioned.');
 if(substitutionLinkage.length)warnings.push('Separate and linked substitution groups produce identical measured fluorescence. WT and substitution-group fractions are not uniquely identifiable; inspect annotation ranges.');
 const boundary=chosen.some(c=>c.model.events.some(e=>e.end-e.start>=maxDeletion||e.inserted.length>=maxInsertion));if(boundary)warnings.push('A supported event reaches the configured path-search boundary.');
 const localResidual=full.positionRmse,unknown=positions.map(p=>chosen.some(c=>c.model.sequence[p]==='N'||c.model.sequence[p-1]==='N')),localDiscordant=localResidual.filter((v,i)=>!unknown[i]&&v>(refs.some(r=>r.cuts.length>1)?Math.max(.003,2*(ctlScore.positionRmse[i]||0)+.002):Math.max(.06,(ctlScore.positionRmse[i]||0)+.04))).length;
 if(localDiscordant)warnings.push(`${localDiscordant} positions have unexplained local signal (possible substitution, artifact or missing edit path). Editing estimates are withheld.`);
 const unknownInsertion=chosen.some(c=>c.model.sequence.includes('N'));if(unknownInsertion)warnings.push('Inserted bases are unresolved; known-sequence inference cannot certify these target fractions.');
 const allowed=!unknownInsertion&&qc.r2>=.995&&!localDiscordant&&fit.converged&&!discoveryLimited&&geometry.condition!==null&&geometry.condition<=100;
 const perRef=refs.map((r,h)=>{const rows=chosen.filter(c=>c.model.reference===h&&!attributionAmbiguous.includes(c)),share=rows.reduce((sum,c)=>sum+c.fraction,0),estimable=allowed&&share>=.02&&!attributionAmbiguous.some(c=>c.model.aliases.some(a=>a.reference===h)),bins=new Map<number,number>();rows.forEach(c=>bins.set(net(c.model.events),(bins.get(net(c.model.events))||0)+c.fraction));const zero=rows.filter(c=>!c.model.events.length).reduce((sum,c)=>sum+c.fraction,0);return {name:r.name,share,wt:estimable?zero/share:null,indel:estimable?1-zero/share:null,inFrame:estimable?rows.filter(c=>c.model.events.length&&net(c.model.events)!==0&&net(c.model.events)%3===0).reduce((sum,c)=>sum+c.fraction,0)/share:null,outOfFrame:estimable?rows.filter(c=>net(c.model.events)%3!==0).reduce((sum,c)=>sum+c.fraction,0)/share:null,components:[...bins].map(([size,fraction])=>({size,fraction:share?fraction/share:0})),estimable,rows};});
 const targets=perRef.flatMap((a,h)=>(refs[h].input.cuts??[refs[h].input.cut]).map((cut,t)=>({reference:a.name,target:t+1,cut,edited:a.estimable?a.rows.filter(c=>inputEvents(h,c.model.events).some(e=>e.target===t+1||e.target===0)).reduce((sum,c)=>sum+c.fraction,0)/a.share:null})));
 const annotations=perRef.map((a,h)=>({sequenceGrouping:true,name:a.name,reference:refs[h].input.sequence,cut:refs[h].input.cut,cuts:refs[h].input.cuts,direction:refs[h].reverse?'reverse' as const:'forward' as const,share:a.share,estimable:a.estimable,components:a.rows.map(c=>({size:net(c.model.events),pathSizes:pathSizes(h,c.model.events),fraction:c.fraction/a.share,events:ambiguous.includes(c)?[]:inputEvents(h,c.model.events),unresolved:ambiguous.includes(c)}))}));
 const exactAlternatives=discoverExactAlternatives(refs,annotations,perRef.map(a=>a.rows),inputEvents,(sequence,p)=>emit(sequence,p,sc.lag));
 const bins=new Map<number,number>();chosen.forEach(c=>bins.set(net(c.model.events),(bins.get(net(c.model.events))||0)+c.fraction));const zero=chosen.filter(c=>!c.model.events.length).reduce((sum,c)=>sum+c.fraction,0);
 const first=Math.min(...refs.flatMap(r=>r.cuts)),plots=[{trace:input.control,trim:cr.trim},{trace:input.sample,trim:sr.trim}].map(({trace,trim})=>plotTrace(trace,first-trim,Math.max(...refs.flatMap(r=>r.cuts))-first+s.window/2));report.positions=positions;report.joint!.candidateCount=models.length;
 if(audit)audit({refs,positions,cols,models,ids,weights:fit.weights,theta,sampleLag:sc.lag,gains:sc.gains,observed:y,fitCache:{hits:fitCache.hits,misses:fitCache.misses,linearFits:fitCache.linearFits,nonlinearFits:fitCache.nonlinearFits,pairHits:fitCache.pairHits,pairMisses:fitCache.pairMisses},rawObserved:rawY,anchor,end,iterations,train,test,tune,controlPositionRmse:ctlScore.positionRmse});
 return {exactAlternatives,method:'pooled',referenceName:refs.length===1?refs[0].name:'Mixed amplicons',sampleName:input.sample.name,controlName:input.control.name,direction:'forward',cutPosition:first,alignmentIdentity:0,alignment:[],variants:[],plots,warnings,synthetic:!!input.synthetic,annotations,diagnostics:{substitutionLinkage:substitutionLinkage.map(a=>({...a,reference:refs[a.reference].name})),algorithmVersion:refs.some(r=>r.cuts.length>1)?'sanger-sequence-groups-oracle-prototype':'sanger-sequence-groups-oracle-prototype',signalModel:'calibrated',requestedMaxIndel:s.maxIndel,discordantPositions:localDiscordant,maxPositionRmse:Math.max(...localResidual),controlCalibration:cc,sampleCalibration:sc,contextEmission:context},mixture:{components:[...bins].map(([size,fraction])=>({size,fraction})),wt:zero,indel:1-zero,inFrame:chosen.filter(c=>c.model.events.length&&net(c.model.events)!==0&&net(c.model.events)%3===0).reduce((sum,c)=>sum+c.fraction,0),outOfFrame:chosen.filter(c=>net(c.model.events)%3!==0).reduce((sum,c)=>sum+c.fraction,0),r2:full.r2,rmse:full.rmse,upstreamError:1-sc.validationR2,bases:positions.length,fitStart:anchor,fitEnd:end,converged:fit.converged,passesChecks:allowed&&!attributionAmbiguous.length&&!substitutionLinkage.length},pooled:{preflight:report,controlR2:ctlScore.r2,sampleTrim:sr.trim,controlTrim:cr.trim,amplicons:perRef.map(({rows,estimable,...a})=>a)},joint:{groups,targets,reconstructionR2:qc.r2,heldOutR2:null,selectedModels:chosen.length,candidateModels:models.length,iterations,maxDeletion,ambiguousFraction:ambiguous.reduce((sum,c)=>sum+c.fraction,0),alignmentSearches:alignments}};
}

END MODULE


BEGIN MODULE src/app/workers/core/sanger-allele-groups.ts
import {discoverExactAlternatives} from './sanger-exact-alternatives';
import {substitutionLinkageAliases} from './sanger-substitution-linkage';
import {compoundBaseProposals} from './sanger-compound-refine';
import {SangerFitCache,rawMixtureMse} from './sanger-fit-cache';
import {CandidateSignalScreen} from './sanger-candidate-screen';
import {polishSignalDictionary} from './sanger-dictionary-polish';
import {substitutionProposals} from './sanger-substitution-search';
import {normalizeMoleculeEdits} from './sanger-edit-normalize';
import {repairSignalTraceCycles} from './sanger-cycle';
import {SangerAnalysisResult,SangerJointEvent,SangerTrace,TRACE_BASES} from '../../models/sanger-analysis';
import {reverseComplement} from '../../utils/biology.utils';
import {registerTrace} from './sanger-pooled';
import {contrastPeaks,integratedPeaks,calibrateSignal,correctGain,gramCondition,rawSignalScore} from './sanger-signal';
import {emissionVector,fitContextEmission,fitNormalizedEmissionMixture as fitEmissionMixtureOriginal} from './sanger-emission';
import {plotTrace} from './sanger-analysis';
const fitEmissionMixture=(columns:number[][],observed:number[])=>fitEmissionMixtureOriginal(columns,observed);
import {SignalPathInput,PathEdit,Model,materialize,prepare,assertSignalTraceGrid,eventsRespectIndelOrigin} from './sanger-path-common';
/** Whole-molecule candidate discovery and joint fluorescence refit. These are
 * best-fit cut-local hypotheses, not a posterior over physical haplotypes.
 * All measured positions enter discovery, so no independent held-out score is claimed. */
export function analyzeAlleleGroups(input:SignalPathInput,progress:(s:string)=>void=()=>{},audit?:(state:any)=>void):SangerAnalysisResult{
 if(!input.ampliconConfirmed||!input.control||!input.sample)throw Error('Confirm submitted products and provide matched control/sample chromatograms.');
 const controlRepair=repairSignalTraceCycles(input.control),sampleRepair=repairSignalTraceCycles(input.sample);input={...input,control:controlRepair.trace,sample:sampleRepair.trace};
 assertSignalTraceGrid(input.control);assertSignalTraceGrid(input.sample);
 const {refs,report,anchor,maxDeletion,maxInsertion}=prepare(input.amplicons,input.primer,input.settings),s=input.settings;
 progress('Registering traces to the sequencing primer');const registered=refs.map(r=>({name:r.name,sequence:r.sequence,cut:Math.min(...r.cuts)}));
 const cr=registerTrace(input.control,registered,s,integratedPeaks(input.control,[0,0,0,0]).vectors),sr=registerTrace(input.sample,registered,s,integratedPeaks(input.sample,[0,0,0,0]).vectors),ctl=contrastPeaks(input.control).vectors,raw=contrastPeaks(input.sample).vectors;
 const calibrate=(vectors:number[][],trim:number)=>{const obs=Array.from({length:vectors.length+trim},(_,p)=>vectors[p-trim]||[0,0,0,0]),rows=Array.from({length:Math.min(...refs.flatMap(r=>r.cuts))-12},(_,p)=>p).filter(p=>p>=Math.max(s.readStart,trim+10)&&p-trim<vectors.length-2);return calibrateSignal(obs,rows,refs.map(r=>Array.from(r.sequence,(_,p)=>TRACE_BASES.map(b=>Number(r.sequence[p]===b)))));};
 const cc=calibrate(ctl,cr.trim),sc=calibrate(raw,sr.trim);if(cc.validationR2<.8||sc.validationR2<.8)throw Error('Signal-path upstream calibration failed.');
 const end=Math.min(Math.max(...report.positions)+1,cr.trim+ctl.length-3,sr.trim+raw.length-3),positions=report.positions.filter(p=>p<end);if(positions.length<120)throw Error('Insufficient actual trace coverage for path inference.');
 const wt=ctl.map(v=>correctGain(v,cc.gains)),sample=raw.map(v=>correctGain(v,sc.gains)),controlObs=Array.from({length:wt.length+cr.trim},(_,p)=>wt[p-cr.trim]||[0,0,0,0]);
 const context=fitContextEmission(refs.map(r=>r.sequence),positions,controlObs,cc.weights,cc.lag),theta=context.theta;
 const emit=(sequence:string,p:number,lag:number)=>{const v=emissionVector(sequence,p,lag,theta);if(sequence[p]==='N')for(let b=0;b<4;b++)v[b]+=(1-Math.abs(lag))/4;return v;};
 const models:Model[]=refs.map((r,h)=>({reference:h,sequence:r.sequence,events:[],aliases:[{reference:h,events:[]}]})),cols=models.map(m=>positions.flatMap(p=>emit(m.sequence,p,sc.lag))),controlCols=models.map(m=>positions.flatMap(p=>emit(m.sequence,p,cc.lag)));
 const ctlFit=fitEmissionMixture(controlCols,positions.flatMap(p=>wt[p-cr.trim])),ctlScore=rawSignalScore(controlCols,positions.flatMap(p=>ctl[p-cr.trim]),ctlFit.weights,cc.gains);if(ctlScore.r2<.85)throw Error('WT product references do not explain the unedited control.');
 const y=positions.flatMap(p=>sample[p-sr.trim]),train=positions.map((_,i)=>i),tune=train,test=train,subset=(v:number[],rows:number[])=>rows.flatMap(i=>v.slice(4*i,4*i+4));
 const positionIndex=new Int32Array(end+maxInsertion+2).fill(-1);positions.forEach((p,i)=>positionIndex[p]=i);
 const rawY=positions.flatMap(p=>raw[p-sr.trim]),fitCache=new SangerFitCache(cols,y);
 const fitIds=(ids:number[])=>fitCache.fit(ids);
 const score=(ids:number[],weights:number[],rows:number[])=>rawSignalScore(ids.map(id=>rows===train?cols[id]:subset(cols[id],rows)),rows===train?rawY:rows.flatMap(i=>raw[positions[i]-sr.trim]),weights,sc.gains);
 const objective=(ids:number[],weights:number[])=>rawMixtureMse(ids.map(id=>cols[id]),rawY,weights,sc.gains)+.0000001*ids.reduce((sum,id,h)=>sum+weights[h]*models[id].events.reduce((a,e)=>a+1+.05*e.inserted.length,0),0);
 let ids=refs.map((_,i)=>i),fit=fitIds(ids),best=objective(ids,fit.weights),iterations=0,alignments=0,substitutionRounds=0,insertionPolishes=0,discoveryLimited=false;
 const multiTarget=refs.some(r=>r.cuts.length>1),screen=new CandidateSignalScreen(cols);
 const modelFamily=(m:Model)=>{const sizes=refs[m.reference].cuts.map(()=>0);for(const e of m.events){const distances=refs[m.reference].cuts.map(c=>Math.min(Math.abs(e.start-c),Math.abs(e.end-c))),t=distances.indexOf(Math.min(...distances));sizes[t]+=e.inserted.length-(e.end-e.start);}return m.reference+':'+sizes.join('/');};
 const seenEdits=refs.map(()=>new Set<string>()),signature=(sequence:string)=>sequence.slice(positions[0],positions.at(-1)!+1).padEnd(positions.length,'-');
 const signatures=new Map(models.map((m,id)=>[signature(m.sequence),id]));

  }

 const first=Math.min(...refs.flatMap(r=>r.cuts)),plots=[{trace:input.control,trim:cr.trim},{trace:input.sample,trim:sr.trim}].map(({trace,trim})=>plotTrace(trace,first-trim,Math.max(...refs.flatMap(r=>r.cuts))-first+s.window/2));report.positions=positions;report.joint!.candidateCount=models.length;
 if(audit)audit({refs,positions,cols,models,ids,weights:fit.weights,theta,sampleLag:sc.lag,gains:sc.gains,observed:y,fitCache:{hits:fitCache.hits,misses:fitCache.misses,linearFits:fitCache.linearFits,nonlinearFits:fitCache.nonlinearFits,pairHits:fitCache.pairHits,pairMisses:fitCache.pairMisses},rawObserved:rawY,anchor,end,iterations,train,test,tune,controlPositionRmse:ctlScore.positionRmse});
 return {exactAlternatives,method:'pooled',referenceName:refs.length===1?refs[0].name:'Mixed amplicons',sampleName:input.sample.name,controlName:input.control.name,direction:'forward',cutPosition:first,alignmentIdentity:0,alignment:[],variants:[],plots,warnings,synthetic:!!input.synthetic,annotations,diagnostics:{substitutionLinkage:substitutionLinkage.map(a=>({...a,reference:refs[a.reference].name})),algorithmVersion:refs.some(r=>r.cuts.length>1)?'sanger-sequence-groups-v4':'sanger-sequence-groups-v2',signalModel:'calibrated',requestedMaxIndel:s.maxIndel,discordantPositions:localDiscordant,maxPositionRmse:Math.max(...localResidual),controlCalibration:cc,sampleCalibration:sc,contextEmission:context},mixture:{components:[...bins].map(([size,fraction])=>({size,fraction})),wt:zero,indel:1-zero,inFrame:chosen.filter(c=>c.model.events.length&&net(c.model.events)!==0&&net(c.model.events)%3===0).reduce((sum,c)=>sum+c.fraction,0),outOfFrame:chosen.filter(c=>net(c.model.events)%3!==0).reduce((sum,c)=>sum+c.fraction,0),r2:full.r2,rmse:full.rmse,upstreamError:1-sc.validationR2,bases:positions.length,fitStart:anchor,fitEnd:end,converged:fit.converged,passesChecks:allowed&&!attributionAmbiguous.length&&!substitutionLinkage.length},pooled:{preflight:report,controlR2:ctlScore.r2,sampleTrim:sr.trim,controlTrim:cr.trim,amplicons:perRef.map(({rows,estimable,...a})=>a)},joint:{groups,targets,reconstructionR2:qc.r2,heldOutR2:null,selectedModels:chosen.length,candidateModels:models.length,iterations,maxDeletion,ambiguousFraction:ambiguous.reduce((sum,c)=>sum+c.fraction,0),alignmentSearches:alignments}};
}

END MODULE


BEGIN MODULE src/app/workers/core/sanger-analysis.ts
import {analyzeSingleJunction} from './sanger-single-junction';
import {matchedSingleSignals} from './sanger-single-signals';
import {alignConsensusSequence,consensusVariants} from './sanger-consensus';
import {integratedPeaks} from './sanger-signal';
import { reverseComplement } from '../../utils/biology.utils';
import { SangerAnalysisResult, SangerAnalysisSettings, SangerTrace, SangerVariant, TraceAlignment, TraceAlignmentColumn, TracePlot, TRACE_BASES, TraceBase } from '../../models/sanger-analysis';

/** Smith–Waterman local alignment with a linear gap penalty; no N matches. */
export function alignTraceSequence(reference:string,query:string):{score:number;identity:number;columns:TraceAlignmentColumn[]} {
  if(reference.length*query.length>24000000)throw new Error('Alignment region is too large.');
  const m=query.length,n=reference.length,trace=new Uint8Array((n+1)*(m+1));
  let prev=new Int32Array(m+1),curr=new Int32Array(m+1),best=0,bi=0,bj=0;
  for(let i=1;i<=n;i++) {
    curr[0]=0;
    for(let j=1;j<=m;j++) {
      const equal=reference[i-1]===query[j-1]&&/[ACGT]/.test(query[j-1]);
      const diag=prev[j-1]+(equal?3:-4),up=prev[j]-5,left=curr[j-1]-5;
      const value=Math.max(0,diag,up,left);curr[j]=value;
      trace[i*(m+1)+j]=value===0?0:value===diag?1:value===up?2:3;
      if(value>best){best=value;bi=i;bj=j;}
    }
    [prev,curr]=[curr,prev];
  }
  let i=bi,j=bj;const columns:TraceAlignmentColumn[]=[];
  while(i>0&&j>0) {
    const step=trace[i*(m+1)+j];if(!step)break;
    if(step===1){columns.push({ref:i-1,query:j-1,a:reference[i-1],b:query[j-1]});i--;j--;}
    else if(step===2){columns.push({ref:i-1,query:null,a:reference[i-1],b:'-'});i--;}
    else{columns.push({ref:null,query:j-1,a:'-',b:query[j-1]});j--;}
  }
  columns.reverse();const matches=columns.filter(c=>c.a===c.b&&/[ACGT]/.test(c.a)).length;
  return {score:best,identity:columns.length?matches/columns.length:0,columns};
}
function referenceAlignment(reference:string,cut:number,trace:SangerTrace,direction:SangerAnalysisSettings['direction'],consensus=false):TraceAlignment {
  const regionRadius=Math.min(1700,trace.sequence.length+100);let best:TraceAlignment|null=null;
  for(const dir of ['forward','reverse'] as const) {
    if(direction!=='auto'&&direction!==dir)continue;
    const oriented=dir==='forward'?reference:reverseComplement(reference),orientedCut=dir==='forward'?cut:reference.length-cut;
    const lo=Math.max(0,orientedCut-regionRadius),hi=Math.min(reference.length,orientedCut+regionRadius);
    const aligned=(consensus?alignConsensusSequence:alignTraceSequence)(oriented.slice(lo,hi),trace.sequence);
    const columns=aligned.columns.map(c=>({...c,ref:c.ref===null?null:c.ref+lo}));
    const coordinates=columns.filter(c=>c.ref!==null).map(c=>c.ref!);
    const item={...aligned,columns,direction:dir,cut:orientedCut,referenceStart:Math.min(...coordinates),referenceEnd:Math.max(...coordinates)+1};
    if(!best||item.score>best.score)best=item;
  }
  if(!best||best.identity<.8||best.columns.length<60||best.referenceStart>best.cut-15||best.referenceEnd<best.cut+15)throw new Error('The trace does not align reliably across this target. Check reference, target and read direction.');
  return best;
}
function traceCut(alignment:TraceAlignment):number {
  const column=alignment.columns.find(c=>c.ref!==null&&c.ref>=alignment.cut&&c.query!==null);
  if(!column)throw new Error('The cut site is not covered by the trace.');return column.query!;
}
export function normalizedPeaks(trace:SangerTrace):number[][] {
  return Array.from(trace.peaks,peak=>{
    const values=TRACE_BASES.map(b=>{
      let sum=0,count=0;for(let at=Math.max(0,peak-2);at<=Math.min(trace.channels[b].length-1,peak+2);at++){sum+=trace.channels[b][at];count++;}
      return sum/Math.max(1,count);
    });
    const total=values.reduce((a,b)=>a+b,0);return values.map(v=>total>0?v/total:0);
  });
}
/** Cyclic coordinate descent NNLS; normalized output fractions are re-scored. */
export function fitTraceMixture(columns:number[][],observed:number[]):{weights:number[];r2:number;rmse:number;converged:boolean} {
  const p=columns.length,n=observed.length,gram=Array.from({length:p},()=>new Float64Array(p)),rhs=new Float64Array(p);
  for(let a=0;a<p;a++) {
    rhs[a]=columns[a].reduce((v,x,i)=>v+x*observed[i],0);
    for(let b=0;b<=a;b++){let dot=0;for(let i=0;i<n;i++)dot+=columns[a][i]*columns[b][i];gram[a][b]=dot;gram[b][a]=dot;}
  }
  for(let a=0;a<p;a++)for(let b=0;b<a;b++)if(gram[a][a]+gram[b][b]-2*gram[a][b]<1e-8)throw new Error('Indel models are indistinguishable in this sequence window. Extend the usable trace range or reduce the indel range.');
  const weights=new Float64Array(p);let converged=false;
  for(let step=0;step<10000;step++) {
    let delta=0;
    for(let a=0;a<p;a++) {
      let residual=rhs[a];for(let b=0;b<p;b++)if(a!==b)residual-=gram[a][b]*weights[b];
      const value=gram[a][a]>0?Math.max(0,residual/gram[a][a]):0;
      delta=Math.max(delta,Math.abs(value-weights[a]));weights[a]=value;
    }
    if(delta<1e-9){converged=true;break;}
  }
  const total=weights.reduce((a,b)=>a+b,0);if(total<=0)throw new Error('The trace mixture model has no usable signal.');
  const fractions=Array.from(weights,w=>w/total),mean=observed.reduce((a,b)=>a+b,0)/n;
  let error=0,variance=0;
  for(let i=0;i<n;i++){let pred=0;for(let a=0;a<p;a++)pred+=fractions[a]*columns[a][i];error+=(observed[i]-pred)**2;variance+=(observed[i]-mean)**2;}
  return {weights:fractions,r2:variance>1e-12?1-error/variance:0,rmse:Math.sqrt(error/n),converged};
}
export function plotTrace(trace:SangerTrace,cutIndex:number,maxAfter:number,maxBefore=30):TracePlot {
  const before=Math.min(maxBefore,cutIndex),after=Math.min(maxAfter,trace.sequence.length-cutIndex-2),start=-before,end=after;
  const width=(end-start)*13+32,paths={} as Record<TraceBase,string>;
  let max=1;
  const samples:{x:number;values:number[]}[]=[];
  for(let offset=start;offset<end;offset++) {
    const i=cutIndex+offset;
    for(let k=0;k<6;k++) {
      const at=trace.peaks[i]+(trace.peaks[i+1]-trace.peaks[i])*k/6,lo=Math.floor(at),fraction=at-lo;
      const values=TRACE_BASES.map(b=>{const a=trace.channels[b][lo]||0,c=trace.channels[b][lo+1]||0;return a+(c-a)*fraction;});
      max=Math.max(max,...values);samples.push({x:16+(offset-start+k/6)*13,values});
    }
  }
  TRACE_BASES.forEach((b,i)=>paths[b]=samples.map((p,j)=>`${j?'L':'M'}${p.x.toFixed(2)},${(124-98*p.values[i]/max).toFixed(2)}`).join(' '));
  return {cutIndex,name:trace.name,start,end,width,cutX:16+(before-.5)*13,paths,letters:Array.from({length:Math.max(0,end-start)},(_,j)=>({x:16+j*13,base:trace.sequence[cutIndex+start+j],offset:start+j}))};
}
export interface SangerAnalysisInput {referenceName:string;reference:string;cut:number;sample:SangerTrace;control?:SangerTrace;settings:SangerAnalysisSettings;synthetic?:boolean}
export function analyzeSanger(input:SangerAnalysisInput,progress:(stage:string)=>void=()=>{}):SangerAnalysisResult {
  const {reference,cut,sample,control,settings:s}=input;
  if(s.joinNearby!==undefined&&(!Number.isInteger(s.joinNearby)||s.joinNearby<0||s.joinNearby>10))throw new Error('Join nearby changes must be 0–10 matched bases.');
  if(s.signalModel!==undefined&&!['classic','calibrated'].includes(s.signalModel))throw new Error('Invalid signal processing mode.');
  if(!reference||reference.length>2000000||/[^ACGTN]/.test(reference)||!Number.isInteger(cut)||cut<1||cut>=reference.length)throw new Error('Provide one A/C/G/T/N reference and one cut position inside it.');
  if(!['mixture','consensus'].includes(s.method)||!['auto','forward','reverse'].includes(s.direction))throw new Error('Invalid Sanger analysis method or direction.');
  if(!Number.isInteger(s.window)||s.window<60||s.window>500||!Number.isInteger(s.maxIndel)||s.maxIndel<1||s.maxIndel>30||!Number.isInteger(s.controlPhred)||s.controlPhred<0||s.controlPhred>60)throw new Error('Use a 60–500 bp window, 1–30 bp maximum indel and Phred 0–60.');
  if(s.method==='mixture'&&(!Number.isInteger(s.readStart)||!Number.isInteger(s.readEnd)||s.readStart<0||s.readEnd>(s.analysisPolicy==='automatic'?3160:1000)||s.readEnd<=s.readStart))throw new Error('Use a measured read range within 0–1,000 bp.');
  const extended=(s.analysisPolicy==='automatic'||!!s.largeDeletions)&&s.method==='mixture';
  const maxDeletion=extended?(s.maxDeletion??200):s.maxIndel;
  const maxInsertion=extended?(s.maxInsertion??s.maxIndel):s.maxIndel;
  if(extended&&(s.signalModel==='classic'||!Number.isInteger(maxDeletion)||maxDeletion<1||maxDeletion>500||!Number.isInteger(maxInsertion)||maxInsertion<1||maxInsertion>30))throw new Error('Large-deletion decomposition requires calibrated signal, deletion limit 1–500 and insertion limit 1–30 bp.');
  const originRadius=s.editStartRadius??5;
  if(!Number.isInteger(originRadius)||originRadius<0||originRadius>100)throw new Error('Indel origin radius must be 0–100 bp.');
  const warnings:string[]=[];
  progress('Aligning trace to the target');
  const anchor=s.method==='mixture'?control:sample;
  if(!anchor)throw new Error('Trace decomposition requires an unedited control AB1 from the same locus and sequencing primer.');
  const aligned=referenceAlignment(reference,cut,anchor,s.direction,s.method==='consensus'),cutIndex=traceCut(aligned);
  const base:SangerAnalysisResult={method:s.method,referenceName:input.referenceName,sampleName:sample.name,controlName:s.method==='mixture'?control!.name:undefined,
    direction:aligned.direction,cutPosition:cut,alignmentIdentity:aligned.identity,alignment:aligned.columns,variants:[],plots:[],warnings,synthetic:!!input.synthetic};
  if(s.method==='consensus') {
    base.variants=consensusVariants(aligned,reference.length,reference.length*2,s.joinNearby??3);
    base.plots=[plotTrace(sample,cutIndex,Math.min(s.window,120))];
    warnings.push('Base calls describe a consensus. Mixed peaks are not decomposed and no editing frequency is estimated.');
    if(sample.quality===null)warnings.push('This file has no per-base quality values.');
    return base;
  }
  if(aligned.identity<.95)throw new Error('Control-to-reference identity is below 95%. Check that this is an unedited control from the intended locus.');
  progress('Matching control and sample upstream of the cut');
  let anchorStart=Math.max(10,cutIndex-140),anchorEnd=cutIndex-12;
  if(anchorEnd-anchorStart<60)throw new Error('At least 60 bp of usable sequence upstream of the cut is required.');
  let upstream=alignTraceSequence(control!.sequence.slice(anchorStart,anchorEnd),sample.sequence.slice(0,Math.min(sample.sequence.length,cutIndex+180)));
  if(s.signalModel!=='classic')for(let start=10;start+100<=cutIndex-12;start+=10){
    const rows=Array.from({length:100},(_,j)=>start+j);
    if(rows.filter(p=>/[ACGT]/.test(control!.sequence[p])&&(!control!.quality||control!.quality[p]>=s.controlPhred)).length<80)continue;
    const candidate=alignTraceSequence(control!.sequence.slice(start,start+100),sample.sequence.slice(0,Math.min(sample.sequence.length,cutIndex+180))),shifts=new Map<number,number>();
    for(const c of candidate.columns)if(c.ref!==null&&c.query!==null){const shift=c.query-(start+c.ref);shifts.set(shift,(shifts.get(shift)||0)+1);}
    if(candidate.identity>=.9&&Math.max(0,...shifts.values())>=Math.max(60,candidate.columns.length*.9)){anchorStart=start;anchorEnd=start+100;upstream=candidate;break;}
  }
  if(upstream.identity<.9||upstream.columns.filter(c=>c.ref!==null&&c.query!==null).length<60)throw new Error('Control and sample do not share a reliable upstream region. Use the same sequencing primer and direction.');
  const offsets=new Map<number,number>();
  for(const c of upstream.columns)if(c.ref!==null&&c.query!==null){const offset=c.query-(anchorStart+c.ref);offsets.set(offset,(offsets.get(offset)||0)+1);}
  const [offset,count]=[...offsets.entries()].sort((a,b)=>b[1]-a[1])[0];
  if(count<upstream.columns.length*.9)throw new Error('Upstream alignment contains conflicting shifts. Trace decomposition requires an intact shared upstream region.');
  const enhanced=s.signalModel!=='classic',sampleCut=cutIndex+offset;
  let upstreamRows=Array.from({length:anchorEnd-anchorStart},(_,j)=>anchorStart+j).filter(i=>i+offset>=0&&i+offset<sample.peaks.length&&(enhanced||!control!.quality||control!.quality[i]>=s.controlPhred));
  if(upstreamRows.length<40)throw new Error('Too few measured upstream control bases for registration.');
  let matched=enhanced?matchedSingleSignals(control!,sample,upstreamRows,offset):undefined;
  if(matched){
    // Select an intact WT calibration interval from measured concordance. This
    // choice is independent of the requested deletion/insertion search limits;
    // all these positions still participate in the final junction likelihood.
    const windows:[[number,number],[number,number],[number,number]]=[[10,Math.min(110,cutIndex-12)],[Math.max(10,cutIndex-140),cutIndex-12],[Math.max(10,cutIndex-240),Math.max(10,cutIndex-140)]];
    let quality=matched.calibration.validationR2-matched.upstreamError**2;
    for(const [lo,hi] of windows){const rows=Array.from({length:Math.max(0,hi-lo)},(_,j)=>lo+j).filter(i=>i+offset>=0&&i+offset<sample.peaks.length&&(enhanced||!control!.quality||control!.quality[i]>=s.controlPhred));if(rows.length<60)continue;const candidate=matchedSingleSignals(control!,sample,rows,offset),score=candidate.calibration.validationR2-candidate.upstreamError**2;if(score>quality){matched=candidate;upstreamRows=rows;quality=score;}}
  }
  if(matched){
    const origins=new Map<number,number>();for(const c of aligned.columns)if(c.ref!==null&&c.query!==null)origins.set(c.ref-c.query,(origins.get(c.ref-c.query)||0)+1);
    const referenceOrigin=[...origins].sort((a,b)=>b[1]-a[1])[0][0],oriented=aligned.direction==='forward'?reference:reverseComplement(reference);
    return analyzeSingleJunction({base,reference:oriented,referenceOrigin,cutIndex,control:control!,sample,settings:s,offset,upstreamRows,matched,maxDeletion,maxInsertion,plots:[plotTrace(control!,cutIndex,Math.min(s.window,120)),plotTrace(sample,sampleCut,Math.min(s.window,120))]});
  }
  // Classic changes signal extraction only; it uses the same physical junction
  // hypotheses and complete measured read axis as calibrated processing.
  const wt=normalizedPeaks(control!),raw=normalizedPeaks(sample),controlSignal={...integratedPeaks(control!),vectors:wt},sampleSignal={...integratedPeaks(sample),vectors:raw};
  const upstreamError=upstreamRows.reduce((sum,i)=>sum+wt[i].reduce((v,x,b)=>v+Math.abs(x-raw[i+offset][b]),0)/2,0)/upstreamRows.length;
  const calibration={gains:[1,1,1,1],lag:0,used:false,trainingBases:0,validationBases:0,rawValidationR2:1,validationR2:1,weights:[1]};
  const identity={method:'area' as const,controlSignal,sampleSignal,wt,raw,edited:raw,calibration,upstreamError,templateUsable:wt.map(()=>true)};
  const origins=new Map<number,number>();for(const c of aligned.columns)if(c.ref!==null&&c.query!==null)origins.set(c.ref-c.query,(origins.get(c.ref-c.query)||0)+1);
  const referenceOrigin=[...origins].sort((a,b)=>b[1]-a[1])[0][0],oriented=aligned.direction==='forward'?reference:reverseComplement(reference);
  return analyzeSingleJunction({base,reference:oriented,referenceOrigin,cutIndex,control:control!,sample,settings:s,offset,upstreamRows,matched:identity,maxDeletion,maxInsertion,plots:[plotTrace(control!,cutIndex,Math.min(s.window,120)),plotTrace(sample,sampleCut,Math.min(s.window,120))]});
}

END MODULE


BEGIN MODULE src/app/workers/core/sanger-automatic.ts
import type{SangerLinearOptimizer}from'./sanger-discrete-phase';
import{analyzeConditionalGroups}from'./sanger-conditional-groups';
import {analyzeOffsetGroups}from'./sanger-offset-groups';
import {analyzeAlleleGroups} from './sanger-allele-groups';
import {analyzePairedSanger} from './sanger-paired';
import {PooledAmplicon,SANGER_ANALYSIS_DEFAULTS,SangerAnalysisResult,SangerAnalysisSettings,SangerTrace} from '../../models/sanger-analysis';
import {analyzeSanger} from './sanger-analysis';
import {analyzePooledSanger} from './sanger-pooled';
export interface AutomaticSangerInput {references:PooledAmplicon[];primer?:string;sample:SangerTrace;control:SangerTrace;maxDeletion:number;maxInsertion:number;additionalReads?:{primer:string;sample:SangerTrace;control:SangerTrace}[];synthetic?:boolean;groupRecovery?:boolean}
/** One public analysis contract. Instrument calibration, direction and measured
 * coverage are automatic. Reference count selects the observation adapter;
 * requested sequence groups use whole-molecule candidates when supported;
 * the existing length model remains available for other observation adapters. */
export function automaticSangerSettings(input:AutomaticSangerInput):SangerAnalysisSettings {
 if(!Number.isInteger(input.maxDeletion)||input.maxDeletion<1||input.maxDeletion>500||!Number.isInteger(input.maxInsertion)||input.maxInsertion<1||input.maxInsertion>30)throw Error('Use deletion limit 1–500 bp and insertion limit 1–30 bp.');
 return {...SANGER_ANALYSIS_DEFAULTS,analysisPolicy:'automatic',scope:input.references.length===1?'single':'pooled',method:'mixture',signalModel:'calibrated',direction:'auto',readStart:0,readEnd:3160,maxDeletion:input.maxDeletion,maxInsertion:input.maxInsertion,maxIndel:Math.min(30,Math.max(input.maxDeletion,input.maxInsertion)),largeDeletions:false,checkWiderRange:false,jointEngine:'staged',window:200};
}
export function analyzeAutomaticSanger(input:AutomaticSangerInput,progress:(stage:string)=>void=()=>{},optimizer?:SangerLinearOptimizer):SangerAnalysisResult {
 if(!input.sample||!input.control)throw Error('Upload an edited sample and an unedited control chromatogram. Reference DNA cannot replace measured control fluorescence.');
 if(!input.references.length||input.references.length>4)throw Error('Enter 1–4 reference sequences.');
 const targetCounts=input.references.map(r=>r.cuts?.length||1);if(targetCounts.some(n=>n<1||n>2)||new Set(targetCounts).size!==1)throw Error('Use one or two corresponding targets in every reference.');
 const multi=targetCounts[0]>1;if(multi&&!input.primer)throw Error('A sequencing primer is required to register multiple targets.');
 const settings=automaticSangerSettings(input),only=input.references[0];
 if(input.additionalReads?.length){
  if(input.additionalReads.length!==1)throw Error('Add one opposite-direction read pair.');
  const pairs=[{primer:input.primer||'',sample:input.sample,control:input.control},...input.additionalReads];
  if(pairs.some(p=>!p.sample||!p.control||!/^[ACGT]{12,40}$/.test(p.primer.replace(/\s/g,'').toUpperCase())))throw Error('Each read direction needs its sequencing primer, edited trace and WT control.');
  return analyzePairedSanger(pairs.map(p=>({...p,primer:p.primer.replace(/\s/g,'').toUpperCase(),amplicons:input.references,ampliconConfirmed:true,settings,synthetic:input.synthetic})),progress);
 }
 let groupWarning='';
 if(input.groupRecovery&&input.primer&&targetCounts[0]<=2&&input.maxDeletion<=30){
  // Fast local factorization is supported only for one target with calibrated
  // unit-cycle emissions and separated edit windows. Its own validation rejects
  // unsupported observations; the general path search remains the fallback.
  if(multi){try{const conditional=analyzeConditionalGroups({amplicons:input.references,primer:input.primer.replace(/\s/g,'').toUpperCase(),ampliconConfirmed:true,sample:input.sample,control:input.control,settings:{...settings,readEnd:1000,editStartRadius:3,maxIndel:input.maxDeletion},synthetic:input.synthetic},progress,optimizer);if(conditional.mixture?.passesChecks)return conditional;}catch{/* General model below handles unsupported conditional observations. */}}
  if(!multi){try{const fast=analyzeOffsetGroups({amplicons:input.references,primer:input.primer.replace(/\s/g,'').toUpperCase(),ampliconConfirmed:true,sample:input.sample,control:input.control,settings:{...settings,readEnd:1000,editStartRadius:3,maxIndel:input.maxDeletion},synthetic:input.synthetic},progress);if(fast.mixture?.passesChecks)return fast;}catch{/* General model below handles unsupported emissions/windows. */}}
  try{return analyzeAlleleGroups({amplicons:input.references,primer:input.primer.replace(/\s/g,'').toUpperCase(),ampliconConfirmed:true,sample:input.sample,control:input.control,settings:{...settings,readEnd:1000,editStartRadius:10,maxIndel:input.maxDeletion},synthetic:input.synthetic},progress);}
  catch(error){groupWarning='Sequence-group fitting unavailable: '+(error instanceof Error?error.message:String(error))+' Length-mixture results are shown.';}
 }else if(input.groupRecovery)groupWarning='Sequence-group fitting requires a sequencing primer, one or two targets and deletion limit ≤30 bp. Length-mixture results are shown.';
 const result=input.references.length===1&&!multi?analyzeSanger({referenceName:only.name,reference:only.sequence,cut:only.cut,sample:input.sample,control:input.control,settings,synthetic:input.synthetic},progress):analyzePooledSanger({amplicons:input.references,primer:input.primer?.replace(/\s/g,'').toUpperCase()||'',ampliconConfirmed:true,sample:input.sample,control:input.control,settings,synthetic:input.synthetic},progress);
 if(groupWarning)result.warnings.push(groupWarning);
 return result;
}

END MODULE


BEGIN MODULE src/app/workers/core/sanger-candidate-screen.ts
/** Cache exact fluorescence differences inside a physical length/target family.
 * It changes candidate screening cost, not the downstream mixture objective. */
export class CandidateSignalScreen {
