 private unitAnchors=new Map<number,number[]>();private unitSparse:{at:number[];v:number[];norm:number;anchor:number}[]=[];
 private anchors=new Map<string,number>();private sparse:{at:number[];v:number[];norm:number;anchor:number;family:string}[]=[];private rhs:number[]=[];
 constructor(private columns:number[][]){}
 update(families:string[]){for(let id=this.sparse.length;id<this.columns.length;id++){const family=families[id];if(!this.anchors.has(family))this.anchors.set(family,id);const anchor=this.anchors.get(family)!,at:number[]=[],v:number[]=[];let norm=0;for(let k=0;k<this.columns[id].length;k++){const d=this.columns[id][k]-this.columns[anchor][k];if(Math.abs(d)>1e-12){at.push(k);v.push(d);norm+=d*d;}}this.sparse.push({at,v,norm,anchor,family});}}
 correlate(residual:number[]){const base=new Map<number,number>();for(const id of this.anchors.values())base.set(id,this.columns[id].reduce((s,v,k)=>s+v*residual[k],0));this.rhs=this.sparse.map(c=>base.get(c.anchor)!+c.at.reduce((s,k,j)=>s+c.v[j]*residual[k],0));return this.rhs;}

 private updateUnit(){for(let id=this.unitSparse.length;id<this.columns.length;id++){const anchor=this.sparse[id].anchor;let unitAnchor=this.unitAnchors.get(anchor);if(!unitAnchor){const col=this.columns[anchor];unitAnchor=col.map((value,k)=>{const at=k-k%4,total=col[at]+col[at+1]+col[at+2]+col[at+3];return total?value/total:0;});this.unitAnchors.set(anchor,unitAnchor);}const ua:number[]=[],uv:number[]=[];let unorm=0;for(let k=0;k<this.columns[id].length;k+=4){const col=this.columns[id],total=col[k]+col[k+1]+col[k+2]+col[k+3];for(let b=0;b<4;b++){const value=total?col[k+b]/total:0,d=value-unitAnchor[k+b];unorm+=value*value;if(Math.abs(d)>1e-12){ua.push(k+b);uv.push(d);}}}this.unitSparse.push({at:ua,v:uv,norm:unorm,anchor});}}
 /** Screening score for adding a candidate to the current mixture. Unit
  * waveforms make the derivative scale comparable across candidate lengths.
  * Full measured-dye fitting still decides acceptance. */
 additionScores(observed:number[],prediction:number[]){this.updateUnit();
  const residual=observed.map((v,k)=>v-prediction[k]),dot=(vector:number[])=>{const base=new Map<number,number>();for(const [id,col]of this.unitAnchors)base.set(id,col.reduce((sum,v,k)=>sum+v*vector[k],0));return this.unitSparse.map(c=>base.get(c.anchor)!+c.at.reduce((sum,k,j)=>sum+c.v[j]*vector[k],0));},rhs=dot(residual),cross=dot(prediction),mean=prediction.reduce((sum,v,k)=>sum+v*residual[k],0),norm=prediction.reduce((sum,v)=>sum+v*v,0);
  return this.unitSparse.map((c,id)=>{const gradient=rhs[id]-mean,curvature=Math.max(0,c.norm-2*cross[id]+norm);if(gradient<=0||curvature<=1e-14)return 0;const step=Math.min(1,gradient/curvature);return 2*step*gradient-step*step*curvature;});
 }
 sameFamily(a:number,b:number){return this.sparse[a].family===this.sparse[b].family;}
 replacementGain(id:number,old:number,weight:number){const a=this.sparse[id],b=this.sparse[old];if(a.anchor!==b.anchor)throw Error('Candidate transfer requires the same physical family.');let i=0,j=0,cross=0;while(i<a.at.length&&j<b.at.length){if(a.at[i]===b.at[j])cross+=a.v[i++]*b.v[j++];else if(a.at[i]<b.at[j])i++;else j++;}return 2*weight*(this.rhs[id]-this.rhs[old])-weight*weight*(a.norm+b.norm-2*cross);}
}

END MODULE


BEGIN MODULE src/app/workers/core/sanger-compound-refine.ts
import type {PathEdit} from './sanger-path-common';
/** Propose bases on the measured READ axis of a compound molecule. All edit
 * coordinates remain on the original reference; earlier indels move later
 * observed cycles. Residual evidence proposes candidates, never truth labels. */
export function compoundBaseProposals(events:PathEdit[],column:number[],residual:number[],weight:number,positionIndex:Int32Array):PathEdit[][]{
 if(events.length<2||weight<=0)return [];const proposals:PathEdit[][]=[];
 for(let e=0;e<events.length;e++){
  const event=events[e];if(!event.inserted.length)continue;
  const shift=events.filter((_,j)=>j!==e).filter(p=>p.end<=event.start).reduce((s,p)=>s+p.inserted.length-(p.end-p.start),0),scores:number[][]=[];let inserted='';
  for(let j=0;j<event.inserted.length;j++){const k=positionIndex[event.start+shift+j]??-1;if(k<0){inserted='';break;}const values=Array.from({length:4},(_,b)=>residual[k*4+b]+weight*column[k*4+b]);scores.push(values);inserted+='ACGT'[values.indexOf(Math.max(...values))];}
  if(inserted.length!==event.inserted.length)continue;
  const replace=(sequence:string)=>events.map((p,j)=>({...p,inserted:j===e?sequence:p.inserted}));if(inserted!==event.inserted)proposals.push(replace(inserted));
  for(let j=0;j<scores.length;j++)for(let b=0;b<4;b++)if('ACGT'[b]!==event.inserted[j]&&scores[j][b]>.1*weight)proposals.push(replace(event.inserted.slice(0,j)+'ACGT'[b]+event.inserted.slice(j+1)));
 }
 return proposals;
}

END MODULE


BEGIN MODULE src/app/workers/core/sanger-conditional-groups.ts
import{rawIntervalScorer}from './sanger-raw-interval-fit';
import{SangerSequenceFitCache,sequenceFitLoss,sequenceFitTolerance}from'./sanger-sequence-fit-cache';
import{refineDiscretePhase,SangerLinearOptimizer}from'./sanger-discrete-phase';
import{proposeStratifiedPaths}from'./sanger-stratified-paths';
import{initializeOffsetProfiles}from'./sanger-offset-groups';
import{SignalPathInput,Prepared,PathEdit,Model,prepare,materialize}from'./sanger-path-common';
import{repairSignalTraceCycles}from'./sanger-cycle';
import{contrastPeaks,pairedReferencePeaks,correctGain,rawSignalScore}from'./sanger-signal';
import{fitNormalizedEmissionMixture}from'./sanger-emission';
import{SangerFitCache}from'./sanger-fit-cache';
import{nonnegativeLeastSquares}from'./sanger-nonnegative-ls';
import{decodeFrequencyPaths}from'./sanger-frequency-path-decoder';
import{sequenceGroupResult}from'./sanger-sequence-group-result';
const bases='ACGT';
interface Family {size:number;fraction:number}
/** Prefix measurements determine upstream length marginals. A convolution
 * inverse proposes downstream profiles; it is ONLY an initializer. Final
 * whole-molecule pairing coefficients are free and scored on measured signal.
 * Unsupported observations are rejected, never silently treated as independent. */
export function analyzeConditionalGroups(input:SignalPathInput,progress:(s:string)=>void=()=>{},optimizer?:SangerLinearOptimizer){
 if(!optimizer)throw Error('The local discrete optimizer must be initialized before conditional inference.');
 const controlRepair=repairSignalTraceCycles(input.control!),sampleRepair=repairSignalTraceCycles(input.sample!);input={...input,control:controlRepair.trace,sample:sampleRepair.trace};
 const s=input.settings,{refs,report,anchor,maxDeletion,maxInsertion}=prepare(input.amplicons,input.primer,s);
 if(maxDeletion>10||maxInsertion>10||refs.some(r=>r.reverse||r.cuts.length!==2||r.input.cuts?.[0]!==r.input.cut||r.input.cuts[0]>=r.input.cuts[1]))throw Error('Conditional profiles currently require two ordered forward targets and ≤10 bp length limits.');
 const prefixEnd=Math.min(...refs.map(r=>r.cuts[1]))-maxDeletion-5;
 progress('Fitting first-target measured prefix');
 const initialized=initializeOffsetProfiles({...input,amplicons:input.amplicons.map(a=>({...a,cuts:[a.cuts![0]]})),settings:{...s,readEnd:prefixEnd,editStartRadius:3}},stage=>progress('Conditional prefix · '+stage),undefined,optimizer),prefix=initialized.result,firstOptions=initialized.profiles,firstSelected=[...initialized.selected],cc=prefix.diagnostics!.controlCalibration!,sc=prefix.diagnostics!.sampleCalibration!,context=prefix.diagnostics!.contextEmission!;
 if(!prefix.mixture?.passesChecks||prefix.annotations!.some(a=>!a.estimable)||cc.lag!==0||sc.lag!==0||context.used||context.theta.some(v=>v!==0))throw Error('Conditional profiles require validated unit-emission prefix measurements.');
 const cr={trim:prefix.pooled!.controlTrim},sr={trim:prefix.pooled!.sampleTrim},peaks=pairedReferencePeaks(input.control!,input.sample!,refs.map(r=>r.sequence),cr.trim,sr.trim,Math.min(...refs.flatMap(r=>r.cuts)),optimizer),ctl=peaks.control.vectors,raw=peaks.sample.vectors,end=Math.min(s.readEnd,cr.trim+ctl.length-3,sr.trim+raw.length-3,...refs.map(r=>r.sequence.length-2*maxDeletion-2)),positions=Array.from({length:end-anchor},(_,i)=>i+anchor),rawY=positions.flatMap(p=>raw[p-sr.trim]),y=positions.flatMap(p=>correctGain(raw[p-sr.trim],sc.gains));
 const emit=(sequence:string,p:number,_lag=0)=>[...bases].map(b=>Number(sequence[p]===b)),controlCols=refs.map(r=>positions.flatMap(p=>emit(r.sequence,p))),controlFit=fitNormalizedEmissionMixture(controlCols,positions.flatMap(p=>correctGain(ctl[p-cr.trim],cc.gains))),ctlScore=rawSignalScore(controlCols,positions.flatMap(p=>ctl[p-cr.trim]),controlFit.weights,cc.gains);
 if(ctlScore.r2<.995)throw Error('The full control does not validate the conditional unit-emission model.');
 const first=prefix.annotations!.map(a=>{const bins=new Map<number,number>();for(const c of a.components)bins.set(c.size,(bins.get(c.size)||0)+c.fraction*a.share);return[...bins].map(([size,fraction])=>({size,fraction}));}),shares=first.map(fs=>fs.reduce((sum,f)=>sum+f.fraction,0));
 progress('Fitting downstream total-length marginals');
 const tail=positions.filter(p=>p>Math.max(...refs.map(r=>r.cuts[1]))+35);if(tail.length<80)throw Error('Insufficient downstream invariant signal for conditional profiles.');
 const totalClasses=refs.flatMap((_,h)=>Array.from({length:2*(maxDeletion+maxInsertion)+1},(_,i)=>({reference:h,size:i-2*maxDeletion}))),tailColumns=totalClasses.map(f=>tail.flatMap(p=>[...bases].map(b=>Number(refs[f.reference].sequence[p-f.size]===b)))),tailY=tail.flatMap(p=>correctGain(raw[p-sr.trim],sc.gains)),tailFit=new SangerFitCache(tailColumns,tailY).fit(totalClasses.map((_,i)=>i)),totals=refs.map((_,h)=>{const own=totalClasses.flatMap((f,i)=>f.reference===h?[{size:f.size,fraction:tailFit.weights[i]}]:[]),mass=own.reduce((sum,f)=>sum+f.fraction,0);return own.map(f=>({...f,fraction:f.fraction*shares[h]/mass}));});
 if(!tailFit.converged)throw Error('Downstream length fit did not converge.');
 const options:{events:PathEdit[];fraction:number}[][][]=[],proposalDetails:any[]=[];
 for(let h=0;h<refs.length;h++){
  progress(`Proposing second-target profiles · reference ${h+1} / ${refs.length}`);
  const r=refs[h],share=shares[h],u=first[h].map(f=>({...f,fraction:f.fraction/share})),sizes=Array.from({length:maxDeletion+maxInsertion+1},(_,i)=>i-maxDeletion),C=sizes.map(size=>totals[h].map(c=>u.find(a=>a.size===c.size-size)?.fraction||0)),lengthFit=nonnegativeLeastSquares(C,totals[h].map(c=>c.fraction));if(!lengthFit.converged)throw Error('Conditional length proposal did not converge.');
  const mass=lengthFit.weights.reduce((a,b)=>a+b,0),v=sizes.map((size,i)=>({size,fraction:lengthFit.weights[i]*share/mass})),cut=r.cuts[1],q=Array.from({length:17},(_,i)=>cut-3+i),from=q[0]+Math.min(...u.map(a=>a.size))-2,to=q.at(-1)!+Math.max(...u.map(a=>a.size))+3,local=Array.from({length:to-from},(_,i)=>from+i),K=q.map(j=>local.map(p=>u.find(a=>a.size===p-j)?.fraction||0));
  const observed=local.map(p=>{const row=correctGain(raw[p-sr.trim],sc.gains);for(let k=0;k<refs.length;k++)if(k!==h){if(p>=refs[k].cuts[1]-maxDeletion-3&&p<=refs[k].cuts[1]+maxInsertion+13)throw Error('Conditional target windows overlap.');const fs=p<refs[k].cuts[1]-13?first[k]:totals[k];for(const f of fs){const b=bases.indexOf(refs[k].sequence[p-f.size]);if(b>=0)row[b]-=f.fraction;}}return row;});
  const known=local.map(p=>{const row=[0,0,0,0];for(const a of u){const j=p-a.size;if(j>=q[0]&&j<=q.at(-1)!)continue;if(j<q[0]){const b=bases.indexOf(r.sequence[j]);if(b>=0)row[b]+=a.fraction*share;}else for(const f of v){const b=bases.indexOf(r.sequence[j-f.size]);if(b>=0)row[b]+=a.fraction*f.fraction;}}return row;}),baseFits=[...bases].map((_,b)=>nonnegativeLeastSquares(K,observed.map((row,i)=>row[b]-known[i][b])));if(baseFits.some(f=>!f.converged))throw Error('Conditional base proposal did not converge.');
  const F=q.map((_,i)=>{const row=baseFits.map(f=>f.weights[i]),total=row.reduce((a,b)=>a+b,0);return row.map(x=>total?x*share/total:0);}),localPositions=Array.from({length:end-30},(_,i)=>i+30),localObserved=localPositions.flatMap(p=>p>=q[0]&&p<=q.at(-1)!?F[p-q[0]]:p<q[0]?[...bases].map(b=>share*Number(r.sequence[p]===b)):[...bases].map(b=>v.reduce((sum,f)=>sum+f.fraction*Number(r.sequence[p-f.size]===b),0))),keep=v.filter(f=>f.size!==0&&f.fraction>=share*.01),families=[{reference:0,size:0,fraction:share-keep.reduce((sum,f)=>sum+f.fraction,0)},...keep.map(f=>({...f,reference:0}))],single:Prepared={...r,cuts:[cut]},decoded=proposeStratifiedPaths(single,families,localPositions,localObserved,.001,5,200,4,stage=>progress('Reference '+(h+1)+' / '+refs.length+' · '+stage));
  if(!decoded.options.length)throw Error('No supported second-target profile proposal · reference '+r.name+' '+JSON.stringify(decoded.details));options.push(decoded.options);proposalDetails.push(decoded.details);
 }
 progress('Jointly fitting free target pairings');
 const models:Model[]=[],sequences:string[]=[],keys=new Map<string,number>(),profileIds=new Map<string,number[]>(),add=(h:number,events:PathEdit[])=>{const sequence=materialize(refs[h].sequence,events);if(!sequence)throw Error('Invalid conditional molecule.');const key=h+':'+sequence,old=keys.get(key),alias={reference:h,events};if(old!==undefined){if(!models[old].aliases.some(a=>JSON.stringify(a.events)===JSON.stringify(events)))models[old].aliases.push(alias);return old;}const id=models.length;keys.set(key,id);models.push({reference:h,sequence,events,aliases:[alias]});sequences.push(sequence);return id;},build=(h:number,index:number,firstIndex=firstSelected[h])=>{const key=h+':'+firstIndex+':'+index,hit=profileIds.get(key);if(hit)return hit;const ids:number[]=[];for(const firstEvents of firstOptions[h][firstIndex])for(const b of options[h][index])ids.push(add(h,[...firstEvents,...b.events]));const unique=[...new Set(ids)];profileIds.set(key,unique);return unique;};
 const selected=refs.map(()=>0);selected.forEach((index,h)=>build(h,index));const fitCache=new SangerSequenceFitCache(sequences,positions,y);let previousFit:{ids:number[];weights:number[]}|undefined;const fitProfiles=(indices:number[],firstIndices=firstSelected,cache=fitCache)=>{const ids=indices.flatMap((index,h)=>build(h,index,firstIndices[h]));const old=new Map(previousFit?.ids.map((id,i)=>[id,previousFit!.weights[i]])||[]),fit=cache.fit(ids,ids.map(id=>old.get(id)||0));previousFit={ids,weights:fit.weights};return{ids,fit};};let best=fitProfiles(selected),iterations=0;
 const optimizeProfiles=(cache=fitCache)=>{if(best.fit.rawResponse&&sequenceFitLoss(best.fit)<1e-7)return;for(let pass=0;pass<4;pass++){iterations++;
  let changed=false;
  for(let h=0;h<refs.length;h++){
   progress(`Comparing joint target hypotheses · reference ${h+1} / ${refs.length} · pass ${pass+1}`);
   let choice=selected[h],firstChoice=firstSelected[h];
   for(let a=0;a<firstOptions[h].length;a++)for(let b=0;b<options[h].length;b++){
    if((a*options[h].length+b)%32===0)progress(`Comparing joint target hypotheses · reference ${h+1} / ${refs.length} · ${a*options[h].length+b+1} / ${firstOptions[h].length*options[h].length}`);const firstTrial=[...firstSelected],secondTrial=[...selected];firstTrial[h]=a;secondTrial[h]=b;const candidate=fitProfiles(secondTrial,firstTrial,cache);
    if(candidate.fit.converged&&sequenceFitLoss(candidate.fit)<sequenceFitLoss(best.fit)-sequenceFitTolerance(best.fit)){choice=b;firstChoice=a;best=candidate;if(best.fit.rawResponse&&sequenceFitLoss(best.fit)<1e-7){selected[h]=choice;firstSelected[h]=firstChoice;return;}}
   }
   if(choice!==selected[h]||firstChoice!==firstSelected[h]){selected[h]=choice;firstSelected[h]=firstChoice;changed=true;}
  }
  if(!changed)return;
 }
 throw Error('Conditional profile search did not stabilize within its budget.');};
 optimizeProfiles();
 best=fitProfiles(selected);
 progress('Resolving joint sequence phase');
 const rawScorer=peaks.method==='quantized-peak-shape'?rawIntervalScorer(input.control!,input.sample!,refs.map(r=>r.sequence),positions,cr.trim,sr.trim,peaks.control.background!,peaks.sample.background!,peaks.calibration,optimizer,y):null;
 const finalCache=rawScorer?new SangerSequenceFitCache(sequences,positions,y,512,rawScorer):fitCache;
 if(rawScorer){best=fitProfiles(selected,firstSelected,finalCache);optimizeProfiles(finalCache);}
 progress(rawScorer?'Raw ADC interval phase · centroid bound '+rawScorer.maximumCentroidMse:'Using measured centroid phase');
 for(let round=0;round<(rawScorer?2:1);round++){
  let changed=false;
  let targets=rawScorer?[0,1]:[1];
  if(rawScorer){const measured=rawScorer(best.ids.map(id=>sequences[id]),best.fit.weights);if(measured&&!measured.lowerBoundOnly){if((measured.loss??Infinity)<1e-7)break;const error=(target:number)=>(measured.residuals||[]).reduce((n,r)=>n+(refs.some((ref,h)=>{const shifts=target===0?[0]:firstOptions[h][firstSelected[h]].map(es=>es.reduce((s,e)=>s+e.inserted.length-e.end+e.start,0));return r.position>=ref.cuts[target]-3+Math.min(...shifts)&&r.position<=ref.cuts[target]+13+Math.max(...shifts);})?r.error:0),0);targets=targets.sort((a,b)=>error(b)-error(a)||a-b);}}
  for(const target of targets){
   progress(`Resolving full-molecule phase · round ${round+1} · target ${target+1}`);
   const firstEvents=firstSelected.map((index,h)=>firstOptions[h][index]),secondEvents=selected.map((index,h)=>options[h][index].map(g=>g.events)),phased=refineDiscretePhase(refs,target===1?firstEvents:secondEvents,target===1?secondEvents:firstEvents,positions,y,optimizer,progress,target,true,rawScorer||undefined);
   if(phased.changes){phased.profiles.forEach((profile,h)=>{if(target===1){options[h].push(profile.map(events=>({events,fraction:0})));selected[h]=options[h].length-1;}else{firstOptions[h].push(profile);firstSelected[h]=firstOptions[h].length-1;}});best=fitProfiles(selected,firstSelected,finalCache);if(!rawScorer)optimizeProfiles();changed=true;}
  }
  if(!changed)break;
 }
 if(!best.fit.converged)throw Error('Final whole-signal coefficient fit did not converge.');
 progress('Validating fitted conditional profiles');
 const chosenIds=new Set(best.ids),cols=models.map((_,id)=>chosenIds.has(id)?fitCache.column(id):[]),train=positions.map((_,i)=>i),result=sequenceGroupResult({input,refs,positions,report,anchor,end,models,cols,ids:best.ids,fit:best.fit,ctlScore,cc,sc,context,cr,sr,rawY,controlRepair,sampleRepair,maxDeletion,maxInsertion,fitCache:finalCache,y,train,test:train,tune:train,emit,iterations:iterations+1,algorithmVersion:'sanger-sequence-groups-v42-near-frequency-phase'});
 return result;
}

END MODULE


BEGIN MODULE src/app/workers/core/sanger-conditional-phase.ts
import {materialize,PathEdit} from './sanger-path-common';
import {SangerSequenceFitCache} from './sanger-sequence-fit-cache';
import {normalizeMoleculeEdits} from './sanger-edit-normalize';
const bases='ACGT';
/** Bounded two-cycle phase exchanges with variable projection. Candidate
 * hypotheses are generated against measured fluorescence with free target
 * pairing coefficients within one reference. Other references are held fixed
 * only during proposal generation; the caller must score returned profiles
 * with the full joint reference fit before accepting them. Only improvements exceeding the incumbent solver dual-gap
 * bound are committed. Search budgets are not identifiability certificates;
 * this function neither reads ground truth nor introduces ambiguous bases. */
export function refineConditionalPhase(refs:{sequence:string;cuts:number[]}[],prefix:PathEdit[][][],initial:PathEdit[][][],positions:number[],observed:number[],progress:(s:string)=>void=()=>{},targetIndex=1){
 const profiles=initial.map(gs=>{const nonWT=gs.filter(es=>es.length).map(es=>es.map(e=>({...e}))),all:PathEdit[][]=[[],...nonWT];while(all.length<6)all.push([]);return all;}),models:{h:number;first:number;second:number;sequence:string}[]=[],sequences:string[]=[],keys=new Map<string,number>(),positionIndex=new Map(positions.map((p,i)=>[p,i]));
 let cache:SangerSequenceFitCache|undefined;
 let previousWeights:number[]|undefined;
 let current:any,previousFit:{ids:number[];weights:number[]}|undefined;
 const blocks=new Map<number,{key:string;cache:SangerSequenceFitCache}>();
 const fit=(scope?:number)=>{const ids:number[]=[];for(let h=0;h<refs.length;h++)for(let i=0;i<prefix[h].length;i++)for(let j=0;j<profiles[h].length;j++){
  const sequence=materialize(refs[h].sequence,[...prefix[h][i],...profiles[h][j]])!,key=h+':'+sequence;let id=keys.get(key);if(id===undefined){id=models.length;keys.set(key,id);models.push({h,first:i,second:j,sequence});sequences.push(sequence);}if(!ids.includes(id))ids.push(id);
 }cache??=new SangerSequenceFitCache(sequences,positions,observed);
 if(scope===undefined){const old=new Map(previousFit?.ids.map((id,i)=>[id,previousFit!.weights[i]])||[]),result=cache.fit(ids,ids.map(id=>old.get(id)||0));previousFit={ids,weights:result.weights};return{...result,ids};}
 const outside=current.ids.flatMap((id:number,i:number)=>models[id].h!==scope?[{id,w:current.weights[i]}]:[]),key=outside.map((x:any)=>x.id+':'+x.w).join('/'),share=1-outside.reduce((s:number,x:any)=>s+x.w,0),prediction=cache.predict(outside.map((x:any)=>x.id),outside.map((x:any)=>x.w)),target=observed.map((v,i)=>(v-prediction[i])/share),own=ids.filter(id=>models[id].h===scope);let block=blocks.get(scope);if(!block||block.key!==key){block={key,cache:new SangerSequenceFitCache(sequences,positions,target)};blocks.set(scope,block);}const result=block.cache.fit(own),weights=ids.map(id=>{const at=own.indexOf(id);return at>=0?result.weights[at]*share:outside.find((x:any)=>x.id===id)?.w||0;});return{...result,weights,rmse:result.rmse*share,optimalityGap:(result.optimalityGap||0)*share*share,ids};};
 current=fit();let changes=0;const history=[current.rmse];
 for(let pass=0;pass<3;pass++){
  let changed=false;
  // Keep a bounded beam of coupled phase exchanges. Two individually worse
  // exchanges can jointly restore a consistent multi-base genotype.
  for(let h=0;h<refs.length;h++){
   const neighbors=(state:PathEdit[][])=>{
    const out:PathEdit[][][]=[],seen=new Set<string>(),cut=refs[h].cuts[targetIndex],size=(es:PathEdit[])=>es.reduce((n,e)=>n+e.inserted.length-e.end+e.start,0),mutable=(es:PathEdit[])=>size(es)<0?[]:size(es)>0?Array.from({length:size(es)},(_,i)=>es[0].start+i):Array.from({length:16},(_,i)=>cut-3+i);
    const add=(state:PathEdit[][])=>{const sorted=[[],...state.slice(1).sort((a,b)=>JSON.stringify(a).localeCompare(JSON.stringify(b)))],key=JSON.stringify(sorted);if(!seen.has(key)){seen.add(key);out.push(sorted);}};
    const change=(es:PathEdit[],q:number,b:string)=>{const n=size(es),seq=materialize(refs[h].sequence,es)!;if(n>0){const start=es[0].start,word=[...seq.slice(start,start+n)];word[q-start]=b;return[{start,end:start,inserted:word.join('')}];}const start=Math.min(q,...es.map(e=>e.start)),end=Math.max(q+1,...es.map(e=>e.end)),word=[...seq.slice(start,end)];word[q-start]=b;const events=normalizeMoleculeEdits(refs[h].sequence,[{start,end,inserted:word.join('')}]);return events.length&&(events[0].start<cut-3||events[0].start>cut+3||events.at(-1)!.end-events[0].start>10)?null:events;};
    for(let a=1;a<state.length;a++){
     const old=state[a],n=size(old),seq=materialize(refs[h].sequence,old)!;
     for(const q of mutable(old))for(const base of bases){if(base===seq[q])continue;const es=change(old,q,base);if(es){const next=[...state];next[a]=es;add(next);}}
     if(old.length)for(let start=cut-3;start<=cut+3;start++){const es=normalizeMoleculeEdits(refs[h].sequence,[{start,end:start+(n<0?-n:n===0?10:0),inserted:n<0?'':seq.slice(start,start+(n>0?n:10))}]),next=[...state];next[a]=es;add(next);}

    }
    // Exchange a whole phase tract, not just one base. Inserted words and
    // substitutions may require several coordinated base assignments before
    // the jointly fitted signal improves.
    const changeTract=(es:PathEdit[],qs:number[],other:string)=>{const n=size(es),seq=materialize(refs[h].sequence,es)!;
     if(n>0){const start=es[0].start,word=[...seq.slice(start,start+n)];for(const q of qs)word[q-start]=other[q];return[{start,end:start,inserted:word.join('')}];}
     const start=Math.min(...qs,...es.map(e=>e.start)),end=Math.max(...qs.map(q=>q+1),...es.map(e=>e.end)),word=[...seq.slice(start,end)];for(const q of qs)word[q-start]=other[q];const events=normalizeMoleculeEdits(refs[h].sequence,[{start,end,inserted:word.join('')}]);return events.length&&(events[0].start<cut-3||events[0].start>cut+3||events.at(-1)!.end-events[0].start>10)?null:events;
    };
    for(let a=1;a<state.length;a++)for(let b=a+1;b<state.length;b++){
     const sa=materialize(refs[h].sequence,state[a])!,sb=materialize(refs[h].sequence,state[b])!,different=mutable(state[a]).filter(q=>mutable(state[b]).includes(q)&&sa[q]!==sb[q]);
     for(let lo=0;lo<different.length;lo++)for(let hi=lo;hi<different.length;hi++){const qs=different.slice(lo,hi+1),na=changeTract(state[a],qs,sb),nb=changeTract(state[b],qs,sa);if(!na||!nb)continue;const next=[...state];next[a]=na;next[b]=nb;add(next);}
    }
    return out;
   };
   const root=profiles[h],first:{state:PathEdit[][];fit:any}[]=[];for(const state of neighbors(root)){profiles[h]=state;first.push({state,fit:fit(h)});}first.sort((a,b)=>a.fit.rmse-b.fit.rmse);let best={state:root,fit:current};for(const [candidate,parent]of first.slice(0,16).entries()){progress(`Sequence phase · pass ${pass+1} · reference ${h+1} / ${refs.length} · ${candidate+1} / ${Math.min(16,first.length)}`);for(const state of [parent.state,...neighbors(parent.state)]){profiles[h]=state;const trial=fit(h);if(trial.rmse*trial.rmse<best.fit.rmse*best.fit.rmse-Math.max(1e-15,2*(best.fit.optimalityGap||0)))best={state,fit:trial};}}
   profiles[h]=best.state;if(best.state!==root){current=best.fit;changes++;changed=true;history.push(current.rmse);}
  }
  progress('Joint sequence phasing · pass '+(pass+1));
  if(!changed)break;
 }
 return{profiles,rmse:current.rmse,changes,history};
}

END MODULE


BEGIN MODULE src/app/workers/core/sanger-consensus.ts
import {SangerVariant,TraceAlignment,TraceAlignmentColumn} from '../../models/sanger-analysis';
import {reverseComplement} from '../../utils/biology.utils';
/** Local affine-gap alignment for consensus variant representation only.
 * Registration/calibration retain their independent alignment policy. */
export function alignConsensusSequence(reference:string,query:string):{score:number;identity:number;columns:TraceAlignmentColumn[]}{
 const n=reference.length,m=query.length;if(n*m>24000000)throw new Error('Alignment region is too large.');
 const width=m+1,trace=[new Uint8Array((n+1)*width),new Uint8Array((n+1)*width),new Uint8Array((n+1)*width)];
 let previous=[new Int32Array(width),new Int32Array(width),new Int32Array(width)],current=[new Int32Array(width),new Int32Array(width),new Int32Array(width)],best=0,bi=0,bj=0,bs=0;
 for(let i=1;i<=n;i++){
  current.forEach(row=>row.fill(0));
  for(let j=1;j<=m;j++){
   const equal=reference[i-1]===query[j-1]&&/[ACGT]/.test(query[j-1]),sub=equal?3:-4,at=i*width+j;
   const choices=[[previous[0][j-1]+sub,previous[1][j-1]+sub,previous[2][j-1]+sub],[previous[0][j]-7,previous[1][j]-1,previous[2][j]-7],[current[0][j-1]-7,current[1][j-1]-7,current[2][j-1]-1]];
   for(let state=0;state<3;state++){let value=0,from=0;for(let k=0;k<3;k++)if(choices[state][k]>value){value=choices[state][k];from=k+1;}current[state][j]=value;trace[state][at]=from;if(value>best){best=value;bi=i;bj=j;bs=state;}}
  }
  [previous,current]=[current,previous];
 }
 const columns:TraceAlignmentColumn[]=[];let i=bi,j=bj,state=bs;
 while(i>0&&j>0){const from=trace[state][i*width+j];if(!from)break;if(state===0){columns.push({ref:i-1,query:j-1,a:reference[i-1],b:query[j-1]});i--;j--;}else if(state===1){columns.push({ref:i-1,query:null,a:reference[i-1],b:'-'});i--;}else{columns.push({ref:null,query:j-1,a:'-',b:query[j-1]});j--;}state=from-1;}
 columns.reverse();return {score:best,identity:columns.length?columns.filter(c=>c.a===c.b&&/[ACGT]/.test(c.a)).length/columns.length:0,columns};
}
/** Join nearby differences as one representation, not a claim of one repair event.
 * Keep primitive parts; only exact matched bridges inside cut±20 may be joined. */
export function consensusVariants(alignment:TraceAlignment,referenceLength:number,window:number,joinGap=3):SangerVariant[]{
 if(!Number.isInteger(joinGap)||joinGap<0||joinGap>10)throw new Error('Join nearby changes must be 0–10 matched bases.');
 type Block={columns:TraceAlignmentColumn[];start:number;end:number;parts:Block[]};
 const blocks:Block[]=[];let run:TraceAlignmentColumn[]=[],cursor=alignment.referenceStart;
 const flush=()=>{if(!run.length)return;const coords=run.flatMap(c=>c.ref===null?[]:[c.ref]);const start=coords.length?Math.min(...coords):cursor,end=coords.length?Math.max(...coords)+1:start;blocks.push({columns:run,start,end,parts:[]});run=[];};
 for(const c of alignment.columns){if(c.a===c.b&&/[ACGT]/.test(c.a)){flush();cursor=c.ref!+1;}else{if(!run.length&&c.ref!==null)cursor=c.ref;run.push(c);if(c.ref!==null)cursor=c.ref+1;}}flush();
 const groups:Block[]=[];
 for(const block of blocks){const last=groups.at(-1),gap=last?block.start-last.end:Infinity;
  if(last&&joinGap>0&&gap>=0&&gap<=joinGap&&last.start>=alignment.cut-20&&block.end<=alignment.cut+20&&last.columns.every(c=>!/[NRYWSKMBDHV]/.test(c.a+c.b))&&block.columns.every(c=>!/[NRYWSKMBDHV]/.test(c.a+c.b))){
   // Recover the exact matched bridge from the alignment, preserving all bases.
   const bridge=alignment.columns.filter(c=>c.ref!==null&&c.ref>=last.end&&c.ref<block.start&&c.a===c.b&&/[ACGT]/.test(c.a));
   if(bridge.length===gap){last.columns.push(...bridge,...block.columns);last.end=block.end;last.parts.push(block);continue;}
  }
  groups.push({...block,columns:[...block.columns],parts:[block]});
 }
 const represent=(block:Block):SangerVariant=>{let ref=block.columns.map(c=>c.a).join('').replace(/-/g,''),alt=block.columns.map(c=>c.b).join('').replace(/-/g,'');if(alignment.direction==='reverse'){ref=reverseComplement(ref);alt=reverseComplement(alt);}const netSize=alt.length-ref.length,hasInsertion=block.columns.some(c=>c.ref===null),hasDeletion=block.columns.some(c=>c.query===null),hasSubstitution=block.columns.some(c=>c.ref!==null&&c.query!==null&&c.a!==c.b),ambiguous=/[^ACGT]/.test(ref+alt);
  const type:SangerVariant['type']=ambiguous?'Ambiguous call':(hasInsertion&&hasDeletion)||((hasInsertion||hasDeletion)&&hasSubstitution)?'Complex replacement':netSize===0?'Substitution':netSize%3===0?'In-frame indel':'Out-of-frame indel';
  const position=alignment.direction==='forward'?block.start+1:referenceLength-block.end+1;
  return {position,endPosition:position+Math.max(1,ref.length)-1,reference:ref||'-',sample:alt||'-',netSize,type};
 };
 return groups.filter(b=>b.end>=alignment.cut-window/2&&b.start<=alignment.cut+window/2).map(g=>{const v=represent(g);if(g.parts.length>1){v.grouped=true;v.parts=g.parts.map(represent).sort((a,b)=>a.position-b.position);v.matchedBridgeBases=g.columns.filter(c=>c.a===c.b&&/[ACGT]/.test(c.a)).length;}return v;}).sort((a,b)=>a.position-b.position);
}

END MODULE


BEGIN MODULE src/app/workers/core/sanger-control-photometry.ts
/** Raw WT peak-strength regression. Normalized one-base fluorescence cannot
 * identify base/context yield; absolute WT strengths can, after a smooth cycle
 * trend. Neighbour main effects plus a ridge-shrunk triplet residual are
 * accepted only when withheld WT cycles improve. No edited sample, mixture
 * fraction, or truth label enters this fit. */
export interface ControlPhotometry {base:number[];left:number[];right:number[];baseUsed:boolean;contextUsed:boolean;triplet:number[];tripletUsed:boolean;tripletValidationMse:number;trainingBases:number;validationBases:number;rawValidationMse:number;baseValidationMse:number;contextValidationMse:number}
const median=(a:number[])=>{const x=[...a].sort((a,b)=>a-b);return x.length?x[Math.floor(x.length/2)]:0;};
export function fitControlPhotometry(reference:string,positions:number[],strengths:number[],options:{minimumBases?:number;context?:boolean}={}):ControlPhotometry{
 const rows=positions.flatMap((p,i)=>/^[ACGT]{3}$/.test(reference.slice(p-1,p+2))&&strengths[i]>0?[{p,y:Math.log(strengths[i])}]:[]),empty={base:[0,0,0,0],left:[0,0,0,0],right:[0,0,0,0],triplet:Array(64).fill(0),tripletUsed:false,tripletValidationMse:0,baseUsed:false,contextUsed:false,trainingBases:0,validationBases:0,rawValidationMse:0,baseValidationMse:0,contextValidationMse:0};if(rows.length<(options.minimumBases??80))return empty;
 const lo=Math.min(...rows.map(r=>r.p)),hi=Math.max(...rows.map(r=>r.p)),knots=Math.max(3,Math.ceil((hi-lo)/60)+1),train=rows.filter((_,i)=>i%5!==0),valid=rows.filter((_,i)=>i%5===0);
 const features=(p:number,groups:number)=>{const x=Array((groups===4?76:4*groups)+knots).fill(0),letters=[reference[p],reference[p-1],reference[p+1]];for(let g=0;g<Math.min(groups,3);g++){for(let b=0;b<4;b++)x[4*g+b]=Number(letters[g]==='ACGT'[b])-.25;}if(groups===4){const triplet='ACGT'.indexOf(reference[p-1])*16+'ACGT'.indexOf(reference[p])*4+'ACGT'.indexOf(reference[p+1]);x[12+triplet]=1;}const offset=groups===4?76:4*groups;const at=(p-lo)/Math.max(1,hi-lo)*(knots-1),left=Math.min(knots-2,Math.floor(at)),fraction=at-left;x[offset+left]=1-fraction;x[offset+left+1]=fraction;return x;};
 const solve=(groups:number,penalty=4)=>{const X=train.map(r=>features(r.p,groups)),n=X[0].length,active=X.map(x=>x.flatMap((v,i)=>v?[[i,v] as [number,number]]:[]));let weights=train.map(()=>1),beta=Array(n).fill(0);for(let iteration=0;iteration<4;iteration++){const matrix=Array.from({length:n},()=>new Float64Array(n+1));active.forEach((entries,i)=>{for(const [a,x]of entries){matrix[a][n]+=weights[i]*x*train[i].y;for(const [b,y]of entries)matrix[a][b]+=weights[i]*x*y;}});for(let a=0;a<n;a++)matrix[a][a]+=groups===4&&a>=12&&a<76?penalty:.01;
   for(let a=0;a<n;a++){let pivot=a;for(let b=a+1;b<n;b++)if(Math.abs(matrix[b][a])>Math.abs(matrix[pivot][a]))pivot=b;[matrix[a],matrix[pivot]]=[matrix[pivot],matrix[a]];const value=matrix[a][a];if(Math.abs(value)<1e-12)return {beta:Array(n).fill(0),mse:Infinity};for(let b=a;b<=n;b++)matrix[a][b]/=value;for(let row=0;row<n;row++)if(row!==a){const scale=matrix[row][a];for(let b=a;b<=n;b++)matrix[row][b]-=scale*matrix[a][b];}}
   beta=matrix.map(r=>r[n]);const residual=X.map((x,i)=>train[i].y-x.reduce((s,v,j)=>s+v*beta[j],0)),center=median(residual),sigma=Math.max(.005,1.4826*median(residual.map(v=>Math.abs(v-center))));weights=residual.map(v=>Math.min(1,1.5*sigma/Math.max(1e-12,Math.abs(v-center))));
  }const mse=valid.reduce((sum,r)=>{const x=features(r.p,groups),p=x.reduce((s,v,j)=>s+v*beta[j],0);return sum+(r.y-p)**2;},0)/valid.length;return {beta,mse};};
 const smooth=solve(0),base=solve(1),context=options.context===false?base:solve(3),baseUsed=base.mse<smooth.mse*.95&&base.mse+1e-5<smooth.mse,contextUsed=context.mse<base.mse*.95&&context.mse+1e-5<base.mse,triplet=options.context===false?context:[1,4,16].map(penalty=>solve(4,penalty)).reduce((a,b)=>a.mse<=b.mse?a:b),tripletUsed=triplet.mse<context.mse*.95&&triplet.mse+1e-5<context.mse,chosen=tripletUsed?triplet:contextUsed?context:baseUsed?base:smooth,center=(v:number[])=>{const mean=v.reduce((s,x)=>s+x,0)/4;return v.map(x=>x-mean);};
 return {...empty,triplet:tripletUsed?chosen.beta.slice(12,76):Array(64).fill(0),tripletUsed,tripletValidationMse:triplet.mse,base:baseUsed||contextUsed||tripletUsed?center(chosen.beta.slice(0,4)):[0,0,0,0],left:contextUsed||tripletUsed?center(chosen.beta.slice(4,8)):[0,0,0,0],right:contextUsed||tripletUsed?center(chosen.beta.slice(8,12)):[0,0,0,0],baseUsed,contextUsed:contextUsed||tripletUsed,trainingBases:train.length,validationBases:valid.length,rawValidationMse:smooth.mse,baseValidationMse:base.mse,contextValidationMse:tripletUsed?triplet.mse:context.mse};
}

END MODULE


BEGIN MODULE src/app/workers/core/sanger-control-response.ts
import {fitEmissionMixture} from './sanger-emission';
import {mixPhase,phaseNeighbor} from './sanger-signal';
export interface ControlResponseProfile {
 table:number[][];endTable?:number[][];knots?:number[][][];start:number;end:number;
 sites?:Record<number,Record<number,number[]>>;
}
const bases='ACGT';
function context(sequence:string,p:number):number {
 const l=bases.indexOf(sequence[p-1]),b=bases.indexOf(sequence[p]),r=bases.indexOf(sequence[p+1]);
 return l<0||b<0||r<0?-1:16*l+4*b+r;
}
function weightsAt(p:number,start:number,end:number,count:number):[number,number,number] {
 const x=Math.max(0,Math.min(count-1,(p-start)/Math.max(1,end-start)*(count-1))),a=Math.floor(x),b=Math.min(count-1,a+1);
 return [a,b,x-a];
}
function responseAt(profile:ControlResponseProfile,id:number,p:number):number[] {
 const tables=profile.knots||(profile.endTable?[profile.table,profile.endTable]:[profile.table]);
 const [a,b,t]=weightsAt(p,profile.start,profile.end,tables.length);
 return tables[a][id].map((v,d)=>(1-t)*v+t*tables[b][id][d]);
}
const responseCache=new WeakMap<ControlResponseProfile,Map<number,number[][]>>();
export function controlKnownContextEmission(left:string,current:string,right:string,profile:ControlResponseProfile,p:number):number[] {
 if(!current)return [0,0,0,0];
 const a=left.length===1?bases.indexOf(left):-1,b=bases.indexOf(current),c=right.length===1?bases.indexOf(right):-1;
 if(a<0||b<0||c<0){const pi=(base:string)=>Array.from(bases,x=>base==='N'?.25:Number(base===x));return controlContextProbability(pi(left),pi(current),pi(right),profile,p);}
 const id=16*a+4*b+c,local=profile.sites?.[p]?.[id];if(local)return local;
 let cache=responseCache.get(profile);if(!cache){cache=new Map();responseCache.set(profile,cache);}let row=cache.get(p);if(!row){row=Array.from({length:64},(_,context)=>responseAt(profile,context,p));cache.set(p,row);}return row[id];
}
export function controlContextProbability(left:number[],current:number[],right:number[],profile:ControlResponseProfile,p:number):number[] {
 const out=[0,0,0,0],l=left.some(x=>x)?left:[.25,.25,.25,.25],r=right.some(x=>x)?right:[.25,.25,.25,.25];
 for(let a=0;a<4;a++)if(l[a])for(let b=0;b<4;b++)if(current[b])for(let c=0;c<4;c++)if(r[c]){
  const id=16*a+4*b+c,amount=l[a]*current[b]*r[c],v=profile.sites?.[p]?.[id]||responseAt(profile,id,p);
  for(let d=0;d<4;d++)out[d]+=amount*v[d];
 }
 return out;
}
export function controlContextEmission(sequence:string,p:number,lag:number,profile:ControlResponseProfile):number[] {
 const probabilities=(at:number)=>Array.from(bases,b=>sequence[at]==='N'?.25:Number(sequence[at]===b));
 const one=(at:number)=>context(sequence,at)<0&&profile.sites?.[at]?.[-1]?profile.sites[at][-1]:controlContextProbability(probabilities(at-1),probabilities(at),probabilities(at+1),profile,at);
 return lag===0?one(p):mixPhase(one(p),one(phaseNeighbor(p,lag)),lag);
}
/** Nonnegative ridge regression of absolute four-channel fluorescence. */
function solveResponse(X:number[][],targets:number[][],prior:number[][],penalty:number):number[][] {
 const n=prior.length,gram=Array.from({length:n},()=>new Float64Array(n)),rhs=Array.from({length:n},()=>[0,0,0,0]);
 X.forEach((x,j)=>{const active=x.flatMap((v,i)=>v?[[i,v]]:[]);for(const [a,v]of active){for(let d=0;d<4;d++)rhs[a][d]+=v*targets[j][d];for(const [b,w]of active)gram[a][b]+=v*w;}});
 for(let a=0;a<n;a++){gram[a][a]+=penalty;for(let d=0;d<4;d++)rhs[a][d]+=penalty*prior[a][d];}
 const neighbors=gram.map((row,a)=>Array.from(row,(v,b)=>({v,b})).filter(x=>x.b!==a&&x.v!==0)),B=prior.map(v=>[...v]);
 for(let iteration=0;iteration<500;iteration++){
  let delta=0;for(let a=0;a<n;a++)for(let d=0;d<4;d++){
   let value=rhs[a][d];for(const {v,b}of neighbors[a])value-=v*B[b][d];
   const next=Math.max(0,value/Math.max(1e-10,gram[a][a]));delta=Math.max(delta,Math.abs(next-B[a][d]));B[a][d]=next;
  }
  if(delta<1e-7)break;
 }
 return B;
}
/** One WT-only calibration for single and pooled traces. Context responses are
 * evaluated at the actual read cycle, never at the pre-edit donor coordinate.
 * A smooth scalar envelope separates read brightness from context yield. */
export function fitControlResponse(sequences:string[],positions:number[],observed:number[][],priorShares:number[]) {
 const rows=positions.filter(p=>observed[p]?.some(v=>v>0)&&sequences.some(s=>context(s,p)>=0)&&sequences.every(s=>!s[p]||context(s,p)>=0));
 if(rows.length<80)throw Error('Too few WT control cycles for response calibration.');
 const totals=rows.map(p=>observed[p].reduce((s,v)=>s+v,0)).sort((a,b)=>a-b),scale=totals[Math.floor(totals.length/2)];
 const Y=new Map(rows.map(p=>[p,observed[p].map(v=>v/scale)])),block=Math.max(2,Math.ceil(Math.cbrt(rows.length)));
 const train=rows.filter(p=>Math.floor(p/block)%5!==0),valid=rows.filter(p=>Math.floor(p/block)%5===0),start=rows[0],end=rows.at(-1)!,bandwidth=Math.max(30,(end-start)/6);
 const ids=new Map(rows.map(p=>[p,sequences.map(s=>context(s,p))]));
 const basePrior=Array.from({length:4},(_,b)=>Array.from(bases,(_,d)=>Number(b===d)));
 const profile=(table:number[][],capacity:number):ControlResponseProfile=>{
  const knots=capacity===4?[Array.from({length:64},(_,c)=>table[Math.floor(c/4)%4])]:Array.from({length:capacity/64},(_,i)=>table.slice(i*64,(i+1)*64));
  return {table:knots[0],endTable:knots.length>1?knots.at(-1):undefined,knots,start,end};
 };
 const fit=(capacity:number,penalty:number,priorTable:number[][],training=train)=>{
  let shares=[...priorShares],table=priorTable.map(v=>[...v]),envelope=new Map(rows.map(p=>[p,1]));
  const features=(p:number)=>{
   const out=Array(capacity).fill(0),[a,b,t]=weightsAt(p,start,end,capacity===4?1:capacity/64);
   ids.get(p)!.forEach((c,h)=>{if(c<0)return;if(capacity===4)out[Math.floor(c/4)%4]+=shares[h];else{out[64*a+c]+=shares[h]*(1-t);out[64*b+c]+=shares[h]*t;}});
   return out;
  };
  const predict=(p:number)=>{const x=features(p),v=[0,0,0,0];for(let c=0;c<capacity;c++)if(x[c])for(let d=0;d<4;d++)v[d]+=x[c]*table[c][d];return v;};
  for(let iteration=0;iteration<8;iteration++){
   table=solveResponse(training.map(features),training.map(p=>Y.get(p)!.map(v=>v/envelope.get(p)!)),priorTable,penalty);
   const ratio=training.map(p=>Math.log(Math.max(1e-8,Y.get(p)!.reduce((s,v)=>s+v,0))/Math.max(1e-8,predict(p).reduce((s,v)=>s+v,0))));
   for(const p of rows){let sum=0,mass=0;for(let j=0;j<training.length;j++){const w=Math.exp(-.5*((p-training[j])/bandwidth)**2);sum+=w*ratio[j];mass+=w;}envelope.set(p,Math.exp(sum/Math.max(1e-10,mass)));}
   const expanded=profile(table,capacity),cols=sequences.map(s=>training.flatMap(p=>controlContextEmission(s,p,0,expanded))),y=training.flatMap(p=>{const a=Y.get(p)!,sum=a.reduce((s,v)=>s+v,0);return a.map(v=>v/sum);});
   shares=fitEmissionMixture(cols,y).weights;
  }
  let raw=0,colour=0,marker=0,markers=0;
  for(const p of valid){
   const v=predict(p),sum=v.reduce((s,x)=>s+x,0),y=Y.get(p)!,total=y.reduce((s,x)=>s+x,0),informative=new Set(ids.get(p)!.filter(id=>id>=0)).size>1;
   for(let d=0;d<4;d++){raw+=(y[d]-v[d]*envelope.get(p)!)**2;const error=(y[d]/total-(sum?v[d]/sum:0))**2;colour+=error;if(informative)marker+=error;}
   if(informative)markers++;
  }
  return {...profile(table,capacity),shares,markerValidationMse:markers>=6?marker/(4*markers):undefined,markerValidationBases:markers,rawValidationMse:raw/(4*valid.length),validationMse:colour/(4*valid.length),penalty,capacity,envelope,priorTable};
 };
 const base=fit(4,.1,basePrior);let best=base;
 // More context/read detail is accepted only if it predicts withheld WT cycles.
 for(const capacity of [64,128,256]){
  const count=capacity/64,prior=Array.from({length:count},(_,i)=>Array.from({length:64},(_,c)=>responseAt(best,c,start+(end-start)*(count>1?i/(count-1):.5)))).flat();
  const candidates=[.1,1,10].map(lambda=>fit(capacity,lambda,prior)).filter(c=>c.validationMse<best.validationMse*.95&&c.rawValidationMse<best.rawValidationMse&&(best.markerValidationMse===undefined||c.markerValidationMse!==undefined&&c.markerValidationMse<=best.markerValidationMse));
  const score=(c:typeof base)=>c.validationMse/Math.max(1e-12,base.validationMse)+(c.markerValidationMse??c.validationMse)/Math.max(1e-12,base.markerValidationMse??base.validationMse);
  best=candidates.reduce((a,b)=>score(a)<score(b)?a:b,best);
 }
 const final=fit(best.capacity,best.penalty,best.priorTable,rows),sites:Record<number,Record<number,number[]>>={};
 // Reuse a measured WT context at this read cycle. For a mixed control the
 // minimum nonnegative correction respects the independently fitted WT shares.
 for(const p of rows){
  const contexts=new Map<number,number>();ids.get(p)!.forEach((id,h)=>{if(id>=0)contexts.set(id,(contexts.get(id)||0)+final.shares[h]);});
  const keys=[...contexts.keys()],weights=keys.map(k=>contexts.get(k)!),prior=keys.map(k=>responseAt(final,k,p)),target=Y.get(p)!.map(v=>v/final.envelope.get(p)!),local=prior.map(v=>[...v]);
  for(let b=0;b<4;b++){
   let active=keys.map((_,i)=>i);
   while(active.length){
    const mass=active.reduce((s,i)=>s+weights[i]**2,0),prediction=active.reduce((s,i)=>s+weights[i]*prior[i][b],0),delta=(target[b]-prediction)/Math.max(1e-12,mass),negative=active.filter(i=>prior[i][b]+delta*weights[i]<0);
    if(!negative.length){for(const i of active)local[i][b]=prior[i][b]+delta*weights[i];break;}
    for(const i of negative)local[i][b]=0;active=active.filter(i=>!negative.includes(i));
   }
  }
  sites[p]=Object.fromEntries(keys.map((k,i)=>[k,local[i]]));
 }
 // Ambiguous WT bases still have measured fluorescence; keep it on its own
 // unchanged WT cycle rather than replacing it with a fabricated base call.
 if(sequences.length===1)for(let p=0;p<observed.length;p++)if(context(sequences[0],p)<0&&observed[p]?.some(v=>v>0)){
  const nearest=Math.max(start,Math.min(end,p)),gain=final.envelope.get(nearest)||1;sites[p]={[-1]:observed[p].map(v=>v/(scale*gain))};
 }
 return {...best,envelope:undefined,priorTable:undefined,table:final.table,endTable:final.endTable,knots:final.knots,shares:final.shares,sites,used:best!==base,baseValidationMse:base.validationMse,trainingBases:train.length,validationBases:valid.length,refitBases:rows.length};
}

END MODULE


BEGIN MODULE src/app/workers/core/sanger-control-transfer.ts
import {ControlResponseProfile,controlContextEmission} from './sanger-control-response';
import {fitEmissionMixture} from './sanger-emission';
import {correctGain} from './sanger-signal';
/** Relative dye gains require absolute WT peak areas: normalizing a pure WT
 * peak first removes nearly all information about its emitting dye's gain.
 * A smooth scalar lane envelope is nuisance. Validation is WT-only and blocked. */
export function fitControlTransfer(sequences:string[],positions:number[],areas:number[][],profile:ControlResponseProfile,lag:number,initialGains:number[],initialShares:number[]) {
 const rows=positions.filter(p=>areas[p]?.some(x=>x>0)),block=Math.max(2,Math.ceil(Math.cbrt(rows.length))),train=rows.filter(p=>Math.floor(p/block)%5!==0),valid=rows.filter(p=>Math.floor(p/block)%5===0);
 const totals=rows.map(p=>areas[p].reduce((s,x)=>s+x,0)).sort((a,b)=>a-b),scale=totals[Math.floor(totals.length/2)]||1,Y=new Map(rows.map(p=>[p,areas[p].map(v=>v/scale)])),E=new Map(rows.map(p=>[p,sequences.map(s=>controlContextEmission(s,p,lag,profile))])),bandwidth=Math.max(30,(rows.at(-1)!-rows[0])/6);
 const fit=(learn:boolean)=>{
  let gains=[...initialGains],shares=[...initialShares],envelope=new Map(rows.map(p=>[p,1]));
  const predict=(p:number)=>Array.from({length:4},(_,b)=>E.get(p)!.reduce((sum,v,h)=>sum+shares[h]*v[b]*gains[b],0));
  for(let iteration=0;iteration<12;iteration++){
   const ratio=train.map(p=>Math.log(Math.max(1e-10,Y.get(p)!.reduce((s,x)=>s+x,0))/Math.max(1e-10,predict(p).reduce((s,x)=>s+x,0))));
   for(const p of rows){let mass=0,sum=0;for(let j=0;j<train.length;j++){const w=Math.exp(-.5*((p-train[j])/bandwidth)**2);mass+=w;sum+=w*ratio[j];}envelope.set(p,Math.exp(sum/Math.max(1e-10,mass)));}
   if(learn){
    const next=Array.from({length:4},(_,b)=>{let xy=0,xx=0;for(const p of train){const x=envelope.get(p)!*E.get(p)!.reduce((s,v,h)=>s+shares[h]*v[b],0);xy+=x*Y.get(p)![b];xx+=x*x;}return Math.max(1e-10,xy/Math.max(1e-10,xx));}),average=next.reduce((s,x)=>s+Math.log(x),0)/4;
    gains=next.map(x=>Math.exp(Math.log(x)-average));
   }
   shares=fitEmissionMixture(sequences.map((_,h)=>train.flatMap(p=>E.get(p)![h])),train.flatMap(p=>correctGain(Y.get(p)!,gains))).weights;
  }
  let raw=0,colour=0,variance=0;const normalized=valid.flatMap(p=>{const v=Y.get(p)!,s=v.reduce((a,b)=>a+b,0);return v.map(x=>x/s);}),mean=normalized.reduce((s,x)=>s+x,0)/Math.max(1,normalized.length);
  for(const p of valid){const v=predict(p),sum=v.reduce((s,x)=>s+x,0),y=Y.get(p)!,total=y.reduce((s,x)=>s+x,0);for(let b=0;b<4;b++){raw+=(y[b]-envelope.get(p)!*v[b])**2;colour+=(y[b]/total-(sum?v[b]/sum:0))**2;variance+=(y[b]/total-mean)**2;}}
  return {gains,shares,rawMse:raw/Math.max(1,4*valid.length),colourMse:colour/Math.max(1,4*valid.length),colourR2:variance?1-colour/variance:0};
 };
 const baseline=fit(false),candidate=train.length>=40&&valid.length>=20?fit(true):baseline,used=candidate!==baseline&&candidate.gains.every(x=>x>=.55&&x<=1.8)&&candidate.rawMse<baseline.rawMse*.95&&candidate.colourMse<=baseline.colourMse+1e-10;
 return {...(used?candidate:baseline),used,initialRawMse:baseline.rawMse,initialColourMse:baseline.colourMse,trainingBases:train.length,validationBases:valid.length};
}

END MODULE


BEGIN MODULE src/app/workers/core/sanger-cross-class-alternatives.ts
import{PathEdit,Prepared,materialize,Model}from'./sanger-path-common';import{SangerAnnotation,SangerExactOption,SangerJointEvent}from'../../models/sanger-analysis';import{exactFromNumber,exactMin,exactSubtract,exactNumber,exactSum,exactWeightedEquality}from'./exact-mixture-fiber';
interface Row {model:Model;fraction:number}
const net=(e:PathEdit[])=>e.reduce((s,x)=>s+x.inserted.length-(x.end-x.start),0);
function describe(ref:string,seq:string,size:number,cut:number,radius:number,span:number):PathEdit[]|null{
 if(size===0){let start=0,end=ref.length;while(start<end&&ref[start]===seq[start])start++;if(start===end)return[];while(end>start&&ref[end-1]===seq[end-1])end--;if(start<cut-radius||end>cut+radius+span||end-start>span)return null;return[{start,end,inserted:seq.slice(start,end)}];}
 for(let start=cut-radius;start<=cut+radius;start++){const e={start,end:start+(size<0?-size:0),inserted:size>0?seq.slice(start,start+size):''};if(materialize(ref,[e])===seq)return[e];}return null;
}
/** Equal-mass exchanges between a zero-net (including WT) and length-changed molecule.
 * Only exact, complete-emission witnesses are offered. Description complexity
 * orders equivalent representatives; it does not refit the trace
 * or use truth to choose an allele. */
export function crossClassAlternatives(ref:Prepared,annotation:SangerAnnotation,source:Row[],toInput:(events:PathEdit[])=>SangerJointEvent[],emit:(sequence:string,p:number)=>number[],limit=4,radius=3,span=10):SangerExactOption[]{
 if(ref.cuts.length!==1||source.length!==annotation.components.length)return[];const cut=ref.cuts[0],weights=source.map(r=>exactFromNumber(r.fraction)),best=new Map<string,{option:SangerExactOption;cost:number;mask:number}>(),columnCache=new Map<string,number[]>(),length=Math.max(ref.sequence.length,...source.map(r=>r.model.sequence.length))+2,column=(sequence:string)=>{let c=columnCache.get(sequence);if(!c){c=Array.from({length},(_,p)=>emit(sequence,p)).flat();columnCache.set(sequence,c);}return c;},changed=(events:PathEdit[])=>events.reduce((s,e)=>s+(e.inserted.length===e.end-e.start?Array.from(e.inserted,(b,i)=>Number(b!==ref.sequence[e.start+i])).reduce((a,b)=>a+b,0):e.inserted.length+e.end-e.start),0),baseCost=source.reduce((sum,r)=>sum+r.fraction*changed(r.model.events),0),sequenceCache=new Map<string,string>(),componentKey=(c:SangerAnnotation['components'][number])=>{const es=c.events||[],key=JSON.stringify(es),hit=sequenceCache.get(key);if(hit!==undefined)return hit;const sequence=materialize(annotation.reference,es.map(e=>({start:e.start,end:e.end,inserted:e.insertedSequence||''})))!;sequenceCache.set(key,sequence);return sequence;},sourceKeys=annotation.components.map(componentKey);
 for(let i=0;i<source.length;i++)for(let j=i+1;j<source.length;j++){
  const a=source[i].model,b=source[j].model,la=net(a.events),lb=net(b.events);if(la===lb||la!==0&&lb!==0||annotation.components[i].unresolved||annotation.components[j].unresolved||a.sequence.includes('N')||b.sequence.includes('N'))continue;
  const common=exactMin(weights[i],weights[j]),mass=exactNumber(common),left=cut-radius,right=cut+radius+Math.max(span,la,lb),fixed=(p:number,start:number,size:number)=>size===0?p>=start&&p<start+span?null:ref.sequence[p]:size>0?p<start?ref.sequence[p]:p<start+size?null:ref.sequence[p-size]:ref.sequence[p<start?p:p-size];
  for(let sa=cut-radius;sa<=cut+radius;sa++)for(let sb=cut-radius;sb<=cut+radius;sb++){
   const aa=a.sequence.split(''),bb=b.sequence.split(''),free:number[]=[];let valid=true;
   for(let p=left;p<right;p++){const x=a.sequence[p],y=b.sequence[p],fa=fixed(p,sa,la),fb=fixed(p,sb,lb);if(fa!==null&&fb!==null){if(!(fa===x&&fb===y||fa===y&&fb===x)){valid=false;break;}aa[p]=fa;bb[p]=fb;}else if(fa!==null){if(fa!==x&&fa!==y){valid=false;break;}aa[p]=fa;bb[p]=fa===x?y:x;}else if(fb!==null){if(fb!==x&&fb!==y){valid=false;break;}bb[p]=fb;aa[p]=fb===y?x:y;}else if(x!==y)free.push(p);}
   if(!valid)continue;const patterns=free.length<=12?Array.from({length:2**free.length},(_,i)=>i):[0,-1,...free.map((_,i)=>2**i)];
   for(const pattern of patterns){free.forEach((p,k)=>{const swap=pattern===-1||Math.floor(pattern/2**k)%2===1;aa[p]=swap?b.sequence[p]:a.sequence[p];bb[p]=swap?a.sequence[p]:b.sequence[p];});const x=aa.join(''),y=bb.join('');if(x===a.sequence&&y===b.sequence)continue;const ex=describe(ref.sequence,x,la,cut,radius,span),ey=describe(ref.sequence,y,lb,cut,radius,span);if(!ex||!ey)continue;const cost=baseCost+mass*(changed(ex)+changed(ey)-changed(a.events)-changed(b.events));
    const components:SangerAnnotation['components']=[],exactFractions:typeof weights=[];for(let k=0;k<source.length;k++){const weight=k===i||k===j?exactSubtract(weights[k],common):weights[k];if(exactNumber(weight)>0){components.push({...annotation.components[k],fraction:exactNumber(weight)/annotation.share});exactFractions.push(weight);}}for(const [id,events]of [[i,ex],[j,ey]]as const){components.push({...annotation.components[id],events:toInput(events),fraction:mass/annotation.share});exactFractions.push(common);}
    const merged=new Map<string,number>();for(let k=components.length-1;k>=0;k--){const key=componentKey(components[k]),old=merged.get(key);if(old===undefined)merged.set(key,k);else{exactFractions[old]=exactSum([exactFractions[old],exactFractions[k]]);components[old]={...components[old],fraction:exactNumber(exactFractions[old])/annotation.share};components.splice(k,1);exactFractions.splice(k,1);for(const [key,id]of merged)if(id>k)merged.set(key,id-1);}}
    // Keep a witness for each affected source-group set, rather than only
    // the cheapest descriptions. Otherwise a short option list can hide a
    // genuinely variable insertion and falsely display it as certain.
    const mask=sourceKeys.reduce((mask,key,i)=>{const ids=components.flatMap((c,k)=>componentKey(c)===key?[k]:[]),total=exactSum(ids.map(k=>exactFractions[k]));return exactSubtract(total,weights[i]).n==='0'?mask:mask|2**i;},0),key=String(mask);if(!mask||best.has(key)&&best.get(key)!.cost<=cost)continue;
    if(!exactWeightedEquality([{weight:weights[i],column:column(a.sequence)},{weight:weights[j],column:column(b.sequence)}],[{weight:exactSubtract(weights[i],common),column:column(a.sequence)},{weight:exactSubtract(weights[j],common),column:column(b.sequence)},{weight:common,column:column(x)},{weight:common,column:column(y)}]))continue;
    best.set(key,{cost,mask,option:{id:'cross-class-'+best.size,components,exactFractions}});
   }
  }
 }
 const candidates=[...best.values()],selected:typeof candidates=[];let covered=0;const count=(mask:number)=>mask.toString(2).replace(/0/g,'').length;while(candidates.length&&selected.length<limit){candidates.sort((a,b)=>count(b.mask&~covered)-count(a.mask&~covered)||a.cost-b.cost);const next=candidates.shift()!;selected.push(next);covered|=next.mask;}return selected.map((v,i)=>({...v.option,id:'cross-class-'+i}));
}

END MODULE


BEGIN MODULE src/app/workers/core/sanger-cross-target-alternatives.ts
import{SangerAnnotation,SangerExactOption,SangerJointEvent}from'../../models/sanger-analysis';
import{Prepared,Model,PathEdit,materialize}from'./sanger-path-common';
import{crossClassAlternatives}from'./sanger-cross-class-alternatives';
import{exactFromNumber,exactSum,exactSubtract,exactNumber}from'./exact-mixture-fiber';
import{sangerSequenceKey}from'../../utils/sanger-sequence-groups';
interface Row{model:Model;fraction:number}
/** Hold the other target's molecule background fixed. Its upstream length
 * shifts the local coordinate system but never changes the full emitter used
 * for certification. No target independence or truth is used. */
export function crossTargetAlternatives(ref:Prepared,annotation:SangerAnnotation,source:Row[],target:number,toInput:(events:PathEdit[])=>SangerJointEvent[],emit:(sequence:string,p:number)=>number[],limit=4):SangerExactOption[]{
 if(ref.cuts.length!==2||ref.reverse)return[];
 const nearest=(e:PathEdit)=>Math.abs(e.start-ref.cuts[0])<=Math.abs(e.start-ref.cuts[1])?0:1,backgrounds=new Map<string,{indices:number[];events:PathEdit[]}>(),weights=source.map(r=>exactFromNumber(r.fraction));
 source.forEach((row,i)=>{if(annotation.components[i].unresolved)return;const events=row.model.events.filter(e=>nearest(e)!==target),key=JSON.stringify(events),group=backgrounds.get(key)||{indices:[],events};group.indices.push(i);backgrounds.set(key,group);});
 const localKey=(events:SangerJointEvent[])=>sangerSequenceKey(annotation.reference,events.filter(e=>e.target===target+1||e.target===0)),sourceKeys=[...new Set(annotation.components.map(c=>localKey(c.events||[])))],baseMass=sourceKeys.map(key=>exactSum(annotation.components.flatMap((c,i)=>localKey(c.events||[])===key?[weights[i]]:[]))),candidates:{option:SangerExactOption;mask:number}[]=[];
 for(const background of [...backgrounds.values()].sort((a,b)=>b.indices.length-a.indices.length)){
  if(background.indices.length<2)continue;
  const sequence=materialize(ref.sequence,background.events);if(!sequence)continue;const shift=background.events.filter(e=>e.end<=ref.cuts[target]).reduce((sum,e)=>sum+e.inserted.length-e.end+e.start,0),cut=ref.cuts[target]+shift,share=background.indices.reduce((sum,i)=>sum+source[i].fraction,0),rows=background.indices.map(i=>{const events=source[i].model.events.filter(e=>nearest(e)===target).map(e=>({...e,start:e.start+shift,end:e.end+shift}));return{fraction:source[i].fraction,model:{...source[i].model,events,aliases:[]}};}),virtual:Prepared={...ref,sequence,cuts:[cut],input:{name:ref.name,sequence,cut},offset:0,reverse:false},localInput=(es:PathEdit[])=>es.map(e=>({target:1,start:e.start,end:e.end,inserted:e.inserted.length,insertedSequence:e.inserted})),a:SangerAnnotation={name:ref.name,reference:sequence,cut,cuts:[cut],share,estimable:true,direction:'forward',components:rows.map(r=>({fraction:r.fraction/share,size:r.model.events.reduce((sum,e)=>sum+e.inserted.length-e.end+e.start,0),events:localInput(r.model.events)}))};
  for(const local of crossClassAlternatives(virtual,a,rows,localInput,emit,limit)){
   const removed=new Set(background.indices),keep=source.flatMap((_,i)=>removed.has(i)?[]:[i]),components=keep.map(i=>annotation.components[i]),exactFractions=keep.map(i=>weights[i]);
   local.components.forEach((c,i)=>{const events=[...background.events,...(c.events||[]).map(e=>({start:e.start-shift,end:e.end-shift,inserted:e.insertedSequence||''}))],converted=toInput(events),sizes=(annotation.cuts||[annotation.cut]).map((_,t)=>converted.filter(e=>e.target===t+1||e.target===0).reduce((sum,e)=>sum+e.inserted-e.end+e.start,0));components.push({fraction:exactNumber(local.exactFractions[i])/annotation.share,size:sizes.reduce((a,b)=>a+b,0),pathSizes:sizes,events:converted});exactFractions.push(local.exactFractions[i]);});
   const mask=sourceKeys.reduce((mask,key,i)=>exactSubtract(exactSum(components.flatMap((c,k)=>localKey(c.events||[])===key?[exactFractions[k]]:[])),baseMass[i]).n==='0'?mask:mask|2**i,0);if(mask)candidates.push({mask,option:{id:'target-cross-'+target+'-'+candidates.length,components,exactFractions}});
  }
 }
 const chosen:typeof candidates=[],count=(n:number)=>n.toString(2).replace(/0/g,'').length;let covered=0;
 while(candidates.length&&chosen.length<limit){candidates.sort((a,b)=>count(b.mask&~covered)-count(a.mask&~covered)||a.option.components.length-b.option.components.length);const next=candidates.shift()!;chosen.push(next);covered|=next.mask;}
 return chosen.map((c,i)=>({...c.option,id:'target-cross-'+target+'-'+i}));
}

END MODULE


BEGIN MODULE src/app/workers/core/sanger-cycle.ts
import {SangerTrace,TRACE_BASES} from '../../models/sanger-analysis';
/** Repair isolated PLOC metadata errors only when an actual raw fluorescence
 * peak supports the missing cycle, or a spurious call lies in a raw trough.
 * No reference, guide, mutation hypothesis or fitted fraction is consulted. */
export function repairSignalTraceCycles(trace:SangerTrace):{trace:SangerTrace;added:number;removed:number}{
 const peaks=Array.from(trace.peaks);let added=0,removed=0;
 const strength=(p:number)=>TRACE_BASES.reduce((sum,b)=>sum+(trace.channels[b][p]||0),0);
 for(let i=9;i<peaks.length-9;i++){
  const spacings=peaks.slice(i-8,i+9).slice(1).map((p,k)=>p-peaks[i-8+k]).sort((a,b)=>a-b),median=spacings[8],gap=peaks[i]-peaks[i-1];if(!median)continue;
  if(gap>1.8*median&&gap<2.2*median){
   const center=(peaks[i]+peaks[i-1])/2;let p=Math.round(center);
   for(let j=Math.ceil(center-.18*median);j<=Math.floor(center+.18*median);j++)if(strength(j)>strength(p))p=j;
   const neighbours=Math.min(strength(peaks[i-1]),strength(peaks[i])),trough=Math.max(strength(Math.round((peaks[i-1]+p)/2)),strength(Math.round((p+peaks[i])/2)));
   if(strength(p)>.25*neighbours&&trough<.65*strength(p)){peaks.splice(i,0,p);added++;i++;}
  }else if(gap<.6*median&&peaks[i+1]-peaks[i]<.6*median&&Math.abs(peaks[i+1]-peaks[i-1]-median)<.22*median&&strength(peaks[i])<.45*Math.min(strength(peaks[i-1]),strength(peaks[i+1]))){peaks.splice(i,1);removed++;i--;}
  if(added+removed>5)throw Error(trace.name+': more than five cycle repairs required; re-basecall the trace.');
 }
 if(!added&&!removed)return {trace,added,removed};
 const sequence=peaks.map(p=>{const values=TRACE_BASES.map(b=>trace.channels[b][p]||0);return TRACE_BASES[values.indexOf(Math.max(...values))];}).join('');
 return {trace:{...trace,peaks:Uint32Array.from(peaks),sequence,quality:null},added,removed};
}

END MODULE


BEGIN MODULE src/app/workers/core/sanger-dictionary-polish.ts
/** Joint sparse dictionary refit used as an initialization, not a claim of
 * unique allele phasing. Family totals come from the measured-flank fit;
 * the caller subsequently frees every retained weight in the full model. */
export function polishSignalDictionary(columns:number[][],observed:number[],families:string[],selected:number[],initial:number[],sweeps=160){
 const weights=columns.map(()=>0);selected.forEach((id,j)=>weights[id]=initial[j]);
 const groups=new Map<string,{ids:number[];anchor:number}>();for(let id=0;id<columns.length;id++){const key=families[id];let group=groups.get(key);if(!group){group={ids:[],anchor:id};groups.set(key,group);}group.ids.push(id);if(weights[id]>weights[group.anchor])group.anchor=id;}
 const activeGroups=[...groups.values()].filter(g=>g.ids.some(id=>weights[id]>0));
 const sparse=new Map<number,{at:number[];values:number[]}>();for(const g of activeGroups)for(const id of g.ids){const at:number[]=[],values:number[]=[];for(let k=0;k<observed.length;k++){const d=columns[id][k]-columns[g.anchor][k];if(Math.abs(d)>1e-10){at.push(k);values.push(d);}}sparse.set(id,{at,values});}
 for(let outer=0;outer<3;outer++){
  const prediction=observed.map(()=>0);for(let id=0;id<weights.length;id++)if(weights[id]>0)for(let k=0;k<observed.length;k++)prediction[k]+=weights[id]*columns[id][k];
  const totals=Array.from({length:observed.length/4},(_,p)=>Math.max(1e-8,prediction[4*p]+prediction[4*p+1]+prediction[4*p+2]+prediction[4*p+3])),residual=observed.map((v,k)=>v*totals[k>>2]-prediction[k]),rowWeights=observed.map((_,k)=>1/totals[k>>2]**2);
  const gradient=(id:number)=>{const c=sparse.get(id)!;let g=0;for(let j=0;j<c.at.length;j++){const k=c.at[j];g+=c.values[j]*residual[k]*rowWeights[k];}return g;};
  for(let sweep=0;sweep<sweeps;sweep++){let maximumGain=0;
   for(const group of activeGroups){let best=group.anchor,worst=group.ids.find(id=>weights[id]>1e-12)!,bestScore=-Infinity,worstScore=Infinity;
    for(const id of group.ids){const g=gradient(id);if(g>bestScore){best=id;bestScore=g;}if(weights[id]>1e-12&&g<worstScore){worst=id;worstScore=g;}}
    if(best===worst||bestScore-worstScore<1e-9)continue;
    const a=sparse.get(best)!,b=sparse.get(worst)!,at:number[]=[],values:number[]=[];let i=0,j=0,norm=0,rhs=0;
    while(i<a.at.length||j<b.at.length){const k=Math.min(a.at[i]??Infinity,b.at[j]??Infinity),v=(a.at[i]===k?a.values[i++]:0)-(b.at[j]===k?b.values[j++]:0);if(!v)continue;at.push(k);values.push(v);norm+=v*v*rowWeights[k];rhs+=v*residual[k]*rowWeights[k];}
    if(norm<1e-15)continue;const step=Math.max(0,Math.min(weights[worst],rhs/norm));if(step<1e-12)continue;weights[best]+=step;weights[worst]-=step;for(let k=0;k<at.length;k++)residual[at[k]]-=step*values[k];maximumGain=Math.max(maximumGain,2*step*rhs-step*step*norm);
   }
   if(maximumGain<1e-11)break;
  }
 }
 const ids=weights.flatMap((w,id)=>w>=.0005?[id]:[]);return {ids,weights:ids.map(id=>weights[id])};
}

END MODULE


BEGIN MODULE src/app/workers/core/sanger-discrete-phase.ts
import {materialize,PathEdit} from './sanger-path-common';
import {normalizeMoleculeEdits} from './sanger-edit-normalize';
import type{RawSequenceScorer}from './sanger-raw-interval-fit';
import {SangerSequenceFitCache,sequenceFitLoss,sequenceFitTolerance} from './sanger-sequence-fit-cache';
const bases='ACGT';
export interface SangerPersistentOptimizer{options:{set(values:Record<string,number|boolean|string>):unknown};passModel(model:unknown):unknown;setBasis(basis:unknown):unknown;getBasis():unknown;run():{modelStatus:number};getSolution():{colValue:ArrayLike<number>};dispose():void}
export interface SangerLinearOptimizer {solve(program:string,options:Record<string,number|boolean|string>):{Status:string;Columns:Record<string,{Primal:number}>};createModel?:()=>SangerPersistentOptimizer}
/** Variable projection alternates whole-molecule mixture fitting with legal
 * binary allele sequences. Validated integer-ADC data use measured rounding
 * intervals; other inputs retain centroid simplex fitting. A geometric search
 * over group frequencies escapes base/rate stationary points. Equal-length
 * transfers preserve invariant-flank marginals; near-coincident cross-length
 * transfers are bounded proposals requiring a complete free refit. Every proposal is scored
 * against the complete measured signal without truth-derived candidates. */
export function refineDiscretePhase(refs:{sequence:string;cuts:number[]}[],prefix:PathEdit[][][],initial:PathEdit[][][],positions:number[],observed:number[],optimizer:SangerLinearOptimizer,progress:(s:string)=>void=()=>{},target=1,exchange=true,rawScorer?:RawSequenceScorer){
 const profiles=initial.map(gs=>gs.map(es=>es.map(e=>({...e})))),sequences:string[]=[],keys=new Map<string,number>(),models:{h:number;first:number;second:number}[]=[],cache=new SangerSequenceFitCache(sequences,positions,observed,512,rawScorer);
 let previous:{ids:number[];weights:number[]}|undefined;
 const fit=()=>{const ids:number[]=[],where=new Map<number,{h:number;first:number;second:number}>();for(let h=0;h<refs.length;h++)for(let i=0;i<prefix[h].length;i++)for(let j=0;j<profiles[h].length;j++){const sequence=materialize(refs[h].sequence,[...prefix[h][i],...profiles[h][j]])!,key=h+':'+sequence;let id=keys.get(key);if(id===undefined){id=sequences.length;keys.set(key,id);sequences.push(sequence);models.push({h,first:i,second:j});}if(!where.has(id))where.set(id,{h,first:i,second:j});if(!ids.includes(id))ids.push(id);}const old=new Map(previous?.ids.map((id,i)=>[id,previous!.weights[i]])||[]),result=cache.fit(ids,ids.map(id=>old.get(id)||0));previous={ids,weights:result.weights};return{...result,ids,where};};
 let current=fit(),changes=0;const history=[current.rmse];
 const active=(h:number)=>{if(!current.rawActivePositions)return true;const shifts=target===0?[0]:prefix[h].map(es=>es.reduce((n,e)=>n+e.inserted.length-e.end+e.start,0)),cut=refs[h].cuts[target],lo=cut-3+Math.min(...shifts),hi=cut+13+Math.max(...shifts);return current.rawActivePositions.some(p=>p>=lo&&p<=hi);};
 const referenceOrder=()=>refs.map((_,h)=>{const shifts=target===0?[0]:prefix[h].map(es=>es.reduce((n,e)=>n+e.inserted.length-e.end+e.start,0)),cut=refs[h].cuts[target],lo=cut-3+Math.min(...shifts),hi=cut+13+Math.max(...shifts);return{h,error:(current.rawResiduals||[]).reduce((n,r)=>n+(r.position>=lo&&r.position<=hi?r.error:0),0)};}).sort((a,b)=>b.error-a.error||a.h-b.h).map(r=>r.h);
 for(let pass=0;pass<6;pass++){
  if(current.selectionLoss!==undefined&&current.selectionLoss<1e-7)break;
  let changed=false;
  for(const h of referenceOrder()){
   if(!active(h))continue;
   progress(`Joint base assignment · pass ${pass+1} · reference ${h+1} / ${refs.length}`);
   const root=profiles[h],groups=root.map((es,j)=>({events:es,size:es.reduce((s,e)=>s+e.inserted.length-e.end+e.start,0),sequence:materialize(refs[h].sequence,es)!,editable:j!==root.findIndex(es=>es.length===0),weights:new Map<number,number>()}));
   current.ids.forEach((id,k)=>{const m=current.where.get(id)!;if(m.h!==h)return;const shift=target===0?0:prefix[h][m.first].reduce((s,e)=>s+e.inserted.length-e.end+e.start,0),g=groups[m.second];g.weights.set(shift,(g.weights.get(shift)||0)+current.weights[k]);});
   const cut=refs[h].cuts[target],q=Array.from({length:16},(_,i)=>cut-3+i),shifts=groups.flatMap(g=>[...g.weights.keys()]),lo=q[0]+Math.min(...shifts),hi=q.at(-1)!+Math.max(...shifts),rows=positions.flatMap((p,i)=>p>=lo&&p<=hi?[i]:[]),outside=current.ids.flatMap((id,i)=>models[id].h!==h?[{id,w:current.weights[i]}]:[]),prediction=cache.predict(outside.map(x=>x.id),outside.map(x=>x.w)),y=rows.map(i=>observed.slice(4*i,4*i+4).map((v,b)=>v-prediction[4*i+b]));
   const totalBounds=current.rawResponse?rawScorer?.boundsFor?.(rows.map(i=>positions[i]),current.rawResponse):undefined,bounds=totalBounds?{scale:current.rawResponse!.amplitude,lower:totalBounds.lower.map((r,i)=>r.map((v,b)=>v-prediction[4*rows[i]+b])),upper:totalBounds.upper.map((r,i)=>r.map((v,b)=>v-prediction[4*rows[i]+b]))}:undefined;
   const candidate=assignSequenceBases(refs[h].sequence,cut,groups,rows.map(i=>positions[i]),y,optimizer,bounds);
   if(candidate){profiles[h]=candidate;const trial=fit();if(trial.converged&&sequenceFitLoss(trial)<sequenceFitLoss(current)-sequenceFitTolerance(current)){current=trial;history.push(current.rmse);changes++;changed=true;continue;}profiles[h]=root;}
   if(rawScorer&&bounds&&current.rawResponse){
    // Escape a frequency/base coordinate stationary point without changing any
    // measured length marginal. Transfer mass only between equal-length groups,
    // preserving each upstream shift total; then discover all bases jointly.
    progress(`Measured frequency continuation · target ${target+1} · reference ${h+1} / ${refs.length}`);
    const step=.125/current.rawResponse.amplitude,rate=(j:number)=>[...groups[j].weights.values()].reduce((s,v)=>s+v,0);
    frequencySearch:for(let a=0;a<groups.length;a++)for(let b=a+1;b<groups.length;b++)if(groups[a].size===groups[b].size||Math.abs(rate(a)-rate(b))<=4/current.rawResponse.amplitude){
     for(const [donor,receiver]of [[a,b],[b,a]]){const total=rate(donor);if(total<=0)continue;
      // Near-coincident weights of different length classes can exchange
      // base assignments too. Small cross-class perturbations are proposals;
      // only the complete free coefficient refit can accept them. No invariant
      // length marginal is claimed for this temporary cross-class proposal.
      const maxStep=groups[a].size===groups[b].size?total/2:Math.min(total/2,4/current.rawResponse.amplitude);
      for(let delta=step;delta<=maxStep;delta*=2){
       const altered=groups.map(g=>({...g,weights:new Map(g.weights)}));for(const [shift,w]of groups[donor].weights){const move=delta*w/total;altered[donor].weights.set(shift,w-move);altered[receiver].weights.set(shift,(altered[receiver].weights.get(shift)||0)+move);}
       const proposal=assignSequenceBases(refs[h].sequence,cut,altered,rows.map(i=>positions[i]),y,optimizer,bounds,1);if(!proposal)continue;profiles[h]=proposal;const trial=fit();
       if(trial.converged&&sequenceFitLoss(trial)<sequenceFitLoss(current)-sequenceFitTolerance(current)){current=trial;history.push(current.rmse);changes++;changed=true;break frequencySearch;}profiles[h]=root;
      }
     }
    }
   }
  }
  if(rawScorer&&changed)continue;
  if(exchange){
   // Deletions have no mutable bases. Move two legal deletion origins together
   // and refit the whole mixture, so a fixed-weight phase step cannot trap them.
   for(const h of referenceOrder()){
   if(!active(h))continue;
    const root=profiles[h],cut=refs[h].cuts[target],seq=refs[h].sequence;
    const alternatives=root.map(es=>es.length===1&&es[0].inserted===''?
     Array.from({length:7},(_,i)=>{const start=cut-3+i;return[{start,end:start+es[0].end-es[0].start,inserted:''}];})
      .filter(es=>es[0].start>=0&&es[0].end<=seq.length):[es]);
    let best=current,bestState=root;progress(`Testing deletion origins · target ${target+1} · reference ${h+1} / ${refs.length}`);
    for(let a=0;a<root.length;a++)if(alternatives[a].length>1)
     for(let b=a+1;b<root.length;b++)if(alternatives[b].length>1){
      for(const left of alternatives[a])for(const right of alternatives[b]){
       const state=[...root];state[a]=left;state[b]=right;profiles[h]=state;
       const trial=fit();
       if(trial.converged&&sequenceFitLoss(trial)<sequenceFitLoss(best)-sequenceFitTolerance(best)){best=trial;bestState=state;}
      }
     }
    profiles[h]=bestState;
    if(bestState!==root){current=best;history.push(current.rmse);changes++;changed=true;}
   }
   // A phase exchange is scored after refitting all mixture coefficients.
   // This crosses coordinate-stationary points without a multi-level beam.
   for(const h of referenceOrder()){
   if(!active(h))continue;
    const root=profiles[h],seq=refs[h].sequence,cut=refs[h].cuts[target],size=(es:PathEdit[])=>es.reduce((n,e)=>n+e.inserted.length-e.end+e.start,0),mutable=(es:PathEdit[])=>size(es)<0?[]:size(es)>0?Array.from({length:size(es)},(_,i)=>es[0].start+i):Array.from({length:16},(_,i)=>cut-3+i),words=root.map(es=>materialize(seq,es)!),bestRoot=current;
    let best=bestRoot,bestState=root;progress(`Testing coupled base tracts · target ${target+1} · reference ${h+1} / ${refs.length}`);
    const replace=(es:PathEdit[],qs:number[],other:string)=>{const n=size(es),word=materialize(seq,es)!;if(n>0){const start=es[0].start,inserted=[...word.slice(start,start+n)];for(const q of qs)inserted[q-start]=other[q];return[{start,end:start,inserted:inserted.join('')}];}const start=Math.min(...qs,...es.map(e=>e.start)),end=Math.max(...qs.map(q=>q+1),...es.map(e=>e.end)),inserted=[...word.slice(start,end)];for(const q of qs)inserted[q-start]=other[q];const out=normalizeMoleculeEdits(seq,[{start,end,inserted:inserted.join('')}]);if(out.length&&Math.max(cut-3,out.at(-1)!.end-10)>Math.min(cut+3,out[0].start))return null;return out;};
    for(let a=0;a<root.length;a++)if(root[a].length)for(let b=a+1;b<root.length;b++)if(root[b].length){const qs=mutable(root[a]).filter(q=>mutable(root[b]).includes(q)&&words[a][q]!==words[b][q]);for(let lo=0;lo<qs.length;lo++)for(let hi=lo;hi<qs.length;hi++){const tract=qs.slice(lo,hi+1),left=replace(root[a],tract,words[b]),right=replace(root[b],tract,words[a]);if(!left||!right)continue;const state=[...root];state[a]=left;state[b]=right;profiles[h]=state;const trial=fit();if(trial.converged&&sequenceFitLoss(trial)<sequenceFitLoss(best)-sequenceFitTolerance(best)){best=trial;bestState=state;}}}
    profiles[h]=bestState;if(bestState!==root){current=best;history.push(current.rmse);changes++;changed=true;}
   }
  }
  if(!changed)break;
 }
 return{profiles,changes,rmse:current.rmse,history};
}
interface Group {events:PathEdit[];size:number;sequence:string;weights:Map<number,number>;editable?:boolean}
export function assignSequenceBases(sequence:string,cut:number,groups:Group[],positions:number[],observed:number[][],optimizer:SangerLinearOptimizer,intervals?:{lower:number[][];upper:number[][];scale?:number},proposalTimeLimit=10){
 const q=Array.from({length:16},(_,i)=>cut-3+i),index=new Map(q.map((p,i)=>[p,i])),lines=['Minimize'],constraints:string[]=[],binary:string[]=[],cost:string[]=[],checks:{terms:[number,string][];op:string;rhs:number}[]=[];let row=0;
 const term=(coefficient:number,name:string)=>(coefficient<0?'- ':'+ ')+Math.abs(coefficient)+' '+name,add=(terms:[number,string][],op:string,rhs:number)=>{checks.push({terms,op,rhs});constraints.push(' r'+row+++': '+terms.filter(([c])=>c!==0).map(([c,n])=>term(c,n)).join(' ')+' '+op+' '+rhs);},bit=(g:number,k:number,b:number)=>`b${g}_${k}_${b}`,origin=(g:number,i:number)=>`z${g}_${i}`;
 groups.forEach((g,j)=>{
  if(!(g.editable??g.events.length>0))return;const origins=Array.from({length:7},(_,i)=>origin(j,i));binary.push(...origins);add(origins.map(n=>[1,n]),'=',1);
  q.forEach((p,k)=>{const bits=Array.from({length:4},(_,b)=>bit(j,k,b));binary.push(...bits);add(bits.map(n=>[1,n]),'=',1);
   for(let i=0;i<7;i++){const start=cut-3+i;if(start<=p&&p<start+(g.size>0?g.size:g.size===0?10:0))continue;const at=p<start?p:p-g.size,b=bases.indexOf(sequence[at]);if(b<0)throw Error('Incomplete reference coverage in discrete phase.');add([[1,origins[i]],[-1,bits[b]]],'<=',0);}
  });
 });
 positions.forEach((p,i)=>{for(let b=0;b<4;b++){
  const terms:[number,string][]=[],error=`e${i}_${b}`;let fixed=0;
  groups.forEach((g,j)=>{for(const [shift,w]of g.weights){const at=p-shift,k=index.get(at);if((g.editable??g.events.length>0)&&k!==undefined)terms.push([w,bit(j,k,b)]);else if((g.events.length?sequence[at<q[0]?at:at-g.size]:g.sequence[at])===bases[b])fixed+=w;}});
  const scale=intervals?.scale||1,scaled=terms.map(([c,n])=>[c*scale,n]as[number,string]);cost.push(error);add([...scaled,[-1,error]],'<=',((intervals?.upper[i][b]??observed[i][b])-fixed)*scale);add([...scaled,[1,error]],'>=',((intervals?.lower[i][b]??observed[i][b])-fixed)*scale);
 }});
 if(!binary.length)return null;lines.push(' obj: '+cost.join(' + '),'Subject To',...constraints,'Binary',...binary,'End');
 const answer=optimizer.solve(lines.join('\n'),{output_flag:false,time_limit:proposalTimeLimit,mip_rel_gap:1e-7,mip_abs_gap:1e-9,mip_feasibility_tolerance:1e-9,primal_feasibility_tolerance:1e-9});
 const read=(name:string)=>answer.Columns[name]?.Primal||0;
 if(answer.Status!=='Optimal'){
  if(!/time/i.test(answer.Status)||binary.some(name=>!Number.isFinite(answer.Columns[name]?.Primal)||Math.abs(read(name)-Math.round(read(name)))>1e-7))return null;
  for(const c of checks){const value=c.terms.reduce((sum,[a,n])=>sum+a*read(n),0),error=c.op==='='?Math.abs(value-c.rhs):c.op==='<='?value-c.rhs:c.rhs-value;if(error>1e-7)return null;}
