 }

 return groups.map((g,j)=>{if(!(g.editable??g.events.length>0))return[];let choice=0;for(let i=1;i<7;i++)if(read(origin(j,i))>read(origin(j,choice)))choice=i;const start=cut-3+choice,length=g.size>0?g.size:g.size===0?10:0,word=Array.from({length},(_,i)=>{const k=index.get(start+i)!;let dye=0;for(let b=1;b<4;b++)if(read(bit(j,k,b))>read(bit(j,k,dye)))dye=b;return bases[dye];}).join('');return normalizeMoleculeEdits(sequence,[{start,end:start+(g.size<0?-g.size:g.size===0?10:0),inserted:word}]);});
}

END MODULE


BEGIN MODULE src/app/workers/core/sanger-edit-normalize.ts
export interface NormalizableEdit {start:number;end:number;inserted:string}
/** Remove matched context and cancelling edit histories while preserving the
 * final molecule sequence exactly. Does not invent a unique physical repair
 * history or collapse alternative placements through microhomology. */
export function normalizeMoleculeEdits(reference:string,input:NormalizableEdit[]):NormalizableEdit[]{
 let events=input.map(e=>({...e})).sort((a,b)=>a.start-b.start);
 const trim=(e:NormalizableEdit)=>{let {start,end,inserted}=e;while(start<end&&inserted.length&&reference[start]===inserted[0]){start++;inserted=inserted.slice(1);}while(start<end&&inserted.length&&reference[end-1]===inserted.at(-1)){end--;inserted=inserted.slice(0,-1);}return {start,end,inserted};};
 events=events.map(trim).filter(e=>e.end!==e.start||e.inserted.length);
 for(let round=0;round<8;round++){let changed=false;
  outer:for(let i=0;i<events.length;i++)for(let j=events.length-1;j>i;j--){let at=events[i].start,sequence='',valid=true;for(let k=i;k<=j;k++){const e=events[k];if(e.start<at){valid=false;break;}sequence+=reference.slice(at,e.start)+e.inserted;at=e.end;}if(!valid)continue;const end=events[j].end;if(sequence===reference.slice(events[i].start,end)){events.splice(i,j-i+1);changed=true;break outer;}}
  if(!changed)break;
 }
 return events;
}

END MODULE


BEGIN MODULE src/app/workers/core/sanger-emission.ts
import {SangerGroupSupport} from '../../models/sanger-analysis';
import {simplexFit,SimplexFit,mixPhase,phaseNeighbor} from './sanger-signal';
export interface ContextEmission {theta:number[];used:boolean;trainingPositions:number;validationPositions:number;rawValidationMse:number;validationMse:number}
const bases='ACGT';
export function sequenceYield(sequence:string,p:number,theta:number[]):number{const l=bases.indexOf(sequence[p-1]),b=bases.indexOf(sequence[p]),r=bases.indexOf(sequence[p+1]);return Math.exp((l>=0?theta[l]:0)+(r>=0?theta[4+r]:0)+(l>=0&&b>=0&&r>=0?(theta[8+16*l+4*b+r]||0):0));}
export function emissionVector(sequence:string,p:number,lag:number,theta:number[]):number[]{const q=phaseNeighbor(p,lag),current=sequenceYield(sequence,p,theta),neighbor=sequenceYield(sequence,q,theta);return mixPhase([...bases].map(b=>Number(sequence[p]===b)*current),[...bases].map(b=>Number(sequence[q]===b)*neighbor),lag);}
/** Bounded categorical neighbour effects, learned from unedited control only.
 * The exact same model is scored on every-third held-out control position. */
export function fitContextEmission(sequences:string[],positions:number[],observed:number[][],shares:number[],lag:number):ContextEmission{
 const train=positions.filter((_,i)=>i%3!==0),valid=positions.filter((_,i)=>i%3===0),score=(theta:number[],rows:number[])=>{let error=0;for(const p of rows){const predicted=[0,0,0,0];sequences.forEach((s,h)=>emissionVector(s,p,lag,theta).forEach((v,b)=>predicted[b]+=shares[h]*v));const sum=predicted.reduce((a,b)=>a+b,0);for(let b=0;b<4;b++)error+=(observed[p][b]-(sum?predicted[b]/sum:0))**2;}return error/Math.max(1,4*rows.length);};
 const zero=Array(8).fill(0);let theta=[...zero];const objective=(v:number[])=>score(v,train)+.00002*v.reduce((s,x)=>s+x*x,0);
 if(train.length>=80&&valid.length>=30)for(const step of [.08,.04,.02,.01,.005])for(let sweep=0;sweep<2;sweep++)for(let k=0;k<8;k++)for(const sign of [-1,1]){const trial=[...theta];trial[k]+=sign*step;for(const offset of [0,4]){const mean=trial.slice(offset,offset+4).reduce((a,b)=>a+b,0)/4;for(let j=offset;j<offset+4;j++)trial[j]-=mean;}if(trial.some(x=>Math.abs(x)>.25))continue;if(objective(trial)<objective(theta))theta=trial;}
 const raw=score(zero,valid),fitted=score(theta,valid),used=fitted<raw*.95&&fitted+1e-6<raw;
 return {theta:used?theta:zero,used,trainingPositions:train.length,validationPositions:valid.length,rawValidationMse:raw,validationMse:used?fitted:raw};
}
/** Normalized fluorescence from unnormalized template emissions. Iteratively
 * update cycle totals; no truth fractions or fixed allele sparsity threshold. */
export function fitEmissionMixture(columns:number[][],observed:number[],positionWeights?:number[],maxIterations=24,initialWeights?:number[]):SimplexFit{
 const n=observed.length,totals=columns.map(c=>Array.from({length:n/4},(_,p)=>c.slice(4*p,4*p+4).reduce((s,v)=>s+v,0)));
 const evaluate=(w:number[])=>{const predicted=Array(n).fill(0),sum=Array(n/4).fill(0);for(let i=0;i<columns.length;i++)if(w[i])for(let p=0;p<n/4;p++){sum[p]+=w[i]*totals[i][p];for(let b=0;b<4;b++)predicted[4*p+b]+=w[i]*columns[i][4*p+b];}let loss=0,raw=0;for(let k=0;k<n;k++){predicted[k]=sum[k>>2]?predicted[k]/sum[k>>2]:0;const residual=predicted[k]-observed[k];raw+=residual*residual;loss+=(positionWeights?.[k>>2]??1)*residual*residual;}return {predicted,sum,loss,raw};};
 let weights=initialWeights?[...initialWeights]:simplexFit(columns.map((c,i)=>c.map((v,k)=>totals[i][Math.floor(k/4)]?v/totals[i][Math.floor(k/4)]:0)),observed,positionWeights).weights,current=evaluate(weights),converged=false,optimalityGap=Infinity;
 for(let iteration=0;iteration<maxIterations;iteration++){
  // The simplex-constrained Gauss–Newton problem uses the derivative of the
  // NORMALIZED four-channel emission, including each molecule's cycle total.
  const linear=columns.map((c,i)=>c.map((v,k)=>current.predicted[k]+(current.sum[k>>2]?(v-current.predicted[k]*totals[i][k>>2])/current.sum[k>>2]:0)));
  const gradient=linear.map(c=>c.reduce((sum,v,k)=>sum+(positionWeights?.[k>>2]??1)*(v-current.predicted[k])*(current.predicted[k]-observed[k]),0));
  optimalityGap=(weights.reduce((s,w,i)=>s+w*gradient[i],0)-Math.min(...gradient))/Math.max(1,n);
  if(optimalityGap<1e-10){converged=true;break;}
  const proposal=simplexFit(linear,observed,positionWeights,weights);let step=1,accepted=false;
  while(step>=1/4096){const next=weights.map((w,i)=>w+step*(proposal.weights[i]-w)),value=evaluate(next);if(value.loss<current.loss-1e-14){weights=next;current=value;accepted=true;break;}step*=.5;}
  if(!accepted){converged=optimalityGap<1e-8;break;}
 }
 const mean=observed.reduce((s,v)=>s+v,0)/n,variance=observed.reduce((s,v)=>s+(v-mean)**2,0);
 return {weights,r2:variance?1-current.raw/variance:0,rmse:Math.sqrt(current.raw/n),converged,optimalityGap};
}

export interface EmissionSupportOptions {refit?:boolean;templateResidual?:number[]}
export interface EmissionSupportEvidence {group:number[];threshold:number;mass:number;standardError:number;score:number}
/** Support on the simplex tangent space, with cycle-block residual covariance.
 * A newly proposed length pays for its searched junction origins. Reference
 * allocation remains an estimate: ambiguity between homeologs is not evidence
 * that an edit is absent. Refit retained lengths without a fraction penalty.
 * This engineering screen is not a confidence interval. */
export function screenEmissionSupport(columns:number[][],observed:number[],fit:SimplexFit,mandatory:number[],positionWeights?:number[],groups?:number[][],originHypotheses?:number[],options:EmissionSupportOptions={}):{fit:SimplexFit;removed:number[];threshold:number;scores?:EmissionSupportEvidence[]}{
 const {templateResidual}=options;
 const n=observed.length,active=fit.weights.flatMap((w,i)=>w>1e-9||mandatory.includes(i)?[i]:[]),pivot=active.reduce((a,b)=>fit.weights[a]>fit.weights[b]?a:b),indices=active.filter(i=>i!==pivot),p=indices.length,threshold=Math.sqrt(Math.log(Math.max(3,n)));
 if(p<1)return {fit,removed:[],threshold};
 const jac=indices.map(()=>new Float64Array(n)),residual=new Float64Array(n),rowWeight=(k:number)=>positionWeights?.[Math.floor(k/4)]??1;let sse=0;
 for(let j=0;j<n/4;j++){const vector=Array.from({length:4},(_,b)=>columns.reduce((s,c,h)=>s+c[j*4+b]*fit.weights[h],0)),total=vector.reduce((a,b)=>a+b,0),totals=columns.map(c=>c.slice(j*4,j*4+4).reduce((a,b)=>a+b,0));if(!total)continue;for(let b=0;b<4;b++){const pred=vector[b]/total;residual[j*4+b]=observed[j*4+b]-pred;sse+=rowWeight(j*4+b)*residual[j*4+b]**2;indices.forEach((i,k)=>{jac[k][j*4+b]=(columns[i][j*4+b]-pred*totals[i]-columns[pivot][j*4+b]+pred*totals[pivot])/total;});}}
 const matrix=Array.from({length:p},(_,i)=>Float64Array.from({length:2*p},(_,j)=>j<p?jac[i].reduce((s,v,k)=>s+rowWeight(k)*v*jac[j][k],0):Number(j-p===i)));
 for(let k=0;k<p;k++){let pivotRow=k;for(let i=k+1;i<p;i++)if(Math.abs(matrix[i][k])>Math.abs(matrix[pivotRow][k]))pivotRow=i;[matrix[k],matrix[pivotRow]]=[matrix[pivotRow],matrix[k]];const value=matrix[k][k];if(Math.abs(value)<1e-8)return {fit,removed:[],threshold};for(let j=0;j<2*p;j++)matrix[k][j]/=value;for(let i=0;i<p;i++)if(i!==k){const scale=matrix[i][k];for(let j=0;j<2*p;j++)matrix[i][j]-=scale*matrix[k][j];}}
 const variance=sse/Math.max(1,n-active.length);
 // Include the dependent pivot when testing group mass. Cycle-block scores
 // retain correlations between dyes and nearby bases, unlike IID residuals.
 const scores:EmissionSupportEvidence[]=[];const removed=groups?groups.filter(group=>!group.some(i=>mandatory.includes(i))).flatMap(group=>{
  const coefficients=indices.map(i=>Number(group.includes(i))-Number(group.includes(pivot)));
  const v=coefficients.reduce((sum,a,k)=>sum+a*coefficients.reduce((s,b,l)=>s+b*matrix[k][p+l],0),0);
  const mass=group.reduce((sum,i)=>sum+fit.weights[i],0);
  const direction=indices.map((_,k)=>coefficients.reduce((s,a,l)=>s+matrix[k][p+l]*a,0));
  const blockLength=Math.max(2,Math.ceil(Math.cbrt(n/4))),blocks=Array.from({length:Math.ceil(n/4/blockLength)},()=>0);
  for(let k=0;k<n;k++)blocks[Math.floor(k/4/blockLength)]+=rowWeight(k)*residual[k]*direction.reduce((sum,a,l)=>sum+a*jac[l][k],0);
  const mean=blocks.reduce((s,x)=>s+x,0)/blocks.length;
  const robustVariance=blocks.reduce((s,x)=>s+(x-mean)**2,0)*blocks.length/Math.max(1,blocks.length-1);
  // WT-control model error is independent of the edited-sample fit. A false
  // class can absorb systematic template error and leave very little sample
  // residual, so residual-only precision can be spuriously high.
  let templateVariance=0;
  if(templateResidual){
   const controlBlocks=blocks.map(()=>0);
   for(let k=0;k<n;k++)controlBlocks[Math.floor(k/4/blockLength)]+=rowWeight(k)*templateResidual[k]*direction.reduce((sum,a,l)=>sum+a*jac[l][k],0);
   const bias=controlBlocks.reduce((s,x)=>s+x,0),average=bias/controlBlocks.length;
   templateVariance=Math.max(bias*bias,controlBlocks.reduce((s,x)=>s+(x-average)**2,0)*controlBlocks.length/Math.max(1,controlBlocks.length-1));
  }
  const groupThreshold=Math.sqrt(threshold**2+2*Math.log(Math.max(1,...group.map(i=>originHypotheses?.[i]??1))));scores.push({group,threshold:groupThreshold,mass,standardError:Math.sqrt(Math.max(0,Math.max(variance*v,robustVariance,templateVariance))),score:mass/Math.max(1e-12,Math.sqrt(Math.max(0,Math.max(variance*v,robustVariance,templateVariance))))});return mass<groupThreshold*Math.sqrt(Math.max(0,Math.max(variance*v,robustVariance,templateVariance)))?group.filter(i=>active.includes(i)):[];
 }):indices.filter((i,k)=>!mandatory.includes(i)&&fit.weights[i]<threshold*Math.sqrt(Math.max(0,variance*matrix[k][p+k])));
 const uniqueRemoved=[...new Set(removed)];removed.splice(0,removed.length,...uniqueRemoved);const keep=active.filter(i=>!removed.includes(i));
 if(options.refit===false||!removed.length)return {fit,removed,threshold,scores};
 const refit=fitEmissionMixture(keep.map(i=>columns[i]),observed,positionWeights),weights=Array(columns.length).fill(0);keep.forEach((i,k)=>weights[i]=refit.weights[k]);return {fit:{...refit,weights},removed,threshold,scores};
}

/** Polish the actual normalized-fluorescence objective when fixed-point scaling
 * stalls, notably when short edited products terminate within the fit region.
 * Uses projected gradients with backtracking; no truth or extra edit models. */
export function fitNormalizedEmissionMixture(columns:number[][],observed:number[]):SimplexFit{
 const initial=fitEmissionMixture(columns,observed,undefined,64);if(initial.converged)return initial;
 const project=(values:number[])=>{const sorted=[...values].sort((a,b)=>b-a);let sum=0,threshold=0;for(let k=0;k<sorted.length;k++){sum+=sorted[k];threshold=(sum-1)/(k+1);if(k===sorted.length-1||sorted[k+1]<=threshold)break;}return values.map(v=>Math.max(0,v-threshold));};
 const totals=columns.map(c=>Array.from({length:observed.length/4},(_,p)=>c.slice(4*p,4*p+4).reduce((s,v)=>s+v,0)));
 const evaluate=(weights:number[],gradient=false)=>{let error=0;const g=weights.map(()=>0);for(let p=0;p<observed.length/4;p++){const v=[0,0,0,0];for(let h=0;h<columns.length;h++)for(let b=0;b<4;b++)v[b]+=weights[h]*columns[h][4*p+b];const total=v.reduce((s,x)=>s+x,0);for(let b=0;b<4;b++){const predicted=total?v[b]/total:0,residual=predicted-observed[4*p+b];error+=residual*residual;if(gradient&&total>1e-9)for(let h=0;h<columns.length;h++)g[h]+=2*residual*(columns[h][4*p+b]-predicted*totals[h][p])/total;}}return {loss:error/observed.length,gradient:g.map(v=>v/observed.length)};};
 let weights=[...initial.weights],value=evaluate(weights).loss,step=1,converged=false;
 for(let iteration=0;iteration<600;iteration++){
  const current=evaluate(weights,true);let accepted=false,next=weights,nextValue=value;
  for(let trial=0;trial<24;trial++){next=project(weights.map((w,k)=>w-step*current.gradient[k]));nextValue=evaluate(next).loss;const descent=next.reduce((sum,w,k)=>sum+current.gradient[k]*(w-weights[k]),0);if(nextValue<=value+1e-4*descent+1e-15){accepted=true;break;}step*=.5;}
  if(!accepted)break;const delta=Math.max(...next.map((w,k)=>Math.abs(w-weights[k])));weights=next;value=nextValue;
  const mapping=project(weights.map((w,k)=>w-current.gradient[k]));if(Math.max(...mapping.map((w,k)=>Math.abs(w-weights[k])))<1e-7&&delta<1e-6){converged=true;break;}step=Math.min(100,step*1.4);
 }
 const mean=observed.reduce((s,v)=>s+v,0)/observed.length,variance=observed.reduce((s,v)=>s+(v-mean)**2,0)/observed.length;
 return {weights,rmse:Math.sqrt(value),r2:variance?1-value/variance:0,converged};
}

/** Residual-based engineering evidence; not a probability of a real edit.
 * Missing/singular evidence stays unresolved and never inherits a QC pass. */
export function emissionGroupSupport(scores:EmissionSupportEvidence[]|undefined,index:number,prediction?:{foldGains:number[];passed:boolean}):SangerGroupSupport {
 const evidence=scores?.find(s=>s.group.includes(index));
 return {status:evidence&&evidence.mass>0&&evidence.score>=evidence.threshold&&(!prediction||prediction.passed)?'supported':'unresolved',score:evidence?.score??null,requiredScore:evidence?.threshold??null,scope:'reference-length',...(prediction?{prediction}:{})};
}

END MODULE


BEGIN MODULE src/app/workers/core/sanger-exact-alternatives.ts
import {normalizeMoleculeEdits} from './sanger-edit-normalize';
import {SangerAnnotation,SangerExactAlternatives,SangerExactOption,SangerJointEvent} from '../../models/sanger-analysis';
import {sangerSequenceKey} from '../../utils/sanger-sequence-groups';
import {exactFromNumber,exactHaplotypeRepresentatives,exactSum,exactWeightedEquality} from './exact-mixture-fiber';
import {materialize,Model,PathEdit,Prepared} from './sanger-path-common';
interface Row {model:Model;fraction:number}
/** Same-site insertion haplotype fibers and identical-sequence deletion placements. Every offered representative is
 * certified under the complete fitted emitter, including context and phase.
 * This enumerates verified representatives, not all possible allele solutions. */
export function discoverExactAlternatives(refs:Prepared[],annotations:SangerAnnotation[],rows:Row[][],toInput:(h:number,events:PathEdit[])=>SangerJointEvent[],emit:(sequence:string,p:number)=>number[],optionLimit=16):SangerExactAlternatives[]{
 const sets:SangerExactAlternatives[]=[];
 for(let h=0;h<refs.length;h++){
  const ref=refs[h],source=rows[h],annotation=annotations[h];if(!annotation.share||source.length!==annotation.components.length)continue;
  const base:SangerExactOption={id:'fit',components:annotation.components.map(c=>({...c})),exactFractions:source.map(c=>exactFromNumber(c.fraction))},options=[base],seen=new Set<string>(),columnCache=new Map<string,number[]>();
  const columnLength=Math.max(ref.sequence.length,...source.map(row=>row.model.sequence.length))+2;
  const column=(sequence:string)=>{let col=columnCache.get(sequence);if(!col){col=Array.from({length:columnLength},(_,p)=>emit(sequence,p)).flat();columnCache.set(sequence,col);}return col;};

  for(let t=0;t<ref.cuts.length&&options.length<optionLimit;t++){
   const targetLimit=1+Math.floor((optionLimit-1)*(t+1)/ref.cuts.length);
   const families=new Map<string,{indices:number[];background:PathEdit[];kind:'insert';start:number;end:number;length:number}>();
   source.forEach((row,index)=>{
    const nearest=(e:PathEdit)=>ref.cuts.map(c=>Math.min(Math.abs(e.start-c),Math.abs(e.end-c))).reduce((best,d,i,ds)=>d<ds[best]?i:best,0),local=row.model.events.filter(e=>nearest(e)===t),background=row.model.events.filter(e=>nearest(e)!==t);if(local.length>1||row.model.sequence.includes('N')||row.fraction<=0||annotation.components[index].unresolved)return;
    const e=local[0];if(!e||e.start!==e.end||!e.inserted.length)return;
    const kind='insert' as const,key=JSON.stringify(background)+':'+e.start+':'+e.inserted.length;let family=families.get(key);if(!family){family={indices:[],background,kind,start:e.start,end:e.end,length:e.inserted.length};families.set(key,family);}family.indices.push(index);

   });
   for(const family of families.values()){
    if(options.length>=targetLimit)break;if(family.indices.length<2||!Number.isFinite(family.start)||family.end<family.start)continue;
    const {start,end,background}=family,length=family.length;if(!length||length>40)continue;
    const localWords=family.indices.map(index=>{const row=source[index],local=row.model.events.filter(e=>!background.some(b=>JSON.stringify(b)===JSON.stringify(e))),sequence=materialize(ref.sequence.slice(start,end),local.map(e=>({...e,start:e.start-start,end:e.end-start})));return {sequence:sequence||'',weight:row.fraction};});
    if(localWords.some(w=>w.sequence.length!==length))continue;const reps=exactHaplotypeRepresentatives(localWords,optionLimit),old=family.indices.map(index=>({weight:exactFromNumber(source[index].fraction),column:column(source[index].model.sequence)}));
    for(const rep of reps.options.slice(1)){
     if(options.length>=targetLimit)break;const replacement=rep.map(word=>{const events=normalizeMoleculeEdits(ref.sequence,[...background,{start,end,inserted:word.sequence}]),sequence=materialize(ref.sequence,events);return {word,events,sequence};});if(replacement.some(r=>!r.sequence))continue;
     if(!exactWeightedEquality(old,replacement.map(r=>({weight:r.word.exactWeight,column:column(r.sequence!)}))))continue;
     const remove=new Set(family.indices),keep=source.flatMap((_,i)=>remove.has(i)?[]:[i]),components=keep.map(i=>base.components[i]),exactFractions=keep.map(i=>base.exactFractions[i]);
     for(const r of replacement){const events=toInput(h,r.events),sizes=(ref.input.cuts??[ref.input.cut]).map((_,target)=>events.filter(e=>e.target===target+1||e.target===0).reduce((sum,e)=>sum+e.inserted-(e.end-e.start),0));components.push({size:sizes.reduce((a,b)=>a+b,0),pathSizes:sizes,fraction:r.word.weight/annotation.share,events});exactFractions.push(r.word.exactWeight);}
     const key=JSON.stringify(components.map((c,i)=>[sangerSequenceKey(annotation.reference,c.events||[]),exactFractions[i]]).sort((a,b)=>String(a[0]).localeCompare(String(b[0]))));if(seen.has(key))continue;seen.add(key);options.push({id:'equivalent-'+options.length,components,exactFractions});
    }
   }
  }
  for(let i=0;i<source.length&&options.length<optionLimit;i++)for(const alias of source[i].model.aliases){
   if(annotation.components[i].unresolved||alias.reference!==h||materialize(ref.sequence,alias.events)!==source[i].model.sequence)continue;
   const events=toInput(h,alias.events);if(JSON.stringify(events)===JSON.stringify(base.components[i].events||[]))continue;
   const components=base.components.map((c,j)=>j===i?{...c,events}:c),key='placement:'+JSON.stringify(components.map(c=>c.events));if(seen.has(key))continue;seen.add(key);options.push({id:'placement-'+options.length,components,exactFractions:base.exactFractions});if(options.length>=optionLimit)break;
  }
  if(options.length<2)continue;const ambiguousSequences:Record<string,string[]>={},ambiguousPlacements:Record<string,string[]>={},targets:number[]=[];
  for(let t=0;t<(annotation.cuts?.length||1);t++){
   const maps=options.map(option=>{const values=new Map<string,typeof option.exactFractions>();option.components.forEach((c,i)=>{const key=sangerSequenceKey(annotation.reference,(c.events||[]).filter(e=>e.target===t+1||e.target===0));values.set(key,[...(values.get(key)||[]),option.exactFractions[i]]);});return new Map([...values].map(([key,v])=>[key,exactSum(v)]));}),keys=[...new Set(maps.flatMap(m=>[...m.keys()]))],changed=keys.filter(key=>new Set(maps.map(m=>{const v=m.get(key)||{n:'0',d:'1'};return v.n+'/'+v.d;})).size>1);
   const placementMaps=options.map(option=>{const m=new Map<string,string[]>();for(const c of option.components){const es=(c.events||[]).filter(e=>e.target===t+1||e.target===0),key=sangerSequenceKey(annotation.reference,es);m.set(key,[...(m.get(key)||[]),JSON.stringify(es)]);}return m;}),placementKeys=keys.filter(key=>placementMaps.every(m=>m.has(key))&&new Set(placementMaps.map(m=>JSON.stringify([...new Set(m.get(key)!)].sort()))).size>1);
   if(changed.length||placementKeys.length){targets.push(t+1);ambiguousSequences[String(t+1)]=changed;ambiguousPlacements[String(t+1)]=placementKeys;}
  }
  if(targets.length)sets.push({reference:annotation.name,certificate:'exact-rational-emission',selected:-1,options,targets,ambiguousSequences,ambiguousPlacements,exhaustive:false});
 }
 return sets;
}

END MODULE


BEGIN MODULE src/app/workers/core/sanger-fit-cache.ts
import{activeSimplex}from'./simplex-active-set';
import {SimplexFit} from './sanger-signal';
import {fitNormalizedEmissionMixture} from './sanger-emission';
/** Immutable waveforms/observations allow reusable fits and Gram products.
 * When all candidates have the SAME cycle total, normalization is independent
 * of the mixture weights and the full problem is exactly simplex least squares.
 * Unequal dye yields or product termination retain the general nonlinear fit. */
export class SangerFitCache {
 private fits=new Map<string,SimplexFit>();private gram=new Map<number,number>();private compatible:boolean[]=[];private inverse:number[];private variance:number;
 hits=0;misses=0;linearFits=0;nonlinearFits=0;pairHits=0;pairMisses=0;
 constructor(private columns:number[][],private observed:number[],private limit=512){this.inverse=Array.from({length:observed.length/4},(_,p)=>{const c=columns[0],k=4*p,total=c[k]+c[k+1]+c[k+2]+c[k+3];return total?1/total:0;});const mean=observed.reduce((s,v)=>s+v,0)/observed.length;this.variance=observed.reduce((s,v)=>s+(v-mean)**2,0);}
 private sharedTotal(id:number){if(this.compatible[id]!==undefined)return this.compatible[id];const c=this.columns[id],base=this.columns[0];for(let k=0;k<c.length;k+=4)if(Math.abs(c[k]+c[k+1]+c[k+2]+c[k+3]-(base[k]+base[k+1]+base[k+2]+base[k+3]))>1e-12)return this.compatible[id]=false;return this.compatible[id]=true;}
 private product(a:number,b:number){const key=Math.max(a,b)*1048576+Math.min(a,b),old=this.gram.get(key);if(old!==undefined){this.pairHits++;this.gram.delete(key);this.gram.set(key,old);return old;}this.pairMisses++;const x=this.columns[a],y=this.columns[b];let value=0;for(let k=0;k<this.observed.length;k++)value+=(x[k]*this.inverse[k>>2]-this.observed[k])*(y[k]*this.inverse[k>>2]-this.observed[k]);this.gram.set(key,value);if(this.gram.size>200000)this.gram.delete(this.gram.keys().next().value!);return value;}
 private linear(ids:number[],initial?:number[]):SimplexFit{
  const p=ids.length,n=this.observed.length,G=ids.map((a,i)=>Float64Array.from({length:p},(_,j)=>j<=i?this.product(a,ids[j]):0));for(let i=0;i<p;i++)for(let j=0;j<i;j++)G[j][i]=G[i][j];
  const solved=activeSimplex(G,n,1e-13,initial),weights=solved.weights,converged=solved.converged,gap=solved.optimalityGap;
  let error=0;for(let k=0;k<n;k++){let predicted=0;for(let j=0;j<p;j++)predicted+=weights[j]*this.columns[ids[j]][k];error+=(this.observed[k]-predicted*this.inverse[k>>2])**2;}
  return {weights,rmse:Math.sqrt(error/n),r2:this.variance?1-error/this.variance:0,converged,optimalityGap:gap};
 }
 fit(ids:number[],initial?:number[]):SimplexFit{const key=ids.join('/'),hit=this.fits.get(key);if(hit){this.hits++;this.fits.delete(key);this.fits.set(key,hit);return hit;}this.misses++;let result:SimplexFit;if(ids.every(id=>id<1048576&&this.sharedTotal(id))){this.linearFits++;result=this.linear(ids,initial);if(!result.converged){this.nonlinearFits++;result=fitNormalizedEmissionMixture(ids.map(id=>this.columns[id]),this.observed);}}else{this.nonlinearFits++;result=fitNormalizedEmissionMixture(ids.map(id=>this.columns[id]),this.observed);}this.fits.set(key,result);if(this.fits.size>this.limit)this.fits.delete(this.fits.keys().next().value!);return result;}
}
/** Same raw-dye loss as rawSignalScore, without allocating QC statistics for
 * every trial. Detailed per-cycle QC is still computed for the final fit. */
export function rawMixtureMse(columns:number[][],observed:number[],weights:number[],gains:number[]){let error=0;for(let p=0;p<observed.length;p+=4){let a=0,c=0,g=0,t=0;for(let j=0;j<columns.length;j++){const col=columns[j],w=weights[j];a+=w*col[p];c+=w*col[p+1];g+=w*col[p+2];t+=w*col[p+3];}a*=gains[0];c*=gains[1];g*=gains[2];t*=gains[3];const total=a+c+g+t;if(total){a/=total;c/=total;g/=total;t/=total;}error+=(observed[p]-a)**2+(observed[p+1]-c)**2+(observed[p+2]-g)**2+(observed[p+3]-t)**2;}return error/observed.length;}

END MODULE


BEGIN MODULE src/app/workers/core/sanger-frequency-path-candidates.ts
import{Prepared,PathEdit,materialize}from'./sanger-path-common';import{normalizeMoleculeEdits}from'./sanger-edit-normalize';import{SangerFitCache}from'./sanger-fit-cache';
interface Atom {size:number;fraction:number}
interface Family extends Atom {reference:number}
interface Allocation {cost:number;code:number}
const bases='ACGT';
function subsetSums(atoms:Atom[]){const sums=[0];for(const a of atoms){const n=sums.length;for(let i=0;i<n;i++)sums.push(sums[i]+a.fraction);}return sums;}
const nearest=(values:number[],v:number)=>values.reduce((best,x)=>Math.min(best,Math.abs(v-x)),Infinity);
function refineAtoms(input:Atom[],levels:number[],tolerance:number){
 let atoms=input.map(a=>({...a}));const families=[...new Set(atoms.map(a=>a.size))],anchors=new Map(families.map(size=>[size,atoms.findIndex(a=>a.size===size)])),variables=atoms.flatMap((a,i)=>anchors.get(a.size)===i?[]:[i]);if(!variables.length)return atoms;
 const totals=new Map(families.map(size=>[size,atoms.filter(a=>a.size===size).reduce((s,a)=>s+a.fraction,0)]));
 for(let iteration=0;iteration<12;iteration++){const sums=subsetSums(atoms),n=variables.length,matrix=Array.from({length:n},()=>Array(n+1).fill(0));let count=0;
  for(const value of levels){let mask=0;for(let k=1;k<sums.length;k++)if(Math.abs(sums[k]-value)<Math.abs(sums[mask]-value))mask=k;if(Math.abs(sums[mask]-value)>2*tolerance)continue;const row=variables.map(i=>Number(!!(mask&(1<<i)))-Number(!!(mask&(1<<anchors.get(atoms[i].size)!)))),base=families.reduce((s,size)=>s+((mask&(1<<anchors.get(size)!))?totals.get(size)!:0),0),rhs=value-base;for(let i=0;i<n;i++){matrix[i][n]+=row[i]*rhs;for(let j=0;j<n;j++)matrix[i][j]+=row[i]*row[j];}count++;}if(count<n)break;
  let valid=true;for(let c=0;c<n;c++){let pivot=c;for(let r=c+1;r<n;r++)if(Math.abs(matrix[r][c])>Math.abs(matrix[pivot][c]))pivot=r;if(Math.abs(matrix[pivot][c])<1e-10){valid=false;break;}[matrix[c],matrix[pivot]]=[matrix[pivot],matrix[c]];const d=matrix[c][c];for(let j=c;j<=n;j++)matrix[c][j]/=d;for(let r=0;r<n;r++)if(r!==c){const f=matrix[r][c];for(let j=c;j<=n;j++)matrix[r][j]-=f*matrix[c][j];}}
  if(!valid)break;const next=atoms.map(a=>({...a}));variables.forEach((id,i)=>next[id].fraction=matrix[i][n]);for(const size of families)next[anchors.get(size)!].fraction=totals.get(size)!-variables.filter(i=>atoms[i].size===size).reduce((s,i)=>s+next[i].fraction,0);if(next.some(a=>a.fraction<=0))break;const change=Math.max(...next.map((a,i)=>Math.abs(a.fraction-atoms[i].fraction)));atoms=next;if(change<1e-9)break;
 }
 return atoms;
}
/** Enumerate bounded edit origins while caching the per-cycle allocation of
 * a small number of frequency atoms to A/C/G/T. This enumerates groups, not
 * every possible inserted string (4^insertionLength). */
function decodePaths(ref:Prepared,atoms:Atom[],y:number[][],radius:number,span:number,maxVariants:number){
 const cut=ref.cuts[0],begin=cut-radius,n=y.length,zero=atoms.flatMap((a,i)=>a.size===0?[i]:[]),choices:[number|null,...number[]]=[null,...zero];let best:{cost:number;groups:{events:PathEdit[];fraction:number}[]}|undefined;
 for(const wt of choices){const variants=atoms.flatMap((_,i)=>i===wt?[]:[i]);if(variants.length>maxVariants)continue;const cache=Array.from({length:n},()=>new Map<number,Allocation>()),states=variants.map(id=>Array.from({length:2*radius+1},(_,s)=>Array.from({length:n},(_,j)=>{const p=begin+j,start=cut-radius+s,a=atoms[id];return(a.size===0?p>=start&&p<start+span?4:bases.indexOf(ref.sequence[p]):a.size>0?p<start?bases.indexOf(ref.sequence[p]):p<start+a.size?4:bases.indexOf(ref.sequence[p-a.size]):bases.indexOf(ref.sequence[p<start?p:p-a.size]))<<(3*id);}))),origins:number[]=Array(variants.length).fill(0);
  const allocate=(j:number,key:number)=>{const hit=cache[j].get(key);if(hit)return hit;const fixed=[0,0,0,0],free:number[]=[];let baseCode=0;for(let i=0;i<atoms.length;i++){const b=(key>>(3*i))&7;if(b===4)free.push(i);else{fixed[b]+=atoms[i].fraction;baseCode|=b<<(2*i);}}let bestCost=Infinity,bestCode=baseCode;for(let code=0;code<4**free.length;code++){const v=[...fixed];let full=baseCode;for(let k=0;k<free.length;k++){const b=(code>>(2*k))&3;v[b]+=atoms[free[k]].fraction;full|=b<<(2*free[k]);}let cost=0;for(let b=0;b<4;b++)cost+=(y[j][b]-v[b])**2;if(cost<bestCost){bestCost=cost;bestCode=full;}}const value={cost:bestCost,code:bestCode};cache[j].set(key,value);return value;};
  const evaluate=()=>{const codes:number[]=[];let cost=0;for(let j=0;j<n;j++){let key=wt===null?0:bases.indexOf(ref.sequence[begin+j])<<(3*wt);for(let k=0;k<variants.length;k++)key|=states[k][origins[k]][j];const a=allocate(j,key);cost+=a.cost;if(best&&cost>best.cost)return;codes.push(a.code);}const groups=atoms.map((a,id)=>{if(id===wt)return{events:[],fraction:a.fraction};const k=variants.indexOf(id),start=cut-radius+origins[k],length=a.size>0?a.size:a.size===0?span:0,inserted=Array.from({length},(_,j)=>bases[(codes[start-begin+j]>>(2*id))&3]).join(''),events=normalizeMoleculeEdits(ref.sequence,[{start,end:start+(a.size<0?-a.size:a.size===0?span:0),inserted}]);return{events,fraction:a.fraction};});if(!best||cost<best.cost)best={cost,groups};};
  const visit=(k:number)=>{if(k===variants.length){evaluate();return;}for(let s=0;s<=2*radius;s++){origins[k]=s;visit(k+1);}};visit(0);
 }
 return best;
}
/** Frequency refinement never merges groups because their percentages are
 * alike. Observational levels propose splits of a length class; the complete
 * sequence model and raw reconstruction must independently validate them. */
export function collectFrequencyPaths(refs:Prepared[],families:Family[],positions:number[],observed:number[],noise:number,radius=3,span=10,maxVariants=5,maxStates=10000,progress:(stage:string)=>void=()=>{}){
 const index=new Map(positions.map((p,i)=>[p,i])),groups:{reference:number;events:PathEdit[];fraction:number}[]=[],details:any[]=[],candidates:{reference:number;groups:{events:PathEdit[];fraction:number}[]}[]=[],candidateKeys=new Set<string>();
 for(let h=0;h<refs.length;h++){
  progress(`Decoding offset edits · reference ${h+1} / ${refs.length}`);
  const ref=refs[h],cut=ref.cuts[0],begin=cut-radius,end=cut+radius+Math.max(span,...families.filter(f=>f.reference===h).map(f=>f.size)),own=families.filter(f=>f.reference===h&&f.fraction>0),share=own.reduce((s,a)=>s+a.fraction,0),tolerance=Math.max(1e-5,4*noise),minimum=share*.01;
  const other=(p:number)=>{const v=[0,0,0,0];for(const f of families)if(f.reference!==h&&f.fraction>0){const r=refs[f.reference],c=r.cuts[0],b=bases.indexOf(r.sequence[p<c-radius?p:p-f.size]);if(b>=0)v[b]+=f.fraction;}return v;};
  const y=Array.from({length:end-begin},(_,j)=>{const p=begin+j,k=index.get(p);if(k===undefined)throw Error('Missing local trace coverage.');for(const [i,r]of refs.entries())if(i!==h&&p>=r.cuts[0]-radius&&p<r.cuts[0]+radius+Math.max(span,10))throw Error('Local windows overlap.');const bg=other(p);return bg.map((v,b)=>observed[4*k+b]-v);}),values=y.flat().filter(v=>v>tolerance&&v<share-tolerance).sort((a,b)=>a-b),clusters:number[][]=[];
  for(const v of values){const last=clusters.at(-1);if(last&&v-last[0]<=tolerance/2)last.push(v);else clusters.push([v]);}const levels=clusters.map(c=>c.reduce((a,b)=>a+b,0)/c.length),key=(a:Atom[])=>a.map(x=>x.size+':'+x.fraction.toFixed(7)).sort().join('/'),ordered=(a:Atom[])=>a.sort((a,b)=>a.size-b.size||b.fraction-a.fraction);
  interface State {atoms:Atom[];missing:number[];error:number}
  const queue:State[]=[],seen=new Set<string>();const enqueue=(atoms:Atom[])=>{atoms=ordered(atoms);atoms=ordered(refineAtoms(atoms,values,tolerance));const k=key(atoms);if(seen.has(k))return;seen.add(k);const sums=subsetSums(atoms),errors=levels.map(v=>nearest(sums,v));queue.push({atoms,missing:levels.filter((_,i)=>errors[i]>tolerance),error:errors.reduce((s,e)=>s+e*e,0)});};enqueue(own.map(({size,fraction})=>({size,fraction})));let accepted:{events:PathEdit[];fraction:number}[]|undefined,states=0,decoded=0,bestError=Infinity,bestState:State|undefined;
  while(queue.length&&states++<maxStates){queue.sort((a,b)=>a.atoms.length-b.atoms.length||a.missing.length-b.missing.length||a.error-b.error);const state=queue.shift()!;if(!bestState||state.missing.length<bestState.missing.length||state.missing.length===bestState.missing.length&&state.error<bestState.error)bestState=state;
   if(!state.missing.length){const candidate=decodePaths(ref,state.atoms,y,radius,span,maxVariants);decoded++;if(candidate){const merged=new Map<string,{events:PathEdit[];sequence:string}>();for(const g of candidate.groups){const sequence=materialize(ref.sequence,g.events)!;if(!merged.has(sequence))merged.set(sequence,{events:g.events,sequence});}const models=[...merged.values()],rows=positions.flatMap((p,i)=>refs.every((r,k)=>k===h||p<r.cuts[0]-radius||p>=r.cuts[0]+radius+Math.max(span,10))?[i]:[]),columns=models.map(m=>rows.flatMap(i=>[...bases].map(b=>Number(m.sequence[positions[i]]===b)))),target=rows.flatMap(i=>{const bg=other(positions[i]);return bg.map((v,b)=>(observed[4*i+b]-v)/share);}),fit=new SangerFitCache(columns,target).fit(models.map((_,i)=>i)),weights=fit.weights.map(w=>w*share);let maximum=0;for(let j=0;j<y.length;j++){const p=begin+j,v=[0,0,0,0];models.forEach((m,i)=>{const b=bases.indexOf(m.sequence[p]);if(b>=0)v[b]+=weights[i];});maximum=Math.max(maximum,Math.sqrt(v.reduce((s,x,b)=>s+(x-y[j][b])**2,0)/4));}bestError=Math.min(bestError,maximum);if(maximum<=tolerance){accepted=models.flatMap((m,i)=>weights[i]>1e-9?[{events:m.events,fraction:weights[i]}]:[]);const candidateKey=models.map(m=>m.sequence).sort().join('|');if(!candidateKeys.has(candidateKey)){candidateKeys.add(candidateKey);candidates.push({reference:h,groups:accepted});}}}}
   if(state.atoms.length>=maxVariants+1)continue;const targets=state.missing.length?[state.missing[0]]:levels;
   for(const value of targets)for(let i=0;i<state.atoms.length;i++){const atom=state.atoms[i],others=state.atoms.filter((_,j)=>j!==i);for(const sum of subsetSums(others)){const u=value-sum;if(u<minimum||atom.fraction-u<minimum)continue;enqueue([...others,{size:atom.size,fraction:u},{size:atom.size,fraction:atom.fraction-u}]);}}
  }
  details.push({reference:ref.name,states,decoded,bestError,accepted:!!accepted});if(!accepted)throw Error('Frequency/path decoding unresolved for '+ref.name+' after '+states+' states; best local error '+bestError+' '+JSON.stringify({tolerance,levels,own,bestState}));groups.push(...accepted.map(g=>({...g,reference:h})));
 }
 return{groups,details,candidates};
}

END MODULE


BEGIN MODULE src/app/workers/core/sanger-frequency-path-decoder.ts
import{Prepared,PathEdit,materialize}from'./sanger-path-common';import{normalizeMoleculeEdits}from'./sanger-edit-normalize';import{SangerFitCache}from'./sanger-fit-cache';
interface Atom {size:number;fraction:number}
interface Family extends Atom {reference:number}
interface Allocation {cost:number;code:number}
const bases='ACGT';
function subsetSums(atoms:Atom[]){const sums=[0];for(const a of atoms){const n=sums.length;for(let i=0;i<n;i++)sums.push(sums[i]+a.fraction);}return sums;}
const nearest=(values:number[],v:number)=>values.reduce((best,x)=>Math.min(best,Math.abs(v-x)),Infinity);
function refineAtoms(input:Atom[],levels:number[],tolerance:number){
 let atoms=input.map(a=>({...a}));const families=[...new Set(atoms.map(a=>a.size))],anchors=new Map(families.map(size=>[size,atoms.findIndex(a=>a.size===size)])),variables=atoms.flatMap((a,i)=>anchors.get(a.size)===i?[]:[i]);if(!variables.length)return atoms;
 const totals=new Map(families.map(size=>[size,atoms.filter(a=>a.size===size).reduce((s,a)=>s+a.fraction,0)]));
 for(let iteration=0;iteration<12;iteration++){const sums=subsetSums(atoms),n=variables.length,matrix=Array.from({length:n},()=>Array(n+1).fill(0));let count=0;
  for(const value of levels){let mask=0;for(let k=1;k<sums.length;k++)if(Math.abs(sums[k]-value)<Math.abs(sums[mask]-value))mask=k;if(Math.abs(sums[mask]-value)>2*tolerance)continue;const row=variables.map(i=>Number(!!(mask&(1<<i)))-Number(!!(mask&(1<<anchors.get(atoms[i].size)!)))),base=families.reduce((s,size)=>s+((mask&(1<<anchors.get(size)!))?totals.get(size)!:0),0),rhs=value-base;for(let i=0;i<n;i++){matrix[i][n]+=row[i]*rhs;for(let j=0;j<n;j++)matrix[i][j]+=row[i]*row[j];}count++;}if(count<n)break;
  let valid=true;for(let c=0;c<n;c++){let pivot=c;for(let r=c+1;r<n;r++)if(Math.abs(matrix[r][c])>Math.abs(matrix[pivot][c]))pivot=r;if(Math.abs(matrix[pivot][c])<1e-10){valid=false;break;}[matrix[c],matrix[pivot]]=[matrix[pivot],matrix[c]];const d=matrix[c][c];for(let j=c;j<=n;j++)matrix[c][j]/=d;for(let r=0;r<n;r++)if(r!==c){const f=matrix[r][c];for(let j=c;j<=n;j++)matrix[r][j]-=f*matrix[c][j];}}
  if(!valid)break;const next=atoms.map(a=>({...a}));variables.forEach((id,i)=>next[id].fraction=matrix[i][n]);for(const size of families)next[anchors.get(size)!].fraction=totals.get(size)!-variables.filter(i=>atoms[i].size===size).reduce((s,i)=>s+next[i].fraction,0);if(next.some(a=>a.fraction<=0))break;const change=Math.max(...next.map((a,i)=>Math.abs(a.fraction-atoms[i].fraction)));atoms=next;if(change<1e-9)break;
 }
 return atoms;
}
/** Enumerate bounded edit origins while caching the per-cycle allocation of
 * a small number of frequency atoms to A/C/G/T. This enumerates groups, not
 * every possible inserted string (4^insertionLength). */
function decodePaths(ref:Prepared,atoms:Atom[],y:number[][],radius:number,span:number,maxVariants:number,distinctGroups=false){
 const cut=ref.cuts[0],begin=cut-radius,n=y.length,zero=atoms.flatMap((a,i)=>a.size===0?[i]:[]),choices:[number|null,...number[]]=[null,...zero];let best:{cost:number;groups:{events:PathEdit[];fraction:number}[]}|undefined;
 for(const wt of choices){const variants=atoms.flatMap((_,i)=>i===wt?[]:[i]);if(variants.length>maxVariants)continue;const cache=Array.from({length:n},()=>new Map<number,Allocation>()),states=variants.map(id=>Array.from({length:2*radius+1},(_,s)=>Array.from({length:n},(_,j)=>{const p=begin+j,start=cut-radius+s,a=atoms[id];return(a.size===0?p>=start&&p<start+span?4:bases.indexOf(ref.sequence[p]):a.size>0?p<start?bases.indexOf(ref.sequence[p]):p<start+a.size?4:bases.indexOf(ref.sequence[p-a.size]):bases.indexOf(ref.sequence[p<start?p:p-a.size]))<<(3*id);}))),origins:number[]=Array(variants.length).fill(0);
  const allocate=(j:number,key:number)=>{const hit=cache[j].get(key);if(hit)return hit;const fixed=[0,0,0,0],free:number[]=[];let baseCode=0;for(let i=0;i<atoms.length;i++){const b=(key>>(3*i))&7;if(b===4)free.push(i);else{fixed[b]+=atoms[i].fraction;baseCode|=b<<(2*i);}}let bestCost=Infinity,bestCode=baseCode;for(let code=0;code<4**free.length;code++){const v=[...fixed];let full=baseCode;for(let k=0;k<free.length;k++){const b=(code>>(2*k))&3;v[b]+=atoms[free[k]].fraction;full|=b<<(2*free[k]);}let cost=0;for(let b=0;b<4;b++)cost+=(y[j][b]-v[b])**2;if(cost<bestCost){bestCost=cost;bestCode=full;}}const value={cost:bestCost,code:bestCode};cache[j].set(key,value);return value;};
  const evaluate=()=>{const codes:number[]=[];let cost=0;for(let j=0;j<n;j++){let key=wt===null?0:bases.indexOf(ref.sequence[begin+j])<<(3*wt);for(let k=0;k<variants.length;k++)key|=states[k][origins[k]][j];const a=allocate(j,key);cost+=a.cost;if(best&&cost>best.cost)return;codes.push(a.code);}const groups=atoms.map((a,id)=>{if(id===wt)return{events:[],fraction:a.fraction};const k=variants.indexOf(id),start=cut-radius+origins[k],length=a.size>0?a.size:a.size===0?span:0,inserted=Array.from({length},(_,j)=>bases[(codes[start-begin+j]>>(2*id))&3]).join(''),events=normalizeMoleculeEdits(ref.sequence,[{start,end:start+(a.size<0?-a.size:a.size===0?span:0),inserted}]);return{events,fraction:a.fraction};});if(distinctGroups&&new Set(groups.map(g=>materialize(ref.sequence,g.events))).size!==groups.length)return;if(!best||cost<best.cost)best={cost,groups};};
  const visit=(k:number)=>{if(k===variants.length){evaluate();return;}for(let s=0;s<=2*radius;s++){origins[k]=s;visit(k+1);}};visit(0);
 }
 return best;
}
/** Frequency refinement never merges groups because their percentages are
 * alike. Observational levels propose splits of a length class; the complete
 * sequence model and raw reconstruction must independently validate them. */
export function decodeFrequencyPaths(refs:Prepared[],families:Family[],positions:number[],observed:number[],noise:number,radius=3,span=10,maxVariants=5,maxStates=10000,progress:(stage:string)=>void=()=>{},collectCandidates=false,requestedCounts?:Record<string,number>){
 const index=new Map(positions.map((p,i)=>[p,i])),groups:{reference:number;events:PathEdit[];fraction:number}[]=[],details:any[]=[],candidates:{reference:number;groups:{events:PathEdit[];fraction:number}[];rmse:number;maximum:number}[]=[],candidateKeys=new Set<string>();
 for(let h=0;h<refs.length;h++){
  progress(`Decoding offset edits · reference ${h+1} / ${refs.length}`);
  const ref=refs[h],cut=ref.cuts[0],begin=cut-radius,end=cut+radius+Math.max(span,...families.filter(f=>f.reference===h).map(f=>f.size)),own=families.filter(f=>f.reference===h&&f.fraction>0),share=own.reduce((s,a)=>s+a.fraction,0),tolerance=Math.max(1e-5,4*noise),minimum=share*.01;
  const other=(p:number)=>{const v=[0,0,0,0];for(const f of families)if(f.reference!==h&&f.fraction>0){const r=refs[f.reference],c=r.cuts[0],b=bases.indexOf(r.sequence[p<c-radius?p:p-f.size]);if(b>=0)v[b]+=f.fraction;}return v;};
  const y=Array.from({length:end-begin},(_,j)=>{const p=begin+j,k=index.get(p);if(k===undefined)throw Error('Missing local trace coverage.');for(const [i,r]of refs.entries())if(i!==h&&p>=r.cuts[0]-radius&&p<r.cuts[0]+radius+Math.max(span,10))throw Error('Local windows overlap.');const bg=other(p);return bg.map((v,b)=>observed[4*k+b]-v);}),values=y.flat().filter(v=>v>tolerance&&v<share-tolerance).sort((a,b)=>a-b),clusters:number[][]=[];
  for(const v of values){const last=clusters.at(-1);if(last&&v-last[0]<=tolerance/2)last.push(v);else clusters.push([v]);}const levels=clusters.map(c=>c.reduce((a,b)=>a+b,0)/c.length),key=(a:Atom[])=>a.map(x=>x.size+':'+x.fraction.toFixed(7)).sort().join('/'),ordered=(a:Atom[])=>a.sort((a,b)=>a.size-b.size||b.fraction-a.fraction);
  interface State {atoms:Atom[];missing:number[];error:number}
  const queue:State[]=[],seen=new Set<string>();const enqueue=(atoms:Atom[])=>{if(requestedCounts&&atoms.some(a=>atoms.filter(b=>b.size===a.size).length>(requestedCounts[String(a.size)]||0)))return;atoms=ordered(atoms);atoms=ordered(refineAtoms(atoms,values,tolerance));const k=key(atoms);if(seen.has(k))return;seen.add(k);const sums=subsetSums(atoms),errors=levels.map(v=>nearest(sums,v));queue.push({atoms,missing:levels.filter((_,i)=>errors[i]>tolerance),error:errors.reduce((s,e)=>s+e*e,0)});};enqueue(own.map(({size,fraction})=>({size,fraction})));let accepted:{events:PathEdit[];fraction:number}[]|undefined,states=0,decoded=0,bestError=Infinity,bestState:State|undefined;
  while(queue.length&&states++<maxStates){queue.sort((a,b)=>requestedCounts?a.missing.length-b.missing.length||a.error-b.error||b.atoms.length-a.atoms.length:a.atoms.length-b.atoms.length||a.missing.length-b.missing.length||a.error-b.error);const state=queue.shift()!;if(!bestState||state.missing.length<bestState.missing.length||state.missing.length===bestState.missing.length&&state.error<bestState.error)bestState=state;
   if((!requestedCounts||Object.entries(requestedCounts).every(([size,count])=>state.atoms.filter(a=>a.size===Number(size)).length===count))&&(collectCandidates||!state.missing.length||state.atoms.length===maxVariants+1)){const candidate=decodePaths(ref,state.atoms,y,radius,span,maxVariants,!!requestedCounts);decoded++;if(candidate){const merged=new Map<string,{events:PathEdit[];sequence:string}>();for(const g of candidate.groups){const sequence=materialize(ref.sequence,g.events)!;if(!merged.has(sequence))merged.set(sequence,{events:g.events,sequence});}const models=[...merged.values()],rows=positions.flatMap((p,i)=>refs.every((r,k)=>k===h||p<r.cuts[0]-radius||p>=r.cuts[0]+radius+Math.max(span,10))?[i]:[]),columns=models.map(m=>rows.flatMap(i=>[...bases].map(b=>Number(m.sequence[positions[i]]===b)))),target=rows.flatMap(i=>{const bg=other(positions[i]);return bg.map((v,b)=>(observed[4*i+b]-v)/share);}),fit=new SangerFitCache(columns,target).fit(models.map((_,i)=>i)),weights=fit.weights.map(w=>w*share);let maximum=0;for(let j=0;j<y.length;j++){const p=begin+j,v=[0,0,0,0];models.forEach((m,i)=>{const b=bases.indexOf(m.sequence[p]);if(b>=0)v[b]+=weights[i];});maximum=Math.max(maximum,Math.sqrt(v.reduce((s,x,b)=>s+(x-y[j][b])**2,0)/4));}bestError=Math.min(bestError,maximum);if(collectCandidates||maximum<=tolerance){accepted=models.flatMap((m,i)=>(collectCandidates||weights[i]>1e-9)?[{events:m.events,fraction:weights[i]}]:[]);if(!collectCandidates)break;const key=models.map(m=>m.sequence).sort().join('|');if(!candidateKeys.has(key)){candidateKeys.add(key);candidates.push({reference:h,groups:accepted,rmse:fit.rmse,maximum});}}}}
   if(state.atoms.length>=maxVariants+1)continue;const targets=state.missing.length?(requestedCounts?state.missing:[state.missing[0]]):levels;
   for(const value of targets)for(let i=0;i<state.atoms.length;i++){const atom=state.atoms[i],others=state.atoms.filter((_,j)=>j!==i);for(const sum of subsetSums(others)){const u=value-sum;if(u<minimum||atom.fraction-u<minimum)continue;enqueue([...others,{size:atom.size,fraction:u},{size:atom.size,fraction:atom.fraction-u}]);}}
  }
  details.push({reference:ref.name,states,decoded,bestError,accepted:!collectCandidates&&!!accepted,proposalOnly:collectCandidates});if(!accepted)throw Error('Frequency/path decoding unresolved for '+ref.name+' after '+states+' states; best local error '+bestError+' '+JSON.stringify({tolerance,levels,own,bestState}));groups.push(...accepted.map(g=>({...g,reference:h})));
 }
 return{groups,details,candidates};
}

END MODULE


BEGIN MODULE src/app/workers/core/sanger-joint.ts
import {PooledAmplicon,PooledPreflight,SangerAnalysisSettings,SangerAnalysisResult,SangerTrace,TRACE_BASES,SangerJointEvent} from '../../models/sanger-analysis';
import {exactBindingSites} from './sanger-primer-designer';
import {reverseComplement} from '../../utils/biology.utils';
import {registerTrace} from './sanger-pooled';
import {contrastPeaks,integratedPeaks,calibrateSignal,correctGain,simplexFit,rawSignalScore,gramCondition} from './sanger-signal';
import {emissionVector,fitContextEmission,sequenceYield,fitEmissionMixture} from './sanger-emission';
import {plotTrace} from './sanger-analysis';

interface Ref {name:string;sequence:string;cuts:number[];input:PooledAmplicon;reverse:boolean;primerEnd:number}
interface Event {target:number;start:number;end:number;insert:number}
interface Proposal {reference:number;events:Event[];sequence:string;size:number;aliases:{reference:number;events:Event[]}[]}
interface Design {refs:Ref[];models:Proposal[];report:PooledPreflight;positions:number[]}
const blank=(reason=''):PooledPreflight=>({passed:false,reason,positions:[],modelCount:0,rank:0,condition:null,minimumDifferences:0,minimumCutOffset:0,amplicons:[]});
export const usesJointSanger=(amplicons:PooledAmplicon[],s:SangerAnalysisSettings)=>!!s.largeDeletions||!!(s.jointEngine&&s.jointEngine!=='staged')||amplicons.some(a=>(a.cuts?.length||1)>1);
/** Edit complete primer-oriented molecules. Downstream coordinates include ALL upstream edits. */
export function applyJointEvents(sequence:string,events:Event[]):string|null {
 let at=0,result='';for(const e of [...events].sort((a,b)=>a.start-b.start||a.end-b.end)){
  if(e.start<at||e.start<0||e.end<e.start||e.end>sequence.length)return null;
  result+=sequence.slice(at,e.start)+'N'.repeat(e.insert);at=e.end;
 }return result+sequence.slice(at);
}
function prepare(amplicons:PooledAmplicon[],primer:string,s:SangerAnalysisSettings):Design{
 const report=blank();
 if(amplicons.length<1||amplicons.length>4||new Set(amplicons.map(a=>a.name)).size!==amplicons.length)throw Error('Use 1–4 uniquely named submitted amplicons.');
 if(!/^[ACGT]{12,40}$/.test(primer))throw Error('Enter the actual sequencing primer (12–40 nt, 5′→3′).');
 if(s.method!=='mixture'||s.signalModel==='classic')throw Error('Joint targets / large deletions require calibrated trace decomposition.');
 const range=s.maxIndel,large=s.largeDeletions?(s.maxDeletion??200):range;
 if(!Number.isInteger(range)||range<1||range>15||!Number.isInteger(large)||large<range||large>500)throw Error('Use small indels ±1–15 bp and a deletion search limit from that range to 500 bp.');
 if(!Number.isInteger(s.window)||s.window<60||s.window>500||!Number.isInteger(s.readStart)||!Number.isInteger(s.readEnd)||s.readStart<0||s.readEnd>1000||s.readEnd<=s.readStart)throw Error('Use a 60–500 bp window and a usable primer extension range within 0–1,000 bp.');
 const refs=amplicons.map(a=>{
  const cuts=a.cuts??[a.cut];if(!a.name.trim()||!/^[ACGT]+$/.test(a.sequence)||a.sequence.length>20000||cuts.length<1||cuts.length>2||new Set(cuts).size!==cuts.length||cuts.some(c=>!Number.isInteger(c)||c<=0||c>=a.sequence.length))throw Error(a.name+': enter one or two distinct cuts inside the actual submitted amplicon.');
  const hits=exactBindingSites(primer,{name:a.name,sequence:a.sequence,cuts});if(hits.length!==1)throw Error(a.name+': sequencing primer must bind exactly once across both strands.');
  const h=hits[0],reverse=h.direction==='reverse',oriented=reverse?reverseComplement(a.sequence):a.sequence,end=reverse?a.sequence.length-h.start:h.end;
  const offsets=cuts.map(c=>(reverse?a.sequence.length-c:c)-end).sort((a,b)=>a-b),sequence=oriented.slice(end);
  const windows=offsets.map(c=>({start:c-Math.floor(s.window/2),end:c+Math.ceil(s.window/2)}));
  const maxWindow=Math.min(500,Math.max(0,Math.floor(Math.min(...offsets.map(c=>2*Math.min(c-s.readStart,Math.min(s.readEnd,sequence.length)-c))))));
  if(offsets[0]<s.readStart+72||offsets.at(-1)!+30>Math.min(s.readEnd,sequence.length))throw Error(`${a.name}: target windows exceed the primer-relative read range; maximum centered window is ${maxWindow} bp, with ≥72 upstream bases required.`);
  if(offsets.length===2&&offsets[1]-offsets[0]<=2*range+20)throw Error(`${a.name}: targets are too close for independent small-indel attribution at ±${range} bp. Reduce the indel range or use one target. Maximum non-overlapping centered window: ${offsets[1]-offsets[0]} bp.`);
  const lo=windows[0].start,hi=windows.at(-1)!.end;
  report.amplicons.push({name:a.name,direction:h.direction,primerStart:h.start+1,primerEnd:h.end,cutOffset:offsets[0],windowStart:lo,windowEnd:hi,targets:offsets.map((cut,i)=>({cutOffset:cut,windowStart:windows[i].start,windowEnd:windows[i].end})),maximumWindow:maxWindow,maximumIndependentWindow:offsets.length===2?offsets[1]-offsets[0]:undefined,mergedWindows:windows.length===2&&windows[0].end>windows[1].start});
  return {name:a.name,sequence,cuts:offsets,input:a,reverse,primerEnd:end};
 });
 const start=s.readStart,end=Math.min(s.readEnd,Math.max(...refs.map(r=>r.sequence.length)));
 
 // Do not give insertion identity an invented base. Mask every possible insertion
 // position, including displacement of target 2 caused by target 1.
 const positions=Array.from({length:end-start},(_,i)=>start+i).filter(p=>!refs.some(r=>r.cuts.some((c,i)=>p>=c-(i?range:0)-5&&p<c+(i?2*range:range)+5)));
 if(positions.length<100)throw Error('Too few joint observation positions. Increase the window or reduce the indel range.');
 const models:Proposal[]=[],seen=new Map<string,Proposal>();let skipped=0;
 const add=(reference:number,events:Event[])=>{
  const seq=applyJointEvents(refs[reference].sequence,events);if(events.some(e=>e.start<s.readStart+84)||seq===null||seq.length<s.readStart+84||positions.some(p=>p<seq.length&&!/[ACGT]/.test(seq[p]))){skipped++;return;}
  const key=positions.map(p=>seq[p]).join(''),alias={reference,events};const previous=seen.get(key);
  if(previous){previous.aliases.push(alias);return;}
  const m={reference,events,sequence:seq,size:events.reduce((sum,e)=>sum+e.insert-(e.end-e.start),0),aliases:[alias]};models.push(m);seen.set(key,m);
 };
 refs.forEach((ref,h)=>{
  const choices=ref.cuts.map((cut,target)=>Array.from({length:2*range+1},(_,j)=>j-range).map(size=>size===0?[]:[{target,start:cut,end:cut+Math.max(0,-size),insert:Math.max(0,size)}]));
  for(const a of choices[0]){if(choices.length===1)add(h,a);else for(const b of choices[1])add(h,[...a,...b]);}
  if(s.largeDeletions){ref.cuts.forEach((cut,target)=>{for(let length=range+1;length<=large;length++)for(const left of [0,Math.floor(length/2),length])add(h,[{target,start:cut-left,end:cut-left+length,insert:0}]);});}
  if(ref.cuts.length===2){const [left,right]=ref.cuts;if(right-left<=large||!s.largeDeletions)for(let dl=-range;dl<=range;dl++)for(let dr=-range;dr<=range;dr++){
   const e={target:-1,start:left+dl,end:right+dr,insert:0};if(e.end>e.start&&(!s.largeDeletions||e.end-e.start<=large))for(let insert=0;insert<=range;insert++)add(h,[{...e,insert}]);
  }}
 });
 if(models.length*positions.length*4>12_000_000)throw Error('Joint proposal dictionary exceeds the browser memory budget. Reduce the small-indel range, deletion limit or target window.');
 const wt=refs.map((_,h)=>models.find(m=>m.aliases.some(a=>a.reference===h&&!a.events.length))!);
 let minimum=Infinity;for(let i=0;i<wt.length;i++)for(let j=0;j<i;j++)minimum=Math.min(minimum,positions.filter(p=>wt[i].sequence[p]!==wt[j].sequence[p]).length);
 report.positions=positions;report.modelCount=models.length;report.minimumDifferences=Number.isFinite(minimum)?minimum:positions.length;report.minimumCutOffset=refs.length>1?Math.min(...refs.flatMap((r,i)=>refs.slice(i+1).map(t=>Math.abs(r.cuts[0]-t.cuts[0])))):0;
 const geometry=gramCondition(wt.map(m=>positions.flatMap(p=>TRACE_BASES.map(b=>Number(m.sequence[p]===b)))));report.rank=geometry.rank;report.condition=geometry.condition;
 if(report.minimumDifferences<3||geometry.rank<refs.length||geometry.condition===null||geometry.condition>100)throw Error('WT references cannot be distinguished on the submitted primer axis. Change the primer, window or references.');
 report.passed=true;report.reason='Joint proposal geometry checked. Selected components require held-out trace validation.';
 report.joint={mode:'staged',candidateCount:models.length,ambiguousClasses:models.filter(m=>m.aliases.length>1).length,coverageExcluded:skipped,maxDeletion:large};
 return {refs,models,report,positions};
}
export function checkJointSeparation(amplicons:PooledAmplicon[],primer:string,s:SangerAnalysisSettings):PooledPreflight{try{return prepare(amplicons,primer,s).report;}catch(e){return blank(e instanceof Error?e.message:String(e));}}
export interface JointInput {amplicons:PooledAmplicon[];primer:string;ampliconConfirmed:boolean;sample:SangerTrace;control:SangerTrace;settings:SangerAnalysisSettings;synthetic?:boolean}
export function analyzeJointSanger(input:JointInput,progress:(s:string)=>void=()=>{}):SangerAnalysisResult{
 if(!input.ampliconConfirmed)throw Error('Confirm the actual full amplicons submitted to Sanger.');
 const s=input.settings;let {refs,models,report,positions}=prepare(input.amplicons,input.primer,s);
 if(!input.sample||!input.control)throw Error('Joint analysis requires an unedited control and a sample using the same sequencing primer.');
 progress('Registering joint traces on the sequencing-primer axis');
 const anchor=Math.min(...models.flatMap(m=>m.events.map(e=>e.start)));
 const prepared=refs.map(r=>({name:r.name,sequence:r.sequence,cut:anchor}));
 const ctlReg=registerTrace(input.control,prepared,s,integratedPeaks(input.control,[0,0,0,0]).vectors),sampleReg=registerTrace(input.sample,prepared,s,integratedPeaks(input.sample,[0,0,0,0]).vectors);
 const ctl=contrastPeaks(input.control).vectors,raw=contrastPeaks(input.sample).vectors,first=Math.min(...refs.map(r=>r.cuts[0]));
 const actualStart=Math.max(s.readStart,ctlReg.trim+10,sampleReg.trim+10),actualEnd=Math.min(s.readEnd,ctlReg.trim+ctl.length-2,sampleReg.trim+raw.length-2);
 if(actualStart>s.readStart||positions.at(-1)!>=actualEnd)({refs,models,report,positions}=prepare(input.amplicons,input.primer,{...s,readStart:actualStart,readEnd:actualEnd}));

 const calibrate=(vectors:number[][],trim:number)=>{const observed=Array.from({length:vectors.length+trim},(_,p)=>vectors[p-trim]||[0,0,0,0]),rows=Array.from({length:anchor-12},(_,p)=>p).filter(p=>p>=Math.max(s.readStart,trim+10)&&p-trim<vectors.length-2);return calibrateSignal(observed,rows,refs.map(r=>Array.from(r.sequence,(_,p)=>TRACE_BASES.map(b=>Number(r.sequence[p]===b)))));};
 const cc=calibrate(ctl,ctlReg.trim),sc=calibrate(raw,sampleReg.trim);if(cc.validationR2<.8||sc.validationR2<.8)throw Error('Joint upstream calibration failed held-out validation.');
 if(positions.some(p=>p-ctlReg.trim<0||p-sampleReg.trim<0||p-ctlReg.trim>=ctl.length||p-sampleReg.trim>=raw.length))throw Error('Actual chromatograms do not cover the checked joint windows.');
 const corrected=raw.map(v=>correctGain(v,sc.gains)),wtCorrected=ctl.map(v=>correctGain(v,cc.gains));
 const observedControl=Array.from({length:ctl.length+ctlReg.trim},(_,p)=>wtCorrected[p-ctlReg.trim]||[0,0,0,0]);
 const context=fitContextEmission(refs.map(r=>r.sequence),positions,observedControl,cc.weights,cc.lag),theta=context.theta;
 const cols=models.map(m=>positions.flatMap(p=>emissionVector(m.sequence,p,sc.lag,theta)));
 const wtIds=refs.map((_,h)=>models.findIndex(m=>m.aliases.some(a=>a.reference===h&&!a.events.length)));
 const controlCols=wtIds.map(i=>positions.flatMap(p=>emissionVector(models[i].sequence,p,cc.lag,theta))),controlFit=fitEmissionMixture(controlCols,positions.flatMap(p=>wtCorrected[p-ctlReg.trim]));
 const controlScore=rawSignalScore(controlCols,positions.flatMap(p=>ctl[p-ctlReg.trim]),controlFit.weights,cc.gains);if(controlScore.r2<.85)throw Error('Submitted WT amplicons do not explain the pooled control.');
 const y=positions.flatMap(p=>corrected[p-sampleReg.trim]);
 // Three disjoint folds: selection training, proposal tuning, untouched final QC.
 const train=positions.flatMap((_,i)=>i%5<3?[i]:[]),tune=positions.flatMap((_,i)=>i%5===3?[i]:[]),test=positions.flatMap((_,i)=>i%5===4?[i]:[]);
 if(tune.length<20||test.length<20)throw Error('Not enough independent positions for joint candidate selection and held-out QC.');
 const subset=(c:number[],rows:number[])=>rows.flatMap(i=>c.slice(i*4,i*4+4)),trainCols=cols.map(c=>subset(c,train)),trainY=subset(y,train),tuneY=subset(y,tune);
 const fitIds=(ids:number[])=>fitEmissionMixture(ids.map(i=>trainCols[i]),trainY);
 const mse=(ids:number[],weights:number[],rows:number[],obs:number[])=>{let error=0;for(let j=0;j<rows.length;j++){const pred=[0,0,0,0];ids.forEach((id,h)=>{for(let b=0;b<4;b++)pred[b]+=weights[h]*cols[id][4*rows[j]+b];});const total=pred.reduce((a,b)=>a+b,0);for(let b=0;b<4;b++)error+=(obs[j*4+b]-(total?pred[b]/total:0))**2;}return error/obs.length;};
 let ids=[...new Set(wtIds)],fit=fitIds(ids),bestTune=mse(ids,fit.weights,tune,tuneY),iterations=0;
 progress(`Screening ${models.length} joint reference/edit proposals`);
 let refinementSkipped=0;
 for(let stage=0;stage<2;stage++){
 for(let step=0;step<24;step++,iterations++){
  const prediction=Array.from({length:trainY.length},(_,j)=>ids.reduce((sum,id,h)=>sum+fit.weights[h]*trainCols[id][j],0));
  const residual=trainY.map((v,j)=>v-prediction[j]);
  const scores=models.flatMap((m,id)=>ids.includes(id)?[]:[{id,score:trainCols[id].reduce((sum,v,j)=>sum+v*residual[j],0)}]).sort((a,b)=>b.score-a.score).slice(0,8);
  let next:{ids:number[];fit:ReturnType<typeof fitEmissionMixture>;error:number}|undefined;
  for(const candidate of scores){const trial=[...ids,candidate.id],f=fitIds(trial);if(f.weights.at(-1)!<.003)continue;const error=mse(trial,f.weights,tune,tuneY);if(error<bestTune-Math.max(.000005,bestTune*.01)&&(!next||error<next.error))next={ids:trial,fit:f,error};}
  if(!next)break;ids=next.ids;fit=next.fit;bestTune=next.error;
 }
 if(stage===0&&s.largeDeletions){
  progress('Refining supported large-deletion classes at cut-touching junctions');
  const seen=new Map(models.map(m=>[positions.map(p=>m.sequence[p]).join(''),m]));
  const classes=new Map<string,{reference:number;target:number;length:number}>();
  ids.forEach((id,i)=>{const m=models[id];if(fit.weights[i]<.01||m.events.length!==1)return;const e=m.events[0],length=e.end-e.start;if(e.target>=0&&!e.insert&&length>s.maxIndel)classes.set(`${m.reference}/${e.target}/${length}`,{reference:m.reference,target:e.target,length});});
  for(const c of classes.values())for(let start=refs[c.reference].cuts[c.target]-c.length;start<=refs[c.reference].cuts[c.target];start++){
   if(start<s.readStart+84)continue;
   const events=[{target:c.target,start,end:start+c.length,insert:0}],sequence=applyJointEvents(refs[c.reference].sequence,events);
   if(!sequence||sequence.length<=positions.at(-1)!)continue;
   const key=positions.map(p=>sequence[p]).join(''),old=seen.get(key),alias={reference:c.reference,events};
   if(old){if(!old.aliases.some(a=>a.reference===alias.reference&&JSON.stringify(a.events)===JSON.stringify(events)))old.aliases.push(alias);continue;}
   if((models.length+1)*positions.length*4>12_000_000){refinementSkipped++;continue;}
   const m={reference:c.reference,events,sequence,size:-c.length,aliases:[alias]},column=positions.flatMap(p=>emissionVector(sequence,p,sc.lag,theta));models.push(m);cols.push(column);trainCols.push(subset(column,train));seen.set(key,m);
  }
 }
 }
 // A forward-only search can retain several wrong junctions of one net-size
 // class after refinement. Refit each supported class against a single complete
 // candidate while retaining the other classes. Accept only tuning improvement;
 // genuine multi-junction mixtures are retained when the collapsed class fits worse.
 const selection=[...train,...tune].sort((a,b)=>a-b),selectionY=subset(y,selection);
 const classes=new Map<string,number[]>();for(const id of ids)if(models[id].events.length){const key=models[id].reference+'/'+models[id].size;classes.set(key,[...(classes.get(key)||[]),id]);}
 for(const members of classes.values()){
  const seed=models[members[0]],rest=ids.filter(id=>!members.includes(id)),mass=ids.reduce((sum,id,k)=>sum+(members.includes(id)?fit.weights[k]:0),0),weights=rest.map(id=>fit.weights[ids.indexOf(id)]),candidates=models.flatMap((m,id)=>m.reference===seed.reference&&m.size===seed.size&&m.events.length?[id]:[]);
  const classObjective=mse(ids,fit.weights,selection,selectionY);
  const ranked=candidates.map(id=>({id,error:mse([...rest,id],[...weights,mass],selection,selectionY)})).sort((a,b)=>a.error-b.error).slice(0,8);
  let replacement:{ids:number[];fit:ReturnType<typeof fitEmissionMixture>;error:number}|undefined;
  for(const candidate of ranked){const trial=[...rest,candidate.id],f=fitIds(trial),error=mse(trial,f.weights,selection,selectionY);if(error<classObjective-1e-7&&(!replacement||error<replacement.error))replacement={ids:trial,fit:f,error};}
  if(replacement){ids=replacement.ids;fit=replacement.fit;bestTune=mse(ids,fit.weights,tune,tuneY);}
 }
 // Drop numerical dust and refit, with no use of final QC fold for selection.
 ids=ids.filter((_,i)=>fit.weights[i]>=.003||wtIds.includes(ids[i]));fit=fitIds(ids);
 const finalTest=rawSignalScore(ids.map(id=>subset(cols[id],test)),test.flatMap(i=>raw[positions[i]-sampleReg.trim]),fit.weights,sc.gains);
 const full=rawSignalScore(ids.map(id=>cols[id]),positions.flatMap(p=>raw[p-sampleReg.trim]),fit.weights,sc.gains);
 const chosen=ids.flatMap((id,i)=>fit.weights[i]>1e-10?[{model:models[id],weight:fit.weights[i]}]:[]);
 const aliasKey=(a:{reference:number;events:Event[]})=>a.reference+':'+a.events.map(e=>`${e.target}/${e.start}/${e.end}/${e.insert}`).join(';');
 const ambiguous=chosen.filter(c=>new Set(c.model.aliases.map(aliasKey)).size>1);
 const semanticKey=(a:{reference:number;events:Event[]})=>a.reference+':'+(a.events.length?'edit':'WT')+':'+refs[a.reference].cuts.map((_,t)=>a.events.filter(e=>e.target===t||e.target===-1).reduce((sum,e)=>sum+e.insert-(e.end-e.start),0)).join('/');
 const attributionAmbiguous=ambiguous.filter(c=>new Set(c.model.aliases.map(semanticKey)).size>1);
 const net=(events:Event[])=>events.reduce((sum,e)=>sum+e.insert-(e.end-e.start),0);
 const netAmbiguous=(m:Proposal)=>new Set(m.aliases.map(a=>net(a.events))).size>1;
 const selectedGeometry=gramCondition(ids.map(id=>cols[id]));
 const warnings:string[]=[];if(refinementSkipped)warnings.push('Deletion junction refinement reached the browser memory budget; exact breakpoint search is incomplete.');if(ambiguous.length)warnings.push('Some supported models have indistinguishable explanations. Only reference/target/size assignments shared by every explanation are reported; exact breakpoints remain unresolved.');
 if(finalTest.r2<.9)warnings.push('Joint model failed untouched held-out trace QC. Results are exploratory.');
 if(report.joint!.coverageExcluded)warnings.push('Some deletion proposals exceed product/trace coverage and were excluded; the search is not exhaustive.');
 const boundary=chosen.some(c=>c.model.events.some(e=>e.end-e.start===(s.maxDeletion??200)&&s.largeDeletions));if(boundary)warnings.push('Supported deletion reaches the search boundary. Increase the deletion limit and reanalyse.');
 if(selectedGeometry.condition===null||selectedGeometry.condition>100)warnings.push('Selected components are poorly conditioned. Individual fractions are unstable.');
 const eventsInInput=(r:Ref,events:Event[]):SangerJointEvent[]=>events.map(e=>{const start=r.primerEnd+e.start,end=r.primerEnd+e.end;return {target:e.target<0?0:(r.reverse?r.cuts.length-e.target:e.target+1),start:r.reverse?r.input.sequence.length-end:start,end:r.reverse?r.input.sequence.length-start:end,inserted:e.insert};}).sort((a,b)=>a.start-b.start);
 const groups=chosen.map((c,i)=>({id:`J${i+1}`,reference:new Set(c.model.aliases.map(a=>a.reference)).size>1?null:refs[c.model.reference].name,fraction:c.weight,netSize:netAmbiguous(c.model)?null:c.model.size,events:ambiguous.includes(c)?[]:eventsInInput(refs[c.model.reference],c.model.events),alternatives:ambiguous.includes(c)?c.model.aliases.map(a=>({reference:refs[a.reference].name,events:eventsInInput(refs[a.reference],a.events)})):[]}));
 const perRef=refs.map((r,h)=>{
  const resolved=chosen.filter(c=>!attributionAmbiguous.includes(c)&&c.model.reference===h),crossAmbiguous=attributionAmbiguous.some(c=>c.model.aliases.some(a=>a.reference===h)),share=resolved.reduce((sum,c)=>sum+c.weight,0);
  const components=new Map<number,number>();resolved.forEach(c=>components.set(c.model.size,(components.get(c.model.size)||0)+c.weight));
  const noedit=resolved.filter(c=>!c.model.events.length).reduce((sum,c)=>sum+c.weight,0),estimable=!crossAmbiguous&&share>=.02&&finalTest.r2>=.9&&fit.converged;
  return {name:r.name,share,wt:estimable?noedit/share:null,indel:estimable?1-noedit/share:null,inFrame:estimable?resolved.filter(c=>c.model.events.length&&c.model.size%3===0).reduce((sum,c)=>sum+c.weight,0)/share:null,outOfFrame:estimable?resolved.filter(c=>c.model.size%3!==0).reduce((sum,c)=>sum+c.weight,0)/share:null,components:[...components].map(([size,fraction])=>({size,fraction:estimable?fraction/share:0})),estimable,groups:resolved};
 });
 const annotations=perRef.map((a,h)=>({name:a.name,reference:refs[h].input.sequence,cut:refs[h].input.cut,cuts:refs[h].input.cuts,direction:refs[h].reverse?'reverse' as const:'forward' as const,share:a.share,estimable:a.estimable,components:a.groups.map(c=>({size:c.model.size,fraction:a.share?c.weight/a.share:0,events:ambiguous.includes(c)?[]:eventsInInput(refs[h],c.model.events),unresolved:ambiguous.includes(c)}))}));
 const totalNoEdit=chosen.filter(c=>!c.model.events.length).reduce((sum,c)=>sum+c.weight,0),sizes=new Map<number,number>();chosen.forEach(c=>sizes.set(c.model.size,(sizes.get(c.model.size)||0)+c.weight));
 const targetResults=perRef.flatMap((a,h)=>refs[h].input.cuts?.map((cut,t)=>({reference:a.name,target:t+1,cut,edited:a.estimable?a.groups.filter(c=>eventsInInput(refs[h],c.model.events).some(e=>e.target===t+1||e.target===0)).reduce((sum,c)=>sum+c.weight,0)/a.share:null}))||[{reference:a.name,target:1,cut:refs[h].input.cut,edited:a.indel}]);
 const plots=[{trace:input.control,trim:ctlReg.trim},{trace:input.sample,trim:sampleReg.trim}].map(({trace,trim})=>{const p=plotTrace(trace,first-trim,Math.min(400,Math.max(...refs.flatMap(r=>r.cuts))-first+s.window/2));p.cuts=refs.flatMap(r=>r.cuts.map((cut,t)=>({name:`${r.name} T${r.reverse?r.cuts.length-t:t+1}`,x:p.cutX+(cut-first)*13}))).filter(c=>c.x<=p.width);return p;});
 return {method:'pooled',referenceName:refs.length===1?refs[0].name:'Mixed amplicons',sampleName:input.sample.name,controlName:input.control.name,direction:'forward',cutPosition:first,alignmentIdentity:0,alignment:[],variants:[],plots,warnings,synthetic:!!input.synthetic,annotations,
  diagnostics:{algorithmVersion:'sanger-joint-v1',signalModel:'calibrated',controlCalibration:cc,sampleCalibration:sc,requestedMaxIndel:s.maxIndel,contextEmission:context},
  mixture:{components:[...sizes].map(([size,fraction])=>({size,fraction})),wt:totalNoEdit,indel:1-totalNoEdit,inFrame:chosen.filter(c=>c.model.events.length&&c.model.size%3===0).reduce((sum,c)=>sum+c.weight,0),outOfFrame:chosen.filter(c=>c.model.size%3!==0).reduce((sum,c)=>sum+c.weight,0),r2:full.r2,rmse:full.rmse,upstreamError:1-sc.validationR2,bases:positions.length,fitStart:positions[0],fitEnd:positions.at(-1)!+1,converged:fit.converged,passesChecks:fit.converged&&finalTest.r2>=.9&&!attributionAmbiguous.length&&!boundary&&selectedGeometry.condition!==null&&selectedGeometry.condition<=100},
  pooled:{preflight:report,controlR2:controlScore.r2,sampleTrim:sampleReg.trim,controlTrim:ctlReg.trim,amplicons:perRef.map(({estimable,groups,...a})=>a)},
  joint:{groups,targets:targetResults,heldOutR2:finalTest.r2,selectedModels:chosen.length,candidateModels:models.length,iterations,maxDeletion:s.largeDeletions?(s.maxDeletion??200):undefined,ambiguousFraction:ambiguous.reduce((sum,c)=>sum+c.weight,0)}};
}

END MODULE


BEGIN MODULE src/app/workers/core/sanger-junction-evidence.ts
import {pathKey,isWildType} from './sanger-junction-paths';
import {emissionFamilies} from './sanger-signal';
import {fitJunctionMixture,JunctionFit} from './sanger-junction-mixture';
import {fitEmissionMixture} from './sanger-emission';

export interface JunctionPredictionEvidence {validationR2?:number[];foldGains:number[];lengthFoldGains:number[];passed:boolean;lengthPassed:boolean}
type ForwardInput=Omit<Parameters<typeof fitJunctionMixture>[0],'fit'|'classes'>;
const key=(m:{reference:number;size:number;sizes?:number[]})=>`${m.reference}:${pathKey(m)}`;
/** Relearn the SAME physical junction model on complementary contiguous cycle
 * blocks. Insertion bases and origin allocations are never copied from the
 * full-data fit. Validate selected lengths against a training-only null refit.
 * This is a conditional predictive guard, not a probability of biological truth.
 * All cycles still enter the final point estimate and one validation fold.
 */
export function junctionPredictionEvidence(input:ForwardInput,full:JunctionFit,targets:number[]):Map<number,JunctionPredictionEvidence>{
 const answer=new Map(targets.map(i=>[i,{validationR2:[] as number[],foldGains:[] as number[],lengthFoldGains:[] as number[],passed:false,lengthPassed:false}]));
 if(!targets.length)return answer;
 const families=emissionFamilies(full.columns,1e-6);
 const active=full.models.flatMap((m,i)=>full.weights[i]>1e-8||isWildType(m)?[m]:[]).map(m=>({reference:m.reference,size:m.size,sizes:m.sizes}));
 const block=Math.max(2,Math.ceil(Math.cbrt(input.positions.length)));
 const phase=input.positions.map(p=>Math.floor(p/block)%2);
 const loss=(columns:number[][],observed:number[],weights:number[],precision:number[])=>{
  let value=0;for(let p=0;p<observed.length/4;p++){
   const predicted=[0,0,0,0];for(let i=0;i<columns.length;i++)if(weights[i])for(let b=0;b<4;b++)predicted[b]+=weights[i]*columns[i][4*p+b];
   const total=predicted.reduce((s,v)=>s+v,0);for(let b=0;b<4;b++)value+=precision[p]*(observed[4*p+b]-(total?predicted[b]/total:0))**2;
  }return value;
 };
 for(let fold=0;fold<2;fold++){
  const train=input.positions.flatMap((_,i)=>phase[i]!==fold?[i]:[]),valid=input.positions.flatMap((_,i)=>phase[i]===fold?[i]:[]);
  if(train.length<30||valid.length<30)continue;
  const y=train.flatMap(i=>input.observed.slice(4*i,4*i+4)),weights=train.map(i=>input.positionWeights?.[i]??1);
  const fitted=fitJunctionMixture({...input,classes:active,positions:train.map(i=>input.positions[i]),observed:y,positionWeights:weights,fit:(c,y,w)=>fitEmissionMixture(c,y,w,24)});
  const validationColumns=fitted.predictColumns(valid.map(i=>input.positions[i])),validation=valid.flatMap(i=>input.observed.slice(4*i,4*i+4)),precision=valid.map(i=>input.positionWeights?.[i]??1),fullLoss=loss(validationColumns,validation,fitted.weights,precision);
  const mean=validation.reduce((s,v)=>s+v,0)/validation.length,variance=validation.reduce((s,v,k)=>s+precision[k>>2]*(v-mean)**2,0),validationR2=variance?1-fullLoss/variance:0;for(const e of answer.values())e.validationR2.push(validationR2);
  const lengthLosses=new Map<string,number>();
  for(const index of targets){
   const id=fitted.models.findIndex(m=>key(m)===key(full.models[index]));
   if(id<0){answer.get(index)!.foldGains.push(0);answer.get(index)!.lengthFoldGains.push(0);continue;}
   const family=families.find(g=>g.includes(index))||[index],exclude=new Set(family.filter(i=>!isWildType(full.models[i])).map(i=>key(full.models[i])));
   const keep=fitted.models.flatMap((m,i)=>exclude.has(key(m))?[]:[i]);
   const nullFit=fitEmissionMixture(keep.map(i=>fitted.columns[i]),y,weights,24);
   const nullLoss=loss(keep.map(i=>validationColumns[i]),validation,nullFit.weights,precision);
   answer.get(index)!.foldGains.push(nullLoss-fullLoss);
   const size=pathKey(full.models[index]);
   if(!lengthLosses.has(size)){
    const same=full.models.flatMap((m,i)=>pathKey(m)===size?[i]:[]),aliases=new Set(families.filter(g=>g.some(i=>same.includes(i))).flat()),excluded=new Set([...aliases].filter(i=>!isWildType(full.models[i])).map(i=>key(full.models[i]))),keepLength=fitted.models.flatMap((m,i)=>excluded.has(key(m))?[]:[i]),nullLength=fitEmissionMixture(keepLength.map(i=>fitted.columns[i]),y,weights,24);
    lengthLosses.set(size,loss(keepLength.map(i=>validationColumns[i]),validation,nullLength.weights,precision));
   }
   answer.get(index)!.lengthFoldGains.push(lengthLosses.get(size)!-fullLoss);
  }
 }
 for(const evidence of answer.values()){evidence.passed=evidence.foldGains.length===2&&evidence.foldGains.every(v=>v>1e-8*input.positions.length);evidence.lengthPassed=evidence.lengthFoldGains.length===2&&evidence.lengthFoldGains.every(v=>v>1e-8*input.positions.length);}
 return answer;
}

END MODULE


BEGIN MODULE src/app/workers/core/sanger-junction-mixture.ts
import {SangerAnnotation} from '../../models/sanger-analysis';
import {fitJunctionPaths,PathEvent,pathAnnotations} from './sanger-junction-paths';
import {leftEquivalentDeletionStart} from '../../utils/sanger-placement';
import {controlContextProbability,controlKnownContextEmission,ControlResponseProfile} from './sanger-control-response';
import {reverseComplement} from '../../utils/biology.utils';
import {fitOrderedOnsets} from './sanger-onset-fit';
import {transitionProbabilityEmission} from './sanger-transition-response';
import {SimplexFit,mixPhase,phaseNeighbor} from './sanger-signal';
import {fitEmissionMixture,emissionVector} from './sanger-emission';

export interface JunctionReference {contextResponse?:ControlResponseProfile;sequence:string;cut:number;cuts?:number[];donors?:number[][];transitionResponse?:number[][];baseYields?:number[]}
export interface JunctionModel {reference:number;size:number;sizes?:number[];path?:PathEvent[];start:number;inserted:string;probabilities?:number[][];origins?:{start:number;weight:number;probabilities?:number[][]}[]}
export interface JunctionFit extends SimplexFit {searchComplete?:boolean;candidateCount?:number;objectiveHistory?:number[];models:JunctionModel[];columns:number[][];iterations:number;duplicateLengths:number;objective:number;predictColumns:(positions:number[])=>number[][]}
export interface JunctionRead {positions:number[];rowIds:number[];references:JunctionReference[];mappings:{reverse:boolean;offset:number}[];lag:number;theta:number[]}
export interface JunctionInput {reads?:JunctionRead[];classes?:{reference:number;size:number;sizes?:number[]}[];references:JunctionReference[];positions:number[];observed:number[];maxDeletion:number;maxInsertion:number;radius:number;lag:number;theta:number[];positionWeights?:number[];fit:(columns:number[][],observed:number[],weights?:number[])=>SimplexFit}
const bases='ACGT';
/** A physical deletion is [start,start+length), on either side of the cut.
 * The radius bounds distance of that interval from the cut, not its direction. */
export function junctionStarts(cut:number,size:number,radius:number,length:number):number[]{
 const lo=Math.max(0,cut-Math.max(0,-size)-radius),hi=Math.min(cut+radius,length-Math.max(0,-size));
 return Array.from({length:Math.max(0,hi-lo+1)},(_,i)=>lo+i);
}
