/** Count one candidate per distinct deleted DNA, retaining an allowed origin. */
export function moleculeJunctionStarts(sequence:string,cut:number,size:number,radius:number):number[]{
 const starts=junctionStarts(cut,size,radius,sequence.length);if(size>=0)return starts;
 const unique=new Map<number,number>();for(const start of starts){const canonical=leftEquivalentDeletionStart(sequence,start,-size);if(!unique.has(canonical))unique.set(canonical,start);}return [...unique.values()];
}
export function junctionBase(reference:string,model:JunctionModel,p:number):string {
 if(p<model.start)return reference[p]||'';
 if(model.size<0)return reference[p-model.size]||'';
 return p<model.start+model.size?model.inserted[p-model.start]||'N':reference[p-model.size]||'';
}
/** Exact DNA aliases over the measured span, including neighbouring contexts.
 * Compare physical origins as well as fitted composite columns: different
 * origin mixtures must not hide an identical-DNA alternative length in repeats.
 * Including gaps between measured cycles makes this check conservative. */
export function aliasedDeletionMass(references:JunctionReference[],models:JunctionModel[],weights:number[],positions:number[],maxDeletion:number,radius:number):number {
 const lo=Math.max(0,Math.min(...positions)-1),hi=Math.max(...positions)+2;
 let mass=0;
 references.forEach((ref,h)=>{
  const active=models.flatMap((m,i)=>m.reference===h&&!m.path&&m.size<0&&weights[i]>.01?[i]:[]);if(!active.length)return;
  const signature=(start:number,length:number)=>(ref.sequence.slice(0,start)+ref.sequence.slice(start+length)).slice(lo,hi);
  const lengths=new Map<string,number|undefined>();
  for(let length=1;length<=Math.min(maxDeletion,ref.sequence.length);length++)for(const start of moleculeJunctionStarts(ref.sequence,ref.cut,-length,radius)){
   const key=signature(start,length);if(!lengths.has(key))lengths.set(key,length);else if(lengths.get(key)!==length)lengths.set(key,undefined);
  }
  for(const i of active){const m=models[i],origins=m.origins?.length?m.origins:[{start:m.start,weight:1}],total=origins.reduce((s,o)=>s+o.weight,0);
   for(const o of origins)if(lengths.has(signature(o.start,-m.size))&&lengths.get(signature(o.start,-m.size))===undefined)mass+=weights[i]*o.weight/total;
  }
 });
 return mass;
}
/** WT and edited molecules are evaluated on the SAME measured cycles. Neither
 * the requested indel range nor possible insertion origins mask observations. */
export function fitJunctionMixture(input:JunctionInput):JunctionFit {
 if(input.reads||input.references.some(r=>(r.cuts?.length||1)>1))return fitJunctionPaths(input);
 const {references:refs,positions,observed,maxDeletion,maxInsertion,radius,lag,theta,positionWeights,fit:fitter}=input;
 if(positions.length*4!==observed.length||!positions.length)throw new Error('Invalid junction-fit observations.');
 const rowAt=new Map(positions.map((p,i)=>[p,i]));
 // Empirical control responses retain instrument-specific dye shapes for single
 // loci. A changed junction or an unmeasured donor uses a WT triplet response,
 // learned from the control, rather than excluding that sample observation.
 const responses=refs.map(ref=>{
  const groups=new Map<string,number[][]>();
  ref.donors?.forEach((v,p)=>{if(!v.some(x=>x>0))return;for(const key of [ref.sequence.slice(p-1,p+2),ref.sequence[p]]){if(!key)continue;const g=groups.get(key)||[];g.push(v);groups.set(key,g);}});
  return new Map([...groups].map(([key,values])=>[key,Array.from({length:4},(_,b)=>{const a=values.map(v=>v[b]).sort((a,b)=>a-b);return a[Math.floor(a.length/2)];})]));
 });
 const vector=(m:JunctionModel,p:number):number[]=>{
  const ref=refs[m.reference];
  const probabilities=(at:number):number[]=>{
   if(m.size>0&&at>=m.start&&at<m.start+m.size&&m.probabilities)return m.probabilities[at-m.start];
   const base=junctionBase(ref.sequence,m,at);return Array.from(bases,b=>base==='N'?.25:Number(b===base));
  };
  if(ref.contextResponse){
   const one=(at:number)=>{
    const profile=ref.contextResponse!,wildcard=profile.sites?.[at]?.[-1];
    if(wildcard&&[-1,0,1].map(d=>junctionBase(ref.sequence,m,at+d)).join('')===ref.sequence.slice(Math.max(0,at-1),at+2))return wildcard;
    if(!m.probabilities||at+1<m.start||at-1>=m.start+m.size)return controlKnownContextEmission(junctionBase(ref.sequence,m,at-1),junctionBase(ref.sequence,m,at),junctionBase(ref.sequence,m,at+1),profile,at);
    return controlContextProbability(probabilities(at-1),probabilities(at),probabilities(at+1),profile,at);
   };
   return lag===0?one(p):mixPhase(one(p),one(phaseNeighbor(p,lag)),lag);
  }
  if(!ref.donors){
   if(ref.transitionResponse){const one=(at:number)=>transitionProbabilityEmission(probabilities(at-1),probabilities(at),probabilities(at+1),theta,ref.transitionResponse!,ref.baseYields||[1,1,1,1]),a=one(p),b=one(phaseNeighbor(p,lag));return mixPhase(a,b,lag);}
   const yieldAt=(at:number)=>{const left=probabilities(at-1),current=probabilities(at),right=probabilities(at+1);if(theta.length>8){let yieldValue=0;for(let l=0;l<4;l++)if(left[l])for(let b=0;b<4;b++)if(current[b])for(let r=0;r<4;r++)if(right[r])yieldValue+=left[l]*current[b]*right[r]*Math.exp((theta[l]||0)+(theta[4+r]||0)+(theta[8+16*l+4*b+r]||0));return yieldValue||1;}const a=left.reduce((s,v,b)=>v?s+v*Math.exp(theta[b]||0):s,0),b=right.reduce((s,v,b)=>v?s+v*Math.exp(theta[4+b]||0):s,0);return (a||1)*(b||1);};
   const current=probabilities(p),previous=probabilities(phaseNeighbor(p,lag)),a=yieldAt(p),b=yieldAt(phaseNeighbor(p,lag));
   return mixPhase(current.map(v=>v*a),previous.map(v=>v*b),lag);
  }
  const one=(at:number)=>{
   const base=junctionBase(ref.sequence,m,at);if(!base)return [0,0,0,0];
   if(m.size>0&&at>=m.start&&at<m.start+m.size&&m.probabilities)return probabilities(at).reduce((sum,w,b)=>{const response=responses[m.reference].get(bases[b])||Array.from(bases,x=>Number(x===bases[b])),total=response.reduce((s,v)=>s+v,0);return sum.map((v,k)=>v+w*response[k]/Math.max(1e-12,total));},[0,0,0,0]);
   const donor=at<m.start?at:m.size<0?at-m.size:at>=m.start+m.size?at-m.size:-1;
   const context=[-1,0,1].map(d=>junctionBase(ref.sequence,m,at+d)).join('');
   if(donor>=0&&ref.donors![donor]?.some(v=>v>0)&&context===ref.sequence.slice(donor-1,donor+2))return ref.donors![donor];
   const response=responses[m.reference].get(context)||responses[m.reference].get(base)||Array.from(bases,b=>Number(b===base));
   const sum=response.reduce((s,v)=>s+v,0);return response.map(v=>sum?v/sum:0);
  };
  const current=one(p),previous=one(phaseNeighbor(p,lag));return mixPhase(current,previous,lag);
 };
 const column=(m:JunctionModel)=>positions.flatMap(p=>vector(m,p));
 const models:JunctionModel[]=[];
 refs.forEach((ref,h)=>{
  for(let size=-maxDeletion;size<=maxInsertion;size++){
   if(input.classes&&!input.classes.some(c=>c.reference===h&&c.size===size))continue;
   const start=size===0?ref.cut:Math.max(0,ref.cut-Math.floor(Math.max(0,-size)/2));
   const inserted=size>0?'N'.repeat(size):'';
   models.push({reference:h,size,start,inserted,probabilities:size>0?Array.from({length:size},()=>[.25,.25,.25,.25]):undefined});
  }
 });
 interface Origin {start:number;weight:number;probabilities?:number[][]}
 interface Group {profiled?:boolean;indices:number[];starts:number[];origins:Origin[];rows:number[]}
 const groups:Group[]=[],byKey=new Map<string,Group>();
 models.forEach((m,i)=>{
  if(!m.size)return;
  const ref=refs[m.reference],starts=moleculeJunctionStarts(ref.sequence,ref.cut,m.size,radius);if(!starts.length)return;const lo=starts[0]-4,hi=starts[starts.length-1]+Math.max(0,-m.size)+5;
  // When the local WT sequence and read-axis coordinates are identical,
  // fluorescence cannot phase an insertion/onset to a distant homeolog SNP.
  // Estimate the observable origin/base marginal once, not an arbitrary hard
  // origin per homeolog. Different local reference sequences remain separate.
  const key=[m.size,ref.cut,ref.donors?m.reference:ref.sequence.slice(Math.max(0,lo),hi)].join(':');
  let group=byKey.get(key);
  if(!group){
   const centre=starts.reduce((best,p)=>Math.abs(p-m.start)<Math.abs(best-m.start)?p:best,starts[0]);
   const origins=starts.map(start=>({start,weight:Number(start===centre),probabilities:m.size>0?Array.from({length:m.size},()=>[.25,.25,.25,.25]):undefined}));
   const low=starts[0]-2,high=starts[starts.length-1]+Math.max(0,m.size)+3,rows:number[]=[];for(let p=low;p<high;p++){const at=rowAt.get(p);if(at!==undefined)rows.push(at);}
   group={indices:[],starts,origins,rows};byKey.set(key,group);groups.push(group);
  }
  group.indices.push(i);
 });
 const mixedColumn=(i:number,g:Group,previous?:number[])=>{
  const m=models[i],first=g.origins.find(o=>o.weight>0)!,out=previous?[...previous]:column({...m,start:first.start,probabilities:first.probabilities});
  for(const row of g.rows){const v=[0,0,0,0];for(const o of g.origins)if(o.weight>1e-12){const at=vector({...m,start:o.start,probabilities:o.probabilities},positions[row]);for(let b=0;b<4;b++)v[b]+=o.weight*at[b];}for(let b=0;b<4;b++)out[4*row+b]=v[b];}
  return out;
 };
 let columns=models.map(column);for(const g of groups)for(const i of g.indices)columns[i]=mixedColumn(i,g,columns[i]);
 let fit=fitter(columns,observed,positionWeights),iterations=0;
 const error=(cols:number[][],y:number[],weights:number[],precision?:number[])=>{let result=0;for(let row=0;row<y.length/4;row++){const v=[0,0,0,0];for(let i=0;i<cols.length;i++)if(weights[i])for(let b=0;b<4;b++)v[b]+=weights[i]*cols[i][4*row+b];const total=v.reduce((s,x)=>s+x,0);for(let b=0;b<4;b++)result+=(precision?.[row]??1)*(y[4*row+b]-(total?v[b]/total:0))**2;}return result;};
 const objective=(cols:number[][],weights:number[])=>error(cols,observed,weights,positionWeights);
 let best=objective(columns,fit.weights);const objectiveHistory=[best];
 for(let round=0;round<12;round++){
  iterations++;
  const sum=Array(observed.length).fill(0);for(let a=0;a<columns.length;a++)if(fit.weights[a])for(let k=0;k<sum.length;k++)sum[k]+=fit.weights[a]*columns[a][k];
  for(const g of groups){
   const mass=g.indices.reduce((sum,i)=>sum+fit.weights[i],0);if(mass<1e-8||!g.rows.length)continue;
   const representative=models[g.indices[0]],other=g.rows.flatMap(row=>Array.from({length:4},(_,b)=>sum[4*row+b]-g.indices.reduce((s,i)=>s+fit.weights[i]*columns[i][4*row+b],0)));
   const variants=g.origins.map(o=>g.rows.flatMap(row=>vector({...representative,start:o.start,probabilities:o.probabilities},positions[row])));
   const localY=g.rows.flatMap(i=>observed.slice(4*i,4*i+4)),localWeights=g.rows.map(i=>positionWeights?.[i]??1);
   const refine=(o:number,contribution:number,current:number[],sweeps:number)=>{
    const origin=g.origins[o];if(contribution<1e-8)return;
    for(let sweep=0;sweep<sweeps;sweep++){
     let delta=0;
     for(let j=0;j<representative.size;j++){
      const centre=origin.start+j,ids=g.rows.flatMap((row,k)=>positions[row]>=centre-2&&positions[row]<=centre+2?[k]:[]);if(!ids.length)continue;
      const trials=Array.from({length:4},(_,b)=>({...representative,start:origin.start,probabilities:origin.probabilities!.map((v,k)=>k===j?Array.from({length:4},(_,x)=>Number(x===b)):v)}));
      const basis=trials.map(trial=>ids.flatMap(k=>{const v=vector(trial,positions[g.rows[k]]);return v.map((x,b)=>current[4*k+b]+contribution*(x-variants[o][4*k+b]));}));
      const y=ids.flatMap(k=>localY.slice(4*k,4*k+4)),w=ids.map(k=>localWeights[k]),previous=origin.probabilities![j],estimate=fitEmissionMixture(basis,y,w,24);
      if(error(basis,y,estimate.weights,w)>error(basis,y,previous,w)+1e-12)continue;
      delta=Math.max(delta,...estimate.weights.map((v,b)=>Math.abs(v-previous[b])));origin.probabilities=origin.probabilities!.map((v,k)=>k===j?estimate.weights:v);
      for(const k of ids){const v=vector({...representative,start:origin.start,probabilities:origin.probabilities},positions[g.rows[k]]);for(let b=0;b<4;b++){current[4*k+b]+=contribution*(v[b]-variants[o][4*k+b]);variants[o][4*k+b]=v[b];}}
     }
     if(delta<1e-6)break;
    }
   };
   // Profile every insertion origin before assigning mass: an origin with an
   // initially unknown inserted sequence must not lose solely to its warm start.
   if(representative.size>0&&!g.profiled){
    for(let o=0;o<g.origins.length;o++)refine(o,mass,other.map((v,k)=>v+mass*variants[o][k]),5);
    g.profiled=true;
   }
   const originColumns=variants.map(c=>c.map((v,k)=>other[k]+mass*v)),previousOriginWeights=g.origins.map(o=>o.weight),ordered=null;
   const originWeights=ordered||fitEmissionMixture(originColumns,localY,localWeights,24).weights;
   if(error(originColumns,localY,originWeights,localWeights)<=error(originColumns,localY,previousOriginWeights,localWeights)+1e-12)g.origins.forEach((o,i)=>o.weight=originWeights[i]);
   if(representative.size>0){const current=other.map((v,k)=>v+mass*variants.reduce((s,c,i)=>s+g.origins[i].weight*c[k],0));for(let o=0;o<g.origins.length;o++)refine(o,mass*g.origins[o].weight,current,3);}
   for(const i of g.indices){const next=mixedColumn(i,g,columns[i]);for(const row of g.rows)for(let b=0;b<4;b++)sum[4*row+b]+=fit.weights[i]*(next[4*row+b]-columns[i][4*row+b]);columns[i]=next;}
  }
  const next=fitter(columns,observed,positionWeights);if(objective(columns,next.weights)<=objective(columns,fit.weights)+1e-12)fit=next;const value=objective(columns,fit.weights);objectiveHistory.push(value);
  if(best-value<1e-8){best=value;break;}best=value;
 }
 for(const g of groups){const largest=g.origins.reduce((a,b)=>a.weight>b.weight?a:b);for(const i of g.indices)models[i]={...models[i],start:largest.start,probabilities:largest.probabilities,origins:g.origins.filter(o=>o.weight>1e-7).map(o=>({...o}))};}
 let duplicateLengths=0;
 for(let a=0;a<models.length;a++)if(models[a].size!==0){const wt=models.findIndex(m=>m.reference===models[a].reference&&m.size===0);if(columns[a].every((v,k)=>Math.abs(v-columns[wt][k])<1e-8))duplicateLengths++;}
const predictColumns=(rows:number[])=>models.map(m=>rows.flatMap(p=>{if(!m.origins)return vector(m,p);const v=[0,0,0,0],mass=m.origins.reduce((s,o)=>s+o.weight,0);for(const o of m.origins){const x=vector({...m,start:o.start,probabilities:o.probabilities},p);for(let b=0;b<4;b++)v[b]+=o.weight*x[b]/mass;}return v;}));
 const mean=observed.reduce((s,v)=>s+v,0)/observed.length,variance=observed.reduce((s,v)=>s+(v-mean)**2,0),residual=error(columns,observed,fit.weights);
 return {...fit,r2:variance?1-residual/variance:0,rmse:Math.sqrt(residual/observed.length),models,columns,iterations,duplicateLengths,objective:best,objectiveHistory,predictColumns};
}

/** Render the actual fitted intervals, rather than a deletion forced to one
 * side of the cut. These remain model alternatives, not phased read alleles. */
export function junctionAnnotationComponents(models:JunctionModel[],weights:number[],origin:number,referenceLength:number,reverse=false):SangerAnnotation['components']{
 return models.flatMap<SangerAnnotation['components'][number]>((m,i)=>{
  if(weights[i]<=1e-10)return [];
  if(m.path)return pathAnnotations(m,weights[i],origin,referenceLength,reverse);
  const origins=m.origins?.length?m.origins:[{start:m.start,weight:1}],total=origins.reduce((s,o)=>s+o.weight,0);
  return origins.flatMap(o=>{if(o.weight<=1e-10)return [];let a=origin+o.start,b=a+Math.max(0,-m.size),inserted=m.inserted;if(reverse){[a,b]=[referenceLength-b,referenceLength-a];inserted=reverseComplement(inserted);}return [{size:m.size,fraction:weights[i]*o.weight/total,events:m.size?[{target:1,start:a,end:b,inserted:Math.max(0,m.size),insertedSequence:inserted,insertedBaseProbabilities:o.probabilities?(reverse?[...o.probabilities].reverse().map(v=>[...v].reverse()):o.probabilities.map(v=>[...v])):undefined}]:[]}];});
 });
}

END MODULE


BEGIN MODULE src/app/workers/core/sanger-junction-paths.ts
import {screenPathCandidates} from './sanger-path-screen';
import {JunctionInput,JunctionFit,JunctionModel,JunctionReference,junctionStarts,moleculeJunctionStarts} from './sanger-junction-mixture';
import {controlKnownContextEmission,controlContextProbability} from './sanger-control-response';
import {fitEmissionMixture,emissionVector} from './sanger-emission';
import {mixPhase,phaseNeighbor} from './sanger-signal';
export interface PathOrigin {start:number;weight:number;probabilities?:number[][]}
export interface PathEvent {target:number;size:number;origins:PathOrigin[];profiled?:boolean}
const bases='ACGT',copy=(v:number[][]|undefined)=>v?.map(a=>[...a]);
export const pathKey=(m:{size:number;sizes?:number[]})=>m.sizes?.join('/')??String(m.size);
export const isWildType=(m:{size:number;sizes?:number[]})=>m.sizes?m.sizes.every(v=>v===0):m.size===0;
/** Ordered physical events on the ORIGINAL reference. Every later measured
 * onset includes the accumulated sizes of all preceding events. */
export function pathBase(sequence:string,events:{start:number;size:number;probabilities?:number[][]}[],p:number):number[]{
 let delta=0;for(const e of events){const start=e.start+delta;if(p<start)break;if(e.size>0&&p<start+e.size)return e.probabilities?.[p-start]||[.25,.25,.25,.25];delta+=e.size;}
 const b=sequence[p-delta];return Array.from(bases,x=>b==='N'?.25:Number(x===b));
}
function combinations(events:PathEvent[]){
 let rows:{events:{target:number;start:number;size:number;probabilities?:number[][]}[];weight:number}[]=[{events:[],weight:1}];
 for(const e of events){const next:typeof rows=[];for(const a of rows)for(const o of e.origins){if(o.weight<=1e-9)continue;const previous=a.events.at(-1);if(previous&&o.start<previous.start+Math.max(0,-previous.size))continue;next.push({events:[...a.events,{target:e.target,start:o.start,size:e.size,probabilities:o.probabilities}],weight:a.weight*o.weight});}rows=next;}
 const total=rows.reduce((s,x)=>s+x.weight,0);return rows.map(x=>({...x,weight:x.weight/Math.max(1e-12,total)}));
}
export function pathColumn(ref:JunctionReference,m:JunctionModel,positions:number[],lag:number,theta:number[]):number[]{
 const choices=combinations(m.path||[]),out=Array(positions.length*4).fill(0);
 for(const choice of choices){
  if(positions.length<48){
   const one=(p:number)=>{const pi=[-1,0,1].map(d=>pathBase(ref.sequence,choice.events,p+d));if(!ref.contextResponse)return pi[1];const hard=pi.map(v=>v.findIndex(x=>x===1));return hard.every(v=>v>=0)?controlKnownContextEmission(bases[hard[0]],bases[hard[1]],bases[hard[2]],ref.contextResponse,p):controlContextProbability(pi[0],pi[1],pi[2],ref.contextResponse,p);};
   positions.forEach((p,j)=>{const v=lag?mixPhase(one(p),one(phaseNeighbor(p,lag)),lag):one(p);for(let b=0;b<4;b++)out[4*j+b]+=choice.weight*v[b];});continue;
  }
  const parts:string[]=[],soft=new Map<number,number[]>();let at=0,delta=0;
  for(const event of choice.events){
   parts.push(ref.sequence.slice(at,event.start));
   if(event.size>0){let inserted='';for(let j=0;j<event.size;j++){const pi=event.probabilities?.[j]||[.25,.25,.25,.25],hard=pi.findIndex(v=>v===1);inserted+=hard<0?'N':bases[hard];if(hard<0)soft.set(event.start+delta+j,pi);}parts.push(inserted);}
   at=event.start+Math.max(0,-event.size);delta+=event.size;
  }
  parts.push(ref.sequence.slice(at));const sequence=parts.join('');
  const probability=(p:number)=>soft.get(p)||Array.from(bases,b=>Number(sequence[p]===b));
  const one=(p:number)=>{
   if(!ref.contextResponse)return probability(p);
   const left=sequence[p-1]||'',current=sequence[p]||'',right=sequence[p+1]||'';
   return left!=='N'&&current!=='N'&&right!=='N'?controlKnownContextEmission(left,current,right,ref.contextResponse,p):controlContextProbability(probability(p-1),probability(p),probability(p+1),ref.contextResponse,p);
  };
  positions.forEach((p,j)=>{const a=one(p),v=lag?mixPhase(a,one(phaseNeighbor(p,lag)),lag):a;for(let b=0;b<4;b++)out[4*j+b]+=choice.weight*v[b];});
 }
 return out;
}
/** Multi-target composition of the calibrated junction model, using its same
 * normalized-mixture regression and conditional origin/base updates. Candidate
 * columns are generated in bounded working sets; no observations are masked. */

/** Map the SAME physical edited molecule to another primer/read axis. Sizes
 * retain their target labels; reverse reads reverse/complement inserted bases. */
export function mapPathModel(m:JunctionModel,map:{reverse:boolean;offset:number}):JunctionModel {
 const paths=(m.path||[]).map(e=>({...e,origins:e.origins.map(o=>({...o,start:map.reverse?map.offset-o.start-Math.max(0,-e.size):map.offset+o.start,probabilities:o.probabilities?(map.reverse?[...o.probabilities].reverse().map(v=>[...v].reverse()):copy(o.probabilities)):undefined}))}));
 return {...m,path:map.reverse?paths.reverse():paths};
}
const readRows=new WeakMap<JunctionInput,Map<number,{read:number;p:number}>>();
function axes(input:JunctionInput){let a=readRows.get(input);if(!a){a=new Map;input.reads!.forEach((r,read)=>r.rowIds.forEach((id,j)=>a!.set(id,{read,p:r.positions[j]})));readRows.set(input,a);}return a;}
export function jointPathColumn(input:JunctionInput,m:JunctionModel,rows=input.positions):number[]{
 if(!input.reads)return pathColumn(input.references[m.reference],m,rows,input.lag,input.theta);
 const axis=axes(input),out=Array(rows.length*4).fill(0);
 input.reads.forEach((read,index)=>{const ids=rows.flatMap((id,j)=>axis.get(id)?.read===index?[j]:[]),positions=ids.map(j=>axis.get(rows[j])!.p),mapped=mapPathModel(m,read.mappings[m.reference]),values=pathColumn(read.references[m.reference],mapped,positions,read.lag,read.theta);ids.forEach((row,k)=>{for(let b=0;b<4;b++)out[4*row+b]=values[4*k+b];});});return out;
}
function originRows(input:JunctionInput,m:JunctionModel,target:number,size:number,starts:number[],delta:number):number[]{
 if(!input.reads){const lo=starts[0]+delta-2,hi=starts.at(-1)!+delta+Math.max(0,size)+3;return input.positions.flatMap((p,j)=>p>=lo&&p<hi?[j]:[]);}
 const axis=axes(input);return input.positions.flatMap((id,j)=>{const {read:index,p}=axis.get(id)!,read=input.reads![index],map=read.mappings[m.reference],cuts=read.references[m.reference].cuts!,cut=cuts[target-1],shift=cuts.reduce((s,c,t)=>s+(c<cut?(m.sizes?.[t]||0):0),0),a=map.reverse?map.offset-starts.at(-1)!-Math.max(0,-size):map.offset+starts[0],b=map.reverse?map.offset-starts[0]-Math.max(0,-size):map.offset+starts.at(-1)!;return p>=a+shift-2&&p<b+shift+Math.max(0,size)+3?[j]:[];});
}
function refinePathSet(input:JunctionInput,seeds:JunctionModel[]=[]):JunctionFit {
 const {references:refs,positions,observed,positionWeights:precision}=input,n=observed.length;
 const cuts=refs.map(r=>r.cuts||[r.cut]),range=Array.from({length:input.maxDeletion+input.maxInsertion+1},(_,i)=>i-input.maxDeletion);
 const models:JunctionModel[]=[],columns:number[][]=[],known=new Set<string>();let currentWeights:number[]=[];const candidateCount=cuts.reduce((sum,c)=>sum+Math.pow(range.length,c.length),0);
 const make=(reference:number,sizes:number[]):JunctionModel=>({reference,size:sizes.reduce((s,v)=>s+v,0),sizes:[...sizes],start:cuts[reference][0],inserted:'',path:sizes.flatMap((size,t)=>{
  if(!size)return [];const starts=moleculeJunctionStarts(refs[reference].sequence,cuts[reference][t],size,input.radius),centre=cuts[reference][t]-Math.floor(Math.max(0,-size)/2),start=starts.reduce((a,b)=>Math.abs(b-centre)<Math.abs(a-centre)?b:a,starts[0]);
  const donor=[...seeds,...models].map((m,i)=>({m,w:i<seeds.length?1:(currentWeights[i-seeds.length]||0)})).filter(x=>x.m.reference===reference&&x.w>1e-8&&x.m.path?.some(e=>e.target===t+1&&e.size===size)).sort((a,b)=>b.w-a.w)[0]?.m.path?.find(e=>e.target===t+1&&e.size===size);
  return [{target:t+1,size,profiled:donor?.profiled,origins:donor?donor.origins.map(o=>({...o,probabilities:copy(o.probabilities)})):start===undefined?[]:[{start,weight:1,probabilities:size>0?Array.from({length:size},()=>[.25,.25,.25,.25]):undefined}]}];
 }).sort((a,b)=>cuts[reference][a.target-1]-cuts[reference][b.target-1])});
 const key=(m:JunctionModel)=>m.reference+':'+pathKey(m),column=(m:JunctionModel,rows=positions)=>jointPathColumn(input,m,rows);
 const add=(m:JunctionModel,c?:number[])=>{if(known.has(key(m)))return;known.add(key(m));models.push(m);columns.push(c||column(m));};
 for(const c of input.classes||[])add(make(c.reference,c.sizes||[c.size]));
 let fit=input.fit(columns,observed,precision),iterations=0,searchComplete=!!input.classes;
 const loss=(cols:number[][],y:number[],w:number[],q?:number[])=>{let error=0;for(let p=0;p<y.length/4;p++){const v=[0,0,0,0];cols.forEach((c,i)=>{if(w[i])for(let b=0;b<4;b++)v[b]+=w[i]*c[4*p+b];});const total=v.reduce((s,x)=>s+x,0);for(let b=0;b<4;b++)error+=(q?.[p]??1)*(y[4*p+b]-(total?v[b]/total:0))**2;}return error;};
 let objective=loss(columns,observed,fit.weights,precision);const objectiveHistory=[objective];
 // A working-set cap controls memory, not allele fraction. Incomplete searches
 // return numerical estimates and an explicit convergence diagnostic.
 for(let round=0;round<12;round++){
  iterations++;currentWeights=fit.weights;
  const sum=Array(n).fill(0);columns.forEach((c,i)=>{if(fit.weights[i])for(let k=0;k<n;k++)sum[k]+=fit.weights[i]*c[k];});
  for(let id=0;id<models.length;id++){
   const mass=fit.weights[id],m=models[id];if(mass<1e-8||!m.path?.length)continue;
   let delta=0;
   for(const target of cuts[m.reference].map((_,t)=>t).sort((a,b)=>cuts[m.reference][a]-cuts[m.reference][b])){
    const event=m.path.find(e=>e.target===target+1),size=m.sizes![target];if(!event){delta+=size;continue;}
    const starts=moleculeJunctionStarts(refs[m.reference].sequence,cuts[m.reference][target],size,input.radius);if(!starts.length)continue;
    const ids=originRows(input,m,target+1,size,starts,delta);if(!ids.length){delta+=size;continue;}
    const rows=ids.map(j=>positions[j]),y=ids.flatMap(j=>observed.slice(4*j,4*j+4)),q=ids.map(j=>precision?.[j]??1),old=ids.flatMap(j=>columns[id].slice(4*j,4*j+4)),other=ids.flatMap(j=>sum.slice(4*j,4*j+4)).map((v,k)=>v-mass*old[k]);
    const seed=event.origins.reduce((a,b)=>a.weight>=b.weight?a:b),origins=starts.map(start=>{const previous=event.origins.find(o=>o.start===start);return {start,weight:previous?.weight||0,probabilities:copy(previous?.probabilities||seed.probabilities)};});
    const conditional=(origin:PathOrigin)=>{const saved=event.origins;event.origins=[{...origin,weight:1}];const c=column(m,rows);event.origins=saved;return c.map((v,k)=>other[k]+mass*v);};
    const variants=origins.map(conditional);
    const refine=(o:number,current:number[],fraction:number,sweeps:number)=>{
     if(fraction<=1e-8||size<=0)return;
     for(let sweep=0;sweep<sweeps;sweep++){
      let change=0;
      for(let k=0;k<size;k++){
       const basis=Array.from({length:4},(_,base)=>conditional({...origins[o],probabilities:origins[o].probabilities!.map((v,j)=>j===k?Array.from(bases,(_,d)=>Number(base===d)):v)}).map((v,j)=>current[j]+fraction*(v-variants[o][j]))),previous=origins[o].probabilities![k],estimate=fitEmissionMixture(basis,y,q);
       if(loss(basis,y,estimate.weights,q)>loss(basis,y,previous,q)+1e-12)continue;
       change=Math.max(change,...estimate.weights.map((v,b)=>Math.abs(v-previous[b])));origins[o].probabilities![k]=estimate.weights;
       const next=conditional(origins[o]);for(let j=0;j<current.length;j++)current[j]+=fraction*(next[j]-variants[o][j]);variants[o]=next;
      }
      if(change<1e-6)break;
     }
    };
    // As in the single-target engine, profile all unknown insertion origins
    // once, then refine only origins carrying conditional molecular mass.
    if(size>0&&!event.profiled){for(let o=0;o<origins.length;o++)refine(o,[...variants[o]],1,5);event.profiled=true;}
    const candidate=fitEmissionMixture(variants,y,q),before=loss([sum],observed,[1],precision);
    if(size>0){const current=y.map((_,j)=>variants.reduce((s,v,o)=>s+candidate.weights[o]*v[j],0));for(let o=0;o<origins.length;o++)refine(o,current,candidate.weights[o],3);}
    const saved=event.origins;event.origins=origins.map((o,i)=>({...o,weight:candidate.weights[i]})).filter(o=>o.weight>1e-8);const next=column(m),after=loss([sum.map((v,k)=>v+mass*(next[k]-columns[id][k]))],observed,[1],precision);
    if(after<=before+1e-12){for(let k=0;k<n;k++)sum[k]+=mass*(next[k]-columns[id][k]);columns[id]=next;}else event.origins=saved;
    delta+=size;
   }
  }
  const next=input.fit(columns,observed,precision);if(loss(columns,observed,next.weights,precision)<=loss(columns,observed,fit.weights,precision)+1e-12)fit=next;
  const value=loss(columns,observed,fit.weights,precision);objectiveHistory.push(value);
  if(objective-value<1e-8){objective=value;break;}objective=value;
 }
 const predictColumns=(rows:number[])=>models.map(m=>column(m,rows));
 return {...fit,models,columns,iterations,duplicateLengths:0,objective,objectiveHistory,predictColumns,searchComplete,candidateCount};
}

/** Sparse working-set search over the same physical forward model. A proposed
 * compound edit is profiled while it can carry mass, before dense single-edit
 * columns absorb its two observable shift marginals. BIC chooses model size;
 * the retained molecular fractions are then unpenalized estimates. */
export function fitJunctionPaths(input:JunctionInput):JunctionFit {
 if(input.classes)return refinePathSet(input);
 const cuts=input.references.map(r=>r.cuts||[r.cut]),range=Array.from({length:input.maxDeletion+input.maxInsertion+1},(_,i)=>i-input.maxDeletion),count=cuts.reduce((n,c)=>n+range.length**c.length,0);
 const classes=(models:JunctionModel[])=>models.map(m=>({reference:m.reference,size:m.size,sizes:m.sizes}));
 const key=(m:JunctionModel)=>m.reference+':'+pathKey(m);
 const wt=input.references.map((r,h)=>({reference:h,size:0,sizes:cuts[h].map(()=>0)}));
 let current=refinePathSet({...input,classes:wt}),iterations=current.iterations,complete=false;
 const loss=(fit:JunctionFit)=>{let sse=0;for(let p=0;p<input.positions.length;p++){const v=[0,0,0,0];fit.columns.forEach((c,i)=>{if(fit.weights[i])for(let b=0;b<4;b++)v[b]+=fit.weights[i]*c[4*p+b];});const sum=v.reduce((s,v)=>s+v,0);for(let b=0;b<4;b++)sse+=(input.positionWeights?.[p]??1)*(input.observed[4*p+b]-(sum?v[b]/sum:0))**2;}return sse;};
 const bic=(fit:JunctionFit)=>{let df=Math.max(0,fit.weights.filter(w=>w>1e-8).length-1);fit.models.forEach((m,i)=>{if(fit.weights[i]<=1e-8)return;for(const e of m.path||[]){const active=e.origins.filter(o=>o.weight>1e-6);df+=Math.max(0,active.length-1)+active.length*3*Math.max(0,e.size);}});const n=3*input.positions.length;return n*Math.log(Math.max(1e-15,loss(fit)/n))+df*Math.log(n);};
 let cost=bic(current);const history=[loss(current)],startCache=new Map<string,number>();
 const initialStart=(h:number,t:number,size:number)=>{const key=h+':'+t+':'+size;if(startCache.has(key))return startCache.get(key)!;const starts=moleculeJunctionStarts(input.references[h].sequence,cuts[h][t],size,input.radius),centre=cuts[h][t]-Math.floor(Math.max(0,-size)/2),start=starts.reduce((a,b)=>Math.abs(b-centre)<Math.abs(a-centre)?b:a,starts[0]);startCache.set(key,start);return start;};
 const make=(h:number,sizes:number[]):JunctionModel=>({reference:h,size:sizes.reduce((s,v)=>s+v,0),sizes:[...sizes],start:Math.min(...cuts[h]),inserted:'',path:sizes.flatMap((size,t)=>{if(!size)return[];const start=initialStart(h,t,size);return[{target:t+1,size,origins:start===undefined?[]:[{start,weight:1,probabilities:size>0?Array.from({length:size},()=>[.25,.25,.25,.25]):undefined}]}];}).sort((a,b)=>cuts[h][a.target-1]-cuts[h][b.target-1])});
 for(let round=0;round<24;round++){
  const ranked=screenPathCandidates(input,current,range,make);
  let best=current,bestCost=cost;
  for(const candidate of ranked){
   const trial=refinePathSet({...input,classes:[...classes(current.models),{reference:candidate.m.reference,size:candidate.m.size,sizes:candidate.m.sizes}]},current.models),value=bic(trial);iterations+=trial.iterations;
   if(value<bestCost-1e-6){best=trial;bestCost=value;}
  }
  if(best===current){complete=true;break;}
  current=best;cost=bestCost;history.push(loss(current));
  // Remove redundant physical groups after every birth, refitting both origins
  // and fractions. WT remains available for every submitted reference.
  for(let sweep=0;sweep<3;sweep++){
   let reduced=current,reducedCost=cost;
   for(let i=0;i<current.models.length;i++){if(isWildType(current.models[i]))continue;const trial=refinePathSet({...input,classes:classes(current.models.filter((_,j)=>i!==j))},current.models),value=bic(trial);iterations+=trial.iterations;if(value<reducedCost-1e-6){reduced=trial;reducedCost=value;}}
   if(reduced===current)break;current=reduced;cost=reducedCost;
  }
 }
 return{...current,iterations,searchComplete:complete,candidateCount:count,objectiveHistory:history};
}

export function pathAnnotations(m:JunctionModel,fraction:number,origin:number,length:number,reverse:boolean){
 return combinations(m.path||[]).map(c=>({size:m.size,pathSizes:m.sizes,fraction:fraction*c.weight,events:c.events.map(e=>{let start=e.start+origin,end=start+Math.max(0,-e.size);if(reverse)[start,end]=[length-end,length-start];return {target:e.target,start,end,inserted:Math.max(0,e.size),insertedBaseProbabilities:e.probabilities?(reverse?[...e.probabilities].reverse().map(v=>[...v].reverse()):copy(e.probabilities)):undefined};})}));
}

END MODULE


BEGIN MODULE src/app/workers/core/sanger-junction-solution.ts
import {findPathAliases} from './sanger-path-aliases';
import {fitJunctionMixture,JunctionFit,JunctionInput} from './sanger-junction-mixture';
import {refineJunctionSupport,rejectNonpredictiveJunctions} from './sanger-junction-support';
import {junctionPredictionEvidence} from './sanger-junction-evidence';
import {isWildType} from './sanger-junction-paths';
/** Selection/evidence is identical for a single observation block and joined
 * reads. No input read gets to fit a different molecular distribution. */
export function solveJunctions(input:JunctionInput,templateError:(fit:JunctionFit)=>number[]|undefined,evidence=true){
 const junction=fitJunctionMixture(input),unprunedWeights=[...junction.weights];
 const support=evidence?refineJunctionSupport(input,junction,templateError(junction)):undefined;
 const referenceEvidence=support?.referenceScores;
 const predictive=evidence?junctionPredictionEvidence(input,junction,junction.models.flatMap((m,i)=>!isWildType(m)&&junction.weights[i]>1e-8?[i]:[])):undefined;
 const predictionRemoved=predictive?rejectNonpredictiveJunctions(input,junction,predictive,referenceEvidence,support!.scores):[];
 if(predictionRemoved.length)support!.removed.push(...predictionRemoved.filter(i=>!support!.removed.includes(i)));
 const aliases=findPathAliases(input,junction);
 return {aliases,junction,unprunedWeights,support,referenceEvidence,predictive,predictionRemoved,stabilityInput:input,stabilityColumns:junction.columns,coveragePass:undefined as boolean|undefined};
}
export type JunctionSolution=ReturnType<typeof solveJunctions>;

END MODULE


BEGIN MODULE src/app/workers/core/sanger-junction-support.ts
import {pathKey,isWildType} from './sanger-junction-paths';
import {fitJunctionMixture,JunctionFit,JunctionInput,moleculeJunctionStarts} from './sanger-junction-mixture';
import {screenEmissionSupport,EmissionSupportEvidence} from './sanger-emission';
function refitSelected(input:JunctionInput,junction:JunctionFit,keep:number[]) {
 const refit=fitJunctionMixture({...input,classes:keep.map(i=>({reference:junction.models[i].reference,size:junction.models[i].size,sizes:junction.models[i].sizes}))}),weights=junction.models.map(()=>0);
 keep.forEach((id,k)=>{weights[id]=refit.weights[k];junction.models[id]=refit.models[k];junction.columns[id]=refit.columns[k];});
 const originalPredict=junction.predictColumns;junction.predictColumns=rows=>{const out=originalPredict(rows),updated=refit.predictColumns(rows);keep.forEach((id,k)=>out[id]=updated[k]);return out;};
 Object.assign(junction,{weights,r2:refit.r2,rmse:refit.rmse,converged:refit.converged,iterations:junction.iterations+refit.iterations,objective:refit.objective,objectiveHistory:refit.objectiveHistory});
 return {weights,r2:refit.r2,rmse:refit.rmse,converged:refit.converged};
}
/** WT-uncertainty selection followed by the SAME junction/base refit. */
export function refineJunctionSupport(input:JunctionInput,junction:JunctionFit,templateResidual?:number[]) {
 const mandatory=junction.models.flatMap((m,i)=>isWildType(m)?[i]:[]),groups=[...new Set(junction.models.map(pathKey))].map(size=>junction.models.flatMap((m,i)=>pathKey(m)===size?[i]:[]));
 const hypotheses=junction.models.map(m=>m.path?m.path.reduce((n,e)=>n*Math.max(1,moleculeJunctionStarts(input.references[m.reference].sequence,input.references[m.reference].cuts![e.target-1],e.size,input.radius).length),1):m.size?moleculeJunctionStarts(input.references[m.reference].sequence,input.references[m.reference].cut,m.size,input.radius).length:1);
 const support=screenEmissionSupport(junction.columns,input.observed,junction,mandatory,input.positionWeights,groups,hypotheses,{templateResidual});
 if(support.removed.length)support.fit=refitSelected(input,junction,junction.models.flatMap((m,i)=>mandatory.includes(i)||support.fit.weights[i]>1e-10?[i]:[]));
 else junction.weights=support.fit.weights;
 const referenceScores=screenEmissionSupport(junction.columns,input.observed,junction,mandatory,input.positionWeights,junction.models.map((_,i)=>[i]),hypotheses,{refit:false,templateResidual}).scores;
 return {...support,referenceScores};
}
/** A class dominated by its null in BOTH independent prediction folds has no
 * predictive reason to remain in the point model. Keep one-sided uncertainty;
 * never use a user display threshold or remove measured observations. */
export function rejectNonpredictiveJunctions(input:JunctionInput,junction:JunctionFit,evidence:Map<number,{foldGains:number[];lengthFoldGains?:number[]}>,precisionEvidence?:EmissionSupportEvidence[],lengthEvidence?:EmissionSupportEvidence[]):number[] {
 const tolerance=1e-8*input.positions.length;
 const removed=[...evidence].flatMap(([i,e])=>{
  if(isWildType(junction.models[i])||e.foldGains.length!==2)return [];
  const precise=(scores:EmissionSupportEvidence[]|undefined)=>{const v=scores?.find(v=>v.group.includes(i));return !!v&&v.score>=v.threshold;},length=e.lengthFoldGains||e.foldGains;
  const lengthRejected=length.length===2&&length.every(g=>g<=tolerance)&&(length.some(g=>g< -tolerance)||!precise(lengthEvidence));
  const attributionRejected=!precise(precisionEvidence)&&e.foldGains.every(g=>g<=tolerance);
  return lengthRejected||attributionRejected?[i]:[];
 });
 if(removed.length)refitSelected(input,junction,junction.models.flatMap((m,i)=>isWildType(m)||junction.weights[i]>1e-10&&!removed.includes(i)?[i]:[]));
 return removed;
}

END MODULE


BEGIN MODULE src/app/workers/core/sanger-latent-insertion-r.ts
import {SimplexFit,phaseNeighbor} from './sanger-signal';
export interface LatentInsertionFit {fit:SimplexFit;columns:number[][];unknown:boolean[];knownTrainingRows:number;probabilities:{position:number;distribution:number[]}[]}
/** Infer mixture weights from known flanks, never from arbitrary N=25% peaks.
 * Coincident unknown inserted bases receive a pooled probability distribution;
 * this does not assign a unique inserted haplotype to each molecule. */
export function fitLatentInsertions(columns:number[][],sequences:string[],eligible:boolean[],positions:number[],observed:number[][],training:number[],lag:number,fitter:(c:number[][],y:number[])=>SimplexFit):LatentInsertionFit{
 const n=positions.length,unknown=positions.map(p=>sequences.some((s,h)=>eligible[h]&&(s[p]==='N'||s[p-1]==='N'||s[p+1]==='N'))),known=training.filter(k=>!unknown[k]);
 if(!unknown.some(Boolean)||known.length<Math.max(100,columns.length*5)||unknown.filter(Boolean).length>n*.4){return {fit:fitter(columns.map(c=>training.flatMap(k=>c.slice(k*4,k*4+4))),training.flatMap(k=>observed[k])),columns,unknown:positions.map(()=>false),knownTrainingRows:training.length,probabilities:[]};}
 const fit=fitter(columns.map(c=>known.flatMap(k=>c.slice(k*4,k*4+4))),known.flatMap(k=>observed[k])),effective=columns.map(c=>[...c]),probabilities:{position:number;distribution:number[]}[]=[],byPosition=new Map<number,number[]>();
 const project=(v:number[],mass:number)=>{const sorted=[...v].sort((a,b)=>b-a);let sum=0,threshold=0;for(let k=0;k<4;k++){sum+=sorted[k];threshold=(sum-mass)/(k+1);if(k===3||sorted[k+1]<=threshold)break;}return v.map(x=>Math.max(0,x-threshold));};
 const order=Array.from({length:n},(_,k)=>k);if(lag<0)order.reverse();
 for(const k of order){
  const p=positions[k],knownSignal=[0,0,0,0];let missing=0;
  for(let h=0;h<columns.length;h++){
   const current=eligible[h]&&sequences[h][p]==='N',previous=eligible[h]&&sequences[h][phaseNeighbor(p,lag)]==='N',prior=byPosition.get(phaseNeighbor(p,lag))||[.25,.25,.25,.25];
   for(let b=0;b<4;b++){effective[h][4*k+b]=columns[h][4*k+b]-(current?(1-Math.abs(lag))/4:0)+(previous?Math.abs(lag)*prior[b]:0);knownSignal[b]+=fit.weights[h]*effective[h][4*k+b];}if(current)missing+=fit.weights[h]*(1-Math.abs(lag));
  }
  if(missing<=1e-9)continue;const total=knownSignal.reduce((s,v)=>s+v,0)+missing,distribution=project(observed[k].map((y,b)=>y*total-knownSignal[b]),missing).map(v=>v/missing);byPosition.set(p,distribution);probabilities.push({position:p,distribution});
  for(let h=0;h<columns.length;h++)if(eligible[h]&&sequences[h][p]==='N')for(let b=0;b<4;b++)effective[h][4*k+b]+=(1-Math.abs(lag))*distribution[b];
 }
 return {fit,columns:effective,unknown,knownTrainingRows:known.length,probabilities};
}

END MODULE


BEGIN MODULE src/app/workers/core/sanger-length-marginals.ts
import {Prepared,materialize}from'./sanger-path-common';import{SangerFitCache}from'./sanger-fit-cache';
/** Flanking observations identify net-length mass without committing to edit
 * placement or inserted strings. Local edit windows (including emission
 * neighbours) are excluded; no genotype recipe is part of this input. */
export function fitLengthMarginals(refs:Prepared[],positions:number[],observed:number[],emit:(s:string,p:number)=>number[],maxDeletion:number,maxInsertion:number,radius:number){
 const localSpan=radius+Math.max(maxDeletion,maxInsertion,10)+2,rows=positions.flatMap((p,i)=>refs.every(r=>r.cuts.every(c=>p<c-radius-2||p>c+localSpan))?[i]:[]);
 if(rows.length<80)throw Error('Insufficient invariant flanking signal for length marginals.');
 const families=refs.flatMap((r,h)=>Array.from({length:maxDeletion+maxInsertion+1},(_,i)=>{const size=i-maxDeletion,cut=r.cuts[0],events=size>0?[{start:cut,end:cut,inserted:'A'.repeat(size)}]:size<0?[{start:cut,end:cut-size,inserted:''}]:[],sequence=materialize(r.sequence,events)!;return{reference:h,size,sequence};})),columns=families.map(f=>rows.flatMap(i=>emit(f.sequence,positions[i]))),y=rows.flatMap(i=>observed.slice(4*i,4*i+4)),fit=new SangerFitCache(columns,y).fit(families.map((_,i)=>i));
 return{families:families.map((f,i)=>({reference:f.reference,size:f.size,fraction:fit.weights[i]})),rmse:fit.rmse,rows:rows.map(i=>positions[i]),converged:fit.converged};
}

BEGIN MODULE src/app/workers/core/sanger-linear-sequence-oracle.ts
import {materialize,PathEdit} from './sanger-path-common';
import {normalizeMoleculeEdits} from './sanger-edit-normalize';
export interface LinearSequenceOracleInput {reference:string;cuts:number[];cost:number[][];radius:number;maxDeletion:number;maxInsertion:number;maxSubstitution?:number}
/** Exact linear minimization over one I/D/S tract per separated target.
 * Cost is additive per emitted base. Context-dependent yields require a
 * higher-order emission oracle and are deliberately outside this contract.
 * Dynamic programming retains one optimum per cumulative read shift. Inserted
 * strings are optimized base by base, so no 4^length dictionary is enumerated. */
export function linearSequenceOracle(input:LinearSequenceOracleInput):{sequence:string;events:PathEdit[];cost:number}{
 const {reference,cuts,cost,radius,maxDeletion,maxInsertion}=input,n=reference.length,sub=input.maxSubstitution??10,bases='ACGT',span=Math.max(maxDeletion,sub);
 if(!cuts.length||cuts.some((c,i)=>c<0||c>n||(i>0&&c-radius<=Math.min(n,cuts[i-1]+radius+span))))throw Error('Linear sequence oracle requires separated target windows.');
 const at=(p:number,b:string)=>cost[p]?.[bases.indexOf(b)]??0,minimum=cost.map(row=>Math.min(...row)),calls=cost.map(row=>bases[row.indexOf(Math.min(...row))]),prefixes=new Map<number,Float64Array>();
 const prefix=(shift:number)=>{let v=prefixes.get(shift);if(!v){v=new Float64Array(n+1);for(let r=0;r<n;r++)v[r+1]=v[r]+at(r+shift,reference[r]);prefixes.set(shift,v);}return v;};
 const word=(p:number,length:number)=>Array.from({length},(_,j)=>calls[p+j]??'A').join(''),wordCost=(p:number,length:number)=>{let sum=0;for(let j=0;j<length;j++)sum+=minimum[p+j]??0;return sum;};
 interface State {cost:number;events:PathEdit[]}
 let states=new Map<number,State>([[0,{cost:0,events:[]}]]),anchor=0;
 for(const cut of cuts){const nextAnchor=Math.min(n,cut+radius+span),next=new Map<number,State>();
  const keep=(shift:number,value:number,events:PathEdit[])=>{const old=next.get(shift);if(!old||value<old.cost||(value===old.cost&&events.length<old.events.length))next.set(shift,{cost:value,events});};
  for(const [shift,state]of states){const pre=prefix(shift);keep(shift,state.cost+pre[nextAnchor]-pre[anchor],state.events);
   for(let start=Math.max(anchor,cut-radius);start<=Math.min(n,cut+radius);start++){
    const before=state.cost+pre[start]-pre[anchor];
    const add=(end:number,length:number)=>{const newShift=shift+length-(end-start),post=prefix(newShift),inserted=word(start+shift,length),value=before+wordCost(start+shift,length)+post[nextAnchor]-post[end];keep(newShift,value,[...state.events,{start,end,inserted}]);};
    for(let length=1;length<=maxDeletion&&start+length<=nextAnchor;length++)add(start+length,0);
    for(let length=1;length<=maxInsertion;length++)add(start,length);
    for(let length=1;length<=sub&&start+length<=nextAnchor;length++)add(start+length,length);
   }
  }
  states=next;anchor=nextAnchor;
 }
 let best:{cost:number;events:PathEdit[]}|undefined;
 for(const [shift,state]of states){const pre=prefix(shift),value=state.cost+pre[n]-pre[anchor];if(!best||value<best.cost||(value===best.cost&&state.events.length<best.events.length))best={cost:value,events:state.events};}
 const events=normalizeMoleculeEdits(reference,best!.events);return {cost:best!.cost,events,sequence:materialize(reference,events)!};
}

END MODULE


BEGIN MODULE src/app/workers/core/sanger-local-length-decoder.ts
import{Prepared,PathEdit,materialize}from'./sanger-path-common';import{normalizeMoleculeEdits}from'./sanger-edit-normalize';
interface Family {reference:number;size:number;fraction:number}
interface Line {b:number;c:number;code:number;base:number;start?:number}
interface Piece {lo:number;hi:number;b:number;c:number}
interface Cycle {active:number[];fixed:number[];y:number[];wt:number;no:{c:number;code:number};lines:Line[];pieces:Piece[]}
function piecesFor(lines:Line[],baseline:number,maximum:number):Piece[]{
 const sorted=[...lines].sort((a,b)=>b.b-a.b||a.c-b.c),hull:(Line&{start:number})[]=[];
 for(const line of sorted){if(hull.at(-1)?.b===line.b)continue;let start=-Infinity;while(hull.length){const last=hull.at(-1)!;start=(last.c-line.c)/(line.b-last.b);if(start>last.start)break;hull.pop();}hull.push({...line,start:hull.length?start:-Infinity});}
 const out:Piece[]=[];for(let i=0;i<hull.length;i++){const line=hull[i],d=line.c-baseline,disc=line.b*line.b-8*d;if(disc<=0)continue;const root=Math.sqrt(disc),right=(-line.b+root)/4,left=right>0?d/(2*right):(-line.b-root)/4,lo=Math.max(0,line.start,left),hi=Math.min(maximum,hull[i+1]?.start??Infinity,right);if(hi>lo)out.push({lo,hi,b:line.b,c:d});}return out;
}
/** Exact continuous one-substitution-weight minimization for fixed indel
 * origins. The per-cycle ALT assignment has a quadratic cost; a lower envelope
 * of lines avoids a percentage grid or an exponential inserted-string list. */
function bestSubstitution(cycles:Cycle[],radius:number,span:number,maximum:number){
 let best={gain:0,weight:0,start:0};
 for(let start=0;start<=2*radius;start++){const events:{x:number;a:number;b:number;c:number}[]=[];for(const p of cycles.slice(start,start+span))for(const q of p.pieces){events.push({x:q.lo,a:2,b:q.b,c:q.c},{x:q.hi,a:-2,b:-q.b,c:-q.c});}events.push({x:0,a:0,b:0,c:0},{x:maximum,a:0,b:0,c:0});events.sort((a,b)=>a.x-b.x);let a=0,b=0,c=0;
  for(let i=0;i<events.length;){const x=events[i].x;while(i<events.length&&events[i].x===x){a+=events[i].a;b+=events[i].b;c+=events[i].c;i++;}const end=events[i]?.x??maximum;if(end<x)continue;const weight=a>0?Math.max(x,Math.min(end,-b/(2*a))):x,gain=a*weight*weight+b*weight+c;if(gain<best.gain)best={gain,weight,start};}
 }
 return best;
}
/** Minimal local hypothesis: one allele per nonzero length and at most one
 * substitution allele. Other references' offset windows must not overlap.
 * This is an experimental unit-emission decoder; residual QC must reject a
 * sample needing a richer hypothesis rather than certify it as recovered. */
export function decodeLocalLengths(refs:Prepared[],families:Family[],positions:number[],observed:number[],radius=3,subSpan=10){
 if(refs.some(r=>r.cuts.length!==1))throw Error('Local length decoder currently supports one target per reference.');
 const index=new Map(positions.map((p,i)=>[p,i])),result:{reference:number;events:PathEdit[];fraction:number}[]=[],details:any[]=[];
 for(let h=0;h<refs.length;h++){
  const ref=refs[h],cut=ref.cuts[0],own=families.filter(f=>f.reference===h),zero=own.find(f=>!f.size)?.fraction??0,indels=own.filter(f=>f.size!==0&&f.fraction>0),extent=Math.max(subSpan,...indels.filter(f=>f.size>0).map(f=>f.size)),begin=cut-radius,end=cut+radius+extent;
  if(indels.length>6)throw Error('Local length decoder hypothesis exceeds six nonzero length classes.');
  const observations=Array.from({length:end-begin},(_,i)=>{const p=begin+i,k=index.get(p);if(k===undefined)throw Error('Local editing window is not covered by the fitted trace.');const y=observed.slice(4*k,4*k+4);for(const f of families)if(f.reference!==h&&f.fraction>0){const r=refs[f.reference],c=r.cuts[0];if(p>=c-radius&&p<c+radius+Math.max(subSpan,10))throw Error('Offset edit windows overlap.');const base=r.sequence[p<c-radius?p:p-f.size],b='ACGT'.indexOf(base);if(b>=0)y[b]-=f.fraction;}return y;});
  let best:{score:number;origins:number[];cycles:Cycle[];sub:ReturnType<typeof bestSubstitution>}|undefined,visited=0;
  const evaluate=(origins:number[])=>{
   const cycles:Cycle[]=observations.map((y,j)=>{const p=begin+j,wt='ACGT'.indexOf(ref.sequence[p]),fixed=[0,0,0,0],active:number[]=[];fixed[wt]+=zero;for(let k=0;k<indels.length;k++){const f=indels[k],start=origins[k];if(f.size>0&&p>=start&&p<start+f.size)active.push(k);else{const pos=p<start?p:p-f.size,b='ACGT'.indexOf(ref.sequence[pos]);if(b>=0)fixed[b]+=f.fraction;}}
    let no={c:Infinity,code:0};const lines:Line[]=[];for(let code=0;code<4**active.length;code++){const v=[...fixed];for(let k=0;k<active.length;k++)v[(code>>(2*k))&3]+=indels[active[k]].fraction;const residual=y.map((x,b)=>x-v[b]),c=residual.reduce((s,x)=>s+x*x,0);if(c<no.c)no={c,code};for(let b=0;b<4;b++)if(b!==wt)lines.push({b:-2*(residual[b]-residual[wt]),c,code,base:b});}return{active,fixed,y,wt,no,lines,pieces:piecesFor(lines,no.c,zero)};
   });
   const sub=bestSubstitution(cycles,radius,subSpan,zero),score=cycles.reduce((s,p)=>s+p.no.c,0)+sub.gain;visited++;if(!best||score<best.score)best={score,origins:[...origins],cycles,sub};
  };
  const enumerate=(origins:number[])=>{if(origins.length===indels.length){evaluate(origins);return;}for(let start=cut-radius;start<=cut+radius;start++)enumerate([...origins,start]);};enumerate([]);
  const chosen=best!,words=indels.map(f=>f.size>0?Array(f.size).fill('A'):[]),subStart=begin+chosen.sub.start,subWord=ref.sequence.slice(subStart,subStart+subSpan).split('');
  chosen.cycles.forEach((cycle,j)=>{let code=cycle.no.code,base=cycle.wt;if(j>=chosen.sub.start&&j<chosen.sub.start+subSpan&&chosen.sub.weight>0){let value=cycle.no.c;for(const line of cycle.lines){const v=line.c+line.b*chosen.sub.weight+2*chosen.sub.weight**2;if(v<value){value=v;code=line.code;base=line.base;}}}for(let k=0;k<cycle.active.length;k++){const id=cycle.active[k];words[id][begin+j-chosen.origins[id]]='ACGT'[(code>>(2*k))&3];}if(j>=chosen.sub.start&&j<chosen.sub.start+subSpan)subWord[j-chosen.sub.start]='ACGT'[base];});
  result.push({reference:h,events:[],fraction:zero-chosen.sub.weight});for(let k=0;k<indels.length;k++){const f=indels[k],start=chosen.origins[k],events=normalizeMoleculeEdits(ref.sequence,[{start,end:f.size<0?start-f.size:start,inserted:words[k].join('')}]);result.push({reference:h,events,fraction:f.fraction});}const subEvents=normalizeMoleculeEdits(ref.sequence,[{start:subStart,end:subStart+subSpan,inserted:subWord.join('')}]);if(chosen.sub.weight>0&&subEvents.length)result.push({reference:h,events:subEvents,fraction:chosen.sub.weight});details.push({reference:ref.name,score:chosen.score,origins:chosen.origins,substitutionFraction:chosen.sub.weight,visited});
 }
 return{groups:result,details};
}

END MODULE


BEGIN MODULE src/app/workers/core/sanger-nonnegative-ls.ts
/** Nonnegative least squares for the small local convolution systems. Cyclic
 * exact coordinate minimization; the projected gradient is the stop criterion. */
export function nonnegativeLeastSquares(columns:number[][],observed:number[],maxIterations=10000){
 const n=columns.length;if(!n||columns.some(c=>c.length!==observed.length))throw Error('Invalid local least-squares dimensions.');
 const gram=columns.map(a=>columns.map(b=>a.reduce((s,v,k)=>s+v*b[k],0))),rhs=columns.map(c=>c.reduce((s,v,k)=>s+v*observed[k],0)),weights=Array(n).fill(0),gradient=rhs.map(x=>-x);let converged=false;
 for(let iteration=0;iteration<maxIterations;iteration++){for(let i=0;i<n;i++){if(gram[i][i]<=0)continue;const next=Math.max(0,weights[i]-gradient[i]/gram[i][i]),delta=next-weights[i];if(delta){weights[i]=next;for(let j=0;j<n;j++)gradient[j]+=delta*gram[j][i];}}const violation=Math.max(...gradient.map((g,i)=>weights[i]>0?Math.abs(g):Math.max(0,-g)));if(violation<1e-10){converged=true;break;}}
 const residual=observed.map((v,k)=>v-columns.reduce((s,c,i)=>s+c[k]*weights[i],0));return{weights,rmse:Math.sqrt(residual.reduce((s,v)=>s+v*v,0)/observed.length),converged};
}

END MODULE


BEGIN MODULE src/app/workers/core/sanger-offset-groups.ts
import{refineDiscretePhase,SangerLinearOptimizer}from'./sanger-discrete-phase';
import {proposeStratifiedPaths} from './sanger-stratified-paths';
import{sequenceGroupResult}from'./sanger-sequence-group-result';
import{fitLengthMarginals}from'./sanger-length-marginals';import{decodeFrequencyPaths}from'./sanger-frequency-path-decoder';
import {SangerFitCache} from './sanger-fit-cache';
import {normalizeMoleculeEdits} from './sanger-edit-normalize';
import {repairSignalTraceCycles} from './sanger-cycle';
import {SangerAnalysisResult,TRACE_BASES} from '../../models/sanger-analysis';
import {registerTrace} from './sanger-pooled';
import {contrastPeaks,integratedPeaks,pairedReferencePeaks,calibrateSignal,correctGain,gramCondition,rawSignalScore} from './sanger-signal';
import {emissionVector,fitContextEmission,fitNormalizedEmissionMixture as fitEmissionMixture} from './sanger-emission';
import {SignalPathInput,PathEdit,Model,materialize,prepare,assertSignalTraceGrid,eventsRespectIndelOrigin} from './sanger-path-common';
/** Invariant-flank length marginals, local frequency/path decoding and one
 * whole-molecule fluorescence refit. These are
 * best-fit cut-local hypotheses, not a posterior over physical haplotypes.
 * All measured positions enter discovery, so no independent held-out score is claimed. */
export function analyzeOffsetGroups(input:SignalPathInput,progress:(s:string)=>void=()=>{},audit?:(state:any)=>void):SangerAnalysisResult{return initializeOffsetProfiles(input,progress,audit).result;}
export function initializeOffsetProfiles(input:SignalPathInput,progress:(s:string)=>void=()=>{},audit?:(state:any)=>void,optimizer?:SangerLinearOptimizer){
 if(!input.ampliconConfirmed||!input.control||!input.sample)throw Error('Confirm submitted products and provide matched control/sample chromatograms.');
 const controlRepair=repairSignalTraceCycles(input.control),sampleRepair=repairSignalTraceCycles(input.sample);input={...input,control:controlRepair.trace,sample:sampleRepair.trace};
 assertSignalTraceGrid(input.control);assertSignalTraceGrid(input.sample);
 const {refs,report,anchor,maxDeletion,maxInsertion}=prepare(input.amplicons,input.primer,input.settings),s=input.settings;
 progress('Registering offset traces to the sequencing primer');const registered=refs.map(r=>({name:r.name,sequence:r.sequence,cut:Math.min(...r.cuts)}));
 const cr=registerTrace(input.control,registered,s,integratedPeaks(input.control,[0,0,0,0]).vectors),sr=registerTrace(input.sample,registered,s,integratedPeaks(input.sample,[0,0,0,0]).vectors),peaks=pairedReferencePeaks(input.control,input.sample,refs.map(r=>r.sequence),cr.trim,sr.trim,Math.min(...refs.flatMap(r=>r.cuts)),optimizer),ctl=peaks.control.vectors,raw=peaks.sample.vectors;
 const calibrate=(vectors:number[][],trim:number)=>{const obs=Array.from({length:vectors.length+trim},(_,p)=>vectors[p-trim]||[0,0,0,0]),rows=Array.from({length:Math.min(...refs.flatMap(r=>r.cuts))-12},(_,p)=>p).filter(p=>p>=Math.max(s.readStart,trim+10)&&p-trim<vectors.length-2);return calibrateSignal(obs,rows,refs.map(r=>Array.from(r.sequence,(_,p)=>TRACE_BASES.map(b=>Number(r.sequence[p]===b)))));};
 const cc=calibrate(ctl,cr.trim),sc=calibrate(raw,sr.trim);if(cc.validationR2<.8||sc.validationR2<.8)throw Error('Signal-path upstream calibration failed.');
 const end=Math.min(Math.max(...report.positions)+1,cr.trim+ctl.length-3,sr.trim+raw.length-3),positions=report.positions.filter(p=>p<end);if(positions.length<120)throw Error('Insufficient actual trace coverage for path inference.');
 const wt=ctl.map(v=>correctGain(v,cc.gains)),sample=raw.map(v=>correctGain(v,sc.gains)),controlObs=Array.from({length:wt.length+cr.trim},(_,p)=>wt[p-cr.trim]||[0,0,0,0]);
 const context=fitContextEmission(refs.map(r=>r.sequence),positions,controlObs,cc.weights,cc.lag),theta=context.theta;
 const emit=(sequence:string,p:number,lag:number)=>{const v=emissionVector(sequence,p,lag,theta);if(sequence[p]==='N')for(let b=0;b<4;b++)v[b]+=(1-Math.abs(lag))/4;return v;};
 const models:Model[]=refs.map((r,h)=>({reference:h,sequence:r.sequence,events:[],aliases:[{reference:h,events:[]}]})),cols=models.map(m=>positions.flatMap(p=>emit(m.sequence,p,sc.lag))),controlCols=models.map(m=>positions.flatMap(p=>emit(m.sequence,p,cc.lag)));
 const ctlFit=fitEmissionMixture(controlCols,positions.flatMap(p=>wt[p-cr.trim])),ctlScore=rawSignalScore(controlCols,positions.flatMap(p=>ctl[p-cr.trim]),ctlFit.weights,cc.gains);if(ctlScore.r2<.85)throw Error('WT product references do not explain the unedited control.');
 const y=positions.flatMap(p=>sample[p-sr.trim]),train=positions.map((_,i)=>i),tune=train,test=train;
 const rawY=positions.flatMap(p=>raw[p-sr.trim]),fitCache=new SangerFitCache(cols,y),fitIds=(ids:number[])=>fitCache.fit(ids);
 let ids=refs.map((_,i)=>i),fit=fitIds(ids),iterations=0;const alignments=0,discoveryLimited=false;

 if(refs.some(r=>r.cuts.length!==1)||context.used||Math.abs(sc.lag)>1e-12)throw Error('Offset decoder requires one target and unit cycle emissions.');
 progress('Estimating indel length fractions from invariant flanks');
 const lengths=fitLengthMarginals(refs,positions,y,(seq,p)=>emit(seq,p,sc.lag),maxDeletion,maxInsertion,s.editStartRadius??10),families=refs.flatMap((r,h)=>{const all=lengths.families.filter(f=>f.reference===h),share=all.reduce((sum,f)=>sum+f.fraction,0),keep=all.filter(f=>f.size!==0&&f.fraction>=share*.01);return[{reference:h,size:0,fraction:share-keep.reduce((sum,f)=>sum+f.fraction,0)},...keep];});
 progress('Decoding offset local edits');
 const profileOptions:PathEdit[][][][]=[],selected:number[]=[];
 const decoded:{groups:{reference:number;events:PathEdit[];fraction:number}[];details:any[]}={groups:[],details:[]};
 for(let h=0;h<refs.length;h++){
  const rows=positions.flatMap((p,i)=>refs.every((r,k)=>k===h||p<r.cuts[0]-3||p>=r.cuts[0]+13)?[i]:[]),ownPositions=rows.map(i=>positions[i]),ownObserved=rows.flatMap(i=>{
   const p=positions[i],v=y.slice(4*i,4*i+4);
   for(const f of families)if(f.reference!==h){const r=refs[f.reference],b='ACGT'.indexOf(r.sequence[p<r.cuts[0]-3?p:p-f.size]);if(b>=0)v[b]-=f.fraction;}
   return v;
  }),own=families.filter(f=>f.reference===h).map(f=>({...f,reference:0})),proposals=proposeStratifiedPaths(refs[h],own,ownPositions,ownObserved,lengths.rmse,5,200,4,stage=>progress('First target reference '+(h+1)+' / '+refs.length+' · '+stage));
  if(optimizer){
   const share=own.reduce((sum,f)=>sum+f.fraction,0),target=ownObserved.map(v=>v/share);
   for(let i=0;i<proposals.options.length;i++){
    progress(`Refining first-target allocations · reference ${h+1} / ${refs.length} · ${i+1} / ${proposals.options.length}`);
    const phased=refineDiscretePhase([refs[h]],[[[]]],[proposals.options[i].map(g=>g.events)],ownPositions,target,optimizer,()=>{},0,false);
    proposals.options[i]=phased.profiles[0].map(events=>({events,fraction:0}));
    const columns=proposals.options[i].map(g=>{const seq=materialize(refs[h].sequence,g.events)!;return ownPositions.flatMap(p=>'ACGT'.split('').map(b=>Number(seq[p]===b)));}),fit=new SangerFitCache(columns,target).fit(columns.map((_,j)=>j));
    proposals.options[i].forEach((g,j)=>g.fraction=share*fit.weights[j]);
    let maximum=0;for(const [row,p]of ownPositions.entries())if(p>=refs[h].cuts[0]-3&&p<refs[h].cuts[0]+13){let error=0;for(let b=0;b<4;b++){const predicted=columns.reduce((sum,col,j)=>sum+col[4*row+b]*fit.weights[j],0);error+=(predicted-target[4*row+b])**2;}maximum=Math.max(maximum,share*Math.sqrt(error/4));}
    proposals.scores[i]={rmse:share*fit.rmse,maximum};
   }
  }
  let best=-1;for(let i=0;i<proposals.options.length;i++)if(proposals.scores[i].maximum<=Math.max(1e-5,4*lengths.rmse)&&(best<0||proposals.scores[i].rmse<proposals.scores[best].rmse))best=i;
  if(best<0)throw Error('No validated first-target profile for '+refs[h].name+' '+JSON.stringify(proposals.details));
  profileOptions.push(proposals.options.map(option=>option.map(g=>g.events)));selected.push(best);
  decoded.groups.push(...proposals.options[best].map(g=>({...g,reference:h})));decoded.details.push(...proposals.details);
 }
 const signature=(sequence:string)=>sequence.slice(positions[0],positions.at(-1)!+1).padEnd(positions.length,'-');
 const seenEdits=refs.map(()=>new Set<string>()),signatures=new Map(models.map((m,id)=>[signature(m.sequence),id]));
  const addVariant=(h:number,events:PathEdit[])=>{events=normalizeMoleculeEdits(refs[h].sequence,events);const editKey=JSON.stringify(events);if(seenEdits[h].has(editKey))return;seenEdits[h].add(editKey);if(!eventsRespectIndelOrigin(events,refs[h].cuts,s.editStartRadius))return;const sequence=materialize(refs[h].sequence,events);if(!sequence)return;const key=signature(sequence),old=signatures.get(key),alias={reference:h,events};if(old!==undefined){if(!models[old].aliases.some(a=>a.reference===h&&JSON.stringify(a.events)===JSON.stringify(events)))models[old].aliases.push(alias);return;}const id=models.length;models.push({reference:h,sequence,events,aliases:[alias]});cols.push(positions.flatMap(p=>emit(sequence,p,sc.lag)));signatures.set(key,id);};

 const proposed:number[]=[];for(const group of decoded.groups){if(group.fraction<=1e-9)continue;addVariant(group.reference,group.events);const id=signatures.get(signature(materialize(refs[group.reference].sequence,group.events)!));if(id!==undefined)proposed.push(id);}
 ids=[...new Set([...ids,...proposed])];fit=fitIds(ids);
 // Equivalent deletion placements annotate the same molecule, not extra mass.
 for(const group of decoded.groups)for(const e of group.events)if(!e.inserted.length)for(let start=refs[group.reference].cuts[0]-3;start<=refs[group.reference].cuts[0]+3;start++)addVariant(group.reference,[{start,end:start+e.end-e.start,inserted:''}]);
 iterations=1;
 progress('Validating offset profiles');
 return {result:sequenceGroupResult({input,refs,positions,report,anchor,end,models,cols,ids,fit,ctlScore,cc,sc,context,cr,sr,rawY,controlRepair,sampleRepair,maxDeletion,maxInsertion,fitCache,y,audit,train,test,tune,emit,iterations}),profiles:profileOptions,selected};
}

END MODULE


BEGIN MODULE src/app/workers/core/sanger-offset-length-prototype.ts
import{fitLengthMarginals}from'./sanger-length-marginals';import{decodeFrequencyPaths}from'./sanger-frequency-path-decoder';
import {discoverExactAlternatives} from './sanger-exact-alternatives';
import {substitutionLinkageAliases} from './sanger-substitution-linkage';
import {compoundBaseProposals} from './sanger-compound-refine';
import {SangerFitCache,rawMixtureMse} from './sanger-fit-cache';
import {CandidateSignalScreen} from './sanger-candidate-screen';
import {polishSignalDictionary} from './sanger-dictionary-polish';
import {substitutionProposals} from './sanger-substitution-search';
import {normalizeMoleculeEdits} from './sanger-edit-normalize';
import {repairSignalTraceCycles} from './sanger-cycle';
import {SangerAnalysisResult,SangerJointEvent,SangerTrace,TRACE_BASES} from '../../models/sanger-analysis';
import {reverseComplement} from '../../utils/biology.utils';
import {registerTrace} from './sanger-pooled';
import {contrastPeaks,integratedPeaks,calibrateSignal,correctGain,gramCondition,rawSignalScore} from './sanger-signal';
import {emissionVector,fitContextEmission,fitNormalizedEmissionMixture as fitEmissionMixtureOriginal} from './sanger-emission';
import {plotTrace} from './sanger-analysis';
const fitEmissionMixture=(columns:number[][],observed:number[])=>fitEmissionMixtureOriginal(columns,observed);
import {SignalPathInput,PathEdit,Model,materialize,prepare,assertSignalTraceGrid,eventsRespectIndelOrigin} from './sanger-path-common';
/** Whole-molecule candidate discovery and joint fluorescence refit. These are
 * best-fit cut-local hypotheses, not a posterior over physical haplotypes.
 * All measured positions enter discovery, so no independent held-out score is claimed. */
export function analyzeOffsetLengthGroups(input:SignalPathInput,progress:(s:string)=>void=()=>{},audit?:(state:any)=>void):SangerAnalysisResult{
 if(!input.ampliconConfirmed||!input.control||!input.sample)throw Error('Confirm submitted products and provide matched control/sample chromatograms.');
 const controlRepair=repairSignalTraceCycles(input.control),sampleRepair=repairSignalTraceCycles(input.sample);input={...input,control:controlRepair.trace,sample:sampleRepair.trace};
 assertSignalTraceGrid(input.control);assertSignalTraceGrid(input.sample);
 const {refs,report,anchor,maxDeletion,maxInsertion}=prepare(input.amplicons,input.primer,input.settings),s=input.settings;
 progress('Registering traces to the sequencing primer');const registered=refs.map(r=>({name:r.name,sequence:r.sequence,cut:Math.min(...r.cuts)}));
 const cr=registerTrace(input.control,registered,s,integratedPeaks(input.control,[0,0,0,0]).vectors),sr=registerTrace(input.sample,registered,s,integratedPeaks(input.sample,[0,0,0,0]).vectors),ctl=contrastPeaks(input.control).vectors,raw=contrastPeaks(input.sample).vectors;
 const calibrate=(vectors:number[][],trim:number)=>{const obs=Array.from({length:vectors.length+trim},(_,p)=>vectors[p-trim]||[0,0,0,0]),rows=Array.from({length:Math.min(...refs.flatMap(r=>r.cuts))-12},(_,p)=>p).filter(p=>p>=Math.max(s.readStart,trim+10)&&p-trim<vectors.length-2);return calibrateSignal(obs,rows,refs.map(r=>Array.from(r.sequence,(_,p)=>TRACE_BASES.map(b=>Number(r.sequence[p]===b)))));};
 const cc=calibrate(ctl,cr.trim),sc=calibrate(raw,sr.trim);if(cc.validationR2<.8||sc.validationR2<.8)throw Error('Signal-path upstream calibration failed.');
 const end=Math.min(Math.max(...report.positions)+1,cr.trim+ctl.length-3,sr.trim+raw.length-3),positions=report.positions.filter(p=>p<end);if(positions.length<120)throw Error('Insufficient actual trace coverage for path inference.');
 const wt=ctl.map(v=>correctGain(v,cc.gains)),sample=raw.map(v=>correctGain(v,sc.gains)),controlObs=Array.from({length:wt.length+cr.trim},(_,p)=>wt[p-cr.trim]||[0,0,0,0]);
 const context=fitContextEmission(refs.map(r=>r.sequence),positions,controlObs,cc.weights,cc.lag),theta=context.theta;
 const emit=(sequence:string,p:number,lag:number)=>{const v=emissionVector(sequence,p,lag,theta);if(sequence[p]==='N')for(let b=0;b<4;b++)v[b]+=(1-Math.abs(lag))/4;return v;};
 const models:Model[]=refs.map((r,h)=>({reference:h,sequence:r.sequence,events:[],aliases:[{reference:h,events:[]}]})),cols=models.map(m=>positions.flatMap(p=>emit(m.sequence,p,sc.lag))),controlCols=models.map(m=>positions.flatMap(p=>emit(m.sequence,p,cc.lag)));
 const ctlFit=fitEmissionMixture(controlCols,positions.flatMap(p=>wt[p-cr.trim])),ctlScore=rawSignalScore(controlCols,positions.flatMap(p=>ctl[p-cr.trim]),ctlFit.weights,cc.gains);if(ctlScore.r2<.85)throw Error('WT product references do not explain the unedited control.');
 const y=positions.flatMap(p=>sample[p-sr.trim]),train=positions.map((_,i)=>i),tune=train,test=train,subset=(v:number[],rows:number[])=>rows.flatMap(i=>v.slice(4*i,4*i+4));
 const positionIndex=new Int32Array(end+maxInsertion+2).fill(-1);positions.forEach((p,i)=>positionIndex[p]=i);
 const rawY=positions.flatMap(p=>raw[p-sr.trim]),fitCache=new SangerFitCache(cols,y);
 const fitIds=(ids:number[])=>fitCache.fit(ids);
 const score=(ids:number[],weights:number[],rows:number[])=>rawSignalScore(ids.map(id=>rows===train?cols[id]:subset(cols[id],rows)),rows===train?rawY:rows.flatMap(i=>raw[positions[i]-sr.trim]),weights,sc.gains);
 const objective=(ids:number[],weights:number[])=>rawMixtureMse(ids.map(id=>cols[id]),rawY,weights,sc.gains)+.0000001*ids.reduce((sum,id,h)=>sum+weights[h]*models[id].events.reduce((a,e)=>a+1+.05*e.inserted.length,0),0);
 let ids=refs.map((_,i)=>i),fit=fitIds(ids),best=objective(ids,fit.weights),iterations=0,alignments=0,substitutionRounds=0,insertionPolishes=0,discoveryLimited=false;
 const multiTarget=refs.some(r=>r.cuts.length>1),screen=new CandidateSignalScreen(cols);

 if(refs.some(r=>r.cuts.length!==1)||context.used||Math.abs(sc.lag)>1e-12)throw Error('Offset decoder prototype requires one target and unit cycle emissions.');
 progress('Estimating indel length fractions from invariant flanks');
 const lengths=fitLengthMarginals(refs,positions,y,(seq,p)=>emit(seq,p,sc.lag),maxDeletion,maxInsertion,s.editStartRadius??10),families=refs.flatMap((r,h)=>{const all=lengths.families.filter(f=>f.reference===h),share=all.reduce((sum,f)=>sum+f.fraction,0),keep=all.filter(f=>f.size!==0&&f.fraction>=share*.01);return[{reference:h,size:0,fraction:share-keep.reduce((sum,f)=>sum+f.fraction,0)},...keep];});
 progress('Decoding offset local edits');
 const decoded=decodeFrequencyPaths(refs,families,positions,y,lengths.rmse,3,10,5);
 const signature=(sequence:string)=>sequence.slice(positions[0],positions.at(-1)!+1).padEnd(positions.length,'-');
 const seenEdits=refs.map(()=>new Set<string>()),signatures=new Map(models.map((m,id)=>[signature(m.sequence),id]));
  const addVariant=(h:number,events:PathEdit[])=>{events=normalizeMoleculeEdits(refs[h].sequence,events);const editKey=JSON.stringify(events);if(seenEdits[h].has(editKey))return;seenEdits[h].add(editKey);if(!eventsRespectIndelOrigin(events,refs[h].cuts,s.editStartRadius))return;const sequence=materialize(refs[h].sequence,events);if(!sequence)return;const key=signature(sequence),old=signatures.get(key),alias={reference:h,events};if(old!==undefined){if(!models[old].aliases.some(a=>a.reference===h&&JSON.stringify(a.events)===JSON.stringify(events)))models[old].aliases.push(alias);return;}const id=models.length;models.push({reference:h,sequence,events,aliases:[alias]});cols.push(positions.flatMap(p=>emit(sequence,p,sc.lag)));signatures.set(key,id);};

 const proposed:number[]=[];for(const group of decoded.groups){if(group.fraction<=1e-9)continue;addVariant(group.reference,group.events);const id=signatures.get(signature(materialize(refs[group.reference].sequence,group.events)!));if(id!==undefined)proposed.push(id);}
 ids=[...new Set([...ids,...proposed])];fit=fitIds(ids);
 // Equivalent deletion placements annotate the same molecule, not extra mass.
 for(const group of decoded.groups)for(const e of group.events)if(!e.inserted.length)for(let start=refs[group.reference].cuts[0]-3;start<=refs[group.reference].cuts[0]+3;start++)addVariant(group.reference,[{start,end:start+e.end-e.start,inserted:''}]);
 iterations=1;
 progress('Validating fitted target profiles');
 const qc=score(ids,fit.weights,test),full=qc,geometry=gramCondition(ids.map(id=>cols[id]));
 const net=(events:PathEdit[])=>events.reduce((sum,e)=>sum+e.inserted.length-(e.end-e.start),0),inputEvents=(h:number,events:PathEdit[]):SangerJointEvent[]=>{const r=refs[h];return events.map(e=>{const distances=r.cuts.map(c=>Math.min(Math.abs(e.start-c),Math.abs(e.end-c))),near=distances.indexOf(Math.min(...distances)),span=r.cuts.length===2&&e.start<=r.cuts[0]&&e.end>=r.cuts[1],target=span?0:Math.min(...distances)>60?-1:(r.reverse?r.cuts.length-near:near+1),a=r.offset+e.start,b=r.offset+e.end;return {target,start:r.reverse?r.input.sequence.length-b:a,end:r.reverse?r.input.sequence.length-a:b,inserted:e.inserted.length,insertedSequence:r.reverse?reverseComplement(e.inserted):e.inserted};}).sort((a,b)=>a.start-b.start);};
 const pathSizes=(h:number,events:PathEdit[])=>{const sizes=Array(refs[h].cuts.length).fill(0);for(const e of inputEvents(h,events)){if(e.target>0)sizes[e.target-1]+=e.inserted-(e.end-e.start);}return sizes;};
 const chosen=ids.flatMap((id,i)=>fit.weights[i]>1e-9?[{model:models[id],fraction:fit.weights[i]}]:[]),aliasKey=(a:{reference:number;events:PathEdit[]})=>a.reference+':'+JSON.stringify(inputEvents(a.reference,a.events));
 const ambiguous=chosen.filter(c=>new Set(c.model.aliases.map(a=>a.reference+':'+net(a.events)+':'+Number(a.events.length===0)+':'+[...new Set(inputEvents(a.reference,a.events).map(e=>e.target))].sort().join('/'))).size>1),refAmbiguous=ambiguous.filter(c=>new Set(c.model.aliases.map(a=>a.reference)).size>1);
 const semantic=(a:{reference:number;events:PathEdit[]})=>a.reference+':'+(a.events.length?'edit':'WT')+':'+net(a.events)+':'+[...new Set(inputEvents(a.reference,a.events).map(e=>e.target))].sort().join('/');
 const attributionAmbiguous=ambiguous.filter(c=>new Set(c.model.aliases.map(semantic)).size>1);
 const groups=chosen.map((c,i)=>({id:`P${i+1}`,reference:refAmbiguous.includes(c)?null:refs[c.model.reference].name,fraction:c.fraction,netSize:new Set(c.model.aliases.map(a=>net(a.events))).size>1?null:net(c.model.events),pathSizes:pathSizes(c.model.reference,c.model.events),events:ambiguous.includes(c)?[]:inputEvents(c.model.reference,c.model.events),alternatives:ambiguous.includes(c)?c.model.aliases.map(a=>({reference:refs[a.reference].name,events:inputEvents(a.reference,a.events)})):[]}));
 const substitutionLinkage=substitutionLinkageAliases(refs,chosen.map(c=>({...c.model,fraction:c.fraction})),sequence=>positions.flatMap(p=>emit(sequence,p,sc.lag)));
 const warnings:string[]=[multiTarget?'Best-fit molecules allow one contiguous edit per target within cut ±10 bp. Target profiles are reported separately; molecule linkage is unresolved.':'Best-fit sequence groups assume one contiguous edit per molecule within cut ±10 bp. A single mixed read does not establish unique allele linkage.'];if(s.editStartRadius!==undefined)warnings.push(`Indel initiation constrained to cut ±${s.editStartRadius} bp; off-target-window edits are outside this model.`);if(controlRepair.added+controlRepair.removed+sampleRepair.added+sampleRepair.removed)warnings.push(`Raw-peak-supported cycle metadata repair: control +${controlRepair.added}/-${controlRepair.removed}, sample +${sampleRepair.added}/-${sampleRepair.removed}.`);if(qc.r2<.995)warnings.push('Signal-guided paths failed internal trace reconstruction QC; editing estimates are withheld.');if(ambiguous.length)warnings.push('Some discovered paths have indistinguishable reference or event explanations. Inspect alternatives.');if(discoveryLimited)warnings.push('Path discovery reached its iteration limit.');if(geometry.condition===null||geometry.condition>100)warnings.push('Selected path contributions are poorly conditioned.');
 if(substitutionLinkage.length)warnings.push('Separate and linked substitution groups produce identical measured fluorescence. WT and substitution-group fractions are not uniquely identifiable; inspect annotation ranges.');
 const boundary=chosen.some(c=>c.model.events.some(e=>e.end-e.start>=maxDeletion||e.inserted.length>=maxInsertion));if(boundary)warnings.push('A supported event reaches the configured path-search boundary.');
 const localResidual=full.positionRmse,unknown=positions.map(p=>chosen.some(c=>c.model.sequence[p]==='N'||c.model.sequence[p-1]==='N')),localDiscordant=localResidual.filter((v,i)=>!unknown[i]&&v>Math.max(.003,2*(ctlScore.positionRmse[i]||0)+.002)).length;
 if(localDiscordant)warnings.push(`${localDiscordant} positions have unexplained local signal (possible substitution, artifact or missing edit path). Editing estimates are withheld.`);
 const unknownInsertion=chosen.some(c=>c.model.sequence.includes('N'));if(unknownInsertion)warnings.push('Inserted bases are unresolved; known-sequence inference cannot certify these target fractions.');
 // Conditioning diagnoses coefficient identifiability, not raw-signal agreement.
 // Preserve its warning and exact alternatives; never call a good trace fit a failed reconstruction solely because its basis is ill-conditioned.
 const allowed=!unknownInsertion&&qc.r2>=.995&&!localDiscordant&&fit.converged&&!discoveryLimited;
 const perRef=refs.map((r,h)=>{const rows=chosen.filter(c=>c.model.reference===h&&!attributionAmbiguous.includes(c)),share=rows.reduce((sum,c)=>sum+c.fraction,0),estimable=allowed&&share>=.02&&!attributionAmbiguous.some(c=>c.model.aliases.some(a=>a.reference===h)),bins=new Map<number,number>();rows.forEach(c=>bins.set(net(c.model.events),(bins.get(net(c.model.events))||0)+c.fraction));const zero=rows.filter(c=>!c.model.events.length).reduce((sum,c)=>sum+c.fraction,0);return {name:r.name,share,wt:estimable?zero/share:null,indel:estimable?1-zero/share:null,inFrame:estimable?rows.filter(c=>c.model.events.length&&net(c.model.events)!==0&&net(c.model.events)%3===0).reduce((sum,c)=>sum+c.fraction,0)/share:null,outOfFrame:estimable?rows.filter(c=>net(c.model.events)%3!==0).reduce((sum,c)=>sum+c.fraction,0)/share:null,components:[...bins].map(([size,fraction])=>({size,fraction:share?fraction/share:0})),estimable,rows};});
 const targets=perRef.flatMap((a,h)=>(refs[h].input.cuts??[refs[h].input.cut]).map((cut,t)=>({reference:a.name,target:t+1,cut,edited:a.estimable?a.rows.filter(c=>inputEvents(h,c.model.events).some(e=>e.target===t+1||e.target===0)).reduce((sum,c)=>sum+c.fraction,0)/a.share:null})));
 const annotations=perRef.map((a,h)=>({sequenceGrouping:true,name:a.name,reference:refs[h].input.sequence,cut:refs[h].input.cut,cuts:refs[h].input.cuts,direction:refs[h].reverse?'reverse' as const:'forward' as const,share:a.share,estimable:a.estimable,components:a.rows.map(c=>({size:net(c.model.events),pathSizes:pathSizes(h,c.model.events),fraction:c.fraction/a.share,events:ambiguous.includes(c)?[]:inputEvents(h,c.model.events),unresolved:ambiguous.includes(c)}))}));
