 const exactAlternatives=discoverExactAlternatives(refs,annotations,perRef.map(a=>a.rows),inputEvents,(sequence,p)=>emit(sequence,p,sc.lag));
 const bins=new Map<number,number>();chosen.forEach(c=>bins.set(net(c.model.events),(bins.get(net(c.model.events))||0)+c.fraction));const zero=chosen.filter(c=>!c.model.events.length).reduce((sum,c)=>sum+c.fraction,0);
 const first=Math.min(...refs.flatMap(r=>r.cuts)),plots=[{trace:input.control,trim:cr.trim},{trace:input.sample,trim:sr.trim}].map(({trace,trim})=>plotTrace(trace,first-trim,Math.max(...refs.flatMap(r=>r.cuts))-first+s.window/2));report.positions=positions;report.joint!.candidateCount=models.length;
 if(audit)audit({refs,positions,cols,models,ids,weights:fit.weights,theta,sampleLag:sc.lag,gains:sc.gains,observed:y,fitCache:{hits:fitCache.hits,misses:fitCache.misses,linearFits:fitCache.linearFits,nonlinearFits:fitCache.nonlinearFits,pairHits:fitCache.pairHits,pairMisses:fitCache.pairMisses},rawObserved:rawY,anchor,end,iterations,train,test,tune,controlPositionRmse:ctlScore.positionRmse});
 return {exactAlternatives,method:'pooled',referenceName:refs.length===1?refs[0].name:'Mixed amplicons',sampleName:input.sample.name,controlName:input.control.name,direction:'forward',cutPosition:first,alignmentIdentity:0,alignment:[],variants:[],plots,warnings,synthetic:!!input.synthetic,annotations,diagnostics:{substitutionLinkage:substitutionLinkage.map(a=>({...a,reference:refs[a.reference].name})),algorithmVersion:'sanger-sequence-groups-v5-offset-prototype',signalModel:'calibrated',requestedMaxIndel:s.maxIndel,discordantPositions:localDiscordant,maxPositionRmse:Math.max(...localResidual),controlCalibration:cc,sampleCalibration:sc,contextEmission:context},mixture:{components:[...bins].map(([size,fraction])=>({size,fraction})),wt:zero,indel:1-zero,inFrame:chosen.filter(c=>c.model.events.length&&net(c.model.events)!==0&&net(c.model.events)%3===0).reduce((sum,c)=>sum+c.fraction,0),outOfFrame:chosen.filter(c=>net(c.model.events)%3!==0).reduce((sum,c)=>sum+c.fraction,0),r2:full.r2,rmse:full.rmse,upstreamError:1-sc.validationR2,bases:positions.length,fitStart:anchor,fitEnd:end,converged:fit.converged,passesChecks:allowed&&!attributionAmbiguous.length},pooled:{preflight:report,controlR2:ctlScore.r2,sampleTrim:sr.trim,controlTrim:cr.trim,amplicons:perRef.map(({rows,estimable,...a})=>a)},joint:{groups,targets,reconstructionR2:qc.r2,heldOutR2:null,selectedModels:chosen.length,candidateModels:models.length,iterations,maxDeletion,ambiguousFraction:ambiguous.reduce((sum,c)=>sum+c.fraction,0),alignmentSearches:alignments}};
}

END MODULE


BEGIN MODULE src/app/workers/core/sanger-onset-fit.ts
/** Ordered deletion onsets can be parameterised by a cumulative distribution.
 * Adjacent onset templates differ at only a few junction cycles. Optimising
 * their CDF with isotonic projection avoids the ill-conditioned dense Gram
 * matrix of almost-identical whole-read templates. This solves the same LS
 * problem, retaining every observation and both deletion directions. */
export function fitOrderedOnsets(columns:number[][],observed:number[],positionWeights:number[]|undefined,initial:number[]):number[]|null {
 const count=columns.length,n=observed.length;if(count<2)return [1];
 const normalized=columns.map(c=>[...c]);
 for(let k=0;k<n;k+=4){const total=columns[0].slice(k,k+4).reduce((s,v)=>s+v,0);if(total<=0)return null;for(let j=0;j<count;j++){const sum=columns[j].slice(k,k+4).reduce((s,v)=>s+v,0);if(Math.abs(sum-total)>1e-8*Math.max(1,total))return null;for(let b=0;b<4;b++)normalized[j][k+b]/=total;}}
 const differences=normalized.slice(0,-1).map((c,j)=>{const indices:number[]=[],values:number[]=[];for(let k=0;k<n;k++){const v=c[k]-normalized[j+1][k];if(Math.abs(v)>1e-14){indices.push(k);values.push(v);}}return {indices,values};});
 const rowSum=new Float64Array(n);for(const d of differences)d.indices.forEach((k,i)=>rowSum[k]+=Math.abs(d.values[i]));
 const weight=(k:number)=>positionWeights?.[Math.floor(k/4)]??1;
 const L=Math.max(...differences.map(d=>d.indices.reduce((s,k,i)=>s+Math.abs(d.values[i])*rowSum[k]*weight(k),0)));if(!L)return [...initial];
 const project=(v:number[])=>{const blocks:{lo:number;hi:number;sum:number;count:number}[]=[];for(let i=0;i<v.length;i++){blocks.push({lo:i,hi:i,sum:v[i],count:1});while(blocks.length>1){const b=blocks[blocks.length-1],a=blocks[blocks.length-2];if(a.sum/a.count<=b.sum/b.count)break;blocks.pop();blocks.pop();blocks.push({lo:a.lo,hi:b.hi,sum:a.sum+b.sum,count:a.count+b.count});}}const out=Array(v.length).fill(0);for(const b of blocks){const value=Math.max(0,Math.min(1,b.sum/b.count));for(let i=b.lo;i<=b.hi;i++)out[i]=value;}return out;};
 let sum=0,cdf=initial.slice(0,-1).map(v=>(sum+=v)),y=[...cdf],t=1;
 for(let iteration=0;iteration<2000;iteration++){
  const residual=normalized[count-1].map((v,k)=>v-observed[k]);differences.forEach((d,j)=>d.indices.forEach((k,i)=>residual[k]+=y[j]*d.values[i]));
  const gradient=differences.map(d=>d.indices.reduce((s,k,i)=>s+d.values[i]*residual[k]*weight(k),0)),next=project(y.map((v,j)=>v-gradient[j]/L)),delta=Math.max(...next.map((v,j)=>Math.abs(v-cdf[j])));
  if(iteration>20&&delta<1e-9){cdf=next;break;}
  const restart=next.reduce((s,v,j)=>s+(v-cdf[j])*(y[j]-v),0)>0,nt=restart?1:(1+Math.sqrt(1+4*t*t))/2;y=restart?[...next]:next.map((v,j)=>v+(t-1)/nt*(v-cdf[j]));cdf=next;t=nt;
 }
 const weights=[cdf[0],...cdf.slice(1).map((v,j)=>v-cdf[j]),1-cdf[cdf.length-1]].map(v=>Math.max(0,v)),mass=weights.reduce((s,v)=>s+v,0);return weights.map(v=>v/mass);
}

END MODULE


BEGIN MODULE src/app/workers/core/sanger-paired.ts
import {SangerAnalysisResult} from '../../models/sanger-analysis';
import {PooledAnalysisInput,preparePooledSanger} from './sanger-pooled';
import {JunctionInput,JunctionRead,JunctionFit} from './sanger-junction-mixture';
import {solveJunctions,JunctionSolution} from './sanger-junction-solution';
import {mapPathModel,pathColumn} from './sanger-junction-paths';
import {fitEmissionMixture} from './sanger-emission';
/** Two independent fluorescence calibrations, one shared physical distribution.
 * No per-read mode, clipping parameter, or second analytical engine. */
export function analyzePairedSanger(inputs:PooledAnalysisInput[],progress:(stage:string)=>void=()=>{}):SangerAnalysisResult {
 if(inputs.length!==2)throw Error('Provide the primary read pair and one opposite-direction read pair.');
 const prepared=inputs.map((input,i)=>preparePooledSanger(input,stage=>progress(`Read ${i+1}/2: ${stage}`))),primary=prepared[0];
 inputs.forEach(input=>{if(input.amplicons.length!==inputs[0].amplicons.length||input.amplicons.some((a,h)=>a.name!==inputs[0].amplicons[h].name||a.sequence!==inputs[0].amplicons[h].sequence||JSON.stringify(a.cuts||[a.cut])!==JSON.stringify(inputs[0].amplicons[h].cuts||[inputs[0].amplicons[h].cut])))throw Error('Both directions must use the same reference products and target positions.');});
 let offset=0;
 const reads:JunctionRead[]=prepared.map((p,i)=>{
  const positions=p.fitInput.positions,rowIds=positions.map((_,j)=>offset+j);offset+=positions.length;
  const mappings=p.report.amplicons.map((site,h)=>{const first=primary.report.amplicons[h],length=inputs[0].amplicons[h].sequence.length,origin=(x:typeof site)=>x.direction==='forward'?x.primerEnd:length-x.primerStart+1,reverse=site.direction!==first.direction;
   if(i===1&&!reverse)throw Error('The additional read pair must use the opposite sequencing primer in every reference.');
   return{reverse,offset:reverse?length-origin(first)-origin(site):origin(first)-origin(site)};
  });
  return{positions,rowIds,references:p.fitInput.references,mappings,lag:p.fitInput.lag,theta:p.fitInput.theta};
 });
 const input:JunctionInput={...primary.fitInput,reads,positions:reads.flatMap(r=>r.rowIds),observed:prepared.flatMap(p=>p.fitInput.observed),positionWeights:prepared.flatMap(p=>p.fitInput.positionWeights||p.fitInput.positions.map(()=>1))};
 progress('Jointly fitting the same physical edits to both read directions');
 const solution=solveJunctions(input,fit=>prepared.flatMap(p=>p.templateError(fit)||p.fitInput.observed.map(()=>0)));
 solution.coveragePass=inputs[0].amplicons.every((a,h)=>(a.cuts||[a.cut]).every((_,t)=>prepared.some(p=>{const cut=p.fitInput.references[h].cuts![t],positions=p.fitInput.positions;return positions[0]<=cut-10&&positions.at(-1)!>=cut+29;})));
 const views=prepared.map((p,i)=>{
  const models=solution.junction.models.map(m=>mapPathModel(m,reads[i].mappings[m.reference])),columns=solution.junction.predictColumns(reads[i].rowIds);
  const junction:JunctionFit={...solution.junction,models,columns,predictColumns:positions=>models.map(m=>pathColumn(p.fitInput.references[m.reference],m,positions,p.fitInput.lag,p.fitInput.theta))};
  const projected:JunctionSolution={...solution,junction};return p.finish(projected);
 });
 const result=views[0],bases=views.reduce((n,v)=>n+v.mixture!.bases,0),error=views.reduce((n,v)=>n+v.mixture!.rmse**2*4*v.mixture!.bases,0),variance=prepared.reduce((n,p)=>n+p.rawVariance,0);
 result.reads=views.map((v,i)=>({primer:inputs[i].primer,sampleName:inputs[i].sample.name,controlName:inputs[i].control.name,preflight:v.pooled!.preflight,sampleTrim:v.pooled!.sampleTrim,controlTrim:v.pooled!.controlTrim,r2:v.mixture!.r2,rmse:v.mixture!.rmse,bases:v.mixture!.bases,fitStart:v.mixture!.fitStart,fitEnd:v.mixture!.fitEnd}));
 // Disagreement is evaluated on each read's observable first-target marginal,
 // not arbitrary one-way allocations of compound molecular paths.
 let agreement=0;
 prepared.forEach((p,i)=>{
  const columns=solution.junction.predictColumns(reads[i].rowIds),independent=fitEmissionMixture(columns,p.fitInput.observed,p.fitInput.positionWeights).weights;
  p.fitInput.references.forEach((ref,h)=>{const t=(ref.cuts||[ref.cut]).indexOf(Math.min(...(ref.cuts||[ref.cut]))),ids=solution.junction.models.flatMap((m,j)=>m.reference===h?[j]:[]),share=(w:number[])=>ids.reduce((s,j)=>s+w[j],0),a=share(independent),b=share(solution.junction.weights);
   agreement=Math.max(agreement,100*Math.abs(a-b));if(Math.min(a,b)<.02)return;
   const sizes=[...new Set(ids.map(j=>solution.junction.models[j].sizes?.[t]||0))];agreement=Math.max(agreement,50*sizes.reduce((sum,size)=>sum+Math.abs(ids.reduce((s,j)=>s+((solution.junction.models[j].sizes?.[t]||0)===size?independent[j]/a-solution.junction.weights[j]/b:0),0)),0));
  });
 });
 result.plots=views.flatMap((v,i)=>v.plots.map(p=>({...p,name:`Read ${i+1} · ${p.name}`})));
 result.warnings=[...new Set(views.flatMap((v,i)=>v.warnings.map(w=>`Read ${i+1}: ${w}`)))];
 if(!solution.coveragePass)result.warnings.push('Some targets lack sufficient flanking signal in both directions; numerical estimates remain displayed.');
 if(agreement>10)result.warnings.push('Opposite-direction reads disagree by more than 10 pp in reference shares or first-target indel distributions. Check that both pairs came from the same edited sample and WT control.');
 result.mixture={...result.mixture!,r2:variance?1-error/variance:0,rmse:Math.sqrt(error/(4*bases)),bases,passesChecks:views.every(v=>v.mixture!.passesChecks)&&agreement<=10};
 if(result.joint)result.joint.reconstructionR2=result.mixture.r2;
 result.pooled={...result.pooled!,controlR2:Math.min(...views.map(v=>v.pooled!.controlR2))};
 if(result.diagnostics)result.diagnostics.readAgreementPp=agreement;
 for(const a of result.annotations||[])a.estimable=result.mixture.passesChecks&&a.share>=.02;
 return result;
}

END MODULE


BEGIN MODULE src/app/workers/core/sanger-path-agreement.ts
import {PooledAmplicon,SangerAnalysisResult} from '../../models/sanger-analysis';
/** A fallback is not an independent replicate. If a similarly good first fit
 * explains the same reference shares and net-size groups but implies different
 * target marginals, retain group evidence and withhold the target claim.
 * Agreement thresholds are prototype resolution policies, not p-values. */
export function guardCompetingTargetPaths(primary:SangerAnalysisResult,alternative:SangerAnalysisResult,products:PooledAmplicon[]):SangerAnalysisResult{
 if(!primary.joint||!alternative.joint||!primary.mixture||!alternative.mixture||!primary.mixture.converged)return alternative;
 if(primary.mixture.r2<.995)return alternative;
 const affected=new Set<string>();
 for(const product of products){
  const a=primary.joint.groups.filter(g=>g.reference===product.name),b=alternative.joint.groups.filter(g=>g.reference===product.name),sa=a.reduce((s,g)=>s+g.fraction,0),sb=b.reduce((s,g)=>s+g.fraction,0);
  if(Math.max(sa,sb)<.02)continue;
  if(Math.min(sa,sb)<.02||Math.abs(sa-sb)>.03||a.some(g=>g.netSize===null)||b.some(g=>g.netSize===null)){affected.add(product.name);continue;}
  const sizes=new Set(a.concat(b).map(g=>g.netSize));let tv=0;
  for(const size of sizes)tv+=Math.abs(a.filter(g=>g.netSize===size).reduce((s,g)=>s+g.fraction,0)/sa-b.filter(g=>g.netSize===size).reduce((s,g)=>s+g.fraction,0)/sb)/2;
  if(tv>.05){affected.add(product.name);continue;}
  for(const target of alternative.joint.targets.filter(t=>t.reference===product.name&&t.edited!==null)){
   let lower=0,upper=0;
   for(const group of a){const explanations=group.alternatives.length?group.alternatives:[{reference:product.name,events:group.events}],present=explanations.map(e=>e.events.some(event=>event.target===0||event.target===target.target));if(present.every(Boolean))lower+=group.fraction/sa;if(present.some(Boolean))upper+=group.fraction/sa;}
   if(Math.max(Math.abs(target.edited!-lower),Math.abs(target.edited!-upper))>.05)affected.add(product.name);
  }
 }
 if(affected.size){
  alternative.mixture.passesChecks=false;
  for(const target of alternative.joint.targets)if(affected.has(target.reference))target.edited=null;
  for(const ref of alternative.pooled?.amplicons??[])if(affected.has(ref.name))for(const key of ['wt','indel','inFrame','outOfFrame'] as const)ref[key]=null;
  for(const annotation of alternative.annotations??[])if(affected.has(annotation.name)){annotation.estimable=false;for(const component of annotation.components)component.unresolved=true;}
  alternative.warnings.push('Competing high-fit paths disagree on reference, net-size or target attribution for '+[...affected].join(', ')+'. Net-size groups are retained; target percentages are withheld.');
 }
 return alternative;
}

END MODULE


BEGIN MODULE src/app/workers/core/sanger-path-aliases.ts
import type{JunctionInput,JunctionFit,JunctionModel}from'./sanger-junction-mixture';
import{junctionStarts}from'./sanger-junction-mixture';
import{jointPathColumn,PathEvent}from'./sanger-junction-paths';
const bases='ACGT';
function molecule(sequence:string,events:PathEvent[]){let out=sequence;for(const e of [...events].reverse()){const o=e.origins[0],insert=e.size>0?o.probabilities!.map(v=>bases[v.indexOf(Math.max(...v))]).join(''):'';out=out.slice(0,o.start)+insert+out.slice(o.start+Math.max(0,-e.size));}return out;}
/** Search physical recombinations that change target-size marginals but leave
 * every measured four-channel emission exactly unchanged. This is an explicit
 * non-identifiability witness, not a posterior probability or exhaustive proof
 * of identifiability when none is found. Point estimates are never withheld. */
export function findPathAliases(input:JunctionInput,fit:JunctionFit){
 const reports:{reference:number;target:number;shiftableJointFraction:number}[]=[];
 input.references.forEach((ref,h)=>{
  const cuts=ref.cuts||[ref.cut];if(cuts.length!==2)return;const first=cuts[0]<cuts[1]?0:1,last=1-first;
  const hard=fit.models.flatMap((m,index)=>{
   if(m.reference!==h||fit.weights[index]<=1e-8||!m.sizes)return[];
   let mass=fit.weights[index];const path=(m.path||[]).map(e=>{const total=e.origins.reduce((s,o)=>s+o.weight,0),o=e.origins.reduce((a,b)=>a.weight>b.weight?a:b);mass*=o.weight/Math.max(1e-12,total);const probabilities=o.probabilities?.map(v=>{const best=v.indexOf(Math.max(...v));mass*=v[best];return v.map((_,b)=>Number(b===best));});return{target:e.target,size:e.size,origins:[{start:o.start,weight:1,probabilities}]};}),model={...m,path},early=path.filter(e=>e.target===first+1),baseline=molecule(ref.sequence,early);
   return[{model,index,mass,early,baseline,sequence:molecule(ref.sequence,path)}];
  });
  const find=(row:typeof hard[number],sequence:string):JunctionModel|null=>{
   const size=sequence.length-row.baseline.length;if(size< -input.maxDeletion||size>input.maxInsertion)return null;
   let event:PathEvent|undefined;
   if(!size){if(sequence!==row.baseline)return null;}
   else{
    const prefix=row.model.sizes![first];
    for(const start of junctionStarts(cuts[last],size,input.radius,ref.sequence.length)){
     if(row.early.some(e=>start<e.origins[0].start+Math.max(0,-e.size)))continue;
     const p=start+prefix,insert=size>0?sequence.slice(p,p+size):'';
     if(row.baseline.slice(0,p)+insert+row.baseline.slice(p+Math.max(0,-size))!==sequence)continue;
     event={target:last+1,size,origins:[{start,weight:1,probabilities:size>0?Array.from(insert,b=>Array.from(bases,v=>Number(v===b))):undefined}]};break;
    }
    if(!event)return null;
   }
   const sizes=[...row.model.sizes!];sizes[last]=size;
   return{...row.model,sizes,size:sizes.reduce((s,v)=>s+v,0),path:[...row.early,...(event?[event]:[])]};
  };
  let shifted=0;
  for(let a=0;a<hard.length;a++)for(let b=0;b<a;b++){
   const A=hard[a],B=hard[b],mass=Math.min(A.mass,B.mass);if(mass<=shifted||A.model.sizes![first]===B.model.sizes![first])continue;
   const firstEnd=Math.max(...[A,B].flatMap(row=>row.early.map(e=>e.origins[0].start+Math.max(0,e.size))) ,0);
   const lo=Math.max(firstEnd+3,cuts[last]-input.maxDeletion-input.radius-input.maxInsertion),hi=Math.min(A.sequence.length,B.sequence.length,cuts[last]+input.radius+2*input.maxInsertion);
   for(let p=lo;p<=hi;p++){
    const C=find(A,A.sequence.slice(0,p)+B.sequence.slice(p)),D=find(B,B.sequence.slice(0,p)+A.sequence.slice(p));if(!C||!D)continue;
    const before=[A.model.sizes![last],B.model.sizes![last]].sort((a,b)=>a-b).join('/'),after=[C.sizes![last],D.sizes![last]].sort((a,b)=>a-b).join('/');if(before===after)continue;
    const oldA=jointPathColumn(input,A.model),oldB=jointPathColumn(input,B.model),newA=jointPathColumn(input,C),newB=jointPathColumn(input,D);
    if(oldA.every((v,k)=>Math.abs(v+oldB[k]-newA[k]-newB[k])<1e-9)){shifted=mass;break;}
   }
  }
  if(shifted>0)reports.push({reference:h,target:last+1,shiftableJointFraction:Math.min(1,2*shifted)});
 });return reports;
}

END MODULE


BEGIN MODULE src/app/workers/core/sanger-path-common.ts
import {normalizeMoleculeEdits} from './sanger-edit-normalize';
import {validateSangerProductBounds} from '../../utils/sanger-product-bounds';
import {repairSignalTraceCycles} from './sanger-cycle';
import {PooledAmplicon,PooledPreflight,SangerAnalysisSettings,SangerAnalysisResult,SangerJointEvent,SangerTrace,TRACE_BASES} from '../../models/sanger-analysis';
import {exactBindingSites} from './sanger-primer-designer';
import {reverseComplement} from '../../utils/biology.utils';
import {registerTrace} from './sanger-pooled';
import {contrastPeaks,integratedPeaks,calibrateSignal,correctGain,gramCondition,rawSignalScore} from './sanger-signal';
import {emissionVector,fitContextEmission,fitNormalizedEmissionMixture as fitEmissionMixtureOriginal} from './sanger-emission';
import {plotTrace} from './sanger-analysis';
const DNA='ACGT';
const fitEmissionMixture=(columns:number[][],observed:number[])=>fitEmissionMixtureOriginal(columns,observed);
export interface PathEdit {start:number;end:number;inserted:string}
export interface DiscoveredPath {sequence:string;events:PathEdit[];score:number}
/** Affine match/substitution/insertion/deletion alignment of a signal profile.
 * Only training-position signal enters profile; unobserved inserted bases are N.
 * This is a bounded Viterbi-style optimization, not a posterior-probability HMM. */
export function discoverSignalPath(reference:string,profile:number[][],anchor:number,end:number,cuts:number[],options:{maxDeletion:number;maxInsertion:number;alpha:number;local:boolean;training:boolean[];startRadius?:number}):DiscoveredPath|null{
 const n=end-anchor,m=Math.min(reference.length-anchor,end-anchor+options.maxDeletion),cols=m+1,cells=(n+1)*cols,NEG=-1e20;
 if(n<40||m<40||cells>2_000_000)throw Error('Alignment grid exceeds the browser budget. Shorten the read region.');
 const M=new Float32Array(cells).fill(NEG),I=new Float32Array(cells).fill(NEG),D=new Float32Array(cells).fill(NEG),pm=new Uint8Array(cells),pi=new Uint8Array(cells),pd=new Uint8Array(cells),mb=new Uint8Array(cells),ib=new Uint8Array(cells),il=new Uint8Array(cells),dl=new Uint16Array(cells);
 const inLocal=(p:number)=>cuts.some(c=>Math.abs(p-c)<=25),penalty=(p:number)=>{const distance=Math.min(...cuts.map(c=>Math.abs(p-c)));return 2.8+1.7*Math.min(1,distance/60)+options.alpha*2;};
 const extend=.015,insertExtend=.25,subPenalty=Math.log(3)+3;
 const insertionAllowed=(p:number)=>options.startRadius===undefined||cuts.some(c=>Math.abs(p-c)<=options.startRadius!),deletionAllowed=(start:number,end:number)=>options.startRadius===undefined||cuts.some(c=>start<=c+options.startRadius!&&end>=c-options.startRadius!);
 M[0]=0;for(let j=1;j<=Math.min(m,options.maxDeletion);j++){if(options.local&&!inLocal(anchor+j-1))break;D[j]=-penalty(anchor)-extend*(j-1);pd[j]=j===1?0:2;dl[j]=j;}
 let terminalScore=NEG,terminalI=n;
 for(let i=1;i<=n;i++){
  const row=i*cols,prev=(i-1)*cols,p=anchor+i-1,observed=profile[p]||[0,0,0,0],trained=options.training[p]||false;
  const maxScore=Math.max(...observed),prob=observed.map(v=>Math.exp(8*(v-maxScore))),sumProb=prob.reduce((a,b)=>a+b,0),logEmission=prob.map(v=>Math.log(4*v/sumProb));
  let bestBase=0;for(let b=1;b<4;b++)if(observed[b]>observed[bestBase])bestBase=b;
  const lo=Math.max(0,i-options.maxInsertion),hi=Math.min(m,i+options.maxDeletion);
  for(let j=lo;j<=hi;j++){
   const at=row+j,refP=anchor+j-1,allowed=!options.local||inLocal(refP);
   if(j>0){const before=prev+j-1;let state=0,value=M[before];if(I[before]>value){state=1;value=I[before];}if(D[before]>value&&deletionAllowed(anchor+j-1-dl[before],anchor+j-1)){state=2;value=D[before];}
    const base=DNA.indexOf(reference[refP]);let emitted=base>=0?logEmission[base]:0,chosen=base;
    if(trained&&allowed&&logEmission[bestBase]-subPenalty>emitted){emitted=logEmission[bestBase]-subPenalty;chosen=bestBase;}
    M[at]=value+emitted;pm[at]=state;mb[at]=chosen<0?4:chosen;
   }
   if(allowed&&j<m){const before=prev+j,open=penalty(anchor+j);let value=insertionAllowed(anchor+j)?M[before]-open:NEG,state=0,length=1;
    if(il[before]<options.maxInsertion&&I[before]-insertExtend>value){value=I[before]-insertExtend;state=1;length=il[before]+1;}
    I[at]=value;pi[at]=state;ib[at]=trained?bestBase:4;il[at]=length;
   }
   if(j>0&&allowed){const before=at-1,open=penalty(refP);let value=M[before]-open,state=0;let length=1;if(dl[before]<options.maxDeletion&&D[before]-extend>value){value=D[before]-extend;state=2;length=dl[before]+1;}D[at]=value;pd[at]=state;dl[at]=length;}
  }
  // A molecule can terminate before other pooled products; it emits no further signal.
  if(m===reference.length-anchor&&m>=lo&&m<=hi&&M[row+m]>terminalScore){terminalScore=M[row+m];terminalI=i;}
 }
 let i=n,j=0,state=0,best=NEG;
 for(let k=Math.max(0,n-options.maxInsertion);k<=Math.min(m,n+options.maxDeletion);k++)for(let st=0;st<2;st++){const v=(st?I:M)[n*cols+k];if(v>best){best=v;j=k;state=st;}}
 if(terminalScore>best){best=terminalScore;i=terminalI;j=m;state=0;}
 if(best<NEG/2)return null;const finalJ=j,ops:{kind:string;p:number;base:string}[]=[];
 while(i>0||j>0){const at=i*cols+j;if(state===0){if(i===0||j===0)return null;ops.push({kind:'M',p:anchor+j-1,base:DNA[mb[at]]||'N'});state=pm[at];i--;j--;}else if(state===1){if(i===0)return null;ops.push({kind:'I',p:anchor+j,base:DNA[ib[at]]||'N'});state=pi[at];i--;}else{if(j===0)return null;ops.push({kind:'D',p:anchor+j-1,base:''});state=pd[at];j--;}}
 ops.reverse();let sequence=reference.slice(0,anchor),events:PathEdit[]=[],active:PathEdit|undefined;
 const close=()=>{if(active){events.push(active);active=undefined;}};
 for(const op of ops){const equal=op.kind==='M'&&op.base===reference[op.p];if(equal){close();sequence+=op.base;continue;}if(!active)active={start:op.p,end:op.p,inserted:''};if(op.kind!=='I')active.end=op.p+1;if(op.kind!=='D'){active.inserted+=op.base;sequence+=op.base;}}
 close();sequence+=reference.slice(anchor+finalJ);
 // Preserve compound edits separated only by a short exact bridge.
 const merged:PathEdit[]=[];for(const e of events){const last=merged.at(-1);if(last&&e.start-last.end<=3){last.inserted+=reference.slice(last.end,e.start)+e.inserted;last.end=e.end;}else merged.push({...e});}
 if(merged.length>8)return null;return {sequence,events:merged,score:best};
}
/** Insertion site must be cut-proximal. A deletion can extend in either
 * direction, so its interval must intersect a cut-proximal initiation zone.
 * Equal-length substitutions do not have an indel origin. */
export function eventsRespectIndelOrigin(events:PathEdit[],cuts:number[],radius:number|undefined):boolean{
 return radius===undefined||events.every(e=>e.inserted.length===e.end-e.start||cuts.some(c=>e.start<=c+radius&&e.end>=c-radius));
}
export function materialize(reference:string,events:PathEdit[]):string|null {let at=0,result='';for(const e of [...events].sort((a,b)=>a.start-b.start)){if(e.start<at||e.end>reference.length||e.start<0)return null;result+=reference.slice(at,e.start)+e.inserted;at=e.end;}return result+reference.slice(at);}
export interface Prepared {name:string;sequence:string;cuts:number[];input:PooledAmplicon;reverse:boolean;offset:number}
export interface Model {reference:number;sequence:string;events:PathEdit[];aliases:{reference:number;events:PathEdit[]}[]}
export const failure=(reason:string):PooledPreflight=>({passed:false,reason,positions:[],modelCount:0,rank:0,condition:null,minimumDifferences:0,minimumCutOffset:0,amplicons:[]});
export function prepare(amplicons:PooledAmplicon[],primer:string,s:SangerAnalysisSettings){
 if(!/^[ACGT]{12,40}$/.test(primer)||amplicons.length<1||amplicons.length>4||new Set(amplicons.map(a=>a.name)).size!==amplicons.length)throw Error('Use 1–4 uniquely named submitted amplicons and the actual sequencing primer.');
 if(s.method!=='mixture'||s.signalModel==='classic')throw Error('Signal-guided alignment requires calibrated trace decomposition.');
 if(!Number.isInteger(s.window)||s.window<60||s.window>500||!Number.isInteger(s.readStart)||!Number.isInteger(s.readEnd)||s.readStart<0||s.readEnd>1000||s.readEnd<=s.readStart)throw Error('Invalid signal-guided window or usable read range.');
 if(s.editStartRadius!==undefined&&(!Number.isInteger(s.editStartRadius)||s.editStartRadius<0||s.editStartRadius>100))throw Error('Indel origin radius must be 0–100 bp, or unrestricted.');
 const maxDeletion=s.largeDeletions?(s.maxDeletion??200):s.maxIndel,maxInsertion=s.maxInsertion??30;
 if(!Number.isInteger(maxDeletion)||maxDeletion<1||maxDeletion>500||!Number.isInteger(maxInsertion)||maxInsertion<1||maxInsertion>60)throw Error('Use deletion limit 1–500 and insertion limit 1–60 bp.');
 const report=failure(''),refs:Prepared[]=amplicons.map(a=>{
  validateSangerProductBounds(a);const cuts=a.cuts??[a.cut];if(!a.name.trim()||!/^[ACGT]+$/.test(a.sequence)||a.sequence.length>20000||cuts.length<1||cuts.length>2||new Set(cuts).size!==cuts.length||cuts.some(c=>!Number.isInteger(c)||c<=0||c>=a.sequence.length))throw Error('Use one or two distinct cuts within each full submitted product.');
  const hits=exactBindingSites(primer,{name:a.name,sequence:a.sequence,cuts});if(hits.length!==1)throw Error(a.name+': sequencing primer must bind exactly once.');
  const h=hits[0],reverse=h.direction==='reverse',offset=reverse?a.sequence.length-h.start:h.end,sequence=(reverse?reverseComplement(a.sequence):a.sequence).slice(offset),positions=cuts.map(c=>(reverse?a.sequence.length-c:c)-offset).sort((a,b)=>a-b);
  if(positions[0]<s.readStart+100||positions.at(-1)!+30>s.readEnd)throw Error(a.name+': preserve ≥100 usable upstream bases and ≥30 downstream bases inside the Sanger range.');
  const windows=positions.map(c=>({cutOffset:c,windowStart:Math.max(s.readStart,c-Math.floor(s.window/2)),windowEnd:Math.min(s.readEnd,c+Math.ceil(s.window/2))}));
  report.amplicons.push({name:a.name,direction:h.direction,primerStart:h.start+1,primerEnd:h.end,cutOffset:positions[0],windowStart:windows[0].windowStart,windowEnd:windows.at(-1)!.windowEnd,targets:windows,maximumWindow:Math.min(500,Math.max(0,2*Math.min(...positions.map(c=>Math.min(c-s.readStart,s.readEnd-c))))),maximumIndependentWindow:positions.length===2?positions[1]-positions[0]:undefined,mergedWindows:windows.length===2&&windows[0].windowEnd>windows[1].windowStart});
  return {name:a.name,sequence,cuts:positions,input:a,reverse,offset};
 });
 const anchor=s.readStart+84,end=s.readEnd;
 const positions=Array.from({length:end-anchor},(_,i)=>i+anchor);if(positions.length<120)throw Error('Too little shared signal for signal-guided path inference.');
 const columns=refs.map(r=>positions.flatMap(p=>TRACE_BASES.map(b=>Number(r.sequence[p]===b))));
 let min=positions.length;for(let i=0;i<refs.length;i++)for(let j=0;j<i;j++)min=Math.min(min,positions.filter(p=>refs[i].sequence[p]!==refs[j].sequence[p]).length);
 const geometry=gramCondition(columns);if(min<3||geometry.rank<refs.length||geometry.condition===null||geometry.condition>100)throw Error('WT references are indistinguishable on the sequencing-primer axis.');
 report.passed=true;report.reason='WT primer geometry passed; edit paths are discovered from training signal and require independent QC.';report.positions=positions;report.modelCount=refs.length;report.minimumDifferences=min;report.rank=geometry.rank;report.condition=geometry.condition;report.joint={mode:s.jointEngine||'signal-path',candidateCount:refs.length,ambiguousClasses:0,coverageExcluded:0,maxDeletion};
 return {refs,report,anchor,end,maxDeletion,maxInsertion};
}
export function checkSignalPathSeparation(amplicons:PooledAmplicon[],primer:string,s:SangerAnalysisSettings){try{return prepare(amplicons,primer,s).report;}catch(e){return failure(e instanceof Error?e.message:String(e));}}
/** Large isolated peak-spacing jumps can mimic a biological phase shift. */
export function assertSignalTraceGrid(trace:SangerTrace):void{
 const spacing=Array.from({length:trace.peaks.length-1},(_,i)=>trace.peaks[i+1]-trace.peaks[i]);
 for(let i=8;i<spacing.length-8;i++){const local=spacing.slice(i-7,i+8).sort((a,b)=>a-b),median=local[7];if(median>0&&(spacing[i]>1.8*median||spacing[i]<.55*median))throw Error(trace.name+': irregular base-call cycle spacing. Re-basecall or inspect the chromatogram before interpreting a phase shift as an indel.');}
}
export interface SignalPathInput {amplicons:PooledAmplicon[];primer:string;ampliconConfirmed:boolean;settings:SangerAnalysisSettings;control:SangerTrace;sample:SangerTrace;synthetic?:boolean}

END MODULE


BEGIN MODULE src/app/workers/core/sanger-path-identifiability.ts
import {PooledAmplicon,SangerAnalysisResult,SangerJointEvent} from '../../models/sanger-analysis';
/** A repeat can place the same final molecule on either side of a cut.
 * Enumerate sequence-equivalent junctions, not just paths found by the search.
 * Size/fraction evidence is retained; non-identifiable target attribution is withheld. */
export function guardEquivalentJunctions(result:SangerAnalysisResult,products:PooledAmplicon[],originRadius?:number):SangerAnalysisResult{
 if(!result.joint)return result;
 const affected=new Set<string>();
 for(const group of result.joint.groups){
  const explanations=group.alternatives.length?group.alternatives:group.reference?[{reference:group.reference,events:group.events}]:[];
  const expanded:{reference:string;events:SangerJointEvent[]}[]=[];
  for(const explanation of explanations){
   const product=products.find(p=>p.name===explanation.reference);if(!product)continue;
   const reference=product.sequence,cuts=[...(product.cuts??[product.cut])].sort((a,b)=>a-b);
   const target=(start:number,end:number)=>{if(cuts.length===2&&start<=cuts[0]&&end>=cuts[1])return 0;const distance=cuts.map(c=>Math.min(Math.abs(start-c),Math.abs(end-c)));const nearest=distance.indexOf(Math.min(...distance));return Math.min(...distance)>60?-1:nearest+1;};
   const semantic=(events:SangerJointEvent[])=>[...new Set(events.flatMap(e=>e.target===0?cuts.map((_,i)=>i+1):[e.target]))].sort().join('/');
   const original=semantic(explanation.events);expanded.push(explanation);
   // A junction inside both cut-origin zones cannot support a single-target
   // claim just because its fitted end falls one base short of the exact cut.
   if(originRadius!==undefined)for(const event of explanation.events){
    const compatible=cuts.flatMap((cut,i)=>event.start<=cut+originRadius&&event.end>=cut-originRadius?[i+1]:[]);
    if(event.end>event.start&&event.target>0&&compatible.length>1)affected.add(explanation.reference);
   }
   for(let i=0;i<explanation.events.length;i++){
    const event=explanation.events[i],insert=event.insertedSequence??'N'.repeat(event.inserted),deleted=event.end-event.start;
    if(deleted===insert.length||insert.includes('N'))continue;
    const molecule=reference.slice(0,event.start)+insert+reference.slice(event.end);let prefix=0,suffix=0;
    while(prefix<Math.min(reference.length,molecule.length)&&reference[prefix]===molecule[prefix])prefix++;
    while(suffix<Math.min(reference.length,molecule.length)&&reference[reference.length-1-suffix]===molecule[molecule.length-1-suffix])suffix++;
    const lo=Math.max(0,reference.length-suffix-deleted,...explanation.events.filter((_,k)=>k!==i).filter(e=>e.end<=event.start).map(e=>e.end));
    const hi=Math.min(prefix,reference.length-deleted,molecule.length-insert.length,...explanation.events.filter((_,k)=>k!==i).filter(e=>e.start>=event.end).map(e=>e.start-deleted));
    const represented=new Set<string>([original]);
    for(let start=lo;start<=hi;start++){
     if(originRadius!==undefined&&!cuts.some(c=>start<=c+originRadius&&start+deleted>=c-originRadius))continue;
     const shifted={...event,start,end:start+deleted,insertedSequence:molecule.slice(start,start+insert.length),target:target(start,start+deleted)},events=explanation.events.map((e,k)=>k===i?shifted:e),key=semantic(events);
     if(represented.has(key))continue;represented.add(key);expanded.push({reference:explanation.reference,events});affected.add(explanation.reference);
    }
   }
  }
  if(expanded.length>explanations.length){group.alternatives=expanded;group.events=[];}
 }
 if(affected.size){
  result.mixture!.passesChecks=false;
  for(const t of result.joint.targets)if(affected.has(t.reference))t.edited=null;
  for(const annotation of result.annotations??[])if(affected.has(annotation.name))for(const component of annotation.components)component.unresolved=true;
  result.warnings.push('Repeat-equivalent junctions or overlapping cut-origin zones leave target attribution unresolved for '+[...affected].join(', ')+'. Target editing estimates are withheld; fitted net sizes and fractions are retained.');
 }
 return result;
}

END MODULE


BEGIN MODULE src/app/workers/core/sanger-path-known.ts
import {normalizeMoleculeEdits} from './sanger-edit-normalize';
import {validateSangerProductBounds} from '../../utils/sanger-product-bounds';
import {repairSignalTraceCycles} from './sanger-cycle';
import {PooledAmplicon,PooledPreflight,SangerAnalysisSettings,SangerAnalysisResult,SangerJointEvent,SangerTrace,TRACE_BASES} from '../../models/sanger-analysis';
import {exactBindingSites} from './sanger-primer-designer';
import {reverseComplement} from '../../utils/biology.utils';
import {registerTrace} from './sanger-pooled';
import {contrastPeaks,integratedPeaks,calibrateSignal,correctGain,gramCondition,rawSignalScore} from './sanger-signal';
import {emissionVector,fitContextEmission,fitNormalizedEmissionMixture as fitEmissionMixtureOriginal} from './sanger-emission';
import {plotTrace} from './sanger-analysis';
const DNA='ACGT';
const fitEmissionMixture=(columns:number[][],observed:number[])=>fitEmissionMixtureOriginal(columns,observed);
import {SignalPathInput,PathEdit,Model,materialize,prepare,assertSignalTraceGrid,eventsRespectIndelOrigin,discoverSignalPath} from './sanger-path-common';
export function analyzeSignalPaths(input:SignalPathInput,progress:(s:string)=>void=()=>{},audit?:(state:any)=>void):SangerAnalysisResult{
 if(!input.ampliconConfirmed||!input.control||!input.sample)throw Error('Confirm submitted products and provide matched control/sample chromatograms.');
 const controlRepair=repairSignalTraceCycles(input.control),sampleRepair=repairSignalTraceCycles(input.sample);input={...input,control:controlRepair.trace,sample:sampleRepair.trace};
 assertSignalTraceGrid(input.control);assertSignalTraceGrid(input.sample);
 const {refs,report,anchor,maxDeletion,maxInsertion}=prepare(input.amplicons,input.primer,input.settings),s=input.settings;
 progress('Registering traces to the sequencing primer');const registered=refs.map(r=>({name:r.name,sequence:r.sequence,cut:anchor}));
 const cr=registerTrace(input.control,registered,s,integratedPeaks(input.control,[0,0,0,0]).vectors),sr=registerTrace(input.sample,registered,s,integratedPeaks(input.sample,[0,0,0,0]).vectors),ctl=contrastPeaks(input.control).vectors,raw=contrastPeaks(input.sample).vectors;
 const calibrate=(vectors:number[][],trim:number)=>{const obs=Array.from({length:vectors.length+trim},(_,p)=>vectors[p-trim]||[0,0,0,0]),rows=Array.from({length:anchor-12},(_,p)=>p).filter(p=>p>=Math.max(s.readStart,trim+10)&&p-trim<vectors.length-2);return calibrateSignal(obs,rows,refs.map(r=>Array.from(r.sequence,(_,p)=>TRACE_BASES.map(b=>Number(r.sequence[p]===b)))));};
 const cc=calibrate(ctl,cr.trim),sc=calibrate(raw,sr.trim);if(cc.validationR2<.8||sc.validationR2<.8)throw Error('Signal-path upstream calibration failed.');
 const end=Math.min(Math.max(...report.positions)+1,cr.trim+ctl.length-3,sr.trim+raw.length-3),positions=report.positions.filter(p=>p<end);if(positions.length<120)throw Error('Insufficient actual trace coverage for path inference.');
 const wt=ctl.map(v=>correctGain(v,cc.gains)),sample=raw.map(v=>correctGain(v,sc.gains)),controlObs=Array.from({length:wt.length+cr.trim},(_,p)=>wt[p-cr.trim]||[0,0,0,0]);
 const context=fitContextEmission(refs.map(r=>r.sequence),positions,controlObs,cc.weights,cc.lag),theta=context.theta;
 const emit=(sequence:string,p:number,lag:number)=>{const v=emissionVector(sequence,p,lag,theta);if(sequence[p]==='N')for(let b=0;b<4;b++)v[b]+=(1-Math.abs(lag))/4;return v;};
 const models:Model[]=refs.map((r,h)=>({reference:h,sequence:r.sequence,events:[],aliases:[{reference:h,events:[]}]})),cols=models.map(m=>positions.flatMap(p=>emit(m.sequence,p,sc.lag))),controlCols=models.map(m=>positions.flatMap(p=>emit(m.sequence,p,cc.lag)));
 const ctlFit=fitEmissionMixture(controlCols,positions.flatMap(p=>wt[p-cr.trim])),ctlScore=rawSignalScore(controlCols,positions.flatMap(p=>ctl[p-cr.trim]),ctlFit.weights,cc.gains);if(ctlScore.r2<.85)throw Error('WT product references do not explain the unedited control.');
 if(s.jointEngine==='signal-hybrid')refs.forEach((r,h)=>r.cuts.forEach(cut=>{for(let size=-Math.min(s.maxIndel,maxDeletion);size<=Math.min(s.maxIndel,maxInsertion);size++){if(!size)continue;const events=[{start:cut,end:cut+Math.max(0,-size),inserted:'N'.repeat(Math.max(0,size))}],sequence=materialize(r.sequence,events);if(sequence){models.push({reference:h,sequence,events,aliases:[{reference:h,events}]});cols.push(positions.flatMap(p=>emit(sequence,p,sc.lag)));}}}));
 if(s.jointEngine==='signal-hybrid')refs.forEach((r,h)=>r.cuts.forEach(cut=>{for(const shift of [-1,0,1])for(const base of TRACE_BASES){const at=cut+shift,b=TRACE_BASES.indexOf(base);if((sample[at-sr.trim]?.[b]||0)<.02||!eventsRespectIndelOrigin([{start:at,end:at,inserted:base}],r.cuts,s.editStartRadius))continue;const events=[{start:at,end:at,inserted:base}],sequence=materialize(r.sequence,events);if(sequence){models.push({reference:h,sequence,events,aliases:[{reference:h,events}]});cols.push(positions.flatMap(p=>emit(sequence,p,sc.lag)));}}}));
 const y=positions.flatMap(p=>sample[p-sr.trim]),train=positions.flatMap((_,i)=>i%5<3?[i]:[]),tune=positions.flatMap((_,i)=>i%5===3?[i]:[]),test=positions.flatMap((_,i)=>i%5===4?[i]:[]),subset=(v:number[],rows:number[])=>rows.flatMap(i=>v.slice(4*i,4*i+4)),trainY=subset(y,train),tuneY=subset(y,tune);
 const trained=Array(end+1).fill(false);train.forEach(i=>trained[positions[i]]=true);
 const fitIds=(ids:number[])=>fitEmissionMixture(ids.map(id=>subset(cols[id],train)),trainY);
 const score=(ids:number[],weights:number[],rows:number[])=>rawSignalScore(ids.map(id=>subset(cols[id],rows)),rows.flatMap(i=>raw[positions[i]-sr.trim]),weights,sc.gains);
 const objective=(ids:number[],weights:number[])=>score(ids,weights,tune).rmse**2+.0001*ids.reduce((sum,id,h)=>sum+weights[h]*models[id].events.reduce((a,e)=>a+1+.05*e.inserted.length,0),0);
 let ids=refs.map((_,i)=>i),fit=fitIds(ids),best=objective(ids,fit.weights),iterations=0,alignments=0;
 const signatures=new Map(models.map((m,id)=>[positions.map(p=>m.sequence[p]||'-').join(''),id]));
 for(let round=0;round<20;round++){
  progress(`Discovering ${s.jointEngine==='signal-local'?'local':'alignment'} paths · round ${round+1}`);
  const pred=positions.map((_,p)=>{const v=TRACE_BASES.map((_,b)=>ids.reduce((sum,id,h)=>sum+fit.weights[h]*cols[id][4*p+b],0)),total=v.reduce((a,b)=>a+b,0);return v.map(x=>total?x/total:0);});
  const candidates:number[]=[];
  for(let h=0;h<refs.length;h++)for(const seed of [
    ...[.01,.06,.18].map(alpha=>({alpha,column:cols[h],normalize:false})),
    ...ids.map((id,k)=>({id,weight:fit.weights[k]})).filter(x=>models[x.id].reference===h&&x.weight>.02).sort((a,b)=>b.weight-a.weight).slice(0,3).map(x=>({alpha:x.weight,column:cols[x.id],normalize:true}))
   ]){const alpha=seed.alpha;
   const profile=Array.from({length:end+1},()=>[0,0,0,0]);positions.forEach((p,k)=>{if(trained[p]){for(let b=0;b<4;b++)profile[p][b]=sample[p-sr.trim][b]-pred[k][b]+alpha*seed.column[k*4+b];if(seed.normalize){const total=profile[p].reduce((sum,v)=>sum+Math.max(0,v),0);if(total>.001)profile[p]=profile[p].map(v=>Math.max(0,v)/total);}}});
   const path=discoverSignalPath(refs[h].sequence,profile,anchor,end,refs[h].cuts,{maxDeletion,maxInsertion,alpha,local:s.jointEngine==='signal-local',training:trained,startRadius:s.editStartRadius});alignments++;
   if(!path||!path.events.length)continue;
   const variants:PathEdit[][]=[path.events,...path.events.map(e=>[e]),...path.events.map((_,i)=>path.events.filter((_,j)=>j!==i))];
   // Reduce unsupported local changes and refine insertion positions/bases using
   // training-signal nucleotides. No exponential enumeration of inserted strings.
   path.events.forEach((e,k)=>{if(e.inserted.length!==1||e.end!==e.start)return;for(let offset=-3;offset<=3;offset++){
    const start=e.start+offset,p=start+path.events.filter(x=>x.end<=start&&x!==e).reduce((sum,x)=>sum+x.inserted.length-(x.end-x.start),0);
    if(start<anchor||p<anchor||p>=end)continue;const bases=trained[p]?DNA.split('').filter((b,i)=>profile[p][i]>.005):['N'];
    for(const base of bases)variants.push(path.events.map((x,i)=>i===k?{start,end:start,inserted:base}:x));
   }});
   for(const rawEvents of variants){const normalized=normalizeMoleculeEdits(refs[h].sequence,rawEvents);if(!eventsRespectIndelOrigin(normalized,refs[h].cuts,s.editStartRadius))continue;let shift=0;const events=normalized.filter(e=>{const observedStart=e.start+shift;shift+=e.inserted.length-(e.end-e.start);return observedStart<end;});const sequence=materialize(refs[h].sequence,events);if(!sequence)continue;const signature=positions.map(p=>sequence[p]||'-').join(''),alias={reference:h,events},old=signatures.get(signature);
    if(old!==undefined){if(!models[old].aliases.some(a=>a.reference===h&&JSON.stringify(a.events)===JSON.stringify(events)))models[old].aliases.push(alias);continue;}
    const id=models.length;models.push({reference:h,sequence,events,aliases:[alias]});cols.push(positions.flatMap(p=>emit(sequence,p,sc.lag)));signatures.set(signature,id);candidates.push(id);
   }
  }
  // Insertion bases have no reference at held-out cycles. Refine the discovered
  // gap scaffold using all observed channels, while keeping coefficient splits.
  // The resulting reconstruction score is an internal check, not independent CV.
  const addVariant=(h:number,events:PathEdit[])=>{events=normalizeMoleculeEdits(refs[h].sequence,events);if(!eventsRespectIndelOrigin(events,refs[h].cuts,s.editStartRadius))return;const sequence=materialize(refs[h].sequence,events);if(!sequence)return;const signature=positions.map(p=>sequence[p]||'-').join(''),old=signatures.get(signature),alias={reference:h,events};if(old!==undefined){if(!models[old].aliases.some(a=>a.reference===h&&JSON.stringify(a.events)===JSON.stringify(events)))models[old].aliases.push(alias);return;}const id=models.length;models.push({reference:h,sequence,events,aliases:[alias]});cols.push(positions.flatMap(p=>emit(sequence,p,sc.lag)));signatures.set(signature,id);};
  for(const item of ids.map((id,k)=>({id,weight:fit.weights[k]})).filter(x=>x.weight>.01)){
   const model=models[item.id];if(!model.events.some(e=>e.inserted.length))continue;
   let shift=0;const events=model.events.map(e=>{let inserted='';for(let j=0;j<e.inserted.length;j++){const p=e.start+shift+j,k=positions.indexOf(p);if(k<0){inserted+='N';continue;}const values=TRACE_BASES.map((_,b)=>sample[p-sr.trim][b]-pred[k][b]+item.weight*cols[item.id][k*4+b]),best=values.indexOf(Math.max(...values));inserted+=values[best]>.005?TRACE_BASES[best]:'N';}shift+=e.inserted.length-(e.end-e.start);return {...e,inserted};});addVariant(model.reference,events);
  }
  // Screen bounded insertion lengths by downstream residual support; construct
  // only the top supported lengths, with inserted bases from observed signal.
  if(round<3)for(let h=0;h<refs.length;h++)for(const cut of refs[h].cuts){
   const ranked=Array.from({length:maxInsertion},(_,i)=>i+1).map(size=>{let support=0,count=0;for(const k of train){const p=positions[k];if(p<cut+size+2||p>cut+size+100)continue;const v=emit(refs[h].sequence,p-size,sc.lag);support+=v.reduce((sum,x,b)=>sum+x*(y[k*4+b]-pred[k][b]),0);count++;}return {size,support:count?support/count:-Infinity};}).sort((a,b)=>b.support-a.support).slice(0,3);
   for(const {size,support}of ranked){if(support<.005)continue;let inserted='';for(let j=0;j<size;j++){const p=cut+j,k=positions.indexOf(p);if(k<0){inserted+='N';continue;}const values=TRACE_BASES.map((_,b)=>sample[p-sr.trim][b]-pred[k][b]+.08*cols[h][k*4+b]),best=values.indexOf(Math.max(...values));inserted+=values[best]>.005?TRACE_BASES[best]:'N';}addVariant(h,[{start:cut,end:cut,inserted}]);}
  }
  // Existing rejected candidates remain eligible; no truth-derived edit list is used.
  const available=models.map((_,id)=>id).filter(id=>!ids.includes(id)).map(id=>({id,score:train.reduce((sum,i)=>sum+TRACE_BASES.reduce((v,_,b)=>v+cols[id][i*4+b]*(y[i*4+b]-pred[i][b]),0),0)})).sort((a,b)=>b.score-a.score).slice(0,12).map(x=>x.id);let next:{ids:number[];fit:ReturnType<typeof fitEmissionMixture>;rmse:number}|undefined;
  for(const id of available){const trial=[...ids,id],f=fitIds(trial);if(f.weights.at(-1)!<.005)continue;const rmse=objective(trial,f.weights);if(rmse<best-Math.max(.0000001,best*.015)&&(!next||rmse<next.rmse))next={ids:trial,fit:f,rmse};}
  if(!next)break;ids=next.ids;fit=next.fit;best=next.rmse;iterations++;
 }
 // Replacement moves let a simpler scaffold compete directly with a selected
 // complex path. Adding it as a correlated extra column can otherwise leave
 // the unpenalized refit trapped on the more complex explanation.
 for(let sweep=0;sweep<3;sweep++){let changed=false;for(const id of [...ids]){const model=models[id];if(!model.events.length)continue;for(let event=0;event<model.events.length;event++){
  const events=normalizeMoleculeEdits(refs[model.reference].sequence,model.events.filter((_,k)=>k!==event)),sequence=materialize(refs[model.reference].sequence,events);if(!sequence)continue;const signature=positions.map(p=>sequence[p]||'-').join('');let replacement=signatures.get(signature);
  if(replacement===undefined){replacement=models.length;models.push({reference:model.reference,sequence,events,aliases:[{reference:model.reference,events}]});cols.push(positions.flatMap(p=>emit(sequence,p,sc.lag)));signatures.set(signature,replacement);}
  const trial=[...new Set(ids.map(x=>x===id?replacement!:x))],f=fitIds(trial),value=objective(trial,f.weights);
  if(value<best-1e-8){ids=trial;fit=f;best=value;changed=true;break;}
 }}if(!changed)break;}
 for(let sweep=0;sweep<3;sweep++){let removed=false;for(const id of [...ids]){if(id<refs.length)continue;const trial=ids.filter(x=>x!==id),f=fitIds(trial),value=objective(trial,f.weights);if(value<=best+1e-7){ids=trial;fit=f;best=value;removed=true;}}if(!removed)break;}
 ids=ids.filter((id,i)=>id<refs.length||fit.weights[i]>=.005);fit=fitIds(ids);
 const qc=score(ids,fit.weights,test),full=score(ids,fit.weights,positions.map((_,i)=>i)),geometry=gramCondition(ids.map(id=>cols[id]));
 const net=(events:PathEdit[])=>events.reduce((sum,e)=>sum+e.inserted.length-(e.end-e.start),0),inputEvents=(h:number,events:PathEdit[]):SangerJointEvent[]=>{const r=refs[h];return events.map(e=>{const distances=r.cuts.map(c=>Math.min(Math.abs(e.start-c),Math.abs(e.end-c))),near=distances.indexOf(Math.min(...distances)),span=r.cuts.length===2&&e.start<=r.cuts[0]&&e.end>=r.cuts[1],target=span?0:Math.min(...distances)>60?-1:(r.reverse?r.cuts.length-near:near+1),a=r.offset+e.start,b=r.offset+e.end;return {target,start:r.reverse?r.input.sequence.length-b:a,end:r.reverse?r.input.sequence.length-a:b,inserted:e.inserted.length,insertedSequence:r.reverse?reverseComplement(e.inserted):e.inserted};}).sort((a,b)=>a.start-b.start);};
 const chosen=ids.flatMap((id,i)=>fit.weights[i]>1e-9?[{model:models[id],fraction:fit.weights[i]}]:[]),aliasKey=(a:{reference:number;events:PathEdit[]})=>a.reference+':'+JSON.stringify(inputEvents(a.reference,a.events));
 const ambiguous=chosen.filter(c=>new Set(c.model.aliases.map(aliasKey)).size>1),refAmbiguous=ambiguous.filter(c=>new Set(c.model.aliases.map(a=>a.reference)).size>1);
 const semantic=(a:{reference:number;events:PathEdit[]})=>a.reference+':'+(a.events.length?'edit':'WT')+':'+net(a.events)+':'+[...new Set(inputEvents(a.reference,a.events).map(e=>e.target))].sort().join('/');
 const attributionAmbiguous=ambiguous.filter(c=>new Set(c.model.aliases.map(semantic)).size>1);
 const groups=chosen.map((c,i)=>({id:`P${i+1}`,reference:refAmbiguous.includes(c)?null:refs[c.model.reference].name,fraction:c.fraction,netSize:new Set(c.model.aliases.map(a=>net(a.events))).size>1?null:net(c.model.events),events:ambiguous.includes(c)?[]:inputEvents(c.model.reference,c.model.events),alternatives:ambiguous.includes(c)?c.model.aliases.map(a=>({reference:refs[a.reference].name,events:inputEvents(a.reference,a.events)})):[]}));
 const warnings:string[]=['Experimental signal-guided inference: a good trace fit does not establish unique allele identities.'];if(s.editStartRadius!==undefined)warnings.push(`Indel initiation constrained to cut ±${s.editStartRadius} bp; off-target-window edits are outside this model.`);if(controlRepair.added+controlRepair.removed+sampleRepair.added+sampleRepair.removed)warnings.push(`Raw-peak-supported cycle metadata repair: control +${controlRepair.added}/-${controlRepair.removed}, sample +${sampleRepair.added}/-${sampleRepair.removed}.`);if(qc.r2<.995)warnings.push('Signal-guided paths failed internal trace reconstruction QC; editing estimates are withheld.');if(ambiguous.length)warnings.push('Some discovered paths have indistinguishable reference or event explanations. Inspect alternatives.');if(iterations===20)warnings.push('Path discovery reached its iteration limit.');if(geometry.condition===null||geometry.condition>100)warnings.push('Selected path contributions are poorly conditioned.');
 const boundary=chosen.some(c=>c.model.events.some(e=>e.end-e.start>=maxDeletion||e.inserted.length>=maxInsertion));if(boundary)warnings.push('A supported event reaches the configured path-search boundary.');
 const localResidual=full.positionRmse,unknown=positions.map(p=>chosen.some(c=>c.model.sequence[p]==='N'||c.model.sequence[p-1]==='N')),localDiscordant=localResidual.filter((v,i)=>!unknown[i]&&v>Math.max(.06,(ctlScore.positionRmse[i]||0)+.04)).length;
 if(localDiscordant)warnings.push(`${localDiscordant} positions have unexplained local signal (possible substitution, artifact or missing edit path). Editing estimates are withheld.`);
 const unknownInsertion=chosen.some(c=>c.model.sequence.includes('N'));if(unknownInsertion)warnings.push('Inserted bases are unresolved; known-sequence inference cannot certify these target fractions.');
 const allowed=!unknownInsertion&&qc.r2>=.995&&!localDiscordant&&fit.converged&&iterations<20&&!boundary&&geometry.condition!==null&&geometry.condition<=100;
 const perRef=refs.map((r,h)=>{const rows=chosen.filter(c=>c.model.reference===h&&!attributionAmbiguous.includes(c)),share=rows.reduce((sum,c)=>sum+c.fraction,0),estimable=allowed&&share>=.02&&!attributionAmbiguous.some(c=>c.model.aliases.some(a=>a.reference===h)),bins=new Map<number,number>();rows.forEach(c=>bins.set(net(c.model.events),(bins.get(net(c.model.events))||0)+c.fraction));const zero=rows.filter(c=>!c.model.events.length).reduce((sum,c)=>sum+c.fraction,0);return {name:r.name,share,wt:estimable?zero/share:null,indel:estimable?1-zero/share:null,inFrame:estimable?rows.filter(c=>c.model.events.length&&net(c.model.events)%3===0).reduce((sum,c)=>sum+c.fraction,0)/share:null,outOfFrame:estimable?rows.filter(c=>net(c.model.events)%3!==0).reduce((sum,c)=>sum+c.fraction,0)/share:null,components:[...bins].map(([size,fraction])=>({size,fraction:share?fraction/share:0})),estimable,rows};});
 const targets=perRef.flatMap((a,h)=>(refs[h].input.cuts??[refs[h].input.cut]).map((cut,t)=>({reference:a.name,target:t+1,cut,edited:a.estimable?a.rows.filter(c=>inputEvents(h,c.model.events).some(e=>e.target===t+1||e.target===0)).reduce((sum,c)=>sum+c.fraction,0)/a.share:null})));
 const annotations=perRef.map((a,h)=>({name:a.name,reference:refs[h].input.sequence,cut:refs[h].input.cut,cuts:refs[h].input.cuts,direction:refs[h].reverse?'reverse' as const:'forward' as const,share:a.share,estimable:a.estimable,components:a.rows.map(c=>({size:net(c.model.events),fraction:c.fraction/a.share,events:ambiguous.includes(c)?[]:inputEvents(h,c.model.events),unresolved:ambiguous.includes(c)}))}));
 const bins=new Map<number,number>();chosen.forEach(c=>bins.set(net(c.model.events),(bins.get(net(c.model.events))||0)+c.fraction));const zero=chosen.filter(c=>!c.model.events.length).reduce((sum,c)=>sum+c.fraction,0);
 const first=Math.min(...refs.flatMap(r=>r.cuts)),plots=[{trace:input.control,trim:cr.trim},{trace:input.sample,trim:sr.trim}].map(({trace,trim})=>plotTrace(trace,first-trim,Math.max(...refs.flatMap(r=>r.cuts))-first+s.window/2));report.positions=positions;report.joint!.candidateCount=models.length;
 if(audit)audit({refs,positions,cols,models,ids,weights:fit.weights,theta,sampleLag:sc.lag,gains:sc.gains,observed:y,rawObserved:positions.flatMap(p=>raw[p-sr.trim]),anchor,end,iterations,train,test,tune,controlPositionRmse:ctlScore.positionRmse});
 return {method:'pooled',referenceName:refs.length===1?refs[0].name:'Mixed amplicons',sampleName:input.sample.name,controlName:input.control.name,direction:'forward',cutPosition:first,alignmentIdentity:0,alignment:[],variants:[],plots,warnings,synthetic:!!input.synthetic,annotations,diagnostics:{algorithmVersion:'sanger-'+(s.jointEngine||'signal-path')+'-v4-known',signalModel:'calibrated',requestedMaxIndel:s.maxIndel,discordantPositions:localDiscordant,maxPositionRmse:Math.max(...localResidual),controlCalibration:cc,sampleCalibration:sc,contextEmission:context},mixture:{components:[...bins].map(([size,fraction])=>({size,fraction})),wt:zero,indel:1-zero,inFrame:chosen.filter(c=>c.model.events.length&&net(c.model.events)%3===0).reduce((sum,c)=>sum+c.fraction,0),outOfFrame:chosen.filter(c=>net(c.model.events)%3!==0).reduce((sum,c)=>sum+c.fraction,0),r2:full.r2,rmse:full.rmse,upstreamError:1-sc.validationR2,bases:positions.length,fitStart:anchor,fitEnd:end,converged:fit.converged,passesChecks:allowed&&!attributionAmbiguous.length},pooled:{preflight:report,controlR2:ctlScore.r2,sampleTrim:sr.trim,controlTrim:cr.trim,amplicons:perRef.map(({rows,estimable,...a})=>a)},joint:{groups,targets,reconstructionR2:qc.r2,heldOutR2:qc.r2,selectedModels:chosen.length,candidateModels:models.length,iterations,maxDeletion,ambiguousFraction:ambiguous.reduce((sum,c)=>sum+c.fraction,0),alignmentSearches:alignments}};
}

END MODULE


BEGIN MODULE src/app/workers/core/sanger-path-latent.ts
import {fitLatentInsertions,LatentInsertionFit} from './sanger-latent-insertion-r';
import {normalizeMoleculeEdits} from './sanger-edit-normalize';
import {validateSangerProductBounds} from '../../utils/sanger-product-bounds';
import {repairSignalTraceCycles} from './sanger-cycle';
import {PooledAmplicon,PooledPreflight,SangerAnalysisSettings,SangerAnalysisResult,SangerJointEvent,SangerTrace,TRACE_BASES} from '../../models/sanger-analysis';
import {exactBindingSites} from './sanger-primer-designer';
import {reverseComplement} from '../../utils/biology.utils';
import {registerTrace} from './sanger-pooled';
import {contrastPeaks,integratedPeaks,calibrateSignal,correctGain,gramCondition,rawSignalScore} from './sanger-signal';
import {emissionVector,fitContextEmission,fitNormalizedEmissionMixture as fitEmissionMixtureOriginal} from './sanger-emission';
import {plotTrace} from './sanger-analysis';
const DNA='ACGT';
const fitEmissionMixture=(columns:number[][],observed:number[])=>fitEmissionMixtureOriginal(columns,observed);
import {SignalPathInput,PathEdit,Model,materialize,prepare,assertSignalTraceGrid,eventsRespectIndelOrigin,discoverSignalPath} from './sanger-path-common';
export function analyzeSignalPaths(input:SignalPathInput,progress:(s:string)=>void=()=>{},audit?:(state:any)=>void):SangerAnalysisResult{
 if(!input.ampliconConfirmed||!input.control||!input.sample)throw Error('Confirm submitted products and provide matched control/sample chromatograms.');
 const controlRepair=repairSignalTraceCycles(input.control),sampleRepair=repairSignalTraceCycles(input.sample);input={...input,control:controlRepair.trace,sample:sampleRepair.trace};
 assertSignalTraceGrid(input.control);assertSignalTraceGrid(input.sample);
 const {refs,report,anchor,maxDeletion,maxInsertion}=prepare(input.amplicons,input.primer,input.settings),s=input.settings;
 progress('Registering traces to the sequencing primer');const registered=refs.map(r=>({name:r.name,sequence:r.sequence,cut:anchor}));
 const cr=registerTrace(input.control,registered,s,integratedPeaks(input.control,[0,0,0,0]).vectors),sr=registerTrace(input.sample,registered,s,integratedPeaks(input.sample,[0,0,0,0]).vectors),ctl=contrastPeaks(input.control).vectors,raw=contrastPeaks(input.sample).vectors;
 const calibrate=(vectors:number[][],trim:number)=>{const obs=Array.from({length:vectors.length+trim},(_,p)=>vectors[p-trim]||[0,0,0,0]),rows=Array.from({length:anchor-12},(_,p)=>p).filter(p=>p>=Math.max(s.readStart,trim+10)&&p-trim<vectors.length-2);return calibrateSignal(obs,rows,refs.map(r=>Array.from(r.sequence,(_,p)=>TRACE_BASES.map(b=>Number(r.sequence[p]===b)))));};
 const cc=calibrate(ctl,cr.trim),sc=calibrate(raw,sr.trim);if(cc.validationR2<.8||sc.validationR2<.8)throw Error('Signal-path upstream calibration failed.');
 const end=Math.min(Math.max(...report.positions)+1,cr.trim+ctl.length-3,sr.trim+raw.length-3),positions=report.positions.filter(p=>p<end);if(positions.length<120)throw Error('Insufficient actual trace coverage for path inference.');
 const wt=ctl.map(v=>correctGain(v,cc.gains)),sample=raw.map(v=>correctGain(v,sc.gains)),controlObs=Array.from({length:wt.length+cr.trim},(_,p)=>wt[p-cr.trim]||[0,0,0,0]);
 const context=fitContextEmission(refs.map(r=>r.sequence),positions,controlObs,cc.weights,cc.lag),theta=context.theta;
 const emit=(sequence:string,p:number,lag:number)=>{const v=emissionVector(sequence,p,lag,theta);if(sequence[p]==='N')for(let b=0;b<4;b++)v[b]+=(1-Math.abs(lag))/4;return v;};
 const models:Model[]=refs.map((r,h)=>({reference:h,sequence:r.sequence,events:[],aliases:[{reference:h,events:[]}]})),cols=models.map(m=>positions.flatMap(p=>emit(m.sequence,p,sc.lag))),controlCols=models.map(m=>positions.flatMap(p=>emit(m.sequence,p,cc.lag)));
 const ctlFit=fitEmissionMixture(controlCols,positions.flatMap(p=>wt[p-cr.trim])),ctlScore=rawSignalScore(controlCols,positions.flatMap(p=>ctl[p-cr.trim]),ctlFit.weights,cc.gains);if(ctlScore.r2<.85)throw Error('WT product references do not explain the unedited control.');
 if(s.jointEngine==='signal-hybrid')refs.forEach((r,h)=>r.cuts.forEach(cut=>{for(let size=-Math.min(s.maxIndel,maxDeletion);size<=Math.min(s.maxIndel,maxInsertion);size++){if(!size)continue;const events=[{start:cut,end:cut+Math.max(0,-size),inserted:'N'.repeat(Math.max(0,size))}],sequence=materialize(r.sequence,events);if(sequence){models.push({reference:h,sequence,events,aliases:[{reference:h,events}]});cols.push(positions.flatMap(p=>emit(sequence,p,sc.lag)));}}}));
 if(s.jointEngine==='signal-hybrid')refs.forEach((r,h)=>r.cuts.forEach(cut=>{for(const shift of [-1,0,1])for(const base of TRACE_BASES){const at=cut+shift,b=TRACE_BASES.indexOf(base);if((sample[at-sr.trim]?.[b]||0)<.02||!eventsRespectIndelOrigin([{start:at,end:at,inserted:base}],r.cuts,s.editStartRadius))continue;const events=[{start:at,end:at,inserted:base}],sequence=materialize(r.sequence,events);if(sequence){models.push({reference:h,sequence,events,aliases:[{reference:h,events}]});cols.push(positions.flatMap(p=>emit(sequence,p,sc.lag)));}}}));
 const y=positions.flatMap(p=>sample[p-sr.trim]),train=positions.map((_,i)=>i),tune=positions.map((_,i)=>i),test=positions.map((_,i)=>i),subset=(v:number[],rows:number[])=>rows.flatMap(i=>v.slice(4*i,4*i+4)),trainY=subset(y,train),tuneY=subset(y,tune);
 const trained=Array(end+1).fill(false);train.forEach(i=>trained[positions[i]]=true);
 const fittedColumns=new WeakMap<number[],LatentInsertionFit>();
 const fitIds=(ids:number[])=>{const latent=fitLatentInsertions(ids.map(id=>cols[id]),ids.map(id=>models[id].sequence),ids.map(id=>models[id].events.some(e=>e.inserted.includes('N')&&e.inserted.length!==e.end-e.start)&&!models[id].events.some(e=>e.inserted.includes('N')&&e.inserted.length===e.end-e.start)),positions,positions.map(p=>sample[p-sr.trim]),train,sc.lag,fitEmissionMixture);fittedColumns.set(latent.fit.weights,latent);return latent.fit;};
 const score=(ids:number[],weights:number[],rows:number[])=>rawSignalScore((fittedColumns.get(weights)?.columns||ids.map(id=>cols[id])).map(c=>subset(c,rows)),rows.flatMap(i=>raw[positions[i]-sr.trim]),weights,sc.gains);
 const objective=(ids:number[],weights:number[])=>score(ids,weights,tune).rmse**2+Math.max(1e-8,ctlScore.rmse**2)*3*(fittedColumns.get(weights)?.unknown.filter(Boolean).length||0)*Math.log(4*positions.length)/(4*positions.length)+.0001*ids.reduce((sum,id,h)=>sum+weights[h]*models[id].events.reduce((a,e)=>a+1+.05*e.inserted.length,0),0);
 let ids=refs.map((_,i)=>i),fit=fitIds(ids),best=objective(ids,fit.weights),iterations=0,alignments=0;
 const signatures=new Map(models.map((m,id)=>[positions.map(p=>m.sequence[p]||'-').join(''),id]));
 const addScaffold=(h:number,raw:PathEdit[])=>{
  const normalized=normalizeMoleculeEdits(refs[h].sequence,raw);if(!eventsRespectIndelOrigin(normalized,refs[h].cuts,s.editStartRadius))return;
  for(const events of [normalized,normalized.map(e=>e.inserted.length&&e.inserted.length!==e.end-e.start?{...e,inserted:'N'.repeat(e.inserted.length)}:e)]){
   const sequence=materialize(refs[h].sequence,events);if(!sequence)continue;const signature=positions.map(p=>sequence[p]||'-').join(''),old=signatures.get(signature),alias={reference:h,events};if(old!==undefined){if(!models[old].aliases.some(a=>a.reference===h&&JSON.stringify(a.events)===JSON.stringify(events)))models[old].aliases.push(alias);continue;}const id=models.length;models.push({reference:h,sequence,events,aliases:[alias]});cols.push(positions.flatMap(p=>emit(sequence,p,sc.lag)));signatures.set(signature,id);
  }
 };
 for(let round=0;round<20;round++){
  progress(`Discovering ${s.jointEngine==='signal-local'?'local':'alignment'} paths · round ${round+1}`);
  const pred=positions.map((_,p)=>{const v=TRACE_BASES.map((_,b)=>ids.reduce((sum,id,h)=>sum+fit.weights[h]*(fittedColumns.get(fit.weights)?.columns[h]||cols[id])[4*p+b],0)),total=v.reduce((a,b)=>a+b,0);return v.map(x=>total?x/total:0);});
  const candidates:number[]=[];
  for(let h=0;h<refs.length;h++)for(const seed of [
    ...[.01,.06,.18].map(alpha=>({alpha,column:cols[h],normalize:false})),
    ...ids.map((id,k)=>({id,weight:fit.weights[k]})).filter(x=>models[x.id].reference===h&&x.weight>.02).sort((a,b)=>b.weight-a.weight).slice(0,3).map(x=>({alpha:x.weight,column:cols[x.id],normalize:true}))
   ]){const alpha=seed.alpha;
   const profile=Array.from({length:end+1},()=>[0,0,0,0]);positions.forEach((p,k)=>{if(trained[p]){for(let b=0;b<4;b++)profile[p][b]=sample[p-sr.trim][b]-pred[k][b]+alpha*seed.column[k*4+b];if(seed.normalize){const total=profile[p].reduce((sum,v)=>sum+Math.max(0,v),0);if(total>.001)profile[p]=profile[p].map(v=>Math.max(0,v)/total);}}});
   const path=discoverSignalPath(refs[h].sequence,profile,anchor,end,refs[h].cuts,{maxDeletion,maxInsertion,alpha,local:s.jointEngine==='signal-local',training:trained,startRadius:s.editStartRadius});alignments++;
   if(!path||!path.events.length)continue;
   const variants:PathEdit[][]=[path.events,...path.events.map(e=>[e]),...path.events.map((_,i)=>path.events.filter((_,j)=>j!==i))];
   // Reduce unsupported local changes and refine insertion positions/bases using
   // training-signal nucleotides. No exponential enumeration of inserted strings.
   path.events.forEach((e,k)=>{if(e.inserted.length!==1||e.end!==e.start)return;for(let offset=-3;offset<=3;offset++){
    const start=e.start+offset,p=start+path.events.filter(x=>x.end<=start&&x!==e).reduce((sum,x)=>sum+x.inserted.length-(x.end-x.start),0);
    if(start<anchor||p<anchor||p>=end)continue;const bases=trained[p]?DNA.split('').filter((b,i)=>profile[p][i]>.005):['N'];
    for(const base of bases)variants.push(path.events.map((x,i)=>i===k?{start,end:start,inserted:base}:x));
   }});
   for(const rawEvents of variants)addScaffold(h,rawEvents);
  }

  // Insertion bases have no reference at held-out cycles. Refine the discovered
  // gap scaffold using all observed channels, while keeping coefficient splits.
  // The resulting reconstruction score is an internal check, not independent CV.
  const addVariant=addScaffold;
  for(const item of ids.map((id,k)=>({id,weight:fit.weights[k]})).filter(x=>x.weight>.01)){
   const model=models[item.id];if(model.sequence.includes('N')||!model.events.some(e=>e.inserted.length))continue;
   let shift=0;const events=model.events.map(e=>{let inserted='';for(let j=0;j<e.inserted.length;j++){const p=e.start+shift+j,k=positions.indexOf(p);if(k<0){inserted+='N';continue;}const values=TRACE_BASES.map((_,b)=>sample[p-sr.trim][b]-pred[k][b]+item.weight*cols[item.id][k*4+b]),best=values.indexOf(Math.max(...values));inserted+=values[best]>.005?TRACE_BASES[best]:'N';}shift+=e.inserted.length-(e.end-e.start);return {...e,inserted};});addVariant(model.reference,events);
  }
  // Screen bounded insertion lengths by downstream residual support; construct
  // only the top supported lengths, with inserted bases from observed signal.
  if(round<3)for(let h=0;h<refs.length;h++)for(const cut of refs[h].cuts){
   const ranked=Array.from({length:maxInsertion},(_,i)=>i+1).map(size=>{let support=0,count=0;for(const k of train){const p=positions[k];if(p<cut+size+2||p>cut+size+100)continue;const v=emit(refs[h].sequence,p-size,sc.lag);support+=v.reduce((sum,x,b)=>sum+x*(y[k*4+b]-pred[k][b]),0);count++;}return {size,support:count?support/count:-Infinity};}).sort((a,b)=>b.support-a.support).slice(0,3);
   for(const {size,support}of ranked){if(support<.005)continue;let inserted='';for(let j=0;j<size;j++){const p=cut+j,k=positions.indexOf(p);if(k<0){inserted+='N';continue;}const values=TRACE_BASES.map((_,b)=>sample[p-sr.trim][b]-pred[k][b]+.08*cols[h][k*4+b]),best=values.indexOf(Math.max(...values));inserted+=values[best]>.005?TRACE_BASES[best]:'N';}addVariant(h,[{start:cut,end:cut,inserted}]);}
  }
  // Existing rejected candidates remain eligible; no truth-derived edit list is used.
  const available=models.map((_,id)=>id).filter(id=>!ids.includes(id)).map(id=>({id,score:train.reduce((sum,i)=>sum+TRACE_BASES.reduce((v,_,b)=>v+cols[id][i*4+b]*(y[i*4+b]-pred[i][b]),0),0)})).sort((a,b)=>b.score-a.score).slice(0,12).map(x=>x.id);let next:{ids:number[];fit:ReturnType<typeof fitEmissionMixture>;rmse:number}|undefined;
  for(const id of available){const trial=[...ids,id],f=fitIds(trial);if(f.weights.at(-1)!<.005)continue;const rmse=objective(trial,f.weights);if(rmse<best-Math.max(.0000001,best*.015)&&(!next||rmse<next.rmse))next={ids:trial,fit:f,rmse};}
  if(!next)break;ids=next.ids;fit=next.fit;best=next.rmse;iterations++;
 }
 // Replacement moves let a simpler scaffold compete directly with a selected
 // complex path. Adding it as a correlated extra column can otherwise leave
 // the unpenalized refit trapped on the more complex explanation.
 for(let sweep=0;sweep<3;sweep++){let changed=false;for(const id of [...ids]){const model=models[id];if(!model.events.length)continue;for(let event=0;event<model.events.length;event++){
  const events=normalizeMoleculeEdits(refs[model.reference].sequence,model.events.filter((_,k)=>k!==event)),sequence=materialize(refs[model.reference].sequence,events);if(!sequence)continue;const signature=positions.map(p=>sequence[p]||'-').join('');let replacement=signatures.get(signature);
  if(replacement===undefined){replacement=models.length;models.push({reference:model.reference,sequence,events,aliases:[{reference:model.reference,events}]});cols.push(positions.flatMap(p=>emit(sequence,p,sc.lag)));signatures.set(signature,replacement);}
  const trial=[...new Set(ids.map(x=>x===id?replacement!:x))],f=fitIds(trial),value=objective(trial,f.weights);
  if(value<best-1e-8){ids=trial;fit=f;best=value;changed=true;break;}
 }}if(!changed)break;}
 for(let sweep=0;sweep<3;sweep++){let removed=false;for(const id of [...ids]){if(id<refs.length)continue;const trial=ids.filter(x=>x!==id),f=fitIds(trial),value=objective(trial,f.weights);if(value<=best+1e-7){ids=trial;fit=f;best=value;removed=true;}}if(!removed)break;}
 ids=ids.filter((id,i)=>id<refs.length||fit.weights[i]>=.005);fit=fitIds(ids);
 const latent=fittedColumns.get(fit.weights),knownTest=test.filter(i=>!latent?.unknown[i]),knownAll=positions.flatMap((_,i)=>!latent?.unknown[i]?[i]:[]),qc=score(ids,fit.weights,knownTest),full=score(ids,fit.weights,positions.map((_,i)=>i)),geometry=gramCondition(ids.map(id=>subset(cols[id],knownAll)));
 const net=(events:PathEdit[])=>events.reduce((sum,e)=>sum+e.inserted.length-(e.end-e.start),0),inputEvents=(h:number,events:PathEdit[]):SangerJointEvent[]=>{const r=refs[h];return events.map(e=>{const distances=r.cuts.map(c=>Math.min(Math.abs(e.start-c),Math.abs(e.end-c))),near=distances.indexOf(Math.min(...distances)),span=r.cuts.length===2&&e.start<=r.cuts[0]&&e.end>=r.cuts[1],target=span?0:Math.min(...distances)>60?-1:(r.reverse?r.cuts.length-near:near+1),a=r.offset+e.start,b=r.offset+e.end;return {target,start:r.reverse?r.input.sequence.length-b:a,end:r.reverse?r.input.sequence.length-a:b,inserted:e.inserted.length,insertedSequence:r.reverse?reverseComplement(e.inserted):e.inserted};}).sort((a,b)=>a.start-b.start);};
 const chosen=ids.flatMap((id,i)=>fit.weights[i]>1e-9?[{model:models[id],fraction:fit.weights[i]}]:[]),aliasKey=(a:{reference:number;events:PathEdit[]})=>a.reference+':'+JSON.stringify(inputEvents(a.reference,a.events));
 const ambiguous=chosen.filter(c=>new Set(c.model.aliases.map(aliasKey)).size>1),refAmbiguous=ambiguous.filter(c=>new Set(c.model.aliases.map(a=>a.reference)).size>1);
 const semantic=(a:{reference:number;events:PathEdit[]})=>a.reference+':'+(a.events.length?'edit':'WT')+':'+net(a.events)+':'+[...new Set(inputEvents(a.reference,a.events).map(e=>e.target))].sort().join('/');
 const attributionAmbiguous=ambiguous.filter(c=>new Set(c.model.aliases.map(semantic)).size>1);
 const groups=chosen.map((c,i)=>({id:`P${i+1}`,reference:refAmbiguous.includes(c)?null:refs[c.model.reference].name,fraction:c.fraction,netSize:new Set(c.model.aliases.map(a=>net(a.events))).size>1?null:net(c.model.events),events:ambiguous.includes(c)?[]:inputEvents(c.model.reference,c.model.events),alternatives:ambiguous.includes(c)?c.model.aliases.map(a=>({reference:refs[a.reference].name,events:inputEvents(a.reference,a.events)})):[]}));
 const warnings:string[]=['Experimental signal-guided inference: a good trace fit does not establish unique allele identities.'];if(s.editStartRadius!==undefined)warnings.push(`Indel initiation constrained to cut ±${s.editStartRadius} bp; off-target-window edits are outside this model.`);if(controlRepair.added+controlRepair.removed+sampleRepair.added+sampleRepair.removed)warnings.push(`Raw-peak-supported cycle metadata repair: control +${controlRepair.added}/-${controlRepair.removed}, sample +${sampleRepair.added}/-${sampleRepair.removed}.`);if(qc.r2<.995)warnings.push('Signal-guided paths failed internal trace reconstruction QC; editing estimates are withheld.');if(ambiguous.length)warnings.push('Some discovered paths have indistinguishable reference or event explanations. Inspect alternatives.');if(iterations===20)warnings.push('Path discovery reached its iteration limit.');if(geometry.condition===null||geometry.condition>100)warnings.push('Selected path contributions are poorly conditioned.');
 const boundary=chosen.some(c=>c.model.events.some(e=>e.end-e.start>=maxDeletion||e.inserted.length>=maxInsertion));if(boundary)warnings.push('A supported event reaches the configured path-search boundary.');
 const localResidual=full.positionRmse,unknown=positions.map(p=>chosen.some(c=>c.model.sequence[p]==='N'||c.model.sequence[p-1]==='N'||c.model.sequence[p+1]==='N')),localDiscordant=localResidual.filter((v,i)=>!unknown[i]&&v>Math.max(.06,(ctlScore.positionRmse[i]||0)+.04)).length;
 if(localDiscordant)warnings.push(`${localDiscordant} positions have unexplained local signal (possible substitution, artifact or missing edit path). Editing estimates are withheld.`);
 if(latent?.probabilities.length)warnings.push(`Insertion bases remain unresolved at ${latent.probabilities.length} cycles; mixture fractions were fitted from known flanks.`);
 const unsupportedUnknown=chosen.some(c=>c.model.sequence.includes('N')&&(c.model.events.some(e=>e.inserted.includes('N')&&e.inserted.length===e.end-e.start)||!latent?.probabilities.length));if(unsupportedUnknown)warnings.push('Unknown inserted bases lack an identifiable known-flank model; editing estimates are withheld.');
 const allowed=!unsupportedUnknown&&knownTest.length>=25&&qc.r2>=.995&&!localDiscordant&&fit.converged&&iterations<20&&!boundary&&geometry.condition!==null&&geometry.condition<=100;
 const perRef=refs.map((r,h)=>{const rows=chosen.filter(c=>c.model.reference===h&&!attributionAmbiguous.includes(c)),share=rows.reduce((sum,c)=>sum+c.fraction,0),estimable=allowed&&share>=.02&&!attributionAmbiguous.some(c=>c.model.aliases.some(a=>a.reference===h)),bins=new Map<number,number>();rows.forEach(c=>bins.set(net(c.model.events),(bins.get(net(c.model.events))||0)+c.fraction));const zero=rows.filter(c=>!c.model.events.length).reduce((sum,c)=>sum+c.fraction,0);return {name:r.name,share,wt:estimable?zero/share:null,indel:estimable?1-zero/share:null,inFrame:estimable?rows.filter(c=>c.model.events.length&&net(c.model.events)%3===0).reduce((sum,c)=>sum+c.fraction,0)/share:null,outOfFrame:estimable?rows.filter(c=>net(c.model.events)%3!==0).reduce((sum,c)=>sum+c.fraction,0)/share:null,components:[...bins].map(([size,fraction])=>({size,fraction:share?fraction/share:0})),estimable,rows};});
 const targets=perRef.flatMap((a,h)=>(refs[h].input.cuts??[refs[h].input.cut]).map((cut,t)=>({reference:a.name,target:t+1,cut,edited:a.estimable?a.rows.filter(c=>inputEvents(h,c.model.events).some(e=>e.target===t+1||e.target===0)).reduce((sum,c)=>sum+c.fraction,0)/a.share:null})));
 const annotations=perRef.map((a,h)=>({name:a.name,reference:refs[h].input.sequence,cut:refs[h].input.cut,cuts:refs[h].input.cuts,direction:refs[h].reverse?'reverse' as const:'forward' as const,share:a.share,estimable:a.estimable,components:a.rows.map(c=>({size:net(c.model.events),fraction:c.fraction/a.share,events:ambiguous.includes(c)?[]:inputEvents(h,c.model.events),unresolved:ambiguous.includes(c)}))}));
 const bins=new Map<number,number>();chosen.forEach(c=>bins.set(net(c.model.events),(bins.get(net(c.model.events))||0)+c.fraction));const zero=chosen.filter(c=>!c.model.events.length).reduce((sum,c)=>sum+c.fraction,0);
 const first=Math.min(...refs.flatMap(r=>r.cuts)),plots=[{trace:input.control,trim:cr.trim},{trace:input.sample,trim:sr.trim}].map(({trace,trim})=>plotTrace(trace,first-trim,Math.max(...refs.flatMap(r=>r.cuts))-first+s.window/2));report.positions=positions;report.joint!.candidateCount=models.length;
 if(audit)audit({refs,positions,cols,models,ids,weights:fit.weights,theta,sampleLag:sc.lag,gains:sc.gains,observed:y,rawObserved:positions.flatMap(p=>raw[p-sr.trim]),anchor,end,iterations,train,test,tune,controlPositionRmse:ctlScore.positionRmse});
 return {method:'pooled',referenceName:refs.length===1?refs[0].name:'Mixed amplicons',sampleName:input.sample.name,controlName:input.control.name,direction:'forward',cutPosition:first,alignmentIdentity:0,alignment:[],variants:[],plots,warnings,synthetic:!!input.synthetic,annotations,diagnostics:{latentInsertion:latent?.probabilities.length?{unknownCycles:latent.probabilities.length,knownTrainingRows:latent.knownTrainingRows,knownFitRows:knownTest.length,knownFlankR2:qc.r2,probabilities:latent.probabilities}:undefined,algorithmVersion:'sanger-'+(s.jointEngine||'signal-path')+'-v4-known-flank',signalModel:'calibrated',requestedMaxIndel:s.maxIndel,discordantPositions:localDiscordant,maxPositionRmse:Math.max(...localResidual),controlCalibration:cc,sampleCalibration:sc,contextEmission:context},mixture:{components:[...bins].map(([size,fraction])=>({size,fraction})),wt:zero,indel:1-zero,inFrame:chosen.filter(c=>c.model.events.length&&net(c.model.events)%3===0).reduce((sum,c)=>sum+c.fraction,0),outOfFrame:chosen.filter(c=>net(c.model.events)%3!==0).reduce((sum,c)=>sum+c.fraction,0),r2:full.r2,rmse:full.rmse,upstreamError:1-sc.validationR2,bases:positions.length,fitStart:anchor,fitEnd:end,converged:fit.converged,passesChecks:allowed&&!attributionAmbiguous.length},pooled:{preflight:report,controlR2:ctlScore.r2,sampleTrim:sr.trim,controlTrim:cr.trim,amplicons:perRef.map(({rows,estimable,...a})=>a)},joint:{groups,targets,reconstructionR2:qc.r2,heldOutR2:qc.r2,selectedModels:chosen.length,candidateModels:models.length,iterations,maxDeletion,ambiguousFraction:ambiguous.reduce((sum,c)=>sum+c.fraction,0),alignmentSearches:alignments}};
}

END MODULE


BEGIN MODULE src/app/workers/core/sanger-path-screen.ts
import type{JunctionInput,JunctionFit,JunctionModel,JunctionRead}from'./sanger-junction-mixture';
