import{jointPathColumn,pathColumn,mapPathModel}from'./sanger-junction-paths';
import{controlKnownContextEmission}from'./sanger-control-response';
import{mixPhase,phaseNeighbor}from'./sanger-signal';
const bases='ACGT';
/** Exact directional scores for canonical two-event columns, using prefix/suffix
 * sums. Every requested size is scanned; full columns are retained only for the
 * working set. Memory grows with shift states, not their Cartesian product. */
export function screenPathCandidates(input:JunctionInput,current:JunctionFit,range:number[],make:(h:number,sizes:number[])=>JunctionModel,limit=8){
 const key=(m:JunctionModel)=>m.reference+':'+m.sizes!.join('/'),known=new Set(current.models.map(key));
 const gradients=input.positions.map((_,p)=>{const v=[0,0,0,0];current.columns.forEach((c,i)=>{if(current.weights[i])for(let b=0;b<4;b++)v[b]+=current.weights[i]*c[4*p+b];});const total=v.reduce((s,v)=>s+v,0),pi=v.map(v=>v/Math.max(1e-12,total)),residual=pi.map((v,b)=>input.observed[4*p+b]-v),mean=residual.reduce((s,v,b)=>s+v*pi[b],0);return residual.map(v=>(input.positionWeights?.[p]??1)*(v-mean)/Math.max(1e-12,total));});
 const reads:JunctionRead[]=input.reads||[{references:input.references,positions:input.positions,rowIds:input.positions,mappings:input.references.map(()=>({reverse:false,offset:0})),lag:input.lag,theta:input.theta}];
 const rowAt=new Map(input.positions.map((p,i)=>[p,i])),best:{m:JunctionModel;gain:number}[]=[];
 const retain=(m:JunctionModel,gain:number)=>{if(best.length<limit||gain>best.at(-1)!.gain){best.push({m,gain});best.sort((a,b)=>b.gain-a.gain);if(best.length>limit)best.pop();}};
 input.references.forEach((ref,h)=>{
  const axes=reads.map(read=>{
   const records=read.positions.flatMap((p,j)=>rowAt.has(read.rowIds[j])?[{p,row:rowAt.get(read.rowIds[j])!}]:[]).sort((a,b)=>a.p-b.p),positions=records.map(v=>v.p),firstCache=new Map<number,Float64Array>(),lastCache=new Map<number,Float64Array>();
   const cuts=read.references[h].cuts||[read.references[h].cut],first=cuts.indexOf(Math.min(...cuts)),last=1-first;
   const prefix=(column:number[])=>{const result=new Float64Array(positions.length+1);for(let i=0;i<positions.length;i++)result[i+1]=result[i]+gradients[records[i].row].reduce((s,g,b)=>s+g*column[4*i+b],0);return result;};
   const firstScore=(size:number)=>{let value=firstCache.get(size);if(!value){const sizes=cuts.map(()=>0);sizes[first]=size;value=prefix(pathColumn(read.references[h],mapPathModel(make(h,sizes),read.mappings[h]),positions,read.lag,read.theta));firstCache.set(size,value);}return value;};
   const lastScore=(size:number)=>{let value=lastCache.get(size);if(!value){const r=read.references[h],one=(p:number)=>{const q=p-size;return r.contextResponse?controlKnownContextEmission(r.sequence[q-1]||'',r.sequence[q]||'',r.sequence[q+1]||'',r.contextResponse,p):Array.from(bases,b=>Number(r.sequence[q]===b));};value=prefix(positions.flatMap(p=>read.lag?mixPhase(one(p),one(phaseNeighbor(p,read.lag)),read.lag):one(p)));lastCache.set(size,value);}return value;};
   const lower=(p:number)=>{let lo=0,hi=positions.length;while(lo<hi){const m=(lo+hi)>>1;if(positions[m]<p)lo=m+1;else hi=m;}return lo;};
   return{read,records,positions,first,last,firstScore,lastScore,lower};
  });
  const scan=(sizes:number[])=>{
   const m=make(h,sizes);if(known.has(key(m))||m.path?.some(e=>!e.origins.length))return;
   if((ref.cuts?.length||1)===1){const column=jointPathColumn(input,m);retain(m,gradients.reduce((s,g,p)=>s+g.reduce((s,v,b)=>s+v*column[4*p+b],0),0));return;}
   const events=m.path||[];if(events.length===2&&events[0].origins[0].start+Math.max(0,-events[0].size)>events[1].origins[0].start)return;
   let gain=0;
   for(const axis of axes){
    const first=axis.firstScore(sizes[axis.first]);if(!sizes[axis.last]){gain+=first.at(-1)!;continue;}
    const mapped=mapPathModel(m,axis.read.mappings[h]),event=mapped.path!.find(e=>e.target===axis.last+1)!,q=event.origins[0].start+sizes[axis.first],lo=axis.lower(q-2),hi=axis.lower(q+Math.max(0,event.size)+3),last=axis.lastScore(m.size);
    gain+=first[lo]+last.at(-1)!-last[hi];
    const column=pathColumn(axis.read.references[h],mapped,axis.positions.slice(lo,hi),axis.read.lag,axis.read.theta);
    for(let j=lo;j<hi;j++)for(let b=0;b<4;b++)gain+=gradients[axis.records[j].row][b]*column[4*(j-lo)+b];
   }
   retain(m,gain);
  };
  if((ref.cuts?.length||1)===1)range.forEach(a=>scan([a]));else for(const a of range)for(const b of range)scan([a,b]);
 });return best;
}

END MODULE


BEGIN MODULE src/app/workers/core/sanger-path.ts
import {guardCompetingTargetPaths} from './sanger-path-agreement';
import {guardEquivalentJunctions} from './sanger-path-identifiability';
import {analyzeSignalPaths as known} from './sanger-path-known';
import {analyzeSignalPaths as latent} from './sanger-path-latent';
import {SignalPathInput} from './sanger-path-common';
import {SangerAnalysisResult} from '../../models/sanger-analysis';
export {discoverSignalPath,assertSignalTraceGrid,checkSignalPathSeparation,eventsRespectIndelOrigin} from './sanger-path-common';
export type {PathEdit,DiscoveredPath,SignalPathInput} from './sanger-path-common';
/** First certify known-sequence paths. If that fails, try a bounded insertion
 * nuisance model fitted on known flanks. Neither attempt can relax its QC.
 * These reconstruction checks are not independent validation. */
export function analyzeSignalPaths(input:SignalPathInput,progress:(s:string)=>void=()=>{},audit?:(state:any)=>void):SangerAnalysisResult{
 const primary=known(input,progress,audit);
 if(primary.mixture?.passesChecks)return guardEquivalentJunctions(primary,input.amplicons,input.settings.editStartRadius);
 progress('Checking unresolved insertion scaffolds against known flanks');
 try{
  const alternative=latent(input,progress,audit);
  if(alternative.mixture?.passesChecks){alternative.warnings.push('Known-flank fallback used after known-sequence inference was not certified.');return guardEquivalentJunctions(guardCompetingTargetPaths(primary,alternative,input.amplicons),input.amplicons,input.settings.editStartRadius);}
  // Preserve the first explanation when neither strategy is certified.
  primary.warnings.push('Known-flank fallback also failed certification; estimates remain unresolved.');
 }catch(error){primary.warnings.push('Known-flank fallback did not complete: '+(error instanceof Error?error.message:String(error)));}
 return guardEquivalentJunctions(primary,input.amplicons,input.settings.editStartRadius);
}

END MODULE


BEGIN MODULE src/app/workers/core/sanger-pooled.ts
import {solveJunctions,JunctionSolution} from './sanger-junction-solution';
import {isWildType,pathKey} from './sanger-junction-paths';
import {fitControlTransfer} from './sanger-control-transfer';
import {refineJunctionSupport,rejectNonpredictiveJunctions} from './sanger-junction-support';
import {fitControlResponse,controlContextEmission,ControlResponseProfile} from './sanger-control-response';
import {junctionPredictionEvidence} from './sanger-junction-evidence';
import {fitTransitionResponse,transitionEmission,TransitionResponse} from './sanger-transition-response';
import {fitJunctionMixture,junctionBase,junctionAnnotationComponents,JunctionModel,JunctionFit,aliasedDeletionMass} from './sanger-junction-mixture';
import {emissionGroupSupport,sequenceYield,emissionVector,fitEmissionMixture} from './sanger-emission';
import {integratedPeaks,signalWeights,simplexFit,calibrateSignal,correctGain,phaseVector,SignalCalibration,PeakSignal,gramCondition,rawSignalScore} from './sanger-signal';
import { PooledAmplicon, PooledPreflight, SangerAnalysisResult, SangerAnalysisSettings, SangerTrace, TRACE_BASES } from '../../models/sanger-analysis';
import { reverseComplement } from '../../utils/biology.utils';
import { exactBindingSites } from './sanger-primer-designer';
import { fitTraceMixture, normalizedPeaks, plotTrace } from './sanger-analysis';
interface Prepared {contextResponse?:ControlResponseProfile;name:string;sequence:string;cut:number;cuts?:number[];transitionResponse?:number[][];baseYields?:number[]}
interface Model {reference:number;size:number;sizes?:number[];path?:JunctionModel['path'];start?:number;inserted?:string;sequence:string;values:number[]}
interface ModelSet {refs:Prepared[];report:PooledPreflight;models:Model[]}
const failed=(reason:string):PooledPreflight=>({passed:false,reason,positions:[],modelCount:0,rank:0,condition:null,minimumDifferences:0,minimumCutOffset:0,amplicons:[]});
/** Eigenvalues of the normalized symmetric Gram matrix (cyclic Jacobi).
 * Geometry gate only; thresholds are prototype design policies, not validated limits. */
export {gramCondition} from './sanger-signal';

function prepare(amplicons:PooledAmplicon[],primer:string,s:SangerAnalysisSettings,measuredCoverage=false):ModelSet {
  const report=failed('');
  if(s.signalModel!==undefined&&!['classic','calibrated'].includes(s.signalModel))throw new Error('Invalid signal processing mode.');
  if(s.checkWiderRange!==undefined&&typeof s.checkWiderRange!=='boolean')throw new Error('Invalid wider-range check setting.');
  if(amplicons.length<1||amplicons.length>4)throw new Error('Use 2–4 submitted amplicons for mixed-amplicon analysis.');
  if(new Set(amplicons.map(r=>r.name)).size!==amplicons.length||amplicons.some(r=>!r.name.trim()))throw new Error('Amplicon names must be unique and non-empty.');
  if(!/^[ACGT]{12,40}$/.test(primer))throw new Error('Enter the one sequencing primer actually submitted to Sanger (12–40 nt, 5′→3′).');
  if(!Number.isInteger(s.editStartRadius??5)||(s.editStartRadius??5)<0||(s.editStartRadius??5)>100)throw new Error('Indel origin radius must be 0–100 bp.');
  if(!Number.isInteger(s.window)||s.window<60||s.window>500||!Number.isInteger(s.maxIndel)||s.maxIndel<1||s.maxIndel>(s.analysisPolicy==='automatic'||amplicons.length===1?30:15)||!Number.isInteger(s.readStart)||!Number.isInteger(s.readEnd)||s.readStart<0||s.readEnd>(s.analysisPolicy==='automatic'?3160:1000)||s.readEnd<=s.readStart)throw new Error('Use a 60–500 bp target window, indel range ±1–15 bp and a usable read range within 0–1,000 bp.');
  const maxDeletion=s.analysisPolicy==='automatic'?s.maxDeletion!:s.maxIndel,maxInsertion=s.analysisPolicy==='automatic'?s.maxInsertion!:s.maxIndel;
  if(!Number.isInteger(maxDeletion)||maxDeletion<1||maxDeletion>500||!Number.isInteger(maxInsertion)||maxInsertion<1||maxInsertion>30)throw Error('Invalid deletion/insertion limits.');
  const refs=amplicons.map(r=>{
    if(!/^[ACGT]+$/.test(r.sequence)||r.sequence.length>20000||!Number.isInteger(r.cut)||r.cut<1||r.cut>=r.sequence.length)throw new Error(`${r.name}: use the full submitted amplicon (A/C/G/T, ≤20 kb) and a cut inside it.`);
    const hits=exactBindingSites(primer,{name:r.name,sequence:r.sequence,cuts:r.cuts||[r.cut]});
    if(hits.length!==1)throw new Error(`${r.name}: the submitted sequencing primer must have one exact binding site across both strands.`);
    const hit=hits[0],oriented=hit.direction==='forward'?r.sequence:reverseComplement(r.sequence),end=hit.direction==='forward'?hit.end:r.sequence.length-hit.start;
    const cuts=(r.cuts||[r.cut]).map(c=>(hit.direction==='forward'?c:r.sequence.length-c)-end),cut=Math.min(...cuts),sequence=oriented.slice(end),lo=cut-Math.floor(s.window/2),hi=Math.max(...cuts)+Math.ceil(s.window/2);
    if(cuts.length>2||cuts.some(c=>!Number.isInteger(c)||c<0||c>=sequence.length)||new Set(cuts).size!==cuts.length)throw Error(r.name+': enter one or two distinct target positions.');
    if(cut<s.readStart+72||(s.analysisPolicy!=='automatic'&&Math.max(...cuts)+30>Math.min(s.readEnd,sequence.length)))throw new Error(`${r.name}: the usable trace must cover the target, at least 60 upstream bases and 30 downstream bases.`);
    report.amplicons.push({name:r.name,direction:hit.direction,primerStart:hit.start+1,primerEnd:hit.end,cutOffset:cut,windowStart:lo,windowEnd:hi,targets:cuts.map(c=>({cutOffset:c,windowStart:c-Math.floor(s.window/2),windowEnd:c+Math.ceil(s.window/2)}))});
    return {name:r.name,sequence,cut,cuts};
  });
  const start=s.readStart,end=measuredCoverage?s.readEnd:Math.min(s.readEnd,...refs.map(r=>r.sequence.length));
  // Fixed measured read axis. Calibrated fits retain all junction observations;
  // the configured indel range changes hypotheses, never the observed cycles.
  const positions=Array.from({length:end-start},(_,j)=>start+j);
  const models:Model[]=[];
  refs.forEach((ref,h)=>{for(let size=-maxDeletion;size<=maxInsertion;size++){
    if(s.analysisPolicy==='automatic'&&size!==0)continue;
    const sequence=positions.map(p=>ref.sequence[p<ref.cut?p:p-size]||'N').join('');
    models.push({reference:h,size,sequence,values:Array.from(sequence).flatMap(base=>TRACE_BASES.map(b=>Number(base===b)))});
  }});
  report.positions=positions;report.modelCount=s.analysisPolicy==='automatic'?refs.length*(maxDeletion+maxInsertion+1):models.length;
  if(positions.length<(s.analysisPolicy==='automatic'?60:Math.max(80,models.length)))throw new Error(s.analysisPolicy==='automatic'?'Too few reference-covered sample cycles. Review trace/reference coverage.':'Too few usable positions for this number of amplicon × indel models. Increase the window or reduce the indel range.');
  const checked=s.analysisPolicy==='automatic'?models.filter(m=>m.size===0):models;
  let minimumDifferences=Infinity;
  for(let a=0;a<checked.length;a++)for(let b=0;b<a;b++){
    let diff=0;for(let j=0;j<positions.length;j++)if(checked[a].sequence[j]!==checked[b].sequence[j])diff++;
    minimumDifferences=Math.min(minimumDifferences,diff);
  }
  report.minimumDifferences=minimumDifferences;
  report.minimumCutOffset=Math.min(...refs.flatMap((r,i)=>refs.slice(i+1).map(t=>Math.abs(r.cut-t.cut))));
  if(minimumDifferences<6){report.reason=s.analysisPolicy==='automatic'?'Submitted WT references have few distinguishing positions. Reference attribution may be unstable.':'At least two amplicon/indel models are identical or too similar on the sequencing-primer axis. Change the window, reduce the indel range or redesign the primer/start positions.';return {refs,report,models};}
  let geometryColumns=checked.map(m=>m.values),requiredRank=checked.length;
  if(s.analysisPolicy==='automatic'&&checked.length>1){
   const pivot=checked.length-1;
   geometryColumns=checked.slice(0,-1).map((m,h)=>positions.flatMap((_,row)=>{
    const v=Array.from({length:4},(_,b)=>checked.reduce((sum,c)=>sum+c.values[4*row+b]/checked.length,0)),total=v.reduce((sum,x)=>sum+x,0),a=m.values.slice(4*row,4*row+4),z=checked[pivot].values.slice(4*row,4*row+4),at=a.reduce((sum,x)=>sum+x,0),zt=z.reduce((sum,x)=>sum+x,0);
    return a.map((x,b)=>total?(x-z[b]-(v[b]/total)*(at-zt))/total:0);
   }));requiredRank=checked.length-1;
  }
  const geometry=gramCondition(geometryColumns);report.rank=geometry.rank+(s.analysisPolicy==='automatic'&&checked.length>1?1:0);report.condition=geometry.condition;
  if(geometry.rank<requiredRank||geometry.condition===null||geometry.condition>100){report.reason='The combined amplicon/indel model is rank-deficient or poorly conditioned after fluorescence normalization. This trace cannot be separated reliably under the current references/window/indel range.';return {refs,report,models};}
  report.passed=true;report.reason='Reference models are distinguishable under the current settings. Experimental trace fit must also pass.';
  return {refs,report,models};
}
export function checkPooledSeparation(amplicons:PooledAmplicon[],primer:string,s:SangerAnalysisSettings):PooledPreflight {
  try{return prepare(amplicons,primer,s).report;}catch(e){return failed(e instanceof Error?e.message:String(e));}
}
export class PooledSeparationError extends Error {constructor(public report:PooledPreflight){super(report.reason);}}
/** Register the first called base to the shared primer extension axis. */
export function registerTrace(trace:SangerTrace,refs:Prepared[],s:SangerAnalysisSettings,signals?:number[][],allowQCWarnings=false):{trim:number;r2:number;warnings?:string[]} {
  const observed=signals||normalizedPeaks(trace),firstCut=Math.min(...refs.map(r=>r.cut));const options:{trim:number;r2:number}[]=[];
  for(let trim=0;trim<=160;trim++){
    const lo=Math.max(s.readStart,trim+10),hi=Math.min(firstCut-12,trim+trace.sequence.length-2);
    if(hi-lo<60)continue;
    const positions=Array.from({length:hi-lo},(_,j)=>lo+j).filter(p=>observed[p-trim]?.some(v=>v>0));if(positions.length<60)continue;
    // Identical upstream WT templates can be merged for registration only.
    const sequences=[...new Set(refs.map(r=>positions.map(p=>r.sequence[p]).join('')))];
    const columns=sequences.map(seq=>Array.from(seq).flatMap(base=>TRACE_BASES.map(b=>Number(base===b))));
    const fit=simplexFit(columns,positions.flatMap(p=>observed[p-trim]));options.push({trim,r2:fit.r2});
  }
  options.sort((a,b)=>b.r2-a.r2);const best=options[0];
  if(!best)throw new Error(`${trace.name}: insufficient upstream coverage for registration.`);
  const warnings:string[]=[];
  if(best.r2<(signals ? .55 : .8)&&!allowQCWarnings)throw new Error(`${trace.name}: the upstream trace cannot be registered to the submitted primer/amplicons (fit R² <0.80 or insufficient coverage).`);
  if(options[1]&&best.r2-options[1].r2<.02&&!allowQCWarnings)throw new Error(`${trace.name}: multiple sequencing start positions explain the upstream trace. Trim/primer registration is ambiguous.`);
  if(best.r2<(signals ? .55 : .8))warnings.push(`${trace.name}: weak upstream registration; the best fitted start is shown.`);
  if(options[1]&&best.r2-options[1].r2<.02)warnings.push(`${trace.name}: sequencing start is ambiguous; the best fitted start is shown.`);
  return {...best,warnings};
}
export interface PooledAnalysisInput {amplicons:PooledAmplicon[];primer:string;ampliconConfirmed:boolean;sample:SangerTrace;control:SangerTrace;settings:SangerAnalysisSettings;synthetic?:boolean}
export function preparePooledSanger(input:PooledAnalysisInput,progress:(stage:string)=>void=()=>{}) {
  if(!input.ampliconConfirmed)throw new PooledSeparationError(failed('Confirm that every reference is the actual full amplicon submitted to Sanger. Genomic/gene reference sequences are not valid here.'));
  progress('Checking amplicon/indel separability');let built:ModelSet;
  try{built=prepare(input.amplicons,input.primer,input.settings);}catch(e){throw new PooledSeparationError(failed(e instanceof Error?e.message:String(e)));}
  let {refs,report,models}=built;const analysisWarnings:string[]=[];
  if(!input.control||!input.sample)throw new Error('Mixed-amplicon analysis requires one unedited pooled control and one pooled sample, using the same sequencing primer.');
  progress('Registering pooled traces to the sequencing primer');
  const enhanced=input.settings.signalModel!=='classic';
  let controlSignal=enhanced?integratedPeaks(input.control,[0,0,0,0]):null,sampleSignal=enhanced?integratedPeaks(input.sample,[0,0,0,0]):null;
  const controlRegistration=registerTrace(input.control,refs,input.settings,controlSignal?.vectors,true),sampleRegistration=registerTrace(input.sample,refs,input.settings,sampleSignal?.vectors,true);
  analysisWarnings.push(...(controlRegistration.warnings||[]),...(sampleRegistration.warnings||[]));
  const firstCut=Math.min(...refs.map(r=>r.cut));
  if(enhanced){controlSignal=integratedPeaks(input.control,[0,0,0,0]);sampleSignal=integratedPeaks(input.sample,[0,0,0,0]);}
  const controlRaw=controlSignal?.vectors||normalizedPeaks(input.control),sampleRaw=sampleSignal?.vectors||normalizedPeaks(input.sample);
  const margin=input.settings.analysisPolicy==='automatic'?0:10,tail=input.settings.analysisPolicy==='automatic'?0:2;
  const actualStart=input.settings.analysisPolicy==='automatic'?Math.max(input.settings.readStart,sampleRegistration.trim):Math.max(input.settings.readStart,controlRegistration.trim+margin,sampleRegistration.trim+margin),actualEnd=input.settings.analysisPolicy==='automatic'?Math.min(input.settings.readEnd,sampleRegistration.trim+sampleRaw.length):Math.min(input.settings.readEnd,controlRegistration.trim+controlRaw.length-tail,sampleRegistration.trim+sampleRaw.length-tail);
  if(input.settings.analysisPolicy==='automatic'||actualStart>input.settings.readStart||report.positions.at(-1)!>=actualEnd){const covered=prepare(input.amplicons,input.primer,{...input.settings,readStart:actualStart,readEnd:actualEnd},input.settings.analysisPolicy==='automatic');({refs,report,models}=covered);}
  const coverageWarnings=refs.flatMap((r,h)=>(r.cuts||[r.cut]).flatMap((cut,t)=>cut+30>actualEnd?[`${r.name} target ${t+1}: this read has fewer than 30 measured downstream bases; estimates use the available signal.`]:[]));
  if(!report.passed)analysisWarnings.push(`Reference QC warning: ${report.reason} Fitted reference attribution may not be unique.`);

  let response:TransitionResponse|undefined,baseYields:number[]|undefined,rawResponse:ReturnType<typeof fitControlResponse>|undefined;
  const calibrate=(raw:number[][],trim:number,theta?:number[],prior?:number[])=>{
    const observed=Array.from({length:raw.length+trim},(_,p)=>raw[p-trim]||[0,0,0,0]);
    const positions=Array.from({length:firstCut-12},(_,p)=>p).filter(p=>p>=Math.max(input.settings.readStart,trim+10)&&p-trim<raw.length-2);
    const templates=refs.map(r=>Array.from(r.sequence,(base,p)=>{if(rawResponse){const value=controlContextEmission(r.sequence,p,0,rawResponse),total=refs.reduce((sum,ref,h)=>sum+(prior?.[h]??1/refs.length)*controlContextEmission(ref.sequence,p,0,rawResponse!).reduce((s,v)=>s+v,0),0);return value.map(v=>v/Math.max(1e-9,total));}if(response?.used){const value=transitionEmission(r.sequence,p,0,theta||Array(8).fill(0),response.table,baseYields||[1,1,1,1]),total=refs.reduce((sum,ref,h)=>sum+(prior?.[h]??1/refs.length)*transitionEmission(ref.sequence,p,0,theta||Array(8).fill(0),response!.table,baseYields||[1,1,1,1]).reduce((s,v)=>s+v,0),0);return value.map(v=>v/Math.max(1e-9,total));}const yieldValue=theta?sequenceYield(r.sequence,p,theta):1,total=theta?refs.reduce((s,ref,h)=>s+(prior?.[h]||0)*sequenceYield(ref.sequence,p,theta),0):1;return [...TRACE_BASES].map(b=>Number(base===b)*yieldValue/Math.max(1e-9,total));}));
    return calibrateSignal(observed,positions,templates,'fine');
  };
  let controlCalibration:SignalCalibration|undefined,sampleCalibration:SignalCalibration|undefined,transfer:ReturnType<typeof fitControlTransfer>|undefined;
  if(enhanced){progress('Calibrating trace gains and phase using held-out upstream WT signal');controlCalibration=calibrate(controlRaw,controlRegistration.trim);sampleCalibration=calibrate(sampleRaw,sampleRegistration.trim);
    if(controlCalibration.validationR2<.8||sampleCalibration.validationR2<.8)analysisWarnings.push('Upstream held-out calibration R² is below0.80; fitted estimates may be unreliable.');}
  let wt=controlRaw.map(v=>controlCalibration?correctGain(v,controlCalibration.gains):v),sample=sampleRaw.map(v=>sampleCalibration?correctGain(v,sampleCalibration.gains):v);
  const emissionRows=Array.from({length:controlRaw.length+controlRegistration.trim},(_,p)=>p).filter(p=>p>=Math.max(input.settings.readStart,controlRegistration.trim+10));
  const theta=Array(8).fill(0),fitter=enhanced?fitEmissionMixture:simplexFit;
  if(!enhanced){
    const rawControl=Array.from({length:controlRaw.length+controlRegistration.trim},(_,p)=>controlRaw[p-controlRegistration.trim]||[0,0,0,0]);
    response=fitTransitionResponse(rawControl,refs.map(r=>r.sequence),emissionRows,0);
    if(response.used){baseYields=Array(16).fill(1);refs=refs.map(ref=>({...ref,transitionResponse:response!.table,baseYields}));}
  }
  if(enhanced){
   const areas=Array.from({length:controlRaw.length+controlRegistration.trim},(_,p)=>controlSignal!.areas?.[p-controlRegistration.trim]||[0,0,0,0]);
   rawResponse=fitControlResponse(refs.map(r=>r.sequence),emissionRows,areas,controlCalibration!.weights);response=undefined;refs=refs.map(r=>({...r,transitionResponse:undefined,contextResponse:rawResponse!}));
   let cp=rawResponse.shares,sp=sampleCalibration!.weights;for(let iteration=0;iteration<3;iteration++){controlCalibration=calibrate(controlRaw,controlRegistration.trim,theta,cp);sampleCalibration=calibrate(sampleRaw,sampleRegistration.trim,theta,sp);cp=controlCalibration.weights;sp=sampleCalibration.weights;}
   const sampleAreas=Array.from({length:sampleRaw.length+sampleRegistration.trim},(_,p)=>sampleSignal!.areas![p-sampleRegistration.trim]||[0,0,0,0]),training=Array.from({length:firstCut-12},(_,p)=>p).filter(p=>p>=Math.max(input.settings.readStart,sampleRegistration.trim+10)&&p-sampleRegistration.trim<sampleRaw.length-2);
   transfer=fitControlTransfer(refs.map(r=>r.sequence),training,sampleAreas,rawResponse,sampleCalibration!.lag,sampleCalibration!.gains,sp);
   if(transfer.used)sampleCalibration={...sampleCalibration!,gains:transfer.gains,weights:transfer.shares,used:true,validationR2:transfer.colourR2,trainingBases:transfer.trainingBases,validationBases:transfer.validationBases};
   wt=controlRaw.map(v=>correctGain(v,controlCalibration!.gains));sample=sampleRaw.map(v=>correctGain(v,sampleCalibration!.gains));
  }
  const positions=report.positions;
  if(positions.some(p=>p-sampleRegistration.trim<0||p-sampleRegistration.trim>=sample.length||!sample[p-sampleRegistration.trim].some(v=>v>0)||(input.settings.analysisPolicy!=='automatic'&&(p-controlRegistration.trim<0||p-controlRegistration.trim>=wt.length||!wt[p-controlRegistration.trim].some(v=>v>0)))))throw new Error(input.settings.analysisPolicy==='automatic'?'Some reference-axis sample cycles have no measured signal. Review trace/reference coverage.':'The actual traces do not cover every checked target-window position. Shorten the window or resequence.');
  const columnsFor=(items:Model[],rows:number[],lag:number)=>items.map(m=>{const ref=refs[m.reference];return rows.flatMap(p=>{if(rawResponse&&m.start===undefined){const start=ref.cut,sequence=ref.sequence.slice(0,start)+(m.size>0?'N'.repeat(m.size):'')+ref.sequence.slice(start+Math.max(0,-m.size));return controlContextEmission(sequence,p,lag,rawResponse);}if(m.start!==undefined)return m.values.slice(4*positions.indexOf(p),4*positions.indexOf(p)+4);if(response?.used){const start=ref.cut,seq=ref.sequence.slice(0,start)+(m.size>0?'N'.repeat(m.size):'')+ref.sequence.slice(start+Math.max(0,-m.size));return transitionEmission(seq,p,lag,theta,response.table,baseYields!);}if(m.start===undefined){const q=p<ref.cut?p:p-m.size;return emissionVector(ref.sequence,q,lag,theta);}const event={reference:m.reference,size:m.size,start:m.start,inserted:m.inserted||''};const local=Array.from({length:4},(_,i)=>junctionBase(ref.sequence,event,p-2+i)).join('');return emissionVector(local,2,lag,theta);});});
  const rawScore=(columns:number[][],rows:number[],weights:number[],raw:number[][],trim:number,gains:number[])=>{
    return rawSignalScore(columns,rows.flatMap(p=>raw[p-trim]),weights,gains);
  };
  const reliability=enhanced?positions.map(p=>wt[p-controlRegistration.trim]?.some(v=>v>0)?signalWeights([controlSignal!,sampleSignal!],[[p-controlRegistration.trim],[p-sampleRegistration.trim]])[0]:signalWeights([sampleSignal!],[[p-sampleRegistration.trim]])[0]):undefined;
  const controlPositions=positions.filter(p=>wt[p-controlRegistration.trim]?.some(v=>v>0)),controlReliability=controlPositions.map(p=>reliability?.[positions.indexOf(p)]??1);
  progress('Checking unedited pooled-control fit');
  const controlColumns=enhanced?columnsFor(models.filter(m=>m.size===0),controlPositions,controlCalibration!.lag):models.filter(m=>m.size===0).map(m=>m.values);
  let controlFit=enhanced?fitter(controlColumns,controlPositions.flatMap(p=>wt[p-controlRegistration.trim]),controlReliability):simplexFit(controlColumns,positions.flatMap(p=>wt[p-controlRegistration.trim]));
  if(enhanced)controlFit={...controlFit,...rawScore(controlColumns,controlPositions,controlFit.weights,controlRaw,controlRegistration.trim,controlCalibration!.gains)};
  if(controlFit.r2<.85)analysisWarnings.push('The pooled control is poorly explained by the submitted amplicons (R² <0.85). Check primer, products, control and trace quality.');
  progress('Fitting calibrated, nonnegative amplicon/indel signals');
  const observed=positions.flatMap(p=>sample[p-sampleRegistration.trim]);
  const fitInput={references:refs,positions,observed,maxDeletion:input.settings.analysisPolicy==='automatic'?input.settings.maxDeletion!:input.settings.maxIndel,maxInsertion:input.settings.analysisPolicy==='automatic'?input.settings.maxInsertion!:input.settings.maxIndel,radius:input.settings.editStartRadius??5,lag:sampleCalibration?.lag||0,theta,positionWeights:reliability,fit:fitter};
  const templateError=(junction:JunctionFit)=>{
    const controlAt=new Map(controlPositions.map((p,i)=>[p,i])),templateScale=Math.max(1,...refs.map((_,h)=>junction.models.reduce((sum,m,i)=>sum+(m.reference===h?junction.weights[i]:0),0)/Math.max(1e-8,controlFit.weights[h])));
    return enhanced?positions.flatMap(p=>{
      const at=controlAt.get(p);if(at===undefined)return [0,0,0,0];
      const value=Array.from({length:4},(_,b)=>controlColumns.reduce((sum,c,h)=>sum+controlFit.weights[h]*c[4*at+b],0)),total=value.reduce((sum,v)=>sum+v,0);
      return value.map((v,b)=>templateScale*(wt[p-controlRegistration.trim][b]-(total?v/total:0)));
    }):undefined;
  };
  const finish=(solution:JunctionSolution):SangerAnalysisResult=>{
  const {junction,unprunedWeights,support,referenceEvidence,predictive,predictionRemoved}=solution;
  models=junction.models.map((m,i)=>({...m,sequence:'',values:junction.columns[i]}));
  const columns=junction.columns;
  let fit:{weights:number[];r2:number;rmse:number;converged:boolean}=junction;
  if(enhanced)fit={...fit,...rawScore(columns,positions,fit.weights,sampleRaw,sampleRegistration.trim,sampleCalibration!.gains)};
  const residuals=enhanced?rawScore(columns,positions,fit.weights,sampleRaw,sampleRegistration.trim,sampleCalibration!.gains).positionRmse:[],discordantPositions=residuals.filter(v=>v>.20).length,maxPositionRmse=residuals.length?Math.max(...residuals):undefined;
  let referenceStability:{jointTVpp:number;maximumConditionalTVpp:number;maximumShareDifferencePp:number}|undefined;
  let splitStabilityPp:number|undefined,rangeSuggestion:{maxIndel:number;validationR2Gain:number;outsideFraction:number}|undefined;
  if(enhanced){
    const stabilityInput=solution.stabilityInput,stabilityColumns=solution.stabilityColumns;
    const halves=[0,1].map(parity=>{const ids=stabilityInput.positions.flatMap((_,i)=>i%2===parity?[i]:[]);return fitter(stabilityColumns.map(c=>ids.flatMap(i=>c.slice(i*4,i*4+4))),ids.flatMap(i=>stabilityInput.observed.slice(i*4,i*4+4)),ids.map(i=>stabilityInput.positionWeights?.[i]??1)).weights;});
    splitStabilityPp=Math.max(...refs.map((_,h)=>{const edit=halves.map(w=>{const share=models.reduce((sum,m,i)=>sum+(m.reference===h?w[i]:0),0),zero=models.reduce((sum,m,i)=>sum+(m.reference===h&&isWildType(m)?w[i]:0),0);return share>=.02?1-zero/share:null;});return edit.some(v=>v===null)?0:100*Math.abs(edit[0]!-edit[1]!);}));
    const share=(weights:number[],h:number)=>models.reduce((sum,m,i)=>sum+(m.reference===h?weights[i]:0),0);
    const jointTVpp=50*halves[0].reduce((sum,w,i)=>sum+Math.abs(w-halves[1][i]),0);
    const maximumShareDifferencePp=Math.max(...refs.map((_,h)=>100*Math.abs(share(halves[0],h)-share(halves[1],h))));
    const maximumConditionalTVpp=Math.max(...refs.map((_,h)=>{const a=share(halves[0],h),b=share(halves[1],h);if(Math.max(a,b)<.02)return 0;if(Math.min(a,b)<.02)return 100;return 50*models.reduce((sum,m,i)=>sum+(m.reference===h?Math.abs(halves[0][i]/a-halves[1][i]/b):0),0);}));
    referenceStability={jointTVpp,maximumConditionalTVpp,maximumShareDifferencePp};
    splitStabilityPp=Math.max(splitStabilityPp,jointTVpp,maximumConditionalTVpp,maximumShareDifferencePp);
    if(input.settings.analysisPolicy!=='automatic'&&input.settings.checkWiderRange!==false&&input.settings.maxIndel<15){
      const expansions:ModelSet[]=[];
      for(let range=Math.min(15,input.settings.maxIndel+5);range<=15;range=Math.min(15,range+5)){
        try{const expanded=prepare(input.amplicons,input.primer,{...input.settings,maxIndel:range});if(expanded.report.passed)expansions.push(expanded);}catch{}
        if(range===15)break;
      }
      if(expansions.length){const common=positions.filter(p=>expansions.every(e=>e.report.positions.includes(p))),train=common.filter((_,i)=>i%4!==0),valid=common.filter((_,i)=>i%4===0);
        if(train.length>=Math.max(...expansions.map(e=>e.models.length))&&valid.length>=30){
          const y=train.flatMap(p=>sample[p-sampleRegistration.trim]),small=fitter(columnsFor(models,train,sampleCalibration!.lag),y),baseScore=rawScore(columnsFor(models,valid,sampleCalibration!.lag),valid,small.weights,sampleRaw,sampleRegistration.trim,sampleCalibration!.gains),accepted:{maxIndel:number;validationR2Gain:number;outsideFraction:number;rmse:number}[]=[];
          for(const expanded of expansions){const fitted=fitter(columnsFor(expanded.models,train,sampleCalibration!.lag),y),score=rawScore(columnsFor(expanded.models,valid,sampleCalibration!.lag),valid,fitted.weights,sampleRaw,sampleRegistration.trim,sampleCalibration!.gains),outside=expanded.models.reduce((sum,m,i)=>sum+(Math.abs(m.size)>input.settings.maxIndel?fitted.weights[i]:0),0);
            if(score.r2-baseScore.r2>=.01&&score.rmse<baseScore.rmse*.85&&outside>=.03)accepted.push({maxIndel:Math.max(...expanded.models.map(m=>m.size)),validationR2Gain:score.r2-baseScore.r2,outsideFraction:outside,rmse:score.rmse});
          }
          if(accepted.length){const best=Math.min(...accepted.map(a=>a.rmse)),pick=accepted.filter(a=>a.rmse<=best*1.02).sort((a,b)=>a.maxIndel-b.maxIndel)[0];rangeSuggestion={maxIndel:pick.maxIndel,validationR2Gain:pick.validationR2Gain,outsideFraction:pick.outsideFraction};}
        }
      }
    }
  }

  const amplicons=refs.map((ref,h)=>{
    const components=models.map((m,i)=>({...m,fraction:fit.weights[i]})).filter(m=>m.reference===h),share=components.reduce((a,b)=>a+b.fraction,0);
    const zero=components.filter(isWildType).reduce((s,c)=>s+c.fraction,0),inside=components.filter(c=>!isWildType(c)&&c.size%3===0).reduce((a,b)=>a+b.fraction,0),outside=components.filter(c=>c.size%3!==0).reduce((a,b)=>a+b.fraction,0);
    return {name:ref.name,share,wt:share>0?zero/share:null,indel:share>0?1-zero/share:null,inFrame:share>0?inside/share:null,outOfFrame:share>0?outside/share:null,components:[...new Set(components.map(c=>c.size))].map(size=>({size,fraction:share>0?components.filter(c=>c.size===size).reduce((s,c)=>s+c.fraction,0)/share:0}))};
  });
  const sizes=[...new Set(models.map(m=>m.size))].sort((a,b)=>a-b),components=sizes.map(size=>({size,fraction:models.reduce((sum,m,i)=>sum+(m.size===size?fit.weights[i]:0),0)}));
  const zero=models.reduce((sum,m,i)=>sum+(isWildType(m)?fit.weights[i]:0),0),inFrame=models.reduce((sum,m,i)=>sum+(!isWildType(m)&&m.size%3===0?fit.weights[i]:0),0),outOfFrame=components.filter(c=>c.size%3!==0).reduce((a,b)=>a+b.fraction,0);
  const warnings:string[]=[...analysisWarnings,...coverageWarnings];if(discordantPositions)warnings.push(`${discordantPositions} local positions have high unexplained signal. Check for trace artifacts or variants outside the modeled classes.`);if(splitStabilityPp!==undefined&&splitStabilityPp>10)warnings.push('Reference shares or within-reference indel spectra differ by more than 10 pp between alternating-position fits. Review component stability.');if(rangeSuggestion)warnings.push(`A wider ±${rangeSuggestion.maxIndel} bp model improves held-out signal fit. Reanalyse with that range before interpreting the indel spectrum.`);if(fit.r2<.9)warnings.push('Pooled sample R² is below 0.90; component estimates are exploratory.');if(!fit.converged)warnings.push('Pooled trace regression did not converge.');
  let automaticGeometryPass=true;
  if(input.settings.analysisPolicy==='automatic'&&aliasedDeletionMass(refs,junction.models,fit.weights,positions,fitInput.maxDeletion,fitInput.radius)>.01){automaticGeometryPass=false;warnings.push('Some fitted deletions have indistinguishable alternative lengths over the measured DNA span. Estimates are shown; attribution may not be unique.');}
  if(input.settings.analysisPolicy==='automatic'){const active=solution.stabilityColumns.filter((_,i)=>Math.max(fit.weights[i],unprunedWeights[i])>.01),g=gramCondition(active);if(g.rank<active.length||g.condition===null||g.condition>100){automaticGeometryPass=false;warnings.push('Fitted components are poorly conditioned; review the reported attribution.');}const aliased=solution.stabilityColumns.some((a,i)=>Math.max(fit.weights[i],unprunedWeights[i])>.01&&solution.stabilityColumns.some((b,j)=>i!==j&&a.every((v,k)=>Math.abs(v-b[k])<1e-8)));if(aliased){automaticGeometryPass=false;warnings.push('At least one fitted component has an indistinguishable alternative. Attribution may not be unique.');}}
  if(amplicons.some(r=>r.share<.02))warnings.push('At least one amplicon contributes less than 2% of fitted signal; its within-reference percentages may be unstable.');
  if(controlFit.weights.some(w=>w<.02))warnings.push('At least one submitted amplicon has less than 2% fitted signal in the control.');
  const plots=[{trace:input.control,trim:controlRegistration.trim},{trace:input.sample,trim:sampleRegistration.trim}].map(({trace,trim})=>{
    const plot=plotTrace(trace,firstCut-trim,Math.min(120,Math.max(...refs.map(r=>r.cut))-firstCut+input.settings.window/2));
    plot.cuts=refs.map(r=>({name:r.name,x:plot.cutX+(r.cut-firstCut)*13})).filter(c=>c.x<=plot.width);return plot;
  });
  const aliases=solution.aliases.map(a=>({reference:refs[a.reference].name,target:a.target,possibleReallocatedFraction:amplicons[a.reference].share?a.shiftableJointFraction/amplicons[a.reference].share:0}));
  if(aliases.some(a=>a.possibleReallocatedFraction>=.01)){automaticGeometryPass=false;warnings.push('Different physical edit mixtures give the same measured signal for '+aliases.map(a=>a.reference+' T'+a.target).join(', ')+'. The best numerical estimates are shown; target attribution is not unique.');}
  const checksPass=(solution.coveragePass??!coverageWarnings.length)&&automaticGeometryPass&&report.passed&&!analysisWarnings.length&&(sampleCalibration?.validationR2??1)>=.8&&(controlCalibration?.validationR2??1)>=.8&&controlFit.r2>=.85&&fit.r2>=.9&&fit.converged&&controlFit.converged&&(splitStabilityPp??0)<=10&&!rangeSuggestion&&!discordantPositions;
  const multi=refs.some(r=>(r.cuts?.length||1)>1);
  const pathComponents=(h:number)=>junctionAnnotationComponents(junction.models.filter(m=>m.reference===h),fit.weights.filter((_,i)=>junction.models[i].reference===h),(report.amplicons[h].direction==='forward'?input.amplicons[h].cut:input.amplicons[h].sequence.length-input.amplicons[h].cut)-(refs[h].cuts?.[0]??refs[h].cut),input.amplicons[h].sequence.length,report.amplicons[h].direction==='reverse');
  const joint=multi?{attributionAmbiguous:aliases,groups:junction.models.flatMap((m,i)=>{if(fit.weights[i]<=1e-8)return [];const components=pathComponents(m.reference).filter(c=>c.pathSizes?.join('/')===m.sizes?.join('/')),best=components.sort((a,b)=>b.fraction-a.fraction)[0];return [{id:m.reference+':'+pathKey(m),reference:refs[m.reference].name,fraction:fit.weights[i],netSize:m.size,pathSizes:m.sizes,events:best?.events||[],alternatives:[]}];}),targets:refs.flatMap((r,h)=>(input.amplicons[h].cuts||[input.amplicons[h].cut]).map((cut,t)=>({reference:r.name,target:t+1,cut,edited:amplicons[h].share?pathComponents(h).filter(c=>c.events?.some(e=>e.target===t+1||e.start<cut&&e.end>cut)).reduce((sum,c)=>sum+c.fraction,0)/amplicons[h].share:0}))),heldOutR2:predictive?.size?Math.min(...[...predictive.values()].flatMap(e=>e.validationR2||[])):null,selectedModels:fit.weights.filter(v=>v>1e-8).length,candidateModels:junction.candidateCount||junction.models.length,iterations:junction.iterations,maxDeletion:fitInput.maxDeletion,ambiguousFraction:0}:undefined;
  if(multi&&!junction.searchComplete)warnings.push('Joint candidate search reached its computation budget; the best numerical estimates are shown.');
  return {joint,method:'pooled',referenceName:'Mixed amplicons',sampleName:input.sample.name,controlName:input.control.name,direction:'forward',cutPosition:firstCut,alignmentIdentity:0,alignment:[],variants:[],plots,warnings,synthetic:!!input.synthetic,diagnostics:{modeledRange:input.settings.analysisPolicy==='automatic'?{maxDeletion:input.settings.maxDeletion!,maxInsertion:input.settings.maxInsertion!}:undefined,transitionResponse:response,junctionFit:junction?{positions,iterations:junction.iterations,models:junction.models.map((m,i)=>({...m,fraction:fit.weights[i]})),duplicateLengths:junction.duplicateLengths}:undefined,referenceStability,algorithmVersion:enhanced?'sanger-junction-v2':'sanger-classic-v1',signalModel:enhanced?'calibrated':'classic',controlCalibration,sampleCalibration,splitStabilityPp,rangeSuggestion,discordantPositions:enhanced?discordantPositions:undefined,maxPositionRmse,requestedMaxIndel:input.settings.maxIndel,controlResponse:rawResponse,controlTransfer:transfer,supportScreen:enhanced?{predictionRemoved:predictionRemoved.map(i=>({reference:models[i].reference,size:models[i].size,foldGains:predictive!.get(i)!.foldGains})),removed: support!.removed.map(i=>({reference:models[i].reference,size:models[i].size})),threshold:support!.threshold,evidence:(referenceEvidence||[]).map(e=>({...e,reference:models[e.group[0]].reference,size:models[e.group[0]].size})),unpruned:models.map((m,i)=>({reference:m.reference,size:m.size,jointFraction:unprunedWeights[i]}))}:undefined,background:enhanced?{method:'integrated-four-channel-area',control:{levels:controlSignal!.background||[],trainingSamples:controlSignal!.backgroundSamples||[]},sample:{levels:sampleSignal!.background||[],trainingSamples:sampleSignal!.backgroundSamples||[]}}:undefined},annotations:input.amplicons.map((a,i)=>({name:a.name,reference:a.sequence,cut:a.cut,cuts:a.cuts,direction:report.amplicons[i].direction,share:amplicons[i].share,components:junction?junctionAnnotationComponents(junction.models.filter(m=>m.reference===i),fit.weights.filter((_,j)=>junction.models[j].reference===i).map(v=>amplicons[i].share>0?v/amplicons[i].share:0),(report.amplicons[i].direction==='forward'?a.cut:a.sequence.length-a.cut)-(refs[i].cuts?.[0]??refs[i].cut),a.sequence.length,report.amplicons[i].direction==='reverse').map(c=>({...c,support:emissionGroupSupport(referenceEvidence,models.findIndex(m=>m.reference===i&&pathKey(m)===(c.pathSizes?.join('/')??String(c.size))),predictive?.get(models.findIndex(m=>m.reference===i&&pathKey(m)===(c.pathSizes?.join('/')??String(c.size)))))})):amplicons[i].components,estimable:checksPass&&amplicons[i].share>=.02})),
    mixture:{components,wt:zero,indel:1-zero,inFrame,outOfFrame,r2:fit.r2,rmse:fit.rmse,upstreamError:1-sampleRegistration.r2,bases:positions.length,fitStart:positions[0],fitEnd:positions[positions.length-1]+1,converged:fit.converged,passesChecks:checksPass},
    pooled:{preflight:report,controlR2:controlFit.r2,sampleTrim:sampleRegistration.trim,controlTrim:controlRegistration.trim,amplicons}};
  };
  const rawObserved=positions.flatMap(p=>sampleRaw[p-sampleRegistration.trim]),mean=rawObserved.reduce((s,v)=>s+v,0)/rawObserved.length,rawVariance=rawObserved.reduce((s,v)=>s+(v-mean)**2,0);
  return {fitInput,templateError,finish,report,enhanced,rawVariance,products:input.amplicons};
}
export function analyzePooledSanger(input:PooledAnalysisInput,progress:(stage:string)=>void=()=>{}):SangerAnalysisResult {
 const prepared=preparePooledSanger(input,progress);
 return prepared.finish(solveJunctions(prepared.fitInput,prepared.templateError,prepared.enhanced));
}

BEGIN MODULE src/app/workers/core/sanger-primer-designer.ts
import {usablePrimerRead,annotatePrimerSafety,comparePrimerSafety} from './sanger-primer-safety';
import {screenSharedSangerWT} from './sanger-primer-separation';
import { alignTraceSequence } from './sanger-analysis';
import { reverseComplement } from '../../utils/biology.utils';
import { CommonPartner, PrimerProperties, PrimerSite, SangerCandidate, SangerDesignResult,
  SangerDesignSettings, SangerReference, ThreePrimeVariant } from '../../models/sanger-design';

// SantaLucia (1998), unified DNA/DNA nearest-neighbour parameters.
const NN: Record<string, [number, number]> = {
  AA: [-7.9,-22.2], TT: [-7.9,-22.2], AT: [-7.2,-20.4], TA: [-7.2,-21.3],
  CA: [-8.5,-22.7], TG: [-8.5,-22.7], GT: [-8.4,-22.4], AC: [-8.4,-22.4],
  CT: [-7.8,-21.0], AG: [-7.8,-21.0], GA: [-8.2,-22.2], TC: [-8.2,-22.2],
  CG: [-10.6,-27.2], GC: [-9.8,-24.4], GG: [-8.0,-19.9], CC: [-8.0,-19.9],
};
export function nearestNeighbourTm(seq: string, sodiumMm: number, primerNm: number): number {
  if (!/^[ACGT]{2,}$/.test(seq) || sodiumMm <= 0 || primerNm <= 0) return NaN;
  let h = .2, s = -5.7;
  for (let i = 0; i < seq.length - 1; i++) { const p = NN[seq.slice(i,i+2)]; h += p[0]; s += p[1]; }
  for (const base of [seq[0],seq[seq.length-1]]) if (base === 'A' || base === 'T') { h += 2.2; s += 6.9; }
  const symmetric = seq === reverseComplement(seq);
  if (symmetric) s -= 1.4;
  s += .368 * (seq.length - 1) * Math.log(sodiumMm / 1000);
  return 1000 * h / (s + 1.987 * Math.log(primerNm * 1e-9 / (symmetric ? 1 : 4))) - 273.15;
}

/** Contiguous complement screens; deliberately not presented as thermodynamic ΔG. */
export function complementarity(a: string, b: string): { any: number; end: number } {
  const rc = reverseComplement(b);
  let any = 0, end = 0;
  for (let shift = -rc.length + 1; shift < a.length; shift++) {
    let run = 0;
    for (let i = 0; i < a.length; i++) {
      const j = i - shift;
      if (j >= 0 && j < rc.length && a[i] === rc[j]) {
        run++; any = Math.max(any,run);
        if (i === a.length-1 || j-run+1 === 0) end = Math.max(end,run);
      } else run = 0;
    }
  }
  return { any, end };
}
const COMPLEMENT:Record<string,string>={A:'T',T:'A',C:'G',G:'C'};
function hairpinStem(seq: string): number {
  let best = 0;
  for (let i = 0; i < seq.length; i++) for (let j = i + 4; j < seq.length; j++) {
    let k = 0;
    while (i+k < j-k-3 && seq[i+k] === COMPLEMENT[seq[j-k]]) k++;
    best = Math.max(best,k);
  }
  return best;
}
function properties(seq: string, s: SangerDesignSettings): PrimerProperties | null {
  if (/[^ACGT]/.test(seq) || /([ACGT])\1{4}/.test(seq)) return null;
  const gc = 100 * (seq.match(/[GC]/g)?.length || 0) / seq.length;
  const tm = nearestNeighbourTm(seq,s.sodiumMm,s.primerNm);
  if (gc < s.gcMin || gc > s.gcMax || tm < s.tmMin || tm > s.tmMax) return null;
  const comp = complementarity(seq,seq), hairpin = hairpinStem(seq);
  if (comp.end >= 5 || comp.any >= 9 || hairpin >= 6) return null;
  return { sequence: seq, tm, gc, selfEnd: comp.end, selfAny: comp.any, hairpinStem: hairpin };
}

type Direction = 'forward' | 'reverse';
/** Enumerate target-local oligos, then scan the entire supplied sequence for
 * those candidates. This avoids indexing every genomic k-mer in a long reference. */
function localOligos(ref: SangerReference, k: number, direction: Direction, reach: number): Set<string> {
  const dna = direction === 'forward' ? ref.sequence : reverseComplement(ref.sequence);
  const cuts = ref.cuts.map(c => direction === 'forward' ? c : dna.length-c);
  const lo = Math.max(0,Math.min(...cuts)-reach-k);
  const hi = Math.min(dna.length-k,Math.max(...cuts)+reach);
  const out = new Set<string>();
  for (let at=lo;at<=hi;at++) {
    const seq=dna.slice(at,at+k);if (!/[^ACGT]/.test(seq)) out.add(seq);
  }
  return out;
}
function candidateIndex(dna: string, wanted: Set<string>, k: number): Map<string,number[]> {
  const out = new Map<string,number[]>();
  for (let at=0;at<=dna.length-k;at++) {
    const seq=dna.slice(at,at+k);
    if (!wanted.has(seq)) continue;
    const positions=out.get(seq);
    // Only uniqueness is needed. Keep at most two positions, including repeats.
    if (!positions) out.set(seq,[at]);else if (positions.length<2) positions.push(at);
  }
  return out;
}
export function exactBindingSites(seq: string, ref: SangerReference): { start: number; end: number; direction: Direction }[] {
  const hits: { start: number; end: number; direction: Direction }[] = [];
  for (const direction of ['forward','reverse'] as const) {
    const dna = direction === 'forward' ? ref.sequence : reverseComplement(ref.sequence);
    for (let at = dna.indexOf(seq); at >= 0; at = dna.indexOf(seq,at+1)) {
      hits.push({ direction, start: direction === 'forward' ? at : dna.length-at-seq.length,
        end: direction === 'forward' ? at+seq.length : dna.length-at });
    }
  }
  return hits;
}
function site(ref: SangerReference, start: number, k: number, direction: Direction, s: SangerDesignSettings): PrimerSite {
  const dna = direction === 'forward' ? ref.sequence : reverseComplement(ref.sequence);
  return { reference: ref.name, direction,
    start: direction === 'forward' ? start : dna.length-start-k,
    end: direction === 'forward' ? start+k : dna.length-start,
    cutOffsets: ref.cuts.map(c => (direction === 'forward' ? c : dna.length-c) - start-k),
    extension: dna.slice(start+k,start+k+s.readEnd) };
}
function windows(ref: SangerReference, s: SangerDesignSettings): [number,number][] {
  return ref.cuts.map(c => [c-Math.floor(s.window/2),c+Math.ceil(s.window/2)]);
}
export function primerTargetDistance(c: SangerCandidate): number {return Math.max(...c.sites.flatMap(hit=>hit.cutOffsets));}
export function comparePrimerDistance(a:SangerCandidate,b:SangerCandidate):number {return primerTargetDistance(a)-primerTargetDistance(b) || Math.abs(a.tm-60)-Math.abs(b.tm-60) || (Math.max(...(a.partner?.products||[0]))-Math.max(...(b.partner?.products||[0]))) || a.id.localeCompare(b.id);}
function coversRead(sites: PrimerSite[], refs: SangerReference[], s: SangerDesignSettings): boolean {
  // Quantitative Sanger needs an intact upstream calibration interval.
  // Match the template handoff contract even for a single PCR product.
  if(s.platform!=='ngs'&&s.workflow!=='shared-pcr'&&sites.some(hit=>hit.cutOffsets.some(c=>c<usablePrimerRead(s,hit.extension.length).start+72)))return false;
  return sites.every((hit,i) => windows(refs[i],s).every(([lo,hi]) => {
    const start = hit.direction === 'forward' ? lo-hit.end : hit.start-hi;
    const end = hit.direction === 'forward' ? hi-hit.end : hit.start-lo;
    return lo >= 0 && hi <= refs[i].sequence.length && (s.platform==='ngs' ? start>=0 : start-s.indelRange >= usablePrimerRead(s,hit.extension.length).start && end+s.indelRange <= usablePrimerRead(s,hit.extension.length).end);
  }));
}
function minOffset(sites: PrimerSite[]): number {
  let gap = sites.length<2 ? 0 : Infinity;
  for (let a = 0; a < sites.length; a++) for (let b = a+1; b < sites.length; b++) {
    // T1 is compared with T1 across references, even on reverse-oriented reads.
    for (let t = 0; t < sites[a].cutOffsets.length; t++) gap = Math.min(gap,Math.abs(sites[a].cutOffsets[t]-sites[b].cutOffsets[t]));
  }
  return gap;
}
function traceDistance(sites: PrimerSite[], s: SangerDesignSettings): [number,number] {
  let differences = sites.length<2 ? 0 : Infinity, compared = sites.length<2 ? Math.max(0,Math.min(s.readEnd,sites[0].extension.length)-s.readStart) : Infinity;
  for (let a = 0; a < sites.length; a++) for (let b = a+1; b < sites.length; b++) {
    const range=s.platform==='ngs'?{start:s.readStart,end:Math.min(s.readEnd,sites[a].extension.length,sites[b].extension.length)}:usablePrimerRead(s,Math.min(sites[a].extension.length,sites[b].extension.length)),end=range.end;
    let diff = 0, observed = 0;
    for (let i = range.start; i < end; i++) if (/[ACGT]/.test(sites[a].extension[i]) && /[ACGT]/.test(sites[b].extension[i])) {
      observed++; if (sites[a].extension[i] !== sites[b].extension[i]) diff++;
    }
    differences = Math.min(differences,diff); compared = Math.min(compared,observed);
  }
  return [differences,compared];
}

export function validateDesign(refs: SangerReference[], s: SangerDesignSettings): void {
  if (s.workflow==='standard-pcr' ? refs.length!==1 : refs.length<2 || refs.length>8) throw new Error(s.workflow==='standard-pcr'?'Use one reference for single-reference PCR.':'Use 2–8 references for shared or homeolog-specific design.');
  if (new Set(refs.map(r => r.name)).size !== refs.length || refs.some(r => !r.name)) throw new Error('Reference names must be non-empty and unique.');
  if (refs.some(r => !r.sequence || /[^ACGTN]/.test(r.sequence))) throw new Error('References must contain A, C, G, T or N.');
  if (refs.reduce((n,r) => n+r.sequence.length,0) > 8000000 || refs.some(r => r.sequence.length > 2000000)) throw new Error('Use full reference sequences up to 2,000,000 bp each and 8,000,000 bp total.');
  if (!refs[0].cuts.length || refs[0].cuts.length > 8 || refs.some(r => r.cuts.length !== refs[0].cuts.length)) throw new Error('Give each reference the same number of corresponding targets (1–8), in the same target order.');
  if (refs.some(r => r.cuts.some(c => !Number.isInteger(c) || c < 0 || c > r.sequence.length))) throw new Error('Invalid cut coordinates.');
  for (const [key,value] of Object.entries(s)) if (typeof value === 'number' && !Number.isFinite(value)) throw new Error(`Invalid ${key}.`);
  const ints = [s.primerMin,s.primerMax,s.window,s.readStart,s.readEnd,s.minOffset,s.indelRange,s.productMin,s.productMax];
  if (ints.some(v => !Number.isInteger(v))) throw new Error('Lengths, positions and offsets must be whole numbers.');
  if (s.primerMin < 16 || s.primerMax > 30 || s.primerMax < s.primerMin) throw new Error('Primer length must be within 16–30 nt.');
  if (s.window < 10 || s.window > 2000 || s.readStart < 0 || s.readEnd > 1500 || s.readEnd <= s.readStart) throw new Error('Check required window and usable read range (maximum 1,500 bp).');
  if (s.minOffset < 1 || s.indelRange < 0 || s.indelRange > 100) throw new Error('Use a positive offset and an indel range of 0–100 bp.');
  if (s.sodiumMm < 1 || s.sodiumMm > 1000 || s.primerNm < 1 || s.primerNm > 10000) throw new Error('Check Na+ and oligo concentration.');
  if (s.gcMin < 0 || s.gcMax > 100 || s.gcMin > s.gcMax || s.tmMin > s.tmMax || s.tmMin < 0 || s.tmMax > 100) throw new Error('Check GC and Tm ranges.');
  if (s.productMin < 1 || s.productMax > 20000 || s.productMin > s.productMax || s.maxTmDifference < 0) throw new Error('Check PCR product and paired-primer Tm limits.');
  if (!['forward','reverse','both'].includes(s.direction)) throw new Error('Invalid reading direction.');
  if (!['shared-sanger','shared-pcr','homeolog-pcr','standard-pcr'].includes(s.workflow)) throw new Error('Invalid design workflow.');
  if (!Number.isInteger(s.specificityMismatches) || s.specificityMismatches < 0 || s.specificityMismatches > 2 || typeof s.specificBoth !== 'boolean') throw new Error('Use 0–2 mismatches for the input-reference specificity screen.');
  if (s.workflow === 'homeolog-pcr' && refs.some(r=>r.sequence.includes('N'))) throw new Error('Resolve N bases before the homeolog-specific mismatch screen (A/C/G/T references required).');
  if (!['three-prime','mismatch'].includes(s.specificityStrategy) || ![1,3].includes(s.threePrimeWindow)) throw new Error('Choose a 3′-variant or mismatch-only specificity strategy and a terminal window of 1 or 3 bases.');
  if(s.platform!==undefined&&!['sanger','ngs'].includes(s.platform))throw new Error('Invalid sequencing platform.');
  if(s.platform==='ngs'&&s.workflow==='shared-sanger')throw new Error('Offset-separated shared Sanger requires the Sanger platform.');
  if (typeof s.requirePartner !== 'boolean') throw new Error('Invalid PCR partner setting.');
}

/** Prefer a usable opposite sequencing read for multi-target designs, without
 * adding a design mode or rejecting otherwise valid one-direction PCR pairs. */
export function oppositeReadReady(sites:PrimerSite[],refs:SangerReference[],s:SangerDesignSettings):boolean {
 if(!refs.some(r=>r.cuts.length>1)||!coversRead(sites,refs,s))return false;
 if(s.workflow==='shared-sanger')for(let h=0;h<sites.length;h++)for(let k=h+1;k<sites.length;k++)for(let t=0;t<refs[h].cuts.length;t++)if(Math.abs(sites[h].cutOffsets[t]-sites[k].cutOffsets[t])<=2*s.indelRange)return false;
 return true;
}
function partnersFor(c: SangerCandidate, refs: SangerReference[], s: SangerDesignSettings,
  pool: { props: PrimerProperties; sites: PrimerSite[]; direction: Direction }[]): CommonPartner[] {
  const direction = c.direction === 'forward' ? 'reverse' : 'forward';
  const ranked:{partner:CommonPartner;score:number}[]=[];
  for (const item of pool) {
    if (item.direction !== direction) continue;
    const props = item.props;
    if (Math.abs(props.tm-c.tm) > s.maxTmDifference) continue;
    const pair = complementarity(c.sequence,props.sequence);
    if (pair.end >= 5 || pair.any >= 9) continue;
    const sites: PrimerSite[] = [], products: number[] = [];
    for (let i = 0; i < refs.length; i++) {
      const hit = item.sites[i], bound = windows(refs[i],s);
      if (direction === 'reverse' ? hit.start < Math.max(...bound.map(w => w[1]))+(s.platform==='ngs'?0:s.indelRange) : hit.end > Math.min(...bound.map(w => w[0]))-(s.platform==='ngs'?0:s.indelRange)) break;
      const product = c.direction === 'forward' ? hit.end-c.sites[i].start : c.sites[i].end-hit.start;
      if (product < s.productMin || product > s.productMax) break;
      sites.push(hit); products.push(product);
    }
    if (sites.length !== refs.length) continue;
    if(s.workflow==='shared-sanger'){const requiredEnd=Math.max(...c.sites.flatMap(hit=>hit.cutOffsets))+Math.ceil(s.window/2)+s.indelRange;if(Math.min(...products.map(product=>product-c.sequence.length))<requiredEnd)continue;}
    const score = Math.abs(props.tm-c.tm)*10 + Math.max(...products)/1000 + pair.end;
    ranked.push({partner:{...props,sites:sites.map((hit,i)=>({...hit,extension:hit.extension.slice(0,products[i]-props.sequence.length)})),products,pairEnd:pair.end},score});
  }
  return ranked.sort((a,b)=>Number(oppositeReadReady(b.partner.sites,refs,s))-Number(oppositeReadReady(a.partner.sites,refs,s))||a.score-b.score||a.partner.sequence.localeCompare(b.partner.sequence)).slice(0,3).map(r=>r.partner);
}

export function designSangerPrimers(refs: SangerReference[], s: SangerDesignSettings,
  progress: (fraction: number, stage: string) => void = () => {}): SangerDesignResult {
  validateDesign(refs,s);
  if (s.workflow === 'homeolog-pcr') return designSpecificPairs(refs,s,progress);
  const stats = { enumerated:0,qualityPassed:0,commonUnique:0,geometryPassed:0,offsetPassed:0,partnerChecked:0 };
  const candidates: SangerCandidate[] = [];
  const canPair=refs.every(r=>r.sequence.length>=s.productMin);
  const sharedPool: { props: PrimerProperties; sites: PrimerSite[]; direction: Direction }[] = [];
  const forward = refs.map(r => r.sequence), reverse = refs.map(r => reverseComplement(r.sequence));
  for (let k = s.primerMin; k <= s.primerMax; k++) {
    progress(.65*(k-s.primerMin)/(s.primerMax-s.primerMin+1),`Searching ${k}-nt shared primers`);
    const wanted = new Set<string>();
    const quality = new Map<string,PrimerProperties>();
    for (const direction of ['forward','reverse'] as const) for (const seq of localOligos(refs[0],k,direction,canPair ? Math.max(s.readEnd,s.productMax) : s.readEnd)) {
      if (wanted.has(seq)) continue;
      const props=properties(seq,s); if (props) {wanted.add(seq);quality.set(seq,props);}
    }
    const fi = forward.map(d => candidateIndex(d,wanted,k)), ri = reverse.map(d => candidateIndex(d,wanted,k));
    for (const direction of ['forward','reverse'] as const) {
      const intended = direction === 'forward' ? fi : ri, opposite = direction === 'forward' ? ri : fi;
      for (const [seq,positions] of intended[0]) {
        stats.enumerated++;
        if (positions.length !== 1 || opposite[0].has(seq)) continue;
        const props = quality.get(seq)!; stats.qualityPassed++;
        const hits: PrimerSite[] = [];
        for (let i = 0; i < refs.length; i++) {
          const list = intended[i].get(seq);
          if (!list || list.length !== 1 || opposite[i].has(seq)) break;
          hits.push(site(refs[i],list[0],k,direction,s));
        }
        if (hits.length !== refs.length) continue; stats.commonUnique++;
        sharedPool.push({ props,sites:hits,direction });
        if (s.direction !== 'both' && s.direction !== direction) continue;
        if (!coversRead(hits,refs,s)) continue; stats.geometryPassed++;
        const offset = minOffset(hits); if (s.workflow==='shared-sanger' && offset < s.minOffset) continue; stats.offsetPassed++;
        const [diff,bases] = traceDistance(hits,s);
        candidates.push({ ...props,id:`${direction}-${seq}`,direction,sites:hits,minOffset:offset,
          minTraceDifferences:diff,traceComparedBases:bases,indelOffsetOverlap:s.workflow==='shared-sanger' && offset <= 2*s.indelRange,partner:null });
      }
    }
  }
  candidates.sort(comparePrimerDistance);
  const returned: SangerCandidate[] = [],excludedCandidates:{id:string;reason:string}[]=[];
  const screen=(c:SangerCandidate)=>{if(s.workflow!=='shared-sanger'||s.platform==='ngs')return true;const range=usablePrimerRead(s,Math.min(...c.sites.map(h=>h.extension.length)));const check=screenSharedSangerWT(c.sites,{readStart:range.start,readEnd:range.end});if(!check.passed){excludedCandidates.push({id:c.id,reason:check.reason});return false;}c.wtSeparation={minimumDifferences:check.minimumDifferences,comparedBases:check.comparedBases,condition:check.condition,warnings:check.warnings};return true;};
  // Bounded primary search, with multiple opposite-primer choices per primary.
  const limit = Math.min(candidates.length,600);
  for (let i = 0; i < limit; i++) {
    progress(.65+.34*i/Math.max(1,limit),`Checking PCR partner ${i+1}/${limit}`);
    const c = candidates[i]; stats.partnerChecked++;
    const partners = canPair ? partnersFor(c,refs,s,sharedPool) : [];
    for(const partner of partners){
      const paired={...c,id:c.id+'-'+partner.sequence,partner,targetDistance:primerTargetDistance(c),sites:c.sites.map((hit,j)=>({...hit,extension:hit.extension.slice(0,partner.products[j]-c.sequence.length)}))};
      [paired.minTraceDifferences,paired.traceComparedBases]=traceDistance(paired.sites,s);if(coversRead(paired.sites,refs,s)&&screen(paired))returned.push(paired);
    }
    if(!partners.length&&!s.requirePartner&&s.workflow==='shared-sanger'){const unpaired={...c,targetDistance:primerTargetDistance(c)};if(screen(unpaired))returned.push(unpaired);}
    if(returned.length>=300&&i+1<limit&&primerTargetDistance(candidates[i+1])>primerTargetDistance(c))break;
  }
  annotatePrimerSafety(returned,refs,s,stage=>progress(.99,stage));
  returned.sort((a,b)=>comparePrimerSafety(a,b)|| (s.platform!=='ngs'&&s.workflow==='shared-sanger'?b.minTraceDifferences-a.minTraceDifferences:0)||Number(!!b.partner&&oppositeReadReady(b.partner.sites,refs,s))-Number(!!a.partner&&oppositeReadReady(a.partner.sites,refs,s))||comparePrimerDistance(a,b));const capped=returned.length>300;
  progress(1,'Complete');
  return { candidates:returned.slice(0,300), stats:{...stats,separabilityRejected:excludedCandidates.length},excludedCandidates, complete:!capped && stats.partnerChecked === candidates.length,
    references:refs,settings:{...s} };
}

/** Pigeonhole seed search for ungapped matches with <=m substitutions.
 * m+1 disjoint seeds guarantee at least one exact seed in every such match. */
export function nearBindingSiteCount(oligo: string, strands: string[], mismatches: number, limit=2): number {
  if (!/^[ACGT]+$/.test(oligo) || mismatches<0 || mismatches>2) throw new Error('Invalid mismatch search.');
  let count=0;
  for (const dna of strands) {
    const visited=new Set<number>();
    for (let part=0;part<=mismatches;part++) {
      const lo=Math.floor(part*oligo.length/(mismatches+1));
      const hi=Math.floor((part+1)*oligo.length/(mismatches+1));
      const seed=oligo.slice(lo,hi);
      for (let hit=dna.indexOf(seed);hit>=0;hit=dna.indexOf(seed,hit+1)) {
        const at=hit-lo;
        if (at<0 || at+oligo.length>dna.length || visited.has(at)) continue;
        visited.add(at);
        let errors=0;
        for (let j=0;j<oligo.length;j++) {if (dna[at+j]!==oligo[j]) errors++;if (errors>mismatches) break;}
        if (errors<=mismatches && ++count>=limit) return count;
      }
    }
  }
  return count;
}

interface VariantMap {background:string;identity:number;positions:Map<number,{base:string;position:number}>}
/** Pairwise homoeolog alignment anchored at the configured corresponding target.
 * SNP evidence is from aligned genomic bases, not arbitrary nearest oligo hits. */
export function homoeologVariantMaps(refs:SangerReference[],own:number,reach=1500):VariantMap[] {
  const target=refs[own],center=target.cuts[0],radius=Math.min(1500,reach)+100;
  const left=Math.max(0,center-radius),right=Math.min(target.sequence.length,center+radius),a=target.sequence.slice(left,right);
  return refs.flatMap((other,b)=>{
    if(b===own)return [];
    const lo=Math.max(0,other.cuts[0]-radius),hi=Math.min(other.sequence.length,other.cuts[0]+radius),raw=other.sequence.slice(lo,hi);
    let best:{identity:number;score:number;reverse:boolean;columns:ReturnType<typeof alignTraceSequence>['columns']}|null=null;
    for(const reverse of [false,true]) {
      const aligned=alignTraceSequence(a,reverse?reverseComplement(raw):raw);
      const anchored=aligned.columns.some(c=>c.ref!==null&&c.query!==null&&Math.abs(c.ref+left-center)<=2&&Math.abs((reverse?hi-c.query-1:lo+c.query)-other.cuts[0])<=8);
      if(!anchored||aligned.identity<.7)continue;
      if(!best||aligned.score>best.score)best={...aligned,reverse};
    }
    const positions=new Map<number,{base:string;position:number}>();
    if(best)for(const c of best.columns)if(c.ref!==null&&c.query!==null&&/[ACGT]/.test(c.a)&&/[ACGT]/.test(c.b)&&c.a!==c.b)positions.set(left+c.ref,{base:c.b,position:best.reverse?hi-c.query-1:lo+c.query});
    return [{background:other.name,identity:best?.identity||0,positions}];
  });
}
function diagnosticVariants(hit:PrimerSite,maps:VariantMap[],terminalWindow:number):ThreePrimeVariant[]|null {
  const variants:ThreePrimeVariant[]=[];
  for(const bg of maps){
    let found:ThreePrimeVariant|null=null;
    for(let j=0;j<terminalWindow;j++){
      const pos=hit.direction==='forward'?hit.end-1-j:hit.start+j,other=bg.positions.get(pos);
      if(!other)continue;
      const base=hit.direction==='forward'?other.base:reverseComplement(other.base);
      // The primer oligo base is assigned by the caller (below) for the same 3′ offset.
      found={background:bg.background,position:pos+1,backgroundPosition:other.position+1,targetBase:'',backgroundBase:base,fromThreePrime:j+1,alignmentIdentity:bg.identity};break;
    }
    if(!found)return null;variants.push(found);
  }
  return variants;
}
/** Reject any near-binding background site whose terminal bases still match. */
export function hasTerminalCompatibleSite(oligo:string,strands:string[],mismatches:number,terminalWindow:number):boolean {
  const suffix=oligo.slice(-terminalWindow);
  for(const dna of strands)for(let hit=dna.indexOf(suffix);hit>=0;hit=dna.indexOf(suffix,hit+1)){
    const at=hit-(oligo.length-terminalWindow);if(at<0||at+oligo.length>dna.length)continue;
    let errors=0;for(let j=0;j<oligo.length;j++){if(dna[at+j]!==oligo[j])errors++;if(errors>mismatches)break;}
    if(errors<=mismatches)return true;
  }
  return false;
}

function designSpecificPairs(refs: SangerReference[], s: SangerDesignSettings,
  progress: (fraction:number,stage:string)=>void): SangerDesignResult {
  const stats={enumerated:0,qualityPassed:0,commonUnique:0,geometryPassed:0,offsetPassed:0,partnerChecked:0};
  const candidates:SangerCandidate[]=[];
  const coverage:{reference:string;candidates:number}[]=[];
  const strands=refs.map(r=>[r.sequence,reverseComplement(r.sequence)]);
  let complete=true;
  const hitCache=new Map<string,number>();
  const count=(seq:string,i:number)=>{
    const key=`${i}:${seq}`;
    if (!hitCache.has(key)) hitCache.set(key,nearBindingSiteCount(seq,strands[i],s.specificityMismatches));
    return hitCache.get(key)!;
  };
  for (let r=0;r<refs.length;r++) {
    progress(r/refs.length,`Designing separate PCR pairs for ${refs[r].name}`);
    const ref=refs[r], bounds=windows(ref,s).map(([lo,hi])=>[lo-(s.platform==='ngs'?0:s.indelRange),hi+(s.platform==='ngs'?0:s.indelRange)] as [number,number]);
    const variantMaps=s.specificityStrategy==='three-prime'?homoeologVariantMaps(refs,r,s.productMax):[];
    if (bounds.some(([lo,hi])=>lo<0 || hi>ref.sequence.length)) {coverage.push({reference:ref.name,candidates:0});continue;}
    const pool:{props:PrimerProperties;hit:PrimerSite;variants:ThreePrimeVariant[]|null}[]=[];
    // Enumerate only possible flanks. Specificity is still checked over the full inputs.
    for (const direction of ['forward','reverse'] as const) {
      const dna=strands[r][direction==='forward'?0:1];
      const oriented=bounds.map(([lo,hi])=>direction==='forward'?[lo,hi]:[dna.length-hi,dna.length-lo]);
      const first=Math.min(...oriented.map(w=>w[0])), last=Math.max(...oriented.map(w=>w[1]));
      for (let k=s.primerMin;k<=s.primerMax;k++) {
        const begin=Math.max(0,last-s.productMax), end=Math.min(dna.length-k,first-k);
        for (let at=begin;at<=end;at++) {
          stats.enumerated++;
          const props=properties(dna.slice(at,at+k),s);if (!props) continue;stats.qualityPassed++;
          const hit=site(ref,at,k,direction,s),variants=s.specificityStrategy==='three-prime'?diagnosticVariants(hit,variantMaps,s.threePrimeWindow):null;
          if(variants)for(const variant of variants)variant.targetBase=props.sequence[k-variant.fromThreePrime];
          pool.push({props,hit,variants});
        }
      }
    }
    const rank=(a:typeof pool[number],b:typeof pool[number])=>Math.max(...a.hit.cutOffsets)-Math.max(...b.hit.cutOffsets) || (s.specificityStrategy==='three-prime'?Number(!!b.variants)-Number(!!a.variants):0) || Math.abs(a.props.tm-60)-Math.abs(b.props.tm-60) || a.props.selfEnd-b.props.selfEnd || a.hit.start-b.hit.start || a.props.sequence.localeCompare(b.props.sequence);
    const byDirection={forward:pool.filter(p=>p.hit.direction==='forward').sort(rank),reverse:pool.filter(p=>p.hit.direction==='reverse').sort(rank)};
    const ownUnique=(p:typeof pool[number])=>count(p.props.sequence,r)===1;
    const specificCache=new Map<string,boolean>();
    const specific=(p:typeof pool[number])=>{
      if(specificCache.has(p.props.sequence))return specificCache.get(p.props.sequence)!;
      const passed=s.specificityStrategy==='three-prime'?!!p.variants&&refs.every((_,i)=>i===r||!hasTerminalCompatibleSite(p.props.sequence,strands[i],s.specificityMismatches,s.threePrimeWindow)):refs.every((_,i)=>i===r||count(p.props.sequence,i)===0);
      specificCache.set(p.props.sequence,passed);return passed;
    };
    const found:SangerCandidate[]=[];
    for (const direction of ['forward','reverse'] as const) {
      if (s.direction!=='both' && s.direction!==direction) continue;
      const primaries=byDirection[direction].filter(p=>coversRead([p.hit],[ref],s)&&(!s.specificBoth||s.specificityStrategy!=='three-prime'||!!p.variants));
      const partners=byDirection[direction==='forward'?'reverse':'forward'];
      stats.geometryPassed+=primaries.length;
      if (primaries.length>240 || partners.length>240) complete=false;
      const allPartners=partners.slice(0,240),diagnosticPartners=partners.filter(q=>!!q.variants).slice(0,240);
      if(partners.filter(q=>!!q.variants).length>240)complete=false;
      for (const p of primaries.slice(0,240)) {
        if (!ownUnique(p)) continue;stats.commonUnique++;
        const primarySpecific=specific(p);
        if (s.specificBoth && !primarySpecific) continue;
        let partnerOptions=0;
        const partnerShortlist=s.specificityStrategy==='three-prime'&&(s.specificBoth||!primarySpecific)?diagnosticPartners:allPartners;
        for (const q of partnerShortlist) {
          stats.partnerChecked++;
          if (Math.abs(p.props.tm-q.props.tm)>s.maxTmDifference) continue;
          const product=direction==='forward'?q.hit.end-p.hit.start:p.hit.end-q.hit.start;
          if (product<s.productMin || product>s.productMax) continue;
          const comp=complementarity(p.props.sequence,q.props.sequence);
          if (comp.end>=5 || comp.any>=9 || !ownUnique(q)) continue;
          const partnerSpecific=specific(q);
          if (s.specificBoth?!partnerSpecific:!primarySpecific&&!partnerSpecific) continue;
          if(s.platform!=='ngs'&&!coversRead([{...p.hit,extension:p.hit.extension.slice(0,product-p.props.sequence.length)}],[ref],s))continue;
          stats.offsetPassed++;
          found.push({...p.props,id:`specific-${r}-${direction}-${p.props.sequence}-${q.props.sequence}`,direction,
            referenceIndex:r,sites:[{...p.hit,extension:p.hit.extension.slice(0,product-p.props.sequence.length)}],
            minOffset:0,minTraceDifferences:0,traceComparedBases:0,indelOffsetOverlap:false,
            specificity:{primary:primarySpecific,partner:partnerSpecific,mismatches:s.specificityMismatches,strategy:s.specificityStrategy,terminalWindow:s.threePrimeWindow,primaryVariants:primarySpecific?p.variants||[]:[],partnerVariants:partnerSpecific?q.variants||[]:[]},
            partner:{...q.props,sites:[q.hit],products:[product],pairEnd:comp.end}});
          if(++partnerOptions>=3)break;
        }
        if (found.length>=100){complete=false;break;}
      }

    }
    found.sort(comparePrimerDistance);if(found.length>100)complete=false;const selected=found.slice(0,100);selected.forEach(c=>c.targetDistance=primerTargetDistance(c));
    candidates.push(...selected);coverage.push({reference:ref.name,candidates:selected.length});
  }
  progress(1,'Complete');
  annotatePrimerSafety(candidates,refs,s,stage=>progress(.99,stage));candidates.sort((a,b)=>comparePrimerSafety(a,b)||comparePrimerDistance(a,b));return {candidates,coverage,stats,complete,references:refs,settings:{...s}};
}

END MODULE


BEGIN MODULE src/app/workers/core/sanger-primer-safety.ts
import {reverseComplement} from '../../utils/biology.utils';
import type {PrimerSite, SangerReference, SangerDesignSettings, SangerCandidate, PrimerBindingScreen} from '../../models/sanger-design';
/** Design guard only: measured read quality still needs checking after sequencing. */
export const SANGER_EDGE_RESERVE = 50;
export function usablePrimerRead(settings:Pick<SangerDesignSettings,'readStart'|'readEnd'>,length:number) {
  return {start:Math.max(SANGER_EDGE_RESERVE,settings.readStart),end:Math.min(settings.readEnd,length)-SANGER_EDGE_RESERVE};
}
/** Enumerate ungapped <=3 substitution sites on both strands. Four disjoint
 * seeds make the search exhaustive at that bound. Intended sites are excluded
 * by reference, coordinates AND orientation, not by similar sequence alone.
 * This is sequence similarity, not a binding-probability prediction. */
export function screenPrimerBinding(oligo:string,refs:SangerReference[],intended:Pick<PrimerSite,'reference'|'start'|'end'|'direction'>[]):PrimerBindingScreen {
  if(!/^[ACGT]{4,}$/.test(oligo))throw Error('Primer screen requires an A/C/G/T oligo.');
  const limit=3;let count=0,minimum=oligo.length+1;
  const sites:PrimerBindingScreen['sites']=[];
  const references=refs.map(ref=>{
    let nearest=oligo.length+1,nearSiteCount=0;
    for(const direction of ['forward','reverse'] as const){
      const dna=direction==='forward'?ref.sequence:reverseComplement(ref.sequence),visited=new Set<number>();
      for(let part=0;part<=limit;part++){
        const lo=Math.floor(part*oligo.length/4),hi=Math.floor((part+1)*oligo.length/4),seed=oligo.slice(lo,hi);
        for(let hit=dna.indexOf(seed);hit>=0;hit=dna.indexOf(seed,hit+1)){
          const at=hit-lo;if(at<0||at+oligo.length>dna.length||visited.has(at))continue;visited.add(at);
          const start=direction==='forward'?at:dna.length-at-oligo.length,end=start+oligo.length;
          if(intended.some(s=>s.reference===ref.name&&s.start===start&&s.end===end&&s.direction===direction))continue;
          let mismatches=0,terminalMismatches=0,unknown=false;
          for(let j=0;j<oligo.length;j++){
            if(!'ACGT'.includes(dna[at+j])){unknown=true;break;}
            if(dna[at+j]!==oligo[j]){mismatches++;if(j>=oligo.length-3)terminalMismatches++;}
            if(mismatches>limit)break;
          }
          if(unknown||mismatches>limit)continue;
          count++;nearSiteCount++;nearest=Math.min(nearest,mismatches);minimum=Math.min(minimum,mismatches);
          const row={reference:ref.name,start,end,direction,mismatches,terminalMismatches};sites.push(row);
          sites.sort((a,b)=>a.mismatches-b.mismatches||a.terminalMismatches-b.terminalMismatches||a.reference.localeCompare(b.reference)||a.start-b.start);
          if(sites.length>12)sites.pop();
        }
      }
    }
    // If no near site was found, obtain the exact nearest known sequence by
    // branch-and-bound Hamming scanning. No binding efficiency is inferred.
    if(nearest>3)for(const direction of ['forward','reverse']as const){
      const dna=direction==='forward'?ref.sequence:reverseComplement(ref.sequence);
      for(let at=0;at+oligo.length<=dna.length;at++){
        const start=direction==='forward'?at:dna.length-at-oligo.length,end=start+oligo.length;
        if(intended.some(s=>s.reference===ref.name&&s.start===start&&s.end===end&&s.direction===direction))continue;
        let errors=0,j=0;
        for(;j<oligo.length;j++){const b=dna[at+j];if(!'ACGT'.includes(b))break;if(b!==oligo[j])errors++;if(errors>=nearest)break;}
        if(j===oligo.length)nearest=errors;
      }
    }
    minimum=Math.min(minimum,nearest);
    return {reference:ref.name,minimumMismatches:nearest<=oligo.length?nearest:4,minimumExact:nearest<=oligo.length,nearSiteCount,hasUnknownBases:ref.sequence.includes('N')};
  });
  return {searchLimit:3,minimumMismatches:minimum<=oligo.length?minimum:4,minimumExact:minimum<=oligo.length,nearSiteCount:count,sites,references};
}
/** Apply only to the existing bounded design shortlist; no edited trace used. */
export function annotatePrimerSafety(candidates:SangerCandidate[],refs:SangerReference[],settings:SangerDesignSettings,progress:(stage:string)=>void=()=>{}){
  if(settings.platform==='ngs')return;
  const cache=new Map<string,PrimerBindingScreen>();
  const check=(sequence:string,sites:PrimerSite[])=>{const key=sequence+'|'+sites.map(s=>`${s.reference}:${s.start}:${s.end}:${s.direction}`).join('|');let value=cache.get(key);if(!value){value=screenPrimerBinding(sequence,refs,sites);cache.set(key,value);}return value;};
  for(const [index,c]of candidates.entries()){if(index%10===0)progress(`Checking unintended primer sites ${index+1}/${candidates.length}`);c.primerSafety={primary:check(c.sequence,c.sites),partner:c.partner?check(c.partner.sequence,c.partner.sites):null};}
}
export function comparePrimerSafety(a:SangerCandidate,b:SangerCandidate):number {
  const ap=a.primerSafety,bp=b.primerSafety;if(!ap||!bp)return 0;
  return bp.primary.minimumMismatches-ap.primary.minimumMismatches || ap.primary.nearSiteCount-bp.primary.nearSiteCount || (bp.partner?.minimumMismatches??4)-(ap.partner?.minimumMismatches??4) || (ap.partner?.nearSiteCount??0)-(bp.partner?.nearSiteCount??0);
}

END MODULE


BEGIN MODULE src/app/workers/core/sanger-primer-separation.ts
