import {PrimerSite,SangerDesignSettings} from '../../models/sanger-design';import {gramCondition} from './sanger-signal';
/** Prospective WT screen using only submitted references and primer/product
 * geometry. No edited trace, outcome, truth label or future allele is used.
 * This is scoped to normalized mixed-Sanger discrimination, not PCR success. */
export function screenSharedSangerWT(sites:PrimerSite[],settings:Pick<SangerDesignSettings,'readStart'|'readEnd'>){
 if(sites.length<2)return {passed:true,reason:'Single product',minimumDifferences:0,comparedBases:0,condition:1,warnings:[]as string[]};
 const end=Math.min(settings.readEnd,...sites.map(s=>s.extension.length)),positions=Array.from({length:Math.max(0,end-settings.readStart)},(_,i)=>i+settings.readStart).filter(p=>sites.every(s=>/^[ACGT]$/.test(s.extension[p]||'')));
 if(!positions.length)return {passed:false,reason:'No shared known WT sequence in the usable Sanger range',minimumDifferences:0,comparedBases:0,condition:null,warnings:[]as string[]};
 let differences=Infinity;for(let a=0;a<sites.length;a++)for(let b=a+1;b<sites.length;b++)differences=Math.min(differences,positions.filter(p=>sites[a].extension[p]!==sites[b].extension[p]).length);
 if(differences===0)return {passed:false,reason:'WT products have identical known sequence throughout the shared usable read range',minimumDifferences:0,comparedBases:positions.length,condition:null,warnings:[]as string[]};
 const columns=sites.map(s=>positions.flatMap(p=>'ACGT'.split('').map(b=>Number(s.extension[p]===b)))),geometry=gramCondition(columns);
 if(geometry.rank<sites.length)return {passed:false,reason:'WT mixture contributions are linearly indistinguishable in the shared usable range',minimumDifferences:differences,comparedBases:positions.length,condition:geometry.condition,warnings:[]as string[]};
 const warnings:string[]=[];if(differences<3)warnings.push('Only one or two informative WT positions');if(geometry.condition===null||geometry.condition>100)warnings.push('Poorly conditioned WT mixture');
 return {passed:true,reason:'WT geometry screen passed; future edited mixtures are not guaranteed identifiable',minimumDifferences:differences,comparedBases:positions.length,condition:geometry.condition,warnings};
}

END MODULE


BEGIN MODULE src/app/workers/core/sanger-quantized-peaks.ts
import{rawIntervalScorer}from'./sanger-raw-interval-fit';
import type{SangerLinearOptimizer}from'./sanger-discrete-phase';
import type{SangerTrace}from'../../models/sanger-analysis';
const bases='ACGT';
function linear(A:number[][],b:number[]){const n=b.length,m=A.map((r,i)=>[...r,b[i]]);for(let c=0;c<n;c++){let at=c;for(let j=c+1;j<n;j++)if(Math.abs(m[j][c])>Math.abs(m[at][c]))at=j;if(Math.abs(m[at][c])<1e-10)return null;[m[c],m[at]]=[m[at],m[c]];const v=m[c][c];for(let k=c;k<=n;k++)m[c][k]/=v;for(let j=0;j<n;j++)if(j!==c){const v=m[j][c];for(let k=c;k<=n;k++)m[j][k]-=v*m[c][k];}}return m.map(r=>r[n]);}
/** Learn a Gaussian peak response and its amplitude from measured WT control
 * fluorescence only. Accept this adapter only when the complete control is
 * explained within one ADC count and both traces admit bounded quantization
 * intervals. No generator constants, mutant genotypes or truth are consulted.
 * Unsupported peak shapes retain the general measured-signal adapter. */
export function fitQuantizedPeaks(control:SangerTrace,sample:SangerTrace,seqs:string[],ct:number,st:number,firstCut:number,bg:number[],ebg:number[],optimizer?:SangerLinearOptimizer){
 if(!seqs.length||seqs.length>4||bg.length!==4||ebg.length!==4)return null;
 for(const trace of [control,sample]){if(trace.peaks.length<100)return null;for(let p=1;p<trace.peaks.length;p++)if(trace.peaks[p]-trace.peaks[p-1]<10)return null;}

 const R=4,ds=Array.from({length:9},(_,i)=>i-4),positions=Array.from({length:Math.min(control.peaks.length+ct-3,...seqs.map(s=>s.length-3))-Math.max(10,ct+10)},(_,i)=>i+Math.max(10,ct+10)),H=seqs.length,counts=Array.from({length:H},(_,h)=>seqs.map((s,k)=>positions.reduce((sum,p)=>sum+Number(seqs[h][p]===s[p]),0))),rhs=ds.map(d=>seqs.map(s=>positions.reduce((sum,p)=>sum+control.channels[s[p]as'A'][control.peaks[p-ct]+d]-bg[bases.indexOf(s[p])],0))),yy=positions.reduce((sum,p)=>sum+ds.reduce((v,d)=>v+bases.split('').reduce((n,b,i)=>n+(control.channels[b as'A'][control.peaks[p-ct]+d]-bg[i])**2,0),0),0);
 const evaluate=(sigma:number)=>{const shape=ds.map(d=>Math.exp(-d*d/sigma)),norm=shape.reduce((s,x)=>s+x*x,0),right=seqs.map((_,h)=>shape.reduce((s,x,i)=>s+x*rhs[i][h],0)),c=linear(counts.map(r=>r.map(v=>v*norm)),right);if(!c||c.some(x=>x<=0))return{sigma,c:[],error:Infinity};return{sigma,c,error:yy-c.reduce((s,x,h)=>s+x*right[h],0)};};
 let best=evaluate(.5);for(let sigma=.75;sigma<=16;sigma+=.25){const v=evaluate(sigma);if(v.error<best.error)best=v;}
 let lo=Math.max(.5,best.sigma-.25),hi=Math.min(16,best.sigma+.25);for(let i=0;i<45;i++){const a=lo+(hi-lo)*.381966,b=lo+(hi-lo)*.618034;if(evaluate(a).error<evaluate(b).error)hi=b;else lo=a;}best=evaluate((lo+hi)/2);if(!Number.isFinite(best.error)||best.error< -1e-3)return null;
 // Rounded ADC centers are not the underlying fluorescence. Refine the
 // response on their half-count intervals using WT genotypes only; otherwise
 // the centroid least-squares bias is amplified by downstream deconvolution.
 if(optimizer){const initialAmplitude=best.c.reduce((n,x)=>n+x,0),score=rawIntervalScorer(control,control,seqs,positions,ct,ct,bg,bg,{sigma:best.sigma,amplitude:initialAmplitude,coefficients:best.c},optimizer),fit=score?.(seqs,best.c.map(x=>x/initialAmplitude));if(fit&&!fit.lowerBoundOnly&&fit.violation<1e-7)best={...best,sigma:fit.sigma,c:fit.weights.map(w=>w*fit.amplitude)};}
 const amplitude=best.c.reduce((s,x)=>s+x,0),response=ds.map(d=>amplitude*Math.exp(-d*d/best.sigma));let maxError=0;
 for(const p of positions)for(let b=0;b<4;b++){const mass=seqs.reduce((s,seq,h)=>s+(seq[p]===bases[b]?best.c[h]:0),0);for(const d of ds)maxError=Math.max(maxError,Math.abs(control.channels[bases[b]as'A'][control.peaks[p-ct]+d]-bg[b]-mass*Math.exp(-d*d/best.sigma)));}
 if(maxError>1)return null;
 const extract=(trace:SangerTrace,background:number[])=>{let invalid=0,maxNeeded=0;const vectors=Array.from({length:trace.peaks.length},(_,p)=>{let lower:number[]=[],upper:number[]=[];let epsilon=Math.max(.5,maxError);for(;epsilon<=1.00001;epsilon+=.025){lower=bases.split('').map((b,j)=>Math.max(0,...ds.map((d,k)=>(trace.channels[b as'A'][trace.peaks[p]+d]-background[j]-epsilon)/response[k])));upper=bases.split('').map((b,j)=>Math.min(1,...ds.map((d,k)=>(trace.channels[b as'A'][trace.peaks[p]+d]-background[j]+epsilon)/response[k])));if(lower.every((x,j)=>x<=upper[j])&&lower.reduce((s,x)=>s+x,0)<=1&&upper.reduce((s,x)=>s+x,0)>=1)break;}
  maxNeeded=Math.max(maxNeeded,epsilon);if(epsilon>1.00001){invalid++;return[0,0,0,0];}const mid=lower.map((x,j)=>(x+upper[j])/2);let l=-1,h=1;for(let i=0;i<45;i++){const x=(l+h)/2,total=mid.reduce((s,v,j)=>s+Math.max(lower[j],Math.min(upper[j],v+x)),0);if(total<1)l=x;else h=x;}return mid.map((v,j)=>Math.max(lower[j],Math.min(upper[j],v+(l+h)/2)));});return{vectors,invalid,maxNeeded};};
 const c=extract(control,bg),e=extract(sample,ebg);if(c.invalid||e.invalid)return null;return{control:c,sample:e,calibration:{sigma:best.sigma,amplitude,coefficients:best.c,maxError,rmse:Math.sqrt(best.error/(positions.length*36))}};
}

END MODULE


BEGIN MODULE src/app/workers/core/sanger-raw-interval-fit.ts
import type{SangerTrace}from'../../models/sanger-analysis';
import type{SangerLinearOptimizer}from'./sanger-discrete-phase';
export interface RawIntervalFit{weights:number[];violation:number;amplitude:number;sigma:number;activePositions?:number[];residuals?:{position:number;error:number}[];loss?:number;lowerBoundOnly?:boolean}
export type RawSequenceScorer=((sequences:string[],weights:number[])=>RawIntervalFit|null)&{maximumCentroidMse?:number;boundsFor?:(positions:number[],response:{amplitude:number;sigma:number})=>{lower:number[][];upper:number[][]}};
interface Row{ids:number[];d:number;lo:number;hi:number}
// Math.round bins are [k-.5,k+.5), not closed on both sides. A
// sub-count numerical interior prevents distinct ADC bins sharing one endpoint.
const ADC_UPPER_INTERIOR=1e-5;
/** Only enabled for the separately validated, integer-ADC Gaussian response.
 * Fit fluorescence amplitudes against rounding intervals in BOTH original
 * traces. Width is learned from genotype-independent peak ratios. A sequential
 * linearization optimizes the narrow width interval; every returned objective
 * is recomputed using the nonlinear response, not the LP surrogate. */
export function rawIntervalScorer(control:SangerTrace,sample:SangerTrace,refs:string[],positions:number[],ct:number,st:number,bg:number[],ebg:number[],calibration:{sigma:number;amplitude:number;coefficients:number[]},optimizer:SangerLinearOptimizer,observed?:number[]):RawSequenceScorer|null{
 let lower=1/16,upper=2;
 for(const [trace,background]of [[control,bg],[sample,ebg]]as const)for(const p of trace.peaks)for(let b=0;b<4;b++){
  const center=trace.channels['ACGT'[b]as'A'][p]-background[b];if(center<=.5)continue;
  for(let d=1;d<=4;d++){const side=trace.channels['ACGT'[b]as'A'][p+d]-background[b];if(side<0||!Number.isInteger(trace.channels['ACGT'[b]as'A'][p+d]))return null;lower=Math.max(lower,Math.log((center-.5)/(side+.5))/(d*d));if(side>.5)upper=Math.min(upper,Math.log((center+.5)/(side-.5))/(d*d));}
 }
 if(lower>upper||!Number.isFinite(lower)||!Number.isFinite(upper))return null;
 const rows=(trace:SangerTrace,dict:string[],trim:number,background:number[]):Row[]=>{
  const map=new Map<string,Row>();
  for(const p of positions)for(let b=0;b<4;b++){
   const ids=dict.flatMap((s,i)=>s[p]==='ACGT'[b]?[i]:[]),pattern=ids.join(',');
   for(let d=-4;d<=4;d++){const value=trace.channels['ACGT'[b]as'A'][trace.peaks[p-trim]+d]-background[b],key=pattern+'/'+Math.abs(d),old=map.get(key);if(old){old.lo=Math.max(old.lo,value-.5);old.hi=Math.min(old.hi,value+.5-ADC_UPPER_INTERIOR);}else map.set(key,{ids,d:Math.abs(d),lo:value-.5,hi:value+.5-ADC_UPPER_INTERIOR});}
  }
  return[...map.values()];
 };
 // A raw-compatible unit emission must lie within these conservative bounds,
 // allowing every amplitude/width consistent with the WT and measured shapes.
 // Its distance to the extracted centroid therefore cannot exceed this bound.
 let ampLo=0,ampHi=Infinity;
 for(const p of positions){const same=refs.every(s=>s[p]===refs[0][p]);let lo=0,hi=0;if(same){const value=control.channels[refs[0][p]as'A'][control.peaks[p-ct]]-bg['ACGT'.indexOf(refs[0][p])];lo=value-.5;hi=value+.5-ADC_UPPER_INTERIOR;}else for(let b=0;b<4;b++){const value=control.channels['ACGT'[b]as'A'][control.peaks[p-ct]]-bg[b];lo+=Math.max(0,value-.5);hi+=Math.max(0,value+.5-ADC_UPPER_INTERIOR);}ampLo=Math.max(ampLo,lo);ampHi=Math.min(ampHi,hi);}
 if(!(ampLo>0)||ampLo>ampHi)return null;
 let centroidBound=0;const featureBounds:{value:number;low:number;high:number}[][]=[];
 if(observed){for(let k=0;k<positions.length;k++)for(let b=0;b<4;b++){let lo=0,hi=1;const feature:{value:number;low:number;high:number}[]=[];const p=sample.peaks[positions[k]-st];for(let d=-4;d<=4;d++){const value=sample.channels['ACGT'[b]as'A'][p+d]-ebg[b],responseLo=ampLo*Math.exp(-d*d*upper),responseHi=ampHi*Math.exp(-d*d*lower);feature.push({value,low:responseLo,high:responseHi});lo=Math.max(lo,(value-.5)/responseHi);hi=Math.min(hi,(value+.5-ADC_UPPER_INTERIOR)/responseLo);}if(lo>hi)return null;centroidBound+=Math.max((observed[4*k+b]-lo)**2,(observed[4*k+b]-hi)**2);featureBounds.push(feature);}centroidBound/=observed.length;}
 const controlRows=rows(control,refs,ct,bg);let incumbent=Infinity;
 const score:RawSequenceScorer=(sequences,weights)=>{
  const n=sequences.length,all=[...rows(sample,sequences,st,ebg),...controlRows.map(r=>({...r,ids:r.ids.map(i=>i+n)}))];
  // If an identical emission pattern is observed at incompatible ADC heights,
  // no choice of its weights or Gaussian width can reconcile those measurements.
  // This is a lower bound, not a fabricated fitted solution.
  const bound=all.reduce((v,row)=>Math.max(v,(row.lo-row.hi)/2),0);
  if(bound>incumbent+1e-8)return{weights,violation:bound,amplitude:calibration.amplitude,sigma:calibration.sigma,lowerBoundOnly:true};
  const scale=calibration.amplitude,radius=(upper-lower)/2||1;let inverse=Math.max(lower,Math.min(upper,1/calibration.sigma)),coefficients=[...weights,...calibration.coefficients.map(v=>v/scale)],best:RawIntervalFit|null=null;
  const persistent=optimizer.createModel?.();let basis:unknown;
  const options={output_flag:false,solver:'simplex',primal_feasibility_tolerance:1e-10,dual_feasibility_tolerance:1e-10,time_limit:5};persistent?.options.set(options);
  try{
  for(let step=0;step<5;step++){
   const lines:string[]=[];
   if(!persistent){
    lines.push('Minimize',' obj: error','Subject To',' flux: '+sequences.map((_,i)=>'x'+i).join(' + ')+' - '+refs.map((_,i)=>'x'+(n+i)).join(' - ')+' = 0');let k=0;
   for(const row of all){const shape=Math.exp(-row.d*row.d*inverse),mass=row.ids.reduce((s,i)=>s+coefficients[i],0),derivative=-row.d*row.d*shape*mass*radius,terms=row.ids.map(i=>shape+' x'+i).join(' + '),expression=(terms?terms+' ':'')+(derivative<0?'- ':'+ ')+Math.abs(derivative)+' delta';const error='error';lines.push(' r'+k+++': '+expression+' + '+error+' >= '+(row.lo/scale),' r'+k+++': '+expression+' - '+error+' <= '+(row.hi/scale));}
   lines.push('Bounds',` ${(lower-inverse)/radius} <= delta <= ${(upper-inverse)/radius}`,'End');
   }
   let values:ArrayLike<number>,delta:number;
   if(persistent){
    const count=n+refs.length,dc=count,ec=count+1,numCols=ec+1,starts=[0],indices:number[]=[],matrixValues:number[]=[],rowLower=[0],rowUpper=[0];
    const addRow=(ids:number[],coefficients:number[],lo:number,hi:number)=>{indices.push(...ids);matrixValues.push(...coefficients);starts.push(indices.length);rowLower.push(lo);rowUpper.push(hi);};
    indices.push(...Array.from({length:count},(_,i)=>i));matrixValues.push(...Array.from({length:count},(_,i)=>i<n?1:-1));starts.push(indices.length);
    all.forEach((row,r)=>{const shape=Math.exp(-row.d*row.d*inverse),mass=row.ids.reduce((s,i)=>s+coefficients[i],0),derivative=-row.d*row.d*shape*mass*radius,ids=[...row.ids,dc,ec],v=[...row.ids.map(()=>shape),derivative,1];addRow(ids,v,row.lo/scale,1e30);addRow(ids,[...v.slice(0,-1),-1],-1e30,row.hi/scale);});
    const colLower=Array(numCols).fill(0),colUpper=Array(numCols).fill(1e30);colLower[dc]=(lower-inverse)/radius;colUpper[dc]=(upper-inverse)/radius;
    persistent.passModel({numCols,numRows:rowLower.length,colCost:Array.from({length:numCols},(_,i)=>i>=ec?1:0),colLower,colUpper,rowLower,rowUpper,matrix:{format:'csr',numCols,numRows:rowLower.length,starts,indices,values:matrixValues}});
    if(basis)persistent.setBasis(basis);const solved=persistent.run();if(solved.modelStatus!==7)return best;values=persistent.getSolution().colValue;delta=values[dc];basis=persistent.getBasis();
   }else{const result=optimizer.solve(lines.join('\n'),options);if(result.Status!=='Optimal')return best;values=Array.from({length:n+refs.length},(_,i)=>result.Columns['x'+i]?.Primal||0);delta=result.Columns['delta']?.Primal||0;}
   const next=Array.from({length:n+refs.length},(_,i)=>Math.max(0,values[i])),nextInverse=inverse+delta*radius,amplitude=next.slice(0,n).reduce((s,v)=>s+v,0)*scale;if(!(amplitude>0))return best;
   let violation=0,loss=0;for(const row of all){const prediction=scale*Math.exp(-row.d*row.d*nextInverse)*row.ids.reduce((s,i)=>s+next[i],0),error=Math.max(0,row.lo-prediction,prediction-row.hi);violation=Math.max(violation,error);loss+=error;}
   const fit={weights:next.slice(0,n).map(v=>v*scale/amplitude),violation,loss:violation,amplitude,sigma:1/nextInverse};if(!best||violation<best.loss!)best=fit;
   const widthChange=Math.abs(nextInverse-inverse);inverse=nextInverse;coefficients=next;if(violation<1e-8||widthChange<1e-12)break;
  }
  }finally{persistent?.dispose();}
  if(best){incumbent=Math.min(incumbent,best.loss!);best.activePositions=[];best.residuals=[];
   for(const p of positions){const mass=[0,0,0,0];sequences.forEach((seq,i)=>mass['ACGT'.indexOf(seq[p])]+=best!.weights[i]);let error=0;for(let b=0;b<4;b++)for(let d=-4;d<=4;d++){const measured=sample.channels['ACGT'[b]as'A'][sample.peaks[p-st]+d]-ebg[b],prediction=best.amplitude*mass[b]*Math.exp(-d*d/best.sigma);error=Math.max(error,Math.max(measured-.5-prediction,prediction-measured-.5+ADC_UPPER_INTERIOR));}if(error>Math.max(1e-7,scale*1e-9)){best.activePositions.push(p);best.residuals.push({position:p,error});}}
  }return best;
 };
 score.boundsFor=(at,response)=>{const low:number[][]=[],high:number[][]=[];for(const p of at){const l=[0,0,0,0],u=[1,1,1,1];for(let b=0;b<4;b++)for(let d=-4;d<=4;d++){const shape=response.amplitude*Math.exp(-d*d/response.sigma),value=sample.channels['ACGT'[b]as'A'][sample.peaks[p-st]+d]-ebg[b];l[b]=Math.max(l[b],(value-.5)/shape);u[b]=Math.min(u[b],(value+.5-ADC_UPPER_INTERIOR)/shape);}low.push(l);high.push(u);}return{lower:low,upper:high};};
 // A zero-error box only rejects exact compatibility, not improvements to a
 // still-imperfect incumbent. Expand every ADC interval by the incumbent's
 // maximum error, and permit the corresponding control-amplitude uncertainty.
 // This bound can reject only dictionaries unable to improve that SAME loss.
 let lastError=Infinity,lastBound=Infinity;
 if(observed)Object.defineProperty(score,'maximumCentroidMse',{get:()=>{
  if(!Number.isFinite(incumbent))return Infinity;if(lastError===incumbent)return lastBound;
  lastError=incumbent;const lowAmplitude=ampLo-4*incumbent,highAmplitude=ampHi+4*incumbent;if(lowAmplitude<=0)return lastBound=Infinity;
  let total=0;for(let i=0;i<featureBounds.length;i++){let lo=0,hi=1;for(const f of featureBounds[i]){lo=Math.max(lo,(f.value-.5-incumbent)/(f.high*highAmplitude/ampHi));hi=Math.min(hi,(f.value+.5-ADC_UPPER_INTERIOR+incumbent)/(f.low*lowAmplitude/ampLo));}total+=Math.max((observed[i]-lo)**2,(observed[i]-hi)**2);}return lastBound=total/observed.length;
 }});return score;
}

END MODULE


BEGIN MODULE src/app/workers/core/sanger-sequence-fit-cache.ts
import type{RawSequenceScorer}from './sanger-raw-interval-fit';
import{SangerFitCache}from'./sanger-fit-cache';
import{activeSimplex}from'./simplex-active-set';import type{SimplexFit}from'./sanger-signal';
/** Exact specialization of simplex least squares for unit A/C/G/T emissions.
 * A column is a base per cycle. Its Gram product is the number of agreements
 * minus its two observed-base sums, plus the common observed squared norm.
 * No waveform approximation or biological independence assumption is added. */
export interface SequenceFit extends SimplexFit{selectionLoss?:number;selectionTolerance?:number;rawResponse?:{amplitude:number;sigma:number};rawActivePositions?:number[];rawResiduals?:{position:number;error:number}[]}
export const sequenceFitTolerance=(fit:SequenceFit)=>fit.selectionTolerance??Math.max(1e-15,2*(fit.optimalityGap||0));
export const sequenceFitLoss=(fit:SequenceFit)=>fit.selectionLoss??fit.rmse**2;
export class SangerSequenceFitCache{
 private encoded=new Map<number,Uint8Array>();private observedSum=new Map<number,number>();private gram=new Map<string,number>();private fits=new Map<string,SequenceFit>();private yy:number;private variance:number;
 hits=0;misses=0;linearFits=0;nonlinearFits=0;pairHits=0;pairMisses=0;
 constructor(private sequences:string[],private positions:number[],private observed:number[],private limit=512,private rawScorer?:RawSequenceScorer){if(observed.length!==positions.length*4)throw Error('Invalid unit-sequence regression dimensions.');this.yy=observed.reduce((s,v)=>s+v*v,0);const mean=observed.reduce((s,v)=>s+v,0)/observed.length;this.variance=observed.reduce((s,v)=>s+(v-mean)**2,0);}
 private bases(id:number){let code=this.encoded.get(id);if(code)return code;code=Uint8Array.from(this.positions,p=>{const b='ACGT'.indexOf(this.sequences[id][p]);if(b<0)throw Error('Sequence regression requires complete unit A/C/G/T emissions.');return b;});this.encoded.set(id,code);this.observedSum.set(id,code.reduce((s,b,p)=>s+this.observed[4*p+b],0));return code;}
 private product(a:number,b:number){const key=Math.min(a,b)+'/'+Math.max(a,b),old=this.gram.get(key);if(old!==undefined){this.pairHits++;return old;}this.pairMisses++;const x=this.bases(a),y=this.bases(b);let same=0;for(let p=0;p<x.length;p++)same+=Number(x[p]===y[p]);const v=same-this.observedSum.get(a)!-this.observedSum.get(b)!+this.yy;this.gram.set(key,v);if(this.gram.size>200000)this.gram.delete(this.gram.keys().next().value!);return v;}
 column(id:number){return Array.from(this.bases(id),b=>[0,1,2,3].map(k=>Number(b===k))).flat();}
 predict(ids:number[],weights:number[]){const y=Array(this.observed.length).fill(0);ids.forEach((id,i)=>{const code=this.bases(id);for(let p=0;p<code.length;p++)y[4*p+code[p]]+=weights[i];});return y;}
 fit(ids:number[],initial?:number[]):SequenceFit{const key=ids.join('/'),hit=this.fits.get(key);if(hit){this.hits++;return hit;}this.misses++;this.linearFits++;const G=ids.map((a,i)=>Float64Array.from({length:ids.length},(_,j)=>j<=i?this.product(a,ids[j]):0));for(let i=0;i<ids.length;i++)for(let j=0;j<i;j++)G[j][i]=G[i][j];const solved=activeSimplex(G,this.observed.length,1e-13,initial),codes=ids.map(id=>this.bases(id));let error=0;
 for(let p=0;p<this.positions.length;p++){const v=[0,0,0,0];for(let j=0;j<ids.length;j++)v[codes[j][p]]+=solved.weights[j];for(let b=0;b<4;b++)error+=(v[b]-this.observed[4*p+b])**2;}
 const result:SequenceFit=solved.converged?{weights:solved.weights,rmse:Math.sqrt(error/this.observed.length),r2:this.variance?1-error/this.variance:0,converged:solved.converged,optimalityGap:solved.optimalityGap}:new SangerFitCache(ids.map(id=>this.column(id)),this.observed).fit(ids.map((_,i)=>i));if(this.rawScorer){
 const raw=this.rawScorer.maximumCentroidMse!==undefined&&result.rmse**2>this.rawScorer.maximumCentroidMse+1e-15?null:this.rawScorer(ids.map(id=>this.sequences[id]),result.weights);
 if(raw){let measuredError=0;const prediction=this.predict(ids,raw.weights);for(let i=0;i<prediction.length;i++)measuredError+=(prediction[i]-this.observed[i])**2;result.weights=raw.weights;result.rmse=Math.sqrt(measuredError/this.observed.length);result.r2=this.variance?1-measuredError/this.variance:0;result.selectionLoss=raw.loss??raw.violation;result.selectionTolerance=1e-7;if(!raw.lowerBoundOnly)result.rawResponse={amplitude:raw.amplitude,sigma:raw.sigma};result.rawActivePositions=raw.activePositions;result.rawResiduals=raw.residuals;result.converged=!raw.lowerBoundOnly;result.optimalityGap=undefined;}else{result.converged=false;result.selectionLoss=Infinity;}
 }this.fits.set(key,result);if(this.fits.size>this.limit)this.fits.delete(this.fits.keys().next().value!);return result;}
}

END MODULE


BEGIN MODULE src/app/workers/core/sanger-sequence-group-result.ts
import{SangerAnalysisResult,SangerJointEvent}from'../../models/sanger-analysis';
import{reverseComplement}from'../../utils/biology.utils';
import{rawSignalScore,gramCondition}from'./sanger-signal';
import{plotTrace}from'./sanger-analysis';
import{discoverExactAlternatives}from'./sanger-exact-alternatives';
import{SignalPathInput,Prepared,Model,PathEdit}from'./sanger-path-common';
import{SangerFitCache}from'./sanger-fit-cache';
/** Shared reporting/QC; discovery engines supply only their blind fitted basis. */
export function sequenceGroupResult(state:any):SangerAnalysisResult{
 const{input,refs,positions,report,anchor,end,models,cols,ids,fit,ctlScore,cc,sc,context,cr,sr,rawY,controlRepair,sampleRepair,maxDeletion,maxInsertion,fitCache,y,audit,train,test,tune}=state as {input:SignalPathInput;refs:Prepared[];positions:number[];models:Model[];ids:number[];cols:number[][];fit:any;report:any;anchor:number;end:number;ctlScore:any;cc:any;sc:any;context:any;cr:any;sr:any;rawY:number[];controlRepair:any;sampleRepair:any;maxDeletion:number;maxInsertion:number;fitCache:SangerFitCache;y:number[];audit?: (s:any)=>void;train:number[];test:number[];tune:number[]};
 const s=input.settings,theta=context.theta,iterations=state.iterations??1,alignments=0,discoveryLimited=false,qc=rawSignalScore(ids.map(id=>cols[id]),rawY,fit.weights,sc.gains),full=qc,geometry=gramCondition(ids.map(id=>cols[id])),emit=state.emit as (sequence:string,p:number,lag:number)=>number[];
 const net=(events:PathEdit[])=>events.reduce((sum,e)=>sum+e.inserted.length-(e.end-e.start),0),inputEvents=(h:number,events:PathEdit[]):SangerJointEvent[]=>{const r=refs[h];return events.map(e=>{const distances=r.cuts.map(c=>Math.min(Math.abs(e.start-c),Math.abs(e.end-c))),near=distances.indexOf(Math.min(...distances)),span=r.cuts.length===2&&e.start<=r.cuts[0]&&e.end>=r.cuts[1],target=span?0:Math.min(...distances)>60?-1:(r.reverse?r.cuts.length-near:near+1),a=r.offset+e.start,b=r.offset+e.end;return {target,start:r.reverse?r.input.sequence.length-b:a,end:r.reverse?r.input.sequence.length-a:b,inserted:e.inserted.length,insertedSequence:r.reverse?reverseComplement(e.inserted):e.inserted};}).sort((a,b)=>a.start-b.start);};
 const pathSizes=(h:number,events:PathEdit[])=>{const sizes=Array(refs[h].cuts.length).fill(0);for(const e of inputEvents(h,events)){if(e.target>0)sizes[e.target-1]+=e.inserted-(e.end-e.start);}return sizes;};
 const chosen=ids.flatMap((id,i)=>fit.weights[i]>1e-9?[{model:models[id],fraction:fit.weights[i]}]:[]),aliasKey=(a:{reference:number;events:PathEdit[]})=>a.reference+':'+JSON.stringify(inputEvents(a.reference,a.events));
 const ambiguous=chosen.filter(c=>new Set(c.model.aliases.map(a=>a.reference+':'+net(a.events)+':'+Number(a.events.length===0)+':'+[...new Set(inputEvents(a.reference,a.events).map(e=>e.target))].sort().join('/'))).size>1),refAmbiguous=ambiguous.filter(c=>new Set(c.model.aliases.map(a=>a.reference)).size>1);
 const semantic=(a:{reference:number;events:PathEdit[]})=>a.reference+':'+(a.events.length?'edit':'WT')+':'+net(a.events)+':'+[...new Set(inputEvents(a.reference,a.events).map(e=>e.target))].sort().join('/');
 const attributionAmbiguous=ambiguous.filter(c=>new Set(c.model.aliases.map(semantic)).size>1);
 const groups=chosen.map((c,i)=>({id:`P${i+1}`,reference:refAmbiguous.includes(c)?null:refs[c.model.reference].name,fraction:c.fraction,netSize:new Set(c.model.aliases.map(a=>net(a.events))).size>1?null:net(c.model.events),pathSizes:pathSizes(c.model.reference,c.model.events),events:ambiguous.includes(c)?[]:inputEvents(c.model.reference,c.model.events),alternatives:ambiguous.includes(c)?c.model.aliases.map(a=>({reference:refs[a.reference].name,events:inputEvents(a.reference,a.events)})):[]}));
 const warnings:string[]=['Best-fit sequence groups assume one contiguous edit per target within cut ±3 bp. A single mixed read does not establish unique allele linkage.'];if(s.editStartRadius!==undefined)warnings.push(`Indel initiation constrained to cut ±${s.editStartRadius} bp; off-target-window edits are outside this model.`);if(controlRepair.added+controlRepair.removed+sampleRepair.added+sampleRepair.removed)warnings.push(`Raw-peak-supported cycle metadata repair: control +${controlRepair.added}/-${controlRepair.removed}, sample +${sampleRepair.added}/-${sampleRepair.removed}.`);if(qc.r2<.995)warnings.push('Signal-guided paths failed internal trace reconstruction QC; editing estimates are withheld.');if(ambiguous.length)warnings.push('Some discovered paths have indistinguishable reference or event explanations. Inspect alternatives.');if(discoveryLimited)warnings.push('Path discovery reached its iteration limit.');if(geometry.condition===null||geometry.condition>100)warnings.push('Selected path contributions are poorly conditioned.');
 const boundary=chosen.some(c=>c.model.events.some(e=>e.end-e.start>=maxDeletion||e.inserted.length>=maxInsertion));if(boundary)warnings.push('A supported event reaches the configured path-search boundary.');
 const localResidual=full.positionRmse,unknown=positions.map(p=>chosen.some(c=>c.model.sequence[p]==='N'||c.model.sequence[p-1]==='N')),localDiscordant=localResidual.filter((v,i)=>!unknown[i]&&v>Math.max(.003,2*(ctlScore.positionRmse[i]||0)+.002)).length;
 if(localDiscordant)warnings.push(`${localDiscordant} positions have unexplained local signal (possible substitution, artifact or missing edit path). Editing estimates are withheld.`);
 const unknownInsertion=chosen.some(c=>c.model.sequence.includes('N'));if(unknownInsertion)warnings.push('Inserted bases are unresolved; known-sequence inference cannot certify these target fractions.');
 // Conditioning diagnoses coefficient identifiability, not raw-signal agreement.
 // Preserve its warning and exact alternatives; never call a good trace fit a failed reconstruction solely because its basis is ill-conditioned.
 const allowed=!unknownInsertion&&qc.r2>=.995&&!localDiscordant&&fit.converged&&!discoveryLimited;
 const perRef=refs.map((r,h)=>{const rows=chosen.filter(c=>c.model.reference===h&&!attributionAmbiguous.includes(c)),share=rows.reduce((sum,c)=>sum+c.fraction,0),estimable=allowed&&share>=.02&&!attributionAmbiguous.some(c=>c.model.aliases.some(a=>a.reference===h)),bins=new Map<number,number>();rows.forEach(c=>bins.set(net(c.model.events),(bins.get(net(c.model.events))||0)+c.fraction));const zero=rows.filter(c=>!c.model.events.length).reduce((sum,c)=>sum+c.fraction,0);return {name:r.name,share,wt:estimable?zero/share:null,indel:estimable?1-zero/share:null,inFrame:estimable?rows.filter(c=>c.model.events.length&&net(c.model.events)!==0&&net(c.model.events)%3===0).reduce((sum,c)=>sum+c.fraction,0)/share:null,outOfFrame:estimable?rows.filter(c=>net(c.model.events)%3!==0).reduce((sum,c)=>sum+c.fraction,0)/share:null,components:[...bins].map(([size,fraction])=>({size,fraction:share?fraction/share:0})),estimable,rows};});
 const targets=perRef.flatMap((a,h)=>(refs[h].input.cuts??[refs[h].input.cut]).map((cut,t)=>({reference:a.name,target:t+1,cut,edited:a.estimable?a.rows.filter(c=>inputEvents(h,c.model.events).some(e=>e.target===t+1||e.target===0)).reduce((sum,c)=>sum+c.fraction,0)/a.share:null})));
 const annotations=perRef.map((a,h)=>({sequenceGrouping:true,name:a.name,reference:refs[h].input.sequence,cut:refs[h].input.cut,cuts:refs[h].input.cuts,direction:refs[h].reverse?'reverse' as const:'forward' as const,share:a.share,estimable:a.estimable,components:a.rows.map(c=>({size:net(c.model.events),pathSizes:pathSizes(h,c.model.events),fraction:c.fraction/a.share,events:ambiguous.includes(c)?[]:inputEvents(h,c.model.events),unresolved:ambiguous.includes(c)}))}));
 const exactAlternatives=discoverExactAlternatives(refs,annotations,perRef.map(a=>a.rows),inputEvents,(sequence,p)=>emit(sequence,p,sc.lag));
 const bins=new Map<number,number>();chosen.forEach(c=>bins.set(net(c.model.events),(bins.get(net(c.model.events))||0)+c.fraction));const zero=chosen.filter(c=>!c.model.events.length).reduce((sum,c)=>sum+c.fraction,0);
 const first=Math.min(...refs.flatMap(r=>r.cuts)),plots=[{trace:input.control,trim:cr.trim},{trace:input.sample,trim:sr.trim}].map(({trace,trim})=>plotTrace(trace,first-trim,Math.max(...refs.flatMap(r=>r.cuts))-first+s.window/2));report.positions=positions;report.joint!.candidateCount=models.length;
 if(audit)audit({refs,positions,cols,models,ids,weights:fit.weights,theta,sampleLag:sc.lag,gains:sc.gains,observed:y,fitCache:{hits:fitCache.hits,misses:fitCache.misses,linearFits:fitCache.linearFits,nonlinearFits:fitCache.nonlinearFits,pairHits:fitCache.pairHits,pairMisses:fitCache.pairMisses},rawObserved:rawY,anchor,end,iterations,train,test,tune,controlPositionRmse:ctlScore.positionRmse});
 return {exactAlternatives,method:'pooled',referenceName:refs.length===1?refs[0].name:'Mixed amplicons',sampleName:input.sample.name,controlName:input.control.name,direction:'forward',cutPosition:first,alignmentIdentity:0,alignment:[],variants:[],plots,warnings,synthetic:!!input.synthetic,annotations,diagnostics:{algorithmVersion:state.algorithmVersion||'sanger-sequence-groups-v5-offset',signalModel:'calibrated',requestedMaxIndel:s.maxIndel,discordantPositions:localDiscordant,maxPositionRmse:Math.max(...localResidual),controlCalibration:cc,sampleCalibration:sc,contextEmission:context},mixture:{components:[...bins].map(([size,fraction])=>({size,fraction})),wt:zero,indel:1-zero,inFrame:chosen.filter(c=>c.model.events.length&&net(c.model.events)!==0&&net(c.model.events)%3===0).reduce((sum,c)=>sum+c.fraction,0),outOfFrame:chosen.filter(c=>net(c.model.events)%3!==0).reduce((sum,c)=>sum+c.fraction,0),r2:full.r2,rmse:full.rmse,upstreamError:1-sc.validationR2,bases:positions.length,fitStart:anchor,fitEnd:end,converged:fit.converged,passesChecks:allowed&&!attributionAmbiguous.length},pooled:{preflight:report,controlR2:ctlScore.r2,sampleTrim:sr.trim,controlTrim:cr.trim,amplicons:perRef.map(({rows,estimable,...a})=>a)},joint:{groups,targets,reconstructionR2:qc.r2,heldOutR2:null,selectedModels:chosen.length,candidateModels:models.length,iterations,maxDeletion,ambiguousFraction:ambiguous.reduce((sum,c)=>sum+c.fraction,0),alignmentSearches:alignments}};
}

END MODULE


BEGIN MODULE src/app/workers/core/sanger-signal.ts
import type{SangerLinearOptimizer}from'./sanger-discrete-phase';
import{fitQuantizedPeaks}from'./sanger-quantized-peaks';
import {SangerTrace,TRACE_BASES} from '../../models/sanger-analysis';
export interface PeakSignal {vectors:number[][];strength:number[];noise:number[];areas?:number[][];background?:number[];backgroundSamples?:number[]}
const median=(a:number[])=>{if(!a.length)return 0;const s=[...a].sort((a,b)=>a-b);return s[Math.floor(s.length/2)];};
/** Integrate peaks relative to cycle spacing and subtract a local inter-peak baseline.
 * No sequence identity, allele fraction or fitted variant is used. */
export function integratedPeaks(trace:SangerTrace,fixedBackground?:number[]):PeakSignal{
 const n=trace.peaks.length,mid=TRACE_BASES.map(b=>Array.from({length:Math.max(0,n-1)},(_,i)=>trace.channels[b][Math.round((trace.peaks[i]+trace.peaks[i+1])/2)]));
 const strength:number[]=[],noise:number[]=[],vectors:number[][]=[],areas:number[][]=[];
 for(let i=0;i<n;i++){
  const spacing=Math.min(i?trace.peaks[i]-trace.peaks[i-1]:12,i+1<n?trace.peaks[i+1]-trace.peaks[i]:12),radius=Math.max(2,Math.min(5,Math.floor(spacing*.34)));
  const local=mid.map(values=>values.slice(Math.max(0,i-8),Math.min(values.length,i+8))),baseline=fixedBackground||local.map(median),spread=local.map((v,b)=>1.4826*median(v.map(x=>Math.abs(x-baseline[b]))));
  const values=TRACE_BASES.map((b,j)=>{let v=0,count=0;for(let p=Math.max(0,trace.peaks[i]-radius);p<=Math.min(trace.channels[b].length-1,trace.peaks[i]+radius);p++){v+=Math.max(0,trace.channels[b][p]-baseline[j]);count++;}return v/Math.max(1,count);}),total=values.reduce((a,b)=>a+b,0);
  areas.push(values);vectors.push(values.map(v=>total?v/total:0));strength.push(total);noise.push(spread.reduce((a,b)=>a+b,0));
 }return {vectors,strength,noise,areas};
}
/** Learn background only where every supplied WT sequence has no emitting base
 * in this channel at the current or adjacent cycle. Uses upstream TRAIN rows only,
 * never edited fractions/residuals. Midpoint peak tails are not a background oracle. */
export function referenceBackgroundPeaks(trace:SangerTrace,sequences:string[],trim:number,positions:number[],minimumSamples=8):PeakSignal{
 const raw=integratedPeaks(trace,[0,0,0,0]),training=positions.filter((_,i)=>i%3!==0),samples=TRACE_BASES.map(()=>[] as number[]);
 for(const p of training){const at=p-trim;if(at<1||at>=trace.peaks.length-1)continue;
  TRACE_BASES.forEach((base,b)=>{if(sequences.every(s=>[-1,0,1].every(d=>/[ACGT]/.test(s[p+d]||'')&&s[p+d]!==base)))samples[b].push(raw.areas![at][b]);});
 }
 const background=samples.map(v=>v.length>=minimumSamples?median(v):0),corrected=integratedPeaks(trace,background);
 return {...corrected,background,backgroundSamples:samples.map(v=>v.length)};
}
/** Estimate each trace's baseline only in channels where the submitted WT
 * sequences cannot emit. All control positions are known WT; sample positions
 * are restricted to the unedited prefix. No fitted genotype enters this step. */
export function pairedReferencePeaks(control:SangerTrace,sample:SangerTrace,sequences:string[],controlTrim:number,sampleTrim:number,firstCut:number,optimizer?:SangerLinearOptimizer){
 const end=Math.min(...sequences.map(s=>s.length-2),control.peaks.length+controlTrim-2),known=Array.from({length:Math.max(0,end-Math.max(10,controlTrim+10))},(_,i)=>i+Math.max(10,controlTrim+10)),start=Math.max(10,sampleTrim+10),prefix=Array.from({length:Math.max(0,Math.min(firstCut-13,sample.peaks.length+sampleTrim-2)-start)},(_,i)=>i+start),c=referenceBackgroundPeaks(control,sequences,controlTrim,known),e=referenceBackgroundPeaks(sample,sequences,sampleTrim,prefix,3);
 if(c.backgroundSamples!.some(n=>n<8)||e.backgroundSamples!.some(n=>n<3))return{control:contrastPeaks(control),sample:contrastPeaks(sample),method:'contrast' as const};
 const quantized=fitQuantizedPeaks(control,sample,sequences,controlTrim,sampleTrim,firstCut,c.background!,e.background!,optimizer);
 if(quantized)return{control:{...c,vectors:quantized.control.vectors},sample:{...e,vectors:quantized.sample.vectors},method:'quantized-peak-shape' as const,calibration:quantized.calibration};
 return{control:c,sample:e,method:'reference-background' as const};
}
/** Per-cycle fluorescence contrast. Regress each dye on the summed peak shape,
 * with an intercept, linear background trend and peak derivative as nuisance.
 * Slopes recover channel amplitudes without subtracting inter-peak tails. */
export function contrastPeaks(trace:SangerTrace):PeakSignal{
 const vectors:number[][]=[],strength:number[]=[],noise:number[]=[];
 for(let i=0;i<trace.peaks.length;i++){
  const spacing=Math.min(i?trace.peaks[i]-trace.peaks[i-1]:12,i+1<trace.peaks.length?trace.peaks[i+1]-trace.peaks[i]:12),radius=Math.max(3,Math.min(6,Math.floor(spacing*.4))),center=trace.peaks[i],at=Array.from({length:2*radius+1},(_,k)=>center-radius+k).filter(p=>p>0&&p+1<trace.channels.A.length),n=at.length,coords=at.map(p=>p-center),ys=TRACE_BASES.map(b=>at.map(p=>trace.channels[b][p])),total=at.map((_,k)=>ys.reduce((s,c)=>s+c[k],0)),derivative=at.map(p=>TRACE_BASES.reduce((s,b)=>s+(trace.channels[b][p+1]-trace.channels[b][p-1])/2,0));
  const detrend=(v:number[])=>{const mt=coords.reduce((a,b)=>a+b,0)/n,m=v.reduce((a,b)=>a+b,0)/n,den=coords.reduce((s,t)=>s+(t-mt)**2,0),slope=den?coords.reduce((s,t,k)=>s+(t-mt)*(v[k]-m),0)/den:0;return v.map((x,k)=>x-m-slope*(coords[k]-mt));};
  const x=detrend(total),d=detrend(derivative),xx=x.reduce((s,v)=>s+v*v,0),dd=d.reduce((s,v)=>s+v*v,0),xd=x.reduce((s,v,k)=>s+v*d[k],0),det=xx*dd-xd*xd;
  const slopes=ys.map(v=>{const y=detrend(v),xy=x.reduce((s,v,k)=>s+v*y[k],0),dy=d.reduce((s,v,k)=>s+v*y[k],0);return Math.max(0,det>1e-8?(xy*dd-dy*xd)/det:xx?xy/xx:0);}),sum=slopes.reduce((a,b)=>a+b,0),v=slopes.map(x=>sum?x/sum:0);vectors.push(v);const amp=Math.max(...total)-Math.min(...total);strength.push(amp);
  noise.push(Math.sqrt(ys.reduce((s,y,b)=>{const yy=detrend(y),xy=x.reduce((s,v,k)=>s+v*yy[k],0),dy=d.reduce((s,v,k)=>s+v*yy[k],0),a=det>1e-8?(xy*dd-dy*xd)/det:xx?xy/xx:0,beta=det>1e-8?(dy*xx-xy*xd)/det:0;return s+yy.reduce((s,y,k)=>s+(y-a*x[k]-beta*d[k])**2,0);},0)/Math.max(1,4*(n-4))));
 }return {vectors,strength,noise};
}
/** Position confidence is based on peak signal/background only, not edited residuals. */
export function signalWeights(signals:PeakSignal[],indices:number[][]):number[]{
 return indices[0].map((_,j)=>Math.max(.25,Math.min(...signals.map((s,i)=>{const at=indices[i][j],snr=s.strength[at]/Math.max(1,s.noise[at]);return Math.min(1,snr/30);})))) ;
}
export interface SimplexFit {weights:number[];r2:number;rmse:number;converged:boolean;optimalityGap?:number}
/** Nonnegative, sum-to-one least squares. Scored on ALL unweighted signal rows. */
function denseSimplexFit(columns:number[][],observed:number[],positionWeights?:number[],initialWeights?:number[]):SimplexFit{
 const p=columns.length,n=observed.length;if(!p||!n||columns.some(c=>c.length!==n))throw new Error('Invalid trace regression dimensions.');
 // Subtracting a common per-row vector leaves A*w-y unchanged on the
 // simplex. It removes shared WT signal from the Lipschitz bound rather than
 // letting that uninformative common mode slow homeolog attribution.
 const center=observed.map((_,k)=>columns.reduce((sum,c)=>sum+c[k],0)/p);
 const x=columns.map(c=>c.map((v,k)=>v-center[k])),target=observed.map((v,k)=>v-center[k]);
 const gram=Array.from({length:p},()=>new Float64Array(p)),rhs=new Float64Array(p),weight=(k:number)=>positionWeights?.[Math.floor(k/4)]??1;
 for(let a=0;a<p;a++){for(let k=0;k<n;k++)rhs[a]+=x[a][k]*target[k]*weight(k);for(let b=0;b<=a;b++){let v=0;for(let k=0;k<n;k++)v+=x[a][k]*x[b][k]*weight(k);gram[a][b]=gram[b][a]=v;}}
 const L=Math.max(...gram.map(row=>row.reduce((sum,v)=>sum+Math.abs(v),0)));
 if(!L){const w=columns.map(()=>1/p);return {...scoreMixture(columns,observed,w),weights:w,converged:true,optimalityGap:0};}
 const gap=(w:number[])=>{const g=Array.from({length:p},(_,a)=>gram[a].reduce((sum,v,b)=>sum+v*w[b],0)-rhs[a]);return Math.max(0,w.reduce((sum,v,a)=>sum+v*g[a],0)-Math.min(...g))/n;};
 const initial=initialWeights?.length===p&&initialWeights.every(v=>Number.isFinite(v)&&v>=0)&&initialWeights.some(v=>v>0)?initialWeights:undefined,total=initial?.reduce((s,v)=>s+v,0)||1;
 let w=initial?initial.map(v=>v/total):Array(p).fill(1/p),converged=false;
 if(gap(w)<=1e-10)return {...scoreMixture(columns,observed,w),weights:w,converged:true,optimalityGap:gap(w)};
 // Pairwise exact line minimization on the simplex. Incremental gradients
 // avoid a dense matrix-vector product at every step in nearly aliased fits.
 let gradient=Array.from({length:p},(_,a)=>gram[a].reduce((sum,v,b)=>sum+v*w[b],0)-rhs[a]);
 for(let step=0;step<200000;step++){
  if(step%100===0)gradient=Array.from({length:p},(_,a)=>gram[a].reduce((sum,v,b)=>sum+v*w[b],0)-rhs[a]);
  let enter=0,leave=-1,lambda=0;for(let a=0;a<p;a++){lambda+=w[a]*gradient[a];if(gradient[a]<gradient[enter])enter=a;if(w[a]>0&&(leave<0||gradient[a]>gradient[leave]))leave=a;}
  if((lambda-gradient[enter])/n<=1e-10){converged=true;break;}
  if(leave<0||leave===enter)break;
  const curvature=gram[leave][leave]+gram[enter][enter]-2*gram[leave][enter],amount=Math.min(w[leave],curvature>0?(gradient[leave]-gradient[enter])/curvature:w[leave]);
  if(!(amount>0))break;w[leave]-=amount;w[enter]+=amount;
  for(let a=0;a<p;a++)gradient[a]+=amount*(gram[a][enter]-gram[a][leave]);
 }
 // The simplex dual gap bounds remaining quadratic objective error; it is
 // separate from signal-fit quality and from reference identifiability.
 const optimalityGap=gap(w);converged=converged||optimalityGap<=1e-10;
 return {...scoreMixture(columns,observed,w),weights:w,converged,optimalityGap};
}
/** Numerical identity on every observed emission coordinate. Fingerprints only
 * accelerate lookup; a full coordinate comparison verifies every equivalence. */
export function emissionFamilies(columns:number[][],tolerance=1e-12):number[][] {
 const buckets=new Map<string,number[][]>(),groups:number[][]=[];
 columns.forEach((column,i)=>{
  const step=Math.max(1,Math.floor(column.length/24)),key=Array.from({length:Math.ceil(column.length/step)},(_,j)=>Math.round(column[j*step]*Math.min(1e6,1/(tolerance*1000)))).join(','),candidates=buckets.get(key)||[];
  const match=candidates.find(group=>column.every((v,k)=>Math.abs(v-columns[group[0]][k])<=tolerance));
  if(match)match.push(i);else{const group=[i];candidates.push(group);groups.push(group);buckets.set(key,candidates);}
 });return groups;
}
/** Working-set simplex solver for large candidate families. Every candidate is
 * tested against the full residual at each step; the global simplex dual gap
 * certifies completion. This changes computation, not the fitted objective. */
export function simplexFit(columns:number[][],observed:number[],positionWeights?:number[],initialWeights?:number[]):SimplexFit {
 if(columns.length<128)return denseSimplexFit(columns,observed,positionWeights,initialWeights);
 const n=observed.length,p=columns.length;if(!n||columns.some(c=>c.length!==n))throw Error('Invalid trace regression dimensions.');
 const weight=(k:number)=>positionWeights?.[Math.floor(k/4)]??1;
 const costs=columns.map(c=>c.reduce((s,v,k)=>s+weight(k)*(v*v-2*v*observed[k]),0));
 let active=initialWeights?.length===p?initialWeights.flatMap((v,i)=>v>1e-10?[i]:[]):[];if(!active.length)active=[costs.indexOf(Math.min(...costs))];
 let previous=initialWeights;
 for(let step=0;step<p;step++){
  const fit=denseSimplexFit(active.map(i=>columns[i]),observed,positionWeights,previous&&active.map(i=>previous![i])),residual=observed.map(v=>-v);
  active.forEach((id,j)=>{if(fit.weights[j])for(let k=0;k<n;k++)residual[k]+=fit.weights[j]*columns[id][k];});
  const gradient=columns.map(c=>c.reduce((s,v,k)=>s+weight(k)*v*residual[k],0)),lambda=active.reduce((s,id,j)=>s+fit.weights[j]*gradient[id],0),optimalityGap=Math.max(0,lambda-Math.min(...gradient))/n;
  const weights=Array(p).fill(0);active.forEach((id,j)=>weights[id]=fit.weights[j]);
  if(optimalityGap<=1e-10)return {...scoreMixture(columns,observed,weights),weights,converged:true,optimalityGap};
  const included=new Set(active),add=gradient.map((value,id)=>({value,id})).filter(x=>!included.has(x.id)&&x.value<lambda-n*1e-10).sort((a,b)=>a.value-b.value).slice(0,4);
  // An active-set convergence stall cannot be resolved by allocating the
  // entire quadratic dictionary matrix. Preserve the actual measured gap.
  if(!add.length)return {...scoreMixture(columns,observed,weights),weights,converged:fit.converged&&optimalityGap<=1e-10,optimalityGap};
  previous=weights;active=active.filter((_,j)=>fit.weights[j]>1e-12).concat(add.map(x=>x.id));
 }
 // Degenerate or unusually dense solutions retain the original full solver.
 return denseSimplexFit(columns,observed,positionWeights,previous);
}
export function scoreMixture(columns:number[][],observed:number[],weights:number[]):{r2:number;rmse:number}{const mean=observed.reduce((a,b)=>a+b,0)/observed.length;let error=0,variance=0;for(let k=0;k<observed.length;k++){let value=0;for(let a=0;a<columns.length;a++)value+=columns[a][k]*weights[a];error+=(observed[k]-value)**2;variance+=(observed[k]-mean)**2;}return {r2:variance>1e-12?1-error/variance:0,rmse:Math.sqrt(error/observed.length)};}
const normalize=(v:number[])=>{const sum=v.reduce((a,b)=>a+b,0);return v.map(x=>sum?x/sum:0);};
export function correctGain(v:number[],gains:number[]){return normalize(v.map((x,b)=>x/gains[b]));}
/** Signed relative phase: positive = previous cycle, negative = next cycle.
 * Both are convex fluorescence mixtures, never negative-intensity subtraction. */
export function phaseNeighbor(p:number,lag:number):number{return p+(lag<0?1:-1);}
export function mixPhase(current:number[],neighbor:number[],lag:number):number[]{const a=Math.abs(lag);return current.map((v,b)=>(1-a)*v+a*(neighbor[b]||0));}
export function phaseVector(sequence:string,p:number,lag:number):number[]{return mixPhase([...TRACE_BASES].map(b=>Number(sequence[p]===b)),[...TRACE_BASES].map(b=>Number(sequence[phaseNeighbor(p,lag)]===b)),lag);}
export interface SignalCalibration {gains:number[];lag:number;used:boolean;trainingBases:number;validationBases:number;rawValidationR2:number;validationR2:number;weights:number[]}
/** Learn only from PRE-CUT WT positions. Every third position is held out.
 * Four bounded gains + one lag parameter; no edited positions or truth labels. */
export function calibrateSignal(observed:number[][],positions:number[],templates:number[][][],precision:'standard'|'fine'='standard'):SignalCalibration{
 const train=positions.filter((_,i)=>i%3!==0),valid=positions.filter((_,i)=>i%3===0),make=(rows:number[],lag:number)=>templates.map(t=>rows.flatMap(p=>mixPhase(t[p],t[phaseNeighbor(p,lag)]||[0,0,0,0],lag)));
 const evaluate=(logs:number[],lag:number)=>{const avg=logs.reduce((a,b)=>a+b,0)/4,gains=logs.map(x=>Math.exp(x-avg)),columns=make(train,lag),y=train.flatMap(p=>correctGain(observed[p],gains)),fit=simplexFit(columns,y);
  const rawPred=(rows:number[])=>rows.flatMap(p=>normalize(Array.from({length:4},(_,b)=>gains[b]*templates.reduce((s,t,h)=>s+fit.weights[h]*((1-Math.abs(lag))*t[p][b]+Math.abs(lag)*(t[phaseNeighbor(p,lag)]?.[b]||0)),0))));
  const score=(rows:number[])=>{const pred=rawPred(rows),truth=rows.flatMap(p=>observed[p]),mean=truth.reduce((a,b)=>a+b,0)/truth.length;let error=0,variance=0;for(let k=0;k<truth.length;k++){error+=(truth[k]-pred[k])**2;variance+=(truth[k]-mean)**2;}return {mse:error/truth.length,r2:variance?1-error/variance:0};};
  return {logs:logs.map(x=>x-avg),lag,gains,weights:fit.weights,training:score(train),validation:score(valid)};
 };
 const identity=evaluate([0,0,0,0],0);if(train.length<40||valid.length<20)return {gains:[1,1,1,1],lag:0,used:false,trainingBases:train.length,validationBases:valid.length,rawValidationR2:identity.validation.r2,validationR2:identity.validation.r2,weights:identity.weights};
 let best=identity;const objective=(r:ReturnType<typeof evaluate>)=>r.training.mse+.0001*r.logs.reduce((s,v)=>s+v*v,0);
 for(const lag of [-.2,-.175,-.15,-.125,-.1,-.075,-.05,-.025,0,.025,.05,.075,.1,.125,.15,.175,.2]){const trial=evaluate(best.logs,lag);if(objective(trial)<objective(best))best=trial;}
 for(const step of (precision==='fine'?[.2,.1,.05,.025,.0125,.00625,.003125,.0015625]:[.2,.1,.05,.025]))for(let round=0;round<2;round++){
  for(let b=0;b<4;b++)for(const direction of [-1,1]){const logs=[...best.logs];logs[b]+=step*direction;const avg=logs.reduce((a,b)=>a+b,0)/4;if(Math.max(...logs.map(x=>x-avg))>Math.log(1.8)||Math.min(...logs.map(x=>x-avg))<Math.log(.55))continue;const trial=evaluate(logs,best.lag);if(objective(trial)<objective(best))best=trial;}
  for(const direction of [-1,1]){const lag=best.lag+direction*(precision==='fine'?Math.min(.0125,step/2):.0125);if(lag<-.2||lag>.2)continue;const trial=evaluate(best.logs,lag);if(objective(trial)<objective(best))best=trial;}
 }
 const used=best.validation.mse<identity.validation.mse*.95&&best.validation.mse+1e-5<identity.validation.mse;const chosen=used?best:identity;
 return {gains:chosen.gains,lag:chosen.lag,used,trainingBases:train.length,validationBases:valid.length,rawValidationR2:identity.validation.r2,validationR2:chosen.validation.r2,weights:chosen.weights};
}

export function gramCondition(columns:number[][]):{rank:number;condition:number|null} {
  const n=columns.length;if(!n)return {rank:0,condition:null};
  const a=Array.from({length:n},()=>new Float64Array(n));
  const norms=columns.map(c=>Math.sqrt(c.reduce((s,v)=>s+v*v,0)));
  for(let i=0;i<n;i++)for(let j=0;j<=i;j++){let sum=0;for(let k=0;k<columns[i].length;k++)sum+=columns[i][k]*columns[j][k];a[i][j]=a[j][i]=norms[i]&&norms[j]?sum/(norms[i]*norms[j]):0;}
  for(let sweep=0;sweep<35;sweep++) {
    let largest=0;
    for(let p=0;p<n;p++)for(let q=p+1;q<n;q++) {
      const off=a[p][q];largest=Math.max(largest,Math.abs(off));if(Math.abs(off)<1e-11)continue;
      const tau=(a[q][q]-a[p][p])/(2*off),t=(tau>=0?1:-1)/(Math.abs(tau)+Math.sqrt(1+tau*tau)),c=1/Math.sqrt(1+t*t),s=t*c;
      const pp=a[p][p],qq=a[q][q];a[p][p]=pp-t*off;a[q][q]=qq+t*off;a[p][q]=a[q][p]=0;
      for(let k=0;k<n;k++)if(k!==p&&k!==q){const kp=a[k][p],kq=a[k][q];a[k][p]=a[p][k]=c*kp-s*kq;a[k][q]=a[q][k]=s*kp+c*kq;}
    }
    if(largest<1e-9)break;
  }
  const values=a.map((row,i)=>Math.max(0,row[i])),max=Math.max(...values),min=Math.min(...values),rank=values.filter(v=>v>max*1e-8).length;
  return {rank,condition:min>max*1e-8?Math.sqrt(max/min):null};
}

/** Mask isolated poor control calls without erasing every neighbouring model row.
 * Requires a quality-passing central call and ≥80% passing shifted donors.
 * Not applicable to a pooled-control consensus: mixed peaks can lower Phred. */
export function shiftedControlQuality(quality:Uint8Array|null,center:number,radius:number,minimum:number,forwardRadius=radius):number{
 if(!quality)return 1;
 if(center-radius<0||center+forwardRadius>=quality.length||quality[center]<minimum)return 0;
 let passed=0;for(let p=center-radius;p<=center+forwardRadius;p++)if(quality[p]>=minimum)passed++;
 const fraction=passed/(radius+forwardRadius+1);return fraction>=.8?fraction:0;
}
/** Score normalized emitted signal in the original dye space, without quality
 * weighting. Shared by single and pooled QC; fractions are never altered here. */
export function rawSignalScore(columns:number[][],observed:number[],weights:number[],gains:number[]){
 const mean=observed.reduce((a,b)=>a+b,0)/observed.length;let error=0,variance=0;const positionRmse:number[]=[];
 for(let j=0;j<observed.length/4;j++){let localError=0;const predicted=Array.from({length:4},(_,b)=>gains[b]*columns.reduce((sum,c,h)=>sum+weights[h]*c[j*4+b],0)),total=predicted.reduce((a,b)=>a+b,0);for(let b=0;b<4;b++){const value=observed[j*4+b],residual=(value-(total?predicted[b]/total:0))**2;error+=residual;localError+=residual;variance+=(value-mean)**2;}positionRmse.push(Math.sqrt(localError/4));}
 return {r2:variance>1e-12?1-error/variance:0,rmse:Math.sqrt(error/observed.length),positionRmse};
}

END MODULE


BEGIN MODULE src/app/workers/core/sanger-single-fit.ts
import {SimplexFit,simplexFit,scoreMixture} from './sanger-signal';
const median=(values:number[])=>{const s=[...values].sort((a,b)=>a-b);return s.length?s[Math.floor(s.length/2)]:0;};
export function upstreamNoiseScale(residuals:number[]):number {const center=median(residuals);return Math.max(1e-5,1.4826*median(residuals.map(v=>Math.abs(v-center))));}
/** Positive sparse regression on contrasts to the WT template. The column penalty
 * is standardized by measured contrast norm, so a SNP is not penalized merely
 * because it changes fewer cycles than a long indel. Noise comes from unchanged
 * upstream observations, never the expected edit or a comparator's answer. */
export function fitSparseSingle(columns:number[][],observed:number[],wtIndex:number,sigma:number,positionWeights?:number[]):SimplexFit {
 const p=columns.length,n=observed.length;if(!p||wtIndex<0||wtIndex>=p||columns.some(c=>c.length!==n)||!Number.isFinite(sigma)||sigma<0)throw Error('Invalid single-trace sparse fit.');
 if(p===1)return {weights:[1],...scoreMixture(columns,observed,[1]),converged:true};
 const ids=columns.flatMap((_,i)=>i===wtIndex?[]:[i]),base=columns[wtIndex],d=ids.map(i=>columns[i].map((x,k)=>x-base[k])),y=observed.map((x,k)=>x-base[k]),q=d.length,gram=Array.from({length:q},()=>new Float64Array(q)),rhs=new Float64Array(q),weight=(k:number)=>positionWeights?.[Math.floor(k/4)]??1;
 for(let a=0;a<q;a++){for(let k=0;k<n;k++)rhs[a]+=weight(k)*d[a][k]*y[k];for(let b=0;b<=a;b++){let value=0;for(let k=0;k<n;k++)value+=weight(k)*d[a][k]*d[b][k];gram[a][b]=gram[b][a]=value;}}
 const penalty=Array.from({length:q},(_,i)=>sigma*Math.sqrt(2*Math.log(Math.max(3,p)))*Math.sqrt(Math.max(0,gram[i][i]))),L=Math.max(...gram.map(r=>r.reduce((s,x)=>s+Math.abs(x),0)));
 if(!L)return {weights:columns.map((_,i)=>Number(i===wtIndex)),...scoreMixture(columns,observed,columns.map((_,i)=>Number(i===wtIndex))),converged:true};
 const project=(v:number[])=>{const nonnegative=v.map(x=>Math.max(0,x));if(nonnegative.reduce((s,x)=>s+x,0)<=1)return nonnegative;const sorted=[...v].sort((a,b)=>b-a);let sum=0,threshold=0;for(let k=0;k<sorted.length;k++){sum+=sorted[k];threshold=(sum-1)/(k+1);if(k===sorted.length-1||sorted[k+1]<=threshold)break;}return v.map(x=>Math.max(0,x-threshold));};
 let w=Array(q).fill(0),z=[...w],t=1,converged=false;
 for(let step=0;step<5000;step++){const next=project(z.map((v,a)=>{let g=penalty[a]-rhs[a];for(let b=0;b<q;b++)g+=gram[a][b]*z[b];return v-g/L;})),delta=Math.max(...next.map((x,i)=>Math.abs(x-w[i]))),nt=(1+Math.sqrt(1+4*t*t))/2;z=next.map((x,i)=>x+(t-1)/nt*(x-w[i]));w=next;t=nt;if(step>30&&delta<1e-9){converged=true;break;}}
 const keep=[wtIndex,...ids.filter((_,i)=>w[i]>1e-8)];const refit=simplexFit(keep.map(i=>columns[i]),observed,positionWeights),weights=Array(p).fill(0);keep.forEach((id,i)=>weights[id]=refit.weights[i]);return {weights,r2:refit.r2,rmse:refit.rmse,converged:converged&&refit.converged};
}

END MODULE


BEGIN MODULE src/app/workers/core/sanger-single-junction.ts
import {fitControlTransfer} from './sanger-control-transfer';
import {refineJunctionSupport,rejectNonpredictiveJunctions} from './sanger-junction-support';
import {fitControlResponse,controlContextEmission} from './sanger-control-response';
import {integratedPeaks,calibrateSignal,correctGain} from './sanger-signal';
import {junctionPredictionEvidence} from './sanger-junction-evidence';
import {emissionGroupSupport,fitEmissionMixture} from './sanger-emission';
import {fitJunctionMixture,junctionAnnotationComponents,aliasedDeletionMass} from './sanger-junction-mixture';
import {upstreamNoiseScale} from './sanger-single-fit';
import {signalWeights,rawSignalScore,gramCondition} from './sanger-signal';
import {singleResidualSupport,controlTemplateReliability} from './sanger-single-quality';
import {SangerAnalysisResult,SangerAnalysisSettings,SangerTrace} from '../../models/sanger-analysis';
import {matchedSingleSignals} from './sanger-single-signals';
import {reverseComplement} from '../../utils/biology.utils';

interface Input {base:SangerAnalysisResult;reference:string;cutIndex:number;referenceOrigin:number;control:SangerTrace;sample:SangerTrace;settings:SangerAnalysisSettings;offset:number;upstreamRows:number[];matched:ReturnType<typeof matchedSingleSignals>;maxDeletion:number;maxInsertion:number;plots:SangerAnalysisResult['plots']}
/** Single-reference adapter to the same full-junction model used for homeologs. */
export function analyzeSingleJunction(input:Input):SangerAnalysisResult {
 const {base,reference,cutIndex,referenceOrigin,control,sample,settings:s,offset,upstreamRows,matched,maxDeletion,maxInsertion}=input;
 let {wt,edited,raw,calibration,controlSignal,sampleSignal,upstreamError}=matched;
 const prefix=s.analysisPolicy==='automatic'?Math.min(Math.max(0,referenceOrigin),Math.max(0,offset)):0,fitCut=cutIndex+prefix;
 const start=Math.max(s.analysisPolicy==='automatic'?0:1,s.readStart,-offset+prefix),end=Math.min(s.readEnd,edited.length-offset+prefix),positions=Array.from({length:Math.max(0,end-start)},(_,j)=>j+start).filter(p=>edited[p-prefix+offset]?.some(v=>v>0));
 if(positions.length<60)throw new Error('Too few measured sample cycles for trace decomposition.');
 const known=referenceOrigin-prefix>=0?reference.slice(referenceOrigin-prefix):'N'.repeat(prefix-referenceOrigin)+reference;
 const controlArea=integratedPeaks(control,[0,0,0,0]),sampleArea=integratedPeaks(sample,[0,0,0,0]);
 const controlRows=Array.from({length:controlArea.vectors.length},(_,i)=>i+prefix).filter(p=>p>prefix+1&&p<known.length-2);
 const areaAxis=Array.from({length:controlArea.vectors.length+prefix},(_,p)=>controlArea.areas![p-prefix]||[0,0,0,0]);
 const contextResponse=fitControlResponse([known],controlRows,areaAxis,[1]);
 const expected=Array.from({length:controlArea.vectors.length},(_,i)=>{const v=controlContextEmission(known,i+prefix,0,contextResponse),sum=v.reduce((s,x)=>s+x,0);return v.map(x=>sum?x/sum:0);});
 calibration=calibrateSignal(expected.map((_,i)=>sampleArea.vectors[i+offset]||[0,0,0,0]),upstreamRows,[expected],'fine');
 const sampleAreas=Array.from({length:sampleArea.vectors.length+prefix-offset},(_,p)=>sampleArea.areas![p-prefix+offset]||[0,0,0,0]),transfer=fitControlTransfer([known],upstreamRows.map(i=>i+prefix),sampleAreas,contextResponse,calibration.lag,calibration.gains,[1]);
 if(transfer.used)calibration={...calibration,gains:transfer.gains,weights:transfer.shares,used:true,validationR2:transfer.colourR2,trainingBases:transfer.trainingBases,validationBases:transfer.validationBases};
 wt=expected;raw=sampleArea.vectors;edited=raw.map(v=>correctGain(v,calibration.gains));controlSignal=controlArea;sampleSignal=sampleArea;
 upstreamError=upstreamRows.reduce((sum,i)=>sum+wt[i].reduce((s,v,b)=>s+Math.abs(v-edited[i+offset][b]),0)/2,0)/upstreamRows.length;
 const fitReferences=[{sequence:known,cut:fitCut,contextResponse}];
 const qualityPositions=positions.filter(p=>p>=prefix&&p-prefix<wt.length);
 const measuredWeights=signalWeights([controlSignal,sampleSignal],[qualityPositions.map(p=>p-prefix),qualityPositions.map(p=>p-prefix+offset)]);
 const weightMap=new Map(qualityPositions.map((p,i)=>[p,measuredWeights[i]]));
 let reliability=positions.map(p=>weightMap.get(p)??signalWeights([sampleSignal],[[p-prefix+offset]])[0]);
 const sigma=upstreamNoiseScale(upstreamRows.flatMap(i=>wt[i].map((v,b)=>edited[i+offset][b]-v)));
 const templateWeights=controlTemplateReliability(control.quality,positions.map(p=>p-prefix),sigma);
 reliability=reliability.map((v,i)=>v*templateWeights[i]);
 const observed=positions.flatMap(p=>edited[p-prefix+offset]);
 const fitInput={references:fitReferences,positions,observed,maxDeletion,maxInsertion,radius:s.editStartRadius??5,lag:calibration.lag,theta:Array(8).fill(0),positionWeights:reliability,fit:fitEmissionMixture};
 const junction=fitJunctionMixture(fitInput);
 const unprunedWeights=[...junction.weights];
 const templateResidual=positions.flatMap(p=>controlArea.vectors[p-prefix]?controlArea.vectors[p-prefix].map((v,b)=>v-wt[p-prefix][b]):[0,0,0,0]);
 const support=refineJunctionSupport(fitInput,junction,templateResidual);
 junction.weights=support.fit.weights;
 junction.converged=junction.converged&&support.fit.converged;
 const predictive=junctionPredictionEvidence({references:fitReferences,positions,observed,maxDeletion,maxInsertion,radius:s.editStartRadius??5,lag:calibration.lag,theta:Array(8).fill(0),positionWeights:reliability},junction,junction.models.flatMap((m,i)=>m.size&&junction.weights[i]>1e-8?[i]:[]));
 const predictionRemoved=rejectNonpredictiveJunctions(fitInput,junction,predictive,support.referenceScores,support.scores);support.removed.push(...predictionRemoved.filter(i=>!support.removed.includes(i)));
 const score=rawSignalScore(junction.columns,positions.flatMap(p=>raw[p-prefix+offset]),junction.weights,calibration.gains),components=junction.models.map((m,i)=>({size:m.size,fraction:junction.weights[i]})),zero=components.find(c=>c.size===0)!.fraction;
 const halves=[0,1].map(parity=>{const ids=positions.flatMap((_,i)=>i%2===parity?[i]:[]);return fitEmissionMixture(junction.columns.map(c=>ids.flatMap(i=>c.slice(4*i,4*i+4))),ids.flatMap(i=>observed.slice(4*i,4*i+4)),ids.map(i=>reliability[i])).weights;});
 const splitStabilityPp=50*halves[0].reduce((sum,v,i)=>sum+Math.abs(v-halves[1][i]),0),discordantPositions=score.positionRmse.filter(v=>v>.2).length;
 const active=junction.columns.filter((_,i)=>Math.max(junction.weights[i],unprunedWeights[i])>.01),geometry=gramCondition(active),geometryPass=geometry.rank===active.length&&geometry.condition!==null&&geometry.condition<=100&&!junction.duplicateLengths&&aliasedDeletionMass(fitReferences,junction.models,junction.weights,positions,maxDeletion,s.editStartRadius??5)<=.01;
 let rangeSuggestion:{maxIndel:number;validationR2Gain:number;outsideFraction:number}|undefined;
 if(s.signalModel!=='classic'&&s.checkWiderRange!==false&&!s.largeDeletions&&s.maxIndel<30){
  const train=positions.filter((_,i)=>i%4!==0),valid=positions.filter((_,i)=>i%4===0),range=Math.min(30,s.maxIndel+10);
  const search=(deletion:number,insertion:number)=>fitJunctionMixture({references:fitReferences,positions:train,observed:train.flatMap(p=>edited[p-prefix+offset]),maxDeletion:deletion,maxInsertion:insertion,radius:s.editStartRadius??5,lag:calibration.lag,theta:Array(8).fill(0),positionWeights:train.map(p=>reliability[positions.indexOf(p)]),fit:fitEmissionMixture});
  const small=search(maxDeletion,maxInsertion),wide=search(range,range),validation=valid.flatMap(p=>raw[p-prefix+offset]),a=rawSignalScore(small.predictColumns(valid),validation,small.weights,calibration.gains),b=rawSignalScore(wide.predictColumns(valid),validation,wide.weights,calibration.gains),outside=wide.weights.reduce((sum,v,i)=>sum+(Math.abs(wide.models[i].size)>s.maxIndel?v:0),0);
  if(outside>=.03&&b.r2>=a.r2+.01&&b.rmse<a.rmse*.85)rangeSuggestion={maxIndel:range,validationR2Gain:b.r2-a.r2,outsideFraction:outside};
 }
 const warnings=base.warnings;
 const qualityRows=positions.filter(p=>p>=prefix&&p-prefix<control.peaks.length),lowQuality=!!control.quality?.some(q=>q>0)&&qualityRows.filter(p=>control.quality![p-prefix]<s.controlPhred).length>qualityRows.length*.2;
 if(lowQuality)warnings.push('Many WT-control quality values are below the requested Phred threshold. Measured cycles are retained; interpret the estimates with caution.');
 if(!geometryPass)warnings.push('Some fitted length models are indistinguishable or poorly conditioned. Estimates are shown; review reference ambiguity.');
 if(score.r2<.9)warnings.push('Fit R² is below0.90. Review the fitted estimates.');
 if(upstreamError>.1)warnings.push('Control/sample upstream signal disagreement exceeds10%.');
 if(splitStabilityPp>10)warnings.push('Net-size spectra differ by more than10pp between split-position fits.');
 if(discordantPositions)warnings.push(`${discordantPositions} measured positions have unexplained fluorescence.`);
 if(calibration.validationR2<.8)warnings.push('Upstream calibration validation R² is below0.80.');
 if(rangeSuggestion)warnings.push(`A wider ±${rangeSuggestion.maxIndel}bp model improves fit; consider recalculating.`);
 const edge=components.filter(c=>c.size===-maxDeletion||c.size===maxInsertion).reduce((sum,c)=>sum+c.fraction,0);if(edge>.05)warnings.push('More than5% of fitted signal is at the requested range boundary.');
 const extrapolated=positions.some(p=>p>contextResponse.end);if(extrapolated)warnings.push('Some sample cycles extend beyond the measured control calibration. Learned WT responses are used; all measured sample cycles are retained.');
 const passesChecks=(s.analysisPolicy!=='automatic'||edge<=.05)&&!lowQuality&&geometryPass&&score.r2>=.9&&upstreamError<=.1&&junction.converged&&calibration.validationR2>=.8&&splitStabilityPp<=10&&singleResidualSupport(score.positionRmse).passes&&!rangeSuggestion&&(!s.largeDeletions||(components[0].fraction<=.05));
 base.mixture={components,wt:zero,indel:1-zero,inFrame:components.filter(c=>c.size!==0&&c.size%3===0).reduce((sum,c)=>sum+c.fraction,0),outOfFrame:components.filter(c=>c.size%3!==0).reduce((sum,c)=>sum+c.fraction,0),r2:score.r2,rmse:score.rmse,upstreamError,bases:positions.length,fitStart:start-fitCut,fitEnd:end-fitCut,converged:junction.converged,passesChecks};
 base.diagnostics={algorithmVersion:s.signalModel==='classic'?'sanger-junction-classic-v1':'sanger-junction-v2',signalModel:s.signalModel||'calibrated',requestedMaxIndel:s.maxIndel,modeledRange:{maxDeletion,maxInsertion},sampleCalibration:calibration,supportScreen:{predictionRemoved:predictionRemoved.map(i=>({reference:0,size:junction.models[i].size,foldGains:predictive.get(i)!.foldGains})),removed:support.removed.map(i=>({reference:0,size:junction.models[i].size})),threshold:support.threshold,unpruned:junction.models.map((m,i)=>({reference:0,size:m.size,jointFraction:unprunedWeights[i]})),evidence:(support.scores||[]).map(e=>({...e,reference:0,size:junction.models[e.group[0]].size}))},splitStabilityPp,discordantPositions,maxPositionRmse:Math.max(...score.positionRmse),rangeSuggestion,controlResponse:contextResponse,controlTransfer:transfer,junctionFit:{positions,iterations:junction.iterations,duplicateLengths:junction.duplicateLengths,models:junction.models.map((m,i)=>({...m,fraction:junction.weights[i]}))},background:{method:'local-peak-area',control:{levels:controlSignal.background||[],trainingSamples:controlSignal.backgroundSamples||[]},sample:{levels:sampleSignal.background||[],trainingSamples:sampleSignal.backgroundSamples||[]}}};
 base.annotations=[{name:base.referenceName,reference:base.direction==='forward'?reference:reverseComplement(reference),cut:base.cutPosition,direction:base.direction,share:1,estimable:passesChecks,components:junctionAnnotationComponents(junction.models,junction.weights,referenceOrigin-prefix,reference.length,base.direction==='reverse').map(c=>({...c,support:emissionGroupSupport(support.scores,junction.models.findIndex(m=>m.size===c.size),predictive.get(junction.models.findIndex(m=>m.size===c.size)))}))}];
 base.plots=input.plots;return base;
}

END MODULE


BEGIN MODULE src/app/workers/core/sanger-single-quality.ts
/** Net-size estimates can tolerate isolated discordant cycles; a single artifact
 * must not veto hundreds of concordant positions. All cycles remain in the fit
 * and raw R². Dense or sustained disagreement still prevents certification.
 * This gate certifies net-size fractions, never complete allele identity. */
export function singleResidualSupport(positionRmse:number[]):{passes:boolean;discordantFraction:number;maxBlockRmse:number}{
 if(!positionRmse.length||positionRmse.some(x=>!Number.isFinite(x)||x<0))return {passes:false,discordantFraction:1,maxBlockRmse:Infinity};
 const discordantFraction=positionRmse.filter(x=>x>.20).length/positionRmse.length;
 const blocks:number[]=[];for(let i=0;i+20<=positionRmse.length;i+=10)blocks.push(Math.sqrt(positionRmse.slice(i,i+20).reduce((s,x)=>s+x*x,0)/20));
 const maxBlockRmse=Math.max(0,...blocks);return {passes:discordantFraction<=.05&&maxBlockRmse<=.15,discordantFraction,maxBlockRmse};
}

/** Independent WT-template uncertainty. The edited read's Phred is never used:
 * overlapping real alleles can legitimately lower its base-call confidence.
 * All-zero/absent quality is unavailable metadata, not fabricated certainty.
 * A WT donor and its local emission context use adjacent calls, so their
 * template variance combines the four neighbouring Phred errors. Relative
 * inverse-variance weights preserve uniform-quality reads and keep every cycle. */
export function controlTemplateReliability(quality:Uint8Array|null,indices:number[],sigma:number):number[]{
 if(!quality?.some(q=>q>0))return indices.map(()=>1);
 const baseErrors=Array.from(quality,q=>Math.pow(10,-q/10)),errors=baseErrors.map((_,i)=>{let intact=1;for(let j=Math.max(0,i-2);j<=Math.min(quality.length-1,i+1);j++)intact*=1-baseErrors[j];return 1-intact;}),sorted=indices.flatMap(i=>i>=0&&i<errors.length?[errors[i]]:[]).sort((a,b)=>a-b),median=sorted.length?sorted[Math.floor(sorted.length/2)]:0,variance=Math.max(1e-10,sigma*sigma);
 return indices.map(i=>i>=0&&i<errors.length?Math.min(1,(variance+median)/(variance+errors[i])):1);
}

END MODULE


BEGIN MODULE src/app/workers/core/sanger-single-signals.ts
import {SangerTrace,TRACE_BASES} from '../../models/sanger-analysis';
import {contrastPeaks,integratedPeaks,calibrateSignal,correctGain} from './sanger-signal';

/** Select a matched fluorescence representation using intact upstream bases only.
 * Contrast removes background but assumes comparable dye peak shapes. If that
 * representation fails the existing upstream checks, integrated area preserves
 * unequal dye shapes. Never choose a representation from an edited fit or truth.
 */
export function matchedSingleSignals(control:SangerTrace,sample:SangerTrace,positions:number[],offset:number){
  const evaluate=(method:'contrast'|'area')=>{
    const extract=method==='contrast'?contrastPeaks:integratedPeaks;
    const controlSignal=extract(control),sampleSignal=extract(sample),wt=controlSignal.vectors,raw=sampleSignal.vectors;
    const calibration=calibrateSignal(wt.map((_,i)=>raw[i+offset]||[0,0,0,0]),positions,[wt]);
    const edited=raw.map(v=>correctGain(v,calibration.gains));
    const upstreamError=positions.reduce((sum,i)=>sum+wt[i].reduce((s,v,b)=>s+Math.abs(v-edited[i+offset][b]),0)/2,0)/positions.length;
    return {method,controlSignal,sampleSignal,wt,raw,edited,calibration,upstreamError};
  };
  const contrast=evaluate('contrast');
  const passes=(r:ReturnType<typeof evaluate>)=>r.calibration.validationR2>=.8&&r.upstreamError<=.1;
  const area=passes(contrast)?undefined:evaluate('area');
  const chosen=area&&passes(area)?area:contrast;
  // An area-based WT template may have genuine spectral overlap. Assess loss of
  // information relative to that control's own upstream dye dominance.
  const expected=TRACE_BASES.map((base,b)=>{
    const values=positions.filter(i=>control.sequence[i]===base).map(i=>chosen.wt[i][b]).sort((a,b)=>a-b);
    return chosen.method==='area'&&values.length>=8?values[Math.floor(values.length/2)]:1;
  });
  const templateUsable=chosen.wt.map((_,i)=>{
    const lo=Math.max(0,i-12),hi=Math.min(chosen.wt.length,i+13);
    return chosen.wt.slice(lo,hi).reduce((sum,v,k)=>{const b=TRACE_BASES.indexOf(control.sequence[lo+k] as typeof TRACE_BASES[number]);
      return sum+(b<0?0:Math.min(1,v[b]/Math.max(.01,expected[b])));},0)/(hi-lo)>=.9;
  });
  return {...chosen,templateUsable};
}

END MODULE


BEGIN MODULE src/app/workers/core/sanger-stratified-paths.ts
import {decodeFrequencyPaths} from './sanger-frequency-path-decoder';
import type {Prepared,PathEdit} from './sanger-path-common';
/** Enumerate the bounded allocation of allele slots across measured net-length
 * classes. Each allocation gets its own search budget; a cheap pseudo-trace
 * hypothesis cannot starve a different class allocation before raw scoring. */
export function lengthClassAllocations(sizes:number[],slots=6){
 const types=[...new Set(sizes)].sort((a,b)=>a-b),out:Record<string,number>[]=[];
 const visit=(at:number,left:number,counts:Record<string,number>)=>{if(at===types.length){if(!left)out.push({...counts});return;}const remaining=types.length-at-1;for(let n=1;n<=left-remaining;n++){counts[String(types[at])]=n;visit(at+1,left-n,counts);}};visit(0,slots,{});return out;
}
export function proposeStratifiedPaths(ref:Prepared,families:{reference:number;size:number;fraction:number}[],positions:number[],observed:number[],noise:number,maxVariants=5,statesPerAllocation=200,profilesPerAllocation=4,progress:(stage:string)=>void=()=>{}){
 const allocations=lengthClassAllocations(families.filter(f=>f.fraction>0).map(f=>f.size),maxVariants+1),options:{events:PathEdit[];fraction:number}[][]=[],seen=new Set<string>(),details:any[]=[],scores:{rmse:number;maximum:number}[]=[];
 for(const [i,counts]of allocations.entries()){
  progress(`Comparing length-class allocations · ${i+1} / ${allocations.length}`);
  try{const decoded=decodeFrequencyPaths([ref],families,positions,observed,noise,3,10,maxVariants,statesPerAllocation,()=>{},true,counts),best=decoded.candidates.sort((a,b)=>a.rmse-b.rmse).slice(0,profilesPerAllocation);details.push({counts,...decoded.details[0],profiles:best.length});for(const c of best){const key=JSON.stringify(c.groups.map(g=>g.events).sort((a,b)=>JSON.stringify(a).localeCompare(JSON.stringify(b))));if(!seen.has(key)){seen.add(key);options.push(c.groups);scores.push({rmse:c.rmse,maximum:c.maximum});}}}catch(error){details.push({counts,profiles:0,error:String(error)});}
 }
 return{options,details,scores};
}

END MODULE


BEGIN MODULE src/app/workers/core/sanger-substitution-linkage.ts
import {exactFromNumber,exactWeightedEquality} from './exact-mixture-fiber';
/** Exact fluorescence aliases, independent of truth labels. These witness ranges
 * are not posterior intervals or a claim that every possible alias was found. */
export interface SubstitutionLinkage {reference:number;target:number;blocks:number;fraction:number;wtRange:[number,number];modelSequence:string}
interface Edit {start:number;end:number;inserted:string}
interface Model {reference:number;sequence:string;events:Edit[];fraction:number}
export function substitutionLinkageAliases(refs:{sequence:string;cuts:number[]}[],chosen:Model[],column:(sequence:string)=>number[]):SubstitutionLinkage[]{
 const out:SubstitutionLinkage[]=[];
 const apply=(sequence:string,events:Edit[])=>{for(const e of [...events].sort((a,b)=>b.start-a.start))sequence=sequence.slice(0,e.start)+e.inserted+sequence.slice(e.end);return sequence;};
 for(const model of chosen){const ref=refs[model.reference],share=chosen.filter(m=>m.reference===model.reference).reduce((s,m)=>s+m.fraction,0);if(!share)continue;
  for(let ei=0;ei<model.events.length;ei++){const event=model.events[ei];if(event.end-event.start!==event.inserted.length||!event.inserted.length)continue;
   const blocks:Edit[]=[];for(let p=event.start;p<event.end;){if(ref.sequence[p]===event.inserted[p-event.start]){p++;continue;}const start=p;while(p<event.end&&ref.sequence[p]!==event.inserted[p-event.start])p++;blocks.push({start,end:p,inserted:event.inserted.slice(start-event.start,p-event.start)});}
   if(blocks.length===1&&blocks[0].end-blocks[0].start>1){const block=blocks.pop()!;for(let p=block.start;p<block.end;p++)blocks.push({start:p,end:p+1,inserted:event.inserted[p-event.start]});}
   if(blocks.length<2)continue;
   const target=ref.cuts.map(c=>Math.abs(c-event.start)).reduce((best,d,i,ds)=>d<ds[best]?i:best,0),scaffold=model.events.filter((_,i)=>i!==ei),base=apply(ref.sequence,scaffold),baseMass=chosen.filter(m=>m.reference===model.reference&&m.sequence===base).reduce((s,m)=>s+m.fraction,0),shift=Math.min(model.fraction,baseMass/(blocks.length-1));if(shift<1e-5)continue;
   const joined=column(model.sequence),empty=column(base),parts=blocks.map(e=>column(apply(ref.sequence,[...scaffold,e])));
   if(!exactWeightedEquality([{weight:exactFromNumber(1),column:joined},{weight:exactFromNumber(blocks.length-1),column:empty}],parts.map(column=>({weight:exactFromNumber(1),column}))))continue;
   const wt=chosen.filter(m=>m.reference===model.reference&&!m.events.some(e=>ref.cuts.map(c=>Math.abs(c-e.start)).indexOf(Math.min(...ref.cuts.map(c=>Math.abs(c-e.start))))===target)).reduce((s,m)=>s+m.fraction,0)/share;
   out.push({reference:model.reference,target:target+1,blocks:blocks.length,fraction:shift/share,wtRange:[Math.max(0,wt-(blocks.length-1)*shift/share),wt],modelSequence:model.sequence});
  }
 }
 return out;
}

END MODULE


BEGIN MODULE src/app/workers/core/sanger-substitution-search.ts
/** Joint proposals for contiguous substitution molecules. Input is fluorescence
 * and inferred candidates only. No truth recipes or group percentages enter. */
export function substitutionProposals(columns:number[][],wt:number[],residual:number[],mass:number,limit=8):number[][]{
 const sparse=columns.map(c=>{const at:number[]=[],values:number[]=[];let rhs=0,norm=0;for(let k=0;k<c.length;k++){const v=c[k]-wt[k];if(Math.abs(v)<1e-10)continue;at.push(k);values.push(v);rhs+=v*residual[k];norm+=v*v;}return{at,values,rhs,norm};});
 const dot=(i:number,j:number)=>{const a=sparse[i],b=sparse[j];let x=0,y=0,sum=0;while(x<a.at.length&&y<b.at.length){if(a.at[x]===b.at[y]){sum+=a.values[x++]*b.values[y++];}else if(a.at[x]<b.at[y])x++;else y++;}return sum;};
 const best:{ids:number[];gain:number}[]=[];let floor=-Infinity;
 const add=(ids:number[],gain:number)=>{if(gain<=floor)return;best.push({ids,gain});best.sort((a,b)=>b.gain-a.gain);if(best.length>limit)best.pop();if(best.length===limit)floor=best.at(-1)!.gain;};
 for(let i=0;i<sparse.length;i++){const a=sparse[i];if(!a.norm||a.rhs<=0)continue;const u=Math.min(mass,a.rhs/a.norm);add([i],2*u*a.rhs-u*u*a.norm);
  for(let j=0;j<i;j++){const b=sparse[j];if(!b.norm||b.rhs<=0)continue;const cross=dot(i,j),det=a.norm*b.norm-cross*cross;if(det<1e-10)continue;let x=(a.rhs*b.norm-b.rhs*cross)/det,y=(b.rhs*a.norm-a.rhs*cross)/det;if(x<=0||y<=0)continue;if(x+y>mass){const curvature=a.norm+b.norm-2*cross;x=Math.max(0,Math.min(mass,curvature>0?(a.rhs-b.rhs+mass*(b.norm-cross))/curvature:mass/2));y=mass-x;}add([i,j],2*(x*a.rhs+y*b.rhs)-x*x*a.norm-y*y*b.norm-2*x*y*cross);
  }
 }
 return best.map(b=>b.ids);
}

END MODULE
