BEGIN MODULE src/app/workers/core/sanger-transition-response.ts
import {mixPhase,phaseNeighbor} from './sanger-signal';
/** Empirical fluorescence for a known WT (previous,current) base pair. This
 * models dye spill and sequence-dependent phase using CONTROL observations,
 * not a truth allele or a sample edit. Every third recurrence is held out. */
export interface TransitionResponse {markerValidation?:{oldMse:number;newMse:number;trainingPositions:number;validationPositions:number;accepted:boolean};used:boolean;table:number[][];pairYields:number[];trainingCounts:number[];validationCounts:number[];rawValidationMse:number;validationMse:number}
const bases='ACGT',median=(v:number[])=>{const s=[...v].sort((a,b)=>a-b);return s.length?s[Math.floor(s.length/2)]:0;};
export function fitTransitionResponse(raw:number[][],sequences:string[],positions:number[],lag:number,baseStrengths:number[]=[1,1,1,1]):TransitionResponse {
 const groups=Array.from({length:16},()=>[] as number[]);
 for(const p of positions){const pair=sequences[0].slice(p-1,p+1),l=bases.indexOf(pair[0]),b=bases.indexOf(pair[1]);if(pair.length!==2||l<0||b<0||!raw[p]?.some(v=>v>0)||!sequences.every(s=>s.slice(p-1,p+1)===pair))continue;groups[4*l+b].push(p);}
 const train=groups.map(g=>g.filter((_,i)=>i%3!==0)),valid=groups.map(g=>g.filter((_,i)=>i%3===0)),basic=groups.map((_,k)=>Array.from(bases,(b,i)=>(i===k%4?1-Math.abs(lag):0)+(lag<0?Math.abs(lag)/4:i===Math.floor(k/4)?lag:0)));
 const table=train.map((g,k)=>{if(g.length<4)return basic[k];const vector=bases.split('').map((_,b)=>median(g.map(p=>raw[p][b]))),total=vector.reduce((s,v)=>s+v,0);return vector.map(v=>total?v/total:0);});
 const score=(vectors:number[][])=>valid.reduce((sum,g,k)=>sum+g.reduce((s,p)=>s+raw[p].reduce((e,v,b)=>e+(v-vectors[k][b])**2,0),0),0)/Math.max(1,4*valid.flat().length);
 const before=score(basic),after=score(table),used=valid.flat().length>=30&&after<before*.95&&after+1e-7<before;
 const chosen=used?table:basic,pairYields=chosen.map((v,k)=>{
  const l=Math.floor(k/4),b=k%4;if(l===b)return baseStrengths[b];
  const current=chosen[4*b+b],previous=chosen[4*l+l],difference=previous.map((x,d)=>x-current[d]),norm=difference.reduce((s,x)=>s+x*x,0);
  const fraction=Math.max(0,Math.min(1,norm?difference.reduce((s,x,d)=>s+x*(v[d]-current[d]),0)/norm:0));
  // A normalized colour mixture loses its absolute photon yield. Recover that
  // yield from the two pure-base responses before mixing molecular fractions.
  return 1/((1-fraction)/baseStrengths[b]+fraction/baseStrengths[l]);
 });
 return {used,table:chosen,pairYields,trainingCounts:train.map(g=>g.length),validationCounts:valid.map(g=>g.length),rawValidationMse:before,validationMse:used?after:before};
}
export function transitionProbabilityEmission(left:number[],current:number[],right:number[],theta:number[],table:number[][],baseYields:number[]):number[]{
 const previous=left.some(v=>v>0)?left:[.25,.25,.25,.25],out=[0,0,0,0];
 for(let l=0;l<4;l++)if(previous[l])for(let b=0;b<4;b++)if(current[b]){const context=right.reduce((sum,v,r)=>v?sum+v*Math.exp((theta[4+r]||0)+(theta[8+16*l+4*b+r]||0)):sum,0)||1;const amount=previous[l]*current[b]*context*Math.exp(theta[l]||0)*(baseYields[4*l+b]??1),response=table[4*l+b];for(let dye=0;dye<4;dye++)out[dye]+=amount*response[dye];}
 return out;
}
export function transitionEmission(sequence:string,p:number,lag:number,theta:number[],table:number[][],baseYields:number[]):number[]{
 const prob=(at:number)=>Array.from(bases,b=>Number(sequence[at]===b)),one=(at:number)=>transitionProbabilityEmission(prob(at-1),prob(at),prob(at+1),theta,table,baseYields),a=one(p),b=one(phaseNeighbor(p,lag));return mixPhase(a,b,lag);
}

END MODULE


BEGIN MODULE src/app/workers/core/sequence-matcher.ts
/**
 * sequence-matcher.ts — TypeScript port of Python's difflib.SequenceMatcher.
 *
 * Faithfully implements the Ratcliffe/Obershelp algorithm used by CPython's
 * difflib module.  Only the subset required by the CRISPR analysis pipeline
 * is ported: get_opcodes(), get_matching_blocks(), and ratio().
 */

export type Opcode = [tag: string, i1: number, i2: number, j1: number, j2: number];

export class SequenceMatcher {
  private a: string;
  private b: string;
  private b2j: Map<string, number[]> = new Map();
  private matchingBlocksCache: [number, number, number][] | null = null;
  private opcodesCache: Opcode[] | null = null;
  private fullBCount: Map<string, number> | null = null;
  private bjunk: Set<string> = new Set();
  private bpopular: Set<string> = new Set();
  private autojunk: boolean;

  constructor(isjunk: null, a: string = '', b: string = '', autojunk: boolean = true) {
    this.a = a;
    this.b = b;
    this.autojunk = autojunk;
    this.chainB();
  }

  setSeqs(a: string, b: string): void {
    this.setSeq1(a);
    this.setSeq2(b);
  }

  setSeq1(a: string): void {
    if (a === this.a) return;
    this.a = a;
    this.matchingBlocksCache = null;
    this.opcodesCache = null;
  }

  setSeq2(b: string): void {
    if (b === this.b) return;
    this.b = b;
    this.matchingBlocksCache = null;
    this.opcodesCache = null;
    this.chainB();
  }

  private chainB(): void {
    const b = this.b;
    const n = b.length;
    this.b2j = new Map();
    const b2j = this.b2j;

    for (let i = 0; i < n; i++) {
      const c = b[i];
      const indices = b2j.get(c);
      if (indices) {
        indices.push(i);
      } else {
        b2j.set(c, [i]);
      }
    }

    // Purge junk elements (none in our usage, isjunk is always null)
    this.bjunk = new Set();

    // Purge popular elements (autojunk heuristic)
    this.bpopular = new Set();
    if (this.autojunk && n >= 200) {
      const ntest = Math.floor(n / 100) + 1;
      for (const [elt, idxs] of b2j) {
        if (idxs.length > ntest) {
          this.bpopular.add(elt);
        }
      }
      for (const elt of this.bpopular) {
        b2j.delete(elt);
      }
    }
  }

  /**
   * Find longest matching block in a[alo:ahi] and b[blo:bhi].
   * Returns [i, j, k] such that a[i:i+k] == b[j:j+k].
   */
  findLongestMatch(alo: number, ahi: number, blo: number, bhi: number): [number, number, number] {
    const a = this.a;
    const b2j = this.b2j;

    let besti = alo;
    let bestj = blo;
    let bestsize = 0;

    // j2len[j] = length of longest match ending with a[i-1] and b[j]
    let j2len: Map<number, number> = new Map();

    for (let i = alo; i < ahi; i++) {
      const newj2len: Map<number, number> = new Map();
      const indices = b2j.get(a[i]);
      if (indices) {
        for (const j of indices) {
          if (j < blo) continue;
          if (j >= bhi) break;
          const k = (j2len.get(j - 1) || 0) + 1;
          newj2len.set(j, k);
          if (k > bestsize) {
            besti = i - k + 1;
            bestj = j - k + 1;
            bestsize = k;
          }
        }
      }
      j2len = newj2len;
    }

    // Extend the match as far as possible (handles junk)
    while (besti > alo && bestj > blo && !this.bjunk.has(this.b[bestj - 1]) && a[besti - 1] === this.b[bestj - 1]) {
      besti--;
      bestj--;
      bestsize++;
    }
    while (besti + bestsize < ahi && bestj + bestsize < bhi && !this.bjunk.has(this.b[bestj + bestsize]) && a[besti + bestsize] === this.b[bestj + bestsize]) {
      bestsize++;
    }

    // Extend with junk on both sides
    while (besti > alo && bestj > blo && this.bjunk.has(this.b[bestj - 1]) && a[besti - 1] === this.b[bestj - 1]) {
      besti--;
      bestj--;
      bestsize++;
    }
    while (besti + bestsize < ahi && bestj + bestsize < bhi && this.bjunk.has(this.b[bestj + bestsize]) && a[besti + bestsize] === this.b[bestj + bestsize]) {
      bestsize++;
    }

    return [besti, bestj, bestsize];
  }

  getMatchingBlocks(): [number, number, number][] {
    if (this.matchingBlocksCache !== null) return this.matchingBlocksCache;

    const la = this.a.length;
    const lb = this.b.length;

    const queue: [number, number, number, number][] = [[0, la, 0, lb]];
    const matching: [number, number, number][] = [];

    while (queue.length > 0) {
      const [alo, ahi, blo, bhi] = queue.pop()!;
      const [i, j, k] = this.findLongestMatch(alo, ahi, blo, bhi);
      if (k > 0) {
        matching.push([i, j, k]);
        if (alo < i && blo < j) queue.push([alo, i, blo, j]);
        if (i + k < ahi && j + k < bhi) queue.push([i + k, ahi, j + k, bhi]);
      }
    }

    matching.sort((a, b) => a[0] - b[0] || a[1] - b[1] || a[2] - b[2]);

    // Collapse adjacent equal blocks
    let i1 = 0, j1 = 0, k1 = 0;
    const nonAdjacent: [number, number, number][] = [];
    for (const [i2, j2, k2] of matching) {
      if (i1 + k1 === i2 && j1 + k1 === j2) {
        k1 += k2;
      } else {
        if (k1) nonAdjacent.push([i1, j1, k1]);
        i1 = i2;
        j1 = j2;
        k1 = k2;
      }
    }
    if (k1) nonAdjacent.push([i1, j1, k1]);

    nonAdjacent.push([la, lb, 0]); // sentinel
    this.matchingBlocksCache = nonAdjacent;
    return nonAdjacent;
  }

  getOpcodes(): Opcode[] {
    if (this.opcodesCache !== null) return this.opcodesCache;

    let i = 0, j = 0;
    const answer: Opcode[] = [];

    for (const [ai, bj, size] of this.getMatchingBlocks()) {
      let tag = '';
      if (i < ai && j < bj) tag = 'replace';
      else if (i < ai) tag = 'delete';
      else if (j < bj) tag = 'insert';

      if (tag) answer.push([tag, i, ai, j, bj]);
      i = ai + size;
      j = bj + size;
      if (size) answer.push(['equal', ai, ai + size, bj, bj + size]);
    }

    this.opcodesCache = answer;
    return answer;
  }

  ratio(): number {
    const matches = this.getMatchingBlocks().reduce((sum, [, , k]) => sum + k, 0);
    const length = this.a.length + this.b.length;
    return length ? (2.0 * matches) / length : 1.0;
  }
}

END MODULE


BEGIN MODULE src/app/workers/core/simplex-active-set.ts
/** Equality-constrained active-set QP on the probability simplex. Singular
 * tangent systems retain free coordinates at their current values. A dual gap
 * independently checks the returned solution; callers can retain a fallback. */
export function activeSimplex(G:ArrayLike<number>[],observations:number,tolerance=1e-13,initial?:number[]){
 const n=G.length,w=initial?.length===n&&initial.every(x=>Number.isFinite(x)&&x>=0)?[...initial]:Array(n).fill(0);if(!w.some(x=>x>0)){let v=0;for(let i=1;i<n;i++)if(G[i][i]<G[v][v])v=i;w[v]=1;}else{const sum=w.reduce((a,b)=>a+b,0);for(let i=0;i<n;i++)w[i]/=sum;}
 let active=w.flatMap((x,i)=>x>0?[i]:[]),gap=Infinity,steps=0;
 const gradient=()=>{const out=Array(n).fill(0);for(let i=0;i<n;i++){let value=0;for(let j=0;j<n;j++)value+=G[i][j]*w[j];out[i]=value;}return out;};
 const solve=(matrix:number[][],rhs:number[])=>{
  const m=rhs.length,rows=matrix.map((r,i)=>[...r,rhs[i]]),pivots:number[]=[];let scale=1;for(const row of matrix)for(const value of row)scale=Math.max(scale,Math.abs(value));const epsilon=1e-12*scale;let at=0;
  for(let c=0;c<m&&at<m;c++){let pivot=at;for(let r=at+1;r<m;r++)if(Math.abs(rows[r][c])>Math.abs(rows[pivot][c]))pivot=r;if(Math.abs(rows[pivot][c])<=epsilon)continue;[rows[at],rows[pivot]]=[rows[pivot],rows[at]];for(let r=at+1;r<m;r++){const f=rows[r][c]/rows[at][c];for(let k=c;k<=m;k++)rows[r][k]-=f*rows[at][k];}pivots.push(c);at++;}
  const x=Array(m).fill(0);for(let r=at-1;r>=0;r--){const c=pivots[r];let value=rows[r][m];for(let j=c+1;j<m;j++)value-=rows[r][j]*x[j];x[c]=value/rows[r][c];}return x;
 };
 for(;steps<Math.max(100,4*n*n);steps++){
  const g=gradient(),lambda=w.reduce((s,x,i)=>s+x*g[i],0);let enter=0;for(let i=1;i<n;i++)if(g[i]<g[enter])enter=i;gap=Math.max(0,lambda-g[enter])/observations;if(gap<=tolerance)break;if(!active.includes(enter))active.push(enter);
  let moved=false;
  for(let inner=0;inner<=n;inner++){
   if(active.length<2)break;const pivot=active[0],rest=active.slice(1),H=rest.map(i=>rest.map(j=>G[i][j]-G[i][pivot]-G[pivot][j]+G[pivot][pivot])),rhs=rest.map(i=>g[pivot]-g[i]),d=solve(H,rhs),direction=Array(n).fill(0);rest.forEach((id,i)=>direction[id]=d[i]);direction[pivot]=-d.reduce((a,b)=>a+b,0);
   if(Math.max(...direction.map(Math.abs))<1e-15)break;let alpha=1;for(const id of active)if(direction[id]<0)alpha=Math.min(alpha,-w[id]/direction[id]);for(const id of active)w[id]=Math.max(0,w[id]+alpha*direction[id]);moved||=alpha>0;
   active=active.filter(id=>w[id]>1e-14);if(alpha>=1-1e-12)break;
   // Recompute the gradient before another constrained step.
   const updated=gradient();for(let i=0;i<n;i++)g[i]=updated[i];
  }
  if(!moved)break;
 }
 const g=gradient(),lambda=w.reduce((s,x,i)=>s+x*g[i],0);gap=Math.max(0,lambda-Math.min(...g))/observations;
 return{weights:w,converged:gap<=tolerance,optimalityGap:gap,steps};
}

END MODULE

  minimumMismatches:number; minimumExact:boolean; nearSiteCount:number;
  sites:{reference:string;start:number;end:number;direction:'forward'|'reverse';mismatches:number;terminalMismatches:number}[];
  references:{reference:string;minimumMismatches:number;minimumExact:boolean;nearSiteCount:number;hasUnknownBases:boolean}[];
}
export interface SangerCandidate extends PrimerProperties {
  primerSafety?:{primary:PrimerBindingScreen;partner:PrimerBindingScreen|null};
  wtSeparation?:{minimumDifferences:number;comparedBases:number;condition:number|null;warnings:string[]};
  id: string; direction: 'forward' | 'reverse'; sites: PrimerSite[];
  targetDistance?: number;
  minOffset: number; minTraceDifferences: number; traceComparedBases: number;
  indelOffsetOverlap: boolean;
  partner: CommonPartner | null;
  referenceIndex?: number;
  specificity?: { primary: boolean; partner: boolean; mismatches: number;strategy?:'three-prime'|'mismatch';terminalWindow?:number;primaryVariants?:ThreePrimeVariant[];partnerVariants?:ThreePrimeVariant[] };
}
export interface SangerDesignResult {
  candidates: SangerCandidate[];
  stats: { enumerated: number; qualityPassed: number; commonUnique: number;
    geometryPassed: number; offsetPassed: number; partnerChecked: number;separabilityRejected?:number };
  excludedCandidates?:{id:string;reason:string}[];
  complete: boolean;
  coverage?: { reference: string; candidates: number }[];
  references: SangerReference[];
  settings: SangerDesignSettings;
}

END MODULE

export type Topology = 'linear' | 'circular';
export type Strand = 1 | -1 | 0;

export interface SequenceFeature {
  id: string;
  name: string;
  type: string;
  start: number; // 0-indexed, inclusive
  end: number;   // 0-indexed, exclusive (so length = end - start, modulo circularity)

  color?: string;
  description?: string;
  qualifiers?: Record<string, string[]>;
}

export interface Primer {
  id: string;

}

}

}

}

BEGIN MODULE src/app/utils/abif.ts
import { SangerTrace, TRACE_BASES, TraceBase } from '../models/sanger-analysis';
interface Entry {type:number;size:number;count:number;bytes:number;offset:number}
/** ABIF big-endian directory reader. Only analyzed sequencing traces are used. */
export function parseAbif(buffer:ArrayBuffer,name='trace.ab1'):SangerTrace {
  const view=new DataView(buffer),bytes=new Uint8Array(buffer);
  const decoder=new TextDecoder();
  const text=(at:number,n:number)=>decoder.decode(bytes.subarray(at,at+n));
  const bounds=(at:number,n:number)=>{if(!Number.isSafeInteger(at)||!Number.isSafeInteger(n)||at<0||n<0||at+n>buffer.byteLength)throw new Error('Truncated or invalid ABIF data offset.');};
  bounds(0,34);if(text(0,4)!=='ABIF') throw new Error('Choose an ABIF Sanger .ab1/.abi file. FASTQ, FASTA and SCF are not supported here.');
  const rootCount=view.getUint32(18),rootOffset=view.getUint32(26);
  if(rootCount<1||rootCount>4096)throw new Error('Invalid ABIF directory length.');
  bounds(rootOffset,rootCount*28);
  const entries=new Map<string,Entry>();
  for(let i=0;i<rootCount;i++) {
    const at=rootOffset+i*28,key=text(at,4)+view.getUint32(at+4);
    const entry:Entry={type:view.getUint16(at+8),size:view.getUint16(at+10),count:view.getUint32(at+12),bytes:view.getUint32(at+16),offset:view.getUint32(at+20)};
    if(entry.bytes<=4)entry.offset=at+20;
    bounds(entry.offset,entry.bytes);
    if(entry.count*entry.size>entry.bytes)throw new Error(`Invalid ABIF element size: ${key}.`);
    entries.set(key,entry);
  }
  const get=(tag:string)=>{const e=entries.get(tag);if(!e)throw new Error(`Missing ${tag}: an analyzed, basecalled Sanger trace is required.`);return e;};
  const chars=(e:Entry)=>text(e.offset,e.bytes).replace(/\0+$/,'');
  const pair=entries.has('PBAS2')&&entries.has('PLOC2')?2:1;
  const baseEntry=get('PBAS'+pair);if(baseEntry.size!==1)throw new Error('Unsupported ABIF base-call type.');
  const sequence=chars(baseEntry).toUpperCase();
  if(sequence.length<40||sequence.length>3000||/[^ACGTRYSWKMBDHVN]/.test(sequence))throw new Error('Use a basecalled Sanger read of 40–3,000 bases.');
  const order=chars(get('FWO_1')).slice(0,4);
  if(order.length!==4||new Set(order).size!==4||/[^ACGT]/.test(order))throw new Error('ABIF dye order FWO_1 is missing or invalid.');
  const numeric=(e:Entry):number[]=>{
    if(!((e.size===1&&[1,2].includes(e.type))||(e.size===2&&[3,4].includes(e.type))||(e.size===4&&e.type===5)))throw new Error('Unsupported ABIF numeric array.');
    return Array.from({length:e.count},(_,i)=>e.size===1?view.getUint8(e.offset+i):e.size===2?(e.type===4?view.getInt16(e.offset+2*i):view.getUint16(e.offset+2*i)):view.getInt32(e.offset+4*i));
  };
  const channels={} as Record<TraceBase,Float32Array>;
  for(let i=0;i<4;i++) {
    const e=get('DATA'+(9+i));if(e.count<40||e.count>1000000)throw new Error('ABIF trace has an unsupported number of data points.');
    channels[order[i] as TraceBase]=Float32Array.from(numeric(e),v=>Math.max(0,v));
  }
  const length=channels.A.length;
  if(TRACE_BASES.some(b=>channels[b].length!==length))throw new Error('ABIF channel lengths do not match.');
  const positions=numeric(get('PLOC'+pair));
  if(positions.length!==sequence.length||positions.some((p,i)=>p<0||p>=length||(i>0&&p<=positions[i-1])))throw new Error('ABIF peak positions are incomplete or not strictly increasing.');
  const q=entries.get('PCON'+pair);const quality=q?Uint8Array.from(numeric(q)):null;
  if(quality&&quality.length!==sequence.length)throw new Error('ABIF quality values do not match base calls.');
  const sample=entries.get('SMPL1');
  let sampleName=sample?chars(sample):name;
  if(sample?.type===18)sampleName=sampleName.slice(1,1+bytes[sample.offset]);
  return {name,sampleName,sequence,peaks:Uint32Array.from(positions),quality,channels};
}

END MODULE


BEGIN MODULE src/app/utils/biology.utils.ts
import { SequenceFeature, Topology } from '../models/sequence.model';

export function reverseComplement(seq: string): string {
  const complementMap: Record<string, string> = {
    A: 'T', T: 'A', U: 'A',
    C: 'G', G: 'C',
    R: 'Y', Y: 'R', // R = A/G, Y = C/T
    K: 'M', M: 'K', // K = G/T, M = A/C
    S: 'S', W: 'W', // S = C/G, W = A/T
    B: 'V', V: 'B', // B = C/G/T, V = A/C/G
    D: 'H', H: 'D', // D = A/G/T, H = A/C/T
    N: 'N',
    a: 't', t: 'a', u: 'a',
    c: 'g', g: 'c',
    r: 'y', y: 'r',
    k: 'm', m: 'k',
    s: 's', w: 'w',
    b: 'v', v: 'b',
    d: 'h', h: 'd',
    n: 'n',
    '-': '-'
  };

  let rc = '';
  for (let i = seq.length - 1; i >= 0; i--) {
    const char = seq[i];
    rc += complementMap[char] || char;
  }
  return rc;
}

export function complement(seq: string): string {
  const complementMap: Record<string, string> = {
    A: 'T', T: 'A', U: 'A',
    C: 'G', G: 'C',
    R: 'Y', Y: 'R', // R = A/G, Y = C/T
    K: 'M', M: 'K', // K = G/T, M = A/C
    S: 'S', W: 'W', // S = C/G, W = A/T
    B: 'V', V: 'B', // B = C/G/T, V = A/C/G
    D: 'H', H: 'D', // D = A/G/T, H = A/C/T
    N: 'N',
    a: 't', t: 'a', u: 'a',
    c: 'g', g: 'c',
    r: 'y', y: 'r',
    k: 'm', m: 'k',
    s: 's', w: 'w',
    b: 'v', v: 'b',
    d: 'h', h: 'd',
    n: 'n',
    '-': '-'
  };

  let c = '';
  for (let i = 0; i < seq.length; i++) {
    const char = seq[i];
    c += complementMap[char] || char;
  }
  return c;
}

export function getGCContent(seq: string): number {
  if (!seq || seq.length === 0) return 0;
  const gcCount = (seq.match(/[GCgcSscg]/g) || []).length;
  return (gcCount / seq.length) * 100;
}

const GENETIC_CODE: Record<string, string> = {
  ATA:'I', ATC:'I', ATT:'I', ATG:'M',
  ACA:'T', ACC:'T', ACG:'T', ACT:'T',
  AAC:'N', AAT:'N', AAA:'K', AAG:'K',
  AGC:'S', AGT:'S', AGA:'R', AGG:'R',
  CTA:'L', CTC:'L', CTG:'L', CTT:'L',
  CCA:'P', CCC:'P', CCG:'P', CCT:'P',
  CAC:'H', CAT:'H', CAA:'Q', CAG:'Q',
  CGA:'R', CGC:'R', CGG:'R', CGT:'R',
  GTA:'V', GTC:'V', GTG:'V', GTT:'V',
  GCA:'A', GCC:'A', GCG:'A', GCT:'A',
  GAC:'D', GAT:'D', GAA:'E', GAG:'E',
  GGA:'G', GGC:'G', GGG:'G', GGT:'G',
  TCA:'S', TCC:'S', TCG:'S', TCT:'S',
  TTC:'F', TTT:'F', TTA:'L', TTG:'L',
  TAC:'Y', TAT:'Y', TAA:'*', TAG:'*',
  TGC:'C', TGT:'C', TGA:'*', TGG:'W',
};

export function translateDNA(seq: string): string {
  const cleanSeq = seq.replace(/[^ATCGU]/gi, '').replace(/U/gi, 'T').toUpperCase();
  let protein = '';
  for (let i = 0; i < cleanSeq.length - 2; i += 3) {
    const codon = cleanSeq.substring(i, i + 3);
    protein += GENETIC_CODE[codon] || 'X';
  }
  return protein;
}

export interface ORF {
  start: number;
  end: number;
  frame: number;
  strand: 1 | -1;
  translation: string;
}

export function findORFs(seq: string, minAminoAcids: number = 30): ORF[] {
  const orfs: ORF[] = [];
  const seqUpper = seq.replace(/U/gi, 'T').toUpperCase();
  const rcSeq = reverseComplement(seqUpper);
  const minLen = minAminoAcids * 3;

  const findInFrame = (dna: string, strand: 1 | -1) => {
    for (let frame = 0; frame < 3; frame++) {
      let currentStart = -1;
      for (let i = frame; i <= dna.length - 3; i += 3) {
        const codon = dna.substring(i, i + 3);
        if (codon === 'ATG' && currentStart === -1) {
          currentStart = i;
        } else if ((codon === 'TAA' || codon === 'TAG' || codon === 'TGA') && currentStart !== -1) {
          if (i + 3 - currentStart >= minLen) {
            let start = currentStart;
            let end = i + 3;
            if (strand === -1) {
              const rcStart = dna.length - end;
              const rcEnd = dna.length - start;
              start = rcStart;
              end = rcEnd;
            }
            const translation = translateDNA(dna.substring(currentStart, i + 3));
            orfs.push({ start, end, frame: strand === 1 ? frame + 1 : -(frame + 1), strand, translation });
          }
          currentStart = -1; // Reset to find next ORF in same frame
        }
      }
    }
  };

  findInFrame(seqUpper, 1);
  findInFrame(rcSeq, -1);

  return orfs;
}

export function calculateTm(seq: string): number {
  const clean = seq.toUpperCase().replace(/[^ATCG]/g, '');
  if (clean.length < 14) {
    const wS = (clean.match(/[AT]/g) || []).length;
    const sS = (clean.match(/[CG]/g) || []).length;
    return (wS * 2) + (sS * 4);
  } else {
    const cg = (seq.match(/[CG]/g) || []).length;
    return 64.9 + 41 * (cg - 16.4) / seq.length;
  }
}

export function shiftFeatures(
  features: SequenceFeature[],
  editStart: number,
  deleteCount: number,
  insertLength: number,
  originalSeqLen: number,
  topology: Topology
): SequenceFeature[] {
  const diff = insertLength - deleteCount;
  if (diff === 0 && deleteCount === 0) return features;

  const newFeatures: SequenceFeature[] = [];

  for (const feat of features) {
    let newStart = feat.start;
    let newEnd = feat.end;
    let deleted = false;

    // Simplify wraparound edits for circular for now
    
    // Shift start
    if (feat.start >= editStart + deleteCount) {
      newStart += diff;
    } else if (feat.start >= editStart && feat.start < editStart + deleteCount) {
      newStart = editStart; // truncated start
    }

    // Shift end
    if (feat.end > editStart + deleteCount) {
      newEnd += diff;
    } else if (feat.end > editStart && feat.end <= editStart + deleteCount) {
      newEnd = editStart; // truncated end
    }

    if (newStart >= newEnd) {
      deleted = true; // feature completely wiped out
    }

    if (!deleted) {
      newFeatures.push({ ...feat, start: newStart, end: newEnd });
    }
  }

  return newFeatures;
}

END MODULE

/** SHA-256 for ASCII DNA. Works on HTTP deployments without WebCrypto. */
export function referenceChecksum(sequence:string):string{
 const text=sequence.replace(/\s/g,'').toUpperCase(),bytes=new TextEncoder().encode(text),length=Math.ceil((bytes.length+9)/64)*64,data=new Uint8Array(length),view=new DataView(data.buffer);data.set(bytes);data[bytes.length]=128;view.setUint32(length-4,bytes.length*8);view.setUint32(length-8,Math.floor(bytes.length/0x20000000));
 const constants=[0x428a2f98,0x71374491,0xb5c0fbcf,0xe9b5dba5,0x3956c25b,0x59f111f1,0x923f82a4,0xab1c5ed5,0xd807aa98,0x12835b01,0x243185be,0x550c7dc3,0x72be5d74,0x80deb1fe,0x9bdc06a7,0xc19bf174,0xe49b69c1,0xefbe4786,0x0fc19dc6,0x240ca1cc,0x2de92c6f,0x4a7484aa,0x5cb0a9dc,0x76f988da,0x983e5152,0xa831c66d,0xb00327c8,0xbf597fc7,0xc6e00bf3,0xd5a79147,0x06ca6351,0x14292967,0x27b70a85,0x2e1b2138,0x4d2c6dfc,0x53380d13,0x650a7354,0x766a0abb,0x81c2c92e,0x92722c85,0xa2bfe8a1,0xa81a664b,0xc24b8b70,0xc76c51a3,0xd192e819,0xd6990624,0xf40e3585,0x106aa070,0x19a4c116,0x1e376c08,0x2748774c,0x34b0bcb5,0x391c0cb3,0x4ed8aa4a,0x5b9cca4f,0x682e6ff3,0x748f82ee,0x78a5636f,0x84c87814,0x8cc70208,0x90befffa,0xa4506ceb,0xbef9a3f7,0xc67178f2];
 const h=[0x6a09e667,0xbb67ae85,0x3c6ef372,0xa54ff53a,0x510e527f,0x9b05688c,0x1f83d9ab,0x5be0cd19],w=new Uint32Array(64),rot=(v:number,n:number)=>(v>>>n)|(v<<(32-n));
 for(let off=0;off<length;off+=64){for(let i=0;i<16;i++)w[i]=view.getUint32(off+i*4);for(let i=16;i<64;i++){const x=w[i-15],y=w[i-2];w[i]=(w[i-16]+(rot(x,7)^rot(x,18)^(x>>>3))+w[i-7]+(rot(y,17)^rot(y,19)^(y>>>10)))>>>0;}let [a,b,c,d,e,f,g,k]=h;
  for(let i=0;i<64;i++){const t1=(k+(rot(e,6)^rot(e,11)^rot(e,25))+((e&f)^(~e&g))+constants[i]+w[i])>>>0,t2=((rot(a,2)^rot(a,13)^rot(a,22))+((a&b)^(a&c)^(b&c)))>>>0;k=g;g=f;f=e;e=(d+t1)>>>0;d=c;c=b;b=a;a=(t1+t2)>>>0;}[a,b,c,d,e,f,g,k].forEach((v,i)=>h[i]=(h[i]+v)>>>0);
 }return h.map(v=>v.toString(16).padStart(8,'0')).join('');
}

BEGIN MODULE src/app/utils/sanger-analysis-demo.ts
import { TRACE_BASES } from '../models/sanger-analysis';
/** Constructed ABIF fixture: explicitly synthetic, exercises the real file reader. */
export function sangerAnalysisDemo():{reference:string;cut:number;control:ArrayBuffer;sample:ArrayBuffer} {
  let seed=718223;const dna=(n:number)=>Array.from({length:n},()=>{seed=(Math.imul(seed,1664525)+1013904223)>>>0;return 'ACGT'[seed>>>30];}).join('');
  const sequence=dna(650),cut=230,flank=dna(60);
  const make=(mixed:boolean)=>{
    const count=sequence.length,peaks=Array.from({length:count},(_,i)=>14+i*10),length=count*10+30;
    const channels=TRACE_BASES.map(()=>new Uint16Array(length));
    for(let i=0;i<count;i++) {
      const mixture=mixed&&i>=cut?[[sequence[i],.6],[sequence[Math.min(count-1,i+1)],.25],[sequence[Math.max(0,i-1)],.15]] as const:[[sequence[i],1]] as const;
      for(const [base,weight] of mixture){const channel=TRACE_BASES.indexOf(base as any);for(let d=-5;d<=5;d++){const at=peaks[i]+d;channels[channel][at]+=Math.round(weight*1100*Math.exp(-d*d/4));}}
    }
    for(const c of channels)for(let j=0;j<c.length;j++)c[j]+=2;
    return writeAbif(sequence,peaks,channels,mixed?'Synthetic_sample_60WT_25del1_15ins1':'Synthetic_control');
  };
  return {reference:flank+sequence+dna(60),cut:flank.length+cut,control:make(false),sample:make(true)};
}
/** Minimal standards-shaped ABIF writer used only for the labelled demo. */
export function writeAbif(sequence:string,peaks:number[],channels:Uint16Array[],name:string,order='ACGT',quality=40):ArrayBuffer {
  const encoder=new TextEncoder(),shorts=(values:ArrayLike<number>)=>{const out=new Uint8Array(values.length*2),v=new DataView(out.buffer);Array.from(values).forEach((x,i)=>v.setUint16(i*2,x));return out;};
  const fields=[{tag:'FWO_',number:1,type:2,size:1,data:encoder.encode(order)},
    {tag:'PBAS',number:2,type:2,size:1,data:encoder.encode(sequence)},
    {tag:'PLOC',number:2,type:3,size:2,data:shorts(peaks)},
    {tag:'PCON',number:2,type:2,size:1,data:new Uint8Array(sequence.length).fill(quality)},
    {tag:'SMPL',number:1,type:19,size:1,data:encoder.encode(name+'\0')},
    ...Array.from(order,(base,i)=>({tag:'DATA',number:9+i,type:3,size:2,data:shorts(channels[TRACE_BASES.indexOf(base as any)])})),
    {tag:'PBAS',number:1,type:2,size:1,data:encoder.encode(sequence)},
    {tag:'PLOC',number:1,type:3,size:2,data:shorts(peaks)},
    {tag:'PCON',number:1,type:2,size:1,data:new Uint8Array(sequence.length).fill(quality)},];
  const rootOffset=128;let offset=rootOffset+28*fields.length;
  const total=offset+fields.reduce((n,f)=>n+(f.data.length>4?f.data.length:0),0),buffer=new ArrayBuffer(total),bytes=new Uint8Array(buffer),view=new DataView(buffer);
  bytes.set(encoder.encode('ABIF'));view.setUint16(4,101);bytes.set(encoder.encode('tdir'),6);view.setUint16(14,1023);view.setUint16(16,28);view.setUint32(18,fields.length);view.setUint32(22,fields.length*28);view.setUint32(26,rootOffset);
  fields.forEach((f,i)=>{const at=rootOffset+i*28;bytes.set(encoder.encode(f.tag),at);view.setUint32(at+4,f.number);view.setUint16(at+8,f.type);view.setUint16(at+10,f.size);view.setUint32(at+12,f.data.length/f.size);view.setUint32(at+16,f.data.length);
    if(f.data.length<=4)bytes.set(f.data,at+20);else{view.setUint32(at+20,offset);bytes.set(f.data,offset);offset+=f.data.length;}
  });
  return buffer;
}

/** Known two-amplicon mixture; constructed traces, not biological validation. */
export function sangerPooledDemo() {
  let seed=3921881;const dna=(n:number)=>Array.from({length:n},()=>{seed=(Math.imul(seed,1664525)+1013904223)>>>0;return 'ACGT'[seed>>>30];}).join('');
  const primer='CCGGCGGGTACGACCATAAC',first=dna(680),second=dna(60)+first.slice(0,620),cuts=[230,290],refs=[first,second],trim=12;
  const make=(edited:boolean)=>{
    const count=580,peaks=Array.from({length:count},(_,i)=>14+i*10),channels=TRACE_BASES.map(()=>new Uint16Array(count*10+30).fill(2)),calls:string[]=[];
    for(let i=0;i<count;i++){
      const p=i+trim,values=[0,0,0,0];
      refs.forEach((ref,h)=>{
        const parts=edited&&p>=cuts[h]?(h===0?[{size:0,f:.6},{size:-1,f:.25},{size:1,f:.15}]:[{size:0,f:.4},{size:-3,f:.6}]):[{size:0,f:1}];
        for(const part of parts){const base=ref[p-part.size];values[TRACE_BASES.indexOf(base as any)]+=(h===0?.55:.45)*part.f;}
      });
      calls.push(TRACE_BASES[values.indexOf(Math.max(...values))]);
      values.forEach((value,c)=>{for(let d=-5;d<=5;d++)channels[c][peaks[i]+d]+=Math.round(value*1200*Math.exp(-d*d/4));});
    }
    return writeAbif(calls.join(''),peaks,channels,edited?'Synthetic_pooled_sample':'Synthetic_pooled_control');
  };
  return {primer,amplicons:refs.map((sequence,i)=>({name:`Demo-${'AB'[i]}`,sequence:primer+sequence,cut:primer.length+cuts[i]})),control:make(false),sample:make(true)};
}

/** Two nearby substitutions with three unchanged bases; consensus-only fixture. */
export function sangerLocalConsensusDemo(){
 const original=sangerAnalysisDemo(),reference=original.reference,cut=original.cut,positions=[cut-2,cut+2],sequence=Array.from(reference).map((b,i)=>positions.includes(i)?(b==='A'?'C':'A'):b).join('');
 const peaks=Array.from({length:sequence.length},(_,i)=>14+i*10),channels=TRACE_BASES.map(()=>new Uint16Array(sequence.length*10+30).fill(2));
 Array.from(sequence).forEach((b,i)=>{const c=TRACE_BASES.indexOf(b as any);if(c>=0)for(let d=-5;d<=5;d++)channels[c][peaks[i]+d]+=Math.round(1100*Math.exp(-d*d/4));});
 return {reference,cut,positions,sample:writeAbif(sequence,peaks,channels,'Synthetic_local_substitutions')};
}

/** Two targets, including a compensating −1/+1 allele. Entire molecules are
 * synthesized independently of the inference proposal generator. */
export function sangerJointDemo(){
 let seed=9201;const dna=()=>Array.from({length:900},()=>{seed=(Math.imul(seed,1664525)+1013904223)>>>0;return 'ACGT'[seed>>>30];}).join('');
 const primer='ACGTTAGCTACGATCGATGC',refs=[dna(),dna()],cuts=[[260,370],[305,415]];
 const edited=[refs[0].slice(0,260)+refs[0].slice(261,370)+'T'+refs[0].slice(370),refs[1].slice(0,305)+refs[1].slice(307)];
 const make=(sample:boolean)=>{const molecules=sample?[{sequence:refs[0],weight:.3},{sequence:edited[0],weight:.3},{sequence:refs[1],weight:.2},{sequence:edited[1],weight:.2}]:[{sequence:refs[0],weight:.6},{sequence:refs[1],weight:.4}];
  const n=720,peaks=Array.from({length:n},(_,i)=>14+i*10),channels=TRACE_BASES.map(()=>new Uint16Array(n*10+30).fill(2)),calls:string[]=[];
  for(let i=0;i<n;i++){const values=TRACE_BASES.map(b=>molecules.reduce((sum,m)=>sum+(m.sequence[i+12]===b?m.weight:0),0));calls.push(TRACE_BASES[values.indexOf(Math.max(...values))]);values.forEach((v,b)=>{for(let d=-5;d<=5;d++)channels[b][peaks[i]+d]+=Math.round(v*12000*Math.exp(-d*d/4));});}
  return writeAbif(calls.join(''),peaks,channels,sample?'Synthetic_joint_sample':'Synthetic_joint_control');
 };
 return {primer,amplicons:refs.map((sequence,i)=>({name:`Joint-${'AB'[i]}`,sequence:primer+sequence,cut:primer.length+cuts[i][0],cuts:cuts[i].map(c=>c+primer.length)})),control:make(false),sample:make(true)};
}

END MODULE


BEGIN MODULE src/app/utils/sanger-annotation.ts
import {sangerSequenceChoices,sangerSequenceKey,SANGER_SEQUENCE_CANDIDATE_LIMIT,SequenceState} from './sanger-sequence-groups';
import {SangerAnnotation, SangerJointEvent, SangerGroupSupport} from '../models/sanger-analysis';
import {AlignmentToken} from '../models/analysis.model';
import {resolveAnnotationCutSite} from '../components/result-dashboard/annotation-cut-site';

export interface SangerPlacementView {
 fraction:number; tokens:AlignmentToken[]; location:string; events:SangerJointEvent[];
}
export interface SangerAnnotationRow {
 fractionRange?:[number,number];id:string;sequenceKey?:string;sequenceState:SequenceState;size:number; edited:boolean; patternLabel?:string; fraction:number; classification:string; tokens:AlignmentToken[];
 placements:SangerPlacementView[]; representative:SangerPlacementView|null; positionLabel:string; support:SangerGroupSupport|null;
}
export interface SangerAnnotationView {
 name:string; direction:'forward'|'reverse'; reference:string; start:number; end:number;
 cutIndex:number; cutIndices:number[]; share:number; estimable:boolean;
 rows:SangerAnnotationRow[]; hiddenFraction:number;
}
type Component=SangerAnnotation['components'][number];

export function tokensFor(a:SangerAnnotation,start:number,end:number,events?:SangerJointEvent[]):AlignmentToken[]{
 const tokens:AlignmentToken[]=[];
 const append=(type:AlignmentToken['type'],val:string)=>{
  const previous=tokens.at(-1);if(previous?.type===type)previous.val+=val;else tokens.push({type,val});
 };
 const substitution=(e:SangerJointEvent)=>e.end>e.start&&e.inserted===e.end-e.start&&e.insertedSequence?.length===e.inserted;
 for(let p=start;p<end;p++){
  for(const e of events||[])if(p===e.start&&e.inserted&&!substitution(e))append('insert',e.insertedSequence||'N'.repeat(e.inserted));
  const edit=events?.find(e=>p>=e.start&&p<e.end);
  if(edit&&substitution(edit)){const base=edit.insertedSequence![p-edit.start];append(base===a.reference[p]?'equal':'substitute',base);}else append(edit?'delete':'equal',a.reference[p]);
 }
 for(const e of events||[])if(e.start===end&&e.inserted&&!substitution(e))append('insert',e.insertedSequence||'N'.repeat(e.inserted));
 return tokens;
}
function locationFor(events:SangerJointEvent[]):string{
 return events.map(e=>e.inserted>0&&e.inserted===e.end-e.start?`Substitution bases ${e.start+1}–${e.end}`:e.end>e.start?e.end===e.start+1?`Deletion base ${e.start+1}`:`Deletion bases ${e.start+1}–${e.end}`:`Insertion after base ${e.start}`).join('; ');
}
/** Sequence-specific rows conserve fitted reference/size mass. Mixed-base
 * combinations remain model-derived candidates, not molecule-level phasing. */
export function sangerAnnotationViews(items:SangerAnnotation[],threshold:number,radius=30,showUnconfirmed=true):SangerAnnotationView[]{
 const cutoff=Math.max(0,Math.min(100,Number(threshold)||0))/100;
 return items.map(a=>{
  const cuts=a.cuts?.length?a.cuts:[a.cut],start=Math.max(0,Math.min(...cuts)-radius),end=Math.min(a.reference.length,Math.max(...cuts)+radius);
  type Group={components:Component[];size:number;fraction:number;events:SangerJointEvent[];sequenceKey?:string;state:SequenceState};
  const grouped=new Map<string,Group>();
  const rank:Record<SequenceState,number>={'base-call':0,mixture:1,unphased:2,unresolved:3};
  for(const c of a.components){
   if(c.fraction<=0)continue;
   const pattern=c.pathSizes?.join('/')??`${c.size}:${c.size===0&&c.events?.length?'edited':'net'}`;
   const choices=c.unresolved||!c.events?.length?[{events:[],weight:1,state:c.size===0&&!c.unresolved?'base-call' as const:'unresolved' as const}]:sangerSequenceChoices(a.reference,c.events);
   for(const choice of choices){if(a.sequenceGrouping&&choice.events.length&&choice.state!=='unresolved')choice.state='unphased';const sequenceKey=choice.state==='unresolved'?undefined:sangerSequenceKey(a.reference,choice.events),key=pattern+':'+(sequenceKey||'unresolved'),fraction=c.fraction*choice.weight,previous=grouped.get(key);
    if(previous){previous.fraction+=fraction;previous.components.push(c);if(rank[choice.state]>rank[previous.state])previous.state=choice.state;}
    else grouped.set(key,{components:[c],size:c.size,fraction,events:choice.events,sequenceKey,state:choice.state});
   }
  }
  const ordered=[...grouped.entries()].sort(([,x],[,y])=>y.fraction-x.fraction||Math.abs(x.size)-Math.abs(y.size)||x.size-y.size);
  // Keep a bounded number of concrete rows without discarding small-group mass.
  let concrete=0;const retained:typeof ordered=[],remainders=new Map<string,Group>();
  for(const [key,g] of ordered){if(g.state==='unresolved'||concrete<SANGER_SEQUENCE_CANDIDATE_LIMIT){retained.push([key,g]);if(g.state!=='unresolved')concrete++;}else{const pattern=g.components[0].pathSizes?.join('/')??String(g.size),old=remainders.get(pattern);if(old)old.fraction+=g.fraction;else remainders.set(pattern,{...g,events:[],sequenceKey:undefined,state:'unresolved'});}}
  for(const [pattern,g] of remainders){const old=retained.find(([,r])=>r.state==='unresolved'&&(r.components[0].pathSizes?.join('/')??String(r.size))===pattern);if(old)old[1].fraction+=g.fraction;else retained.push(['remainder:'+pattern,g]);}
  const displayable=a.estimable||(showUnconfirmed&&a.share>0);
  const rows:SangerAnnotationRow[]=displayable?retained.filter(([,g])=>g.fraction+1e-12>=cutoff).sort(([,x],[,y])=>y.fraction-x.fraction||Math.abs(x.size)-Math.abs(y.size)).map(([id,g])=>{
   const c=g.components[0],isBaseline=g.size===0&&!c.events?.length&&!c.unresolved,events=g.state==='unresolved'?[]:g.events;
   const placements:SangerPlacementView[]=events.length?[{fraction:g.fraction,events,tokens:tokensFor(a,start,end,events),location:locationFor(events)}]:[];
   const classification=g.state==='unresolved'?'Sequence / breakpoint unresolved':isBaseline?'No net indel model':g.state==='unphased'?'Unphased sequence candidate':g.size===0?(events.every(e=>e.inserted===e.end-e.start)?'Substitution':'Net-zero multi-target edit'):g.size%3===0?'In-frame indel':'Out-of-frame indel';
   const representative=placements[0]||null,positionLabel=isBaseline?'No net indel placement':!representative?'Sequence / position not estimated':`Best fit: ${representative.location}`;
   return {id,sequenceKey:g.sequenceKey,sequenceState:g.state,size:g.size,edited:!isBaseline,patternLabel:c.pathSizes?.map((v,t)=>`T${t+1}: ${v>0?'+':''}${v} bp`).join(' · '),fraction:g.fraction,classification,placements,representative,positionLabel,support:c.support||null,tokens:representative?.tokens||tokensFor(a,start,end)};
  }):[];
  const reference=a.reference.slice(start,end);
  return {name:a.name,direction:a.direction,reference,start,end,
   cutIndex:resolveAnnotationCutSite({ref_sequence:reference,cut_site_index:a.cut-start})!,cutIndices:cuts.map(c=>c-start),
   share:a.share,estimable:a.estimable,rows,
   hiddenFraction:displayable?retained.map(([,g])=>g).filter(g=>g.fraction+1e-12<cutoff).reduce((s,g)=>s+g.fraction,0):0};
 });
}

END MODULE


BEGIN MODULE src/app/utils/sanger-automatic-template.ts
import {resolveSangerReference} from './sanger-design.utils';
/** Read design metadata without importing obsolete analysis-engine or read-range
 * choices. Measured coverage is established later from the uploaded traces. */
export function parseAutomaticSangerTemplate(value:unknown){
 const v=value as any;if(v?.format!=='casmango-sanger-analysis-v1'||!Array.isArray(v.amplicons)||!v.amplicons.length||v.amplicons.length>4)throw Error('Use a Sanger primer template with 1–4 references.');
 const references=v.amplicons.map((a:any)=>{if(!Array.isArray(a.cuts)||a.cuts.length<1||a.cuts.length>2)throw Error('Use one or two targets per reference.');const r=resolveSangerReference(a.name,a.sequence,a.cuts.join(','),'cuts');if(Array.isArray(a.guides)&&a.guides.length===r.cuts.length&&a.guides.every((g:unknown)=>typeof g==='string'&&/^[ACGT]{17,30}$/i.test(g))){const g=resolveSangerReference(a.name,a.sequence,a.guides.join(','),'guides');if(g.cuts.some((cut,i)=>cut!==r.cuts[i]))throw Error('Template gRNA and cut positions disagree.');return {name:r.name,sequence:r.sequence,targets:a.guides.join(','),mode:'guides' as 'guides'|'cuts'};}return {name:r.name,sequence:r.sequence,targets:r.cuts.join(','),mode:'cuts' as 'guides'|'cuts'};});
 if(new Set(v.amplicons.map((a:any)=>a.cuts.length)).size!==1)throw Error('Provide corresponding targets in each reference.');
 if(new Set(references.map((r:any)=>r.name)).size!==references.length)throw Error('Reference names must be unique.');
 const primer=typeof v.primer==='string'?v.primer.replace(/\s/g,'').toUpperCase():'';
 if((references.length>1||references.some((r:any)=>r.targets.includes(',')))&&!/^[ACGT]{12,40}$/.test(primer))throw Error('A common sequencing primer is required for multiple references.');
 const s=v.settings||{},automatic=s.analysisPolicy==='automatic',maxDeletion=automatic?(s.maxDeletion??s.maxIndel??10):s.largeDeletions?(s.maxDeletion??200):(s.maxIndel??10),maxInsertion=automatic?(s.maxInsertion??s.maxIndel??10):s.largeDeletions?(s.maxInsertion??s.maxIndel??10):(s.maxIndel??10);
 if(!Number.isInteger(maxDeletion)||maxDeletion<1||maxDeletion>500||!Number.isInteger(maxInsertion)||maxInsertion<1||maxInsertion>30)throw Error('Use deletion limit 1–500 and insertion limit 1–30 bp.');
 const oppositeCandidates=v.amplicons.map((a:any)=>[a.forwardPrimer,a.reversePrimer].find((p:unknown)=>typeof p==='string'&&p.replace(/\s/g,'').toUpperCase()!==primer));
 const oppositePrimer=typeof v.oppositePrimer==='string'?v.oppositePrimer.replace(/\s/g,'').toUpperCase():oppositeCandidates.every((p:unknown)=>typeof p==='string')&&new Set(oppositeCandidates).size===1?String(oppositeCandidates[0]).replace(/\s/g,'').toUpperCase():'';
 if(oppositePrimer&&!/^[ACGT]{12,40}$/.test(oppositePrimer))throw Error('The opposite sequencing primer must contain 12–40 A/C/G/T bases.');
 return {references,primer,oppositePrimer,maxDeletion,maxInsertion,window:Number.isInteger(s.window)&&s.window>=60&&s.window<=500?s.window:200};
}

END MODULE


BEGIN MODULE src/app/utils/sanger-benchmark-assessment.ts
import type{NativeBenchmarkCertificate}from'./sanger-native-signal-certificate';
import {benchmarkComparison,BenchmarkTruth,BenchmarkReference} from './sanger-benchmark';
import {certifySignalEquivalence} from './sanger-signal-equivalence-benchmark';
import type {SangerAnalysisResult} from '../models/sanger-analysis';
/** Keep point recovery and certified equivalence visible as separate outcomes.
 * The latter is accepted only with an exact, complete, zero-extra witness. */
export function assessSangerBenchmark(data:BenchmarkTruth,refs:BenchmarkReference[],result:SangerAnalysisResult,threshold=1,nativeCertificate?:NativeBenchmarkCertificate|null){
 const rows=benchmarkComparison(data,refs,result,threshold),certificates=certifySignalEquivalence(data,result),native=nativeCertificate?.kind==='exact-native-ab1-equivalence'&&nativeCertificate.sourceAnnotationsKey===JSON.stringify(result.annotations)&&nativeCertificate.truthKey===JSON.stringify(data.truth)?nativeCertificate:null,certified=new Set([...certificates.map(c=>c.reference),...(native?.references||[])]),correct=(r:typeof rows[number])=>(r.estimated??0)>0&&r.delta!==null&&Math.abs(r.delta)<=2,edited=rows.filter(r=>r.scope==='group'&&(r.truth??0)>0&&r.group!=='WT'),wt=rows.filter(r=>r.group==='WT'),extras=rows.filter(r=>r.scope==='group'&&r.truth===0&&(r.estimated??0)>=threshold),referenceRows=rows.filter(r=>r.scope==='reference'),accepted=(r:typeof rows[number])=>correct(r)||certified.has(r.reference),unexplained=edited.filter(r=>!accepted(r)),falseExtras=extras.filter(r=>!certified.has(r.reference));
 return{rows,certificates,nativeCertificate:native,total:edited.length,pointRecovered:edited.filter(correct).length,signalEquivalent:edited.filter(r=>!correct(r)&&certified.has(r.reference)).length,unexplained:unexplained.length,unexplainedGroups:unexplained,wtPointRecovered:wt.filter(correct).length,wtAccepted:wt.filter(accepted).length,wtTotal:wt.length,extraGroups:extras.length,falseExtraGroups:falseExtras.length,equivalentExtraGroups:extras.filter(r=>certified.has(r.reference)).length,referenceAllocationPassed:referenceRows.every(r=>r.delta!==null&&Math.abs(r.delta)<=2),signalQc:result.mixture?.passesChecks===true,byTarget:[...new Set(edited.map(r=>r.target??0))].sort().map(target=>({target:target+1,total:edited.filter(r=>(r.target??0)===target).length,pointRecovered:edited.filter(r=>(r.target??0)===target&&correct(r)).length,signalEquivalent:edited.filter(r=>(r.target??0)===target&&!correct(r)&&certified.has(r.reference)).length,unexplained:edited.filter(r=>(r.target??0)===target&&!accepted(r)).length})),pass:edited.length>0&&!unexplained.length&&!falseExtras.length&&wt.every(accepted)&&referenceRows.every(r=>r.delta!==null&&Math.abs(r.delta)<=2)&&result.mixture?.passesChecks===true};
}

END MODULE


BEGIN MODULE src/app/utils/sanger-benchmark.ts
import{exactDisplayView}from'./sanger-exact-display';
import {compareExactProfiles}from'./sanger-exact-profile-benchmark';
import {compareExactOptions} from './sanger-exact-benchmark';
import {projectTargetAnnotation,projectTargetTruth} from './sanger-target-profile';
import {SynthTruth,SynthReference,SynthComparison,compareSangerSynth} from './sanger-synthetic-benchmark';
import {SangerAnalysisResult} from '../models/sanger-analysis';
import {reverseComplement} from './biology.utils';
export interface BenchmarkTruth {truth:SynthTruth[];recipe?:SynthReference[];sequenceTruth:boolean}
export interface BenchmarkReference {name:string;sequence:string;cuts:number[]}
const number=(v:unknown,label:string)=>{if(typeof v==='string'&&!v.trim()||v===null||v===undefined||!Number.isFinite(Number(v))||Number(v)<0||Number(v)>100)throw Error('Invalid ground truth '+label+' (0–100%).');return Number(v);};
function csv(text:string){const rows:string[][]=[];let row:string[]=[],cell='',quoted=false;for(let i=0;i<text.length;i++){const c=text[i];if(c==='"'){if(quoted&&text[i+1]==='"'){cell+='"';i++;}else quoted=!quoted;}else if(!quoted&&(c===','||c==='\n'||c==='\r')){row.push(cell);cell='';if(c!==','){if(c==='\r'&&text[i+1]==='\n')i++;if(row.some(v=>v.trim()))rows.push(row);row=[];}}else cell+=c;}if(quoted)throw Error('Unclosed quote in ground truth CSV.');row.push(cell);if(row.some(v=>v.trim()))rows.push(row);return rows;}
export function parseBenchmarkTruth(value:unknown):BenchmarkTruth {
 let raw:any=value,recipe:SynthReference[]|undefined;
 if(typeof raw==='string'){const text=raw.replace(/^\uFEFF/,'').trim();if(text.startsWith('{')||text.startsWith('['))raw=JSON.parse(text);else {const [header,...rows]=csv(text),required=['Reference','Source reference','Group','Reference %','Within-reference %','Total sample %','Target size pattern','Edits'];if(!header||required.some(h=>!header.includes(h)))throw Error('Use the generated truth.csv or truth.json file.');raw=rows.map(c=>{const get=(key:string)=>c[header.indexOf(key)];return {reference:get('Reference'),sourceReference:get('Source reference'),allele:get('Group'),share:get('Reference %'),percent:get('Within-reference %'),totalPercent:get('Total sample %'),pattern:get('Target size pattern'),description:get('Edits'),events:[],sequence:'',inputSequence:'',substitution:/substitution|→/.test(get('Edits')||'')};});}}
 if(!Array.isArray(raw)){recipe=raw?.recipe;raw=raw?.truth;}
 if(!Array.isArray(raw)||!raw.length||raw.length>4096)throw Error('Ground truth requires 1–4096 group rows.');
 const truth:SynthTruth[]=raw.map((t:any)=>{if(!t||typeof t.reference!=='string'||!t.reference.trim()||t.reference.length>200||typeof t.allele!=='string'||typeof t.description!=='string'||!/^[-+]?\d+(\/[-+]?\d+)?$/.test(String(t.pattern))||String(t.pattern).split('/').some((s:string)=>Math.abs(Number(s))>500))throw Error('Invalid ground truth reference, group or target size pattern.');const share=number(t.share,'reference share'),percent=number(t.percent,'group percentage'),totalPercent=number(t.totalPercent,'sample percentage');if(Math.abs(totalPercent-share*percent/100)>.002)throw Error('Ground truth sample percentages disagree with reference × group percentages.');if(t.inputSequence&&!/^[ACGTN]+$/.test(t.inputSequence)||t.sequence&&!/^[ACGTN]+$/.test(t.sequence))throw Error('Invalid ground truth sequence.');return {...t,pattern:String(t.pattern),share,percent,totalPercent,events:Array.isArray(t.events)?t.events:[],sequence:t.sequence||'',inputSequence:t.inputSequence||'',substitution:!!t.substitution};});
 const names=[...new Set(truth.map(t=>t.reference))];let shareTotal=0;
 for(const name of names){const rows=truth.filter(t=>t.reference===name),share=rows[0].share;shareTotal+=share;if(rows.some(t=>Math.abs(t.share-share)>.001)||Math.abs(rows.reduce((s,t)=>s+t.percent,0)-100)>.002)throw Error(name+': ground truth group percentages must total 100%, with one reference share.');if(new Set(rows.map(t=>t.allele)).size!==rows.length)throw Error(name+': ground truth group names must be unique.');}
 if(Math.abs(shareTotal-100)>.002)throw Error('Ground truth reference shares must total 100%.');
 if(recipe!==undefined&&(!Array.isArray(recipe)||recipe.length>4||recipe.some(r=>!r||typeof r.name!=='string'||typeof r.sequence!=='string'||!/^[ACGTN]+$/.test(r.sequence)||!Array.isArray(r.cuts)||r.cuts.some(c=>!Number.isInteger(c)||c<0||c>r.sequence.length))))throw Error('Invalid ground truth reference recipe.');
 return {truth,recipe,sequenceTruth:truth.every(t=>!!t.inputSequence)};
}
export function validateBenchmarkTruth(data:BenchmarkTruth,refs:BenchmarkReference[]){
 const names=new Set(refs.map(r=>r.name));if(data.truth.some(t=>!names.has(t.reference))||data.recipe?.some(r=>!names.has(r.name)))throw Error('Ground truth reference names do not match the analysis. Load the matching analysis-template.json.');
 for(const ref of refs){const rows=data.truth.filter(t=>t.reference===ref.name),recipe=data.recipe?.find(r=>r.name===ref.name);if(!rows.length&&recipe?.share!==0)throw Error(ref.name+': missing ground truth reference.');if(rows.some(t=>t.pattern.split('/').length!==ref.cuts.length))throw Error(ref.name+': ground truth target count does not match the analysis.');if(recipe){const forward=recipe.sequence===ref.sequence,reverse=recipe.sequence===reverseComplement(ref.sequence),cuts=forward?recipe.cuts:[...recipe.cuts].map(c=>ref.sequence.length-c);if(!forward&&!reverse||cuts.length!==ref.cuts.length||cuts.some((c,i)=>c!==ref.cuts[i]))throw Error(ref.name+': ground truth sequence or cut positions do not match.');}const wt=rows.find(t=>t.description==='WT'&&t.inputSequence);if(wt&&wt.inputSequence!==ref.sequence)throw Error(ref.name+': WT ground truth sequence does not match the analysis reference.');}
}
function detectedViews(result:SangerAnalysisResult,target:number,threshold:number){const cutoff=Math.max(0,Math.min(100,Number(threshold)||0))/100;return(result.annotations||[]).map(a=>{const view=exactDisplayView(a,target,60,result.exactAlternatives?.find(s=>s.reference===a.name&&s.targets.includes(target+1)));return{...view,rows:view.rows.filter(row=>!row.edited||row.fraction+1e-12>=cutoff)};});}
function rawBenchmarkComparison(data:BenchmarkTruth,refs:BenchmarkReference[],result:SangerAnalysisResult,threshold=1):SynthComparison[]{if(refs.some(r=>r.cuts.length>1)){return [0,1].flatMap(target=>{const annotations=(result.annotations||[]).map(a=>projectTargetAnnotation(a,target)),truth=refs.flatMap(r=>{const a=result.annotations?.find(a=>a.name===r.name);return a?projectTargetTruth(data,a,target).truth:[];}),projected={...result,annotations};return compareSangerSynth({truth,template:{amplicons:refs.map(r=>({...r,cuts:[r.cuts[target]]}))}},projected,detectedViews(result,target,threshold)).map(row=>({...row,target}));});}return compareSangerSynth({truth:data.truth,template:{amplicons:refs}},result,detectedViews(result,0,threshold));}
export function benchmarkMetrics(data:BenchmarkTruth,name:string,target?:number,joint=false,sequenceGroups=false){const rows=data.truth.filter(t=>t.reference===name);if(!rows.length)return null;let wt=0,inFrame=0,outOfFrame=0,substitution=0;for(const row of rows){const sizes=row.pattern.split('/').map(Number),size=target===undefined?sizes.reduce((s,n)=>s+n,0):sizes[target],noEdit=row.description==='WT';if(size===undefined)return null;const fraction=row.percent/100;if(sequenceGroups&&target!==undefined){if(row.substitution&&!row.events.length)return null;const events=row.events.filter(e=>e.target===target);if(noEdit||!events.length&&size===0){wt+=fraction;continue;}if(events.length&&events.every(e=>e.kind==='substitution')){substitution+=fraction;continue;}if(size%3===0)inFrame+=fraction;else outOfFrame+=fraction;continue;}if(row.substitution)substitution+=fraction;if(joint&&target===undefined?noEdit:size===0)wt+=fraction;else if(size%3===0){if(!(joint&&target===undefined&&row.substitution&&sizes.every(n=>n===0)))inFrame+=fraction;}else outOfFrame+=fraction;}return {share:(rows[0]?.share||0)/100,wt,inFrame,outOfFrame,substitution};}
export function benchmarkPattern(row:{size:number;patternLabel?:string}){return row.patternLabel?row.patternLabel.split(' · ').map(p=>Number(p.split(': ')[1].replace(' bp',''))).join('/'):String(row.size);}
export function benchmarkCsv(rows:SynthComparison[]){const multi=rows.some(r=>r.target!==undefined);return [['Reference',...(multi?['Target']:[]),'Group','Ground truth %','Estimated %','Delta pp','Recovery','Position / sequence','Detected sequence'],...rows.map(r=>[r.reference,...(multi?['T'+((r.target??0)+1)]:[]),r.group,r.truth??'',r.estimated??'',r.delta??'',r.recovery,r.placement,r.scope==='group'&&r.estimated!==null&&r.estimated>0])].map(row=>row.map(v=>'"'+String(v).replace(/"/g,'""')+'"').join(',')).join('\r\n');}

/** Public scoring uses only the current inferred sequences and fractions.
 * Membership of an alternative signal fiber never earns detection credit. */
export function benchmarkComparison(data:BenchmarkTruth,refs:BenchmarkReference[],result:SangerAnalysisResult,threshold=1):SynthComparison[]{return rawBenchmarkComparison(data,refs,result,threshold);}
/** Explicit research diagnostic, separate from detection scores and UI switching. */
export function benchmarkCompatibilityDiagnostics(data:BenchmarkTruth,refs:BenchmarkReference[],result:SangerAnalysisResult):SynthComparison[]{return compareExactProfiles(data,compareExactOptions(rawBenchmarkComparison(data,refs,result),result),result);}

END MODULE


BEGIN MODULE src/app/utils/sanger-design-export.ts
import {validateSangerProductBounds} from './sanger-product-bounds';
import {SangerCandidate,SangerDesignResult} from '../models/sanger-design';
import {SANGER_ANALYSIS_DEFAULTS,SangerAnalysisSettings} from '../models/sanger-analysis';
import {cleanReference} from './sanger-design.utils';
import {exactBindingSites} from '../workers/core/sanger-primer-designer';
export interface DesignedAmplicon {forwardPrimer?:string;reversePrimer?:string;name:string;sequence:string;cuts:number[];targetNames?:string[];guides?:string[];sourceReference:string;start:number;end:number}
export interface SangerAnalysisTemplate {format:'casmango-sanger-analysis-v1';primer:string;oppositePrimer?:string;amplicons:DesignedAmplicon[];settings:SangerAnalysisSettings;scopeNote:string}
export function selectedAmplicons(result:SangerDesignResult,c:SangerCandidate):DesignedAmplicon[]{
 if(!c.partner)throw new Error('Select a candidate with an opposite PCR primer to export complete amplicons.');
 return c.sites.map((hit,i)=>{const ref=result.references[c.referenceIndex??i],other=c.partner!.sites[i],start=Math.min(hit.start,other.start),end=Math.max(hit.end,other.end);return {name:ref.name.replace(/[^a-zA-Z0-9_-]/g,'_')+'_'+(start+1)+'-'+end,sequence:ref.sequence.slice(start,end),forwardPrimer:c.direction==='forward'?c.sequence:c.partner!.sequence,reversePrimer:c.direction==='reverse'?c.sequence:c.partner!.sequence,cuts:ref.cuts.map(cut=>cut-start),sourceReference:ref.name,start,end,...(ref.targetNames?{targetNames:[...ref.targetNames]}:{}),...(ref.guides?{guides:[...ref.guides]}:{})};});
}
export function makeAnalysisTemplate(result:SangerDesignResult,c:SangerCandidate):SangerAnalysisTemplate{
 if(result.settings.platform==='ngs')throw new Error('This is an NGS design. Download Amplicon FASTA and Targets CSV for the main analysis.');
 const amplicons=selectedAmplicons(result,c),settings={...SANGER_ANALYSIS_DEFAULTS,analysisPolicy:'automatic' as const,maxDeletion:result.settings.indelRange,maxInsertion:Math.min(30,result.settings.indelRange),scope:amplicons.length===1?'single' as const:'pooled' as const,window:result.settings.window,maxIndel:Math.min(30,result.settings.indelRange),readStart:result.settings.readStart,readEnd:result.settings.readEnd,direction:amplicons.length===1?c.direction:'auto' as const};
 return validateAnalysisTemplate({format:'casmango-sanger-analysis-v1',primer:c.sequence,oppositePrimer:c.partner!.sequence,amplicons,settings,scopeNote:'Predicted PCR products. Confirm actual submitted amplicons after sequencing; no trace data or experimental validation included.'});
}
export function validateAnalysisTemplate(value:unknown):SangerAnalysisTemplate{
 const v=value as SangerAnalysisTemplate;
 if(v?.format!=='casmango-sanger-analysis-v1'||!Array.isArray(v.amplicons)||!v.amplicons.length||v.amplicons.length>4)throw new Error('Sanger templates support 1–4 submitted amplicons.');
 const a=v.amplicons.map(r=>{if(typeof r.name!=='string'||!r.name.trim()||typeof r.sequence!=='string'||!Array.isArray(r.cuts)||(r.cuts.length<1||r.cuts.length>2))throw new Error('Sanger analysis supports one or two cut sites per amplicon.');const sequence=cleanReference(r.sequence);if(sequence.length>20000||new Set(r.cuts).size!==r.cuts.length||r.cuts.some(c=>!Number.isInteger(c)||c<=0||c>=sequence.length))throw new Error('Invalid submitted amplicon or cut position.');const product={...r,sequence};validateSangerProductBounds({...product,cut:r.cuts[0]});return product;});
 if(new Set(a.map(r=>r.name)).size!==a.length)throw new Error('Template amplicon names must be unique.');
 const primer=typeof v.primer==='string'?v.primer.replace(/\s/g,'').toUpperCase():'';if(!/^[ACGT]{16,30}$/.test(primer))throw new Error('Template requires the sequencing primer (16–30 nt).');
 const s={...SANGER_ANALYSIS_DEFAULTS,...v.settings,scope:a.length===1?'single' as const:'pooled' as const,method:'mixture' as const};
 const automatic=s.analysisPolicy==='automatic';if(automatic){s.maxDeletion=v.settings?.maxDeletion??s.maxIndel;s.maxInsertion=v.settings?.maxInsertion??Math.min(30,s.maxIndel);}
 const reserve=automatic?Math.max(s.maxDeletion??s.maxIndel,s.maxInsertion??s.maxIndel):s.maxIndel;
 if(!Number.isInteger(s.window)||s.window<60||s.window>500||!Number.isInteger(s.maxIndel)||s.maxIndel<1||s.maxIndel>(automatic?30:a.length>1?15:30))throw new Error('Sanger analysis requires a 60–500 bp window and indel range ±1–15 bp (single: ±1–30). Adjust the design before making a template.');
 if(!Number.isInteger(s.readStart)||!Number.isInteger(s.readEnd)||s.readStart<0||s.readEnd>1000||s.readEnd<=s.readStart)throw new Error('Analysis templates require a usable Sanger range within 0–1,000 bp.');
 if(s.joinNearby!==undefined&&(!Number.isInteger(s.joinNearby)||s.joinNearby<0||s.joinNearby>10))throw new Error('Join nearby changes must be 0–10 matched bases.');
 if(!['auto','forward','reverse'].includes(s.direction)||!Number.isInteger(s.controlPhred)||s.controlPhred<0||s.controlPhred>60||!['classic','calibrated'].includes(s.signalModel||''))throw new Error('Invalid analysis settings.');
 a.forEach(r=>{const hits=exactBindingSites(primer,{...r,cuts:r.cuts});if(hits.length!==1)throw new Error(r.name+': sequencing primer must bind once in the submitted product.');const h=hits[0];for(const inputCut of r.cuts){const cut=h.direction==='forward'?inputCut-h.end:h.start-inputCut;const extensionLength=h.direction==='forward'?r.sequence.length-h.end:h.start;if(cut+Math.ceil(s.window/2)+reserve>extensionLength)throw new Error(r.name+': PCR product ends before the complete analysis region.');if((automatic||a.length>1||r.cuts.length>1||s.largeDeletions)&&cut<s.readStart+72)throw new Error(r.name+': mixed Sanger needs at least 72 upstream bases after the usable read start.');if(cut-Math.floor(s.window/2)-reserve<s.readStart||cut+Math.ceil(s.window/2)+reserve>s.readEnd)throw new Error(r.name+': target window exceeds the usable Sanger range.');}});
 if(s.largeDeletions!==undefined&&typeof s.largeDeletions!=='boolean')throw new Error('Invalid large-deletion setting.');
 if(s.maxDeletion!==undefined&&(!Number.isInteger(s.maxDeletion)||s.maxDeletion<(automatic||s.jointEngine&&s.jointEngine!=='staged'?1:s.maxIndel)||s.maxDeletion>500))throw new Error('Deletion search limit must be 1–500 bp for signal paths, or at least the small-indel range for staged models.');
 if(s.jointEngine!==undefined&&!['staged','signal-local','signal-path','signal-hybrid'].includes(s.jointEngine))throw new Error('Invalid joint inference method.');
 if(s.editStartRadius!==undefined&&(!Number.isInteger(s.editStartRadius)||s.editStartRadius<0||s.editStartRadius>100))throw new Error('Indel origin radius must be 0–100 bp.');
 if(s.maxInsertion!==undefined&&(!Number.isInteger(s.maxInsertion)||s.maxInsertion<1||s.maxInsertion>(automatic?30:60)))throw new Error('Insertion search limit must be 1–60 bp.');
 return {...v,primer,amplicons:a,settings:s};
}

END MODULE


BEGIN MODULE src/app/utils/sanger-design.utils.ts
import {findGrnaCutSite,resolveMatchedCutSite} from '../workers/core/classifier';
import { reverseComplement } from './biology.utils';
import { SangerReference } from '../models/sanger-design';

export function cleanReference(value: string): string {
  const seq = value.replace(/\s/g, '').toUpperCase();
  if (!seq || /[^ACGTN]/.test(seq)) throw new Error('References must contain A, C, G, T or N only.');
  return seq;
}

export function parseSangerFasta(text: string): { name: string; sequence: string }[] {
  const entries: { name: string; sequence: string }[] = [];
  let name = '', lines: string[] = [];
  const flush = () => { if (name) entries.push({ name, sequence: cleanReference(lines.join('')) }); };
  for (const line of text.replace(/^\uFEFF/, '').split(/\r?\n/)) {
    if (!line.trim()) continue;
    if (line.startsWith('>')) {
      flush(); name = line.slice(1).trim(); lines = [];
      if (!name) throw new Error('Each FASTA record needs a name.');
    } else {
      if (!name) throw new Error('Use FASTA headers beginning with >.');
      lines.push(line.trim());
    }
  }
  flush();
  if (!entries.length) throw new Error('No FASTA records found.');
  if (new Set(entries.map(e => e.name)).size !== entries.length) throw new Error('Reference names must be unique.');
  return entries;
}

/** Same matched-target cut convention as CRISPR Analysis. PAM-confirmed
 * sites take priority; a unique exact target is also usable without a PAM. */
export function guideCut(sequence:string,input:string):number {
 const guide=input.replace(/\s/g,'').toUpperCase();
 if(!/^[ACGT]{17,30}$/.test(guide))throw Error('Use a 17–30 nt target sequence, or enter cut coordinates.');
 const hits:{start:number;direction:'forward'|'reverse'}[]=[];
 for(const direction of ['forward','reverse']as const){const query=direction==='forward'?guide:reverseComplement(guide);for(let start=sequence.indexOf(query);start>=0;start=sequence.indexOf(query,start+1))hits.push({start,direction});}
 const confirmed=[...new Set(hits.flatMap(h=>{const cut=resolveMatchedCutSite(sequence,h.start,guide.length,h.direction);return cut.pam_found?[cut.cut_site]:[];}))];
 if(confirmed.length===1)return confirmed[0];
 if(confirmed.length>1||hits.length>1)throw Error('Target has multiple matching sites; use explicit cut coordinates.');
 if(!hits.length)throw Error('No exact target sequence found in either strand.');
 return findGrnaCutSite(sequence,guide).cut_site;
}

export function resolveSangerReference(name: string, dna: string, targets: string, mode: 'cuts' | 'guides'): SangerReference {
  const sequence = cleanReference(dna);
  const tokens = targets.trim().split(/[\s,;]+/).filter(Boolean);
  if (!tokens.length) throw new Error(`${name || 'Reference'}: add at least one target.`);
  const cuts = mode === 'guides' ? tokens.map(g => guideCut(sequence, g)) : tokens.map(t => {
    if (!/^\d+$/.test(t)) throw new Error('Cut positions must be whole numbers.');
    return Number(t);
  });
  if (cuts.some(c => c < 0 || c > sequence.length)) throw new Error(`${name}: cut position is outside the reference.`);
  if (new Set(cuts).size !== cuts.length) throw new Error(`${name}: duplicate target positions.`);
  return { name: name.trim(), sequence, cuts };
}

/** Reproducible constructed geometry example, not biological validation data. */
export function sangerDesignExample(targetCount: 1 | 2 = 1): SangerReference[] {
  let seed = 19481;
  const dna = (n: number) => Array.from({ length: n }, () => {
    seed = (Math.imul(seed, 1664525) + 1013904223) >>> 0;
    return 'ACGT'[seed >>> 30];
  }).join('');
  const primer = 'CCGGCGGGTACGACCATAAC';
  const partner = 'CAGCGACTCCTTGAGTGACG';
  const shared = dna(targetCount === 2 ? 700 : 450);
  return [0, 50, 105].map((offset, i) => {
    const upstream = dna(6000+i*1700) + primer + dna(50 + offset);
    return { name: `Demo-${'ABD'[i]}`, sequence: upstream + shared + reverseComplement(partner) + dna(6500), cuts: (targetCount === 2 ? [100, 350] : [100]).map(cut => upstream.length + cut) };
  });
}

END MODULE


BEGIN MODULE src/app/utils/sanger-exact-benchmark.ts
import {SangerAnalysisResult}from'../models/sanger-analysis';import type{SynthComparison}from'./sanger-synthetic-benchmark';import{sangerSequenceKey}from'./sanger-sequence-groups';import{targetEvents}from'./sanger-target-profile';
/** Evaluation only. Never selects an option, changes inference or reads truth
 * before the result exists. Per-group coverage and joint profile coverage are
 * separate: compatible groups may belong to mutually exclusive alternatives. */
export function compareExactOptions(rows:SynthComparison[],result:SangerAnalysisResult,tolerancePp=2):SynthComparison[]{
 return rows.map(row=>{const set=result.exactAlternatives?.find(s=>s.reference===row.reference),a=result.annotations?.find(a=>a.name===row.reference);if(!set||!a||row.scope!=='group'||row.sequenceScope!=='sequence'||!row.sequenceKey)return row;const target=row.target??0,variable=set.ambiguousSequences[String(target+1)]||[];const sequenceVariable=variable.includes(row.sequenceKey),placementVariable=set.ambiguousPlacements?.[String(target+1)]?.includes(row.sequenceKey);if(!sequenceVariable&&!placementVariable)return row;
 const percent=(option:typeof set.options[number],r:SynthComparison)=>option.components.reduce((sum,c)=>{const events=targetEvents(c.events||[],r.target??0);return sum+(sangerSequenceKey(a.reference,events)===r.sequenceKey?c.fraction*100:0);},0),values=set.options.map(o=>percent(o,row)),matches=row.truth!==null&&row.truth>0?values.flatMap((v,i)=>v>0&&Math.abs(v-row.truth!)<=tolerancePp?[i]:[]):[],profileRows=rows.filter(r=>r.reference===row.reference&&r.scope==='group'&&r.sequenceScope==='sequence'&&!!r.sequenceKey&&(r.truth??0)>0),profileOptions=set.options.flatMap((o,i)=>profileRows.every(r=>Math.abs(percent(o,r)-r.truth!)<=tolerancePp)&&[...new Set(profileRows.map(r=>r.target??0))].every(target=>{const keys=new Set(profileRows.filter(r=>(r.target??0)===target).map(r=>r.sequenceKey));return o.components.reduce((sum,c)=>sum+(keys.has(sangerSequenceKey(a.reference,targetEvents(c.events||[],target)))?0:c.fraction*100),0)<=tolerancePp;})?[i]:[]),optionMatch={indices:matches,sequenceIndices:values.flatMap((v,i)=>v>0?[i]:[]),withinTolerance:matches.length>0,range:[Math.min(...values),Math.max(...values)]as[number,number],profileOptions,tolerancePp};
 return {...row,...(set.selected<0&&sequenceVariable?{estimated:null,delta:null}:{}),optionMatch,recovery:matches.length?'Compatible option':set.selected<0?'Unresolved options':row.recovery,placement:matches.length?'Sequence and percentage match a certified option':row.placement};
 });
}

END MODULE


BEGIN MODULE src/app/utils/sanger-exact-display.ts
import{exactSum,exactSubtract,ExactFraction}from'../workers/core/exact-mixture-fiber';
import{canonicalSangerEvents}from'./sanger-placement';
import{sangerSequenceKey}from'./sanger-sequence-groups';
import {SangerAnnotation,SangerExactAlternatives}from'../models/sanger-analysis';
import {SangerAnnotationView,sangerAnnotationViews,tokensFor}from'./sanger-annotation';
import {projectTargetAnnotation,targetEvents}from'./sanger-target-profile';
/** Only an isolated, exactly certified same-site/length insertion fiber may
 * become an N block. Other ambiguities keep separate fitted groups and point
 * fractions; substitutions, changed origins and mass transfers are never pooled. */
export function exactDisplayView(annotation:SangerAnnotation,target:number,radius:number,set?:SangerExactAlternatives):SangerAnnotationView {
 const projected=(annotation.cuts?.length||1)>1?projectTargetAnnotation(annotation,target):annotation,view=sangerAnnotationViews([projected],0,radius,true)[0];
 if(!set||set.selected>=0||set.certificate!=='exact-rational-emission')return view;
 const base=set.options[0];if(!base||base.exactFractions.length!==base.components.length)return view;
 const local=(c:typeof base.components[number])=>canonicalSangerEvents(annotation.reference,targetEvents(c.events||[],target)),geometry=(events:ReturnType<typeof local>)=>events.length===1&&events[0].start===events[0].end&&events[0].inserted>0&&/^[ACGT]+$/.test(events[0].insertedSequence||'')?events[0].start+':'+events[0].inserted:null;
 const equal=(a:Map<string,ExactFraction>,b:Map<string,ExactFraction>)=>a.size===b.size&&[...a].every(([key,v])=>b.has(key)&&exactSubtract(v,b.get(key)!).n==='0'),distribution=(option:typeof base,geo:string)=>{const inside=new Map<string,ExactFraction[]>(),outside=new Map<string,ExactFraction[]>(),words=new Set<string>();option.components.forEach((c,i)=>{const events=local(c),isInside=geometry(events)===geo,key=sangerSequenceKey(annotation.reference,events),map=isInside?inside:outside;map.set(key,[...(map.get(key)||[]),option.exactFractions[i]]);if(isInside)words.add(events[0].insertedSequence!);});return{inside:new Map([...inside].map(([k,v])=>[k,exactSum(v)])),outside:new Map([...outside].map(([k,v])=>[k,exactSum(v)])),mass:exactSum([...inside.values()].flat()),words};};
 let rows=view.rows;
 for(const geo of new Set(base.components.map(c=>geometry(local(c))).filter((g):g is string=>!!g))){
  const original=distribution(base,geo);if(original.words.size<2)continue;const allowed=set.options.slice(1).filter(o=>o.exactFractions.length===o.components.length).map(o=>distribution(o,geo)).filter(d=>equal(original.outside,d.outside)&&exactSubtract(original.mass,d.mass).n==='0'&&!equal(original.inside,d.inside));if(!allowed.length)continue;
  const members=rows.filter(r=>geometry(r.representative?.events||[])===geo);if(members.length<2)continue;const words=[...new Set([original,...allowed].flatMap(d=>[...d.words]))],length=words[0].length,insertedSequence=Array.from({length},(_,i)=>words.every(w=>w[i]===words[0][i])?words[0][i]:'N').join('');if(!insertedSequence.includes('N'))continue;
  const start=Number(geo.split(':')[0]),events=[{target:target+1,start,end:start,inserted:length,insertedSequence}],fraction=members.reduce((sum,r)=>sum+r.fraction,0),tokens=tokensFor(projected,view.start,view.end,events),memberIds=new Set(members.map(r=>r.id));rows=rows.filter(r=>!memberIds.has(r.id));rows.push({...members[0],id:'exact-insertion:'+geo,sequenceKey:undefined,sequenceState:'unphased',fraction,classification:'Signal-identical insertion sequences at the same site',positionLabel:'Insertion sequence unresolved',tokens,placements:[],representative:{fraction,events,tokens,location:'Insertion sequence unresolved'}});
 }
 return rows===view.rows?view:{...view,rows:rows.sort((a,b)=>b.fraction-a.fraction)};
}

export function exactMetricRange(annotation:SangerAnnotation,target:number,set:SangerExactAlternatives,kind:'wt'|'substitution'):[number,number]{
 const values=set.options.map(o=>o.components.reduce((sum,c)=>{const es=targetEvents(c.events||[],target),matches=kind==='wt'?!es.length:!!es.length&&es.every(e=>e.inserted===e.end-e.start);return sum+(matches?c.fraction:0);},0));return [Math.min(...values),Math.max(...values)];
}

export function exactFractionLabel(row:{fraction:number;fractionRange?:[number,number]}):string{return row.fraction<.0001?'<0.01':(row.fraction*100).toFixed(2);}

END MODULE


BEGIN MODULE src/app/utils/sanger-exact-profile-benchmark.ts
import{projectTargetTruth,targetEvents}from'./sanger-target-profile';
import type{BenchmarkTruth}from'./sanger-benchmark';
import type{SangerAnalysisResult}from'../models/sanger-analysis';
import type{SynthComparison}from'./sanger-synthetic-benchmark';
import{sangerEditedSequence}from'./sanger-sequence-groups';
import{reverseComplement}from'./biology.utils';
import{exactBoundedMixture,exactFromNumber,exactNumber,exactSum,exactWeightedEquality,exactSubtract,exactScale,exactProduct}from'../workers/core/exact-mixture-fiber';
function localSequence(reference:string,sequence:string,cut:number){
 const size=sequence.length-reference.length;if(!size){let start=0,end=reference.length;while(start<end&&reference[start]===sequence[start])start++;if(start===end)return true;while(end>start&&reference[end-1]===sequence[end-1])end--;return start>=cut-3&&start<=cut+3&&end-start<=10;}
 for(let start=Math.max(0,cut-3);start<=cut+3;start++){const end=start+Math.max(0,-size),inserted=size>0?sequence.slice(start,start+size):'';if(reference.slice(0,start)+inserted+reference.slice(end)===sequence)return true;}return false;
}
/** Post-inference membership of a whole profile in the fixed signal fiber.
 * This never generates inferred alleles, selects a dropdown option or changes
 * point estimates. Finite displayed representatives need not include every
 * compatible point of a continuous mixture polytope. */
export function compareExactProfiles(data:BenchmarkTruth,rows:SynthComparison[],result:SangerAnalysisResult,tolerancePp=2):SynthComparison[]{
 const d=result.diagnostics;if(!d||!/^sanger-sequence-groups-v(?:5-offset|6-conditional)/.test(d?.algorithmVersion||'')||d.contextEmission?.used||!d.contextEmission?.theta.every(x=>x===0)||d.sampleCalibration?.lag!==0)return rows;
 const certificates=new Map<string,NonNullable<SynthComparison['profileCertificate']>>();
 for(const a of result.annotations||[]){
  if((a.cuts?.length||1)===2){const certificate=twoTargetCertificate(data,a,result,tolerancePp);if(certificate)certificates.set(a.name,certificate);continue;}
  if((a.cuts?.length||1)!==1||!a.share)continue;
  const truth=data.truth.filter(t=>t.reference===a.name);if(!truth.length||truth.length>16||truth.some(t=>!t.inputSequence||/[^ACGT]/.test(t.inputSequence)))continue;
  const oriented=(s:string)=>a.direction==='reverse'?reverseComplement(s):s,set=result.exactAlternatives?.find(s=>s.reference===a.name),base=set?.options[0],components=base?.components||a.components;if(components.length>24||components.some(c=>c.unresolved))continue;
  const weights=base?.exactFractions||components.map(c=>exactFromNumber(c.fraction*a.share)),totalMass=exactSum(weights),cap=exactScale(totalMass,1,100),massAt=(percent:number)=>exactScale(exactProduct(exactFromNumber(percent),totalMass),1,100),share=exactNumber(totalMass),gt=truth.map(t=>oriented(t.inputSequence)),fitted=components.map(c=>oriented(sangerEditedSequence(a.reference,c.events||[]))),extras=[...new Set(fitted.filter(s=>!gt.includes(s)))],sequences=[...gt,...extras];if(new Set(gt).size!==gt.length||gt.some(s=>!localSequence(oriented(a.reference),s,a.direction==='reverse'?a.reference.length-a.cut:a.cut)))continue;
  const length=Math.max(...sequences.map(s=>s.length));if(length>4000)continue;
  const column=(s:string)=>Array.from({length},(_,p)=>Array.from('ACGT',b=>Number(s[p]===b))).flat(),columns=sequences.map(column),source=fitted.map((s,i)=>({column:column(s),weight:weights[i]})),bounds=[...truth.map(t=>({lower:massAt(Math.max(0,t.percent-tolerancePp)),upper:massAt(Math.min(100,t.percent+tolerancePp))})),...extras.map(()=>({lower:exactFromNumber(0),upper:exactFromNumber(.01*share)}))],witness=exactBoundedMixture(columns,source,bounds);
  if(!witness)continue;const offProfilePp=witness.slice(gt.length).reduce((sum,w)=>sum+exactNumber(w),0)/share*100;if(offProfilePp>1||!exactWeightedEquality(source,columns.map((column,i)=>({column,weight:witness[i]}))))continue;
  certificates.set(a.name,{kind:'exact-complete-emission',maxDeltaPp:Math.max(...truth.map((t,i)=>Math.abs(exactNumber(witness[i])/share*100-t.percent))),offProfilePp});
 }
 return rows.map(row=>certificates.has(row.reference)?{...row,profileCertificate:certificates.get(row.reference)}:row);
}

/** Auxiliary bounded variables encode target marginals. Their defining rows
 * conserve each complete target partition, so the augmented mass constraint is
 * redundant with physical mass; one witness couples both targets together. */
function twoTargetCertificate(data:BenchmarkTruth,a:NonNullable<SangerAnalysisResult['annotations']>[number],result:SangerAnalysisResult,tolerancePp:number):NonNullable<SynthComparison['profileCertificate']>|null{
 if(a.direction!=='forward')return null;
 const truth=data.truth.filter(t=>t.reference===a.name);if(!truth.length||truth.length>64||truth.some(t=>!t.inputSequence||/[^ACGT]/.test(t.inputSequence)))return null;
 const convert=(t:typeof truth[number])=>t.events.map(e=>{const start=Number(e.kind==='substitution'?e.selectionStart:a.cuts![e.target]+e.offset),end=start+(e.kind==='insertion'?0:e.length),insertedSequence=e.kind==='deletion'?'':e.sequence;return{target:e.target+1,start,end,inserted:insertedSequence.length,insertedSequence};}),truthComponents=truth.map(t=>({events:convert(t)}));
 if(truth.some((t,i)=>sangerEditedSequence(a.reference,truthComponents[i].events)!==t.inputSequence))return null;
 const projected=[0,1].map(t=>projectTargetTruth(data,a,t));if(projected.some((p,t)=>p.truth.some(row=>!localSequence(a.reference,row.inputSequence,a.cuts![t]))))return null;
