 const base=result.exactAlternatives?.find(s=>s.reference===a.name)?.options[0],source=base?.components||a.components;if(source.some(c=>c.unresolved))return null;
 const weights=base?.exactFractions||source.map(c=>exactFromNumber(c.fraction*a.share)),totalMass=exactSum(weights),cap=exactScale(totalMass,1,100),massAt=(percent:number)=>exactScale(exactProduct(exactFromNumber(percent),totalMass),1,100),share=exactNumber(totalMass),gt=truth.map(t=>t.inputSequence),fitted=source.map(c=>sangerEditedSequence(a.reference,c.events||[])),extras=[...new Set(fitted.filter(s=>!gt.includes(s)))],sequences=[...gt,...extras],components=[...truthComponents,...extras.map(sequence=>source.find(c=>sangerEditedSequence(a.reference,c.events||[])===sequence)!)];if(new Set(gt).size!==gt.length)return null;
 const buckets:[number,string,number][]=[];for(const [target,p]of projected.entries()){for(const row of p.truth)buckets.push([target,row.inputSequence,row.percent]);buckets.push([target,'unmatched',0]);}buckets.push([-1,'unmatched',0],[-1,'full-truth',100]);if(sequences.length+buckets.length>128)return null;
 const known=[0,1].map(t=>new Set(buckets.filter(b=>b[0]===t&&b[1]!=='unmatched').map(b=>b[1]))),matches=(events:NonNullable<typeof a.components[number]['events']>,bucket:typeof buckets[number])=>{if(bucket[0]===-1){const inTruth=gt.includes(sangerEditedSequence(a.reference,events));return bucket[1]==='full-truth'?inTruth:!inTruth;}const key=sangerEditedSequence(a.reference,targetEvents(events,bucket[0]));return bucket[1]==='unmatched'?!known[bucket[0]].has(key):key===bucket[1];},length=Math.max(...sequences.map(s=>s.length));if(length>4000)return null;
 const column=(s:string)=>Array.from({length},(_,p)=>Array.from('ACGT',b=>Number(s[p]===b))).flat(),physical=sequences.map(column),columns=physical.map((c,i)=>[...c,...buckets.map(b=>Number(matches(components[i].events||[],b)))]),bounds=sequences.map((_,i)=>({lower:exactFromNumber(0),upper:i<gt.length?totalMass:cap})),original=fitted.map((s,i)=>({column:column(s),weight:weights[i]})),sourceColumns=fitted.map((s,i)=>({column:[...column(s),...buckets.map(b=>Number(matches(source[i].events||[],b)))],weight:weights[i]}));
 for(const [j,bucket]of buckets.entries()){const aux=[...Array(4*length).fill(0),...buckets.map((_,k)=>j===k?-1:0)],mass=exactSum(source.flatMap((c,i)=>matches(c.events||[],bucket)?[weights[i]]:[]));columns.push(aux);bounds.push({lower:massAt(Math.max(0,bucket[2]-tolerancePp)),upper:bucket[1]==='unmatched'?cap:massAt(Math.min(100,bucket[2]+tolerancePp))});sourceColumns.push({column:aux,weight:mass});}
 const witness=exactBoundedMixture(columns,sourceColumns,bounds);if(!witness)return null;const offMass=exactSum(witness.slice(gt.length,sequences.length)),offProfilePp=Math.min(1,exactNumber(offMass)/share*100);if(BigInt(exactSubtract(offMass,cap).n)>0n||!exactWeightedEquality(original,physical.map((column,i)=>({column,weight:witness[i]}))))return null;
 return{kind:'exact-complete-emission',offProfilePp,maxDeltaPp:Math.max(...buckets.flatMap((b,i)=>b[1]==='unmatched'||b[0]===-1?[]:[Math.abs(exactNumber(witness[sequences.length+i])/share*100-b[2])]))};
}

END MODULE


BEGIN MODULE src/app/utils/sanger-exact-selection.ts
import {SangerAnalysisResult,SangerAnnotation} from '../models/sanger-analysis';
/** Select a certified whole-reference representative. This never refits the trace
 * or consults benchmark truth, and preserves all fit/QC diagnostics. */
export function selectExactRepresentative(result:SangerAnalysisResult,reference:string,index:number):SangerAnalysisResult {
 const set=result.exactAlternatives?.find(s=>s.reference===reference);
 if(!set||set.certificate!=='exact-rational-emission'||!Number.isInteger(index)||(index!==-1&&!set.options[index]))throw new Error('Invalid exact-signal representative');
 const annotation=result.annotations?.find(a=>a.name===reference);if(!annotation)throw new Error('Missing reference');
 const components=set.options[Math.max(0,index)].components.map(c=>({...c,events:c.events?.map(e=>({...e}))})),updated={...annotation,components};
 const annotations=result.annotations!.map(a=>a.name===reference?updated:a);
 const totals=(a:SangerAnnotation)=>{const bins=new Map<number,number>();a.components.forEach(c=>bins.set(c.size,(bins.get(c.size)||0)+c.fraction));const wt=a.components.filter(c=>!c.unresolved&&!c.events?.length).reduce((s,c)=>s+c.fraction,0);return {wt,indel:1-wt,inFrame:a.components.filter(c=>c.size!==0&&c.size%3===0).reduce((s,c)=>s+c.fraction,0),outOfFrame:a.components.filter(c=>c.size%3!==0).reduce((s,c)=>s+c.fraction,0),components:[...bins].map(([size,fraction])=>({size,fraction}))};};
 const stats=totals(updated),out:SangerAnalysisResult={...result,annotations,exactAlternatives:result.exactAlternatives!.map(s=>s===set?{...s,selected:index}:s)};
 if(result.pooled)out.pooled={...result.pooled,amplicons:result.pooled.amplicons.map(a=>a.name===reference?{...a,...stats,...(!annotation.estimable?{wt:null,indel:null,inFrame:null,outOfFrame:null}:{})}:a)};
 if(result.joint){const groups=result.joint.groups.filter(g=>g.reference!==reference),replacement=components.map((c,i)=>({id:'E'+index+'-'+reference+'-'+i,reference,fraction:c.fraction*annotation.share,netSize:c.size,pathSizes:c.pathSizes,events:c.events||[],alternatives:[]}));out.joint={...result.joint,groups:[...groups,...replacement],selectedModels:groups.length+replacement.length,targets:result.joint.targets.map(t=>t.reference===reference?{...t,edited:t.edited===null?null:components.filter(c=>c.events?.some(e=>e.target===t.target||e.target===0)).reduce((s,c)=>s+c.fraction,0)}:t)};}
 if(result.mixture){const before=totals(annotation),bins=new Map(result.mixture.components.map(c=>[c.size,c.fraction]));for(const c of before.components)bins.set(c.size,(bins.get(c.size)||0)-c.fraction*annotation.share);for(const c of stats.components)bins.set(c.size,(bins.get(c.size)||0)+c.fraction*annotation.share);const wt=result.mixture.wt+(stats.wt-before.wt)*annotation.share;out.mixture={...result.mixture,wt,indel:1-wt,inFrame:result.mixture.inFrame+(stats.inFrame-before.inFrame)*annotation.share,outOfFrame:result.mixture.outOfFrame+(stats.outOfFrame-before.outOfFrame)*annotation.share,components:[...bins].filter(([,f])=>f>1e-12).map(([size,fraction])=>({size,fraction}))};}
 return out;
}

END MODULE


BEGIN MODULE src/app/utils/sanger-native-signal-certificate.ts
import type{SangerLinearOptimizer}from'../workers/core/sanger-discrete-phase';
import type{SangerAnalysisResult}from'../models/sanger-analysis';
import{parseSangerTrace}from'./sanger-trace';
import{writeSynthTrace}from'./sanger-synthetic-benchmark';
import{targetEvents}from'./sanger-target-profile';
import{sangerEditedSequence}from'./sanger-sequence-groups';
type Part={sequence:string;reference:number;states:number[];weight:number};
type Row={ids:number[];lower:number;upper:number};
/** Post-inference only. Accept only independently re-rendered native files,
 * identical byte for byte. Search failure does not prove impossibility. */
export function certifyNativeSangerPair(control:ArrayBuffer,edit:ArrayBuffer,result:SangerAnalysisResult,optimizer:SangerLinearOptimizer){
 const annotations=result.annotations;if(!annotations?.length||annotations.some(a=>a.direction!=='forward'||a.components.some(c=>c.unresolved)))return null;
 const c=parseSangerTrace(control,'control'),e=parseSangerTrace(edit,'edit');if(c.peaks.length!==e.peaks.length)return null;
 const refs=annotations.map((a,h)=>{const offset=result.pooled?.preflight.amplicons[h]?.primerEnd??20,axes=(a.cuts||[a.cut]).map((_,t)=>{const unique=new Map<string,{events:any[];fraction:number}>();for(const g of a.components){const events=targetEvents(g.events||[],t),key=sangerEditedSequence(a.reference,events),old=unique.get(key);if(old)old.fraction+=g.fraction;else unique.set(key,{events,fraction:g.fraction});}return[...unique.values()];});return{a,offset,axes};}),parts:Part[]=[];if(refs.some(r=>r.axes.some(axis=>axis.length>6||axis.some(g=>!Number.isFinite(g.fraction)||g.fraction<0))))return null;
 for(const [h,r]of refs.entries()){
  const visit=(t:number,states:number[],events:any[],mass:number)=>{if(t===r.axes.length){parts.push({reference:h,states,sequence:sangerEditedSequence(r.a.reference,events).slice(r.offset),weight:r.a.share*mass});return;}r.axes[t].forEach((g,j)=>visit(t+1,[...states,j],[...events,...g.events],mass*g.fraction));};visit(0,[],[],1);
 }
 if(!parts.length||parts.length>200)return null;const rowMap=new Map<string,Row>(),calls:{a:number[];b:number[];margin:number}[]=[];let impossible=false,reason="";
 const add=(ids:number[],lower:number,upper:number)=>{lower=Math.max(0,lower);upper=Math.min(1,upper);if(lower>upper+1e-15){impossible=true;reason="interval "+lower+" "+upper;return;}if(!ids.length){if(lower>0){impossible=true;reason="empty "+lower;}return;}if(ids.length===parts.length){if(lower>1||upper<1){impossible=true;reason='total';}return;}const key=ids.join(','),old=rowMap.get(key);if(old){old.lower=Math.max(old.lower,lower);old.upper=Math.min(old.upper,upper);if(old.lower>old.upper){impossible=true;reason="intersection "+old.lower+" "+old.upper;}}else rowMap.set(key,{ids,lower,upper});};
 for(const [trace,wt]of [[c,true],[e,false]]as const)for(let p=0;p<trace.peaks.length;p++){
  const groups=[...'ACGT'].map(b=>parts.flatMap((part,i)=>(wt?refs[part.reference].a.reference.slice(refs[part.reference].offset):part.sequence)[p]===b?[i]:[]));
  for(let b=0;b<4;b++){let lo=0,hi=1;for(let d=-4;d<=4;d++){const amplitude=1200*Math.exp(-d*d/4),v=trace.channels[(['A','C','G','T']as const)[b]][trace.peaks[p]+d]-2;lo=Math.max(lo,(v-.5+1e-8)/amplitude);hi=Math.min(hi,(v+.5-1e-8)/amplitude);}add(groups[b],lo,hi);}
  const called='ACGT'.indexOf(trace.sequence[p]);if(called<0)return null;for(let b=0;b<4;b++)if(b!==called)calls.push({a:groups[called],b:groups[b],margin:b<called?1e-10:0});
 }
 for(const [h,r]of refs.entries())for(let t=0;t<r.axes.length;t++)r.axes[t].forEach((g,j)=>add(parts.flatMap((part,i)=>part.reference===h&&part.states[t]===j?[i]:[]),r.a.share*Math.max(0,g.fraction-.02),r.a.share*Math.min(1,g.fraction+.02)));
 if(impossible)return null;const rows=[...rowMap.values()],w=parts.map(p=>p.weight),sum=w.reduce((a,b)=>a+b,0);for(let i=0;i<w.length;i++)w[i]/=sum;
 const equal=(a:ArrayBuffer,b:ArrayBuffer)=>{const x=new Uint8Array(a),y=new Uint8Array(b);return x.length===y.length&&x.every((v,i)=>v===y[i]);};
 const verify=(sweeps:number)=>{const masses=refs.map((_,h)=>parts.reduce((s,p,i)=>s+(p.reference===h?w[i]:0),0));let maxDelta=0;for(const [h,r]of refs.entries())for(let t=0;t<r.axes.length;t++)r.axes[t].forEach((g,j)=>{const mass=parts.reduce((s,p,i)=>s+(p.reference===h&&p.states[t]===j?w[i]:0),0);maxDelta=Math.max(maxDelta,Math.abs(mass/masses[h]-g.fraction)*100);});if(maxDelta>2+1e-7)return null;
  const sample=writeSynthTrace(parts.map((p,i)=>({sequence:p.sequence,weight:w[i]})),e.peaks.length,'edit');if(!equal(sample,edit))return null;const controlOut=writeSynthTrace(refs.map((r,h)=>({sequence:r.a.reference.slice(r.offset),weight:masses[h]})),c.peaks.length,'control');if(!equal(controlOut,control))return null;return{kind:'exact-native-ab1-pair' as const,sweeps,maxDeltaPp:maxDelta,parts:parts.map((p,i)=>({...p,weight:w[i]})),bothFilesIdentical:true};
 };
 const first=verify(0);if(first)return first;
 const lines=['Minimize',' obj: 0 x0','Subject To'];let row=0;
 const expression=(ids:number[])=>ids.map(i=>'1000 x'+i).join(' + ');
 for(const r of rows){const expr=expression(r.ids);if(r.lower>0)lines.push(' r'+row+++'l: '+expr+' >= '+(r.lower*1000));if(r.upper<1)lines.push(' r'+row+++'u: '+expr+' <= '+(r.upper*1000));}
 const seenCalls=new Set<string>();for(const call of calls){const key=call.a.join(',')+'/'+call.b.join(',')+'/'+call.margin;if(seenCalls.has(key))continue;seenCalls.add(key);const expr=call.a.map(i=>'+ 1000 x'+i).concat(call.b.map(i=>'- 1000 x'+i)).join(' ');if(expr)lines.push(' c'+row+++': '+expr+' >= '+(call.margin*1000));}
 lines.push(' total: '+parts.map((_,i)=>'x'+i).join(' + ')+' = 1','Bounds',...parts.map((_,i)=>' 0 <= x'+i+' <= 1'),'End');
 const answer=optimizer.solve(lines.join('\n'),{output_flag:false,primal_feasibility_tolerance:1e-10,dual_feasibility_tolerance:1e-10,time_limit:10});if(answer.Status!=='Optimal')return null;w.fill(0);for(let i=0;i<w.length;i++)w[i]=answer.Columns['x'+i]?.Primal||0;
 return verify(1);
}
import type{BenchmarkTruth}from'./sanger-benchmark';
/** A benchmark credit needs TWO independently verified witnesses: inferred
 * genotypes and the supplied truth, each rendering the same complete control
 * and edited files. A source-only reconstruction is not evidence that an
 * arbitrary truth table is compatible. Both searches happen after inference. */
export function certifyNativeBenchmark(data:BenchmarkTruth,control:ArrayBuffer,edit:ArrayBuffer,result:SangerAnalysisResult,optimizer:SangerLinearOptimizer){
 if(!data.sequenceTruth||!result.annotations?.length)return null;
 const annotations=result.annotations.map(a=>{const rows=data.truth.filter(t=>t.reference===a.name),cuts=a.cuts||[a.cut];if(!rows.length)return null;
  const components=rows.map(t=>{const events=t.events.map(e=>{const start=e.kind==='substitution'?e.selectionStart!:cuts[e.target]+e.offset,word=e.kind==='deletion'?'':e.sequence,end=start+(e.kind==='insertion'?0:e.kind==='deletion'?e.length:(e.before||'').length);return{target:e.target+1,start,end,inserted:word.length,insertedSequence:word};});if(sangerEditedSequence(a.reference,events)!==t.inputSequence)return null;return{events,size:t.inputSequence.length-a.reference.length,fraction:t.percent/100};});
  if(components.some(c=>!c))return null;return{...a,share:rows[0].share/100,components:components as NonNullable<typeof components[number]>[]};
 });
 if(annotations.some(a=>!a))return null;
 const source=certifyNativeSangerPair(control,edit,result,optimizer);if(!source)return null;
 const truth=certifyNativeSangerPair(control,edit,{...result,annotations:annotations as NonNullable<typeof annotations[number]>[]},optimizer);if(!truth)return null;
 return{kind:'exact-native-ab1-equivalence' as const,references:result.annotations.map(a=>a.name),sourceAnnotationsKey:JSON.stringify(result.annotations),truthKey:JSON.stringify(data.truth),source,truth};
}
export type NativeBenchmarkCertificate=NonNullable<ReturnType<typeof certifyNativeBenchmark>>;

END MODULE


BEGIN MODULE src/app/utils/sanger-placement.ts
import type {SangerJointEvent} from '../models/sanger-analysis';

/** One canonical representation for exactly the same final DNA. This does not
 * infer the physical repair history or merge different edited sequences. */
export function leftEquivalentDeletionStart(reference:string,start:number,length:number):number {
 while(start>0&&/^[ACGT]$/.test(reference[start-1])&&reference[start-1]===reference[start+length-1])start--;
 return start;
}
export function canonicalSangerEvents(reference:string,events:SangerJointEvent[]):SangerJointEvent[]{
 if(events.length!==1)return events.map(e=>({...e}));
 const e={...events[0]};
 if(e.end>e.start&&e.inserted===0){const length=e.end-e.start;e.start=leftEquivalentDeletionStart(reference,e.start,length);e.end=e.start+length;}
 else if(e.end===e.start&&e.inserted>0&&e.insertedSequence?.length===e.inserted&&/^[ACGT]+$/.test(e.insertedSequence)){
  let sequence=e.insertedSequence;
  while(e.start>0&&reference[e.start-1]===sequence.at(-1)){sequence=sequence.at(-1)!+sequence.slice(0,-1);e.start--;e.end--;}
  e.insertedSequence=sequence;
 }
 return [e];
}

/** Push a fitted independent-base insertion distribution through DNA-equivalent
 * left normalization exactly. A shift requires successive inserted suffix bases
 * to match the WT bases on the left. No guessed inserted sequence is used. */
export function canonicalSangerPlacements(reference:string,events:SangerJointEvent[]):{events:SangerJointEvent[];weight:number}[]{
 const e=events[0],pi=e?.insertedBaseProbabilities,bases='ACGT';
 if(events.length!==1||e.start!==e.end||!e.inserted||pi?.length!==e.inserted||pi.some(v=>v.length!==4||v.some(x=>!Number.isFinite(x)||x<0)||Math.abs(v.reduce((s,x)=>s+x,0)-1)>1e-6))return [{events:canonicalSangerEvents(reference,events),weight:1}];
 const out:{events:SangerJointEvent[];weight:number}[]=[];let remaining=1,shift=0;
 while(remaining>1e-15&&shift<Math.min(e.inserted,e.start)){
  const b=bases.indexOf(reference[e.start-shift-1]);if(b<0)break;
  const match=pi[e.inserted-shift-1][b],mass=remaining*(1-match);
  if(mass>1e-15)out.push({events:[{...e,start:e.start-shift,end:e.start-shift,insertedSequence:'N'.repeat(e.inserted),insertedBaseProbabilities:undefined}],weight:mass});
  remaining*=match;shift++;
 }
 if(remaining>1e-15){
  let start=e.start-shift;
  if(shift===e.inserted){const sequence=reference.slice(e.start-e.inserted,e.start),normalized=canonicalSangerEvents(reference,[{...e,start,end:start,insertedSequence:sequence,insertedBaseProbabilities:undefined}])[0];start=normalized.start;}
  out.push({events:[{...e,start,end:start,insertedSequence:'N'.repeat(e.inserted),insertedBaseProbabilities:undefined}],weight:remaining});
 }
 return out;
}

END MODULE


BEGIN MODULE src/app/utils/sanger-product-bounds.ts
import {PooledAmplicon} from '../models/sanger-analysis';
import {reverseComplement} from './biology.utils';
/** Product orientation is forward-primer 5'→3'. Reverse primer is entered 5'→3'.
 * This verifies submitted reference boundaries, not experimental PCR specificity. */
export function validateSangerProductBounds(product:PooledAmplicon):boolean{
 const forward=(product.forwardPrimer||'').replace(/\s/g,'').toUpperCase(),reverse=(product.reversePrimer||'').replace(/\s/g,'').toUpperCase();
 if(!forward&&!reverse)return false;
 if(!/^[ACGT]{12,60}$/.test(forward)||!/^[ACGT]{12,60}$/.test(reverse))throw Error(product.name+': provide both PCR primers, 12–60 A/C/G/T bases each, in 5′→3′ orientation.');
 if(!product.sequence.startsWith(forward)||!product.sequence.endsWith(reverseComplement(reverse)))throw Error(product.name+': the submitted amplicon must start with the forward PCR primer and end with the reverse complement of the reverse PCR primer.');
 if(forward.length+reverse.length>=product.sequence.length)throw Error(product.name+': PCR primer-binding ends overlap.');return true;
}

END MODULE


BEGIN MODULE src/app/utils/sanger-progress.ts
/** Estimated stage budget, not a measurement of wall-clock completion. */
export function sangerProgress(stage:string):{percent:number;label:string}{
 if(stage==='Checking ground truth after inference')return{percent:99,label:'Ground-truth comparison'};
 if(stage.startsWith('Conditional prefix · ')){const inner=sangerProgress(stage.slice(21));return{percent:5+inner.percent*.2,label:'1 / 5 · First-target prefix'};}
 const refine=stage.match(/Refining first-target allocations · reference (\d+) \/ (\d+) · (\d+) \/ (\d+)/);
 if(refine){const fraction=(Number(refine[1])-1+Number(refine[3])/Number(refine[4]))/Number(refine[2]);return{percent:60+30*fraction,label:'3 / 4 · Joint base assignment · reference '+refine[1]+' / '+refine[2]+' · '+refine[3]+' / '+refine[4]};}
 const discrete=stage.match(/Joint base assignment · pass (\d+) · reference (\d+) \/ (\d+)/);
 if(discrete)return{percent:82+Math.min(12,2*(Number(discrete[1])-1+Number(discrete[2])/Number(discrete[3]))),label:'4 / 5 · Joint base assignment · reference '+discrete[2]+' / '+discrete[3]+' · pass '+discrete[1]};
 if(stage.includes('first-target measured prefix'))return{percent:5,label:'1 / 5 · First-target prefix'};
 if(stage.includes('downstream total-length'))return{percent:30,label:'2 / 5 · Downstream length fractions'};
 if(stage.includes('Proposing second-target')){const m=stage.match(/reference (\d+) \/ (\d+)/);return{percent:40+(m?(Number(m[1])-1)/Number(m[2])*25:0),label:'3 / 5 · Second-target candidates'+(m?' · reference '+m[1]+' / '+m[2]:'')};}
 const allocations=stage.match(/^(First target reference|Reference) (\d+) \/ (\d+) · Comparing length-class allocations · (\d+) \/ (\d+)/);
 if(allocations){const fraction=(Number(allocations[2])-1+Number(allocations[4])/Number(allocations[5]))/Number(allocations[3]);return{percent:allocations[1]==='Reference'?40+25*fraction:40+40*fraction,label:(allocations[1]==='Reference'?'3 / 5 · Second-target candidates':'3 / 4 · Sequence groups')+' · reference '+allocations[2]+' / '+allocations[3]+' · '+allocations[4]+' / '+allocations[5]};}
 const joint=stage.match(/Comparing joint target hypotheses · reference (\d+) \/ (\d+) · (\d+) \/ (\d+)/);
 if(joint){const fraction=(Number(joint[1])-1+Number(joint[3])/Number(joint[4]))/Number(joint[2]);return{percent:65+15*fraction,label:'4 / 5 · Joint signal fit · reference '+joint[1]+' / '+joint[2]+' · '+joint[3]+' / '+joint[4]};}
 const phase=stage.match(/Sequence phase · pass (\d+) · reference (\d+) \/ (\d+) · (\d+) \/ (\d+)/);
 if(phase){const fraction=(Number(phase[1])-1+(Number(phase[2])-1+Number(phase[4])/Number(phase[5]))/Number(phase[3]))/3;return{percent:82+12*fraction,label:'4 / 5 · Sequence groups · reference '+phase[2]+' / '+phase[3]+' · '+phase[4]+' / '+phase[5]};}
 if(stage.includes('Resolving joint sequence phase'))return{percent:82,label:'4 / 5 · Sequence groups'};
 if(stage.includes('Joint sequence phasing')){const pass=Number(stage.match(/pass (\d+)/)?.[1]||1);return{percent:82+Math.min(12,4*pass),label:'4 / 5 · Sequence groups · pass '+pass};}
 if(stage.includes('Jointly fitting free target pairings')){const pass=Number(stage.match(/pass (\d+)/)?.[1]||1);return{percent:65+Math.min(15,5*(pass-1)),label:'4 / 5 · Joint signal fit · pass '+pass};}
 if(stage.includes('Validating fitted conditional'))return{percent:97,label:'5 / 5 · Profile validation'};
 if(stage.includes('Registering offset traces'))return {percent:5,label:'1 / 4 · Trace registration'};
 if(stage.includes('Estimating indel length fractions'))return {percent:25,label:'2 / 4 · Length fractions'};
 if(stage.includes('Decoding offset')){const m=stage.match(/reference (\d+) \/ (\d+)/),done=m?Math.max(0,(Number(m[1])-1)/Number(m[2])):0;return {percent:40+40*done,label:'3 / 4 · Sequence groups'+(m?' · reference '+m[1]+' / '+m[2]:'')};}
 if(stage.includes('Validating offset profiles'))return {percent:95,label:'4 / 4 · Profile validation'};
 if(stage.includes('Validating fitted'))return {percent:97,label:'7 / 7 · Profile validation'};
 const round=Number(stage.match(/round (\d+)/)?.[1]||0);
 if(stage.includes('Rechecking sequence'))return {percent:92,label:'6 / 7 · Candidate recheck'};
 if(stage.includes('Jointly refitting'))return {percent:88,label:'5 / 7 · Joint signal fit'};
 if(stage.includes('substitution combinations'))return {percent:70,label:'4 / 7 · Substitution combinations'};
 if(stage.includes('sequence groups'))return {percent:20+Math.min(42,round/80*42),label:'3 / 7 · Sequence search'+(round?' · round '+round+' / 80':'')};
 if(/Calibrat|WT product|unedited pooled/.test(stage))return {percent:15,label:'2 / 7 · Control calibration'};
 if(/Fitting|separability/.test(stage))return {percent:35,label:'3 / 7 · Signal fitting'};
 return {percent:5,label:'1 / 7 · Trace registration'};
}

END MODULE


BEGIN MODULE src/app/utils/sanger-result-window.ts
import {SangerAnalysisResult,SangerJointEvent} from '../models/sanger-analysis';
export interface SangerWindowSummary {
 window:number;targets:NonNullable<SangerAnalysisResult['joint']>['targets'];
 references:{name:string;share:number;wt:number|null;indel:number|null;inFrame:number|null;outOfFrame:number|null;unresolvedFraction:number;components:{size:number;fraction:number}[]}[];
}
/** Summarise immutable full-molecule fits within editable target windows.
 * No fitting, renormalisation, discarded raw event or new confidence claim. */
export function summarizeSangerWindow(result:SangerAnalysisResult,window:number):SangerWindowSummary|null {
 if(!result.joint||!Number.isInteger(window)||window<1||window>1000)return null;
 const joint=result.joint,within=(e:SangerJointEvent,cut:number)=>e.end>e.start?e.start<cut+Math.ceil(window/2)&&e.end>cut-Math.floor(window/2):e.start>=cut-Math.floor(window/2)&&e.start<cut+Math.ceil(window/2);
 const refs=(result.pooled?.amplicons??[]).map(ref=>{
  const targets=joint.targets.filter(t=>t.reference===ref.name),groups=joint.groups.filter(g=>g.reference===ref.name),bins=new Map<number,number>();let wt=0,edited=0,inFrame=0,outOfFrame=0,unresolved=0;
  for(const g of groups){
   if(g.netSize===null){unresolved+=g.fraction;continue;}
   const alternatives=g.alternatives.length?g.alternatives:[{reference:ref.name,events:g.events}];
   const states=alternatives.map(a=>{if(a.reference!==ref.name)return null;const events=a.events.filter(e=>targets.some(t=>within(e,t.cut))),size=events.reduce((sum,e)=>sum+e.inserted-e.end+e.start,0);return {size,edited:events.length>0};});
   const first=states[0];if(!first||states.some(s=>!s||s.size!==first.size||s.edited!==first.edited)){unresolved+=g.fraction;continue;}
   bins.set(first.size,(bins.get(first.size)||0)+g.fraction);if(!first.edited)wt+=g.fraction;else{edited+=g.fraction;if(first.size%3===0)inFrame+=g.fraction;else outOfFrame+=g.fraction;}
  }
  const estimable=!!result.mixture?.passesChecks&&ref.wt!==null&&ref.share>=.02&&unresolved<1e-8,ratio=(v:number)=>estimable?v/ref.share:null;
  return {name:ref.name,share:ref.share,wt:ratio(wt),indel:ratio(edited),inFrame:ratio(inFrame),outOfFrame:ratio(outOfFrame),unresolvedFraction:ref.share?unresolved/ref.share:0,components:[...bins].sort((a,b)=>a[0]-b[0]).map(([size,fraction])=>({size,fraction:ref.share?fraction/ref.share:0}))};
 });
 const targets=joint.targets.map(target=>{
  if(target.edited===null)return {...target};
  const ref=refs.find(r=>r.name===target.reference);if(!ref||ref.share<.02)return {...target,edited:null};let edited=0;
  for(const g of joint.groups.filter(g=>g.reference===target.reference)){
   const alternatives=g.alternatives.length?g.alternatives:[{reference:g.reference!,events:g.events}],states=alternatives.map(a=>a.reference===target.reference?a.events.some(e=>within(e,target.cut)):null);
   if(states.some(s=>s===null||s!==states[0]))return {...target,edited:null};if(states[0])edited+=g.fraction;
  }
  return {...target,edited:edited/ref.share};
 });
 return {window,targets,references:refs};
}

END MODULE


BEGIN MODULE src/app/utils/sanger-sequence-groups.ts
import type {SangerJointEvent} from '../models/sanger-analysis';
import {canonicalSangerEvents} from './sanger-placement';
import {referenceChecksum} from './reference-checksum';
export type SequenceState='base-call'|'mixture'|'unphased'|'unresolved';
export interface SequenceChoice {events:SangerJointEvent[];weight:number;state:SequenceState}
export const SANGER_BASE_CALL_THRESHOLD=.99;
export const SANGER_SEQUENCE_CANDIDATE_LIMIT=64;
export function sangerEditedSequence(reference:string,events:SangerJointEvent[]){let sequence=reference;for(const e of [...events].sort((a,b)=>b.start-a.start||b.end-a.end))sequence=sequence.slice(0,e.start)+(e.insertedSequence||'N'.repeat(e.inserted))+sequence.slice(e.end);return sequence;}
export function sangerSequenceKey(reference:string,events:SangerJointEvent[]){return referenceChecksum(sangerEditedSequence(reference,events));}
/** Expand the fitted independent-base emission model, not sequenced molecules.
 * Multiple mixed positions are unphased candidates. Truncation retains its mass
 * in an explicit unresolved remainder; near-certain calls use a 1% tolerance. */
export function sangerSequenceChoices(reference:string,events:SangerJointEvent[],limit=SANGER_SEQUENCE_CANDIDATE_LIMIT):SequenceChoice[]{
 let rows:{events:SangerJointEvent[];weight:number}[]=[{events:events.map(e=>({...e,insertedSequence:e.insertedSequence||'N'.repeat(e.inserted),insertedBaseProbabilities:undefined})),weight:1}],mixed=0,missing=false;
 for(let e=0;e<events.length;e++)for(let j=0;j<events[e].inserted;j++){
  const raw=events[e].insertedBaseProbabilities?.[j],sum=raw?.reduce((a,b)=>a+b,0)||0,valid=raw?.length===4&&raw.every(v=>Number.isFinite(v)&&v>=0)&&sum>0;
  let options:{base:string;weight:number}[];
  if(valid){const p=raw!.map(v=>v/sum),max=Math.max(...p);options=max>=SANGER_BASE_CALL_THRESHOLD?[{base:'ACGT'[p.indexOf(max)],weight:1}]:p.flatMap((weight,b)=>weight>0?[{base:'ACGT'[b],weight}]:[]);if(options.length>1)mixed++;}
  else {const base=events[e].insertedSequence?.[j]||'N';options=[{base:/^[ACGT]$/.test(base)?base:'N',weight:1}];if(options[0].base==='N')missing=true;}
  rows=rows.flatMap(row=>options.map(o=>({weight:row.weight*o.weight,events:row.events.map((event,i)=>i!==e?event:{...event,insertedSequence:event.insertedSequence!.slice(0,j)+o.base+event.insertedSequence!.slice(j+1)})}))).sort((a,b)=>b.weight-a.weight||JSON.stringify(a.events).localeCompare(JSON.stringify(b.events))).slice(0,Math.max(1,limit));
 }
 const state:SequenceState=missing?'unresolved':mixed>1?'unphased':mixed?'mixture':'base-call';
 const out:SequenceChoice[]=rows.map(row=>({...row,events:canonicalSangerEvents(reference,row.events),state})),remaining=1-rows.reduce((sum,row)=>sum+row.weight,0);
 if(remaining>1e-12)out.push({events:events.map(e=>({...e,insertedSequence:e.inserted?'N'.repeat(e.inserted):'',insertedBaseProbabilities:undefined})),weight:remaining,state:'unresolved'});
 return out;
}

END MODULE


BEGIN MODULE src/app/utils/sanger-signal-equivalence-benchmark.ts
import type {BenchmarkTruth} from './sanger-benchmark';
import type {SangerAnalysisResult,SangerAnnotation} from '../models/sanger-analysis';
import {sangerEditedSequence} from './sanger-sequence-groups';
import {projectTargetTruth,targetEvents} from './sanger-target-profile';
import {reverseComplement} from './biology.utils';
import {exactBoundedMixture,exactFromNumber,exactNumber,exactSum,exactProduct,exactScale,exactSubtract,exactWeightedEquality} from '../workers/core/exact-mixture-fiber';
import type {ExactFraction} from '../workers/core/exact-mixture-fiber';

export interface SignalEquivalenceCertificate {
 reference:string;
 kind:'exact-complete-emission';
 maxDeltaPp:number;
 offProfilePp:0;
 /** One coherent whole-molecule witness, not independent matches per target. */
 truthWeights:ExactFraction[];
 sourceWeights:ExactFraction[];
 comparedBases:number;
}
/** Post-inference scoring only. A certificate must reproduce EVERY modeled
 * cycle/channel exactly using ONLY truth molecules. No extra allele budget,
 * approximate equality, added inference candidates or selected UI option.
 * Null means uncertified, not a claim that a different solution cannot exist. */
export function certifySignalEquivalence(data:BenchmarkTruth,result:SangerAnalysisResult,tolerancePp=2):SignalEquivalenceCertificate[]{
 const d=result.diagnostics;
 if(!d||d.contextEmission?.used||!d.contextEmission?.theta?.every(x=>x===0)||d.sampleCalibration?.lag!==0)return[];
 return(result.annotations||[]).flatMap(a=>{const c=certifyReference(data,result,a,tolerancePp);return c?[c]:[];});
}
function certifyReference(data:BenchmarkTruth,result:SangerAnalysisResult,a:SangerAnnotation,tolerancePp:number):SignalEquivalenceCertificate|null {
 const truth=data.truth.filter(t=>t.reference===a.name),source=a.components;
 if(!truth.length||truth.length>64||!source.length||source.some(c=>c.unresolved)||truth.some(t=>!t.inputSequence||/[^ACGT]/.test(t.inputSequence)))return null;
 const cuts=a.cuts||[a.cut],convert=(events:typeof truth[number]['events'])=>events.map(e=>{const start=Number(e.kind==='substitution'?e.selectionStart:cuts[e.target]+e.offset),end=start+(e.kind==='insertion'?0:e.kind==='deletion'?e.length:(e.before||'').length),word=e.kind==='deletion'?'':e.sequence;return{target:e.target+1,start,end,inserted:word.length,insertedSequence:word};}),truthEvents=truth.map(t=>convert(t.events));
 if(truth.some((t,i)=>sangerEditedSequence(a.reference,truthEvents[i])!==t.inputSequence))return null;
 const oriented=(s:string)=>a.direction==='reverse'?reverseComplement(s):s,sourceSequences=source.map(c=>oriented(sangerEditedSequence(a.reference,c.events||[]))),truthSequences=truth.map(t=>oriented(t.inputSequence));
 // Prefer original fitted masses to a round trip through within-ref fractions.
 const base=result.exactAlternatives?.find(s=>s.reference===a.name)?.options[0],joint=result.joint?.groups.filter(g=>g.reference===a.name),weights=base?.exactFractions.length===source.length?base.exactFractions:joint?.length===source.length&&joint.every((g,i)=>oriented(sangerEditedSequence(a.reference,g.events))===sourceSequences[i])?joint.map(g=>exactFromNumber(g.fraction)):source.map(c=>exactFromNumber(c.fraction*a.share)),total=exactSum(weights),massAt=(percent:number)=>exactScale(exactProduct(exactFromNumber(percent),total),1,100),length=Math.max(...truthSequences.map(s=>s.length),...sourceSequences.map(s=>s.length))+2;
 if(length>4000||new Set(truthSequences).size!==truth.length||sourceSequences.some(s=>/[^ACGT]/.test(s)))return null;
 const column=(s:string)=>Array.from({length},(_,p)=>Array.from('ACGT',b=>Number(s[p]===b))).flat(),physical=truthSequences.map(column),original=sourceSequences.map((s,i)=>({column:column(s),weight:weights[i]}));
 type Bucket={target:number;sequence:string;percent:number};const buckets:Bucket[]=[],known=new Map<number,Set<string>>();
 for(let target=0;target<cuts.length;target++){
  const projected=projectTargetTruth(data,a,target);known.set(target,new Set(projected.truth.map(t=>t.inputSequence)));
  for(const t of projected.truth)buckets.push({target,sequence:t.inputSequence,percent:t.percent});
  // These partition rows make the auxiliary total-mass equation redundant.
  buckets.push({target,sequence:'unmatched',percent:0});
 }
 const matches=(events:NonNullable<typeof source[number]['events']>,b:Bucket)=>{const local=sangerEditedSequence(a.reference,targetEvents(events,b.target));return b.sequence==='unmatched'?!known.get(b.target)!.has(local):local===b.sequence;},columns=physical.map((c,i)=>[...c,...buckets.map(b=>Number(matches(truthEvents[i],b)))]),bounds=truth.map(()=>({lower:exactFromNumber(0),upper:total})),rhs=original.map((row,i)=>({column:[...row.column,...buckets.map(b=>Number(matches(source[i].events||[],b)))],weight:row.weight}));
 for(const [j,b]of buckets.entries()){
  const auxiliary=[...Array(4*length).fill(0),...buckets.map((_,i)=>i===j?-1:0)],mass=exactSum(source.flatMap((c,i)=>matches(c.events||[],b)?[weights[i]]:[]));columns.push(auxiliary);rhs.push({column:auxiliary,weight:mass});bounds.push({lower:b.sequence==='unmatched'?exactFromNumber(0):massAt(Math.max(0,b.percent-tolerancePp)),upper:b.sequence==='unmatched'?exactFromNumber(0):massAt(Math.min(100,b.percent+tolerancePp))});
 }
 // Identical coefficient rows are the same exact equation, not extra evidence.
 const rowIds:number[]=[],seenRows=new Set<string>();for(let p=0;p<columns[0].length;p++){const key=columns.map(c=>c[p]).join(',')+'|'+rhs.map(r=>r.column[p]).join(',');if(!seenRows.has(key)){seenRows.add(key);rowIds.push(p);}}
 const witness=exactBoundedMixture(columns.map(c=>rowIds.map(p=>c[p])),rhs.map(r=>({...r,column:rowIds.map(p=>r.column[p])})),bounds);
 if(!witness)return null;const physicalWeights=witness.slice(0,truth.length);
 if(exactSubtract(exactSum(physicalWeights),total).n!=='0'||!exactWeightedEquality(original,physical.map((c,i)=>({column:c,weight:physicalWeights[i]}))))return null;
 const maxDeltaPp=Math.max(...buckets.map((b,j)=>b.sequence==='unmatched'?0:Math.abs(exactNumber(witness[truth.length+j])/exactNumber(total)*100-b.percent)));
 return{reference:a.name,kind:'exact-complete-emission',maxDeltaPp,offProfilePp:0,truthWeights:physicalWeights,sourceWeights:weights,comparedBases:length};
}

END MODULE


BEGIN MODULE src/app/utils/sanger-synth-profiles.ts
import type {SynthReference,SynthAllele,SynthEvent} from './sanger-synthetic-benchmark';
export interface SynthTargetProfile {target:number;name:string;alleles:SynthAllele[]}
export function initSynthProfiles(ref:SynthReference){ref.profiles=ref.cuts.map((_,target)=>({target,name:ref.targetNames[target]||'Target '+(target+1),alleles:[]}));return ref.profiles;}
export function targetWT(profile:SynthTargetProfile){return 100-profile.alleles.reduce((s,a)=>s+Number(a.percent),0);}
/** Finite random pairing preserves every target marginal exactly to 0.01%.
 * It is an explicit simulation coupling, not inferred biological linkage. */
export function mixTargetProfiles(ref:SynthReference,random:()=>number){
 const profiles=ref.profiles!;if(profiles.length!==ref.cuts.length||profiles.some((p,i)=>p.target!==i))throw Error(ref.name+': give each target one profile.');
 const population=10000,axes=profiles.map(profile=>{
  if(!Number.isFinite(targetWT(profile))||targetWT(profile)<-1e-8)throw Error(ref.name+' '+profile.name+': group percentages must total at most 100%.');
  const values=new Uint16Array(population);let at=0;
  profile.alleles.forEach((allele,i)=>{const percent=Number(allele.percent),n=Math.round(percent*100);if(!Number.isFinite(percent)||percent<0||percent>100||Math.abs(n-percent*100)>1e-6||allele.events.length!==1||allele.events[0].target!==profile.target)throw Error('Each target group needs one edit and a percentage with at most two decimals.');values.fill(i+1,at,at+n);at+=n;});
  for(let j=population-1;j>0;j--){const k=Math.min(j,Math.floor(random()*(j+1))),v=values[j];values[j]=values[k];values[k]=v;}return values;
 });
 const counts=new Map<string,number>();for(let i=0;i<population;i++){const key=axes.map(a=>a[i]).join('/');counts.set(key,(counts.get(key)||0)+1);}
 return [...counts].sort((a,b)=>a[0].localeCompare(b[0],undefined,{numeric:true})).map(([key,count])=>{const choices=key.split('/').map(Number),events:SynthEvent[]=choices.flatMap((group,t)=>group?structuredClone(profiles[t].alleles[group-1].events):[]);return {allele:events.length?choices.map((group,t)=>'T'+(t+1)+' '+(group?'Group '+group:'WT')).join(' · '):'WT',percent:count/100,events,targetStates:choices};});
}

END MODULE


BEGIN MODULE src/app/utils/sanger-synthetic-benchmark.ts
import {SynthTargetProfile,mixTargetProfiles} from './sanger-synth-profiles';
import {referenceChecksum} from './reference-checksum';
import {SangerCandidate,SangerDesignResult} from '../models/sanger-design';
import {SangerAnalysisResult,SangerJointEvent,TRACE_BASES} from '../models/sanger-analysis';
import {selectedAmplicons,makeAnalysisTemplate,SangerAnalysisTemplate} from './sanger-design-export';
import {reverseComplement} from './biology.utils';
import {writeAbif} from './sanger-analysis-demo';
import {parseSangerTrace} from './sanger-trace';
import {AutomaticSangerInput} from '../workers/core/sanger-automatic';
import {sangerAnnotationViews,SangerAnnotationView} from './sanger-annotation';
export interface SynthEvent {kind:'insertion'|'deletion'|'substitution';target:number;offset:number;sequence:string;length:number;selectionStart?:number;before?:string}
export interface SynthAllele {id:number;percent:number;events:SynthEvent[]}
export interface SynthReference {name:string;sourceReference?:string;sequence:string;cuts:number[];targetNames:string[];share:number;primerLength:number;partnerLength:number;alleles:SynthAllele[];profiles?:SynthTargetProfile[]}
export interface SynthTruth {targetStates?:number[];reference:string;sourceReference:string;allele:string;share:number;percent:number;totalPercent:number;pattern:string;description:string;events:SynthEvent[];sequence:string;inputSequence:string;substitution:boolean}
export interface SynthDataset {control:ArrayBuffer;edit:ArrayBuffer;truth:SynthTruth[];template:SangerAnalysisTemplate;cycles:number;recipe:SynthReference[];mixing?:{method:'random-target-pairing';seed:number;populationPerReference:number};input:AutomaticSangerInput}
export interface SynthComparison {profileCertificate?:{kind:'exact-complete-emission';maxDeltaPp:number;offProfilePp:number};optionMatch?:{indices:number[];sequenceIndices:number[];withinTolerance:boolean;range:[number,number];profileOptions:number[];tolerancePp:number};target?:number;reference:string;group:string;truth:number|null;estimated:number|null;delta:number|null;recovery:string;placement:string;pattern?:string;scope?:'reference'|'group';sequenceKey?:string;sequenceScope?:'sequence'|'pattern'}
const dna=(s:string)=>s.replace(/\s/g,'').toUpperCase();
export function synthReferences(result:SangerDesignResult,c:SangerCandidate):SynthReference[]{const a=selectedAmplicons(result,c);return a.map((r,i)=>({name:r.name,sourceReference:r.sourceReference,sequence:c.direction==='reverse'?reverseComplement(r.sequence):r.sequence,cuts:r.cuts.map(cut=>c.direction==='reverse'?r.sequence.length-cut:cut),targetNames:r.targetNames||r.cuts.map((_,t)=>'T'+(t+1)),share:i===a.length-1?100-Math.round(10000/a.length)/100*(a.length-1):Math.round(10000/a.length)/100,primerLength:c.sequence.length,partnerLength:c.partner!.sequence.length,alleles:[]}));}
export function synthWT(ref:SynthReference){return 100-ref.alleles.reduce((s,a)=>s+Number(a.percent),0);}
/** Editable random defaults in sequencing orientation; never alter either primer site. */
export function randomSynthEvent(ref:SynthReference,random:()=>number=Math.random,onlyTarget?:number):SynthEvent {
 const pick=<T>(items:T[])=>items[Math.min(items.length-1,Math.floor(random()*items.length))];
 const end=ref.sequence.length-ref.partnerLength;
 const choices=(['insertion','deletion','substitution']as const).map(kind=>({kind,targets:ref.cuts.map((cut,target)=>({target,positions:Array.from({length:7},(_,i)=>cut+i-3).filter(start=>start>=ref.primerLength&&start<=end&&(kind==='insertion'||start<end&&/^[ACGT]$/.test(ref.sequence[start])))})).filter(t=>t.positions.length&&(onlyTarget===undefined||t.target===onlyTarget))})).filter(c=>c.targets.length);
 if(!choices.length)throw Error(ref.name+': no editable bases within ±3 bp of a cut.');
 const {kind,targets}=pick(choices),{target,positions}=pick(targets),start=pick(positions),offset=start-ref.cuts[target];
 let maxLength=kind==='insertion'?10:Math.min(10,end-start);
 if(kind==='substitution'){let valid=0;while(valid<maxLength&&/^[ACGT]$/.test(ref.sequence[start+valid]))valid++;maxLength=valid;}
 const length=1+Math.floor(random()*maxLength);
 const sequence=kind==='deletion'?'':Array.from({length},(_,i)=>pick('ACGT'.split('').filter(base=>kind!=='substitution'||base!==ref.sequence[start+i]))).join('');
 return {kind,target,offset,length,sequence,...(kind==='substitution'?{selectionStart:start,before:ref.sequence.slice(start,start+length)}:{})};
}

/** Random editable default, drawn without replacing existing or manually edited
 * percentages. Integer hundredths keep WT subtraction and finite mixing exact. */
export function randomSynthPercent(existing:number[],random:()=>number=Math.random,avoid:number[]=[]):number {
 const remaining=Math.floor((100-existing.reduce((sum,value)=>sum+Number(value),0))*100+1e-6);
 if(remaining<1)throw Error('No WT percentage remains. Reduce an existing group first.');
 const upper=Math.min(1700,remaining),lower=Math.min(300,upper),used=new Set([...existing,...avoid].map(value=>Math.round(value*100))),choices:number[]=[];
 for(let value=lower;value<=upper;value++)if(!used.has(value))choices.push(value);
 if(!choices.length)for(let value=1;value<=remaining;value++)if(!used.has(value))choices.push(value);
 if(!choices.length)throw Error('No distinct positive percentage remains. Adjust existing groups first.');
 return choices[Math.min(choices.length-1,Math.floor(random()*choices.length))]/100;
}

function resolved(ref:SynthReference,event:SynthEvent){
 const sequence=dna(event.sequence||''),start=event.kind==='substitution'?event.selectionStart:ref.cuts[event.target]+Number(event.offset);
 if(!Number.isInteger(start)||start===undefined)throw Error(ref.name+': select a valid edit position.');
 const removed=event.kind==='insertion'?0:event.kind==='deletion'?Number(event.length):(event.before||'').length;
 if(event.kind==='insertion'&&(!/^[ACGT]+$/.test(sequence)||sequence.length>30))throw Error('Insertion requires 1–30 A/C/G/T bases.');
 if(event.kind==='deletion'&&(!Number.isInteger(removed)||removed<1||removed>500))throw Error('Deletion length must be 1–500 bp.');
 if(event.kind==='substitution'&&(!event.before||sequence.length!==event.before.length||!/^[ACGT]+$/.test(sequence)||sequence===event.before||ref.sequence.slice(start,start+removed)!==event.before))throw Error('Select the original bases and enter a different A/C/G/T sequence of the same length.');
 if(start<ref.primerLength||start+removed>ref.sequence.length-ref.partnerLength)throw Error('Edits must stay between the two primer binding sites.');
 return {start,end:start+removed,inserted:event.kind==='deletion'?'':sequence,event};
}
export function synthAlleleSequence(ref:SynthReference,events:SynthEvent[]):string {
 if(!events.length)throw Error('Add at least one edit to each variant allele.');
 const edits=events.map(e=>resolved(ref,e)).sort((a,b)=>a.start-b.start);
 for(let i=1;i<edits.length;i++)if(edits[i].start<edits[i-1].end||edits[i].start===edits[i-1].start)throw Error('Edits on one allele must not overlap or share an insertion boundary.');
 let out=ref.sequence;for(const e of edits.reverse())out=out.slice(0,e.start)+e.inserted+out.slice(e.end);return out;
}
export function synthPattern(ref:SynthReference,events:SynthEvent[]){const sizes=ref.cuts.map(()=>0);for(const e of events){if(e.kind!=='substitution')sizes[e.target]+=(e.kind==='insertion'?dna(e.sequence).length:-Number(e.length));}return sizes.join('/');}
export function synthDescription(events:SynthEvent[]){return events.length?events.map(e=>e.kind==='substitution'?`${e.before}→${dna(e.sequence)} at amplicon ${Number(e.selectionStart)+1}`:`T${e.target+1} ${e.kind} ${e.offset>=0?'+':''}${e.offset}: ${e.kind==='insertion'?dna(e.sequence):e.length+' bp'}`).join('; '):'WT';}
export function writeSynthTrace(parts:{sequence:string;weight:number}[],cycles:number,name:string){const peaks=Array.from({length:cycles},(_,i)=>14+i*10),channels=TRACE_BASES.map(()=>new Uint16Array(cycles*10+30).fill(2)),calls:string[]=[];
 for(let i=0;i<cycles;i++){const values=TRACE_BASES.map(b=>parts.reduce((s,p)=>s+(p.sequence[i]===b?p.weight:0),0));calls.push(TRACE_BASES[values.indexOf(Math.max(...values))]);for(let b=0;b<4;b++)for(let d=-5;d<=5;d++)channels[b][peaks[i]+d]+=Math.round(1200*values[b]*Math.exp(-d*d/4));}
 return writeAbif(calls.join(''),peaks,channels,'SYNTHETIC_'+name);
}
export function generateSangerSynth(result:SangerDesignResult,c:SangerCandidate,refs:SynthReference[],mixSeed?:number):SynthDataset {
 const seed=(mixSeed??Math.floor(Math.random()*4294967296))>>>0;let randomState=seed;const random=()=>{randomState=(Math.imul(randomState,1664525)+1013904223)>>>0;return randomState/4294967296;};
 if(refs.some(r=>!Number.isFinite(r.share)||r.share<0)||Math.abs(refs.reduce((s,r)=>s+r.share,0)-100)>.001)throw Error('Reference shares must total 100%.');
 const template=makeAnalysisTemplate(result,c),products=selectedAmplicons(result,c);
 if(refs.length!==products.length||refs.some((r,i)=>r.name!==products[i].name))throw Error('The recipe no longer matches the selected primer pair.');
 const truth:SynthTruth[]=[],controlParts:{sequence:string;weight:number}[]=[],editParts:{sequence:string;weight:number}[]=[];let maxDeletion=10,maxInsertion=10;
 refs.forEach((ref,i)=>{
  const wt=ref.profiles?100:synthWT(ref);if(!Number.isFinite(wt)||wt<-.000001||ref.alleles.some(a=>!Number.isFinite(a.percent)||a.percent<0||a.percent>100))throw Error(ref.name+': variant percentages must total no more than 100%.');
  if(/[^ACGT]/.test(ref.sequence))throw Error('Resolve N or other ambiguous bases before generating synthetic traces.');
  const expected=c.direction==='reverse'?reverseComplement(products[i].sequence):products[i].sequence;
  if(ref.sequence!==expected)throw Error('Reference sequence cannot be edited directly.');
  const add=(allele:string,percent:number,events:SynthEvent[],targetStates?:number[])=>{if(percent<=0||ref.share===0)return;const sequence=events.length?synthAlleleSequence(ref,events):ref.sequence;for(const e of events){if(e.kind==='deletion')maxDeletion=Math.max(maxDeletion,e.length);if(e.kind==='insertion')maxInsertion=Math.max(maxInsertion,dna(e.sequence).length);}const inputSequence=c.direction==='reverse'?reverseComplement(sequence):sequence;truth.push({targetStates,reference:ref.name,sourceReference:products[i].sourceReference,allele,share:ref.share,percent,totalPercent:ref.share*percent/100,pattern:synthPattern(ref,events),description:synthDescription(events),events:structuredClone(events),sequence,inputSequence,substitution:events.some(e=>e.kind==='substitution')});editParts.push({sequence:sequence.slice(ref.primerLength),weight:ref.share/100*percent/100});};
  controlParts.push({sequence:ref.sequence.slice(ref.primerLength),weight:ref.share/100});if(ref.profiles){for(const group of mixTargetProfiles(ref,random))add(group.allele,group.percent,group.events,group.targetStates);}else{add('WT',Math.max(0,wt),[]);ref.alleles.forEach((a,j)=>{if(a.events.length!==1)throw Error('Each group must contain exactly one edit.');add('Group '+(j+1),a.percent,a.events);});}
 });
 const active=[...controlParts,...editParts].filter(p=>p.weight>0),cycles=Math.min(result.settings.readEnd,...active.map(p=>p.sequence.length))-20;
 if(cycles<150)throw Error('The edited products leave fewer than 150 complete sequencing cycles.');
 if(c.sites.some(h=>h.cutOffsets.some(cut=>cut+Math.ceil(result.settings.window/2)>cycles)))throw Error('An edited product ends before the full target window. Use a longer product or a shorter deletion.');
 const control=writeSynthTrace(controlParts,cycles,'control'),edit=writeSynthTrace(editParts,cycles,'edit');template.settings={...template.settings,maxDeletion,maxInsertion,maxIndel:Math.min(30,Math.max(maxDeletion,maxInsertion)),analysisPolicy:'automatic'};
 const input:AutomaticSangerInput={references:template.amplicons.map(a=>({name:a.name,sequence:a.sequence,cut:a.cuts[0],cuts:a.cuts})),primer:template.primer,control:{...parseSangerTrace(control,'SYNTHETIC_control.ab1'),synthetic:true},sample:{...parseSangerTrace(edit,'SYNTHETIC_edit.ab1'),synthetic:true},maxDeletion,maxInsertion,synthetic:true};
 return {control,edit,truth,template,cycles,recipe:structuredClone(refs),mixing:refs.some(r=>r.profiles)?{method:'random-target-pairing',seed,populationPerReference:10000}:undefined,input};
}
export function synthTruthCsv(data:SynthDataset){const rows=[['Reference','Source reference','Group','Reference %','Within-reference %','Total sample %','Target size pattern','Edits'],...data.truth.map(t=>[t.reference,t.sourceReference,t.allele,t.share,t.percent,t.totalPercent,t.pattern,t.description])];return rows.map(r=>r.map(v=>'"'+String(v).replace(/"/g,'""')+'"').join(',')).join('\r\n');}
export function compareSangerSynth(data:{truth:SynthTruth[];template:{amplicons:{name:string;sequence:string;cuts:number[]}[]}},result:SangerAnalysisResult,displayViews?:SangerAnnotationView[]):SynthComparison[]{
 const out:SynthComparison[]=[],views=displayViews||sangerAnnotationViews(result.annotations||[],0,60,true);
 for(const ref of data.template.amplicons){
  const truth=data.truth.filter(t=>t.reference===ref.name),view=views.find(a=>a.name===ref.name),count=ref.cuts.length,zero=ref.cuts.map(()=>0).join('/'),withSub=truth.some(t=>t.substitution),sequenceMode=truth.every(t=>!!t.inputSequence),groups=new Map<string,typeof truth>();
  const sequenceGroups=result.diagnostics?.algorithmVersion?.startsWith('sanger-sequence-groups-v');
  const expectedShare=truth[0]?.share??0,estimatedShare=view?view.share*100:result.pooled?.amplicons.find(a=>a.name===ref.name)?.share!==undefined?result.pooled.amplicons.find(a=>a.name===ref.name)!.share*100:data.template.amplicons.length===1?100:0;
  out.push({reference:ref.name,group:'Reference share',scope:'reference',truth:expectedShare,estimated:estimatedShare,delta:estimatedShare-expectedShare,recovery:'Reference allocation',placement:'—'});
  for(const row of truth){const key=row.substitution&&!sequenceGroups?'sub:'+row.description:sequenceMode?row.pattern+':'+referenceChecksum(row.inputSequence):row.pattern;groups.set(key,[...(groups.get(key)||[]),row]);}
  type Inferred={fraction:number;pattern:string;sequenceKey?:string;unphased:boolean;unknown:boolean};const inferred=new Map<string,Inferred>();
  for(const row of view?.rows||[]){const pattern=row.patternLabel?row.patternLabel.split(' · ').map(p=>Number(p.split(': ')[1].replace(' bp',''))).join('/'):count===1?String(row.size):null;if(pattern===null)continue;const unknown=!row.sequenceKey,key=sequenceMode?pattern+':'+(row.sequenceKey||'unresolved'):pattern,old=inferred.get(key);if(old){old.fraction+=row.fraction*100;old.unphased||=row.sequenceState==='unphased';old.unknown||=unknown;}else inferred.set(key,{fraction:row.fraction*100,pattern,sequenceKey:row.sequenceKey,unphased:row.sequenceState==='unphased',unknown});}
  for(const [key,rows] of groups){
   const pattern=rows[0].pattern,expected=rows.reduce((sum,t)=>sum+t.percent,0),unresolved=key.startsWith('sub:')||!sequenceGroups&&withSub&&pattern===zero,actual=unresolved?undefined:inferred.get(key),unknownMass=sequenceMode&&[...inferred.values()].some(r=>r.pattern===pattern&&r.unknown&&r.fraction>0),estimated=unresolved||!actual&&unknownMass?null:actual?.fraction??0;
   const placement=unresolved?'Substitution attribution not resolved by current engine':!actual?unknownMass?'Sequence not attributable':'Not recovered':!sequenceMode?'Truth sequence not provided':actual.unphased?'Sequence candidate matched; linkage unresolved':'Sequence matched';
   out.push({reference:ref.name,group:rows.map(r=>r.description).join(' | '),scope:'group',pattern,sequenceKey:sequenceMode&&!unresolved?referenceChecksum(rows[0].inputSequence):undefined,sequenceScope:sequenceMode?'sequence':'pattern',truth:expected,estimated,delta:estimated===null?null:estimated-expected,recovery:estimated===null?'Not attributable':actual?.unphased?'Candidate group':estimated>0?'Recovered group':'Missed group',placement});
   if(!unresolved)inferred.delete(key);
  }
  for(const row of inferred.values()){const unresolved=row.unknown||!sequenceGroups&&withSub&&row.pattern===zero;out.push({reference:ref.name,group:'Estimated '+row.pattern+' bp',scope:'group',pattern:row.pattern,sequenceKey:row.sequenceKey,sequenceScope:sequenceMode?'sequence':'pattern',truth:unresolved?null:0,estimated:row.fraction,delta:unresolved?null:row.fraction,recovery:unresolved?'Unresolved sequences':'Extra group',placement:row.unknown?'Sequence not attributable':row.unphased?'Unphased sequence candidate':'Estimated sequence available'});}
 }
 return out;
}

export function synthPickerContext(ref:SynthReference,target:number,guides:string[]=[]) {
 const cut=ref.cuts[target],start=Math.max(ref.primerLength,cut-60),end=Math.min(ref.sequence.length-ref.partnerLength,cut+60);
 const hits:{start:number;end:number;target:number}[]=[];
 guides.forEach((g,t)=>{g=dna(g);if(!g)return;for(const query of new Set([g,reverseComplement(g)])){let at=ref.sequence.indexOf(query,start);while(at>=0&&at<end){if(at<=ref.cuts[t]&&at+query.length>=ref.cuts[t])hits.push({start:at,end:at+query.length,target:t});at=ref.sequence.indexOf(query,at+1);}}});
 const cells=Array.from({length:Math.max(0,end-start)},(_,i)=>{const index=start+i;return {index,base:ref.sequence[index],relative:index-cut,cut:ref.cuts.includes(index),cutName:ref.targetNames[ref.cuts.indexOf(index)]||'',guide:hits.some(g=>index>=g.start&&index<g.end)};});
 const rows=Array.from({length:Math.ceil(cells.length/60)},(_,i)=>({relative:cells[i*60].relative,cells:cells.slice(i*60,(i+1)*60)}));
 return {start,end,cut,rows,hasGuide:hits.length>0};
}

END MODULE


BEGIN MODULE src/app/utils/sanger-target-profile.ts
import type {SangerAnnotation,SangerJointEvent} from '../models/sanger-analysis';
import type {BenchmarkTruth} from './sanger-benchmark';
import type {SynthTruth} from './sanger-synthetic-benchmark';
import {sangerEditedSequence,sangerSequenceKey} from './sanger-sequence-groups';
import {reverseComplement} from './biology.utils';
export function targetEvents(events:SangerJointEvent[],target:number){return events.filter(e=>e.target===target+1||e.target===0);}
export function projectTargetAnnotation(a:SangerAnnotation,target:number):SangerAnnotation {
 const cut=a.cuts?.[target]??a.cut;return {...a,cut,cuts:[cut],components:a.components.map(c=>{const events=targetEvents(c.events||[],target),size=c.pathSizes?.[target]??events.reduce((s,e)=>s+e.inserted-(e.end-e.start),0);return {...c,size,pathSizes:[size],events};})};
}
/** Marginalize whole-molecule truth for display/evaluation only. */
export function projectTargetTruth(data:BenchmarkTruth,a:SangerAnnotation,target:number):BenchmarkTruth {
 const recipe=data.recipe?.find(r=>r.name===a.name),reverse=recipe?recipe.sequence!==a.reference&&recipe.sequence===reverseComplement(a.reference):a.direction==='reverse',cuts=recipe?.cuts||a.cuts||[a.cut],profile=recipe?.profiles?.find(p=>p.target===target),groups=new Map<string,SynthTruth>();
 for(const t of data.truth.filter(t=>t.reference===a.name)){
  const events=t.events.filter(e=>e.target===target),converted:SangerJointEvent[]=events.map(e=>{const start=Number(e.kind==='substitution'?e.selectionStart:cuts[e.target]+e.offset),end=start+(e.kind==='insertion'?0:e.kind==='deletion'?e.length:(e.before||'').length),inserted=e.kind==='deletion'?'':e.sequence;return {target:e.target+1,start:reverse?a.reference.length-end:start,end:reverse?a.reference.length-start:end,inserted:inserted.length,insertedSequence:reverse?reverseComplement(inserted):inserted};});
  const inputSequence=t.inputSequence?sangerEditedSequence(a.reference,converted):'',pattern=t.pattern.split('/')[target]||'0',key=pattern+':'+(inputSequence||JSON.stringify(events)),old=groups.get(key),index=profile?.alleles.findIndex(g=>JSON.stringify(g.events)===JSON.stringify(events))??-1;
  if(old){old.percent+=t.percent;old.totalPercent+=t.totalPercent;continue;}
  const description=!events.length?'WT':events.map(e=>e.kind==='substitution'?`${e.before}→${e.sequence}`:`${e.kind} ${e.offset>=0?'+':''}${e.offset}: ${e.kind==='insertion'?e.sequence:e.length+' bp'}`).join('; ');
  groups.set(key,{...t,allele:!events.length?'WT':index>=0?'Group '+(index+1):t.allele,events,pattern,description,substitution:events.some(e=>e.kind==='substitution'),inputSequence,sequence:reverse?reverseComplement(inputSequence):inputSequence});
 }
 return {...data,truth:[...groups.values()]};
}

END MODULE


BEGIN MODULE src/app/utils/sanger-trace-preview.ts
import {SangerTrace,SANGER_ANALYSIS_DEFAULTS} from '../models/sanger-analysis';
import {alignTraceSequence} from '../workers/core/sanger-analysis';
import {registerTrace} from '../workers/core/sanger-pooled';
import {exactBindingSites} from '../workers/core/sanger-primer-designer';
import {reverseComplement} from './biology.utils';
export interface PreviewReference {name:string;sequence:string;cuts:number[];targetNames?:string[]}
export interface PreviewSite {name:string;cut:number}
/** Preview registration only: no edit fitting, ground truth or mixture changes. */
export function tracePreviewSites(refs:PreviewReference[],primer:string,trace:SangerTrace,knownTrim?:number):PreviewSite[]{
 if(!refs.length||!trace.sequence.length)return [];
 const label=(r:PreviewReference,t:number)=>r.name.replace(/_\d+-\d+$/,'')+' · '+(r.targetNames?.[t]||'T'+(t+1));
 if(/^[ACGT]{12,40}$/.test(primer)){
  try{const prepared=refs.map(r=>{const hits=exactBindingSites(primer,{...r,cuts:r.cuts});if(hits.length!==1)throw Error('Ambiguous primer site.');const hit=hits[0],sequence=hit.direction==='forward'?r.sequence:reverseComplement(r.sequence),origin=hit.direction==='forward'?hit.end:r.sequence.length-hit.start,cuts=r.cuts.map(c=>(hit.direction==='forward'?c:r.sequence.length-c)-origin);return {name:r.name,sequence:sequence.slice(origin),cut:Math.min(...cuts),cuts};});
   const trim=knownTrim??registerTrace(trace,prepared,{...SANGER_ANALYSIS_DEFAULTS,readStart:0}).trim;
   return prepared.flatMap((r,i)=>r.cuts.map((cut,t)=>({name:label(refs[i],t),cut:cut-trim}))).filter(s=>s.cut>=0&&s.cut<trace.sequence.length-2);
  }catch{if(refs.length>1)return [];}
 }
 if(refs.length!==1)return [];
 const ref=refs[0],sites:PreviewSite[]=[];
 for(const [t,cut] of ref.cuts.entries()){
  const choices:{score:number;cut:number}[]=[];
  for(const reverse of [false,true]){const seq=reverse?reverseComplement(ref.sequence):ref.sequence,c=reverse?ref.sequence.length-cut:cut,anchor=seq.slice(Math.max(0,c-20),c+20);if(anchor.length<30||seq.indexOf(anchor)!==seq.lastIndexOf(anchor))continue;const radius=Math.min(900,trace.sequence.length+100),lo=Math.max(0,c-radius),hi=Math.min(seq.length,c+radius);
   try{const alignment=alignTraceSequence(seq.slice(lo,hi),trace.sequence),mapped=alignment.columns.filter(x=>x.ref!==null&&x.query!==null),before=mapped.filter(x=>x.ref!+lo<c),after=mapped.filter(x=>x.ref!+lo>=c);if(alignment.identity<.8||mapped.length<60||before.length<15||after.length<15)continue;choices.push({score:alignment.score,cut:after[0].query!});}catch{continue;}
  }
  choices.sort((a,b)=>b.score-a.score);if(choices[0]&&(!choices[1]||choices[0].score>choices[1].score))sites.push({name:label(ref,t),cut:choices[0].cut});
 }
 return sites;
}

END MODULE


BEGIN MODULE src/app/utils/sanger-trace.ts
import {parseAbif} from './abif';
import {parseScf} from './scf';
/** Detect by content, not by extension. FASTA/FASTQ cannot supply a chromatogram. */
export function parseSangerTrace(buffer:ArrayBuffer,name='trace.ab1'){
 const signature=new TextDecoder().decode(new Uint8Array(buffer,0,Math.min(4,buffer.byteLength)));
 if(signature==='ABIF')return parseAbif(buffer,name);
 if(signature==='.scf')return parseScf(buffer,name);
 throw Error('Choose a basecalled Sanger AB1/ABI or SCF chromatogram.');
}

BEGIN MODULE src/app/utils/sanger-truth-groups.ts
import {BenchmarkTruth} from './sanger-benchmark';
import {SangerAnnotation,SangerJointEvent} from '../models/sanger-analysis';
import {sangerAnnotationViews,SangerAnnotationRow} from './sanger-annotation';
import {reverseComplement} from './biology.utils';
export interface TruthGroupView {label:string;description:string;sequence:string;row:SangerAnnotationRow;fraction:number;edited:boolean}
/** Display adapter only: ground truth never enters the analysis worker. */
export function sangerTruthGroups(data:BenchmarkTruth|null,annotation:SangerAnnotation,radius:number,focusCut=annotation.cut):TruthGroupView[]{
 if(!data)return [];
 return data.truth.filter(t=>t.reference===annotation.name).map(t=>{
  const recipe=data.recipe?.find(r=>r.name===annotation.name),reverse=recipe?recipe.sequence!==annotation.reference&&recipe.sequence===reverseComplement(annotation.reference):t.sequence!==t.inputSequence&&reverseComplement(t.sequence)===t.inputSequence;
  const cuts=recipe?.cuts||(annotation.cuts||[annotation.cut]).map(c=>reverse?annotation.reference.length-c:c);
  const events:SangerJointEvent[]=t.events.map(e=>{const start=e.kind==='substitution'?e.selectionStart:cuts[e.target]+e.offset,end=Number(start)+(e.kind==='insertion'?0:e.kind==='deletion'?e.length:(e.before||'').length),inserted=e.kind==='deletion'?'':e.sequence;return {target:e.target+1,start:reverse?annotation.reference.length-end:Number(start),end:reverse?annotation.reference.length-Number(start):end,inserted:inserted.length,insertedSequence:reverse?reverseComplement(inserted):inserted};});
  const wt=t.description==='WT',sequence=t.inputSequence||'',valid=sequence&&materializeGroup(annotation.reference,events)===sequence;
  const row=sangerAnnotationViews([{...annotation,sequenceGrouping:false,cut:focusCut,cuts:[focusCut],estimable:true,components:[{size:t.pattern.split('/').map(Number).reduce((sum,v)=>sum+v,0),fraction:t.percent/100,pathSizes:t.pattern.includes('/')?t.pattern.split('/').map(Number):undefined,events:valid?events:[],unresolved:!valid&&!wt}]}],0,radius)[0].rows[0];
  return {label:t.allele,description:t.description,sequence,row,fraction:t.percent/100,edited:!wt};
 }).filter(t=>!!t.row);
}
export function materializeGroup(reference:string,events:SangerJointEvent[]):string{
 let result=reference;for(const e of [...events].sort((a,b)=>b.start-a.start))result=result.slice(0,e.start)+(e.insertedSequence||'')+result.slice(e.end);return result;
}

BEGIN MODULE src/app/utils/sanger-window-check.ts
import {PooledAmplicon,SangerAnalysisSettings} from '../models/sanger-analysis';
/** Conservative independence rule on the reference axis. Not a biological
 * minimum guide spacing: the joint decoder can fit overlapping target regions.
 * For one target the window is expanded on both sides by origin+max(deletion,insertion) uncertainty.
 * This covers either deletion direction. Require a one-base separating gap. */
export function sangerWindowCheck(products:PooledAmplicon[],settings:SangerAnalysisSettings){
 const window=settings.window,origin=settings.editStartRadius??null,deletion=settings.largeDeletions?(settings.maxDeletion??200):settings.maxIndel,insertion=settings.maxInsertion??30,requiredDistance=origin===null?null:window+2*Math.max(deletion,insertion)+2*origin+1;
 return {window,origin,deletion,insertion,requiredDistance,references:products.map(product=>{const cuts=[...(product.cuts??[product.cut])].sort((a,b)=>a-b),windows=cuts.map(cut=>({cut,nominalStart:cut-Math.floor(window/2),nominalEnd:cut+Math.ceil(window/2),supportStart:origin===null?0:cut-Math.floor(window/2)-origin-Math.max(deletion,insertion),supportEnd:origin===null?product.sequence.length:cut+Math.ceil(window/2)+origin+Math.max(deletion,insertion)})),gaps=cuts.slice(1).map((cut,i)=>{const actual=cut-cuts[i];return {actual,betweenCutDeletion:origin===null?null:actual+2*origin,betweenCutCovered:origin!==null&&deletion>=actual+2*origin,overlap:requiredDistance===null||actual<requiredDistance,maximumIndependentWindow:origin===null?0:Math.max(0,actual-2*Math.max(deletion,insertion)-2*origin-1),maximumIndependentDeletion:origin===null||insertion>Math.floor((actual-window-2*origin-1)/2)?0:Math.max(0,Math.floor((actual-window-2*origin-1)/2)),maximumIndependentShift:origin===null?0:Math.max(0,Math.floor((actual-window-2*origin-1)/2))};}),viewStart=Math.max(0,Math.min(...windows.map(w=>w.supportStart))-20),viewEnd=Math.min(product.sequence.length,Math.max(...windows.map(w=>w.supportEnd))+20),span=Math.max(1,viewEnd-viewStart),x=(p:number)=>Math.max(0,Math.min(800,800*(p-viewStart)/span));return {name:product.name,length:product.sequence.length,windows,gaps,viewStart,viewEnd,x};})};
}

END MODULE


BEGIN MODULE src/app/utils/scf.ts
import {SangerTrace,TRACE_BASES,TraceBase} from '../models/sanger-analysis';
/** Staden SCF 2.x/3.x. V3 stores channel-major second differences modulo 2^bits.
 * https://staden.sourceforge.net/manual/formats_unix_8.html */
export function parseScf(buffer:ArrayBuffer,name='trace.scf'):SangerTrace{
 const v=new DataView(buffer),bytes=new Uint8Array(buffer),text=(p:number,n:number)=>new TextDecoder().decode(bytes.subarray(p,p+n));
 const bounds=(p:number,n:number)=>{if(!Number.isSafeInteger(p)||!Number.isSafeInteger(n)||p<128||n<0||p+n>bytes.length)throw Error('Truncated or invalid SCF data offset.');};
 if(bytes.length<128)throw Error('Truncated SCF header.');if(text(0,4)!=='.scf')throw Error('Invalid SCF signature.');
 const samples=v.getUint32(4),sampleAt=v.getUint32(8),bases=v.getUint32(12),baseAt=v.getUint32(24),version=Number(text(36,4)),size=v.getUint32(40);
 if(!(version>=2&&version<4)||![1,2].includes(size))throw Error('Supported SCF versions are 2.x/3.x with 8- or 16-bit samples.');
 if(samples<40||samples>1000000||bases<40||bases>3000)throw Error('Use a basecalled SCF read of 40–3,000 bases and at most 1,000,000 scan points.');
 bounds(sampleAt,4*samples*size);bounds(baseAt,12*bases);
 const channels={} as Record<TraceBase,Float32Array>,mod=size===1?256:65536,read=(p:number)=>size===1?v.getUint8(p):v.getUint16(p);
 TRACE_BASES.forEach((b,k)=>{const values=Array.from({length:samples},(_,i)=>read(sampleAt+size*(version>=3?k*samples+i:4*i+k)));if(version>=3)for(let pass=0;pass<2;pass++){let sum=0;for(let i=0;i<samples;i++){sum=(sum+values[i])%mod;values[i]=sum;}}channels[b]=Float32Array.from(values);});
 const peaks=new Uint32Array(bases),quality=new Uint8Array(bases);let sequence='';
 for(let i=0;i<bases;i++){
  const peak=v.getUint32(baseAt+(version>=3?4*i:12*i)),base=text(baseAt+(version>=3?8*bases+i:12*i+8),1).toUpperCase().replace('-','N');
  if(!/^[ACGTRYSWKMBDHVN]$/.test(base))throw Error('Invalid SCF base call.');
  if(peak>=samples||(i>0&&peak<=peaks[i-1]))throw Error('SCF peak positions are incomplete or not strictly increasing.');
  peaks[i]=peak;sequence+=base;const k=TRACE_BASES.indexOf(base as TraceBase);quality[i]=k<0?0:v.getUint8(baseAt+(version>=3?4*bases+k*bases+i:12*i+4+k));
 }
 const n=v.getUint32(28),at=v.getUint32(32);let sampleName=name;if(n){bounds(at,n);sampleName=text(at,n).match(/(?:^|\n)NAME=([^\n\0]+)/)?.[1]||name;}
 return {name,sampleName,sequence,peaks,quality:quality.some(q=>q>0)?quality:null,channels};
}

END MODULE

BEGIN MODULE src/app/workers/sanger-analysis.worker.ts
/// <reference lib="webworker" />
import{certifyNativeBenchmark}from'../utils/sanger-native-signal-certificate';
import{assessSangerBenchmark}from'../utils/sanger-benchmark-assessment';
import {analyzeAutomaticSanger} from './core/sanger-automatic';
let optimizer:Promise<any>|undefined;
const localOptimizer=()=>optimizer??=import(/* @vite-ignore */new URL('vendor/highs-1.15.4/highs.mjs',self.location.href).href).then(module=>module.default());
addEventListener('message',async({data})=>{
 try{
  if(data.action==='benchmark'){
   postMessage({type:'progress',stage:'Checking ground truth after inference'});
   const solver=await localOptimizer(),native=certifyNativeBenchmark(data.truth,data.controlBytes,data.sampleBytes,data.result,solver),assessment=assessSangerBenchmark(data.truth,data.references,data.result,data.threshold,native);
   postMessage({type:'benchmark',assessment});return;
  }
  if(data.action!=='automatic')throw Error('Unsupported Sanger request. Use automatic analysis.');
  const needsOptimizer=data.groupRecovery&&data.references?.some((r:any)=>r.cuts?.length===2)&&data.maxDeletion<=10&&data.maxInsertion<=10;
  if(needsOptimizer)postMessage({type:'progress',stage:'Loading local sequence optimizer'});
  const solver=needsOptimizer?await localOptimizer():undefined;
  const result=analyzeAutomaticSanger(data,(stage:string)=>postMessage({type:'progress',stage}),solver);
  postMessage({type:'result',result});
 }catch(error){postMessage({type:'error',message:error instanceof Error?error.message:String(error)});}
});
