BEGIN MODULE src/app/services/ncbi-reference-library.service.ts
import {Injectable} from '@angular/core';
import {LibraryGene,LibrarySpecies,LibraryReference,ReferenceSource,EnsemblDivision} from '../models/reference-source';
import {referenceChecksum} from '../utils/reference-checksum';
const BASE='https://eutils.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/entrez/eutils/';
const endpoint=(method:string,params:Record<string,string|number>)=>BASE+method+'.fcgi?'+new URLSearchParams(Object.fromEntries(Object.entries({tool:'CasMango',...params}).map(([k,v])=>[k,String(v)])));
@Injectable({providedIn:'root'})
export class NcbiReferenceLibraryService {
 private nextRequest=0;
 private async request(method:string,params:Record<string,string|number>,signal?:AbortSignal,text=false):Promise<any>{
  const wait=Math.max(0,this.nextRequest-Date.now());this.nextRequest=Date.now()+wait+350;
  if(wait)await new Promise<void>(resolve=>setTimeout(resolve,wait));
  if(signal?.aborted)throw Error('Request cancelled.');
  const controller=new AbortController(),cancel=()=>controller.abort(),timer=setTimeout(cancel,30000);signal?.addEventListener('abort',cancel,{once:true});
  try{const response=await fetch(endpoint(method,params),{signal:controller.signal,credentials:'omit',cache:'no-store'});if(!response.ok)throw Error(response.status===429?'NCBI request limit reached. Retry shortly.':`NCBI returned ${response.status}.`);const data=text?await response.text():await response.json();if(data?.error||data?.esearchresult?.ERROR)throw Error(String(data.error||data.esearchresult.ERROR));return data;}
  catch(e){if(controller.signal.aborted)throw Error(signal?.aborted?'Request cancelled.':'NCBI timed out. Retry or choose another source.');if(e instanceof TypeError)throw Error('Could not reach NCBI. Retry or choose another source.');throw e;}
  finally{clearTimeout(timer);signal?.removeEventListener('abort',cancel);}
 }
 async genome(name:string,signal?:AbortSignal):Promise<LibrarySpecies>{
  const scientific=name.trim().replace(/_/g,' ');if(!/^[A-Za-z][A-Za-z .()-]{1,100}$/.test(scientific))throw Error('Enter a common or scientific species name.');
  const search=await this.request('esearch',{db:'taxonomy',term:scientific+'[Scientific Name]',retmode:'json',retmax:2},signal),ids=search.esearchresult?.idlist;
  if(!Array.isArray(ids)||ids.length!==1||Number(search.esearchresult.count)!==1)throw Error('NCBI species not uniquely identified. Enter its full scientific name.');
  const data=await this.request('esummary',{db:'taxonomy',id:ids[0],retmode:'json'},signal),tax=data.result?.[ids[0]];
  if(String(tax?.scientificname||'').toLowerCase()!==scientific.toLowerCase()||String(tax.taxid??tax.uid)!==String(ids[0]))throw Error('NCBI returned unverified species metadata.');
  return {name:String(ids[0]),display_name:tax.scientificname,assembly:'RefSeq genomic accession selected per gene'};
 }
 private gene(data:any,taxonomyId:string):LibraryGene{
  if(String(data?.organism?.taxid)!==taxonomyId)throw Error('The NCBI gene belongs to a different species.');
  if(!/^\d+$/.test(String(data.uid))||data.status||data.currentid&&data.currentid!=='0')throw Error('The NCBI gene record is discontinued or replaced. Search its current Gene ID.');
  const loci=(data.genomicinfo||[]).filter((g:any)=>/^(NC|NW|NT)_\d+\.\d+$/.test(g.chraccver)&&Number.isInteger(g.chrstart)&&Number.isInteger(g.chrstop)&&g.chrstart>=0&&g.chrstop>=0);
  if(loci.length!==1)throw Error('This NCBI gene lacks one unambiguous RefSeq genomic locus. Use a verified accession/FASTA or Ensembl.');
  const g=loci[0];return {id:String(data.uid),display_name:data.name,description:data.description,species:taxonomyId,taxonomyId,assembly_name:'RefSeq '+g.chraccver,seq_region_name:g.chraccver,start:Math.min(g.chrstart,g.chrstop)+1,end:Math.max(g.chrstart,g.chrstop)+1,strand:g.chrstart<=g.chrstop?1:-1,object_type:'Gene'};
 }
 async lookup(taxonomyId:string,id:string,signal?:AbortSignal):Promise<LibraryGene>{
  if(!/^\d+$/.test(id)||!/^\d+$/.test(taxonomyId))throw Error('NCBI requires a numeric Gene ID and taxonomy ID. Ensembl IDs require the Ensembl source.');
  const data=await this.request('esummary',{db:'gene',id,retmode:'json'},signal);const gene=this.gene(data.result?.[id],taxonomyId);if(gene.id!==id)throw Error('NCBI returned a different Gene ID.');return gene;
 }
 async search(taxonomyId:string,query:string,mode:'id'|'symbol',signal?:AbortSignal):Promise<LibraryGene[]>{
  if(!/^\d+$/.test(taxonomyId))throw Error('Choose a verified NCBI species.');
  let ids:string[];
  if(mode==='id'){ids=[...new Set(query.trim().split(/[\s,;]+/).filter(Boolean))];if(!ids.length||ids.length>8||ids.some(id=>!/^\d+$/.test(id)))throw Error('Enter 1–8 numeric NCBI Gene IDs, or choose Ensembl for Ensembl IDs.');}
  else {if(!/^[A-Za-z0-9_. -]{1,100}$/.test(query.trim()))throw Error('Enter a gene name or numeric NCBI Gene ID.');const data=await this.request('esearch',{db:'gene',term:`${query.trim()}[Gene Name] AND ${taxonomyId}[Taxonomy ID] AND alive[prop]`,retmode:'json',retmax:9},signal);if(Number(data.esearchresult?.count)>8)throw Error('More than eight genes match. Use numeric NCBI Gene IDs.');ids=data.esearchresult?.idlist;if(!Array.isArray(ids))throw Error('NCBI returned an invalid gene search.');}
  if(!ids.length)return [];const data=await this.request('esummary',{db:'gene',id:ids.join(','),retmode:'json'},signal);return ids.map(id=>{const gene=this.gene(data.result?.[id],taxonomyId);if(gene.id!==id)throw Error('NCBI returned a different Gene ID.');return gene;});
 }
 async importGene(gene:Pick<LibraryGene,'id'>,choice:LibrarySpecies,division:EnsemblDivision,upstream:number,downstream:number,signal?:AbortSignal):Promise<LibraryReference>{
  if(![upstream,downstream].every(n=>Number.isInteger(n)&&n>=0&&n<=20000))throw Error('Use whole-number flanks within 0–20,000 bp.');
  const current=await this.lookup(choice.name,gene.id,signal),data=await this.request('esummary',{db:'nuccore',id:current.seq_region_name,retmode:'json'},signal),uids=data.result?.uids,chrom=Array.isArray(uids)&&uids.length===1?data.result[uids[0]]:null;
  if(chrom?.accessionversion!==current.seq_region_name||!Number.isInteger(chrom.slen)||chrom.slen<current.end||Number(chrom.taxid)!==Number(choice.name))throw Error('NCBI could not verify the genomic accession, species and bounds.');
  const start=Math.max(1,current.start-(current.strand===1?upstream:downstream)),end=Math.min(chrom.slen,current.end+(current.strand===1?downstream:upstream));
  if(end-start+1>500000)throw Error('The gene + flanks exceeds 500 kb. Import a smaller verified region manually.');
  const params={db:'nuccore',id:current.seq_region_name,seq_start:start,seq_stop:end,strand:current.strand===1?1:2,rettype:'fasta',retmode:'text'},fasta=String(await this.request('efetch',params,signal,true)).trim(),lines=fasta.split(/\r?\n/),header=lines.shift()||'',sequence=lines.join('').replace(/\s/g,'').toUpperCase();
  const expected='>'+current.seq_region_name+':'+(current.strand===1?`${start}-${end}`:`c${end}-${start}`);
  if(header.split(/\s/)[0]!==expected||!/^[ACGTN]+$/.test(sequence)||sequence.length!==end-start+1)throw Error('NCBI returned a different accession, interval, strand or sequence length. Nothing was imported.');
  return {name:current.display_name||current.id,sequence,source:{provider:'ncbi',division,species:choice.name,speciesLabel:choice.display_name,geneId:current.id,geneVersion:null,assembly:current.assembly_name,chromosome:current.seq_region_name,geneStart:current.start,geneEnd:current.end,strand:current.strand,sequenceStart:start,sequenceEnd:end,upstream,downstream,retrievedAt:new Date().toISOString(),sha256:referenceChecksum(sequence),lookupUrl:endpoint('esummary',{db:'gene',id:current.id,retmode:'json'}),sequenceUrl:endpoint('efetch',params),viewUrl:'https://www.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/gene/'+current.id}};
 }
 latest(source:ReferenceSource,signal?:AbortSignal){return this.importGene({id:source.geneId},{name:source.species,display_name:source.speciesLabel,assembly:source.assembly},source.division,source.upstream,source.downstream,signal);}
}

END MODULE


BEGIN MODULE src/app/services/reference-library.service.ts
import {Injectable} from '@angular/core';
import {EnsemblDivision,LibrarySpecies,LibraryGene,LibraryReference,ReferenceSource} from '../models/reference-source';
import {NcbiReferenceLibraryService} from './ncbi-reference-library.service';
import {referenceChecksum} from '../utils/reference-checksum';
const BASE='https://rest.ensembl.org';
const url=(endpoint:string,params:Record<string,string|number>={})=>BASE+endpoint+'?'+new URLSearchParams({'content-type':'application/json',...Object.fromEntries(Object.entries(params).map(([k,v])=>[k,String(v)]))});
const identifier=(s:string)=>{if(!/^[A-Za-z0-9_.-]{1,100}$/.test(s))throw new Error('Use a valid species name or stable gene ID.');return encodeURIComponent(s);};
@Injectable({providedIn:'root'})
export class ReferenceLibraryService{
 private async json(endpoint:string,params:Record<string,string|number>,signal?:AbortSignal,timeoutMs=30000):Promise<any>{
  const controller=new AbortController(),cancel=()=>controller.abort(),timer=setTimeout(cancel,timeoutMs);signal?.addEventListener('abort',cancel,{once:true});if(signal?.aborted)cancel();
  try{let response:Response;for(let attempt=0;;attempt++){response=await fetch(url(endpoint,params),{signal:controller.signal,cache:'no-store',credentials:'omit',headers:{Accept:'application/json'}});if(attempt===0&&!endpoint.startsWith('/xrefs/')&&[500,502,503,504].includes(response.status)){await new Promise<void>(resolve=>setTimeout(resolve,750));if(controller.signal.aborted)throw new Error('Request cancelled.');continue;}break;}if(!response.ok)throw new Error(response.status===429?'Ensembl request limit reached. Please retry shortly.':response.status>=500?`Ensembl is temporarily unavailable (${response.status}). Retry or choose NCBI / RefSeq.`:`Ensembl returned ${response.status}. Check the ID/species or try again.`);const data=await response.json();if(data?.error)throw new Error(String(data.error));return data;}catch(e){if(controller.signal.aborted)throw new Error(signal?.aborted?'Request cancelled.':'Ensembl timed out. Please retry.');if(e instanceof SyntaxError)throw new Error('Ensembl returned an unreadable response. Please retry.');if(e instanceof TypeError)throw new Error('Could not reach Ensembl. Check your connection or retry; manual FASTA input remains available.');throw e;}finally{clearTimeout(timer);signal?.removeEventListener('abort',cancel);}
 }
 async species(division:EnsemblDivision,signal?:AbortSignal):Promise<LibrarySpecies[]>{const data=await this.json('/info/species',{division},signal,30000);if(!Array.isArray(data.species))throw new Error('Invalid Ensembl species response.');return data.species.filter((s:any)=>typeof s.name==='string'&&s.assembly&&s.display_name).sort((a:LibrarySpecies,b:LibrarySpecies)=>a.display_name.localeCompare(b.display_name)||a.name.localeCompare(b.name));}
 async genome(name:string,signal?:AbortSignal):Promise<LibrarySpecies>{const data=await this.json('/info/genomes/'+identifier(name),{},signal,30000);const divisions=['EnsemblPlants','EnsemblVertebrates','EnsemblMetazoa','EnsemblFungi','EnsemblProtists'];if(data.name!==name||!data.assembly_name||!divisions.includes(data.division))throw Error('Ensembl did not return a verified species/assembly.');return {name:data.name,display_name:data.display_name||data.scientific_name||data.name,assembly:data.assembly_name,strain:data.strain,division:data.division};}
 private gene(data:any,species:string):LibraryGene{if(data.species!==species)throw new Error('The returned gene belongs to a different species/cultivar. Select the matching species.');if(data.object_type!=='Gene')throw new Error('Choose a genomic gene ID, not a transcript or protein ID.');if(!data.id||!data.assembly_name||!data.seq_region_name||!Number.isInteger(data.start)||!Number.isInteger(data.end)||data.start<1||data.end<data.start||![1,-1].includes(data.strand))throw new Error('The gene response lacks valid genomic coordinates.');return data;}
 async lookup(species:string,id:string,signal?:AbortSignal):Promise<LibraryGene>{return this.gene(await this.json('/lookup/id/'+identifier(id),{species},signal),species);}
 async search(species:string,query:string,mode:'id'|'symbol',signal?:AbortSignal):Promise<LibraryGene[]>{identifier(species);if(mode==='id'){const ids=[...new Set(query.trim().split(/[\s,;]+/).filter(Boolean))];if(!ids.length||ids.length>8)throw new Error('Enter 1–8 stable gene IDs.');const found:LibraryGene[]=[];for(const id of ids)found.push(await this.lookup(species,id,signal));return found;}
  if(!query.trim()||query.length>100)throw new Error('Enter a gene symbol.');let matches:any;try{matches=await this.json('/xrefs/symbol/'+identifier(species)+'/'+encodeURIComponent(query.trim()),{object_type:'gene'},signal,8000);}catch(e){if(signal?.aborted||e instanceof Error&&e.message.includes('request limit'))throw e;const direct=await this.json('/lookup/symbol/'+identifier(species)+'/'+encodeURIComponent(query.trim()),{},signal);return [{...this.gene(direct,species),lookupNote:'Direct symbol match; linked-gene listing unavailable. Verify the stable gene ID.'}];}if(!Array.isArray(matches))throw new Error('Invalid Ensembl symbol response.');const linked=matches.filter((m:any)=>m.type==='gene'&&typeof m.id==='string'),hasLrg=linked.some((m:any)=>/^LRG_/.test(m.id)),ids=[...new Set<string>(linked.filter((m:any)=>!/^LRG_/.test(m.id)).map((m:any)=>m.id))];if(!ids.length&&hasLrg)throw new Error('Only LRG reference records matched. Use an Ensembl genomic gene ID.');if(ids.length>8)throw new Error('This symbol maps to more than eight genes. Use stable gene IDs to choose the intended loci.');const found:LibraryGene[]=[];for(const id of ids){const gene=await this.lookup(species,id,signal);found.push(hasLrg?{...gene,lookupNote:'Reference-assembly loci shown; separate LRG records excluded.'}:gene);}return found;
 }
 async importGene(gene:Pick<LibraryGene,'id'>,choice:LibrarySpecies,division:EnsemblDivision,upstream:number,downstream:number,signal?:AbortSignal):Promise<LibraryReference>{
  if(![upstream,downstream].every(v=>Number.isInteger(v)&&v>=0&&v<=20000))throw new Error('Use whole-number flanks within 0–20,000 bp.');
  // Resolve again at import time. Search results must not pin an old live lookup silently.
  const current=await this.lookup(choice.name,gene.id,signal),assembly=await this.json('/info/assembly/'+identifier(choice.name),{},signal);
  const currentCoordinates=assembly.default_coord_system_version===current.assembly_name&&Array.isArray(assembly.coord_system_versions)&&assembly.coord_system_versions.includes(current.assembly_name);
  if(current.assembly_name!==assembly.assembly_name&&!currentCoordinates)throw new Error('Gene and current assembly disagree. Refresh the search before importing.');
  const region=assembly.top_level_region?.find((r:any)=>r.name===current.seq_region_name);if(!region||!Number.isInteger(region.length))throw new Error('Chromosome/contig bounds are unavailable for this gene. Use a verified FASTA reference.');
  const start=Math.max(1,current.start-(current.strand===1?upstream:downstream)),end=Math.min(region.length,current.end+(current.strand===1?downstream:upstream));if(end-start+1>500000)throw new Error('The gene + flanks exceeds 500 kb. Import a smaller verified region manually.');
  const endpoint='/sequence/region/'+identifier(choice.name)+'/'+encodeURIComponent(`${current.seq_region_name}:${start}:${end}:${current.strand}`),params={coord_system_version:current.assembly_name},data=await this.json(endpoint,params,signal),sequence=String(data.seq||'').toUpperCase();
  if(!/^[ACGTN]+$/.test(sequence)||sequence.length!==end-start+1)throw new Error('Ensembl returned an unexpected sequence or length. Nothing was imported.');
  // Region endpoint header identifies the exact assembly, locus and orientation used.
  const parts=String(data.id||data.desc||'').split(':');if(parts.length<6||parts[1]!==current.assembly_name||parts.slice(2,-3).join(':')!==current.seq_region_name||Number(parts.at(-3))!==start||Number(parts.at(-2))!==end||Number(parts.at(-1))!==current.strand)throw new Error('The returned sequence coordinates/assembly do not match the requested locus. Nothing was imported.');
  return {name:current.id,sequence,source:{provider:'ensembl',division,species:choice.name,speciesLabel:choice.display_name,strain:choice.strain,release:choice.release,geneId:current.id,geneVersion:current.version??null,assembly:current.assembly_name,chromosome:current.seq_region_name,geneStart:current.start,geneEnd:current.end,strand:current.strand,sequenceStart:start,sequenceEnd:end,upstream,downstream,retrievedAt:new Date().toISOString(),sha256:referenceChecksum(sequence),lookupUrl:url('/lookup/id/'+identifier(current.id),{species:choice.name}),sequenceUrl:url(endpoint,params),viewUrl:'https://rest.ensembl.org/lookup/id/'+identifier(current.id)+'?'+new URLSearchParams({species:choice.name,'content-type':'application/json'})}};
 }
 async latest(source:ReferenceSource,signal?:AbortSignal){if(source.provider==='ncbi')return new NcbiReferenceLibraryService().latest(source,signal);if(source.provider!=='ensembl')throw new Error('Unsupported reference source.');identifier(source.species);return this.importGene({id:source.geneId},{name:source.species,display_name:source.speciesLabel,assembly:source.assembly,strain:source.strain},source.division,source.upstream,source.downstream,signal);}
}

END MODULE
